# Atharva-Veda Samhitā \[Sanskrit\]

## Atharva-Veda Samhitā \[San\]

TITUS

Text collection: AV

Atharva-Veda

Text: ŚS

Atharva-Veda Samhitā

Śaunaka Recension

On the basis of the editions

Gli inni dell’ Atharvaveda (Saunaka),

trasliterazione a cura di Chatia Orlandi,

Pisa 1991

and

Atharva Veda Sanhita,

herausgegeben von R. Roth und W.D. Whitney,

Berlin 1856

entered by Vladimir Petr und Petr Vavrouek,

Praha 1996;

TITUS version by Jost Gippert,

Frankfurt a.M., 31.1.1997 / 28.2.1998 / 21.6.1998 / 20.10.1999 / 1.6.2000

## Book: 1

1

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye triṣaptāḥ pariyanti viśvā rūpāṇi bibhrataḥ /

Halfverse: c vācas patir balā teṣām tanvo adya dadhātu me //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a punar ehi vacas pate devena manasā saha /

Halfverse: c vasoṣ pate ni ramaya mayy evāstu mayi śrutam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ihaivābhi vi tanūbhe ārtnī iva jyayā /

Halfverse: c vācas patir ni yachatu mayy evāstu mayi śrutam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a upahūto vācas patir upāsmān vācaspatir hvayatām /

Halfverse: c sam śrutena gamemahi mā śrutena vi rādhiṣi //4//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vidmā śarasya pitaram parjanyam bhūridhāyasam /

Halfverse: c vidmo ṣv asya mātaram pṛthivīm bhūrivarpasam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jyāke pari ṇo namāśmānam tanvam kṛdhi /

Halfverse: c vīḍur varīyo ‘rātīr apa dveṣāmsy ā kṛdhi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vṛkṣam yad gāvaḥ pariṣasvajānā anusphuram śaram arcanty ṛbhum /

Halfverse: c śarum asmad yāvaya didyum indra //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yathā dyām ca pṛthivīm cāntas tiṣṭhati tejanam /

Halfverse: c evā rogam cāsrāvam cāntas tiṣṭhatu muñja it //4//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vidmā śarasya pitaram parjanyam śatavṛṣṇyam /

Halfverse: c tenā te tanvṝ3 śam karam pṛthivyām te niṣecanam bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vidmā śarasya pitaram mitram śatavṛṣṇyam /

Halfverse: c tenā te tanvṝ3 śam karam pṛthivyām te niṣecanam bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vidmā śarasya pitaram varuṇam śatavṛṣṇyam /

Halfverse: c tenā te tanvṝ3 śam karam pṛthivyām te niṣecanam bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vidmā śarasya pitaram candram śatavṛṣṇyam /

Halfverse: c tenā te tanvṝ3 śam karam pṛthivyām te niṣecanam bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vidmā śarasya pitaram sūryam śatavṛṣṇyam /

Halfverse: c tenā te tanvṝ3 śam karam pṛthivyām te niṣecanam bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yad āntreṣu gavīnyor yad vastāv adhi samśritam /

Halfverse: c evā te mūtram mucyatām bahir bāl iti sarvakam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a pra te bhinadmi mehanam vartram veśantyā iva /

Halfverse: c evā te mūtram mucyatām bahir bāl iti sarvakam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a viṣitam te vastibilam samudrasyodadher iva /

Halfverse: c evā te mūtram mucyatām bahir bāl iti sarvakam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yatheṣukā parāpatad avasṛṣṭādhi dhanvanaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā te mūtram mucyatām bahir bāl iti sarvakam //9//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ambayo yanty adhvabhir jāmayo adhvarīyatām /

Halfverse: c pṛñcatīr madhunā payaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a amūr yā upa sūrye yābhir vā sūryaḥ saha /

Halfverse: c tā no hinvantv adhvaram //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apo devīr upa hvaye yatra gāvaḥ pibanti naḥ /

Halfverse: c sindhubhyaḥ kartvam haviḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a apsv a1ntar amṛtam apsu bheṣajam /

Halfverse: c apām uta praśastibhir aśvā bhavatha vājino gāvo bhavatha vājinīḥ //4//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvas tā na ūrje dadhātana /

Halfverse: c mahe raṇāya cakṣase //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo vaḥ śivatamo rasas tasya bhājayateha naḥ /

Halfverse: c uśatīr iva mātaraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tasmā aram gamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha /

Halfverse: c āpo janayathā ca naḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a īśānā vāryāṇām kṣayantīś carṣaṇīnām /

Halfverse: c apo yācāmi bheṣajam //4//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śam no devīr abhiṣṭaya āpo bhavantu pītaye /

Halfverse: c śam yor abhi sravantu naḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a apsu me somo abravīd antar viśvāni bheṣajā /

Halfverse: c agnim ca viśvaśambhuvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āpaḥ pṛṇīta bheṣajam varūtham tanvṝ3 mama /

Halfverse: c jyok ca sūryam dṛśe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śam na āpo dhanvanyā3ḥ śam u santv anūpyāḥ /

Halfverse: c śam naḥ khanitrimā āpaḥ śam u yāḥ kumbha ābhṛtāḥ /

Halfverse: e śivā naḥ santu vārṣikīḥ //4//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a stuvānam agna ā vaha yātudhānam kimīdinam /

Halfverse: c tvam hi deva vandito hantā dasyor babhūvitha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ājyasya parameṣṭhin jātavedas tanūvaśin /

Halfverse: c agne taulasya prāśāna yātudhānān vi lāpaya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vi lapantu yātudhānā attriṇo ye kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c athedam agne no havir indraś ca prati haryatam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a agniḥ pūrva ā rabhatām prendro nudatu bāhumān /

Halfverse: c bravītu sarvo yātumān ayam asmīty etya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a paśyāma te vīryam jātavedaḥ pra ṇo brūhi yātudhānān nṛcakṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvayā sarve paritaptāḥ purastāt ta ā yantu prabruvāṇā upedam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ā rabhasva jātavedo ‘smākārthāya jajñiṣe /

Halfverse: c dūto no agne bhūtvā yātudhānān vi lāpaya //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tvam agne yātudhānān upabaddhāṁ ihā vaha /

Halfverse: c athaiṣām indro vajreṇāpi śīrṣāṇi vṛścatu //7//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam havir yātudhānān nādī phenam iva ā vahat /

Halfverse: c ya idam strī pumān akar iha sa stuvatām janaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayam stuvāna āgamad imam sma prati haryata /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspate vaśe labdhvāgnīṣomā vi vidhyatam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yātudhānasya somapa jahi prajām nayasva ca /

Halfverse: c ni stuvānasya pātaya param akṣy utāvaram //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yatraiṣām agne janimāni vettha guhā satām attriṇām jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c tāms tvam brahmaṇā vāvṛdhāno jahy ṝṣām śatatarham agne //4//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asmin vasu vasavo dhārayantv indraḥ pūṣā varuṇo mitro agniḥ /

Halfverse: c imam ādityā uta viśve ca devā uttarasmin jyotiṣi dhārayantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asya devāḥ pradiśi jyotir astu sūryo agnir uta vā hiraṇyam /

Halfverse: c sapatnā asmad adhare bhavantūttamam nākam adhi rohayemam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yenendrāya samabharaḥ payāmsy uttamena brahmaṇā jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c tena tvam agna iha vardhayemam sajātānām śraiṣṭhya ā dhehy enam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a aiṣām yajñam uta varco dade ‘ham rāyas poṣam uta cittāny agne /

Halfverse: c sapatnā asmad adhare bhavantūttamam nākam adhi rohayemam //4//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam devānām asuro vi rājati vaśā hi satyā varuṇasya rājñaḥ /

Halfverse: c tatas pari brahmaṇā śāśadāna ugrasya manyor ud imam nayāmi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a namas te rajan varuṇāstu manyave viśvam hy ḥgra nicikeṣi drugdham /

Halfverse: c sahasram anyān pra suvāmi sākam śatam jīvāti śaradas tavāyam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad uvakthānṛtam jihvayā vṛjinam bahu /

Halfverse: c rājñas tvā satyadharmaṇo muñcāmi varuṇād aham //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a muñcāmi tvā vaiśvānarād arṇavān mahatas pari /

Halfverse: c sajātān ugrehā vada brahma cāpa cikīhi naḥ //4//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vaṣaṭ te pūṣann asmint sūtāv aryamā hotā kṛṇotu vedhāḥ /

Halfverse: c sisratām nāry ṛtaprajātā vi parvāṇi jihatām sūtavā u //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a catasro divaḥ pradiśaś catasro bhūmyā uta /

Halfverse: c devā garbham sam airayan tam vy ūrṇuvantu sūtave //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sūṣā vy ūrṇotu vi yonim hāpayāmasi /

Halfverse: c śrathayā sūṣaṇe tvam ava tvam biṣkale sṛja //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a neva māmse na pīvasi neva majjasv āhatam /

Halfverse: c avaitu pṛśni śevalam sune jarāyv attave ‘va jarāyu padyatām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vi te bhinadmi mehanam vi yonim vi gavīnike /

Halfverse: c vi mātaram ca putram ca vi kumāram jarāyuṇāva jarāyu padyatām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yathā vāto yathā mano yathā patanti pakṣiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā tvam daśamāsya sākam jarāyuṇā patāva jarāyu padyatām //6//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a jarāyujaḥ prathama usriyo vṛṣā vātābhrajā stanayann eti vṛṣṭyā /

Halfverse: c sa no mṛḍāti tanva ṛjugo rujan ya ekam ojas tredhā vicakrame //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aṅgeaṅge śociṣā śiśriyāṇam namasyantas tvā haviṣā vidhema /

Halfverse: c aṅkānt samaṅkān haviṣā vidhema yo agrabhīt parvāsyā grabhītā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a muñca śīrṣaktyā uta kāsa enam paruṣparur āviveśā yo asya /

Halfverse: c yo abhrajā vātajā yaś ca śuṣmo vanaspatīnt sacatām parvatāmś ca //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śam me parasmai gātrāya śam astv avarāya me /

Halfverse: c śam me caturbhyo aṅgebhyaḥ śam astu tanvṝ3 mama //4//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a namas te astu vidyute namas te stanayitnave /

Halfverse: c namas te astv aśmane yenā dūḍāśe asyasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a namas te pravato napād yatas tapaḥ samūhasi /

Halfverse: c mṛḍayā nas tanūbhyo mayas tokebhyas kṛdhi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pravato napān nama evāstu tubhyam namas te hetaye tapuṣe ca kṛṇmaḥ /

Halfverse: c vidma te dhāma paramam guhā yat samudre antar nihitāsi nābhiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yām tvā devā asṛjanta viśva iṣum kṛṇvānā asanāya dhṛṣṇum /

Halfverse: c sā no mṛḍa vidathe gṛṇānā tasyai te namo astu devi //4//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhagam asyā varca ādiṣy adhi vṛkṣād iva srajam /

Halfverse: c mahābudhna iva parvato jyok pitṛṣv āstām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a eṣā te rājan kanyā vadhūr ni dhūyatām yama /

Halfverse: c sā mātur badhyatām gṛhe ‘tho bhrātur atho pituḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a eṣā te kulapā rājan tām u te pari dadmasi /

Halfverse: c jyok pitṛṣv āsātā ā śīrṣṇaḥ śamopyāt //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a asitasya te brahmaṇā kaśyapasya gayasya ca /

Halfverse: c antaḥkośam iva jāmayo ‘pi nahyāmi te bhagam //4 //

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam sam sravantu sindhavaḥ sam vātāḥ sam patatriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c imam yajñam pradivo me juṣantām samsrāvyṝṇa haviṣā juhomi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ihaiva havam ā yāta ma iha samsrāvaṇā utemam vardhayatā giraḥ /

Halfverse: c ihaitu sarvo yaḥ paśur asmin tiṣṭhatu yā rayiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye nadīnām samsravanty utsāsaḥ sadam akṣitāḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhir me sarvaiḥ samsrāvair dhanam sam srāvayāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ye sarpiṣaḥ samsravanti kṣīrasya codakasya ca /

Halfverse: c tebhir me sarvaiḥ samsrāvair dhanam sam srāvayāmasi //4//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye ‘māvāsyā3m rātrim udasthur vrājam attriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c agnis turīyo yātuhā so asmabhyam adhi bravat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sīsāyādhy āha varuṇaḥ sīsāyāgnir upāvati /

Halfverse: c sīsam ma indraḥ prāyachat tad aṅga yātucātanam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a idam viṣkandham sahata idam bādhate attriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c anena viśvā sasahe yā jātāni piśācyāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yadi no gām hamsi yady aśvam yadi pūruṣam /

Halfverse: c tam tvā sīsena vidhyāmo yathā no ‘so avīrahā //4//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a amūr yā yanti yoṣito hirā lohitavāsasaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhrātara iva jāmayas tiṣṭhantu hatavarcasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tiṣṭhāvare tiṣṭha para uta tvam tiṣṭha madhyame /

Halfverse: c kaniṣṭhikā ca tiṣṭhati tiṣṭhād id dhamanir mahī //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śatasya dhamanīnām sahasrasya hirāṇām /

Halfverse: c asthur in madhyamā imāḥ sākam antā aramsata //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pari vaḥ sikatāvatī dhanūr bṛhaty akramīt /

Halfverse: c tiṣṭhatelayatā su kam //4//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nir lakṣmyam lalāmya1m nir arātim suvāmasi /

Halfverse: c atha yā bhadrā tāni naḥ prajāyā arātim nayāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nir araṇim savitā sāviṣak pador nir hastayor varuṇo mitro aryamā /

Halfverse: c nir asmabhyam anumatī rarāṇā premām devā asāviṣuḥ saubhagāya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat ta ātmani tanvām ghoram asti yad vā keśeṣu praticakṣaṇe vā /

Halfverse: c sarvam tad vācāpa hanmo vayam devas tvā savitā sūdayatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a riśyapadīm vṛṣadatīm goṣedhām vidhamām uta /

Halfverse: c vilīḍhyam lalāmya1m tā asman nāśayāmasi //4//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mā no vidan vivyādhino mo abhivyādhino vidan /

Halfverse: c ārāc charavyā asmad viṣūcīr indra pātaya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a viṣvañco asmac charavaḥ patantu ye astā ye cāsyāḥ /

Halfverse: c daivīr manuṣyesavo mamāmitrān vi vidhyata //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo naḥ svo yo araṇaḥ sajāta uta niṣṭyo yo asmāṁ abhidāsati /

Halfverse: c rudraḥ śaravyayaitān mamāmitrān vi vidhyatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yaḥ sapatno yo ‘sapatno yaś ca dviṣan chapāti naḥ /

Halfverse: c devās tam sarve dhūrvantu brahma varma mamāntaram //4//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a adārasṛd bhavatu deva somāsmin yajñe maruto mṛḍatā naḥ /

Halfverse: c mā no vidad abhibhā mo aśastir mā no vidad vṛjinā dveṣyā yā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo adya senyo vadho ‘ghāyūnām udīrate /

Halfverse: c yuvam tam mitrāvaruṇāv asmad yāvayatam pari //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a itaś ca yad amutaś ca yad vadham varuṇa yāvaya /

Halfverse: c vi mahac charma yacha varīyo yāvayā vadham //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śāsa itthā mahāṁ asy amitrasāho astṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c na yasya hanyate sakhā na jīyate kadā cana //4//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a svastidā viśām patir vṛtrahā vimṛdho vaśī /

Halfverse: c vṛṣendraḥ pura etu naḥ somapā abhayamkaraḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vi na indra mṛdho jahi nīcā yacha pṛtanyataḥ /

Halfverse: c adhamam gamayā tamo yo asmāṁ abhidāsati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vi rakṣo vi mṛdho jahi vi vṛtrasya hanū ruja /

Halfverse: c vi manyum indra vṛtrahann amitrasyābhidāsataḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a apendra dviṣato mano ‘pa jijyāsato vadham /

Halfverse: c vi mahac charma yacha varīyo yāvayā vadham //4//

Hymn: 22

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a anu sūryam ud ayatām hṛddyoto harimā ca te /

Halfverse: c go rohitasya varṇena tena tvā pari dadhmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pari tvā rohitair varṇair dīrghāyutvāya dadhmasi /

Halfverse: c yathāyam arapā asad atho aharito bhuvat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yā rohiṇīr devatyā3 gāvo yā uta rohiṇīḥ /

Halfverse: c rūpamrūpam vayovayas tābhiṣ ṭvā pari dadhmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śukeṣu te harimāṇam ropaṇākāsu dadhmasi /

Halfverse: c atho hāridraveṣu te harimāṇam ni dadhmasi //4//

Hymn: 23

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a naktamjātāsi oṣadhe rāme kṛṣṇe asikni ca /

Halfverse: c idam rajani rajaya kilāsam palitam ca yat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kilāsam ca palitam ca nir ito nāśayā pṛṣat /

Halfverse: c ā tvā svo viśatām varṇaḥ parā śuklāni pātaya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asitam te pralayanam āsthānam asitam tava /

Halfverse: c asiknī asy oṣadhe nir ito nāśayā pṛṣat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a asthijasya kilāsasya tanūjasya ca yat tvaci /

Halfverse: c dūṣyā kṛtasya brahmaṇā lakṣma śvetam anīnaśam //4//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a suparṇo jātaḥ prathamas tasya tvam pittam āsitha /

Halfverse: c tad āsurī yudhā jitā rūpam cakre vanaspatīn //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āsurī cakre prathamedam kilāsabheṣajam idam kilāsanāśanam /

Halfverse: c anīnaśat kilāsam sarūpām akarat tvacam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sarūpā nāma te mātā sarūpo nāma te pitā /

Halfverse: c sarūpakṛt tvam oṣadhe sā sarūpam idam kṛdhi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śyāmā sarūpamkaraṇī pṛthivyā adhy udbhṛtā /

Halfverse: c idam ū ṣu pra sādhaya punā rūpāṇi kalpaya //4//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad agnir āpo adahat praviśya yatrākṛṇvan dharmadhṛto namāmsi /

Halfverse: c tatra ta āhuḥ paramam janitram sa naḥ samvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yady arcir yadi vāsi śociḥ śakalyeṣi yadi vā te janitram /

Halfverse: c hrūḍur nāmāsi haritasya deva sa naḥ samvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yadi śoko yadi vābhiśoko yadi vā rājño varuṇasyāsi putraḥ /

Halfverse: c hrūḍur nāmāsi haritasya deva sa naḥ samvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a namaḥ śītāya takmane namo rūrāya śociṣe kṛṇomi /

Halfverse: c yo anyedyur ubhayadyur abhyeti tṛtīyakāya namo astu takmane //4//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ārṝ3 ‘sāv asmad astu hetir devāso asat /

Halfverse: c āre aśmā yam asyatha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sakhāsāv asmabhyam astu rātiḥ sakhendro bhagaḥ /

Halfverse: c savitā citrarādhāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yūyam naḥ pravato napān marutaḥ sūryatvacasaḥ /

Halfverse: c śarma yachatha saprathāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a suṣūdata mṛḍata mṛḍayā nas tanūbhyo /

Halfverse: c mayas tokebhyas kṛdhi //4//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a amūḥ pāre pṛdākvas triṣaptā nirjarāyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tāsām jarāyubhir vayam akṣyāv api vyayāmasy aghāyoḥ paripanthinaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a viṣūcy etu kṛntatī pinākam iva bibhratī /

Halfverse: c viṣvak punarbhuvā mano ‘samṛddhā aghāyavaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a na bahavaḥ sam aśakan nārbhakā abhi dādhṛṣuḥ /

Halfverse: c veṇor adgā ivābhito ‘samṛddhā aghāyavaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pretam pādau pra sphuratam vahatam pṛṇato gṛhān /

Halfverse: c indrāny ṝtu prathamājītāmuṣitā puraḥ //4//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a upa prāgād devo agnī rakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ /

Halfverse: c dahann apa dvayāvino yātudhānān kimīdinaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a prati daha yātudhānān prati deva kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c pratīcīḥ kṛṣṇavartane sam daha yātudhānyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yā śaśāpa śapanena yāgham mūram ādadhe /

Halfverse: c yā rasasya haraṇāya jātam ārebhe tokam attu sā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a putram attu yātudhānīḥ svasāram uta naptyam /

Halfverse: c adhā mitho vikeśyo3 vi ghnatām yātudhānyo3 vi tṛhyantām arāyyaḥ //4//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhīvartena maṇinā yenendro abhivavṛdhe /

Halfverse: c tenāsmān brahmaṇas pate ‘bhi rāṣṭrāya vardhaya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a abhivṛtya sapatnān abhi yā no arātayaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhi pṛtanyantam tiṣṭhābhi yo no durasyati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a abhi tvā devaḥ savitābhi ṣomo avīvṛdhat /

Halfverse: c abhi tvā viśvā bhūtāny abhīvarto yathāsasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a abhīvarto abhibhavaḥ sapatnakṣayaṇo maṇiḥ /

Halfverse: c rāṣṭrāya mahyam badhyatām sapatnebhyaḥ parābhuve //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ud asau sūryo agād ud idam māmakam vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c yathāham śatruho ‘sāny asapatnaḥ sapatnahā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sapatnakṣayaṇo vṛṣābhiraṣṭro viṣāsahiḥ /

Halfverse: c yathāham eṣām vīrāṇām virājāni janasya ca //6//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a viśve devā vasavo rakṣatemam utādityā jāgṛta yūyam asmin /

Halfverse: c memam sanābhir uta vānyanābhir memam prāpat pauruṣeyo vadho yaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ye vo devāḥ pitaro ye ca putrāḥ sacetaso me śṛṇutedam uktam /

Halfverse: c sarvebhyo vaḥ pari dadāmy etam svasty ṝnam jarase vahātha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye devā divi ṣṭha ye pṛthivyām ye antarikṣa oṣadhīṣu paśuṣv apsv a1ntaḥ /

Halfverse: c te kṛṇuta jarasam āyur asmai śatam anyān pari vṛṇaktu mṛtyūn //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yeṣām prayājā uta vānuyājā hutabhāgā ahutādaś ca devāḥ /

Halfverse: c yeṣām vaḥ pañca pradiśo vibhaktās tān vo asmai satrasadaḥ kṛṇomi //4//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āśānām āśāpālebhyaś caturbhyo amṛtebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c idam bhūtasyādhyakṣebhyo vidhema haviṣā vayam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ya āśānām āśāpālāś catvāra sthana devāḥ /

Halfverse: c te no nirṛtyāḥ pāśebhyo muñcatāmhasoamhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asrāmas tvā haviṣā yajāmy aśloṇas tvā ghṛtena juhomi /

Halfverse: c ya āśānām āśāpālas turīyo devaḥ sa naḥ subhūtam eha vakṣat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a svasti mātra uta pitre no astu svasti gobhyo jagate puruṣebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvam subhūtam suvidatram no astu jyog eva dṛśema sūryam //4//

Hymn: 32

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam janāso vidatha mahad brahma vadiṣyati /

Halfverse: c na tat pṛthivyām no divi yena prāṇanti vīrudhaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a antarikṣa āsām sthāma śrāntasadām iva /

Halfverse: c āsthānam asya bhūtasya viduṣ ṭad vedhaso na vā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad rodasī rejamāne bhūmiś ca niratakṣatam /

Halfverse: c ārdram tad adya sarvadā samudrasyeva śrotyāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a viśvam anyām abhīvāra tad anyasyām adhi śritam /

Halfverse: c dive ca viśvavedase pṛthivyai cākaram namaḥ //4//

Hymn: 33

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā yāsu jātaḥ savitā yāsv agniḥ /

Halfverse: c yā agnim garbham dadhire suvarṇās tā na āpaḥ śam syonā bhavantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yāsām rājā varuṇo yāti madhye satyānṛte avapaśyan janānām /

Halfverse: c yā agnim garbham dadhire suvarṇās tā na āpaḥ śam syonā bhavantu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yāsām devā divi kṛṇvanti bhakṣam yā antarikṣe bahudhā bhavanti /

Halfverse: c yā agnim garbham dadhire suvarṇās tā na āpaḥ śam syonā bhavantu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śivena mā cakṣuṣā paśyatāpaḥ śivayā tanvopa spṛśata tvacam me /

Halfverse: c ghṛtaścutaḥ śucayo yāḥ pāvakās tā na āpaḥ śam syonā bhavantu //4//

Hymn: 34

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a iyam vīrun madhujātā madhunā tvā khanāmasi /

Halfverse: c madhor adhi prajātāsi sā no madhumatas kṛdhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jihvāyā agre madhu me jihvāmūle madhūlakam /

Halfverse: c mamed aha kratāv aso mama cittam upāyasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a madhuman me nikramaṇam madhuman me parāyaṇam /

Halfverse: c vācā vadāmi madhumad bhūyāsam madhusamdṛśaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a madhor asmi madhutaro madughān madhumattaraḥ /

Halfverse: c mām it kila tvam vanāḥ śākhām madhumatīm iva //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pari tvā paritatnunekṣuṇāgām avidviṣe /

Halfverse: c yathā mām kaminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ //5//

Hymn: 35

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad ābadhnan dākṣāyaṇā hiraṇyam śatānīkāya sumanasyamānāḥ /

Halfverse: c tat te badnāmy āyuṣe varcase balāya dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nainam rakṣāmsi na piśācāḥ sahante devānām ojaḥ prathamajam hy ṝtat /

Halfverse: c yo bibharti dākṣāyaṇam hiraṇyam sa jīveṣu kṛṇute dīrgham āyuḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apām tejo jyotir ojo balam ca vanaspatīnām uta vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: c indra ivendriyāṇy adhi dhārayāmo asmin tad dakṣamāṇo bibharad dhiraṇyam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a samānām māsām ṛtubhiṣ ṭvā vayam samvatsarasya payasā piparmi /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī viśve devās te ‘nu manyantām ahṛṇīyamānāḥ //4//

## Book: 2

2

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a venas tat paśyat paramam guhā yad yatra viśvam bhavaty ekarūpam /

Halfverse: c idam pṛśnir aduhaj jāyamānāḥ svarvido abhy anūṣata vrāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pra tad voced amṛtasya vidvān gandharvo dhāma paramam guhā yat /

Halfverse: c trīṇi padāni nihitā guhāsya yas tāni veda sa pituṣ pitāsat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sa naḥ pitā janitā sa uta bandhur dhāmāni veda bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c yo devānām nāmadha eka eva tam sampraśnam bhuvanā yanti sarvā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pari dyāvāpṛthivī sadya āyam upātiṣṭhe prathamajām ṛtasya /

Halfverse: c vācam iva vaktari bhuvaneṣṭhā dhāsyur eṣa nanv ṝ3ṣo agniḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pari viśvā bhuvanāny āyam ṛtasya tantum vitatam dṛśe kam /

Halfverse: c yatra devā amṛtam ānaśānāḥ samāne yonāv adhy airayanta //5//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a divyo gandharvo bhuvanasya yas patir eka eva namasyo vikṣv īḍyaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam tvā yaumi brahmaṇā divya deva namas te astu divi te sadhastham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a divi spṛṣṭo yajataḥ sūryatvag avayātā haraso daivyasya /

Halfverse: c mṛḍāt gandharvo bhuvanasya yas patir eka eva namasyaḥ suśevāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anavadyābhiḥ sam u jagma ābhir apsarāsv api gandharva āsīt /

Halfverse: c samudra āsām sadanam ma āhur yataḥ sadya ā ca parā ca yanti //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a abhriye didyun nakṣatriye yā viśvāvasum gandharvam sacadhve /

Halfverse: c tābhyo vo devīr nama it kṛṇomi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yāḥ klandās tamiṣīcayo ‘kṣakāmā manomuhaḥ /

Halfverse: c tābhyo gandharvabhyo ‘psarābhyo ‘karam namaḥ //5//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ado yad avadhāvaty avatkam adhi parvatāt /

Halfverse: c tat te kṛṇomi bheṣajam subheṣajam yathāsasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ād aṅgā kuvid aṅga śatam yā bheṣajāni te /

Halfverse: c teṣām asi tvam uttamam anāsrāvam arogaṇam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nīcaiḥ khananty asurā arusrāṇam idam mahat /

Halfverse: c tad āsrāvasya bheṣajam tad u rogam anīnaśat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a upajīkā ud bharanti samudrād adhi bheṣajam /

Halfverse: c tad āsrāvasya bheṣajam tad u rogam aśīśamat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a arusrāṇam idam mahat pṛthivyā adhy udbhṛtam /

Halfverse: c tad āsrāvasya bheṣajam tad u rogam anīnaśat //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śam no bhavantv apa oṣadhayaḥ śivāḥ /

Halfverse: c indrasya vajro apa hantu rakṣasa ārād visṛṣṭā iṣavaḥ patantu rakṣasām //6//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dīrghāyutvāya bṛhatm raṇāyāriṣyanto dakṣamāṇāḥ sadaiva /

Halfverse: c maṇim viṣkandhadūṣaṇam jaṅgiḍam bibhṛmo vayam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jaṅgiḍo jambhād viśarād viṣkandhād abhiśocanāt /

Halfverse: c maṇiḥ sahasravīryaḥ pari ṇaḥ pātu viśvataḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ayam viṣkandham sahate ‘yam bādhate attriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c ayam no viśvabheṣajo jaṅgiḍaḥ pātv amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a devair dattena maṇinā jaṅgiḍena mayobhuvā /

Halfverse: c viṣkandham sarvā rakṣāmsi vyāyāme sahāmahe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śaṇaś ca mā jaṅgiḍaś ca viṣkandhād abhi rakṣatām /

Halfverse: c araṇyād anya ābhṛtaḥ kṛṣyā anyo rasebhyaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kṛtyādūṣir ayam maṇir atho arātidūṣiḥ /

Halfverse: c atho sahasvān jaṅgiḍaḥ pra ṇa āyumṣi tāriṣat //6//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indra juṣasva pra vahā yāhi śūra haribhyām /

Halfverse: c pibā sutasya mater iha madhoś cakānaś cārur madāya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indra jaṭharam navyo na pṛṇasva madhor divo na /

Halfverse: c asya sutasya sva1r ṇopa tvā madāḥ suvāco aguḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indras turāṣāṇ mitro vṛtram yo jaghāna yatīr na /

Halfverse: c bibheda valam bhṛgur na sasahe śatrūn made somasya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ā tvā viśantu sutāsa indra pṛṇasva kukṣī viḍḍhi śakra dhiyehy ā naḥ /

Halfverse: c śrudhī havam giro me juṣasvendra svayugbhir matsveha mahe raṇāya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indrasya nu pra vocam vīryāṇi yāni cakāra prathamāni vajrī /

Halfverse: c ahann ahim anu apas tatarda pra vakṣaṇā abhinat parvatānām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ahann ahim parvate śiśriyāṇam tvaṣṭāsmai vajram svaryam tatakṣa /

Halfverse: c vāśrā iva dhenavaḥ syandamānā añjaḥ samudram ava jagmur āpaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a vṛṣāyamāṇo avṛṇīta somam trikadrukeṣu apibat sutasya /

Halfverse: c ā sāyakam maghavādatta vajram ahann enam prathamajām ahīnām //7//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samās tvāgna ṛtavo vardhayantu samvatsarā ṛṣayo yāni satyā /

Halfverse: c sam divyena dīdihi rocanena viśvā ā māhi pradiśaś catasraḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam cedhyasvāgne pra ca vardhayemam uc ca tiṣṭha mahate saubhagāya /

Halfverse: c mā te riṣann upasattāro agne brahmāṇas te yaśasaḥ santu mānye //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvām agne vṛṇate brāhmaṇā ime śivo agne samvaraṇe bhavā naḥ /

Halfverse: c sapatnahāgne abhimātijid bhava sve gaye jāgṛhy aprayuchan //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kṣatreṇāgne svena sam rabhasva mitreṇāgne mitradhā yatasva /

Halfverse: c sajātānām madhyameṣṭhā rājñām agne vihavyo dīdihīha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ati niho ati sṛdho ‘ty acittīr ati dviṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvā hy agne duritā tara tvam athāsmabhyam sahavīram rayim dāḥ //5//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aghadviṣṭā devajātā vīruc chapathayopanī /

Halfverse: c āpo malam iva prāṇaikṣīt sarvān mac chapathāṁ adhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yaś ca sāpatnaḥ śapatho jāmyāḥ śapathaś ca yaḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmā yan manyutaḥ śapāt sarvam tan no adhaspadam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a divo mūlam avatatam pṛthivyā adhy uttatam /

Halfverse: c tena sahasrakāṇḍena pari ṇaḥ pāhi viśvataḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pari mām pari me prajām pari ṇaḥ pāhi yad dhanam /

Halfverse: c arātir no mā tārīn mā nas tāriśur abhimātayaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śaptāram etu śapatho yaḥ suhārt tena naḥ saha /

Halfverse: c cakṣurmantrasya durhārdaḥ pṛṣṭīr api śṛṇīmasi //5//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud agātām bhagavatī vicṛtau nāma tārake /

Halfverse: c vi kṣetriyasya muñcatām adhamam pāśam uttamam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a apeyam rātry uchatv apochantv abhikṛtvarīḥ /

Halfverse: c vīrut kṣetriyanāśany apa kṣetriyam uchatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a babhror arjunakāṇḍasya yavasya te palālyā tilasya tilapiñjyā /

Halfverse: c vīrut kṣetriyanāśany apa kṣetriyam uchatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a namas te lāṅgalebhyo nama īṣāyugebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c vīrut kṣetriyanāśany apa kṣetriyam uchatu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a namaḥ sanisrasākṣebhyo namaḥ samdeśyṝbhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c namaḥ kṣetrasya pataye vīrut kṣetriyanāśany apa kṣetriyam uchatu //5//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a daśavṛkṣa muñcemam rakṣaso grāhyā adhi yainam jagrāha parvasu /

Halfverse: c atho enam vanaspate jīvānām lokam un naya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āgād ud agād ayam jīvānām vrātam apy agāt /

Halfverse: c abhūd u putrāṇām pitā nṛṇām ca bhagavattamaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a adhītīr adhy agād ayam adhi jīvapurā agān /

Halfverse: c śatam hy asya bhiṣajaḥ sahasram uta vīrudhaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a devās te cītim avidan brahmāṇa uta vīrudhaḥ /

Halfverse: c cītim te viśve devā avidan bhūmyām adhi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yaś cakāra sa niṣ karat sa eva subhiṣaktamaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa eva tubhyam bheṣajāni kṛṇavad bhiṣajā śuciḥ //5//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kṣetriyāt tvā nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: c anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śam te agniḥ sahādbhir astu śam somaḥ sahauṣadhībhiḥ /

Halfverse: c evāham tvām kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: e anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śam te vāto antarikṣe vayo dhāc cham te bhavantu pradiśaś catasraḥ /

Halfverse: c evāham tvām kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: e anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a imā yā devīḥ pradiśaś catasro vātapatnīr abhi sūryo vicaṣṭe /

Halfverse: c evāham tvām kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: e anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛnomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tāsu tvāntar jarasy ā dadhāmi pra yakṣma etu nirṛtiḥ parācaiḥ /

Halfverse: c evāham tvām kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: e anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a amukthā yakṣmād duritād avadyād druhaḥ pāśād grāhyāś cod amukthāḥ /

Halfverse: c evāham tvām kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: e anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ahā arātim avidaḥ syonam apy abhūr bhadre sukṛtasya loke /

Halfverse: c evāham tvām kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: e anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a sūryam ṛtam tamaso grāhyā adhi devā muñcanto asṛjan nir enasaḥ /

Halfverse: c evāham tvām kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśamsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt /

Halfverse: e anāgasam brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //8//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dūṣyā dūṣir asi hetyā hetir asi menyā menir asi /

Halfverse: c āpnuhi śreyāmsam ati samam krāma //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sraktyo ‘si pratiṣaro ‘si pratyabhicaraṇo ‘si /

Halfverse: c āpnuhi śreyāmsam ati samam krāma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a prati tam abhi cara yo ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: c āpnuhi śreyāmsam ati samam krāma //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sūrir asi varcodhā asi tanūpāno ‘si /

Halfverse: c āpnuhi śreyāmsam ati samam krāma //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śukro ‘si bhrājo ‘si svar asi jyotir asi /

Halfverse: c āpnuhi śreyāmsam ati samam krāma //5//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dyāvāpṛthivī urv a1ntarikṣam kṣetrasya patny urugāyo ‘dbhutaḥ /

Halfverse: c utāntarikṣam uru vātagopam ta iha tapyantām mayi tapyamāne //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a idam devāḥ śṛṇuta ye yajñiyā stha bharadvājo mahyam ukthāni śamsati /

Halfverse: c pāśe sa baddho durite ni yujyatām yo asmākam mana idam hinasti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a idam indra śṛṇuhi somapa yat tvā hṛdā śocatā johavīmi /

Halfverse: c vṛścāmi tam kuliśeneva vṛkṣam yo asmākam mana idam hinasti //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a aśītibhis tisṛbhiḥ sāmagebhir ādityebhir vasubhir aṅgirobhiḥ /

Halfverse: c iṣṭāpūrtam avatu naḥ pitṝṇām āmum dade harasā daivyena //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dyāvāpṛthivī anu mā dīdhīthām viśve devāso anu mā rabhadhvam /

Halfverse: c aṅgirasaḥ pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ pāpam ā ṛchatv apakāmasya kartā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a atīva yo maruto manyate no brahma vā yo nindiṣat kriyamāṇam /

Halfverse: c tapūmṣi tasmai vṛjināni santu brahmadviṣam dyaur abhisamtapāti //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sapta prāṇān aṣṭau manyas tāms te vṛścāmi brahmaṇā /

Halfverse: c ayā yamasya sādanam agnidūto aramkṛtaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ā dadhāmi te padam samiddhe jātavedasi /

Halfverse: c agniḥ śarīram veveṣṭv asum vāg api gachatu //8//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āyurdā agne jarasam vṛṇāno ghṛtapratīko ghṛtapṛṣṭho agne /

Halfverse: c ghṛtam pītvā madhu cāru gavyam piteva putrān abhi rakṣatād imam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pari dhatta dhatta no varcasemam jarāmṛtyum kṛṇuta dīrgham āyuḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatiḥ prāyachad vāsa etat somāya rājñe paridhātavā u //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a parīdam vāso adhithāḥ svastaye ‘bhūr gṛṣṭīnām abhiśastipā u /

Halfverse: c śatam ca jīva śaradaḥ purūcī rāyaś ca poṣam upasamvyayasva //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ehy aśmānam ā tiṣṭhāśmā bhavatu te tanūḥ /

Halfverse: c kṛṇvantu viśve devā āyuṣṭe śaradaḥ śatam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasya te vāsaḥ prathamavāsya1m harāmas tam tvā viśve ‘vantu devāḥ /

Halfverse: c tam tvā bhrātaraḥ suvṛdhā vardhamānam anu jāyantām bahavaḥ sujātam //5//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a niḥsālām dhṛṣṇum dhiṣaṇam ekavādyām jighatsvam /

Halfverse: c sarvāś caṇḍasya naptyo nāśayāmaḥ sadānvāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nir vo goṣṭhād ajāmasi nir akṣān nir upānaśāt /

Halfverse: c nir vo magundyā duhitaro gṛhebhyaś cātayāmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asau yo adharād gṛhas tatra santv arāyyaḥ /

Halfverse: c tatra sedir ny ḥcyatu sarvāś ca yātudhānyaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a bhūtapatir nir ajatv indraś cetaḥ sadānvāḥ /

Halfverse: c gṛhasya budhna āsīnās tā indro vajreṇādhi tiṣṭhatu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yadi stha kṣetriyāṇām yadi vā puruṣeṣitāḥ /

Halfverse: c yadi stha dasyubhyo jātā naśyatetaḥ sadānvāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pari dhāmāny āsām āśur gāṣṭhām ivāsaram /

Halfverse: c ajaiṣam sarvān ājīn vo naśyatetaḥ sadānvāḥ //6//

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathā dyauś ca pṛthivī ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathāhaś ca rātrī ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā sūryaś ca candraś ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yathā brahma ca kṣatram ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yathā satyam cānṛtam ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yathā bhūtam ca bhavyam ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ //6//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prāṇāpānau mṛtyor mā pātam svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dyāvāpṛthivī upaśrutyā mā pātam svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sūrya cakṣuṣā mā pāhi svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a agne vaiśvānara viśvair mā devaiḥ pāhi svāhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a viśvambhara viśvena mā bharasā pāhi svāhā //5//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ojo ‘sy ojo me dāḥ svāhā /1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a saho ‘si saho me dāḥ svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a balam asi balam dāḥ svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a āyur asy āyur me dāḥ svāha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śrotram asi śrotram me dāḥ svāha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a cakṣur asi cakṣur me dāḥ svāha //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a paripāṇam asi paripāṇam me dāḥ svāha //7//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhrātṛvyakṣayaṇam asi bhrātṛvyacātanam me dāḥ svāha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sapatnakṣayaṇam asi sapatnacātanam me dāḥ svāha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a arāyakṣayaṇam asy arāyacātanam me dāḥ svāha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a piśācakṣayaṇam asi piśācacātanam me dāḥ svāha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sadānvākṣayaṇam asi sadānvācātanam me dāḥ svāha //5//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agne yat te tapas tena tam prati tapa yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agne yat te haras tena tam prati hara yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a agne yat te ‘rcis tena tam praty arca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a agne yat te śocis tena tam prati śoca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a agne yat te tejas tena tam atejasam kṛṇu yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //5//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vāyo yat te tapas tena tam prati tapa yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //1//

Halfverse: a vāyo yat te haras tena tam prati hara yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vāyo yat te ‘rcis tena tam praty arca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vāyo yat te śocis tena tam prati śoca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vāyo yat te tejas tena tam atejasam kṛṇu yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //5//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sūrya yat te tapas tena tam prati tapa yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //1//

Halfverse: a sūrya yat te haras tena tam prati hara yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sūrya yat te ‘rcis tena tam praty arca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sūrya yat te śocis tena tam prati śoca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sūrya yat te tejas tena tam atejasam kṛṇu yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //5//

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a candra yat te tapas tena tam prati tapa yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a candra yat te haras tena tam prati hara yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //2//

Halfverse: a candra yat te ‘rcis tena tam praty arca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //3//

Halfverse: a candra yat te śocis tena tam prati śoca yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //4//

Halfverse: a candra yat te tejas tena tam atejasam kṛṇu yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //5//

Hymn: 23

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āpo yad vas tapas tena tam prati tapata yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āpo yad vas haras tena tam prati harata yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āpo yad vas ‘rcis tena tam prati arcata yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a āpo yad vas śocis tena tam prati śocata yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a āpo yad vas tejas tena tam atejasam kṛṇuta yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //5//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śerabhaka śerabha punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śevṛdhaka śevṛdha punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mrokānumroka punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sarpānusarpa punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a jūrṇi punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a upabde punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a arjuni punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bharūji punar vo yantu yātavaḥ punar hetiḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya stha tam atta yo vo prāhait tam atta svā māmsāny atta //8//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śam no devī pṛśniparṇy aśam nirṛtyā akaḥ /

Halfverse: c ugrā hi kaṇvajambhanī tām abhakṣi sahasvatīm //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sahamāneyam prathamā pṛśniparṇy ajāyata /

Halfverse: c tayāham durṇāmnām śiro vṛścāmi śakuner iva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a arāyam asṛkpāvānam yaś ca sphātim jihīrṣati /

Halfverse: c garbhādam kaṇvam nāśaya pṛśniparṇi sahasva ca //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a girim enāṁ ā veśaya kaṇvān jīvitayopanān /

Halfverse: c tāms tvam devi pṛśniparṇy agnir ivānudahann ihi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a parāca enān pra ṇuda kaṇvān jīvitayopanān /

Halfverse: c tamāmsi yatra gachanti tat kravyādo ajīgamam //5//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a eha yantu paśavo ye pareyur vāyur yeṣām sahacāram jujoṣa /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭā yeṣām rūpadheyāni vedāsmin tān goṣṭhe savitā ni yachatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a imam goṣṭham paśavaḥ sam sravantu bṛhaspatir ā nayatu prajānan /

Halfverse: c sinīvālī nayatv āgram eṣām ājagmuṣo anumate ni yacha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sam sam sravantu paśavaḥ sam aśvāḥ sam u pūruṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c sam dhānyasya yā sphātiḥ samsrāvyṝṇa haviṣā juhomi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sam siñcāmi gavām kṣīram sam ājyena balam rasam /

Halfverse: c samsiktā asmākam vīrā dhruvā gāvo mayi gopatau //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā harāmi gavām kṣīram āhārṣam dhānya1m rasam /

Halfverse: c āhṛtā asmākam vīrā ā patnīr idam astakam //5//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nec chatruḥ prāśam jayāti sahamānābhibhūr asi /

Halfverse: c prāśam pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛnv oṣadhe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a suparṇas tvānv avindat sūkaras tvākhanan nasā /

Halfverse: c prāśam pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indro ha cakre tvā bāhāv asurebhya starītave /

Halfverse: c prāśam pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pāṭām indro vy āśnād asurebhya starītave /

Halfverse: c prāśam pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tayāham śatrūnt sākṣe indraḥ sālāvṛkāṁ iva /

Halfverse: c prāśam pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a rudra jalāṣabheṣaja nīlaśikhaṇḍa karmakṛt /

Halfverse: c prāśam pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tasya prāśam tvam jahi yo na indrābhidāsati /

Halfverse: c adhi no brūhi śaktibhiḥ prāśi mām uttaram kṛdhi //7//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tubhyam eva jariman vardhatām ayam memam anye mṛtyavo himsiṣuḥ śatam ye /

Halfverse: c māteva putram pramanā upasthe mitra enam mitriyāt pātv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mitra enam varuṇo vā riśādā jarāmṛtyum kṛṇutām samvidānau /

Halfverse: c tad agnir hotā vayunāni vidvān viśvā devānām janimā vivakti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvam īśiṣe paśūnām pārthivānām ye jātā uta vā ye janitrāḥ /

Halfverse: c memam prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno memam mitrā vadhiṣur mo amitrāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a dyauṣ ṭvā pitā pṛthivī mātā jarāmṛtyum kṛṇutām samvidāne /

Halfverse: c yathā jīvā aditer upasthe prāṇāpānābhyām gupitaḥ śatam himāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a imam agne āyuṣe varcase naya priyam reto varuṇa mitra rājan /

Halfverse: c mātevāsmā adite śarma yacha viśve devā jaradaṣṭir yathāsat //5//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pārthivasya rase devā bhagasya tanvo3 bale /

Halfverse: c āyuṣyam asmā agniḥ sūryo varca ā dhād bṛhaspatiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āyur asmai dhehi jātavedaḥ prajām tvaṣṭar adhinidhehi asmai /

Halfverse: c rāyas poṣam savitar ā suvāsmai śatam jīvāti śaradas tavāyam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āśīr ṇa ūrjam uta sauprajāstvam dakṣam dhattam draviṇam sacetasau /

Halfverse: c jayam kṣetrāṇi sahasāyam indra kṛṇvāno anyān adharānt sapatnān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indreṇa datto varuṇena śiṣṭo marudbhir ugraḥ prahito no āgan /

Halfverse: c eṣa vām dyāvāpṛthivī upasthe mā kṣudhan mā tṛṣat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ūrjam asmā ūrjasvatī dhattam payo asmai payasvatī dhattam /

Halfverse: c ūrjam asmai dyāvapṛthivī adhātām viśve devā maruta ūrjam āpaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śivābhiṣ ṭe hṛdayam tarpayāmy anamīvo modiṣīṣṭhāḥ suvarcāḥ /

Halfverse: c savāsinau pibatām mantham etam aśvino rūpam paridhāya māyām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a indra etām sasṛje viddho agra ūrjām svadhām ajarām sā ta eṣā /

Halfverse: c tayā tvam jīva śaradah suvarcā mā ta ā susrod bhiṣajas te akran //7//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathedam bhūmyā adhi tṛṇam vāto mathāyati /

Halfverse: c evā mathnāmi te mano yathā mām kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam cen nayātho aśvinā kāminā sam ca vakṣathaḥ /

Halfverse: c sam vām bhagāso agmata sam cittāni sam u vratā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat suparṇā vivakṣavo anamīvā vivakṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tatra me gachatād dhavam śalya iva kulmalam yathā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad antaram tad bāhyam yad bāhyam tad antaram /

Halfverse: c kanyānām viśvarūpāṇām mano gṛbhāyauṣadhe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a eyam agan patikāmā janikāmo ‘ham āgamam /

Halfverse: c aśvaḥ kanikradad yathā bhagenāham sahāgamam //5//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrasya yā mahī dṛṣat krimer viśvasya tarhaṇī /

Halfverse: c tayā pinaṣmi sam krimīn dṛṣadā khalvāṁ iva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dṛṣṭam adṛṣṭam atṛham atho kurūrum atṛham /

Halfverse: c algaṇḍūnt sarvān chalunān krimīn vacasā jambhayāmasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a algaṇḍūn hanmi mahatā vadhena dūnā adūnā arasā abhūvan /

Halfverse: c śiṣṭān aśiṣṭān ni tirāmi vācā yathā krimīṇām nakir uchiṣātai //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anvāntryam śīrṣaṇya1m atho pārṣṭeyam krimīn /

Halfverse: c avaskavam vyadhvaram krimīn vacasā jambhayāmasi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye krimayaḥ parvateśu vaneṣv oṣadhīṣu paśuṣv apsv a1ntaḥ /

Halfverse: c ye asmākam tanvam āviviśuḥ sarvam tad dhanmi janima krimīṇām //5//

Hymn: 32

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a udyann ādityaḥ krimīn hantu nimrocan hantu raśmibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c ye antaḥ krimayo gavi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a viśvarūpam caturakṣam krimim sāraṅgam arjunam /

Halfverse: c śṛṇāmy asya pṛṣṭīr api vṛścāmi yac chiraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a atrivad vaḥ krimayo hanmi kaṇvavaj jamadagnivat /

Halfverse: c agastyasya brahmaṇā sam pinaṣmy aham krimīn //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a hato rājā krimīṇām utaiṣām sthapatir hataḥ /

Halfverse: c hato hatamātā krimir hatabhrātā hatasvasā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a hatāso asya veśaso hatāsaḥ pariveśasaḥ /

Halfverse: c atho ye kṣullakā iva sarve te krimayo hatāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pra te śṛṇāmi śṛṅge yābhyām vitudāyasi /

Halfverse: c bhinādmi te kuṣumbham yas te viṣadhānaḥ //6//

Hymn: 33

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a akṣībhyām te nāsikābhyām karṇābhyām chubukād adhi /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam śīrṣaṇyam mastiṣkāj jihvāyā vi vṛhāmi te //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a grīvābhyas ta uṣṇihābhyaḥ kīkasābhyo anūkyāt /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam doṣanya1m amsābhyām bāhubhyām vi vṛhāmi te //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a hṛdayāt te pari klomno halīkṣṇāt pārśvābhyām /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam matasnābhyām plīhno yaknas te vi vṛhāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a āntrebhyas te gudābhyo vaniṣṭhor udarād adhi /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam kukṣibhyām plāśer nābhyā vi vṛhāmi te //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ūrubhyām te aṣṭhīvadbhyām pārṣṇibhyām prapadābhyām /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam bhasadya1m śroṇibhyām bhāsadam bhamsaso vi vṛhāmi te //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a asthibhyas te majjabhyaḥ snāvabhyo dhamanibhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam pāṇibhyām aṅgulibhyo nakhebhyo vi vṛhāmi te //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a aṅgeaṅge lomnilomni yas te parvaṇiparvaṇi /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam tvacasyam te vayam kaśyapasya vībarheṇa viṣvañcam vi vṛhāmasi //7//

Hymn: 34

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ya īśe paśupatiḥ paśūnām catuṣpadām uta yo dvipadām /

Halfverse: c niṣkrītaḥ sa yajñiyam bhāgam etu rāyas poṣā yajamānam sacantām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pramuñcanto bhuvanasya reto gātum dhatta yajamānāya devāḥ /

Halfverse: c upākṛtam śaśamānam yad asthāt priyam devānām apy etu pāthaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye badhyamānam anu dīdhyānā anvaikṣanta manasā cakṣuṣā ca /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭān agre pra mumoktu devo viśvakarmā prajayā samrarāṇaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpā virūpāḥ santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ /

Halfverse: c vāyuṣ ṭān agre pra mumoktu devaḥ prajāpatiḥ prajayā samrarāṇaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a prajānantaḥ prati gṛhṇantu pūrve prāṇam aṅgebhyaḥ pary ācarantam /

Halfverse: c divam gacha prati tiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ svargam yāhi pathibhir devayānaiḥ //5//

Hymn: 35

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye bhakṣayanto na vasūny ānṛdhur yān agnayo anvatapyanta dhiṣṇyāḥ /

Halfverse: c yā teṣām avayā duriṣṭiḥ svṅṣṭim nas tām kṛṇavad viśvakarmā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yajñapatim ṛṣayaḥ enasāhur nirbhaktam prajā anutapyamānam /

Halfverse: c mathavyānt stokān apa yān rarādha sam naṣ ṭebhiḥ sṛjatu viśvakarmā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a adānyānt somapān manyamāno yajñasya vidvānt samaye na dhīraḥ /

Halfverse: c yad enaś cakṛvān baddha eṣa tam viśvakarman pra muñcā svastaye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ghorā ṛṣayo namo astv ebhyaś cakṣur yad eṣām manasaś ca satyam /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspataye mahiṣa dyumann namo viśvakarman namas te pāhy a1smān //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yajñasya cakṣuḥ prabhṛtir mukham ca vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi /

Halfverse: c imam yajñam vitatam viśvakarmaṇā devā yantu sumanasyamānāḥ //5//

Hymn: 36

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā no agne sumatim sambhalo gamed imām kumārīm saha no bhagena /

Halfverse: c juṣṭā vareṣu samaneṣu valgur oṣam patyā saubhagam astu asyai //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a somajuṣṭam brahmajuṣṭam aryamnā sambhṛtam bhagam /

Halfverse: c dhātur devasya satyena kṛṇomi pativedanam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a iyam agne nārī patim videṣṭa somo hi rājā subhagām kṛṇoti /

Halfverse: c suvānā putrān mahiṣī bhavāti gatvā patim subhagā vi rājatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yathākharo maghavamś cārur eṣa priyo mṛgāṇām suṣadā babhūva /

Halfverse: c evā bhagasya juṣṭeyam astu nārī sampriyā patyāvirādhayantī //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bhagasya nāvam ā roha pūrṇām anupadasvatīm /

Halfverse: c tayopapratāraya yo varaḥ pratikāmyaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ā krandaya dhanapate varam āmanasam kṛṇu /

Halfverse: c sarvam pradakṣiṇam kṛṇu yo varaḥ pratikāmyaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a idam hiraṇyam gulgulv ayam aukṣo atho bhagaḥ /

Halfverse: c ete patibhyas tvām aduḥ pratikāmāya vettave //7

Halfverse: e ā te nayatu savitā nayatu patir yaḥ pratikāmyaḥ /

Halfverse: g tvam asyai dhehi oṣadhe //8//

## Book: 3

3

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnir naḥ śatrūn praty etu vidvān pratidahann abhiśastim arātim /

Halfverse: c sa senām mohayatu pareṣām nirhastāmś ca kṛṇavaj jātavedāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yūyam ugrā maruta īdṛśe sthābhi preta mṛṇata sahadhvam /

Halfverse: c amīmṛṇan vasavo nāthitā ime agnir hy ṝṣām dūtaḥ pratyetu vidvān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a amitrasenām maghavann asmān chatrūyatīm abhi /

Halfverse: c yuvam tām indra vṛtrahann agniś ca dahatam prati //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prasūta indra pravatā haribhyām pra te vajraḥ pramṛṇann etu śatrūn /

Halfverse: c jahi pratīco anūcaḥ parāco viṣvak satyam kṛṇuhi cittam eṣām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indra senām mohayāmitrāṇām /

Halfverse: c agner vātasya dhrājyā tān viṣūco vi nāśaya //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indraḥ senām mohayatu maruto ghnantv ojasā /

Halfverse: c cakṣūmsy agnir ā dattām punar etu parājitā //6//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnir no dūtaḥ pratyetu vidvān pratidahann abhiśastim arātim /

Halfverse: c sa cittāni mohayatu pareṣām nirhastāmś ca kṛṇavaj jātavedāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayam agnir amūmuhad yāni cittāni vo hṛdi /

Halfverse: c vi vo dhamatv okasaḥ pra vo dhamatu sarvataḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indra cittāni mohayann arvāṅ ākūtyā cara /

Halfverse: c agner vātasya dhrājyā tān viṣūco vi nāśaya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vy ākūtaya eṣām itātho cittāni muhyata /

Halfverse: c atho yad adyaiṣām hṛdi tad eṣām pari nir jahi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a amīṣām cittāni pratimohayantī gṛhāṇāṅgāny apve parehi /

Halfverse: c abhi prehi nir daha hṛtsu śokair grāhyāmitrāms tamasā vidhya śatrūn //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a asau yā senā marutaḥ pareṣām asmān aity abhy ojasā spardhamānā /

Halfverse: c tām vidhyata tamasāpavratena yathaiṣām anyo anyam na jānāt //6//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a acikradat svapā iha bhuvad agne vy acasva rodasī urūcī /

Halfverse: c yuñjantu tvā maruto viśvavedasa āmum naya namasā rātahavyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dūre cit santam aruṣāsa indram ā cyāvayantu sakhyāya vipram /

Halfverse: c yad gāyatrīm bṛhatīm arkam asmai sautrāmaṇyā dadhṛṣanta devāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a adbhyas tvā rāja varuṇo hvayatu somas tvā hvayatu parvatebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c indras tvā hvayatu viḍbhya ābhyaḥ śyeno bhūtvā viśa ā patemāḥ //3// {4}

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śyeno havyam nayatv ā parasmād anyakṣetre aparuddham carantam /

Halfverse: c aśvinā panthām kṛṇutām sugam ta imam sajātā abhisamviśadhvam //4// {5}

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a hvayantu tvā pratijanāḥ prati mitrā avṛṣata /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī viśve devās te viśi kṣemam adīdharan //5// {6}

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yas te havam vivadat sajāto yaś ca niṣṭyaḥ /

Halfverse: c apāñcam indra tam kṛtvāthemam ihāva gamaya //6// {7}

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā tvā gan rāṣtram saha varcasod ihi prāṅ viśām patir ekarāṭ tvam vi rāja /

Halfverse: c sarvās tvā rājan pradiśo hvayantūpasadyo namasyo bhaveha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvām viśo vṛṇatām rājyāya tvām imāḥ pradiśaḥ pañca devīḥ /

Halfverse: c varṣman rāṣṭrasya kakudi śrayasva tato na ugro vi bhajā vasūni //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a acha tvā yantu havinaḥ sajātā agnir dūto ajiraḥ sam carātai /

Halfverse: c jāyāḥ putrāḥ sumanaso bhavantu bahum balim prati paśyāsā ugraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a aśvinā tvāgre mitrāvaruṇobhā viśve devā marutas tvā hvayantu /

Halfverse: c adhā mano vasudeyāya kṛṇuṣva tato na ugro vi bhajā vasūni //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā pra drava paramasyāḥ parāvataḥ śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām /

Halfverse: c tad ayam rājā varuṇas tathāha sa tvāyam ahvat sa upedam ehi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indrendra manuṣyā3ḥ parehi sam hy ajñāsthā varuṇaiḥ samvidānaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa tvāyam ahvat sve sadhasthe sa devān yakṣat sa u kalpayad viśaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a pathyā revatīr bahudhā virūpāḥ sarvāḥ samgatya varīyas te akran /

Halfverse: c tās tvā sarvāḥ samvidānā hvayantu daśamīm ugraḥ sumanā vaśeha //7//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āyam agan parṇamaṇir balī balena pramṛṇant sapatnān /

Halfverse: c ojo devānām paya oṣadhīnām varcasā mā jinvantv aprayāvan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mayi kṣatram parṇamaṇe mayi dhārayatād rayim /

Halfverse: c aham rāṣṭrasyābhīvarge nijo bhūyāsam uttamaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yam nidadhur vanaspatau guhyam devāḥ priyam maṇim /

Halfverse: c tam asmabhyam sahāyuṣā devā dadatu bhartave //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a somasya parṇaḥ saha ugram āgann indreṇa datto varuṇena śiṣṭaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam priyāsam bahu rocamāno dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā mārukṣat parṇamaṇir mahyā ariṣṭatātaye /

Halfverse: c yathāham uttaro ‘sāny aryamṇa uta samvidaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ye dhīvāno rathakārāḥ karmārā ye manīṣiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c upastīn parṇa mahyam tvam sarvān kṛṇv abhito janān //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ye rājāno rājakṛtaḥ sūtā grāmaṇyaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c upastīn parṇa mahyam tvam sarvān kṛṇv abhito janān //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a parṇo ‘si tanūpānaḥ sayonir vīro vīreṇa mayā /

Halfverse: c samvatsarasya tejasā tena badhnāmi tvā maṇe //8//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pumān pumsaḥ parijāto ‘śvatthaḥ khadirād adhi /

Halfverse: c sa hantu śatrūn māmakān yān aham dveṣmi ye ca mām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tān aśvattha niḥ śṛṇīhi śatrūn vaibādhadodhataḥ /

Halfverse: c indreṇa vṛtraghnā medī mitreṇa varuṇena ca //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathāśvattha nirabhano ‘ntar mahaty arṇave /

Halfverse: c evā tānt sarvān nir bhaṅgdhi yān aham dveṣmi ye ca mām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yaḥ sahamānaś carasi sāsahāna iva ṛṣabhaḥ /

Halfverse: c tenāśvattha tvayā vayam sapatnānt sahiṣīmahi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sinātv enān nirṛtir mṛtyoḥ pāśair amokyaiḥ /

Halfverse: c aśvattha śatrūn māmakān yān aham dveṣmi ye ca mām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yathāśvattha vānaspatyān ārohan kṛṇuṣe ‘dharān /

Halfverse: c evā me śatror mūrdhānam viṣvag bhinddhi sahasva ca //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a te ‘dharāñcaḥ pra plavantām chinnā naur iva bandhanāt /

Halfverse: c na vaibādhapraṇuttānām punar asti nivartanam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a praiṇān nude manasā pra cittenota brahmaṇā /

Halfverse: c praiṇān vṛkṣasya śākhayāśvatthasya nudāmahe //8//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a hariṇasya raghuṣyado ‘dhi śīrṣaṇi bheṣajam /

Halfverse: c sa kṣetriyam viṣāṇayā viṣūcīnam anīnaśat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anu tvā hariṇo vṛṣā padbhiś caturbhir akramīt /

Halfverse: c viṣāṇe vi ṣya guṣpitam yad asya kṣetriyam hṛdi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ado yad avarocate catuṣpakṣam iva chadiḥ /

Halfverse: c tenā te sarvam kṣetriyam aṅgebhyo nāśayāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a amū ye divi subhage vicṛtau nāma tārake /

Halfverse: c vi kṣetriyasya muñcatām adhamam pāśam uttamam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a āpa id vā u bheṣajīr āpo amīvacātanīḥ /

Halfverse: c āpo viśvasya bheṣajīs tās tvā muñcantu kṣetriyāt //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yad āsuteḥ kriyamānāyāḥ kṣetriyam tvā vyānaśe /

Halfverse: c vedāham tasya bheṣajam kṣetriyam nāśayāmi tvat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a apavāse nakṣatrāṇām apavāsa uṣasām uta /

Halfverse: c apāsmat sarvam durbhūtam apa kṣetriyam uchatu //7//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā yātu mitra ṛtubhiḥ kalpamānaḥ samveśayan pṛthivīm usriyābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c athāsmabhyam varuṇo vāyur agnir bṛhad rāṣṭram samveśyam dadhātu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dhātā rātiḥ savitedam juśantām indras tvaṣṭā prati haryantu me vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c huve devīm aditim śūraputrām sajātānām madhyameṣṭhā yathāsāni //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a huve somam savitāram namobhir viśvān ādityāṁ aham uttaratve /

Halfverse: c ayam agnir dīdāyad dīrgham eva sajātair iddho ‘pratibruvadbhiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ihed asātha na paro gamātheryo gopāḥ puṣṭapatir va ājat /

Halfverse: c asmai kāmāyopa kāminīr viśve vo devā upasamyantu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sam vo manāmsi sam vratā sam ākūtīr namāmasi /

Halfverse: c amī ye vivratā sthana tān vaḥ sam namayāmasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a aham gṛbhṇāmi manasā manāmsi mama cittam anu cittebhir eta /

Halfverse: c mama vaśeṣu hṛdayāni vaḥ kṛṇomi mama yātam anuvartmāna eta //6//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a karśaphasya viśaphasya dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā /

Halfverse: c yathābhicakra devās tathāpa kṛṇutā punaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aśreṣmāṇo adhārayan tathā tan manunā kṛtam /

Halfverse: c kṛṇomi vadhri viṣkandham muṣkābarho gavām iva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a piśaṅge sūtre khṛgalam tad ā badhnanti vedhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c śravasyum śuṣmam kābavam vadhrim kṛṇvantu bandhuraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yenā śravasyavaś caratha devā ivāsuramāyayā /

Halfverse: c śunām kapir iva dūṣaṇo bandhurā kābavasya ca //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a duṣṭyai hi tvā bhatsyāmi dūṣayiṣyāmi kābavam /

Halfverse: c ud āśavo rathā iva śapathebhiḥ sariṣyatha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ekaśatam viṣkandhāni viṣṭhitā pṛthivīm anu /

Halfverse: c teṣām tvām agre uj jaharur maṇim viṣkandhadūṣaṇam //6//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prathamā ha vy ḥvāsa sā dhenur abhavad yame /

Halfverse: c sā naḥ payasvatī duhām uttarāmuttarām samām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yām devāḥ pratinandanti rātrim dhenum upāyatīm /

Halfverse: c samvatsarasya yā patnī sā no astu sumaṅgalī //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samvatsarasya pratimām yām tvā rātry upāsmahe /

Halfverse: c sā na āyuṣmatīm prajām rāyas poṣeṇa sam sṛja //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a iyam eva sā yā prathamā vyauchad āsv itarāsu carati praviṣṭā /

Halfverse: c mahānto asyām mahimāno antar vadhūr jigāya navagaj janitrī //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vānaspatyā grāvāṇo ghoṣam akrata haviṣ kṛṇvantaḥ parivatsarīṇam /

Halfverse: c ekāṣṭake suprajasaḥ suvīrā vayam syāma patayo rayīṇām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a iḍāyās padam ghṛtavat sarīsṛpam jātavedaḥ prati havyā gṛbhāya /

Halfverse: c ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpās teṣām saptānām mayi rantir astu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ā mā puṣṭe ca poṣe ca rātri devānām sumatau syāma /

Halfverse: c pūrṇā darve parā pata supūrṇā punar ā pata /

Halfverse: e sarvān yajñānt sambhuñjatīṣam ūrjam na ā bhara //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a āyam agant samvatsaraḥ patir ekāṣṭake tava /

Halfverse: c sā na āyuṣmatīm prajām rāyas poṣeṇa sam sṛja //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ṛtūn yaja ṛtupatīn ārtavān uta hāyanān /

Halfverse: c samāḥ samvatsarān māsān bhūtasya pataye yaje //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ṛtubhyaṣ ṭvārtavebhyo mādbhyaḥ samvatsarebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c dhātre vidhātre samṛdhe bhūtasya pataye yaje //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a iḍayā juhvato vayam devān ghṛtavatā yaje /

Halfverse: c gṛhān alubhyato vayam sam viśemopa gomataḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ekāṣṭakā tapasā tapyamānā jajāna garbham mahimānam indram /

Halfverse: c tena devā vy asahanta śatrūn hantā dasyūnām abhavac chacīpatiḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a indraputre somaputre duhitāsi prajāpateḥ /

Halfverse: c kāmān asmākam pūraya prati gṛhṇāhi no haviḥ //13//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a muñcāmi tvā haviṣā jīvanāya kam ajñātayakṣmād uta rājayakṣmāt /

Halfverse: c grāhir jagrāha yady etad enam tasyā indrāgnī pra mumuktam enam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yadi kṣitāyur yadi vā pareto yadi mṛtyor antikam eva /

Halfverse: c tam ā harāmi nirṛter upasthād aspārśam enam śataśāradāya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sahasrākṣeṇa śatavīryeṇa śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣam enam /

Halfverse: c indro yathainam śarado nayāty ati viśvasya duritasya pāram //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śatam jīva śarado vardhamānaḥ śatam hemantān chatam u vasantān /

Halfverse: c śatam te indro agniḥ savitā bṛhaspatiḥ śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣam enam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pra viśatam prāṇāpānāv anaḍvāhāv iva vrajam /

Halfverse: c vy anye yantu mṛtyavo yān āhur itarān chatam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ihaiva stam prāṇāpānau māpa gātam ito yuvam /

Halfverse: c śarīram asyāṅgāni jarase vahatam punaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a jarāyai tvā pari dadāmi jarāyai ni dhuvāmi tvā /

Halfverse: c jarā tvā bhadrā neṣṭa vy anye yantu mṛtyavo yān āhur itarān chatam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a abhi tvā jarimāhita gām ukṣaṇam iva rajjvā /

Halfverse: c yas tvā mṛtyur abhyadhatta jāyamānam supāśayā /

Halfverse: e tam te satyasya hastābhyām ud amuñcad bṛhaspatiḥ //8//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ihaiva dhruvām ni minomi śālām kṣeme tiṣṭhāti ghṛtam ukṣamāṇā /

Halfverse: c tām tvā śāle sarvavīrāḥ suvīrā ariṣṭavīrā upa sam carema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ihaiva dhruvā prati tiṣṭha śāle ‘śvāvatī gomatī sūnṛtāvatī /

Halfverse: c ūrjasvatī ghṛtavatī payasvaty uc chrayasva mahate saubhagāya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dharuṇy asi śāle bṛhachandāḥ pūtidhānyā /

Halfverse: c ā tvā vatso gamed ā kumāra ā dhenavaḥ sāyam āspandamānāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a imām śālām savitā vāyur indro bṛhaspatir ni minotu prajānan /

Halfverse: c ukṣantūdrā maruto ghṛtena bhago no rājā ni kṛṣim tanotu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mānasya patni śaraṇā syonā devī devebhir nimitāsy agre /

Halfverse: c tṛṇam vasānā sumanā asas tvam athāsmabhyam sahavīram rayim dāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ṛtena sthūṇām adhi roha vamśogro virājann apa vṛṅkṣva śatrūn /

Halfverse: c mā te riṣann upasattāro gṛhāṇām śāle śatam jīvema śaradaḥ sarvavīrāḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a emām kumāras taruṇa ā vatso jagatā saha /

Halfverse: c emām parisrutaḥ kumbha ā dadhnaḥ kalaśair aguḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a pūrṇam nāri pra bhara kumbham etam ghṛtasya dhārām amṛtena sambhṛtām /

Halfverse: c imām pātṝn amṛtena sam aṅgdhīṣṭāpūrtam abhi rakṣāty enām //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a imā āpaḥ pra bharāmy ayakṣmā yakṣmanāśanīḥ /

Halfverse: c gṛhān upa pra sīdāmy amṛtena sahāgninā //9//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad adaḥ samprayatīr ahāv anadatā hate /

Halfverse: c tasmād ā nadyo3 nāma stha tā vo nāmāni sindhavaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yat preṣitā varuṇenāc chībham samavalgata /

Halfverse: c tad āpnod indro vo yatīs tasmād āpo anu ṣṭhana //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apakāmam syandamānā avīvarata vo hi kam /

Halfverse: c indro vaḥ śaktibhir devīs tasmād vār nāma vo hitam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ekaḥ vo devo ‘py atiṣṭhat syandamānā yathāvaśam /

Halfverse: c ud āniṣur mahīr iti tasmād udakam ucyate //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a āpo bhadrā ghṛtam id āpa āsann agnīṣomau bibhraty āpa it tāḥ /

Halfverse: c tīvro raso madhupṛcām aramgama ā mā prāṇena saha varcasā gamet //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ād it paśyāmy uta vā śṛṇomy ā mā ghoṣo gachati vāṅ māsām /

Halfverse: c manye bhejāno amṛtasya tarhi hiraṇyavarṇā atṛpam yadā vaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a idam va āpo hṛdayam ayam vatsa ṛtāvarīḥ /

Halfverse: c ihettham eta śakvarīr yatredam veśayāmi vaḥ //7//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam vo goṣṭhena suṣadā sam rayyā sam subhūtyā /

Halfverse: c aharjātasya yan nāma tenā vaḥ sam sṛjāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam vaḥ sṛjatv aryamā sam pūṣā sam bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c sam indro yo dhanamjayo mayi puṣyata yad vasu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samjagmānā abibhyuṣīr asmin goṣṭhe karīṣiṇīḥ /

Halfverse: c bibhratīḥ somyam madhv anamīvā upetana //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ihaiva gāva etaneho śakeva puṣyata /

Halfverse: c ihaivota pra jāyadhvam mayi samjñānam astu vaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śivo vo goṣṭho bhavatu śāriśākeva puṣyata /

Halfverse: c ihaivota pra jāyadhvam mayā vaḥ sam sṛjāmasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a mayā gāvo gopatinā sacadhvam ayam vo goṣṭha iha poṣayiṣṇuḥ /

Halfverse: c rāyas poṣeṇa bahulā bhavantīr jīvā jīvantīr upa vaḥ sadema //6//

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indram aham vaṇijam codayāmi sa na aitu puraetā no astu /

Halfverse: c nudann arātim paripanthinam mṛgam sa īśāno dhanadā astu mahyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ye panthāno bahavo devayānā antarā dyāvāpṛthivī samcaranti /

Halfverse: c te mā juṣantām payasā ghṛtena yathā krītvā dhanam āharāṇi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a idhmenāgna ichamāno ghṛtena juhomi havyam tarase balāya /

Halfverse: c yāvad īśe brahmaṇā vandamāna imām dhiyam śataseyāya devīm //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a imām agne śaraṇim mīmṛṣo no yam adhvānam agāma dūram /

Halfverse: c śunam no astu prapaṇo vikrayaś ca pratipaṇaḥ phalinam mā kṛṇotu /

Halfverse: e idam havyam samvidānau juṣethām śunam no astu caritam utthitam ca //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yena dhanena prapaṇam carāmi dhanena devā dhanam ichamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c tan me bhūyo bhavatu mā kanīyo ‘gne sātaghno devān haviṣā ni ṣedha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yena dhanena prapaṇam carāmi dhanena devā dhanam ichamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmin ma indro rucim ā dadhātu prajāpatiḥ savitā somo agniḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a upa tvā namasā vayam hotar vaiśvānara stumaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ prajāsv ātmasu goṣu prāṇeṣu jāgṛhi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a viśvāhā te sadam id bharemāśvāyeva tiṣṭhate jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c rāyas poṣeṇa sam iṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma //8//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prātar agnim prātar indram havāmahe prātar mitrāvaruṇā prātar aśvinā /

Halfverse: c prātar bhagam pūṣaṇam brahmaṇas patim prātaḥ somam uta rudram havāmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a prātarjitam bhagam ugram havāmahe vayam putram aditer yo vidhartā /

Halfverse: c ādhraś cid yam manyamānas turaś cid rājā cid yam bhagam bhakṣīty āha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bhaga praṇetar bhaga satyarādho bhagemām dhiyam ud avā dadan naḥ /

Halfverse: c bhaga pra ṇo janaya gobhir aśvair bhaga pra nṛbhir nṛvantaḥ syāma //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a utedānīm bhagavantaḥ syāmota prapitva uta madhye ahnām /

Halfverse: c utoditau maghavant sūryasya vayam devānām sumatau syāma //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bhaga eva bhagavāṁ astu devas tenā vayam bhagavantaḥ syāma /

Halfverse: c tam tvā bhaga sarva ij johavīmi sa no bhaga puraetā bhaveha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sam adhvarāyoṣaso namanta dadhikrāveva śucaye padāya /

Halfverse: c arvācīnam vasuvidam bhagam me ratham ivāśvā vājina ā vahantu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a aśvāvatīr gomatīr na uṣāso vīravatīḥ sadam uchantu bhadrāḥ /

Halfverse: c ghṛtam duhānā viśvataḥ prapītā yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ //7//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sīrā yuñjanti kavayo yugā vi tanvate pṛthak /

Halfverse: c dhīrā deveṣu sumnayau //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yunakta sīrā vi yugā tanota kṛte yonau vapateha bījam /

Halfverse: c virājaḥ śnuṣṭiḥ sabharā asan no nedīya it sṛṇyaḥ pakvam ā yavan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a lāṅgalam pavīravat suśīmam somasatsaru /

Halfverse: c ud id vapatu gām avim prasthāvad rathavāhanam pībarīm ca prapharvyam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indraḥ sītām ni gṛhṇātu tām pūṣābhi rakṣatu /

Halfverse: c sā naḥ payasvatī duhām uttarāmuttarām samām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śunam suphālā vi tudantu bhūmim śunam kīnāśā anu yantu vāhān /

Halfverse: c śunāsīrā haviṣā tośamānā supippalā oṣadhīḥ kartam asmai //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śunam vāhāḥ śunam naraḥ śunam kṛṣatu lāṅgalam /

Halfverse: c śunam varatrā badhyantām śunam aṣṭrām ud iṅgaya //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śunāsīreha sma me juṣethām /

Halfverse: c yad divi cakrathuḥ payas tenemām upa siñcatam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a sīte vandāmahe tvārvācī subhage bhava /

Halfverse: c yathā naḥ sumanā aso yathā naḥ suphalā bhuvaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ghṛtena sītā madhunā samaktā viśvair devair anumatā marudbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sā naḥ sīte payasābhyāvavṛtsvorjasvatī ghṛtavat pinvamānā //9//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imām khanāmy oṣadhim vīrudhām balavattamām /

Halfverse: c yayā sapatnīm bādhate yayā samvindate patim //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a uttānaparṇe subhage devajūte sahasvati /

Halfverse: c sapatnīm me parā ṇuda patim me kevalam kṛdhi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nahi te nāma jagrāha no asmin ramase patau /

Halfverse: c parām eva parāvatam sapatnīm gamayāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a uttarāham uttara uttared uttarābhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c adhaḥ sapatnī yā mamādharā sādharābhyaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a aham asmi sahamānātho tvam asi sāsahiḥ /

Halfverse: c ubhe sahasvatī bhūtvā sapatnīm me sahāvahai //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a abhi te ‘dhām sahamānām upa te ‘dhām sahīyasīm /

Halfverse: c mām anu pra te mano vatsam gaur iva dhāvatu pathā vār iva dhāvatu //6//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samśitam ma idam brahma samśitam vīrya1m balam /

Halfverse: c samśitam kṣatram ajaram astu jiṣṇur yeṣām asmi purohitaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam aham eṣām rāṣṭram syāmi sam ojo vīrya1m balam /

Halfverse: c vṛścāmi śatrūṇām bāhūn anena haviṣā aham //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nīcaiḥ padyantām adhare bhavantu ye naḥ sūrim maghavānam pṛtanyān /

Halfverse: c kṣiṇāmi brahmaṇāmitrān un nayāmi svān aham //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tīkṣṇīyāmsaḥ paraśor agnes tīkṣṇatarā uta /

Halfverse: c indrasya vajrāt tīkṣṇīyāmso yeṣām asmi purohitaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a eṣām aham āyudhā sam syāmy eṣām rāṣṭram suvīram vardhayāmi /

Halfverse: c eṣām kṣatram ajaram astu jiṣṇv ṝṣām cittam viśve ‘vantu devāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ud dharṣantām maghavan vājināny ud vīrāṇām jayatām etu ghoṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c pṛthag ghoṣā ululayaḥ ketumanta ud īratām /

Halfverse: e devā indrajyeṣṭhā maruto yantu senayā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a pretā jayatā nara ugrā vaḥ santu bāhavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tīkṣṇeṣavo ‘baladhanvano hatogrāyudhā abalān ugrabāhavaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a avasṛṣṭā parā pata śaravye brahmasamśite /

Halfverse: c jaya amitrān pra padyasva jahy ṝṣām varamvaram māmīṣām moci kaś cana //8//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam te yonir ṛtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ /

Halfverse: c tam jānann agna ā rohādhā no vardhaya rayim //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agne achā vadeha naḥ pratyaṅ naḥ sumanā bhava /

Halfverse: c pra ṇo yacha viśām pate dhanadā asi nas tvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pra ṇo yachatv aryamā pra bhagaḥ pra bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c pra devīḥ prota sūnṛtā rayim devī dadhātu me //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a somam rājānam avase ‘gnim gīrbhir havāmahe /

Halfverse: c ādityam viṣṇum sūryam brahmāṇam ca bṛhaspatim //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tvam no agne agnibhir brahma yajñam vardhaya /

Halfverse: c tvam no deva dātave rayim dānāya codaya //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indravāyū ubhāv iha suhaveha havāmahe /

Halfverse: c yathā naḥ sarva ij janaḥ samgatyām sumanā asad dānakāmaś ca no bhuvat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a aryamaṇam bṛhaspatim indram dānāya codaya /

Halfverse: c vātam viṣṇum sarasvatīm savitāram ca vājinam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a vājasya nu prasave sam babhūvimemā ca viśvā bhuvanāni antaḥ /

Halfverse: c utāditsantam dāpayatu prajānan rayim ca naḥ sarvavīram ni yacha //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a duhrām me pañca pradiṣo duhrām urvīr yathābalam /

Halfverse: c prāpeyam sarvā ākūtīr manasā hṛdayena ca //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a gosanim vācam udeyam varcasā mābhyudihi /

Halfverse: c ā rundhām sarvato vāyus tvaṣṭā poṣam dadhātu me //10//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye agnayo apsv a1ntar ye vṛtre ye puruṣe ye aśmasu /

Halfverse: c ya āviveśoṣadhīr yo vanaspatīms tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yaḥ some antar yo goṣv antar ya āviṣṭo vayaḥsu yo mṛgeṣu /

Halfverse: c ya āviveśa dvipado yas catuṣpadas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ya indreṇa saratham yāti devo vaiśvānara uta viśvadāvyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yam johavīmi pṛtanāsu sāsahim tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yo devo viśvād yam u kāmam āhur yam dātāram pratigṛhṇantam āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c yo dhīraḥ śakraḥ paribhūr adābhyas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yam tvā hotāram manasābhi samvidus trayodaśa bhauvanāḥ pañca mānavāḥ /

Halfverse: c varcodhase yaśase sūnṛtāvate tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ukṣānnāya vaśānnāya somapṛṣṭhāya vedhase /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānarajyeṣṭhebhyas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a divam pṛthivīm anv antarikṣam ye vidyutam anusamcaranti /

Halfverse: c ye dikṣv a1ntar ye vāte antas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a hiraṇyapāṇim savitāram indram bṛhaspatim varuṇam mitram agnim /

Halfverse: c viśvān devān aṅgiraso havāmahe imam kravyādam śamayantv agnim //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a śānto agniḥ kravyāc chāntaḥ puruṣareṣaṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c atho yo viśvadāvya1s tam kravyādam aśīśamam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ye parvatāḥ somapṛṣṭhā āpa uttānaśīvarīḥ /

Halfverse: c vātaḥ parjanya ād agnis te kravyādam aśīśaman //10//

Hymn: 22

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a hastivarcasam prathatām bṛhad yaśo adityā yat tanvaḥ sambabhūva /

Halfverse: c tat sarve sam adur mahyam etad viśve devā aditiḥ sajoṣāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mitraś ca varuṇaś cendro rudraś ca cetatu /

Halfverse: c devāso viśvadhāyasas te māñjantu varcasā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yena hastī varcasā sambabhūva yena rājā manuṣyṝsv apsv a1ntaḥ /

Halfverse: c yena devā devatām agra āyan tena mām adya varcasāgne varcasvinam kṛṇu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yat te varco jātavedo bṛhad bhavaty āhuteḥ /

Halfverse: c yāvat sūryasya varca āsurasya ca hastinaḥ /

Halfverse: e tāvan me aśvinā varca ā dhattām puṣkarasrajā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yāvac catasraḥ pradiśaś cakṣur yāvat samaśnute /

Halfverse: c tāvat samaitv indriyam mayi tad dhastivarcasam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a hastī mṛgāṇām suṣadām atiṣṭhāvān babhūva hi /

Halfverse: c tasya bhagena varcasābhi ṣiñcāmi mām aham //6//

Hymn: 23

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yena vehad babhūvitha nāśayāmasi tat tvat /

Halfverse: c idam tad anyatra tvad apa dūre ni dadhmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā te yonim garbha etu pumān bāṇa iveṣudhim /

Halfverse: c ā vīro ‘tra jāyatām putras te daśamāsyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pumāmsam putram janaya tam pumān anu jāyatām /

Halfverse: c bhavāsi putrāṇām mātā jātānām janayāś ca yān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yāni badrāṇi bījāny ṛṣabhā janayanti ca /

Halfverse: c tais tvam putram vindasva sā prasūr dhenukā bhava //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kṛṇomi te prājāpatyam ā yonim garbha etu te /

Halfverse: c vindasva tvam putram nāri yas tubhyam śam asac cham u tasmai tvam bhava //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yāsām dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā samudro mūlam vīrudhām babhūva /

Halfverse: c tās tvā putravidyāya daivīḥ prāvantv oṣadhayaḥ //6//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a payasvatīr oṣadhayaḥ payasvan māmakam vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c atho payasvatīnām ā bhare ‘ham sahasraśaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vedāham payasvantam cakāra dhānyam bahu /

Halfverse: c sambhṛtvā nāma yo devas tam vayam havāmahe yoyo ayajvano gṛhe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a imā yāḥ pañca pradiśo mānavīḥ pañca kṛṣṭayaḥ /

Halfverse: c vṛṣṭe śāpam nadīr iveha sphātim samāvahān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ud utsam śatadhāram sahasradhāram akṣitam /

Halfverse: c evāsmākedam dhānyam sahasradhāram akṣitam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śatahasta samāhara sahasrahasta sam kira /

Halfverse: c kṛtasya kāryasya ceha sphātim samāvaha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tisro mātrā gandharvāṇām catasro gṛhapatnyāḥ /

Halfverse: c tāsām yā sphātimattamā tayā tvābhi mṛśāmasi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a upohaś ca samūhaś ca kṣattārau te prajāpate /

Halfverse: c tāv ihā vahatām sphātim bahum bhūmānam akṣitam //7//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a uttudas tvot tudatu mā dhṛthāḥ śayane sve /

Halfverse: c iṣuḥ kāmasya yā bhīmā tayā vidhyāmi tvā hṛdi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ādhīparṇām kāmaśalyām iṣum samkalpakulmalām /

Halfverse: c tām susamnatām kṛtvā kāmo vidhyatu tvā hṛdi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yā plīhānam śoṣayati kāmasyeṣuḥ susamnatā /

Halfverse: c prācīnapakṣā vyoṣā tayā vidhyāmi tvā hṛdi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śucā viddhā vyoṣayā śuṣkāsyābhi sarpa mā /

Halfverse: c mṛdur nimanyuḥ kevalī priyavādiny anuvratā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ājāmi tvājanyā pari mātur atho pituḥ /

Halfverse: c yathā mama kratāv aso mama cittam upāyasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a vy asyai mitrāvaruṇau hṛdaś cittāny asyatam /

Halfverse: c athainām akratum kṛtvā mamaiva kṛṇutam vaśe //6//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yṝ3 ‘syām stha prācyām diśi hetayo nāma devās teṣām vo agnir iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c te no mṛḍata te no ‘dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yṝ3 ‘syām stha dakṣiṇāyām diśy aviṣyavo nāma devās teṣām vaḥ kāma iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c te no mṛḍata te no ‘dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā //2//

Verse: 3
