Halfverse: a yṝ3 ‘syām stha pratīcyām diśi vairājā nāma devās teṣām va āpa iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c te no mṛḍata te no ‘dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yṝ3 ‘syām sthodīcyām diśi pravidhyanto nāma devās teṣām vo vāta iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c te no mṛḍata te no ‘dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yṝ3 ‘syām stha dhruvāyām diśi nilimpā nāma devās teṣām va oṣadhīr iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c te no mṛḍata te no ‘dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yṝ3 ‘syām sthordhvāyām diśy avasvanto nāma devās teṣām vo bṛhaspatir iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c te no mṛḍata te no ‘dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā //6//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prācī dig agnir adhipatir asito rakṣitādityā iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo namo ‘dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu /

Halfverse: e yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmas tam vo jambhe dadhmaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dakṣiṇā dig indro ‘dhipatis tiraścirājī rakṣitā pitara iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo namo ‘dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu /

Halfverse: e yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmas tam vo jambhe dadhmaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pratīcī dig varuṇo ‘dhipatiḥ pṛdākū rakṣitānnam iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo namo ‘dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu /

Halfverse: e yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmas tam vo jambhe dadhmaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a udīcī dik somo ‘dhipatiḥ svajo rakṣitāśanir iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo namo ‘dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu /

Halfverse: e yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmas tam vo jambhe dadhmaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dhruvā dig viṣṇur adhipatiḥ kalmāṣagrīvo rakṣitā vīrudha iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo namo ‘dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu /

Halfverse: e yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmas tam vo jambhe dadhmaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ūrdhvā dig bṛhaspatir adhipatiḥ śvitro rakṣitā varṣam iṣavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo namo ‘dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu /

Halfverse: e yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmas tam vo jambhe dadhmaḥ //6//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ekaikayaiṣā sṛṣṭyā sam babhūva yatra gā asṛjanta bhūtakṛto viśvarūpāḥ /

Halfverse: c yatra vijāyate yaminy apartuḥ sā paśūn kṣiṇāti riphatī ruśatī //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a eṣā paśūnt sam kṣiṇāti kravyād bhūtvā vyadvarī /

Halfverse: c utainām brahmaṇe dadyāt tathā syonā śivā syāt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śivā bhava puruṣebhyo gobhyo aśvebhyaḥ śivā /

Halfverse: c śivāsmai sarvasmai kṣetrāya śivā na ihaidhi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a iha puṣṭir iha rasa iha sahasrasātamā bhava /

Halfverse: c paśūn yamini poṣaya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yatrā suhārdaḥ sukṛto madanti vihāya rogam tanva1ḥ svāyāh /

Halfverse: c tam lokam yaminy abhisambabhūva sā no mā himsīt puruṣān paśūmś ca //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yatrā suhārdām sukṛtām agnihotrahutām yatra lokaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam lokam yaminy abhisambabhūva sā no mā himsīt puruṣān paśūmś ca //6//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad rājāno vibhajanta iṣṭāpūrtasya ṣoḍaśam yamasyāmī sabhāsadaḥ /

Halfverse: c avis tasmāt pra muñcati dattaḥ śitipāt svadhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sarvān kāmān pūrayaty ābhavan prabhavan bhavan /

Halfverse: c ākūtipro ‘vir dattaḥ śitipānn nopa dasyati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo dadāti śitipādam avim lokena sammitam /

Halfverse: c sa nākam abhyārohati yatra śulko na kriyate abalena balīyase //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pañcāpūpam śitipādam avim lokena sammitam /

Halfverse: c pradātopa jīvati pitṝṇām loke ‘kṣitam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pañcāpūpam śitipādam avim lokena sammitam /

Halfverse: c pradātopa jīvati sūryāmāsayor akṣitam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ireva nopa dasyati samudra iva payo mahat /

Halfverse: c devau savāsināv iva śitipān nopa dasyati //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ka idam kasmā adāt kāmaḥ kāmāyādāt /

Halfverse: c kāmo dātā kāmaḥ pratigrahītā kāmaḥ samudram ā viveśa /

Halfverse: e kāmena tvā prati gṛhnāmi kāmaitat te //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bhūmiṣ ṭvā prati gṛhṇātv antarikṣam idam mahat /

Halfverse: c māham prāṇena mātmanā mā prajayā pratigṛhya vi rādhiṣi //8//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sahṛdayam sāmmanasyam avidveṣam kṛṇomi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c anyo anyam abhi haryata vatsam jātam ivāghnyā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anuvrataḥ pituḥ putro mātrā bhavatu sammanāḥ /

Halfverse: c jāyā patye madhumatīm vācam vadatu śantivām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mā bhrātā bhrātaram dvikṣan mā svasāram uta svasā /

Halfverse: c samyañcaḥ savratā bhūtvā vācam vadata bhadrayā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yena devā na viyanti no ca vidviṣate mithaḥ /

Halfverse: c tat kṛṇmo brahma vo gṛhe samjñānam puruṣebhyaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a jyāyasvantaś cittino mā vi yauṣṭa samrādhayantaḥ sadhurāś carantaḥ /

Halfverse: c anyo anyasmai valgu vadanta eta sadhrīcīnān vaḥ sammanasas krṇomi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a samānī prapā saha vo ‘nnabhāgaḥ samāne yoktre saha vo yunajmi /

Halfverse: c samyañco ‘gnim saparyatārā nābhim ivābhitaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sadhrīcīnān vaḥ sammanasas kṛṇomy ekaśnuṣṭīnt samvananena sarvān /

Halfverse: c devā ivāmṛtam rakṣamāṇāḥ sāyamprātaḥ saumanaso vo astu //7//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vi devā jarasāvṛtan vi tvam agne arātyā /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vy ārtyā pavamāno vi śakraḥ pāpakṛtyayā /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vi grāmyāḥ paśava āraṇyair vy āpas tṛṣṇayāsaran /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vī3 me dyāvāpṛthivī ito vi panthāno diśamdiśam /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tvaṣṭā duhitre vahatum yunaktītīdam viśvam bhuvanam vi yāti /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a agniḥ prāṇānt sam dadhāti candraḥ prāṇena samhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a prāṇena viśvatovīryam devāḥ sūryam sam airayan /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a āyuṣmatām āyuṣkṛtām prāṇena jīva mā mṛthāḥ /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a prāṇena prāṇatām prāṇehaiva bhava mā mṛthāḥ /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ud āyuṣā sam āyuṣod oṣadhīnām rasena /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ā parjanyasya vṛṣṭyod asthāmāmṛtā vayam /

Halfverse: c vy a1ham sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā //11//

## Book: 4

4

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a brahma jajñānam prathamam purastād vi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa budhnyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ sataś ca yonim asataś ca vi vaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a iyam pitryā rāṣṭry etv agre prathamāya januṣe bhuvaneṣṭhāḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmā etam surucam hvāram ahyam gharmam śrīṇantu prathamāya dhāsyave //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pra yo jajñe vidvān asya bandhur viśvā devānām janimā vivakti /

Halfverse: c brahma brahmaṇa uj jabhāra madhyān nicair uccaiḥ svadhā abhi pra tasthau //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sa hi vidaḥ sa pṛthivyā ṛtasthā mahī kṣemam rodasī askabhāyat /

Halfverse: c mahān mahī askabhāyad vi jāto dyām sadma pārthivam ca rajaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sa bhudhnyād āṣṭra januṣo ‘bhy agram bṛhaspatir devatā tasya samrāṭ /

Halfverse: c ahar yac chukram jyotiṣo janiṣṭātha dyumanto vi vasantu viprāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a nūnam tad asya kāvyo hinoti maho devasya pūrvyasya dhāma /

Halfverse: c eṣa jajñe bahubhiḥ sākam itthā pūrve ardhe viṣite sasan nu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yo ‘tharvāṇam pitaram devabandhum bṛhaspatim namasāva ca gachāt /

Halfverse: c tvam viśveṣām janitā yathāsaḥ kavir devo na dabhāyat svadhāvān //7//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ya ātmadā baladā yasya viśva upāsate praśiṣam yasya devāḥ /

Halfverse: c yo3 ‘syeśe dvipado yaś catuṣpadaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yaḥ prāṇato nimiṣato mahitvaiko rājā jagato babhūva /

Halfverse: c yasya chāyāmṛtam yasya mṛtyuḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yam krandasī avataś caskabhāne bhiyasāne rodasī ahvayethām /

Halfverse: c yasyāsau panthā rajaso vimānaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yasya dyaur urvī pṛthivī ca mahī yasyāda urv antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c yasyāsau sūro vitato mahitvā kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasya viśve himavanto mahitvā samudre yasya rasām id āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c imāś ca pradiśo yasya bāhū kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a āpo agre viśvam āvan garbham dadhānā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ /

Halfverse: c yāsu devīṣv adhi deva āsīt kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a hiraṇyagarbhaḥ sam avartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patir eka āsīt /

Halfverse: c sa dādhāra pṛthivīm uta dyām kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a āpo vatsam janayantīr garbham agre sam airayan /

Halfverse: c tasyota jāyamānasyolba āsīd dhiraṇyayaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema //8//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud itas trayo akraman vyāghraḥ puruṣo vṛkaḥ /

Halfverse: c hirug ghi yanti sindhavo hirug devo vanaspatir hiruṅ namantu śatravaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pareṇaitu pathā vṛkaḥ parameṇota taskaraḥ /

Halfverse: c pareṇa datvatī rajjuḥ pareṇāghāyur arṣatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a akṣyaḥ ca te mukham ca te vyāghra jambhayāmasi /

Halfverse: c āt sarvān vimśatim nakhān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vyāghram datvatām vayam prathamam jambhayāmasi /

Halfverse: c ād u ṣṭenam atho ahim yātudhānam atho vṛkam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yo adya stena āyati sa sampiṣṭo apāyati /

Halfverse: c pathām apadhvamsenaitv indro vajreṇa hantu tam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a mūrṇā mṛgasya dantā apiśīrṇā u pṛṣṭayaḥ /

Halfverse: c nimruk te godhā bhavatu nīcāyac chaśayur mṛgaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yat samyamo na vi yamo vi yamo yan na samyamaḥ /

Halfverse: c indrajāh somajā ātharvaṇam asi vyāghrajambhanam //7//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yām tvā gandharvo akhanad varuṇāya mṛtabhraje /

Halfverse: c tām tvā vayam khanāmasy oṣadhim śepaharṣaṇīm //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ud uṣā ud u sūrya ud idam māmakam vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c ud ejatu prajāpatir vṛṣā śuṣmeṇa vājinā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā sma te virohato ‘bhitaptam ivānati /

Halfverse: c tatas te śuṣmavattaram iyam kṛṇotv oṣadhiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a uc chuṣmauṣadhīnām sārā ṛṣabhāṇām /

Halfverse: c sam pumsām indra vṛṣṇyam asmin dhehi tanūvaśin //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a apām rasaḥ prathamajo ‘tho vanaspatīnām /

Halfverse: c uta somasya bhrātāsy utārśam asi vṛṣṇyam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a adyāgne adya savitar adya devi sarasvati /

Halfverse: c adyāsya brahmaṇas pate dhanur ivā tānayā pasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a āham tanomi te paso adhi jyām iva dhanvani /

Halfverse: c kramasvarśa iva rohitam anavaglāyatā sadā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a aśvasyāśvatarasyājasya petvasya ca /

Halfverse: c atha ṛṣabhasya ye vājās tān asmin dhehi tanūvaśin //8//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho yaḥ samudrād udācarat /

Halfverse: c tenā sahasyṝnā vayam ni janānt svāpayāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na bhūmim vāto ati vāti nāti paśyati kaś cana /

Halfverse: c striyaś ca sarvāḥ svāpaya śunaś cendrasakhā caran //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a proṣṭheśayās talpeśayā nārīr yā vahyaśīvarīḥ /

Halfverse: c striyo yāḥ puṇyagandhayas tāḥ sarvāḥ svāpayāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ejadejad ajagrabham cakṣuḥ prāṇam ajagrabham /

Halfverse: c aṅgāny ajagrabham sarvā rātrīṇām atiśarvare //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ya āste yaś carati yaś ca tiṣṭhan vipaśyati /

Halfverse: c teṣām sam dadhmo akṣīṇi yathedam harmyam tathā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a svaptu mātā svaptu pitā svaptu śvā svaptu viśpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c svapantv asyai jñātayaḥ svaptv ayam abhito janaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a svapna svapnābhikaraṇena sarvam ni svāpayā janam /

Halfverse: c otsūryam anyānt svāpayāvyuṣam jāgṛtād aham indra ivāriṣṭo akṣitaḥ //7//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a brāhmaṇo jajñe prathamo daśaśīrṣo daśāsyaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa somam prathamaḥ papau sa cakārārasam viṣam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yāvatī dyāvāpṛthivī varimṇā yāvat sapta sindhavo vitaṣṭhire /

Halfverse: c vācam viṣasya dūṣaṇīm tām ito nir avādiṣam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a suparṇas tvā garutmān viṣa prathamam āvayat /

Halfverse: c nāmīmado nārūrupa utāsmā abhavaḥ pituḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yas ta āsyat pañcāṅgurir vakrāc cid adhi dhanvanaḥ /

Halfverse: c apaskambhasya śalyān nir avocam aham viṣam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śalyād viṣam nir avocam prāñjanād uta parṇadheḥ /

Halfverse: c apāṣṭhāc chṛṅgāt kulmalān nir avocam aham viṣam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a arasas ta iṣo śalyo ‘tho te arasam viṣam /

Halfverse: c utārasasya vṛkṣasya dhanuṣ ṭe arasārasam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ye apīṣan ye adihan ya āsyan ye avāsṛjan /

Halfverse: c sarve te vadhrayaḥ kṛtā vadhrir viṣagiriḥ kṛtaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a vadhrayas te khanitāro vadhris tvam asy oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c vadhriḥ sa parvato girir yato jātam idam viṣam //8//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vār idam vārayātai varaṇāvatyām adhi /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasyāsiktam tenā te vāraye viṣam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a arasam prācyam viṣam arasam yad udīcyam /

Halfverse: c athedam adharācyam karambheṇa vi kalpate //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a karambham kṛtvā tiryam pībaspākam udārathim /

Halfverse: c kṣudhā kila tvā duṣṭano jakṣivānt sa na rūrupaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vi te madam madāvati śaram iva pātayāmasi /

Halfverse: c pra tvā carum iva yeṣantam vacasā sthāpayāmasi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pari grāmam ivācitam vacasā sthāpayāmasi /

Halfverse: c tiṣṭhā vṛkṣa iva sthāmny abhrikhāte na rūrupaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pavastais tvā pary akrīṇan dūrśebhir ajinair uta /

Halfverse: c prakrīr asi tvam oṣadhe ‘bhrikhāte na rūrupaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a anāptā ye vaḥ prathamā yāni karmāṇi cakrire /

Halfverse: c vīrān no atra mā dabhan tad va etat puro dadhe //7//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhūto bhūteṣu paya ā dadhāti sa bhūtānām adhipatir babhūva /

Halfverse: c tasya mṛtyuś carati rājasūyam sa rājā anu manyatām idam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a abhi prehi māpa vena ugraś cettā sapatnahā /

Halfverse: c ā tiṣṭha mitravardhana tubhyam devā adhi bruvan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ātiṣṭhantam pari viśve abhūṣam chriyam vasānaś carati svarociḥ /

Halfverse: c mahat tad vṛṣṇo asurasya nāmā viśvarūpo amṛtāni tasthau //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vyāghro adhi vaiyāghre vi kramasva diśo mahīḥ /

Halfverse: c viśas tvā sarvā vāñchantv āpo divyāḥ payasvatīḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yā āpo divyāḥ payasā madanty antarikṣa uta vā pṛthivyām /

Halfverse: c tāsām tvā sarvāsām apām abhi ṣiñcāmi varcasā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a abhi tvā varcasāsicann āpo divyāḥ payasvatīh /

Halfverse: c yathāso mitravardhanas tathā tvā savitā karat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a enā vyāghram pariṣasvajānāḥ simham hinvanti mahate saubhagāya /

Halfverse: c samudram na subhuvas tasthivāmsam marmṛjyante dvīpinam apsv a1ntaḥ //7//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ehi jīvam trāyamāṇam parvatasyāsy akṣyam /

Halfverse: c viśvebhir devair dattam paridhir jīvanāya kam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a paripāṇam puruṣāṇām paripāṇam gavām asi /

Halfverse: c aśvānām arvatām paripāṇāya tasthiṣe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a utāsi paripāṇam yātujambhanam āñjana /

Halfverse: c utāmṛtasya tvam vetthātho asi jīvabhojanam atho haritabheṣajam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yasyāñjana prasarpasy aṅgamaṅgam paruṣparuḥ /

Halfverse: c tato yakṣmam vi bādhasa ugro madhyamaśīr iva //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a nainam prāpnoti śapatho na kṛtyā nābhiśocanam /

Halfverse: c nainam viṣkandham aśnute yas tvā bibharty āñjana //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a asanmantrād duṣvapnyād duṣkṛtāc chamalād uta /

Halfverse: c durhārdaś cakṣuṣo ghorāt tasmān naḥ pāhy āñjana //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a idam vidvān āñjana satyam vakṣyāmi nānṛtam /

Halfverse: c saneyam aśvam gām aham ātmānam tava pūruṣa //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a trayo dāsā āñjanasya takmā balāsa ād ahiḥ /

Halfverse: c varṣiṣṭhaḥ parvatānām trikakun nāma te pitā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yad āñjanam traikakudam jātam himavatas pari /

Halfverse: c yātūmś ca sarvāñ jambhayat sarvāś ca yātudhānyaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yadi vāsi traikakudam yadi yāmunam ucyase /

Halfverse: c ubhe te bhadre nāmnī tābhyām naḥ pāhy āñjana //10//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vātāj jāto antarikṣād vidyuto jyotiṣas pari /

Halfverse: c sa no hiraṇyajāḥ śaṅkhaḥ kṛśanaḥ pātv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo agrato rocanānām samudrād adhi jajñiṣe /

Halfverse: c śaṅkhena hatvā rakṣāmsy attriṇo vi ṣahāmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śaṅkhenāmīvām amatim śaṅkhenota sadānvāḥ /

Halfverse: c śaṅkho no viśvabheṣajaḥ kṛśanaḥ pātv amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a divi jātaḥ samudrajaḥ sindhutas pary ābhṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa no hiraṇyajāḥ śaṅkha āyuṣprataraṇo maṇiḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a samudrāj jāto maṇir vṛtrāj jāto divākaraḥ /

Halfverse: c so asmānt sarvataḥ pātu hetyā devāsurebhyaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a hiraṇyānām eko ‘si somāt tvam adhi jajñiṣe /

Halfverse: c rathe tvam asi darśata iṣudhau rocanas tvam pra ṇa āyūmṣi tāriṣat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a devānām asthi kṛśanam babhūva tad ātmanvac caraty apsv a1ntaḥ /

Halfverse: c tat te badhnāmy āyuṣe varcase balāya dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya kārśanas tvābhi rakṣatu //7//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a anaḍvān dādhāra pṛthivīm uta dyām anaḍvān dādhārorv a1ntarikṣam /

Halfverse: c anaḍvān dādhāra pradiśaḥ ṣaḍ urvīr anaḍvān viśvam bhuvanam ā viveśa //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anaḍvān indraḥ sa paśubhyo vi caṣṭe trayām chakro vi mimīte adhvanaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhūtam bhaviṣyad bhuvanā duhānaḥ sarvā devānām carati vratāni //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indro jāto manuṣyṝṣv antar gharmas taptaś carati śośucānaḥ /

Halfverse: c suprajāḥ sant sa udāre na sarṣad yo nāśnīyād anaḍuho vijānan //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anaḍvān duhe sukṛtasya loka ainam pyāyayati pavamānaḥ purastāt /

Halfverse: c parjanyo dhārā maruta ūdho asya yajñaḥ payo dakṣiṇā doho asya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasya neśe yajñapatir na yajño nāsya dāteśe na pratigrahītā /

Halfverse: c yo viśvajid viśvabhṛd viśvakarmā gharmam no brūta yatamaś catuṣpāt //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yena devāḥ svar āruruhur hitvā śarīram amṛtasya nābhim /

Halfverse: c tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokam gharmasya vratena tapasā yaśasyavaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a indro rūpeṇāgnir vahena prajāpatiḥ parameṣṭhī virāṭ /

Halfverse: c viśvānare akramata vaiśvānare akramatānaduhy akramata /

Halfverse: e so ‘dṛmhayata so ‘dhārayata //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a madhyam etad anaḍuho yatraiṣa vaha āhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c etāvad asya prācīnam yāvān pratyaṅ samāhitaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yo vedānaduho dohān saptānupadasvataḥ /

Halfverse: c prajām ca lokam cāpnoti tathā saptaṛṣayo viduḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a padbhiḥ sedim avakrāmann irām jaṅghābhir utkhidan /

Halfverse: c srameṇānaḍvān kīlālam kīnāśaś cābhi gachataḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a dvādaśa vā etā rātrīr vratyā āhuḥ prajāpateḥ /

Halfverse: c tatropa brahma yo veda tad vā anaḍuho vratam //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a duhe sāyam duhe prātar duhe madhyamdinam pari /

Halfverse: c dohā ye asya samyanti tān vidmānupadasvataḥ //12//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a rohaṇy asi rohaṇy asthnaś chinnasya rohaṇī /

Halfverse: c rohayedam arundhati //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yat te riṣṭam yat te dyuttam asti peṣṭram ta ātmani /

Halfverse: c dhātā tad bhadrayā punaḥ sam dadhat paruṣā paruḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sam te majjā majñā bhavatu sam u te paruṣā paruḥ /

Halfverse: c sam te māmsasya visrastam sam asthy api rohatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a majjā majñā sam dhīyatām carmaṇā carma rohatu /

Halfverse: c asṛk te asthi rohatu māmsam māmsena rohatu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a loma lomnā sam kalpayā tvacā sam kalpayā tvacam /

Halfverse: c asṛk te asthi rohatu chinnam sam dhehy oṣadhe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sa ut tiṣṭha prehi pra drava rathaḥ sucakraḥ /

Halfverse: c supaviḥ sunābhiḥ prati tiṣṭhordhvaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yadi kartam patitvā samśaśre yadi vāsmā prahṛto jaghāna /

Halfverse: c ṛbhū rathasyevāṅgāni sam dadhat paruṣā paruḥ //7//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a uta devā avahitam devā un nayathā punaḥ /

Halfverse: c utāgaś cakruṣam devā devā jīvayathā punaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dvāv imau vātau vāta ā sindhor ā parāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c dakṣam te anya āvātu vy anyo vātu yad rapaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ā vāta vāhi bheṣajam vi vāta vāhi yad rapaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvam hi viśvabheṣaja devānām dūta īyase //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a trāyantām imam devās trāyantām marutām gaṇāḥ /

Halfverse: c trāyantām viśvā bhūtāni yathāyam arapā asat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā tvāgamam śamtātibhir atho ariṣṭatātibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c dakṣam ta ugram ābhāriṣam parā yakṣmam suvāmi te //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ayam me hasto bhagavān ayam me bhagavattaraḥ /

Halfverse: c ayam me viśvabheṣajo ‘yam śivābhimarśanaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a hastābhyām daśaśākhābhyām jihvā vācaḥ purogavī /

Halfverse: c anāmayitnubhyām hastābhyām tābhyām tvābhi mṛśāmasi //7//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ajo hy agner ajaniṣṭa śokāt so apaśyaj janitāram agre /

Halfverse: c tena devā devatām agrā āyan tena rohān ruruhur medhyāsaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kramadhvam agninā nākam ukhyān hasteṣu bibhrataḥ /

Halfverse: c divas pṛṣṭham svar gatvā miśrā devebhir ādhvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pṛṣṭhāt pṛthivyā aham antarikṣam āruham antarikṣād divam āruham /

Halfverse: c divo nākasya pṛṣṭhāt sva1r jyotir agām aham //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sva1r yanto nāpekṣanta ā dyām rohanti rodasī /

Halfverse: c yajñam ye viśvatodhāram suvidvāmso vitenire //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a agne prehi prathamo devatānām cakṣur devānām uta mānuṣānām /

Halfverse: c iyakṣamāṇā bhṛgubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ svar yantu yajamānāḥ svasti //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ajam anajmi payasā ghṛtena divyam suparnam payasam bṛhantam /

Halfverse: c tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokam svar ārohanto abhi nākam uttamam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a pañcaudanam pañcabhir aṅgulibhir darvyoddhara pañcadhaitam odanam /

Halfverse: c prācyām diśi śiro ajasya dhehi dakṣiṇāyām diśi dakṣiṇam dhehi pārśvam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a pratīcyām diśi bhasadam asya dhehy uttarasyām diśy uttaram dhehi pārśvam /

Halfverse: c ūrdhvāyām diśy a1jasyānūkam dhehi diśi dhruvāyām dhehi pājasyam antarikṣe madhyato madhyam asya //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a śṛtam ajam śṛtayā prorṇuhi tvacā sarvair aṅgaiḥ sambhṛtam viśvarūpam /

Halfverse: c sa ut tisṭheto abhi nākam uttamam padbhiś caturbhiḥ prati tiṣṭha dikṣu //9//

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samutpatantu pradiśo nabhasvatīḥ sam abhrāṇi vātajūtāni yantu /

Halfverse: c mahaṛṣabhasya nadato nabhasvato vāśrā āpaḥ pṛthivīm tarpayantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam īkṣayantu taviṣāḥ sudānavo ‘pām rasā oṣadhībhiḥ sacantām /

Halfverse: c varṣasya sargā mahayantu bhūmim pṛthag jāyantām oṣadhayo viśvarūpāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sam īkṣayasva gāyato nabhāmsy apām vegāsah pṛthag ud vijantām /

Halfverse: c varṣasya sargā mahayantu bhūmim pṛthag jāyantām vīrudho viśvarūpāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a gaṇās tvopa gāyantu mārutāḥ parjanya ghoṣiṇaḥ pṛthak /

Halfverse: c sargā varṣasya varṣato varṣantu pṛthivīm anu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ud īrayata marutaḥ samudratas tveṣo arko nabha ut pātayātha /

Halfverse: c mahaṛṣabhasya nadato nabhasvato vāśrā āpaḥ pṛthivīm tarpayantu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a abhi kranda stanayārdayodadhim bhūmim parjanya payasā sam aṅdhi /

Halfverse: c tvayā sṛṣṭam bahulam aitu varṣam āśāraiṣī kṛśagur etv astam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sam vo ‘vantu sudānava utsā ajagarā uta /

Halfverse: c marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghā varṣantu pṛthivīm anu //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a āśāmāśām vi dyotatām vātā vāntu diśodiśaḥ /

Halfverse: c marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghāḥ sam yantu pṛthivīm anu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a āpo vidyud abhram varṣam sam vo ‘vantu sudānava utsā ajagarā uta /

Halfverse: c marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghāḥ prāvantu pṛthivīm anu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a apām agnis tanūbhiḥ samvidāno ya oṣadhīnām adhipā babhūva /

Halfverse: c sa no varṣam vanutām jātavedāḥ prāṇam prajābhyo amṛtam divas pari //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a prajāpatiḥ salilād ā samudrād āpa īrayann udadhim ardayāti /

Halfverse: c pra pyāyatām vṛṣṇo aśvasya reto ‘rvān etena stanayitnunehi //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a apo niṣiñcann asuraḥ pitā naḥ śvasantu gargarā apām varuṇāva nīcīr apaḥ sṛja /

Halfverse: c vadantu pṛśnibāhavo maṇḍūkā iriṇānu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a samvatsaram śaśayānā brāhmaṇā vratacāriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c vācam parjanyajinvitām pra maṇdūkā avādiṣuḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a upapravada maṇḍūki varṣam ā vada tāduri /

Halfverse: c madhye hradasya plavasva vigṛhya caturaḥ padaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a khaṇvakhā3i khaimakhā3i madhye taduri /

Halfverse: c varṣam vanudhvam pitaro marutām mana ichata //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a mahāntam kośam ud acābhi ṣiñca savidyutam bhavatu vātu vātaḥ /

Halfverse: c tanvatām yajñam bahudhā visṛṣṭā ānandinīr oṣadhayo bhavantu //16//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bṛhan eṣām adhiṣṭhātā antikād iva paśyati /

Halfverse: c ya stāyan manyate carant sarvam devā idam viduḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yas tiṣṭhati carati yaś ca vañcati yo nilāyam carati yaḥ prataṅkam /

Halfverse: c dvau samniṣadya yan mantrayete rājā tad veda varuṇas tṛtīyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a uteyam bhūmir varuṇasya rājña utāsau dyaur bṛhatī dūreantā /

Halfverse: c uto samudrau varuṇasya kukṣī utāsminn alpa udake nilīnaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a uta yo dyām atisarpāt parastān na sa mucyātai varuṇasya rājñaḥ /

Halfverse: c diva spaśaḥ pra carantīdam asya sahasrākṣā ati paśyanti bhūmim //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sarvam tad rājā varuṇo vi caṣṭe yad antarā rodasī yat parastāt /

Halfverse: c samkhyātā asya nimiṣo janānām akṣān iva śvaghnī ni minoti tāni //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ye te pāśā varuṇa saptasapta tredhā tiṣṭhanti viṣitā ruṣantaḥ /

Halfverse: c chinantu sarve anṛtam vadantam yaḥ satyavādy ati tam sṛjantu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śatena pāśair abhi dhehi varuṇainam mā te mocy anṛtavāṅ nṛcakṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c āstām jālma udaram śramśayitvā kośa ivābandhaḥ parikṛtyamānaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yaḥ samābhyo3 varuṇo yo vyābhyo3 yaḥ samdeśyo3 varuṇo yo videśyo /

Halfverse: c yo daivo varuṇo yaś ca mānuṣaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tais tvā sarvair abhi ṣyāmi pāśair asāv āmuṣyāyaṇāmuṣyāḥ putra /

Halfverse: c tān u te sarvān anusamdiśāmi //9//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a īśāṇām tvā bheṣajānām ujjeṣa ā rabhāmahe /

Halfverse: c cakre sahasravīryam sarvasmā oṣadhe tvā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a satyajitam śapathayāvanīm sahamānām punaḥsarām /

Halfverse: c sarvāḥ sam ahvy oṣadhīr ito naḥ pārayād iti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yā śaśāpa śapanena yāgham mūram ādadhe /

Halfverse: c yā rasasya haraṇāya jātam ārebhe tokam attu sā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yām te cakrur āme pātre yām cakrur nīlalohite /

Halfverse: c āme māmse kṛtyām yām cakrus tayā kṛtyākṛto jahi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dauṣvapnyam daurjīvityam rakṣo abhvam arāyyaḥ /

Halfverse: c durṇāmnīḥ sarvā durvācas tā asman nāśayāmasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kṣudhāmāram tṛṣṇāmāram agotām anapatyatām /

Halfverse: c apāmārga tvayā vayam sarvam tad apa mṛjmahe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tṛṣṇāmāram kṣudhāmāram atho akṣaparājayam /

Halfverse: c apāmārga tvayā vayam sarvam tad apa mṛjmahe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a apāmārga oṣadhīnām sarvāsām eka id vaśī /

Halfverse: c tena te mṛjma āsthitam atha tvam agadaś cara //8//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samam jyotiḥ sūryeṇāhnā rātrī samāvatī /

Halfverse: c kṛṇomi satyam ūtaye ‘rasāḥ santu kṛtvarīḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo devāḥ kṛtyām kṛtvā harād aviduṣo gṛham /

Halfverse: c vatso dhārur iva mātaram tam pratyag upa padyatām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a amā kṛtvā pāpmānam yas tenānyam jighāmsati /

Halfverse: c aśmānas tasyām dagdhāyām bahulāḥ phaṭ karikrati //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sahasradhāman viśikhān vigrīvām chāyayā tvam /

Halfverse: c prati sma cakruṣe kṛtyām priyām priyāvate hara //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a anayāham oṣadhyā sarvāḥ kṛtyā adūduṣam /

Halfverse: c yām kṣetre cakrur yām goṣu yām vā te puruṣeṣu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yaś cakāra na śaśāka kartum śaśre pādam aṅgurim /

Halfverse: c cakāra bhadram asmabhyam ātmane tapanam tu saḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a apāmārgo ‘pa mārṣṭu kṣetriyam śapathaś ca yaḥ /

Halfverse: c apāha yātudhānīr apa sarvā arāyyaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a apamṛjya yātudhānān apa sarvā arāyyaḥ /

Halfverse: c apāmārga tvayā vayam sarvam tad apa mṛjmahe //8//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a uto asy abandhukṛd uto asi nu jāmikṛt /

Halfverse: c uto kṛtyākṛtaḥ prajām nadam ivā chindhi vārṣikam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a brāhmaṇena paryuktāsi kaṇvena nārṣadena /

Halfverse: c senevaiṣi tviṣīmatī na tatra bhayam asti yatra prāpnoṣy oṣadhe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a agram eṣy oṣadhīnām jyotiṣevābhidīpayan /

Halfverse: c uta trātāsi pākasyātho hantāsi rakṣasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad ado devā asurāms tvayāgre nirakurvata /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam adhy oṣadhe ‘pāmārgo ajāyathāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vibhindatī śataśākhā vibhindan nāma te pitā /

Halfverse: c pratyag vi bhindhi tvam tam yo asmām abhidāsati //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a asad bhūmyāḥ sam abhavat tad yām eti mahad vyacaḥ /

Halfverse: c tad vai tato vidhūpāyat pratyak kartāram ṛchatu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a pratyaṅ hi sambabhūvitha pratīcīnaphalas tvam /

Halfverse: c sarvān mac chapathām adhi varīyo yāvayā vadham //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a śatena mā pari pāhi sahasreṇābhi rakṣā mā /

Halfverse: c indras te vīrudhām pata ugra ojmānam ā dadhat //8//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā paśyati prati paśyati parā paśyati paśyati /

Halfverse: c divam antarikṣam ād bhūmim sarvam tad devi paśyati //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tisro divas tisraḥ pṛthivīḥ ṣaṭ cemāḥ pradiśāḥ pṛthak /

Halfverse: c tvayāham sarvā bhūtāni paśyāni devy oṣadhe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a divyasya suparṇasya tasya hāsi kanīnikā /

Halfverse: c sā bhūmim ā rurohitha vahyam śrāntā vadhūr iva //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tām me sahasrākṣo devo dakṣiṇe hasta ā dadhat /

Halfverse: c tayāham sarvam paśyāmi yaś ca śūdra utāryaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a āviṣ kṛṇuṣva rūpāni mātmānam apa gūhathāḥ /

Halfverse: c atho sahasracakṣo tvam prati paśyāḥ kimīdinaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a darśaya mā yātudhānān darśaya yātudhānyaḥ /

Halfverse: c piśācānt sarvān darśayeti tvā rabha oṣadhe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a kaśyapasya cakṣur asi śunyāś ca caturakṣyāḥ /

Halfverse: c vīdhre sūryam iva sarpantam mā piśācam tiras karaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ud agrabham paripāṇād yātudhānam kimīdinam /

Halfverse: c tenāham sarvam paśyāmy uta śūdram utāryam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yo antarikṣeṇa patati divam yaś ca atisarpati /

Halfverse: c bhūmim yo manyate nātham tam piśācam pra darśaya //9//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā gāvo agmann uta bhadram akrant sīdantu goṣṭhe raṇayantv asme /

Halfverse: c prajāvatīḥ pururūpā iha syur indrāya pūrvīr uṣaso duhānāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indro yajvane gṛṇate ca śikṣata uped dadāti na svam muṣāyati /

Halfverse: c bhūyobhūyo rayim id asya vardhayann abhinne khilye ni dadhāti devayum //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a na tā naśanti na dabhāti taskaro nāsām āmitro vyathir ā dadharṣati /

Halfverse: c devāmś ca yābhir yajate dadāti ca jyog it tābhiḥ sacate gopatiḥ saha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a na tā arvā reṇukakāṭo ‘śnute na samskṛtatram upa yanti tā abhi /

Halfverse: c urugāyam abhayam tasya tā anu gāvo martasya vi caranti yajvanaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a gāvo bhago gāva indro ma ichād gāva somasya prathamasya bhakṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c imā yā gāvaḥ sa janāsa indra ichāmi hṛdā manasā cid indram //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yūyam gāvo medayatha kṛśam cid aśrīram cit kṛṇuthā supratīkam /

Halfverse: c bhadram gṛham kṛṇutha bhadravāco bṛhad vo vaya ucyate sabhāsu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a prajāvatīḥ sūyavase ruśantīḥ śuddhā apaḥ suprapāṇe pibantīḥ /

Halfverse: c mā va stena īśata māghaśamsaḥ pari vo rudrasya hetir vṛṇaktu //7//

Hymn: 22

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imam indra vardhaya kṣatriyam me imam viśām ekavṛṣam kṛṇu tvam /

Halfverse: c nir amitrān akṣṇuhy asya sarvāms tān randhayāsmā ahamuttareṣu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a emam bhaja grāme aśveṣu goṣu niṣ ṭam bhaja yo amitro asya /

Halfverse: c varṣma kṣatrāṇām ayam astu rājendra śatrum randhaya sarvam asmai //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ayam astu dhanapatir dhanānām ayam viśām viśpatir astu rājā /

Halfverse: c asminn indra mahi varcāmsi dhehy avarcasam kṛṇuhi śatrum asya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a asmai dyāvāpṛthivī bhūri vāmam duhāthām gharmadughe iva dhenū /

Halfverse: c ayam rājā priya indrasya bhūyāt priyo gavām oṣadhīnām paśūnām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yunajmi ta uttarāvantam indram yena jayanti na parājayante /

Halfverse: c yas tvā karad ekavṛṣam janānām uta rājñām uttamam mānavānām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a uttaras tvam adhare te sapatnā ye ke ca rājan pratiśatravas te /

Halfverse: c ekavṛṣa indrasakhā jigīvām chatrūyatām ā bharā bhojanāni //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a simhapratīko viśo addhi sarvā vyāghrapratīko ‘va bādhasva śatrūn /

Halfverse: c ekavṛṣa indrasakhā jigīvām chatrūyatām ā khidā bhojanāni //7//

Hymn: 23

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agner manve prathamasya pracetasaḥ pāñcajanyasya bahudhā yam indhate /

Halfverse: c viśoviśaḥ praviśivāmsam īmahe sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathā havyam vahasi jātavedo yathā yajñam kalpayasi prajānan /

Halfverse: c evā devebhyaḥ sumatim na ā vaha sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yāmanyāmann upayuktam vahiṣṭham karmaṅkarmann ābhagam agnim īḍe /

Halfverse: c rakṣohaṇam yajñavṛdham ghṛtāhutam sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sujātam jātavedasam agnim vaiśvānaram vibhum /

Halfverse: c havyavāham havāmahe sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yena ṛṣayo balam adyotayan yujā yenāsurāṇām ayuvanta māyāḥ /

Halfverse: c yenāgninā paṇīn indro jigāya sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yena devā amṛtam anvavindan yenauṣadhīr madhumatīr akṛṇvan /

Halfverse: c yena devāḥ sva1r ābharant sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yasyedam pradiśi yad virocate yaj jātam janitavyam ca kevalam /

Halfverse: c staumy agnim nāthito johavīmi sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //7//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrasya manmahe śaśvad id asya manmahe vṛtraghna stomā upa mema āguḥ /

Halfverse: c yo dāśuṣaḥ sukṛto havam eti sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ya ugrīṇām ugrabāhur yayur yo dānavānām balam āruroja /

Halfverse: c yena jitāḥ sindhavo yena gāvaḥ sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaś carṣaṇipro vṛṣabhaḥ svarvid yasmai grāvāṇaḥ pravadanti nṛmṇam /

Halfverse: c yasyādhvaraḥ saptahotā madiṣṭhaḥ sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yasya vaśāsa ṛṣabhāsa ukṣaṇo yasmai mīyante svaravaḥ svarvide /

Halfverse: c yasmai śukraḥ pavate brahmaśumbhitaḥ sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasya juṣṭim sominaḥ kāmayante yam havanta iṣumantam gaviṣṭau /

Halfverse: c yasminn arkaḥ śiśriye yasminn ojaḥ sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yaḥ prathamaḥ karmakṛtyāya jajñe yasya vīryam prathamasyānubuddham /

Halfverse: c yenodyato vajro ‘bhyāyatāhim sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yaḥ samgrāmān nayati sam yudhe vaśī yaḥ puṣṭāni samsṛjati dūyāni /

Halfverse: c staumīndram nāthito johavīmi sa no muñcatv amhasaḥ //7//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vāyoḥ savitur vidathāni manmahe yāv ātmanvad viśatho yau ca rakṣathaḥ /

Halfverse: c yau viśvasya paribhū babhūvathus tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yayoḥ samkhyātā varimā pārhivāni yābhyām rajo yupitam antarikṣe /

Halfverse: c yayoḥ prāyam nānv ānaśe kaś cana tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tava vrate ni viśante janāsas tvayy udite prerate citrabhāno /

Halfverse: c yuvam vāyo savitā ca bhuvanāni rakṣathas tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a apeto vāyo savitā ca duṣkṛtam apa rakṣāmsi śimidām ca sedhatam /

Halfverse: c sam hy ūrjayā sṛjathaḥ sam balena tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a rayim me poṣam savitota vāyus tanū dakṣam ā suvatām suśevam /

Halfverse: c ayakṣmatātim maha iha dhattam tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pra sumatim savitar vāya ūtaye mahasvantam matsaram mādayāthaḥ /

Halfverse: c arvāg vāmasya pravato ni yachatam tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a upa śreṣṭhā na āśiṣo devayor dhāmann asthiran /

Halfverse: c staumi devam savitāram ca vāyum tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //7//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a manve vām dyāvāpṛthivī subhojasau sacetasau ye aprathethām amitā yojanāni /

Halfverse: c pratiṣṭhe hy abhavatam vasūnām te no muñcatam amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pratiṣṭhe hy abhavatam vasūnām pravṛddhe devī subhage urūcī /

Halfverse: c dyāvāpṛthivī bhavatam me syone te no muñcatam amhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asantāpe sutapasau huve ‘ham urvī gambhīre kavibhir namasyṝ /

Halfverse: c dyāvāpṛthivī bhavatam me syone te no muñcatam amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ye amṛtam bibhṛtho ye havīmṣi ye srotyā bibhṛtho ye manuṣyān /

Halfverse: c dyāvāpṛthivī bhavatam me syone te no muñcatam amhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye usriyā bibhṛtho ye vanaspatīn yayor vām viśvā bhuvanāny antaḥ /

Halfverse: c dyāvāpṛthivī bhavatam me syone te no muñcatam amhasaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ye kīlālena tarpayatho ye ghṛtena yābhyām ṛte na kim cana śaknuvanti /

Halfverse: c dyāvāpṛthivī bhavatam me syone te no muñcatam amhasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yan medam abhiśocati yenayena vā kṛtam pauruṣeyān na daivāt /

Halfverse: c staumi dyāvāpṛthivī nāthito johavīmi te no muñcatam amhasaḥ //7//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a marutām manve adhi me bruvantu premam vājam vājasāte avantu /

Halfverse: c āśūn iva suyamān ahva ūtaye te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a utsam akṣitam vyacanti ye sadā ya āsiñcanti rasam oṣadhīṣu /

Halfverse: c puro dadhe marutaḥ pṛśnimātṝms te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a payo dhenūnām rasam oṣadhīnām javam arvatām kavayo ya invatha /

Halfverse: c śagmā bhavantu maruto naḥ syonās te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a apaḥ samudrād divam ud vahanti divas pṛthivīm abhi ye sṛjanti /

Halfverse: c ye adbhir īśānā marutaś caranti te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye kīlālena tarpayanti ye ghṛtena ye vā vayo medasā samsṛjanti /

Halfverse: c ye adbhir īśānā maruto varṣayanti te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yadīd idam maruto mārutena yadi devā daivyenedṛg āra /

Halfverse: c yūyam īśidhve vasavas tasya niṣkṛtes te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tigmam anīkam viditam sahasvan mārutam śardhaḥ pṛtanāsūgram /

Halfverse: c staumi maruto nāthito johavīmi te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //7//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhavāśarvau manve vām tasya vittam yayor vām idam pradiśi yad virocate /

Halfverse: c yāv asyeśāthe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yayor abhyabhva uta yad dūre cid yau viditāv iṣubhṛtām asiṣṭhau /

Halfverse: c yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sahasrākṣau vṛtrahanā huveham dūregavyūtī stuvann emy ugrau /

Halfverse: c yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yāv ārebhāthe bahu sākam agre pra ced asrāṣṭram abhibhām janeṣu /

Halfverse: c yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yayor vadhān nāpapadyate kaś canāntar deveṣūta mānuṣeṣu /

Halfverse: c yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yaḥ kṛtyākṛn mūlakṛd yātudhāno ni tasmin dhattam vajram ugrau /

Halfverse: c yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a adhi no brūtam pṛtanāsūgrau sam vajreṇa sṛjatam yaḥ kimīdī /

Halfverse: c staumi bhavāśarvau nāthito johavīmi tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //7//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a manve vām mitrāvaruṇāv ṛtāvṛdhau sacetasau druhvaṇo yau nudethe /

Halfverse: c pra satyāvānam avatho bhareṣu tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sacetasau druhvaṇo yau nudethe pra satyāvānam avatho bhareṣu /

Halfverse: c yau gachatho nṛcakṣasau babhruṇā sutam tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yāv aṅgirasam avatho yāv agastim mitrāvaruṇā jamadagnim attrim /

Halfverse: c yau kaśyapam avatho yau vasiṣṭham tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yau śyāvāśvam avatho vādhryaśvam mitrāvaruṇā purumīḍham attrim /

Halfverse: c yau vimadam avatho saptavadhrim tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yau bharadvājam avatho yau gaviṣṭhiram viśvāmitram varuṇa mitra kutsam /

Halfverse: c yau kakṣīvantam avatho prota kaṇvam tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yau medhātithim avatho yau triśokam mitrāvaruṇāv uśanām kāvyam yau /

Halfverse: c yau gotamam avatho prota mugdalam tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yayo rathaḥ satyavartma rjuraśmir mithuyā carantam abhiyāti dūṣayan /

Halfverse: c staumi mitrāvaruṇau nāthito johavīmi tau no muñcatam amhasaḥ //7//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aham rudrebhir vasubhiś carāmy aham ādityair uta viśvadevaiḥ /

Halfverse: c aham mitrāvarunobhā bibharmy aham indrāgnī aham aśvinobhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aham rāṣṭrī samgamanī vasūnām cikituṣī prathamā yajñiyānām /

Halfverse: c tām mā devā vy adadhuḥ purutrā bhūristhātrām bhūry āveśayantaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a aham eva svayam idam vadāmi juṣṭam devānām uta mānuṣāṇām /

Halfverse: c yam kāmaye tantam ugram kṛṇomi tam brahmāṇam tam ṛṣim tam sumedhām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a mayā so ‘nnam atti yo vipaśyati yaḥ prāṇati ya īm śṛṇoty uktam /

Halfverse: c amantavo mām ta upa kṣiyanti śrudhi śruta śruddheyam te vadāmi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a aham rudrāya dhanur ā tanomi brahmadviṣe śarave hantavā u /

Halfverse: c aham janāya samadam kṛṇomi aham dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a aham somam āhanasam bibharmy aham tvaṣṭāram uta pūṣaṇam bhagam /

Halfverse: c aham dadhāmi draviṇā haviṣmate suprāvyā3 yajamānāya sunvate //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a aham suve pitaram asya mūrdhan mama yonir apsv a1ntaḥ samudre /

Halfverse: c tato vi tiṣṭhe bhuvanāni viśvotāmūm dyām varṣmaṇopa spṛśāmi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a aham eva vātaiva pra vāmy ārabhamāṇā bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c paro divā para enā pṛthivyaitāvatī mahimnā sam babhūva //8//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvayā manyo saratham ārujanto harṣamāṇā hṛṣitāso marutvan /

Halfverse: c tigmeṣava āyudhā samśiśānā upa pra yantu naro agnirūpāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agnir iva manyo tviṣitaḥ sahasva senānīr naḥ sahure hūta edhi /

Halfverse: c hatvāya śatrūn vi bhajasva veda ojo mimāno vi mṛdho nudasva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sahasva manyo abhimātim asmai rujan mṛṇan pramṛṇan prehi śatrūn /

Halfverse: c ugram te pājo nanv ā rurudhre vaśī vaśam nayāsā ekaja tvam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a eko bahūnām asi manya īḍitā viśamviśam yuddhāya sam śiśādhi /

Halfverse: c akṛttaruk tvayā yujā vayam dyumantam ghoṣam vijayāya kṛṇmasi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vijeṣakṛd indra ivānavabravo ‘smākam manyo adhipā bhaveha /

Halfverse: c priyam te nāma sahure gṛṇīmasi vidmā tam utsam yata ābabhūtha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ābhūtyā sahajā vajra sāyaka saho bibharṣi sahabhūte uttaram /

Halfverse: c kratvā no manyo saha medy ṝdhi mahādhanasya puruhūta samsṛji //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a samsṛṣṭam dhanam ubhayam samākṛtam asmabhyam dhattām varuṇaś ca manyuḥ /

Halfverse: c bhiyo dadhānā hṛdayeṣu śatravah parājitāso apa ni layantām //7//

Hymn: 32

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yas te manyo ‘vidhad vajra sāyaka saha ojaḥ puṣyati viśvam ānuṣak /

Halfverse: c sāhyāma dāsam āryam tvayā yujā vayam sahaskṛtena sahasā sahasvatā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a manyur indro manyur evāsa devo manyur hotā varuṇo jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c manyum viśa īḍate mānuṣīr yāḥ pahi no manyo tapasā sajoṣāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a abhīhi manyo tavasas tavīyān tapasā yujā vi jahi śatrūn /

Halfverse: c amitrahā vṛtrahā dasyuhā ca viśvā vasūny ā bharā tvam naḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tvam hi manyo abhibhūtyojāḥ svayambhūr bhāmo abhimātiṣāhaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvacarṣaṇiḥ sahuriḥ sahīyān asmāsv ojaḥ pṛtanāsu dhehi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a abhāgaḥ sann apa pareto asmi tava kratvā taviṣasya pracetaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam tvā manyo akratur jihīḍāham svā tanūr baladāvā na ehi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ayam te asmy upa na ehy arvāṅ pratīcīnaḥ sahure viśvadāvan /

Halfverse: c manyo vajrinn abhi na ā vavṛtsva hanāva dasyūmr uta bodhy āpeḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a abhi prehi dakṣiṇato bhavā no ‘dhā vṛtrāṇi jaṅghanāva bhūri /

Halfverse: c juhomi te dharuṇam madhvo agram ubhāv upāmśu prathamā pibāva //7//

Hymn: 33

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apa naḥ śośucad agham agne śuśugdhy ā rayim /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sukṣetriyā sugātuyā vasūyā ca yajāmahe /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pra yad bhandiṣṭha eṣām prāsmākāsaś ca sūrayaḥ /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pra yat te agne sūrayo jāyemahi pra te vayam /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pra yad agneḥ sahasvato viśvato yanti bhānavaḥ /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tvam hi viśvatomukha viśvataḥ paribhūr asi /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a dviṣo no viśvatomukhāti nāveva pāraya /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a sa naḥ sindhum iva nāvāti parṣa svastaye /

Halfverse: c apa naḥ śośucad agham //8//

Hymn: 34

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a brahmāsya śīrṣam bṛhad asya pṛṣṭham vāmadevyam udaram odanasya /

Halfverse: c chandāmsi pakṣau mukham asya satyam viṣṭārī jātas tapaso’dhi yajñaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anasthāḥ pūtāḥ pavanena śuddhāḥ śucayaḥ śucim api yanti lokam /

Halfverse: c naiṣām śiśnam pra dahati jātavedāḥ svarge loke bahu straiṇam eṣām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a viṣṭāriṇam odanam ye pacanti nainān avartiḥ sacate kadā cana /

Halfverse: c āste yama upa yāti devānt sam gandharvair madate somyebhiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a viṣṭāriṇam odanam ye pacanti nainān yamaḥ pari muṣṇāti retaḥ /

Halfverse: c rathī ha bhūtvā rathayāna īyate pakṣī ha bhūtvāti divaḥ sam eti //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a eṣa yajñānām vitato vahiṣṭho viṣṭāriṇam paktvā divam ā viveśa /

Halfverse: c āṇḍīkam kumudam sam tanoti bisam śālūkam śaphako mulālī /

Halfverse: e etās tvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumat pinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ghṛtahradā madhukūlāḥ surodakāḥ kṣīreṇa pūrṇā udakena dadhnā /

Halfverse: c etās tvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumat pinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a caturaḥ kumbhāmś caturdhā dadāmi kṣīreṇa pūrnāṁ udakena dadhnā /

Halfverse: c etās tvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumat pinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a imam odanam ni dadhe brāhmaṇeṣu viṣṭāriṇam lokajitam svargam /

Halfverse: c sa me mā kṣeṣṭa svadhayā pinvamāno viśvarūpā dhenuḥ kāmadughā me astu //8//

Hymn: 35

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yam odanam prathamajā ṛtasya prajāpatis tapasā brahmaṇe ‘pacat /

Halfverse: c yo lokānām vidhṛtir nābhireṣāt tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yenātaran bhūtakṛto ‘ti mṛtyum yam anvavindan tapasā śrameṇa /

Halfverse: c yam papāca brahmaṇe brahma pūrvam tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo dādhāra pṛthivīm viśvabhojasam yo antarikṣam āpṛṇād rasena /

Halfverse: c yo astabhnād divam ūrdhvo mahimnā tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yasmān māsā nirmitās trimśadarāḥ samvatsaro yasmān nirmito dvādaśāraḥ /

Halfverse: c ahorātrā yam pariyanto nāpus tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yaḥ prāṇadaḥ prāṇadavān babhūva yasmai lokā ghṛtavantaḥ kṣaranti /

Halfverse: c jyotiṣmatīḥ pradiśo yasya sarvās tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yasmāt pakvād amṛtam sambabhūva yo gāyatryā adhipatir babhūva /

Halfverse: c yasmin vedā nihitā viśvarūpās tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ava bādhe dviṣantam devapīyum sapatnā ye me ‘pa te bhavantu /

Halfverse: c brahmaudanam viśvajitam pacāmi śṛṇvantu me śraddadhānasya devāḥ //7//

Hymn: 36

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tānt satyaujāḥ pra dahatv agnir vaiśvānaro vṛṣā /

Halfverse: c yo no durasyād dipsāc cātho yo no arātiyāt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo no dipsād adipsato dipsato yaś ca dipsati /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānarasya damṣṭrayor agner api dadhāmi tam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ya āgare mṛgayante pratikrośe ‘māvāsyṝ /

Halfverse: c kravyādo anyān dipsataḥ sarvāms tānt sahasā sahe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sahe piśācānt sahasaiṣām draviṇam dade /

Halfverse: c sarvān durasyato hanmi sam ma ākūtir ṛdyatām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye devās tena hāsante sūryeṇa mimate javam /

Halfverse: c nadīṣu parvateṣu ye sam taiḥ paśubhir vide //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tapano asmi piśācānām vyāghro gomatām iva /

Halfverse: c śvānaḥ simham iva dṛṣṭvā te na vindante nyañcanam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a na piśācaiḥ sam śaknomi na stenaiḥ na vanargubhiḥ /

Halfverse: c piśācās tasmān naśyanti yam aham grāmam āviśe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yam grāmam āviśata idam ugram saho mama /

Halfverse: c piśācās tasmān naśyanti na pāpam upa jānate //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ye mā krodhayanti lapitā hastinam maśakā iva /

Halfverse: c tān aham manye durhitān jane alpaśayūn iva //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a abhi tam nirṛtir dhattām aśvam iva aśvābhidhānyā /

Halfverse: c malvo yo mahyam krudhyati sa u pāśān na mucyate //10//

Hymn: 37

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvayā pūrvam atharvāṇo jaghnū rakṣāmsy oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c tvayā jaghāna kaśyapas tvayā kaṇvo agastyaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvayā vayam apsaraso gandharvāms cātayāmahe /

Halfverse: c ajaśṛṅgy aja rakṣaḥ sarvān gandhena nāśaya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nadīm yantv apsaraso ‘pām tāram avaśvasam /

Halfverse: c gulgulūḥ pīlā nalady aḥ3kṣagandhiḥ pramandanī /

Halfverse: e tat paretāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yatrāśvatthā nyagrodhā mahāvṛkṣāḥ śikhaṇḍinaḥ /

Halfverse: c tat paretāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yatra vaḥ preṅkhā haritā arjunā uta yatrāghātāḥ karkaryaḥ samvadanti /

Halfverse: c tat paretāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a eyam agann oṣadhīnām vīrudhām vīryāvatī /

Halfverse: c ajaśṛṅgy arāṭakī tīkṣṇaśṛṅgī vyṛṣatu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ānṛtyataḥ śikhaṇḍino gandharvasyāpsarāpateḥ /

Halfverse: c bhinadmi muṣkāv api yāmi śepaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bhīmā indrasya hetayaḥ śatam ṛṣṭīr ayasmayīḥ /

Halfverse: c tābhir haviradān gandharvān avakādān vyṛṣatu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a bhīmā indrasya hetayaḥ śatam ṛṣṭīr hiraṇyayīḥ /

Halfverse: c tābhir haviradān gandharvān avakādān vyṛṣatu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a avakādān abhiśocān apsu jyotaya māmakān /

Halfverse: c piśācānt sarvān oṣadhe pra mṛṇīhi sahasva ca //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a śvevaikaḥ kapir ivaikaḥ kumāraḥ sarvakeśakaḥ /

Halfverse: c priyo dṛśa iva bhūtvā gandharvaḥ sacate striyas /

Halfverse: e tam ito nāśayāmasi brahmaṇā vīryāvatā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a jāyā id vo apsaraso gandharvāḥ patayo yuyam /

Halfverse: c apa dhāvatāmartyā martyān mā sacadhvam //12//

Hymn: 38

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a udbhindatīm samjayantīm apsarām sādhudevinīm /

Halfverse: c glahe kṛtāni kṛṇvānām apsarām tām iha huve //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vicinvatīm ākirantīm apsarām sādhudevinīm /

Halfverse: c glahe kṛtāni gṛhṇānām apsarām tām iha huve //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yāyaiḥ parinṛtyaty ādadānā kṛtam glahāt /

Halfverse: c sā naḥ kṛtāni sīṣatī prahām āpnotu māyayā /

Halfverse: e sā naḥ payasvaty aitu mā no jaiṣur idam dhanam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yā akṣeṣu pramodante śucam krodham ca bibhratī /

Halfverse: c ānandinīm pramodinīm apsarām tām iha huve //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sūryasya raśmīn anu yāḥ sañcaranti marīcīr vā yā anusañcaranti /

Halfverse: c yāsām ṛṣabho dūrato vājinīvānt sadyaḥ sarvān lokān paryeti rakṣan /

Halfverse: e sa na aitu homam imam jusāṇo3 ‘ntarikṣeṇa saha vājinīvān //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a antarikṣena saha vājinīvan karkīm vatsām iha rakṣa vājin /

Halfverse: c ime te stokā bahulā ehy arvāṅ iyam te karkīha te mano ‘stu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a antarikṣeṇa saha vājinīvan karkīm vatsām iha rakṣa vājin /

Halfverse: c ayam ghāso ayam vraja iha vatsām ni badhnīmaḥ /

Halfverse: e yathānāma va īśmahe svāhā //7//

Hymn: 39

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pṛthivyām agnaye sam anamant sa ārdhnot /

Halfverse: c yathā pṛthivyām agnaye samanamann evā mahyam samnamaḥ sam namantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pṛthivī dhenus tasyā agnir vatsaḥ /

Halfverse: c sā me ‘gninā vatseneṣam ūrjam kāmam duhām /

Halfverse: e āyuḥ prathamam prajām poṣam rayim svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a antarikṣe vāyave sam anamant sa ārdhnot /

Halfverse: c yathāntarikṣe vāyave samanamann evā mahyam samnamaḥ sam namantu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a antarikṣam dhenus tasyā vatsaḥ /

Halfverse: c sā me vāyunā vatseneṣam ūrjam kāmam duhām /

Halfverse: e āyuḥ prathamam prajām poṣam rayim svāhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a divyādityāya sam anamant sa ārdhnot /

Halfverse: c yathā divyādityāya samanamann evā mahyam samnamaḥ sam namantu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a dyaur dhenus tasyā ādityo vatsaḥ /

Halfverse: c sā ma ādityena vatseneṣam ūrjam kāmam duhām /

Halfverse: e āyuḥ prathamam prajām poṣam rayim svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a dikṣu candrāya sam anamant sa ārdhnot /

Halfverse: c yathā dikṣu candrāya samanamann evā mahyam samnamaḥ sam namantu //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a diśo dhenavas tāsām candro vatsaḥ /

Halfverse: c tā me candreṇa vatseneṣam ūrjam kāmam duhām āyuḥ prathamam prajām posam rayim svāhā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a agnāv agniś carati praviṣṭa ṛṣīṇām putro abhiśastipā u /

Halfverse: c namaskāreṇa namasā te juhomi mā devānām mithuyā karma bhāgam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a hṛdā pūtam manasā jātavedo viśvāni deva vayunāni vidvān /

Halfverse: c saptāsyāni tava jātavedas tebhyo juhomi sa juṣasva havyam //10//

Hymn: 40

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye purastāj juhvati jātavedaḥ prācyā diśo ‘bhidāsanty asmān /

Halfverse: c agnim ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ye dakṣiṇato juhvati jātavedo dakṣiṇāyā diśo ‘bhidāsanty asmān /

Halfverse: c yamam ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enā pratisareṇa hanmi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye paścāj juhvati jātavedaḥ pratīcyā diśo ‘bhidāsanty asmān /

Halfverse: c varuṇam ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ya uttarato juhvati jātaveda udīcyā diśo ‘bhidāsanty asmān /

Halfverse: c somam ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye ‘dhastāj juhvati jātaveda udīcyā diśo ‘bhidāsanty asmān /

Halfverse: c bhūmim ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yṝ3 ‘ntarikṣāj juhvati jātavedo vyadhvāyā diśo ‘bhidāsanty asmān /

Halfverse: c vāyum ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ya upariṣṭāj juhvati jātaveda ūrdhvāyā diśo ‘bhidāsanty asmān /

Halfverse: c sūryam ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ye diśām antardeśebhyo juhvati jātavedaḥ sarvābhyo digbhyo ‘bhidāsanti asmān /

Halfverse: c brahma rtvā te parāñco vyathantām pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi //8//

## Book: 5

5

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ṛdhaṅmantro yonim ya ābabhūvamṛtāsur vardhamānaḥ sujanmā /

Halfverse: c adabdhāsur bhrājamāno ‘heva trito dhartā dādhāra trīṇi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā yo dharmāṇi prathamaḥ sasāda tato vapūmṣi kṛṇuṣe purūṇi /

Halfverse: c dhāsyur yonim prathama ā viveśā yo vācam anuditām ciketa //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yas te śokāya tanvam rireca kṣarad dhiraṇyam śucayo ‘nu svāḥ /

Halfverse: c atrā dadhete amṛtāni nāmāsme vastrāṇi viśa erayantām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pra yad ete prataram pūrvyam guḥ sadaḥsada ātiṣṭhanto ajuryam /

Halfverse: c kaviḥ śuṣasya mātarā rihāṇe jāmyai dhuryam patim erayethām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tad ū ṣu te mahat pṛthujman namaḥ kaviḥ kāvyena kṛṇomi /

Halfverse: c yat samyañcāv abhiyantāv abhi kṣām atrā mahī rodhacakre vāvṛdhete //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sapta maryādāḥ kavayas tatakṣus tāsām id ekām abhy amhuro gāt /

Halfverse: c āyor ha skambha upamasya nīḍe pathām visarge dharuṇeṣu tasthau //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a utāmṛtāsur vrata emi kṛnvann asur ātmā tanva1s tat sumadguḥ /

Halfverse: c uta vā śakro ratnam dadhāty ūrjayā vā yat sacate havirdāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a uta putraḥ pitaram kṣatram īḍe jyeṣṭham maryādam ahvayant svastaye /

Halfverse: c darśan nu tā varuṇa yās te viṣṭhā āvarvratataḥ kṛṇavo vapūmṣi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ardham ardhena payasā pṛṇakṣy ardhena śuṣma vardhase amura /

Halfverse: c avim vṛdhāma śagmiyam sakhāyam varuṇam putram adityā iṣiram /

Halfverse: e kaviśastāny asmai vapūmṣy avocāma rodasī satyavācā //9//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tad id āsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭham yato yajña ugras tveṣanṛmṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c sadyo jajñāno ni riṇāti śatrūn anu yad enam madanti viśva ūmāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vavṛdhānaḥ śavasā bhūryojāḥ śatrur dāsāya bhiyasam dadhāti /

Halfverse: c avyanac ca vyanac ca sasni sam te navanta prabhṛtā madeṣu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tve kratum api pṛñcanti bhūri dvir yad ete trir bhavanty ūmāḥ /

Halfverse: c svadoḥ svādīyaḥ svādunā sṛjā sam adaḥ su madhu madhunābhi yodhīḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yadi cin nu tvā dhanā jayantam raṇeraṇe anumadanti viprāḥ /

Halfverse: c ojīyaḥ śuṣmint sthiram ā tanuṣva mā tvā dabhan durevāsaḥ kaśokāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tvayā vayam śāśadmahe raṇeṣu prapaśyanto yudhenyāni bhūri /

Halfverse: c codayāmi ta āyudhā vacobhiḥ sam te śiśāmi brahmaṇā vayāmsi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ni tad dadhiṣe ‘vare pare ca yasminn āvithāvasā duroṇe /

Halfverse: c ā sthāpayata mātaram jigatnum ata invata karvarāṇi bhūri //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a stuṣva varṣman puruvartmānam sam ṛbhvāṇam inatamam āptam āptyānām /

Halfverse: c ā darśati śavasā bhūryojāḥ pra sakṣati pratimānam pṛthivyāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a imā brahma bṛhaddivaḥ kṛṇavad indrāya śūṣam agriyaḥ svarṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c maho gotrasya kṣayati svarājā turaś cid viśvam arṇavat tapasvān //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a evā mahān bṛhaddivo atharvāvocat svām tanva1m indram eva /

Halfverse: c svasārau mātaribhvarī aripre hinvanti caine śavasā vardhayanti ca //9//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mamāgne varco vihaveṣv astu vayam tvendhānās tanvam puṣema /

Halfverse: c mahyam namantām pradiśaś catasras tvayādhyakṣeṇa pṛtanā jayema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agne manyum pratinudan pareṣām tvam no gopāḥ pari pāhi viśvataḥ /

Halfverse: c apāñco yantu nivatā durasyavo ‘maiṣām cittam prabudhām vi neśat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mama devā vihave santu sarva indravanto maruto viṣṇur agniḥ /

Halfverse: c mamāntarikṣam urulokam astu mahyam vātaḥ pavatām kāmāyāsmai //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a mahyam yajantām mama yānīṣṭākūtiḥ satyā manaso me astu /

Halfverse: c eno mā ni gām katamac canāham viśve devā abhi rakṣantu meha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mayi devā draviṇam ā yajantām mayi āśīr astu mayi devahūtiḥ /

Halfverse: c daivāḥ hotāraḥ saniṣan na etad ariṣṭāḥ syāma tanvā suvīrāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a daivīḥ ṣaḍ urvīr uru naḥ kṛṇota viśve devāsa iha mādayadhvam /

Halfverse: c mā no vidad abhibhā mo aśastir mā no vidad vṛjinā dveṣyā yā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tisro devīr mahi naḥ śarma yachata prajāyai nas tanvṝ3 yac ca puṣṭam /

Halfverse: c mā hāsmahi prajayā mā tanūbhir mā radhāma dviṣate soma rājan //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a uruvyacā no mahiṣaḥ śarma yachatv asmin have puruhūtaḥ purukṣu /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ prajāyai haryaśva mṛḍendra mā no rīriṣo mā parā dāḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a dhātā vidhātā bhuvanasya yas patir devaḥ savitābhimātiṣāhaḥ /

Halfverse: c ādityā rudrā aśvinobhā devāḥ pāntu yajamānam nirṛthāt //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ye naḥ sapatnā apa te bhavantv indrāgnibhyām ava bādhāmaha enān /

Halfverse: c ādityā rudrā uparispṛśo no ugram cettāram adhirājam akrata //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a arvāñcam indram amuto havāmahe yo gojid dhanajid aśvajid yaḥ /

Halfverse: c imam no yajñam vihave śṛṇotv asmākam abhūr haryaśva medī //11//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo giriṣv ajāyathā vīrudhām balavattamaḥ /

Halfverse: c kuṣṭhehi takmanāśana takmānam nāśayann itaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a suparṇasuvane girau jātam himavatas pari /

Halfverse: c dhanair abhi śrutvā yanti vidur hi takmanāśanam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a aśvattho devasadanas tṛtīyasyām ito divi /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇam devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a hiraṇyayī naur acarad dhiraṇyabandhanā divi /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasya puṣpam devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a hiraṇyayāḥ panthāna āsann aritrāṇi hiraṇyayā /

Halfverse: c nāvo hiraṇyayīr āsan yābhiḥ kuṣṭham nirāvahan //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a imam me kuṣṭha pūruṣam tam ā vaha tam niṣ kuru /

Halfverse: c tam u me agadam kṛdhi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a devebhyo adhi jāto ‘si somasyāsi sakhā hitaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa prāṇāya vyānāya cakṣuṣe me asmai mṛḍa //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a udaṅ jāto himavataḥ sa prācyām nīyase janam /

Halfverse: c tatra kuṣṭhasya nāmāny uttamāni vi bhejire //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a uttamo nāma kuṣṭhasy uttamo nāma te pitā /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam ca sarvam nāśaya takmānam cārasam kṛdhi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a śīrṣāmayam upahatyām akṣyos tanvo3 rapaḥ /

Halfverse: c kuṣṭhas tat sarvam niṣ karad daivam samaha vṛṣṇyam //10//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a rātrī mātā nabhaḥ pitāryamā te pitāmahaḥ /

Halfverse: c silācī nāma vā asi sā devānām asi svasā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yas tvā pibati jīvati trāyase puruṣam tvam /

Halfverse: c bhartrī hi śaśvatām asi janānām ca nyañcanī //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vṛkṣamvṛkṣam ā rohasi vṛṣaṇyantīva kanyalā /

Halfverse: c jayantī pratyātiṣṭhantī sparaṇī nāma vā asi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad daṇḍena yad iṣvā yad vārur harasā kṛtam /

Halfverse: c tasya tvam asi niṣkṛtiḥ semam niṣ kṛdhi pūruṣam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bhadrāt plakṣān nis tiṣṭhasy aśvatthāt khadirād dhavāt /

Halfverse: c bhadrān nyagrodhāt parṇāt sā na ehy arundhati //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a hiraṇyavarṇe subhage sūryavarṇe vapuṣṭame /

Halfverse: c rutam gachāsi niṣkṛte niṣkṛtir nāma vā asi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a hiraṇyavarṇe subhage śuṣme lomaśavakṣane /

Halfverse: c apām asi svasā lākṣe vāto hātmā babhūva te //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a silācī nāma kānīno ‘jababhru pitā tava /

Halfverse: c aśvo yamasya yaḥ śyāvas tasya hāsnāsy ukṣitā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a aśvasyāsnaḥ sampatitā sā vṛkṣāṁ abhi siṣyade /

Halfverse: c sarā patatriṇī bhūtvā sā na ehy arundhati //9//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a brahma jajñānam prathamam purastād vi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa budhnyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ sataś ca yonim asataś ca vi vaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anāptā ye vaḥ prathamā yāni karmāṇi cakrire /

Halfverse: c vīrān no atra mā dabhan tad va etat puro dadhe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sahasradhāra eva te sam asvaran divo nāke madhujihvā asaścataḥ /

Halfverse: c tasya spaśo na ni miṣanti bhūrṇayaḥ padepade pāśinaḥ santi setave //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pary ū ṣu pra dhanvā vājasātaye pari vṛtrāṇi sakṣaṇiḥ /

Halfverse: c dviṣas tad adhy arṇaveneyase sanisraso nāmāsi trayodaśo māsa indrasya gṛhaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a nv ṝ3tenārātsīr asau svāhā /

Halfverse: c tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suśevau somārudrāv iha su mṛḍatam naḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a avaitenārātsīr asau svāhā /

Halfverse: c tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suśevau somārudrāv iha su mṛḍatam naḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a apaitenārātsīr asau svāhā /

Halfverse: c tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suśevau somārudrāv iha su mṛḍatam naḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mumuktam asmān duritād avadyāj juṣethām yajñam amṛtam asmāsu dhattam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a cakṣuṣo hete manaso hete brahmaṇo hete tapasaś ca hete /

Halfverse: c menyā menir asy amenayas te santu ye ‘smāṁ abhyaghāyanti //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yo3 ‘smāmś cakṣuṣā manasā cittyākūtyā ca yo aghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c tvam tān agne menyāmenīn kṛṇu svāhā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a indrasya gṛho ‘si /

Halfverse: c tam tvā pra padye tam tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me ‘sti tena //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a indrasya śarmāsi /

Halfverse: c tam tvā pra padye tam tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me ‘sti tena //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a indrasya varmāsi /

Halfverse: c tam tvā pra padye tam tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me ‘sti tena //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a indrasya varūtham asi /

Halfverse: c tam tvā pra padye tam tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me ‘sti tena //14//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā no bhara mā pari ṣṭhā arāte mā no rakṣīr dakṣiṇām nīyamānām /

Halfverse: c namo vīrtsāyā asamṛddhaye namo astv arātaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yam arāte purodhatse puruṣam parirāpiṇam /

Halfverse: c namas te tasmai kṛṇmo mā vanim vyathayīr mama //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pra ṇo vanir devakṛtā divā naktam ca kalpatām /

Halfverse: c arātim anupremo vayam namo astv arātaye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sarasvatīm anumatim bhagam yanto havāmahe /

Halfverse: c vācam juṣṭām madhumatīm avādiṣam devānām devahūtiṣu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yam yācāmy aham vācā sarasvatyā manoyujā /

Halfverse: c śraddhā tam adya vindatu dattā somena babhruṇā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a mā vanim mā vācam no vīrtsīr ubhāv indrāgnī ā bharatām no vasūni /

Halfverse: c sarve no adya ditsanto ‘rātim prati haryata //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a paro ‘pehy asamṛddhe vi te hetim nayāmasi /

Halfverse: c veda tvāham nimīvantīm nitudantīm arāte //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a uta nagnā bobhuvatī svapnayā sacase janam /

Halfverse: c arāte cittam vīrtsanty ākūtim puruṣasya ca //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yā mahatī mahonmānā viśvā āśā vyānaśe /

Halfverse: c tasyai hiraṇyakeśyai nirṛtyā akaram namaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a hiraṇyavarṇā subhagā hiraṇyakaśipur mahī /

Halfverse: c tasyai hiraṇyadrāpaye ‘rātyā akaram namaḥ //10//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vaikaṅkatenedhmena devebhya ājyam vaha /

Halfverse: c agne tām iha mādaya sarva ā yantu me havam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indrā yāhi me havam idam kariṣyāmi tac chṛṇu /

Halfverse: c ima aindrā atisarā ākūtim sam namantu me /

Halfverse: e tebhiḥ śakema vīrya1m jātavedas tanūvaśin //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad asāv amuto devā adevaḥ samś cikīrṣati /

Halfverse: c mā tasyāgnir havyam vākṣīd dhavam devā asya mopa gur mamaiva havam etana //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ati dhāvatātisarā indrasya vacasā hata /

Halfverse: c avim vṛka iva mathnīta sa vo jīvan mā moci prānam asyāpi nahyata //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yam amī purodadhire brahmāṇam apabhūtaye /

Halfverse: c indra sa te adhaspadam tam praty asyāmi mṛtyave //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yadi preyur devapurā brahma varmāṇi cakrire /

Halfverse: c tanūpānam paripāṇam kṛṇvānā yad upocire sarvam tad arasam kṛdhi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yān asāv atisarāmś cakāra kṛṇavac ca yān /

Halfverse: c tvam tān indra vṛtrahan pratīcaḥ punar ā kṛdhi yathāmum tṛṇahām janam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yathendra udvācanam labdhvā cakre adhaspadam /

Halfverse: c kṛṇve ‘ham adharān tathā amūñ chaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a atrainān indra vṛtrahann ugro marmaṇi vidhya /

Halfverse: c atraivainān abhi tiṣṭhendra medy a1ham tava /

Halfverse: e anu tvendrā rabhāmahe syāma sumatau tava //9//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dive svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pṛthivyai svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a antarikṣāya svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a antarikṣāya svāhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dive svāhā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pṛthivyai svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sūryo me cakṣur vātaḥ prāṇo ‘ntarikṣam ātmā pṛthivī śarīram /

Halfverse: c astṛto nāmāham ayam asmi sa ātmānam ni dadhe dyāvāpṛthivībhyām gopīthāya //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ud āyur ud balam ut kṛtam ut kṛtyām un manīṣām ud indriyam /

Halfverse: c āyuṣkṛd āyuṣpatnī svadhāvantau gopā me stam gopāyatam mā /

Halfverse: e ātmasadau me stam mā mā himsiṣṭam //8//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aśmavarma me ‘si yo mā prācyā diśo ‘ghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c etat sa ṛchāt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aśmavarma me ‘si yo mā dakṣiṇāyā diśo ‘ghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c etat sa ṛchāt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a aśmavarma me ‘si yo mā pratīcyā diśo ‘ghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c etat sa ṛchāt //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a aśmavarma me ‘si yo modīcyā diśo ‘ghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c etat sa ṛchāt //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a aśmavarma me ‘si yo mā dhruvāyā diśo ‘ghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c etat sa ṛchāt //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a aśmavarma me ‘si yo mordhvāyā diśo ‘ghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c etat sa ṛchāt //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a aśmavarma me ‘si yo mā diśām antardeśebhyo ‘ghāyur abhidāsāt /

Halfverse: c etat sa ṛchāt //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bṛhatā mana upa hvaye mātariśvanā prāṇāpānau /

Halfverse: c sūryāc cakṣur antarikṣāc chrotram pṛthivyāḥ śarīram /

Halfverse: e sarasvatyā vācam upa hvayāmahe manoyujā //8//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a katham mahe asurāyābravīr iha katham pitre haraye tveṣanṛmṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c pṛśnim varuṇa dakṣiṇām dadāvān punarmagha tvam manasācikitsīḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na kāmena punarmagho bhavāmi sam cakṣe kam pṛśnim etām upāje /

Halfverse: c kena nu tvam atharvan kāvyena kena jātenāsi jātavedāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a satyam aham gabhīraḥ kāvyena satyam jātenāsmi jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c na me dāso nāryo mahitvā vratam mīmāya yad aham dhariṣye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a na tvad anyaḥ kavitaro na medhayā dhīrataro varuṇa svadhāvan /

Halfverse: c tvam tā viśvā bhuvanāni vettha sa cin nu tvaj jano māyī bibhāya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tvam hy aṅga varuṇa svadhāvan viśvā vettha janima supraṇīte /

Halfverse: c kim rajasa enā paro anyad asty enā kim pareṇāvaram amura //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ekam rajasa enā paro anyad asty enā para ekena durṇaśam cid arvāk /

Halfverse: c tat te vidvān varuṇa pra bravīmy adhovacasaḥ paṇayo bhavantu nīcair dāsā upa sarpantu bhūmim //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tvam hy aṅga varuṇa bravīṣi punarmagheṣv avadyāni bhūri /

Halfverse: c mo ṣu paṇīṁr abhy etāvato bhūn mā tvā vocann arādhasam janāsaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mā mā vocann arādhasam janāsaḥ punas te pṛśnim jaritar dadāmi /

Halfverse: c stotram me viśvam ā yāhi śacībhir antar viśvāsu mānuṣīṣu dikṣu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ā te stotrāṇy udyatāni yantv antar viśvāsu mānuṣīṣu dikṣu /

Halfverse: c dehi nu me yan me adatto asi yujyo me saptapadaḥ sakhāsi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a samā nau bandhur varuṇa samā jā vedāham tad yan nāv eṣā samā jā /

Halfverse: c dadāmi tad yat te adatto asmi yujyas te saptapadaḥ sakhāsmi //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a devo devāya gṛṇate vayodhā vipro viprāya stuvate sumedhāḥ /

Halfverse: c ajījano hi varuṇa svadhāvann atharvāṇam pitaram devabandhum /

Halfverse: e tasmā u rādhaḥ kṛṇuhi supraśastam sakhā no asi paramam ca bandhuḥ //11//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samiddho adya manuṣo duroṇe devo devān yajasi jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā ca vaha mitramahaś cikitvān tvam dūtaḥ kavir asi pracetāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tanūnapāt patha ṛtasya yānān madhvā samañjant svadayā sujihva /

Halfverse: c manmāni dhībhir uta yajñam ṛndhan devatrā ca kṛṇuhy adhvaram naḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ājuhvāna īḍyo bandyaś cā yāhy agne vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c tvam devānām asi yahva hotā sa enān yakṣīṣito yajīyān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prācīnam barhiḥ pradiśā pṛthivyā vastor asyā vṛjyate agre ahnām /

Halfverse: c vy ḥ prathate vitaram varīyo devebhyo aditaye syonam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vyacasvatīr urviyā vi śrayantām patibhyo na janayaḥ śumbhamānāḥ /

Halfverse: c devīr dvāro bṛhatīr viśvaminvā devebhyo bhavata suprāyaṇāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ā suṣvayantī yajate upāke uṣāsānaktā sadatām ni yonau /

Halfverse: c divye yoṣaṇe bṛhatī surukme adhi śriyam śukrapiśam dadhāne //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a daivyā hotārā prathamā suvācā mimānā yajñam manuṣo yajadhyai /

Halfverse: c pracodayantā vidatheṣu kārū prācīnam jyotiḥ pradiśā diśantā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ā no yajñam bhāratī tūyam etv iḍā manuṣvad iha cetayantī /

Halfverse: c tisro devīr barhir edam syonam sarasvatīḥ svapasaḥ sadantām //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī janitrī rūpair apimśad bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c tam adya hotar iṣito yajīyān devam tvaṣṭāram iha yakṣi vidvān //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a upāvasṛja tmanyā samañjan devānām pātha ṛtuthā havīmṣi /

Halfverse: c vanaspatiḥ śamitā devo agniḥ svadantu havyam madhunā ghṛtena //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a sadyo jāto vy amimīta yajñam agnir devānām abhavat purogāḥ /

Halfverse: c asya hotuḥ praśiṣy ṛtasya vāci svāhākṛtam havir adantu devāḥ //11//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dadir hi mahyam varuṇo divaḥ kavir vacobhir ugrair ni riṇāmi te viṣam /

Halfverse: c khātam akhātam uta saktam agrabham ireva dhanvan ni jajāsa te viṣam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yat te apodakam viṣam tat ta etāsv agrabham /

Halfverse: c gṛhṇāmi te madhyamam uttamam rasam utāvamam bhiyasā neśad ād u te //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vṛṣā me ravo nabhasā na tanyatur ugreṇa te vacasā bādha ād u te /

Halfverse: c aham tam asya nṛbhir agrabham rasam tamasa iva jyotir ud etu sūryaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a cakṣuṣā te cakṣur hanmi viṣeṇa hanmi te viṣam /

Halfverse: c ahe mriyasva mā jīvīḥ pratyag abhy ṝtu tvā viṣam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kairāta pṛśna upatṛṇya babhra ā me śṛṇutāsitā alīkāḥ /

Halfverse: c mā me sakhyuḥ stāmānam api ṣṭhātāśrāvayanto ni viṣe ramadhvam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a asitasya taimātasya babhror apodakasya ca /

Halfverse: c sātrāsāhasyāham manyor ava jyām iva dhanvano vi muñcāmi rathāṁ iva //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a āligī ca viligī ca pitā ca matā ca /

Halfverse: c vidma vaḥ sarvato bandhv arasāḥ kim kariṣyatha //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a urugūlāyā duhitā jātā dāsy asiknyā /

Halfverse: c prataṅkam dadruṣīṇām sarvāsām arasam viṣam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a karṇā śvāvit tad abravīd girer avacarantikā /

Halfverse: c yāḥ kāś cemāḥ khanitrimās tāsām arasatamam viṣam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tābuvam na tābuvam na ghet tvam asi tābuvam /

Halfverse: c tābuvenārasam viṣam //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tastuvam na tastuvam na ghet tvam asi tastuvam /

Halfverse: c tastuvenārasam viṣam //11//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a suparṇas tvānv avindat sūkaras tvākhanan nasā /

Halfverse: c dipsauṣadhe tvam dipsantam ava kṛtyākṛtam jahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ava jahi yātudhānān ava kṛtyākṛtam jahi /

Halfverse: c atho yo asmān dipsati tam u tvam jahy oṣadhe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a riśyasyeva parīśāsam parikṛtya pari tvacaḥ /

Halfverse: c kṛtyām kṛtyākṛte devā niṣkam iva prati muñcata //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a punaḥ kṛtyām kṛtyākṛte hastagṛhya parā ṇaya /

Halfverse: c samakṣam asma ā dhehi yathā kṛtyākṛtam hanat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kṛtyāḥ santu kṛtyākṛte śapathaḥ śapathīyate /

Halfverse: c sukho ratha iva vartatām kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtam punaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yadi strī yadi vā pumān kṛtyām cakāra pāpmane /

Halfverse: c tām u tasmai nayāmasy aśvam ivāśvābhidhānyā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yadi vāsi devakṛtā yadi vā puruṣaiḥ kṛtā /

Halfverse: c tām tvā punar ṇayāmasīndreṇa sayujā vayam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a agne pṛtanāṣāṭ pṛtanāḥ sahasva /

Halfverse: c punaḥ kṛtyām kṛtyākṛte pratiharaṇena harāmasi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a kṛtavyadhani vidya tam yaś cakāra tam ij jahi /

Halfverse: c na tvām acakruṣe vayam vadhāya sam śiśīmahi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a putra iva pitaram gacha svaja ivābhiṣṭhito daśa /

Halfverse: c bandham ivāvakrāmī gacha kṛtye kṛtyākṛtam punaḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ud eṇīva vāraṇy abhiskandham mṛgīva /

Halfverse: c kṛtyā kartāram ṛchatu //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a iṣvā ṛjīyaḥ patatu dyāvāpṛthivī tam prati /

Halfverse: c sā tam mṛgam iva gṛhṇatu kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtam punaḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a agnir ivaitu pratikūlam anukūlam ivodakam /

Halfverse: c sukho ratha iva vartatām kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtam punaḥ //13//

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ekā ca me daśa ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dve ca me vimśatiś ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tisraś ca me trimśac ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a catasraś ca me catvārimśac ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karah //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pañca ca me pañcāśac ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ṣaṭ ca me ṣaṣṭiś ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sapta ca me saptatiś ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a aṣṭa ca me ‘śītiś ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a nava ca me navatiś ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a daśa ca me śatam ca me ‘pavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a śatam ca me sahasram cāpavaktāra oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ //11//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yady ekavṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yadi dvivṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yadi trivṛso ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yadi caturvṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yadi pañcavṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yadi ṣaḍvṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //6//

Verse: 7
