Halfverse: a yadi saptavṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yady aṣṭavṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yadi navavṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yadi daśavṛṣo ‘si sṛjāraso ‘si //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yady ekādaśo ‘si so ‘podako ‘si //11//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a te ‘vadan prathamā brahmakilbiṣe ‘kūpāraḥ salilo mātariśvā /

Halfverse: c vīḍuharās tapa ugram mayobhūr āpo devīḥ prathamajā ṛtasya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a somo rājā prathamo brahmajāyām punaḥ prāyachad ahṛṇīyamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c anvartitā varuṇo mitra āsīd agnir hotā hastagṛhyā nināya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a hastenaiva grāhya ādhir asyā brahmajāyeti ced avocat /

Halfverse: c na dūtāya praheyā tastha eṣā tathā rāṣṭram gupitam kṣatriyasya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yām āhus tārakaiṣā vikeśīti duchunām grāmam avapadyamānām /

Halfverse: c sā brahmajāyā vi dunoti rāṣṭram yatra prāpādi śaśa ulkuṣīmān //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a brahmacārī carati veviṣad viṣaḥ sa devānām bhavaty ekam aṅgam /

Halfverse: c tena jāyām anv avindad bṛhaspatiḥ somena nītām juhva1m na devāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a devā vā etasyām avadanta pūrve saptaṛṣayas tapasā ye niṣeduḥ /

Halfverse: c bhīmā jāyā brāhmaṇasyāpanītā durdhām dadhāti parame vyoman //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ye garbhā avapadyante jagad yac cāpalupyate /

Halfverse: c vīrā ye tṛhyante mitho brahmajāyā hinasti tān //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a uta yat patayo daśa striyāḥ pūrve abrāhmaṇāḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmā ced dhastam agrahīt sa eva patir ekadhā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a brāhmaṇa eva patir na rājanyo3 na vaiśyaḥ /

Halfverse: c tat sūryaḥ prabruvann eti pañcabhyo mānavebhyaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a punar vai devā adaduḥ punar manuṣyā adaduḥ /

Halfverse: c rājānaḥ satyam gṛhṇānā brahmajāyām punar daduḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a punardāya brahmajāyām kṛtvā devair nikilbiṣam /

Halfverse: c ūrjam pṛthivyā bhaktvorugāyam upāsate //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a nāsya jāyā śatavāhī kalyāṇī talpam ā śaye /

Halfverse: c yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a na vikarṇaḥ pṛthuśirās tasmin veśmani jāyate /

Halfverse: c yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a nāsya ksattā niṣkagrīvaḥ sūnānām ety agrataḥ /

Halfverse: c yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a nāsya śvetaḥ kṛṣṇakarṇo dhuri yukto mahīyate /

Halfverse: c yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a nāsya kṣetre puṣkariṇī nāṇḍīkam jāyate bisam /

Halfverse: c yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a nāsmai pṛśnim vi duhanti ye ‘syā doham upāsate /

Halfverse: c yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a nāsya dhenuḥ kalyāṇī nānaḍvānt sahate dhuram /

Halfverse: c vijānir yatra brahmaṇo rātrim vasati pāpayā //18//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a naitām te devā adadus tubhyam nṛpate attave /

Halfverse: c mā brāhmaṇasya rājanya gām jighatso anādyām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a akṣadrugdho rājanyaḥ pāpa ātmaparājitaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa brāhmaṇasya gām adyād adya jīvāni mā śvaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āviṣṭitāghaviṣā pṛdākūr iva carmaṇā /

Halfverse: c sā brāhmaṇasya rājanya tṛṣṭaiṣā gaur anādyā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a nir vai kṣatram nayati hanti varco ‘gnir ivārabdho vi dunoti sarvam /

Halfverse: c yo brāhmaṇam manyate annam eva sa viṣasya pibati taimātasya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ya enam hanti mṛdum manyamāno devapīyur dhanakāmo na cittāt /

Halfverse: c sam tasyendro hṛdaye ‘gnim indhe ubhe enam dviṣṭo nabhasī carantam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a na brāhmaṇo himsitavyo3 ‘gniḥ priyatanor iva /

Halfverse: c somo hy asya dāyāda indro asyābhiśastipāḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śatāpāṣṭhām ni girati tām na śaknoti niḥkhidam /

Halfverse: c annam yo brahmaṇām malvaḥ svādv a1dmīti manyate //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a jihvā jyā bhavati kulmalam vāṅ nāḍīkā dantās tapasābhidigdhāḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhir brahmā vidhyati devapīyūn hṛdbalair dhanurbhir devajūtaiḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tīkṣṇeṣavo brāhmaṇā hetimanto yām asyanti śaravyā3m na sā mṛṣā /

Halfverse: c anuhāya tapasā manyunā cota durād ava bhindanty enam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ye sahasram arājann āsan daśaśatā uta /

Halfverse: c te brāhmaṇasya gām jagdhvā vaitahavyāḥ parābhavan //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a gaur eva tān hanyamānā vaitahavyāṁ avātirat /

Halfverse: c ye kesaraprābandhāyāś caramājām apeciran //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ekaśatam tā janatā yā bhūmir vyadhūnuta /

Halfverse: c prajām himsitvā brāhmaṇīm asambhavyam parābhavan //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a devapīyuś carati martyeṣu garagīrṇo bhavaty asthibhūyān /

Halfverse: c yo brāhmaṇam devabandhum hinasti na sa pitṛyāṇam apy eti lokam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a agnir vai naḥ padavāyaḥ somo dāyāda ucyate /

Halfverse: c hantābhiśastendras tathā tad vedhaso viduḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a iṣur iva digdhā nṛpate pṛdākūr iva gopate /

Halfverse: c sā brāhmaṇasyeṣur ghorā tayā vidhyati pīyataḥ //15//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a atimātram avardhanta nod iva divam aspṛśan /

Halfverse: c bhṛgum himsitvā sṛñjayā vaitahavyāḥ parābhavan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ye bṛhatsāmānam āṅgirasam ārpayan brāhmaṇam janāḥ /

Halfverse: c petvas teṣām ubhayādam avis tokāny āvayat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye brāhmaṇam pratyaṣṭhīvan ye vāsmiñ chulkam īṣire /

Halfverse: c asnas te madhye kulyāyāḥ keśān khādanta āsate //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a brahmagavī pacyamānā yāvat sābhi vijaṅgahe /

Halfverse: c tejo rāṣṭrasya nir hanti na vīro jāyate vṛṣā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a krūram asyā āśasanam tṛṣṭam piśitam asyate /

Halfverse: c kṣīram yad asyāḥ pīyate tad vai pitṛṣu kilbiṣam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ugro rājā manyamāno brāhmaṇam yo jighatsati /

Halfverse: c parā tat sicyate rāṣṭram brāhmaṇo yatra jīyate //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a aṣṭāpadī caturakṣī catuḥśrotrā caturhanuḥ /

Halfverse: c dvyāsyā dvijihvā bhūtvā sā rāṣṭram ava dhūnute brahmajyasya //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tad vai rāṣṭram ā sravati nāvam bhinnām ivodakam /

Halfverse: c brahmāṇam yatra himsanti tad rāṣṭram hanti duchunā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tam vṛkṣā apa sedhanti chāyām no mopa gā iti /

Halfverse: c yo brāhmaṇasya sad dhanam abhi nārada manyate //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a viṣam etad devakṛtam rājā varuṇo ‘bravīt /

Halfverse: c na brāhmaṇasya gām jagdhvā rāstre jāgāra kaś cana //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a navaiva tā navatayo yā bhūmir vyadhūnuta /

Halfverse: c prajām himsitvā brāhmaṇīm asambhavyam parābhavan //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yām mṛtāyānubadhnanti kūdyam padayopanīm /

Halfverse: c tad vai brahmajya te devā upastaraṇam abruvan //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a aśrūṇi kṛpamānasya yāni jītasya vāvṛtuḥ /

Halfverse: c tam vai brahmajya te devā apām bhāgam adhārayan //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yena mṛtam snapayanti śmaśrūṇi yenondate /

Halfverse: c tam vai brahmajya te devā apām bhāgam adhārayan //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a na varṣam maitrāvaruṇam brahmajyam abhi varṣati /

Halfverse: c nāsmai samitiḥ kalpate na mitram nayate vaśam //15//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a uccairghoṣo dundubhiḥ satvanāyan vānaspatyaḥ sambhṛta usṛiyābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c vācam kṣuṇuvāno damayant sapatnānt simha iva jeṣyann abhi tamstanīhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a simha ivāstānīd druvayo vibaddho ‘bhikrandann ṛṣabho vāsitām iva /

Halfverse: c vṛṣā tvam vadhrayas te sapatnā aindras te śuṣmo abhimātiṣāhaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vṛṣeva yūthe sahasā vidāno gavyann abhi ruva samdhanājit /

Halfverse: c śucā vidhya hṛdayam pareṣām hitvā grāmān pracyutā yantu śatravaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a samjayan pṛtanā ūrdhvamāyur gṛhyā gṛhṇāno bahudhā vi cakṣva /

Halfverse: c daivīm vācam dundubha ā gurasva vedhāḥ śatrūṇām upa bharasva vedaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dundubher vācam prayatām vadantīm āśṛṇvatī nāthitā ghoṣabuddhā /

Halfverse: c nārī putram dhāvatu hastagṛhyāmitrī bhītā samare vadhānām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pūrvo dundubhe pra vadāsi vācam bhūmyāḥ pṛṣṭhe vada rocamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c amitrasenām abhijañjabhāno dyumad vada dundubhe sūnṛtāvat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a antareme nabhasī ghoṣo astu pṛthak te dhvanayo yantu śībham /

Halfverse: c abhi kranda stanayotpipānaḥ ślokakṛn mitratūryāya svardhī //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a dhībhiḥ kṛtaḥ pra vadāti vācam ud dharṣaya satvanām āyudhāni /

Halfverse: c indramedī satvano ni hvayasva mitrair amitrāṁ ava jaṅghanīhi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a samkrandanaḥ pravado dhṛṣṇuṣeṇaḥ pravedakṛd bahudhā grāmaghoṣī /

Halfverse: c śriyo vanvano vayunāni vidvān kīrtim bahubhyo vi hara dvirāje //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a śreyaḥketo vasujit sahīyānt samgrāmajit samśito brahmaṇāsi /

Halfverse: c amśūn iva grāvādhiṣavaṇe adrir gavyan dundubhe’dhi nṛtya vedaḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a śatrūṣāṇ nīṣād abhimātiṣāho gaveṣaṇaḥ sahamāna udbhit /

Halfverse: c vāgvīva mantram pra bharasva vācam sāmgrāmajityāyeṣam ud vadeha //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a acyutacyut samado gamiṣṭho mṛdho jetā puraetāyodhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c indreṇa gupto vidathā nicikyad dhṛddyotano dviṣatām yāhi śībham //12//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vihṛdayam vaimanasyam vadāmitreṣu dundubhe /

Halfverse: c vidveṣam kaśmaśam bhayam amitreṣu ni dadhmasy ava enān dundubhe jahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a udvepamānā manasā cakṣuṣā hṛdayena ca /

Halfverse: c dhāvantu bibhyato ‘mitrāḥ pratrāsenājye hute //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vānaspatyaḥ sambhṛta usriyābhir viśvagotryaḥ /

Halfverse: c pratrāsam amitrebhyo vadājyenābhighāritaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yathā mṛgāḥ samvijanta āraṇyāḥ puruṣād adhi /

Halfverse: c eva tvam dundubhe ‘mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yathā vṛkād ajāvayo dhāvanti bahu bibhyatīḥ /

Halfverse: c eva tvam dundubhe ‘mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yathā śyenāt patatriṇaḥ samvijante ahardivi simhasya stanathor yathā /

Halfverse: c eva tvam dundubhe ‘mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a parāmitrān dundubhinā hariṇasyājinena ca /

Halfverse: c sarve devā atitrasan ye samgrāmasyeṣate //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yair indraḥ prakrīḍate padghoṣaiś chāyayā saha /

Halfverse: c tair amitrās trasantu no ‘mī ye yanty anīkaśaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a jyāghoṣā dundubhayo ‘bhi krośantu yā diśaḥ /

Halfverse: c senāḥ parājitā yatīr amitrāṇām anīkaśaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a āditya cakṣur ā datsva marīcayo ‘nu dhāvata /

Halfverse: c patsaṅginīr ā sajantu vigate bāhuvīryṝ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yūyam ugrā marutaḥ pṛśnimātara indreṇa yujā pra mṛnīta śatrūn /

Halfverse: c somo rājā varuṇo rājā mahādeva uta mṛtyur indraḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a etā devasenāḥ sūryaketavaḥ sacetasaḥ /

Halfverse: c amitrān no jayantu svāhā //12//

Hymn: 22

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnis takmānam apa bādhatām itaḥ somo grāvā varuṇaḥ pūtadakṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c vedir barhiḥ samidhaḥ śośucānā apa dveṣāmsy amuyā bhavantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayam yo viśvān haritān kṛṇoṣy ucchocayann agnir ivābhidunvan /

Halfverse: c adhā hi takmann araso hi bhūyā adhā nyaṅṅ adharān vā parehi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaḥ paruṣaḥ pāruṣeyo ‘vadhvamsa ivāruṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c takmānam viśvadhāvīryādharāñcam parā suvā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a adharāñcam pra hiṇomi namaḥ kṛtvā takmane /

Halfverse: c śakambharasya muṣṭihā punar etu mahāvṛṣān //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a oko asya mūjavanta oko asya mahāvṛṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c yāvaj jātas takmams tāvān asi balhikeṣu nyocaraḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a takman vyāla vi gada vyaṅga bhūri yāvaya /

Halfverse: c dāsīm niṣṭakvarīm icha tām vajreṇa sam arpaya //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a takman mūjavato gacha balhikān vā parastarām /

Halfverse: c śūdrām icha prapharvya1m tām takman vīva dhūnuhi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mahāvṛṣān mūjavato bandhv addhi paretya /

Halfverse: c praitāni takmane brūmo anyakṣetrāṇi vā imā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a anyakṣetre na ramase vaśī san mṛḍayāsi naḥ /

Halfverse: c abhūd u prārthas takmā sa gamiṣyati balhikān //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yat tvam śīto ‘tho rūraḥ saha kāsāvepayaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhīmās te takman hetayas tābhiḥ sma pari vṛṅgdhi naḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a mā smaitānt sakhīn kuruthā balāsam kāsam udyugam /

Halfverse: c mā smāto’rvāṅ aiḥ punas tat tvā takmann upa bruve //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a takman bhrātrā balāsena svasrā kāsikayā saha /

Halfverse: c pāpmā bhrātṛvyeṇa saha gachāmum araṇam janam //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a tṛtīyakam vitṛtīyam sadandim uta śāradam /

Halfverse: c takmānam śītam rūram graiṣmam nāśaya vārṣikam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a gandhāribhyo mūjavadbhyo ‘ṅgebhyo magadhebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c praiṣyan janam iva śevadhim takmānam pari dadmasi //14//

Hymn: 23

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ote me dyāvāpṛthivī otā devī sarasvatī /

Halfverse: c otau ma indraś cāgniś ca krimim jambhayatām iti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asyendra kumārasya krimīn dhanapate jahi /

Halfverse: c hatā viśvā arātaya ugreṇa vacasā mama //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo akṣyaḥ parisarpati yo nāse parisarpati /

Halfverse: c datām yo madyam gachati tam krimim jambhayāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sarūpau dvau virūpau dvau kṛṣṇau dvau rohitau dvau /

Halfverse: c babhruś ca babhrukarṇaś ca gṛdhraḥ kokaś ca te hatāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye krimayaḥ śitikakṣā ye kṛṣṇāḥ śitibāhavaḥ /

Halfverse: c ye ke ca viśvarūpās tān krimīn jambhayāmasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ut purastāt sūrya eti viśvadṛṣṭo adṛṣṭahā /

Halfverse: c dṛṣṭāmś ca ghnann adṛṣṭāmś ca sarvāmś ca pramṛṇan krimīn //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yevāṣāsaḥ kaṣkaṣāsa ejatkāḥ śipavitnukāḥ /

Halfverse: c dṛṣṭāmś ca hanyatām krimir utādṛṣṭaś ca hanyatām //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a hato yevāṣaḥ krimīṇām hato nadanimota /

Halfverse: c sarvān ni maṣmaṣākaram dṛṣadā khalvāṁ iva //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a triśīrṣāṇam trikakudam krimim sāraṅgam arjunam /

Halfverse: c śṛṇāmy asya pṛṣṭīr api vṛścāmi yac chiraḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a atrivad vaḥ krimayo hanmi kaṇvavaj jamadagnivat /

Halfverse: c agastyasya brahmaṇā sam pinaṣmy aham krimīn //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a hato rājā krimīṇām utaiṣām sthapatir hataḥ /

Halfverse: c hato hatamātā krimir hatabhrātā hatasvasā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a hatāso asya veśaso hatāsaḥ pariveśasaḥ /

Halfverse: c atho ye kṣullakā iva sarve te krimayo hatāḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sarveṣām ca krimīṇām sarvāsām ca krimīnām /

Halfverse: c bhinadmy aśmanā śira dahāmy agninā mukham //13//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a savitā prasavānām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agnir vanaspatīnām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dyāvāpṛthivī dātṝṇām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a varuṇo ‘pām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mitrāvaruṇau vṛṣṭyādhipatī tau māvatām /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a marutaḥ parvatānām adhipatayas te māvantu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a somo vīrudhām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a vāyur antarikṣasyādhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a sūryaś cakṣuṣām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a candramā nakṣatrāṇām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a indro divo ‘dhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a marutām pitā paśūnām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a mṛtyuḥ prajānām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yamaḥ pitṝṇām adhipatiḥ sa māvatu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a pitaraḥ pare te māvantu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a tatā avare te māvantu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratisṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a tatas tatāmahās te māvantu /

Halfverse: c asmin brahmaṇy asmin karmaṇy asyām purodhāyām asyām pratiṣṭhāyām asyām /

Halfverse: e cittyām asyām ākūtyām asyām āśiṣy asyām devahūtyām svāhā //17//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a parvatād divo yoner aṅgādaṅgāt samābhṛtam /

Halfverse: c śepo garbhasya retodhāḥ sarau parṇam ivā dadhat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yatheyam pṛthivī mahī bhūtānām garbham ādadhe /

Halfverse: c evā dadhāmi te garbham tasmai tvām avase huve //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a garbham dhehi sinīvāli garbham dhehi sarasvati /

Halfverse: c garbham te aśvinobhā dhattām puṣkarasrajā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a garbham te mitrāvaruṇau garbham devo bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c garbham ta indraś cāgniś ca garbham dhātā dadhātu te //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a viṣṇur yonim kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi pimśatu /

Halfverse: c ā siñcatu prajāpatir dhātā garbham dadhātu te //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yad veda rājā varuṇo yad vā devī sarasvatī /

Halfverse: c yad indro vṛtrahā veda tad garbhakaraṇam piba //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a garbho asy oṣadhīnām garbho vanaspatīnām /

Halfverse: c garbho viśvasya bhūtasya so agne garbham eha dhāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a adhi skanda vīrayasva garbham ā dhehi yonyām /

Halfverse: c vṛṣāsi vṛṣṇyāvan prajāyai tvā nayāmasi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a vi jihīṣva bārhatsāme garbhas te yonim ā śayām /

Halfverse: c aduṣ ṭe devāḥ putram somapā ubhayāvinam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a dhātaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ /

Halfverse: c pumāmsam putram ā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tvaṣṭaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ /

Halfverse: c pumāmsam putram ā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a savitaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ /

Halfverse: c pumāmsam putram ā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a prajāpate śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ /

Halfverse: c pumāmsam putram ā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave //13//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yajūmṣi yajñe samidhaḥ svāhāgniḥ pravidvān iha vo yunaktu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yunaktu devaḥ savitā prajānann asmin yajñe mahiṣaḥ svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indra ukthāmadāny asmin yajñe pravidvān yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a praiṣā yajñe nividaḥ svāhā śiṣṭāḥ patnībhir vahateha yuktāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a chandāmsi yajñe marutaḥ svāhā māteva putram pipṛteha yuktāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a eyam agan barhiṣā prokṣaṇībhir yajñam tanvānāditiḥ svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a viṣṇur yunaktu bahudhā tapāmsy asmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tvaṣṭā yunaktu bahudhā nu rūpā asmin yajñe yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a bhago yunaktvāśiṣo nv asmā asmin yajñe pravidvān yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a somo yunaktu bahudhā payāmsy asmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a indro yunaktu bahudhā payāmsy asmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a aśvinā brahmaṇā yātam arvāñcau vaṣaṭkāreṇa yajñam vardhayantau /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspate brahmaṇā yāhy arvāṅ yajño ayam svar idam yajamānāya svāhā //12//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ūrdhvā asya samidho bhavanty ūrdhvā śukrā śocīṣy agneḥ /

Halfverse: c dyumattamā supratīkaḥ sasūnus tanūnapād asuro bhūripāṇiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a devo deveṣu devaḥ patho anakti madhvā ghṛtena //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a madhvā yajñam nakṣati praiṇāno narāśamso agniḥ sukṛd devaḥ savitā viśvavāraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a achāyam eti śavasā ghṛtā cid īdāno vahnir namasā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a agniḥ sruco adhvareṣu prayakṣu sa yakṣad asya mahimānam agneḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tarī mandrāsu prayakṣu vasavaś cātiṣṭhan vasudhātaraś ca //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a dvāro devīr anv asya viśve vratam rakṣanti viśvahā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a uruvyacasāgner dhāmnā patyamāne /

Halfverse: c ā suṣvayantī yajate upāke uṣāsānaktemam yajñam avatām adhvaram naḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a daivā hotāra ūrdhvam adhvaram no ‘gner jihvayābhi gṛnata gṛnatā naḥ svṅṣṭaye /

Halfverse: c tisro devīr barhir edam sadantām iḍā sarasvatī mahī bhāratī gṛṇānā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tan nas turīpam adbhutam purukṣu /

Halfverse: c deva tvaṣṭā rāyas poṣam vi ṣya nābhim asya //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a vanaspate ‘va sṛjā rarāṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c tmanā devebhyo agnir havyam śamitā svadayatu //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a agne svāhā kṛṇuhi jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c indrāya yajñam viśve devā havir idam juṣantām //12//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nava prāṇān navabhiḥ sam mimīte dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya /

Halfverse: c harite trīṇi rajate trīṇy ayasi trīṇi tapasāviṣṭhitāni //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agniḥ sūryaś candramā bhūmir āpo dyaur antarikṣam pradiśo diśaś ca /

Halfverse: c ārtavā ṛtubhiḥ samvidānā anena mā trivṛtā pārayantu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a trayaḥ poṣas trivṛti śrayantām anaktu pūṣā payasā ghṛtena /

Halfverse: c annasya bhūmā puruṣasya bhūmā bhūmā paśūnām ta iha śrayantām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a imam ādityā vasunā sam ukṣatemam agne vardhaya vavṛdhānaḥ /

Halfverse: c imam indra sam sṛja vīryṝṇāsmin trivṛc chrayatām poṣayiṣṇu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bhūmiṣ ṭvā pātu haritena viśvabhṛd agniḥ pipartv ayasā sajoṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c vīrudbhiṣ ṭe arjunam samvidānam dakṣam dadhātu sumanasyamānam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tredhā jātam janmanedam hiraṇyam agner ekam priyatamam babhūva somasyaikam himsitasya parāpatat /

Halfverse: c apām ekam vedhasām reta āhus tat te hiraṇyam trivṛd astv āyuṣe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tryāyuṣam jamadagneḥ kaśyapasya tryāyuṣam /

Halfverse: c tredhāmṛtasya cakṣaṇam trīṇy āyūmṣi te ‘karam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a trayah suparṇās trivṛtā yad āyann ekākṣaram abhisambhūya śakrāḥ /

Halfverse: c praty auhan mṛtyum amṛtena sākam antardadhānā duritāni viśvā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a divas tvā pātu haritam madhyāt tvā pātv arjunam /

Halfverse: c bhūmyā ayasmayam pātu prāgād devapurā ayam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a imās tisro devapurās tās tvā rakṣantu sarvataḥ /

Halfverse: c tās tvam bibhrad varcasvy uttaro dviṣatām bhava //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a puram devānām amṛtam hiraṇyam ya ābedhe prathamo devo agre /

Halfverse: c tasmai namo daśa prācīḥ kṛṇomy anu manyatām trivṛd ābadhe me //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ā tvā cṛtatv aryamā pūṣā bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c aharjātasya yan nāma tena tvāti cṛtāmasi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ṛtubhiṣ ṭvārtavair āyuṣe varcase tvā /

Halfverse: c samvatsarasya tejasā tena samhanu kṛṇmasi //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ghṛtād ulluptam madhunā samaktam bhūmidṛmham acyutam pārayiṣṇu /

Halfverse: c bhindat sapatnān adharāmś ca kṛṇvad ā mā roha mahate saubhagāya //14//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a purastād yukto vaha jātavedo ‘gne viddhi kriyamāṇam yathedam /

Halfverse: c tvam bhiṣag bheṣajasyāsi kartā tvayā gām aśvam puruṣam sanema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tathā tad agne kṛṇu jātavedo viśvebhir devaiḥ saha samvidānaḥ /

Halfverse: c yo no dideva yatamo jaghāsa yathā so asya paridhiṣ patāti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā so asya paridhiṣ patāti tathā tad agne kṛṇu jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvebhir devair saha samvidānaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a akṣyaḥ3 ni vidhya hṛdayam ni vidhya jihvām ni tṛnddhi pra dato mṛṇīhi /

Halfverse: c piśāco asya yatamo jaghāsāgne yaviṣṭha prati śṛṇīhi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yad asya hṛtam vihṛtam yat parābhṛtam ātmano jagdham yatamat piśācaiḥ /

Halfverse: c tad agne vidvān punar ā bhara tvam śarīre māmsam asum erayāmaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a āme supakve śabale vipakve yo mā piśāco aśane dadambha /

Halfverse: c tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado ‘yam astu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a kṣire mā manthe yatamo dadambhākṛṣṭapacye aśane dhānyṝ3 yaḥ /

Halfverse: c tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado ‘yam astu //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a apām mā pāne yatamo dadambha kravyād yātūnām śayane śayānam /

Halfverse: c tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado ‘yam astu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a divā mā naktam yatamo dadambha kravyād yātūnām śayane śayānam /

Halfverse: c tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado ‘yam astu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a kravyādam agne rudhiram piśācam manohanam jahi jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam indro vājī vajreṇa hantu chinattu somaḥ śiro asya dhṛṣṇuḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a sanād agne mṛṇasi yātudhānān na tvā rakṣāmsi pṛtanāsu jigyuḥ /

Halfverse: c sahamūrān anu daha kravyādo mā te hetyā mukṣata daivyāyāḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a samāhara jātavedo yad dhṛtam yat parābhṛtam /

Halfverse: c gātrāṇy asya vardhantām amśur ivā pyāyatām ayam //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a somasyeva jātavedo amśur ā pyāyatām ayam /

Halfverse: c agne virapśinam medhyam ayakṣmam kṛṇu jīvatu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a etās te agne samidhaḥ piśācajambhanīḥ /

Halfverse: c tās tvam juṣasva prati cainā gṛhāṇa jātavedaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a tārṣṭāghīr agne samidhaḥ prati gṛhṇāhy arciṣā /

Halfverse: c jahātu kravyād rūpam yo asya māmsam jihīrṣati //15//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āvatas ta āvataḥ parāvatas ta āvataḥ /

Halfverse: c ihaiva bhava mā nu gā mā pūrvān anu gāḥ pitṝn asum badhnāmi te dṛḍham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yat tvābhiceruḥ puruṣaḥ svo yad araṇo janaḥ /

Halfverse: c unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad dudrohitha śepiṣe striyai pumse acittyā /

Halfverse: c unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yat enaso mātṛkṛtāc cheṣe pitṛkṛtāc ca yat /

Halfverse: c unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yat te mātā yat te pitā jamir bhrātā ca sarjataḥ /

Halfverse: c pratyak sevasva bheṣajam jaradaṣṭim kṛṇomi tvā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ihaidhi puruṣa sarveṇa manasā saha /

Halfverse: c dūtau yamasya mānu gā adhi jīvapurā ihi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a anuhūtaḥ punar ehi vidvān udayanam pathaḥ /

Halfverse: c ārohaṇam ākramaṇam jīvatojīvato ‘yanam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mā bibher na mariṣyasi jaradaṣṭim kṛṇomi tvā /

Halfverse: c nir avocam aham yakṣmam aṅgebhyo aṅgajvaram tava //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a aṅgabhedo aṅgajvaro yaś ca te hṛdayāmayaḥ /

Halfverse: c yakṣmaḥ śyena iva prāpaptad vacā sāḍhaḥ parastarām //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ṛṣī bodhapratībodhāv asvapno yaś ca jāgṛviḥ /

Halfverse: c tau te prāṇasya goptārau divā naktam ca jāgṛtām //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ayam agnir upasadya iha sūrya ud etu te /

Halfverse: c udehi mṛtyor gambhīrāt kṛṣṇāc cit tamasas pari //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a namo yamāya namo astu mṛtyave namaḥ pitṛbhya uta ye nayanti /

Halfverse: c utpāraṇasya yo veda tam agnim puro dadhe ‘smā ariṣṭatātaye //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a aitu prāṇa aitu mana aitu cakṣur atho balam /

Halfverse: c śarīram asya sam vidām tat padbhyām prati tiṣṭhatu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a prāṇenāgne cakṣuṣā sam sṛjemam sam īraya tanvā3 sam balena /

Halfverse: c vetthāmṛtasya mā nu gān mā nu bhūmigṛho bhuvat //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a mā te prāṇa upa dasan mo apāno ‘pi dhāyi te /

Halfverse: c sūryas tvādhipatir mṛtyor udāyachatu raśmibhiḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ivam antar vadati jihvā baddhā paniṣpadā /

Halfverse: c tvayā yakṣmam nir avocam śatam ropīś ca takmanaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ayam lokaḥ priyatamo devānām aparājitaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasmai tvam iha mṛtyave diṣṭaḥ puruṣa jajñiṣe /

Halfverse: e sa ca tvānu hvayāmasi mā purā jaraso mṛthāḥ //17//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yām te cakrur āme pātre yām cakrur miśradhānye /

Halfverse: c āme māmse kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yām te cakruḥ kṛkavākāv aje vā yām kurīriṇi /

Halfverse: c avyām te kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yām te cakrur ekaśaphe paśūnām ubhayādati /

Halfverse: c gardabhe kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yām te cakrur amūlāyām valagam vā narācyām /

Halfverse: c kṣetre te kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yām te cakrur gārhapatye pūrvāgnāv uta duścitah /

Halfverse: c śālāyām kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yām te cakruḥ sabhāyām yām cakrur adhidevane /

Halfverse: c akṣeṣu kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yām te cakruḥ senāyām yām cakrur iṣvāyudhe /

Halfverse: c dundubhau kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yām te kṛtyām kūpe ‘vadadhuḥ śmaśāne vā nicakhnuḥ /

Halfverse: c sadmani kṛtyām yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yām te cakruḥ puruṣāsthe agnau samkasuke ca yām /

Halfverse: c mrokam nirdāham kravyādam punaḥ prati harāmi tām //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a apathenā jabhārainām tām pathetaḥ pra hiṇmasi /

Halfverse: c adhīro maryādhīrebhyaḥ sam jabhārācittyā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yaś cakāra na śaśāka kartum śaśre pādam aṅgurim /

Halfverse: c cakāra bhadram asmabhyam abhago bhagavadbhyaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a kṛtyākṛtam valaginam mūlinam śapatheyyam /

Halfverse: c indras tam hantu mahatā vadhenāgnir vidhyatv astayā //12//

## Book: 6

6

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a doṣo gāya bṛhad gāya dyumad dhehi /

Halfverse: c ātharvaṇa stuhi devam savitāram //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tam u ṣṭuhi yo antaḥ sindhau sūnuḥ /

Halfverse: c satyasya yuvānam adroghavācam suśevam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sa ghā no devaḥ savitā sāviṣad amṛtāni bhūri /

Halfverse: c ubhe suṣṭutī sugātave //3//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrāya somam ṛtvijaḥ sunotā ca dhāvata /

Halfverse: c stotur yo vacaḥ śṛṇavad dhavam ca me //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā yam viśantīndavo vayo na vṛkṣam andhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c virapśin vi mṛdho jahi rakṣasvinīḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sunotā somapāvne somam indrāya vajriṇe /

Halfverse: c yuvā jeteśānah sa puruṣṭutaḥ //3//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pātam na indrāpūṣaṇāditiḥ pāntu marutaḥ /

Halfverse: c apām napāt sindhavaḥ sapta pātana pātu no viṣṇur uta dyauḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pātām no dyāvāpṛthivī abhiṣṭaye pātu grāvā pātu somo no amhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c pātu no devī subhagā sarasvatī pātv agniḥ śivā ye asya pāyavaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pātām no devāśvinā śubhas patī uṣāsānaktota na uruṣyatām /

Halfverse: c apām napād abhihrutī gayasya cid deva tvaṣṭar vardhaya sarvatātaye //3//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvaṣṭā me daivyam vacaḥ parjanyo brahmaṇas patiḥ /

Halfverse: c putrair bhrātṛbhir aditir nu pātu no duṣṭaram trāyamāṇam sahaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a amśo bhago varuṇo mitro aryamāditiḥ pāntu marutaḥ /

Halfverse: c apa tasya dveṣo gamed abhihruto yāvayac chatrum antitam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dhiye sam aśvinā prāvatam na uruṣyā ṇa urujmann aprayuchan /

Halfverse: c dyaḥ3ṣ pitar yāvaya duchunā yā //3//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud enam uttaram nayāgne ghṛtenāhuta /

Halfverse: c sam enam varcasā sṛja prajayā ca bahum kṛdhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indremam prataram kṛdhi sajātānām asad vaśī /

Halfverse: c rāyas poṣeṇa sam sṛja jīvātave jarase naya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yasya kṛṇmo havir gṛhe tam agne vardhayā tvam /

Halfverse: c tasmai somo adhi bravad ayam ca brahmaṇas patiḥ //3//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo ‘smān brahmaṇas pate ‘devo abhimanyate /

Halfverse: c sarvam tam randhayāsi me yajamānāya sunvate //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo naḥ soma suśamsino duḥśamsa ādideśati /

Halfverse: c vajreṇāsya mukhe jahi sa sampiṣṭo apāyati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo naḥ somābhidāsati sanābhir yaś ca niṣṭyaḥ /

Halfverse: c apa tasya balam tira mahīva dyaur vadhatmanā //3//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yena somāditiḥ pathā mitrā vā yanty adruhaḥ /

Halfverse: c tenā no ‘vasā gahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yena soma sāhantyāsurān randhayāsi naḥ /

Halfverse: c tenā no adhi vocata //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yena devā asurāṇām ojāmsy avṛṇīdhvam /

Halfverse: c tenā naḥ śarma yachata //3//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathā vṛkṣam libujā samantam pariṣasvaje /

Halfverse: c evā pari ṣvajasva mām yathā mām kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathā suparṇaḥ prapatan pakṣau nihanti bhūmyām /

Halfverse: c evā ni hanmi te mano yathā mām kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yatheme dyāvāpṛthivī sadyaḥ paryeti sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā pary emi te mano yathā mām kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ //3//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vāñcha me tanva1m pādau vāñchākṣyaḥ3 vāñcha sakthyaḥ /

Halfverse: c akṣyaḥ vṛṣaṇyantyāḥ keśā mām te kāmena śuṣyantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mama tvā doṣaṇiśriṣam kṛṇomi hṛdayaśriṣam /

Halfverse: c yathā mama kratāv aso mama cittam upāyasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yāsām nābhir ārehaṇam hṛdi samvananam kṛtam gāvo ghṛtasya mātaro ‘mūm sam vānayantu me //3//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pṛthivyai śrotrāya vanaspatibhyo ‘gnaye ‘dhipataye svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a prāṇāyāntarikṣāya vayobhyo vāyave ‘dhipataye svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dive cakṣuṣe nakṣatrebhyaḥ sūryāyādhipataye svāhā //3//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samīm aśvattha ārūḍhas tatra pumsuvanam kṛtam /

Halfverse: c tad vai putrasya vedanam tat strīṣv ā bharāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pumsi vai reto bhavati tat striyām anu ṣicyate /

Halfverse: c tad vai putrasya vedanam tat prajāpatir abravīt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a prajāpatir anumatiḥ sinīvāly acīkḷpat /

Halfverse: c straiṣūyam anyatra dadhat pumāmsam u dadhat iha //3//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pari dyām iva sūryo ‘hīnām janimāgamam /

Halfverse: c rātrī jagad ivānyad dhamsāt tenā te vāraye viṣam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad brahmabhir yad ṛṣibhir yad devair viditam purā /

Halfverse: c yad bhūtam bhavyam āsanvat tenā te vāraye viṣam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a madhvā pṛñce nadya1ḥ parvatā girayo madhu /

Halfverse: c madhu paruṣṇī śīpālā śam āsne astu śam hṛde //3//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a namo devavadhebhyo namo rājavadhebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c atho ye viśyānām vadhās tebhyo mṛtyo namo ‘stu te //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a namas te adhivākāya parāvākāya te namaḥ /

Halfverse: c sumatyai mṛtyo te namo durmatyai te idam namaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a namas te yātudhānebhyo namas te bheṣajebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c namas te mṛtyo mūlebhyo brāhmaṇebhya idam namaḥ //3//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asthisramsam parusramsam āsthitam hṛdayāmayam /

Halfverse: c balāsam sarvam nāśayāṅgeṣṭhā yaś ca parvasu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nir balāsam balāsinaḥ kṣiṇomi muṣkaram yathā /

Halfverse: c chinadmy asya bandhanam mūlam urvārvā iva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nir balāsetaḥ pra patāśuṅgaḥ śiśuko yathā /

Halfverse: c atho ita iva hāyano ‘pa drāhy avīrahā //3//

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a uttamo asy oṣadhīnām tava vṛkṣā upastayaḥ /

Halfverse: c upastir astu so ‘smākam yo asmāṁ abhidāsati //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sabandhuś cāsabandhuś ca yo asmāṁ abhidāsati /

Halfverse: c teṣām sā vṛkṣāṇām ivāham bhūyāsam uttamaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā soma oṣadhīnām uttamo haviṣām kṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c talāśā vṛkṣānām ivāham bhūyāsam uttamaḥ //3//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ābayo anābayo rasas ta ugra ābayo /

Halfverse: c ā te karambham admasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vihahlo nāma te pitā madāvatī nāma te mātā /

Halfverse: c sa hina tvam asi yas tvam ātmānam āvayaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tauvilike ‘velayāvāyam ailaba ailayīt /

Halfverse: c babhruś ca babhrukarṇaś cāpehi nir āla //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a alasālāsi pūrva silāñjālāsy uttarā /

Halfverse: c nīlāgalasāla //4//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yatheyam pṛthivī mahī bhūtānām garbham ādadhe /

Halfverse: c evā te dhriyatām garbho anu sūtum savitave //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yatheyam pṛthivī mahī dādhāremān vanaspatīn /

Halfverse: c evā te dhriyatām garbho anu sūtum savitave //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yatheyam pṛthivī mahī dādhāra parvatān girīn /

Halfverse: c evā te dhriyatām garbho anu sūtum savitave //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yatheyam pṛthivī mahī dādhāra viṣṭhitam jagat /

Halfverse: c evā te dhriyatām garbho anu sūtum savitave //4//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a īrṣyāyā dhrājim prathamām prathamasyā utāparām /

Halfverse: c agnim hṛdayya1m śokam tam te nir vāpayāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathā bhūmir mṛtamanā mṛtān mṛtamanastarā /

Halfverse: c yathota mamruṣo mana everṣyor mṛtam manaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ado yat te hṛdi śritam manaskam patayiṣṇukam /

Halfverse: c tatas ta īrṣyām muñcāmi nir ūṣmāṇam dṛter iva //3//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a punantu mā devajanāḥ punantu manavo dhiyā /

Halfverse: c punantu viśvā bhūtāni pavamānaḥ punātu mā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pavamānaḥ punātu mā kratve dakṣāya jīvase /

Halfverse: c atho ariṣṭatātaye //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ubhābhyām deva savitaḥ pavitreṇa savena ca /

Halfverse: c asmān punīhi cakṣase //3//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agner ivāsya dahata eti śuṣmiṇa uteva matto vilapann apāyati /

Halfverse: c anyam asmad ichatu kam cid avratas tapurvadhāya namo astu takmane //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a namo rudrāya namo astu takmane namo rājñe varuṇāya tviṣīmate /

Halfverse: c namo dive namaḥ pṛthivyai nama oṣadhībhyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ayam yo abhiśocayiṣṇur viśvā rūpāṇi haritā kṛṇoṣi /

Halfverse: c tasmai te ‘ruṇāya babhrave namaḥ kṛṇomi vanyāya takmane //3//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imā yās tisraḥ pṛthivīs tāsām ha bhūmir uttamā /

Halfverse: c tāsām adhi tvaco aham bheṣajam sam u jagrabham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śreṣṭham asi bheṣajānām vasiṣṭham vīrudhānām /

Halfverse: c somo bhaga iva yāmeṣu deveṣu varuṇo yathā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a revatīr anādhṛṣaḥ siṣāsavaḥ siṣāsatha /

Halfverse: c uta stha keśadṛmhaṇīr atho ha keśavardhanīḥ //3//

Hymn: 22

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kṛṣṇam niyānam harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divam ut patanti /

Halfverse: c ta āvavṛtrant sadanād ṛtasyād id ghṛtena pṛthivīm vy ūduḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a payasvatīḥ kṛṇuthāpa oṣadhīḥ śivā yad ejathā maruto rukmavakṣasaḥ /

Halfverse: c ūrjam ca tatra sumatim ca pinvata yatrā naro marutaḥ siñcathā madhu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a udapruto marutas tāṁ iyarta vṛṣṭir yā viśvā nivatas pṛṇāti /

Halfverse: c ejāti glahā kanyṝva tunnairum tundānā patyeva jāyā //3//

Hymn: 23

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sasruṣīs tad apaso divā naktam ca sasruṣīḥ /

Halfverse: c vareṇyakratur aham apo devīr upa hvaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a otā āpaḥ karmaṇyā muñcantv itaḥ praṇītaye /

Halfverse: c sadyaḥ kṛṇvantv etave //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a devasya savituḥ save karma kṛṇvantu mānuṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c śam no bhavantv apa oṣadhīḥ śivāḥ //3//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a himavataḥ pra sravanti sindhau samaha saṅgamaḥ /

Halfverse: c āpo ha mahyam tad devīr dadan hṛddyotabheṣajam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yan me akṣyor ādidyota pārṣṇyoḥ prapadoś ca yat /

Halfverse: c āpas tat sarvam niṣ karan bhiṣajām subhiṣaktamāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sindhupatnīḥ sindhurājñīḥ sarvā yā nadya1 sthana /

Halfverse: c datta nas tasya bheṣajam tenā vo bhunajāmahai //3//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pañca ca yāḥ pañcāśac ca samyanti manyā abhi /

Halfverse: c itas tāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitām iva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sapta ca yāḥ saptatiś ca samyanti graivyā abhi /

Halfverse: c itas tāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitām iva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nava ca yā navatiś ca samyanti skandhyā abhi /

Halfverse: c itas tāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitām iva //3//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ava mā pāpman sṛja vaśī san mṛdayāsi naḥ /

Halfverse: c ā mā bhadrasya loke pāpman dhehy avihrutam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo naḥ pāpman na jahāsi tam u tvā jahimo vayam /

Halfverse: c pathām anu vyāvartane ‘nyam pāpmānu padyatām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anyatrāsman ny ḥcyatu sahasrākṣo amartyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yam dveṣāma tam ṛchatu yam u dviṣmas tam ij jahi //3//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a devāḥ kapota iṣito yad ichan dūto nirṛtyā idam ājagāma /

Halfverse: c tasmā arcāma kṛṇavāma niṣkṛtim śam no astu dvipade śam catuṣpade //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śivaḥ kapota iṣito no astv anāgā devāḥ śakuno gṛham naḥ /

Halfverse: c agnir hi vipro juṣatām havir naḥ pari hetiḥ pakṣiṇī no vṛṇaktu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a hetiḥ pakṣiṇī na dabhāty asmān āṣṭrī padam kṛṇute agnidhāne /

Halfverse: c śivo gobhya uta puruṣebhyo no astu mā no devā iha himsīt kapota //3//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ṛcā kapotam nudata praṇodam iṣam madantaḥ pari gām nayāmaḥ /

Halfverse: c samlobhayanto duritā padāni hitvā na ūrjam pra padāt pathiṣṭhaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a parīme ‘gnim arṣata parīme gām aneṣata /

Halfverse: c deveṣv akrata śravaḥ ka imāṁ ā dadharṣati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaḥ prathamaḥ pravatam āsasāda bahubhyaḥ panthām anupaspaśānaḥ /

Halfverse: c yo ‘syeśe dvipado yaś catuṣpadas tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave //3//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a amūn hetiḥ patatriṇī ny ṝtu yad ulūko vadati mogham etat /

Halfverse: c yad vā kapota padam agnau kṛṇoti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yau te dūtau nirṛta idam eto ‘prahitau prahitau vā gṛham naḥ /

Halfverse: c kapotolūkābhyām apadam tad astu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a avairahatyāyedam ā papatyāt suvīratāyā idam ā sasadyāt /

Halfverse: c parāṅ eva parā vada parācīm anu samvatam /

Halfverse: e yathā yamasya tvā gṛhe ‘rasam praticākaśān ābhūkam praticākaśān //3//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a devā imam madhunā samyutam yavam sarasvatyām adhi maṇāv acarkṛṣuḥ /

Halfverse: c indra āsīt sīrapatiḥ śatakratuḥ kīnāśā āsan marutaḥ sudānavaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yas te mado ‘vakeśo vikeśo yenābhihasyam puruṣam kṛṇoṣi /

Halfverse: c ārāt tvad anyā vanāni vṛkṣi tvam śami śatavalśā vi roha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bṛhatpalāśe subhage varṣavṛddha ṛtāvari /

Halfverse: c māteva putrebhyo mṛḍa keśebhyaḥ śami //3//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āyam gauḥ pṛśnir akramīd asadan mātaram puraḥ /

Halfverse: c pitaram ca prayant svaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a antaś carati rocanā asya prāṇād apānataḥ /

Halfverse: c vy akhyan mahiṣaḥ svaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a trimśad dhāmā vi rājati vāk pataṅgo aśiśriyat /

Halfverse: c prati vastor ahar dyubhiḥ //3//

Hymn: 32

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a antardāve juhuta sv ṝtad yātudhānakṣayaṇam ghṛtena /

Halfverse: c ārād rakṣāmsi prati daha tvam agne na no gṛhāṇām upa tītapāsi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a rudro vo grīvā aśarait piśācāh pṛṣṭīr vo ‘pi śṛṇātu yātudhānāḥ /

Halfverse: c vīrud vo viśvatovīryā yamena sam ajīgamat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a abhayam mitrāvaruṇāv ihāstu no ‘rciṣāttriṇo nudatam pratīcaḥ /

Halfverse: c mā jñātāram mā pratiṣṭhām vidanta mitho vighnānā upa yantu mṛtyum //3//

Hymn: 33

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yasyedam ā rajo yujas tuje janā navam svaḥ /

Halfverse: c indrasya rantyam bṛhat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nādhṛṣa ā dadhṛṣate dhṛṣāṇo dhṛṣitaḥ śavaḥ /

Halfverse: c purā yathā vyathiḥ śrava indrasya nādhṛṣe śavaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sa no dadātu tām rayim urum piśaṅgasamdṛśam /

Halfverse: c indraḥ patis tuviṣṭamo janeṣv ā //3//

Hymn: 34

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prāgnaye vācam īraya vṛṣabhāya kṣitīnām /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo rakṣāmsi nijūrvaty agnis tigmena śociṣā /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaḥ parasyāḥ parāvatas tiro dhanvātirocate /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yo viśvābhi vipaśyati bhuvanā sam ca paśyati /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yo asya pāre rajasaḥ śukro agnir ajāyata /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ //5//

Hymn: 35

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vaiśvānaro na ūtaya ā pra yātu parāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c agnir naḥ suṣṭutīr upa //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vaiśvānaro na āgamad imam yajñam sajūr upa /

Halfverse: c agnir uktheṣv amhasu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vaiśvānaro ‘ṅgirasām stomam uktham ca cākḷpat /

Halfverse: c aiṣu dyumnam svar yamat //3//

Hymn: 36

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ṛtāvānam vaiśvānaram ṛtasya jyotiṣas patim /

Halfverse: c ajasram gharmam īmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sa viśvā prati cākḷpa ṛtūmr ut sṛjate vaśī /

Halfverse: c yajñasya vaya uttiran //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a agniḥ pareṣu dhāmasu kāmo bhūtasya bhavyasya /

Halfverse: c samrād eko vi rājati //3//

Hymn: 37

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a upa prāgāt sahasrākṣo yuktvā śapatho ratham /

Halfverse: c śaptāram anvichan mama vṛka ivāvimato gṛham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pari ṇo vṛṅgdhi śapatha hradam agnir ivā dahan /

Halfverse: c śaptāram atra no jahi divo vṛkṣam ivāśaniḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo naḥ śapād aśapataḥ śapato yaś ca naḥ śapāt /

Halfverse: c śune peṣṭram ivāvakṣāmam tam praty asyāmi mṛtyave //3//

Hymn: 38

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a simhe vyāghra uta yā pṛdākau tviṣir agnau brāhmaṇe sūrye yā /

Halfverse: c indram yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā samvidānā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yā hastini dvīpini yā hiraṇye tviṣir apsu goṣu yā puruṣeṣu /

Halfverse: c indram yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā samvidānā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a rathe akṣeṣv ṛṣabhasya vāje vāte parjanye varuṇasya śuṣme /

Halfverse: c indram yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā samvidānā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a rājanyṝ dundubhāv āyatāyām aśvasya vāje puruṣasya māyau /

Halfverse: c indram yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā samvidānā //4//

Hymn: 39

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yaśo havir vardhatām indrajūtam sahasravīryam subhṛtam sahaskṛtam /

Halfverse: c prasarsrāṇam anu dīrghāya cakṣase haviṣmantam mā vardhaya jyeṣṭhatātaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a achā na indram yaśasam yaśobhir yaśasvinam namasānā vidhema /

Halfverse: c sa no rāsva rāṣṭram indrajūtam tasya te rātau yaśasaḥ syāma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaśā indro yaśā agnir yaśāḥ somo ajāyata /

Halfverse: c yaśā viśvasya bhūtasya aham asmi yaśastamaḥ //3//

Hymn: 40

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhayam dyāvāpṛthivī ihāstu no ‘bhayam somaḥ savitā naḥ kṛṇotu /

Halfverse: c abhayam no ‘stūrv antarikṣam saptaṛṣīṇām ca haviṣābhayam no astu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asmai grāmāya pradiśaś catasra ūrjam subhūtam svasti savitā naḥ kṛṇotu /

Halfverse: c aśatrv indro abhayam naḥ kṛṇotv anyatra rājñām abhi yātu manyuḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anamitram no adharād anamitram na uttarāt /

Halfverse: c indrānamitram naḥ paścād anamitram puras kṛdhi //3//

Hymn: 41

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a manase cetase dhiya ākūtaya uta cittaye /

Halfverse: c matyai śrutāya cakṣase vidhema haviṣā vayam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a apānāya vyānāya prāṇāya bhūridhāyase /

Halfverse: c sarasvatyā uruvyace vidhema haviṣā vayam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mā no hāsiṣur ṛṣayo daivyā ye tanūpā ye nas tanvas tanūjāḥ /

Halfverse: c amartyā martyām abhi naḥ sacadhvam āyur dhatta prataram jīvase naḥ //3//

Hymn: 42

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ava jyām iva dhanvano manyum tanomi te hṛdaḥ /

Halfverse: c yathā sammanasau bhūtvā sakhāyāv iva sacāvahai //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sakhāyāv iva sacāvahā ava manyum tanomi te /

Halfverse: c adhas te aśmano manyum upāsyāmasi yo guruḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a abhi tiṣṭhāmi te manyum pārṣṇyā prapadena ca /

Halfverse: c yathāvaśo na vādiṣo mama cittam upāyasi //3//

Hymn: 43

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam darbho vimanyukaḥ svāya cāraṇāya ca /

Halfverse: c manyor vimanyukasyāyam manyuśamana ucyate //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayam yo bhūrimūlaḥ samudram avatiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: c darbhaḥ pṛthivyā utthito manyuśamana ucyate //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vi te hanavyām śaraṇim vi te mukhyām nayāmasi /

Halfverse: c yathāvaśo na vādiṣo mama cittam upāyasi //3//

Hymn: 44

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asthād dyaur asthāt pṛthivy asthād viśvam idam jagat /

Halfverse: c asthur vṛkṣā ūrdhvasvapnās tiṣṭhād rogo ayam tava //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śatam yā bheṣajāni te sahasram samgatāni ca /

Halfverse: c śreṣṭham āsrāvabheṣajam vasiṣṭham roganāśanam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a rudrasya mūtram asy amṛtasya nābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c viṣāṇakā nāma vā asi pitṝṇām mūlād utthitā vātīkṛtanāśanī //3//

Hymn: 45

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a paro ‘pehi manaspāpa kim aśastāni śamsasi /

Halfverse: c parehi na tvā kāmaye vṛkṣām vanāni sam cara gṛheṣu goṣu me manaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a avaśasā niḥśasā yat parāśasopārima jāgrato yat svapantaḥ /

Halfverse: c agnir viśvāny apa duṣkṛtāny ajuṣṭāny āre asmad dadhātu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad indra brahmaṇas pate ‘pi mṛṣā carāmasi /

Halfverse: c pracetā na āṅgiraso duritāt pātv amhasaḥ //3//

Hymn: 46

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo na jīvo ‘si na mṛto devānām amṛtagarbho ‘si svapna /

Halfverse: c varuṇānī te mātā yamaḥ pitārarur nāmāsi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vidma te svapna janitram devajāmīnām putro ‘si yamasya karaṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c antako ‘si mṛtyur asi tam tvā svapna tathā sam vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyāt pāhi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā kalām yathā śapham yatharṇam samnayanti /

Halfverse: c evā duṣvapnyam sarvam dviṣate sam nayāmasi //3//

Hymn: 47

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agniḥ prātaḥsavane pātv asmān vaiśvānaro viśvakṛd viśvaśambhūḥ /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ pāvako draviṇe dadhātv āyuṣmantaḥ sahabhakṣāḥ syāma //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a viśve devā maruta indro asmān asmin dvitīye savane na jahyuḥ /

Halfverse: c āyuṣmantaḥ priyam eṣām vadanto vayam devānām sumatau syāma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a idam tṛtīyam savanam kavīnām ṛtena ye camasam airayanta /

Halfverse: c te saudhanvanāḥ svar ānaśānāḥ svṅṣṭim no abhi vasyo nayantu //3//

Hymn: 48

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śyeno ‘si gāyatrachandā anu tvā rabhe /

Halfverse: c svasti mā sam vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ṛbhur asi jagachandā anu tvā rabhe /

Halfverse: c svasti mā sam vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vṛṣāsi triṣṭupchandā anu tvā rabhe /

Halfverse: c svasti mā sam vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā //3//

Hymn: 49

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nahi te agne tanvaḥ krūram ānamśa martyaḥ /

Halfverse: c kapir babhasti tejanam svam jarāyu gaur iva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a meṣa iva vai sam ca vi corv acyase yad uttaradrāv uparaś ca khādataḥ /

Halfverse: c śīrṣṇā śiro ‘psasāpso ardayann amśūn babhasti haritebhir āsabhiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a suparṇā vācam akratopa dyavy ākhare kṛṣṇā iṣirā anartiṣuḥ /

Halfverse: c ni yan niyanti uparasya niṣkṛtim purū reto dadhire sūryaśritaḥ //3//

Hymn: 50

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a hatam tardam samaṅkam ākhum aśvinā chintam śiro api pṛṣṭīḥ śṛṇītam /

Halfverse: c yavān ned adān api nahyatam mukham athābhayam kṛṇutam dhānyāya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tarda hai pataṅga hai jabhya hā upakvasa /

Halfverse: c brahmevāsamsthitam havir anadanta imān yavān ahimsanto apodita //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tardāpate vaghāpate tṛṣṭajambhā ā śṛṇota me /

Halfverse: c ya āraṇyā vyadvarā ye ke ca stha vyadvarās tānt sarvān jambhayāmasi //3//

Hymn: 51

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vāyoḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa pratyaṅ somo ati drutaḥ /

Halfverse: c indrasya yujaḥ sakhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āpo asmān mātaraḥ sūdayantu ghṛtena no ghṛtapvah punantu /

Halfverse: c viśvam hi ripram pravahanti devīr ud id ābhyaḥ śucir ā pūta emi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat kim cedam varuṇa daivye jane ‘bhidroham manuṣyā3ś caranti /

Halfverse: c acittyā cet tava dharma yuyopima mā nas tasmād enaso deva rīriṣaḥ //3//

Hymn: 52

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ut sūryo diva eti puro raksāmsi nijūrvan /

Halfverse: c ādityaḥ parvatebhyo viśvadṛṣṭo adṛṣṭahā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ni gāvo goṣṭhe asadan ni mṛgāso avikṣata /

Halfverse: c ny ū3rmayo nadīnam ny adṛṣṭā alipsata //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āyurdadam vipaścitam śrutām kaṇvasya vīrudham /

Halfverse: c ābhāriṣam viśvabheṣajīm asyādṛṣṭān ni śamayat //3//

Hymn: 53

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dyauś ca ma idam pṛthivī ca pracetasau śukro bṛhan dakṣiṇayā pipartu /

Halfverse: c anu svadhā cikitām somo agnir vāyur naḥ pātu savitā bhagaś ca //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a punaḥ prāṇaḥ punar ātmā na aitu punaś cakṣuḥ punar asur na aitu /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānaro no adabdhas tanūpā antas tiṣṭhāti duritāni viśvā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sam varcasā payasā sam tanūbhir aganmahi manasā sam śivena /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭā no atra varīyaḥ kṛṇotv anu no mārṣṭu tanvo3 yad viriṣṭam //3//

Hymn: 54

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam tad yuja uttaram indram śumbhāmy aṣṭaye /

Halfverse: c asya kṣatram śriyam mahīm vṛṣṭir iva vardhayā tṛṇam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asmai kṣatram agnīṣomāv asmai dhārayatam rayim /

Halfverse: c imam rāṣṭrasyābhīvarge kṛṇutam yuja uttaram //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sabandhuś cāsabandhuś ca yo asmāṁ abhidāsati /

Halfverse: c sarvam tam randhayāsi me yajamānāya sunvate //3//

Hymn: 55

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye panthāno bahavo devayānā antarā dyāvāpṛthivī samcaranti /

Halfverse: c teṣām ajyānim yatamo vahāti tasmai mā devāḥ pari datteha sarve //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a grīṣmo hemantaḥ śiśiro vasantaḥ śarad varṣāḥ svite no dadhāta /

Halfverse: c ā no goṣu bhajatā prajāyām nivāta id vaḥ śaraṇe syāma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a idāvatsarāya parivatsarāya samvatsarāya kṛṇutā bṛhan namaḥ /

Halfverse: c teṣām vayam sumatau yajñiyānām api bhadre sau manase syāma //3//

Hymn: 56

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mā no devā ahir vadhīt satokānt sahapuruṣān /

Halfverse: c samyatam na vi ṣparad vyāttam na sam yaman namo devajanebhyaḥ /

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a namo ‘stv asitāya namas tiraścirājaye /

Halfverse: c svajāya babhrave namo namo devajanebhyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sam te hanmi datā dataḥ sam u te hanvā hanū /

Halfverse: c sam te jihvayā jihvām sam v āsnāha āsyam //3//

Hymn: 57

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam id vā u bheṣajam idam rudrasya bheṣajam /

Halfverse: c yeneṣum ekatejanām śataśalyām apabravat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jālāṣeṇābhi ṣiñcata jālāṣeṇopa siñcata /

Halfverse: c jālāṣam ugram bheṣajam tena no mṛḍa jīvase //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śam ca no mayaś ca no mā ca naḥ kim canāmamat /

Halfverse: c kṣamā rapo viśvam no astu bheṣajam sarvam no astu bheṣajam //3//

Hymn: 58

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yaśasam mendro maghavān kṛṇotu yaśasam dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime /

Halfverse: c yaśasam mā devaḥ savitā kṛṇotu priyo dātur dakṣiṇāyā iha syām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathendro dyāvāpṛthivyor yaśasvān yathāpa oṣadhīṣu yaśasvatīḥ /

Halfverse: c evā viśveṣu deveṣu vayam sarveṣu yaśasaḥ syāma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaśā indro yaśā agnir yaśāḥ somo ajāyata /

Halfverse: c yaśā viśvasya bhūtasyāham asmi yaśastamaḥ //3//

Hymn: 59

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a anaḍudbhyas tvam prathamam dhenubhyas tvam arundhati /

Halfverse: c adhenave vayase śarma yacha catuṣpade //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śarma yachatv oṣadhiḥ saha devīr arundhatī /

Halfverse: c karat payasvantam goṣṭham ayakṣmāṁ uta pūruṣān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a viśvarūpām subhagām achāvadāmi jīvalām /

Halfverse: c sā no rudrasyāstām hetim dūram nayatu gobhyaḥ //3//

Hymn: 60

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam ā yāty aryamā purastād viṣitastupaḥ /

Halfverse: c asyā ichann agruvai patim uta jāyām ajānaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aśramad iyam aryamann anyāsām samanam yatī /

Halfverse: c aṅgo nv aryamann asyā anyāḥ samanam āyati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dhātā dādhāra pṛthivīm dhātā dyām uta sūryam /

Halfverse: c dhātāsyā agruvai patim dadhātu pratikāmyam //3//

Hymn: 61

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mahyam āpo madhumad erayantām mahyam sūro abharaj jyotiṣe kam /

Halfverse: c mahyam devā uta viśve tapojā mahyam devaḥ savitā vyaco dhāt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aham viveca pṛthivīm uta dyām aham ṛtūmr ajanayam sapta sākam /

Halfverse: c aham satyam anṛtam yad vadāmy aham daivīm pari vācam viśaś ca //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a aham jajāna pṛthivīm uta dyām aham ṛtūmr ajanayam sapta sindhūn /

Halfverse: c aham satvam anṛtam yad vadāmi yo agnīṣomāv ajuṣe sakhāyā //3//

Hymn: 62

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vaiśvānaro raśmibhir naḥ punātu vātaḥ prāṇeneṣiro nabhobhiḥ /

Halfverse: c dyāvāpṛthivī payasā payasvatī ṛtāvarī yajñiye na punītām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vaiśvānarīm sūnṛtām ā rabhadhvam yasyā āśās tanvo vītapṛṣṭhāḥ /

Halfverse: c tayā gṛṇantaḥ sadhamādeṣu vayam syāma patayo rayīnām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vaiśvānarīm varcasa ā rabhadhvam śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ /

Halfverse: c iheḍayā sadhamādam madanto jyok paśyema sūryam uccarantam //3//

Hymn: 63

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yat te devī nirṛtir ābabandha dāma grīvāsv avimokyam yat /

Halfverse: c tat te vi ṣyāmy āyuṣe varcase balāyādomadam annam addhi prasūtaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a namo ‘stu te nirṛte tigmatejo ‘yasmayān vi cṛtā bandhapāśān /

Halfverse: c yamo mahyam punar it tvām dadāti tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ayasmaye drupade bedhiṣe ihābhihito mṛtyubhir ye sahasram /

Halfverse: c yamena tvam pitṛbhiḥ samvidāna uttamam nākam adhi rohayemam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a samsam id yuvase vṛṣann agne viśvāny arya ā /

Halfverse: c iḍas pade sam idhyase sa no vasūny ā bhara //4//

Hymn: 64

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam jānīdhvam sam pṛcyadhvam sam vo manāmsi jānatām /

Halfverse: c devā bhāgam yathā pūrve samjānānā upāsate //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a samāno mantraḥ samitiḥ samānī samānam vratam saha cittam eṣām /

Halfverse: c samānena vo haviṣā juhomi samānam ceto abhisamviśadhvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samānī va ākūtiḥ samānā hṛdayāni vaḥ /

Halfverse: c samānam astu vo manaḥ yathā vaḥ susahāsati //3//

Hymn: 65

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ava manyur avāyatāva bāhū manoyujā /

Halfverse: c parāśara tvam teṣām parāñcam śuṣmam ardayādhā no rayim ā kṛdhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nirhastebhyo nairhastam yam devāḥ śarum asyatha /

Halfverse: c vṛścāmi śatrūṇām bāhūn anena haviṣā ‘ham //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indraś cakāra prathamam nairhastam asurebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c jayantu satvāno mama sthireṇendreṇa medinā //3//

Hymn: 66

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nirhastaḥ śatrur abhidāsann astu ye senābhir yudham āyanty asmān /

Halfverse: c sam arpayendra mahatā vadhena drātv eṣām aghahāro vividdhaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ātanvānā āyachanto ‘syanto ye ca dhāvatha /

Halfverse: c nirhastāḥ śatravaḥ sthanendro vo ‘dya parāśarīt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nirhastāḥ santu śatravo ‘ṅgaiṣām mlāpayāmasi /

Halfverse: c athaiṣām indra vedāmsi śataśo vi bhajāmahai //3//

Hymn: 67

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pari vartmāni sarvata indraḥ pūṣā ca sasratuḥ /

Halfverse: c muhyantv adyāmūḥ senā amitrāṇām parastarām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mūḍhā amitrāś caratāśīrṣāṇa ivāhayaḥ /

Halfverse: c teṣām vo agnimūḍhānām indro hantu varamvaram //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a aiṣu nahya vṛṣājinam hariṇasya bhiyam kṛdhi /

Halfverse: c parāṅ amitra eṣatv arvācī gaur upeṣatu //3//

Hymn: 68

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āyam agant savitā kṣureṇoṣṇena vāya udakenehi /

Halfverse: c ādityā rudrā vasava undantu sacetasaḥ somasya rājño vapata pracetasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aditiḥ śmaśru vapatv āpa undantu varcasā /

Halfverse: c cikitsatu prajāpatir dīrghāyutvāya cakṣase //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yenāvapat savitā kṣureṇa somasya rājño varuṇasya vidvān /

Halfverse: c tena brahmāṇo vapatedam asya gomān aśvavān ayam astu prajāvān //3//

Hymn: 69

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a girāv aragarāṭeṣu hiranye goṣu yad yaśaḥ /

Halfverse: c surāyām sicyamānāyām kīlāle madhu tan mayi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aśvinā sāragheṇa mā madhunāṅktam śubhas patī /

Halfverse: c yathā bhargasvatīm vācam āvadāni janāṁ anu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mayi varco atho yaśo ‘tho yajñasya yat payaḥ /

Halfverse: c tan mayi prajāpatir divi dyām iva dṛmhatu //3//

Hymn: 70

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathā māmsam yathā surā yathākṣā adhidevane /

Halfverse: c yathā pumso vṛṣaṇyata striyām nihanyate manaḥ /

Halfverse: e evā te aghnye mano ‘dhi vatse ni hanyatām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathā hastī hastinyāḥ padena padam udyuje /

Halfverse: c yathā pumso vṛṣaṇyata striyām nihanyate manaḥ /

Halfverse: e evā te aghnye mano ‘dhi vatse ni hanyatām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā pradhir yathopadhir yathā nabhyam pradhāv adhi /

Halfverse: c yathā pumso vṛṣaṇyata striyām nihanyate manaḥ /

Halfverse: e evā te aghnye mano ‘dhi vatse ni hanyatām //3//

Hymn: 71

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad annam admi bahudhā virūpam hiraṇyam aśvam uta gām ajām avim /

Halfverse: c yad eva kim ca pratijagrahāham agniṣ ṭad dhotā suhutam kṛnotu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yan mā hutam ahutam ājagāma dattam pitṛbhir anumatam manuṣyaiḥ /

Halfverse: c yasmān me mana ud iva rārajīty agniṣ ṭad dhotā suhutam kṛṇotu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad annam admy anṛtena devā dāsyann adāsyann uta samgṛṇāmi /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānarasya mahato mahimnā śivam mahyam madhumad astv annam //3//

Hymn: 72

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathāsitaḥ prathayate vaśām anu vapūmṣi kṛṇvann asurasya māyayā /

Halfverse: c evā te śepaḥ sahasāyam arko ‘ṅgenāṅgam samsamakam kṛṇotu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathā pasas tāyādaram vātena sthūlabham kṛtam /

Halfverse: c yāvat parasvataḥ pasas tāvat te vardhatām pasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yāvad aṅgīnam pārasvatam hāstinam gārdabham ca yat /

Halfverse: c yāvad aśvasya vajinas tāvat te vardhatām pasaḥ //3//

Hymn: 73

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a eha yātu varuṇaḥ somo agnir bṛhaspatir vasubhir eha yātu /

Halfverse: c asya śriyam upasamyāta sarva ugrasya cettuḥ sammanasaḥ sajātāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo vaḥ śuṣmo hṛdayeṣv antar ākūtir yā vo manasi praviṣṭā /

Halfverse: c tānt sīvayāmi haviṣā ghṛtena mayi sajātā ramatir vo astu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ihaiva sta māpa yātādhy asmat pūṣā parastād apatham vaḥ kṛṇotu /

Halfverse: c vāstoṣ patir anu vo johavītu mayi sajātā ramatiḥ vo astu //3//

Hymn: 74

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam vaḥ pṛcyantām tanva1ḥ sam manāmsi sam u vratā /

Halfverse: c sam vo ‘yam brahmaṇas patir bhagaḥ sam vo ajīgamat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a samjñapanam vo manaso ‘tho samjñapanam hṛdaḥ /

Halfverse: c atho bhagasya yac chrāntam tena samjñapayāmi vaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathādityā vasubhiḥ sambabhūvur marudbhir ugrā ahṛṇīyamānāḥ /

Halfverse: c evā triṇāmann ahṛṇīyamāna imān janānt sammanasas kṛdhīha //3//

Hymn: 75

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nir amum nuda okasaḥ sapatno yaḥ pṛtanyati /

Halfverse: c nairbādhyṝna haviṣendra enam parāśarīt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a paramām tam parāvatam indro nudatu vṛtrahā /

Halfverse: c yato na punar āyati śaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a etu tisraḥ parāvata etu pañca janāṁ ati /

Halfverse: c etu tisro ‘ti rocanā yato na punar āyati /

Halfverse: e śaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyo yāvat sūryo asad divi //3//

Hymn: 76

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ya enam pariṣīdanti samādadhati cakṣase /

Halfverse: c sampreddho agnir jihvābhir ud etu hṛdayād adhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agneḥ sāmtapanasyāham āyuṣe padam ā rabhe /

Halfverse: c addhātir yasya paśyati dhūmam udyantam āsyataḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo asya samidham veda kṣatriyeṇa samāhitām /

Halfverse: c nābhihvāre padam ni dadhāti sa mṛtyave //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a nainam ghnanti paryāyino na sannāṁ ava gachati /

Halfverse: c agner yaḥ kṣatriyo vidvān nāma gṛhnāti āyuṣe //4//

Hymn: 77

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asthād dyaur asthāt pṛthivy asthād viśvam idam jagat /

Halfverse: c āsthāne parvatā asthu sthāmny aśvāṁ atiṣṭhipam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ya udānat parāyaṇam ya udānaṇ nyāyanam /

Halfverse: c āvartanam nivartanam yo gopā api tam huve //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a jātavedo ni vartaya śatam te santv āvṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c sahasram ta upāvṛtas tabhir naḥ punar ā kṛdhi //3//

Hymn: 78

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tena bhūtena haviṣāyam ā pyāyatām punaḥ /

Halfverse: c jāyām yām asmā āvākṣus tām rasenābhi vardhatām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a abhi vardhatām payasābhi rāṣṭreṇa vardhatām /

Halfverse: c rayyā sahasravarcasemau stām anupakṣitau //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvaṣṭā jāyām ajanayat tvaṣṭāsyai tvām patim /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭā sahasram āyumṣi dīrgham āyuḥ kṛṇotu vām //3//

Hymn: 79

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam no nabhasas patiḥ samsphāno abhi rakṣatu /

Halfverse: c asamātim gṛheṣu naḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvam no nabhasas pate ūrjam gṛhesu dhāraya /

Halfverse: c ā puṣṭam etv ā vasu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a deva samsphāna sahasrāpoṣasyeśiṣe /

Halfverse: c tasya no rāsva tasya no dhehi tasya te bhaktivāmsaḥ syāma //3//

Hymn: 80

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a antarikseṇa patati viśvā bhūtāvacākaśat /

Halfverse: c śuno divyasya yan mahas tenā te haviṣā vidhema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ye trayaḥ kālakāñjā divi devā iva śritāḥ /

Halfverse: c tān sarvān ahva ūtaye ‘smā ariṣṭatātaye //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apsu te janma divi te sadhastham samudre antar mahimā te pṛthivyām /

Halfverse: c śuno divyasya yan mahas tenā te haviṣā vidhema //3//

Hymn: 81

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yantāsi yachase hastāv apa rakṣāmsi sedhasi /

Halfverse: c prajām dhanam ca gṛhṇānaḥ parihasto abhūd ayam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a parihasta vi dhāraya yonim garbhāya dhātave /

Halfverse: c maryāde putram ā dhehi tam tvam ā gamayāgame //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yam parihastam abibhar aditiḥ putrakāmyā /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭā tam asyā ā badhnād yathā putram janād //3//

Hymn: 82

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āgachata āgatasya nāma gṛhṇāmy āyataḥ /

Halfverse: c indrasya vṛtraghno vanve vāsavasya śatakratoḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yena sūryām sāvitrīm aśvinohatuḥ pathā /

Halfverse: c tena mām abravīd bhago jayām ā vahatād iti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yas te ‘ṅkuśo vasudāno bṛhann indra hiraṇyayaḥ /

Halfverse: c tenā janīyate jāyām mahyam dhehi śacīpate //3//

Hymn: 83

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apacitaḥ pra patata suparṇo vasater iva /

Halfverse: c sūryaḥ kṛṇotu bheṣajam candramā vo ‘pochatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a eny ekā śyeny ekā kṛṣṇaikā rohiṇī dve /

Halfverse: c sarvāsām agrabham nāmāvīraghnīr apetana //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asūtikā rāmāyaṇy apacit pra patiṣyati /

Halfverse: c glaur itaḥ pra patiṣyati sa galunto naśiṣyati //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vīhi svām āhutim juṣāno manasā svāhā manasā yad idam juhomi //4//

Hymn: 84

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yasyās ta āsani ghore juhomy eṣām baddhānām avasarjanāya kam /

Halfverse: c bhūmir iti tvābhipramanvate janā nirṛtir iti tvāham pari veda sarvataḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a bhūte haviṣmatī bhavaiṣa te bhāgo yo asmāsu /

Halfverse: c muñcemān amūn enasaḥ svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a evo ṣv asman nirṛte ‘nehā tvam ayasmayān vi cṛtā bandhapāśān /

Halfverse: c yamo mahyam punar it tvām dadāti tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ayasmaye drupade bedhiṣa ihābhihito mṛtyubhir ye sahasram /

Halfverse: c yamena tvam pitṛbhiḥ samvidāna uttamam nākam adhi rohayemam //4//

Hymn: 85

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a varaṇo vārayātā ayam devo vanaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c yakṣmo yo asminn āviṣṭas tam u devā avīvaran //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indrasya vacasā vayam mitrasya varuṇasya ca /

Halfverse: c devānām sarveṣām vācā yakṣmam te vārayāmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā vṛtra imā āpas tastambha viśvadhā yatīḥ /

Halfverse: c evā te agninā yakṣmam vaiśvānareṇa vāraye //3//

Hymn: 86

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vṛṣendrasya vṛṣā divo vṛsā pṛthivyā ayam /

Halfverse: c vṛṣā viśvasya bhūtasya tvam ekavṛṣo bhava //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a samudra īśe sravatām agniḥ pṛthivyā vaśī /

Halfverse: c candramā nakṣatrāṇām īśe tvam ekavṛṣo bhava //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samrāḍ asy asurāṇām kakun manuṣyānām /

Halfverse: c devānām ardhabhāg asi tvam ekavṛṣo bhava //3//

Hymn: 87

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā tvāhārṣam antar abhūr dhruvas tiṣṭhāvicācalat /

Halfverse: c viśas tvā sarvā vāñchantu mā tvad rāṣṭram adhi bhraśat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ihaivaidhi māpa cyoṣṭhāḥ parvata ivāvicācalat /

Halfverse: c indra iveha dhruvas tiṣṭheha rāṣṭram u dhāraya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indra etam adīdharat dhruvam dhruveṇa haviṣā /

Halfverse: c tasmai somo adhi bravad ayam ca brahmaṇas patiḥ //3//

Hymn: 88

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dhruvā dyaur dhruvā pṛthivī dhruvam viśvam idam jagat /

Halfverse: c dhruvāsaḥ parvatā ime dhruvo rājā viśām ayam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dhruvam te rājā varuṇo dhruvam devo bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c dhruvam ta indraś cāgniś ca rāṣṭram dhārayatām dhruvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dhruvo ‘cyutaḥ pra mṛṇīhi śatrūn chatrūyato ‘dharān pādayasva /

Halfverse: c sarvā diśaḥ sammanasaḥ sadhrīcīr dhruvāya te samitiḥ kalpatām iha //3//

Hymn: 89

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam yat preṇyaḥ śiro dattam somena vṛṣṇyam /

Halfverse: c tataḥ pari prajātena hārdim te śocayāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śocayāmasi te hārdim śocayāmasi te manaḥ /

Halfverse: c vātam dhūma iva sadhrya1ṅ mām evānv etu ye manaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mahyam tvā mitrāvaruṇau mahyam devī sarasvatī /

Halfverse: c mahyam tvā madhyam bhūmyā ubhāv antau sam asyatām //3//

Hymn: 90

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yām te rudra iṣum āsyad aṅgebhyo hṛdayāya ca /

Halfverse: c idam tām adya tvad vayam viṣūcīm vi vṛhāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yās te śatam dhamanayo ‘ṅgāny anu viṣṭhitāḥ /

Halfverse: c tāsām te sarvāsām vayam nir viṣāṇi hvayāmasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a namas te rudrāsyate namaḥ pratihitāyai /

Halfverse: c namo visṛjyamānāyai namo nipatitāyai //3//

Hymn: 91

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imam yavam aṣṭāyogaiḥ ṣadyogebhir acarkṛṣuḥ /

Halfverse: c tenā te tanvo3 rapo ‘pācīnam apa vyaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nya1g vāto vāti nyak tapati sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c nīcīnam aghnyā duhe nyag bhavatu te rapaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āpa id vā u bheṣajīr āpo amīvacātanīḥ /

Halfverse: c āpo viśvasya bheṣajīs tās te kṛṇvantu bheṣajam //3//

Hymn: 92

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vātaramhā bhava vājin yujamāna indrasya yāhi prasave manojavāḥ /

Halfverse: c yuñjantu tvā maruto viśvavedasa ā te tvastā patsu javam dadhātu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a javas te arvan nihito guhā yaḥ śyene vāte uta yo ‘carat parīttaḥ /

Halfverse: c tena tvam vājin balavān balenājim jaya samane parayiṣṇuḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tanūṣ ṭe vājin tanva1m nayantī vāmam asmabhyam dhāvatu śarma tubhyam /

Halfverse: c ahruto maho dharuṇāya devo divīva jyotih svam ā mimīyāt //3//

Hymn: 93

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yamo mṛtyur aghamāro nirṛtho babhruḥ śarvo ‘stā nīlaśikhaṇḍaḥ /

Halfverse: c devajanāḥ senayottasthivāmsas te asmākam pari vṛñjantu vīrān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a manasā homair harasā ghṛtena śarvāyāstra uta rājñe bhavāya /

Halfverse: c namasyṝbhyo nama ebhyaḥ kṛṇomy anyatrāsmad aghaviṣā nayantu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a trāyadhvam no aghaviṣābhyo vadhād viśve devā maruto viśvavedasaḥ /

Halfverse: c agnīṣomā varuṇaḥ pūtadakṣā vātāparjanyayoḥ sumatau syāma //3//

Hymn: 94

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam vo manāmsi sam vratā sam ākūtīr namāmasi /

Halfverse: c amī ye vivratā sthana tān vaḥ sam namayāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aham gṛbhṇāmi manasā manāmsi mama cittam anu cittebhir eta /

Halfverse: c mama vaśeṣu hṛdayāni vaḥ kṛṇomi mama yātam anuvartmāna eta //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ote me dyāvāpṛthivī otā devī sarasvatī /

Halfverse: c otau ma indraś cāgniś ca rdhyāsmedam sarasvati //3//

Hymn: 95

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aśvattho devasadanas tṛtīyasyām ito divi /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇam devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a hiraṇyayī naur acarad dhiraṇyabandhanā divi /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasya puṣpam devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a garbho asy oṣadhīnām garbho himavatām uta /

Halfverse: c garbho viśvasya bhūtasyemam me agadam kṛdhi //3//

Hymn: 96

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yā oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīr bahvīḥ śatavicakṣaṇāḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatiprasūtās tā no muñcantv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a muñcantu mā śapathyā3d atho varuṇyād uta /

Halfverse: c atho yamasya paḍvīṣād viśvasmād devakilbiṣāt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yac cakṣuṣā manasā yac ca vācopārima jāgrato yat svapantaḥ /

Halfverse: c somas tāni svadhayā naḥ punātu //3//

Hymn: 97

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhibhūr yajño abhibhūr agnir abhibhūḥ somo abhibhūr indraḥ /

Halfverse: c abhy aham viśvāḥ pṛtanā yathāsāny evā vidhemāgnihotrā idam haviḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a svadhāstu mitrāvaruṇā vipaścitā prajāvat kṣatram madhuneha pinvatam /

Halfverse: c bādhethām dūram nirṛtim parācaiḥ kṛtam cid enaḥ pra mumuktam asmat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a imam vīram anu harṣadhvam ugram indram sakhāyo anu sam rabhadhvam /

Halfverse: c grāmajitam gojitam vajrabāhum jayantam ajma pramṛṇantam ojasā //3//

Hymn: 98

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indro jayāti na parā jayātā adhirājo rājasu rājayātai /

Halfverse: c carkṛtya īḍyo vandyaś copasadyo namasyś bhaveha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvam indrādhirājaḥ śravasyus tvam bhūr abhibhūtir janānām /

Halfverse: c tvam daivīr viśa imā vi rājāyuṣmat kṣatram ajaram te astu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a prācyā diśas tvam indrāsi rājotodīcyā diśo vṛtrahan chatruho ‘si /

Halfverse: c yatra yanti srotyās taj jitam te dakṣiṇato vṛṣabha eṣi havyaḥ //3//

Hymn: 99

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi tvendra varimataḥ purā tvāmhūraṇād dhuve /

Halfverse: c hvayāmy ugram cettāram puruṇāmānam ekajam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo adya senyo vadho jighāmsan na udīrate /

Halfverse: c indrasya tatra bāhū samantam pari dadmaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pari dadma indrasya bāhū samantam trātus trāyatām naḥ /

Halfverse: c deva savitaḥ soma rājan sumanasam mā kṛṇu svastaye //3//

Hymn: 100

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a devā aduḥ sūryo dyaur adāt pṛthivy adāt /

Halfverse: c tisraḥ sarasvatir aduḥ sacittā viṣadūṣaṇam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad vo devā upajīkā āsiñcan dhanvany udakam /

Halfverse: c tena devaprasūtenedam dūṣayatā viṣam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asurāṇām duhitāsi sā devānām asi svasā /

Halfverse: c divas pṛthivyāḥ sambhūtā sā cakarthārasam viṣam //3//

Hymn: 101

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā vṛṣāyasva śvasihi vardhasva prathayasva ca /

Halfverse: c yathāṅgam vardhatām śepas tena yoṣitam ij jahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yena kṛṣam vājayanti yena hinvanty āturam /

Halfverse: c tenāsya brahmaṇas pate dhanur ivā tānayā pasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āham tanomi te paso adhi jyām iva dhanvani /

Halfverse: c kramasva ṛṣa iva rohitam anavaglāyatā sadā //3//

Hymn: 102

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathayam vāho aśvinā samaiti sam ca vartate /

Halfverse: c evā mām abhi te manaḥ samaitu sam ca vartatām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āham khidāmi te mano rājāśvaḥ pṛṣṭyām iva /

Halfverse: c reṣmachinnam yathā tṛṇam mayi te veṣṭatām manaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āñjanasya madughasya kuṣṭhasya naladasya ca /

Halfverse: c turo bhagasya hastābhyām anurodhanam ud bhare //3//

Hymn: 103

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samdānam vo bṛhaspatiḥ samdānam savitā karat /

Halfverse: c samdānam mitro aryamā samdānam bhago aśvinā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam paramānt sam avamān atho sam dyāmi madhyamān /

Halfverse: c indras tān pary ahār dāmnā tān agne sam dyā tvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a amī ye yudham āyanti ketūn kṛtvānīkaśaḥ /

Halfverse: c indras tān pary ahār dāmna tān agne sam dyā tvam //3//

Hymn: 104

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ādānena samdānenāmitrān ā dyāmasi /

Halfverse: c apānā ye caiṣām prāṇā asunāsūnt sam achidan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a idam ādānam akaram tapasendreṇa samśitam /

Halfverse: c amitrā ye ‘tra naḥ santi tān agna ā dyā tvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ainān dyatām indrāgnī somo rājā ca medinau /

Halfverse: c indro marutvān ādānam amitrebhyaḥ kṛṇotu naḥ //3//

Hymn: 105

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathā mano manasketaiḥ parāpataty āśumat /

Halfverse: c evā tvam kāse pra pata manaso ‘nu pravāyyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yathā bāṇaḥ susamśitaḥ parāpataty āśumat /

Halfverse: c evā tvam kāse pra pata pṛthivyā anu samvatam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā sūryasya raśmayaḥ parāpatanty āśumat /

Halfverse: c evā tvam kāse pra pata samudrasyānu vikṣaram //3//

Hymn: 106

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āyane te parāyane dūrvā rohantu puṣpiṇīḥ /

Halfverse: c utso vā tatra jāyatām hrado vā puṇḍarīkavān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a apām idam nyayanam samudrasya niveśanam /

Halfverse: c madhye hradasya no gṛhāḥ parācīnā mukhā kṛdhi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a himasya tvā jarāyuṇā śāle pari vyayāmasi /

Halfverse: c śītahradā hi no bhuvo ‘gniṣ kṛṇotu bheṣajam //3//

Hymn: 107

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a viśvajit trāyamāṇāyai mā pari dehi /

Halfverse: c trāyamāṇe dvipāc ca sarvam no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a trāyamāṇe viśvajite mā pari dehi /

Halfverse: c viśvajid dvipāc ca sarvam no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a viśvajit kalyāṇyai mā pari dehi /

Halfverse: c kalyāṇi dvipāc ca sarvam no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kalyāṇi sarvavide mā pari dehi /

Halfverse: c sarvavid dvipāc ca sarvam no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam //4//

Hymn: 108

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvam no medhe prathamā gobhir aśvebhir ā gahi /

Halfverse: c tvam sūryasya raśmibhis tvam no asi yajñiyā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a medhām aham prathamām brahmaṇvatīm brahmajūtām ṛṣiṣṭutām /

Halfverse: c prapītām brahmacāribhir devānām avase huve //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yām medhām ṛbhavo vidur yām medhām asurā viduḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛṣayo bhadrām medhām yām vidus tām mayy ā veśayāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yām ṛṣayo bhūtakṛto medhām medhāvino viduḥ /

Halfverse: c tayā mām adya medhayāgne medhāvinam kṛṇu //4//

Verse: 5
