Halfverse: a medhām sāyam medhām prātar medhām madhyandinam pari /

Halfverse: c medhām sūryasya raśmibhir vacasā veśayāmahe //5//

Hymn: 109

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pippalī kṣiptabheṣajy ḥ3tātividdhabheṣajī /

Halfverse: c tām devāḥ sam akalpayann iyam jīvitavā alam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pippalya1ḥ sam avadantāyatīr jananād adhi /

Halfverse: c yam jīvam aśnavāmahai na sa riṣyāti pūruṣaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asurās tvā ny akhanan devās tvod avapan punaḥ /

Halfverse: c vātīkṛtasya bheṣajīm atho kṣiptasya bheṣajīm //3//

Hymn: 110

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pratno hi kam īḍyo adhvareṣu sanāc ca hotā navyaś ca satsi /

Halfverse: c svām cāgne tanvam piprāyasvāsmabhyam ca saubhagam ā yajasva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jyeṣṭhaghnyām jāto vicṛtor yamasya mūlabarhaṇāt pari pāhy enam /

Halfverse: c aty enam neṣad duritāni viśvā dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vyāghre ‘hny ajaniṣṭa vīro nakṣatrajā jāyamānaḥ suvīraḥ /

Halfverse: c sa mā vadhīt pitaram vardhamāno mā mātaram pra minīj janitrīm //3//

Hymn: 111

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imam me agne puruṣam mumugdhy ayam yo baddhaḥ suyato lālapīti /

Halfverse: c ato ‘dhi te kṛṇavad bhāgadheyam yadānunmadito ‘sati //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agniṣ ṭe ni śamayatu yadi te mana udyatam /

Halfverse: c kṛṇomi vidvān bheṣajam yathānunmadito ‘sasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a devainasād unmaditam unmattam rakṣasas pari /

Halfverse: c kṛṇomi vidvān bheṣajam yadānunmadito ‘sati //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a punas tvā dur apsarasaḥ punar indraḥ punar bhagaḥ /

Halfverse: c punas tvā dur viśve devā yathānunmadito ‘sasi //4//

Hymn: 112

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mā jyeṣṭham vadhīd ayam agna eṣām mūlabarhaṇāt pari pāhy enam /

Halfverse: c sa grāhyāḥ pāśān vi cṛta prajānan tubhyam devā anu jānantu viśve //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a un muñca pāśāms tvam agna eṣām trayas tribhir utsitā yebhir āsan /

Halfverse: c sa grāhyāḥ pāśān vi cṛta prajānan pitāputrau mātaram muñca sarvān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yebhiḥ pāśaiḥ parivitto vibaddho ‘ṅgeaṅga ārpita utsitaś ca /

Halfverse: c vi te mucyantam vimuco hi santi bhrūṇaghni pūṣan duritāni mṛkṣva //3//

Hymn: 113

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a trite devā amṛjataitad enas trita enan manuṣyṝṣu mamṛje /

Halfverse: c tato yadi tvā grāhir ānaśe tām te devā brahmaṇā nāśayantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a marīcīr dhūmān pra viśānu pāpmann udārān gachota vā nīhārān /

Halfverse: c nadīnam phenām anu tān vi naśya bhrūṇaghni pūṣan duritāni mṛkṣva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dvādaśadhā nihitam tritasyāpamṛṣṭam manuṣyainasāni /

Halfverse: c tato yadi tvā grāhir ānaśe tām te devā brahmaṇā nāśayantu //3//

Hymn: 114

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad devā devahedanam devāsaś cakṛma vayam /

Halfverse: c ādityās tasmān no yuyam ṛtasya ṛtena muñcata //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ṛtasya ṛtenādityā yajatrā muñcateha naḥ /

Halfverse: c yajñam yad yajñavāhasaḥ śikṣanto nopaśekima //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a medasvatā yajamānāḥ srucājyāni juhvataḥ /

Halfverse: c akāmā viśve vo devāḥ śikṣanto nopa śekima //3//

Hymn: 115

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad vidvāmso yad avidvāmsa enāmsi cakṛmā vayam /

Halfverse: c yūyam nas tasmān muñcata viśve devāḥ sajoṣasah //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yadi jāgrad yadi svapann ena enasyo ‘karam /

Halfverse: c bhūtam mā tasmād bhavyam ca drupadād iva muñcatām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a drupadād iva mumucānaḥ svinnaḥ snātvā malād iva /

Halfverse: c pūtam pavitreṇevājyam viśve śumbhantu mainasaḥ //3//

Hymn: 116

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad yāmam cakrur nikhananto agre kārṣīvaṇā annavido na vidyayā /

Halfverse: c vaivasvate rājani taj juhomy atha yajñiyam madhumad astu no ‘nnam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vaivasvataḥ kṛṇavad bhāgadheyam madhubhāgo madhunā sam sṛjāti /

Halfverse: c mātur yad ena iṣitam na āgan yad vā pitā ‘parāddho jihīde //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yadīdam mātur yadi pitur naḥ pari bhrātuḥ putrāc cetasa ena āgan /

Halfverse: c yāvanto asmān pitaraḥ sacante teṣām sarveṣām śivo astu manyuḥ //3//

Hymn: 117

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apamityam apratīttam yad asmi yamasya yena balinā carāmi /

Halfverse: c idam tad agne anṛṇo bhavāmi tvam pāśān vicṛtam vettha sarvān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ihaiva santaḥ prati dadma enaj jīvā jīvebhyo ni harāma enat /

Halfverse: c apamitya dhānya1m yaj jaghasāham idam tad agne anṛṇo bhavāmi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anṛṇā asminn anṛṇāḥ parasmin tṛtīye loke anṛṇāḥ syāma /

Halfverse: c ye devayānāḥ pitṛyāṇaś ca lokāḥ sarvān patho anṛṇā ā kṣiyema //3//

Hymn: 118

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad dhastābhyām cakṛma kilbiṣāny akṣānām gatnum upalipsamānāḥ /

Halfverse: c ugrampaśye ugrajitau tad adyāpsarasāv anu dattām ṛṇam naḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ugrampaśye rāṣṭrabhṛt kilbiṣāṇi yad akṣavṛttam anu dattam na etat /

Halfverse: c ṛṇān no na ṛṇam ertsamāno yamasya loke adhirajjur āyat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yasmā ṛṇam yasya jāyām upaimi yam yācamāno abhyaimi devāḥ /

Halfverse: c te vācam vādiṣur mottarām mad devapatnī apsarasāv adhītam //3//

Hymn: 119

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad adīvyann ṛṇam aham kṛṇomy adāsyann agne uta samgṛṇāmi /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānaro no adhipā vasiṣṭha ud in nayāti sukṛtasya lokam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vaiśvānarāya prati vedayāmi yadi ṛṇam samgaro devatāsu /

Halfverse: c sa etān pāśān vicṛtam veda sarvān atha pakvena saha sam bhavema //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vaiśvānaraḥ pavitā mā punātu yat samgaram abhidhāvāmy āśām /

Halfverse: c anājānan manasā yācamāno yat tatraino apa tat suvāmi //3//

Hymn: 120

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad antarikṣam pṛthivīm uta dyām yan mātaram pitaram vā jihimsima /

Halfverse: c ayam tasmād gārhapatyo no agnir ud in nayāti sukṛtasya lokam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a bhūmir mātāditir no janitram bhrātāntarikṣam abhiśastyā naḥ /

Halfverse: c dyaur naḥ pitā pitryāc cham bhavāti jāmim ṛtvā māva patsi lokāt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yatrā suhārdaḥ sukṛto madanti vihāya rogam tanva1ḥ svāyāḥ /

Halfverse: c aślonā aṅgair ahrutāḥ svarge tatra paśyema pitarau ca putrān //3//

Hymn: 121

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a viṣāṇā pāśān vi ṣyādhy asmad ya uttamā adhamā vāruṇā ye /

Halfverse: c duṣvapnyam duritam ni ṣvāsmad atha gachema sukṛtasya lokam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad dāruṇi badhyase yac ca rajjvām yad bhūmyām badhyase yac ca vācā /

Halfverse: c ayam tasmād gārhapatyo no agnir ud in nayāti sukṛtasya lokam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ud agātām bhagavatī vicṛtau nāma tārake /

Halfverse: c prehāmṛtasya yachatām praitu baddhakamocanam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vi jihīṣva lokam kṛṇu bandhān muñcāsi baddhakam /

Halfverse: c yonyā iva pracyuto garbhaḥ pathaḥ sarvāṁ anu kṣiya //4//

Hymn: 122

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a etam bhāgam pari dadāmi vidvān viśvakarman prathamajā ṛtasya /

Halfverse: c asmābhir dattam jarasaḥ parastād achinnam tantum anu sam tarema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tatam tantum anv eke taranti yeṣām dattam pitryam āyanena /

Halfverse: c abandhv eke dadataḥ prayachanto dātum cec chikṣānt sa svarga eva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anvārabhethām anusamrabhethām etam lokam śraddadhānāḥ sacante /

Halfverse: c yad vām pakvam pariviṣṭam agnau tasya guptaye dampatī sam śrayethām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yajñam yantam manasā bṛhantam anvārohāmi tapasā sayoniḥ /

Halfverse: c upahūtā agne jarasaḥ parastāt tṛtīye nāke sadhamādam madema //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śuddhāḥ pūtā yoṣito yajñiyā imā brahmaṇām hasteṣu prapṛthak sādayāmi /

Halfverse: c yatkāma idam abhiṣiñcāmi vo ‘ham indro marutvānt sa dadātu tan me //5//

Hymn: 123

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a etam sadhasthāḥ pari vo dadāmi yam śevadhim āvahāj jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c anvāgantā yajamānaḥ svasti tam sma jānīta parame vyoman //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jānīta smainam parame vyoman devāḥ sadhasthā vida lokam atra /

Halfverse: c anvāgantā yajamānaḥ svastīṣṭāpūrtam sma kṛṇutāvir asmai //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a devāḥ pitaraḥ pitaro devāḥ /

Halfverse: c yo asmi so asmi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sa pacāmi sa dadāmi /

Halfverse: c sa yaje sa dattān mā yūṣam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a nāke rājan prati tiṣṭha tatraitat prati tiṣṭhatu /

Halfverse: c viddhi pūrtasya no rājant sa deva sumanā bhava //5//

Hymn: 124

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a divo nu mām bṛhato antarikṣād apām stoko abhy apaptad rasena /

Halfverse: c sam indriyena payasāham agne chandobhir yajñaiḥ sukṛtām kṛtena //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yadi vṛkṣād abhyapaptat phalam tad yady antarikṣāt sa u vāyur eva /

Halfverse: c yatrāspṛkṣat tanvo3 yac ca vāsasa āpo nudantu nirṛtim parācaiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a abhyañjanam surabhi sā samṛddhir hiraṇyam varcas tad u pūtrimam eva /

Halfverse: c sarvā pavitrā vitatādhy asmat tan mā tārīn nirṛtir mo arātiḥ //3//

Hymn: 125

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vanaspate vīḍvaṅgo hi bhūyā asmatsakhā prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ /

Halfverse: c gobhiḥ samnaddho asi vīḍayasvāsthātā te jayatu jetvāni //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a divas pṛthivyāḥ pary oja udbhṛtam vanaspatibhyaḥ pary ābhṛtam sahaḥ /

Halfverse: c apām ojmānam pari gobhir āvṛtam indrasya vajram haviṣā ratham yaja //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indrasyaujo marutām anīkam mitrasya garbho varuṇasya nābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sa imām no havyadātim juṣāṇo deva ratha prati havyā gṛbhāya //3//

Hymn: 126

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a upa śvāsaya pṛthivīm uta dyam purutrā te vanvatām viṣṭhitam jagat /

Halfverse: c sa dundubhe sajūr indreṇa devair dūrād davīyo apa sedha śatrūn //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā krandaya balam ojo na ā dhā abhi ṣṭana duritā bādhamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c apa sedha dundubhe duchunām ita indrasya muṣṭir asi vīḍayasva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a prāmūm jayābhīme jayantu ketumad dundubhir vāvadītu /

Halfverse: c sam aśvaparṇāḥ patantu no naro ‘smākam indra rathino jayantu //3//

Hymn: 127

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vidradhasya balāsasya lohitasya vanaspate /

Halfverse: c visalpakasyoṣadhe moc chiṣaḥ piśitam cana //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yau te balāsa tiṣṭhataḥ kakṣe muṣkāv apaśritau /

Halfverse: c vedāham tasya bheṣajam cīpudrur abhicakṣaṇam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo aṅgyo yaḥ karṇyo yo akṣyor visalpakaḥ /

Halfverse: c vi vṛhāmo visalpakam vidradham hṛdayāmayam /

Halfverse: e parā tam ajñātam yakṣmam adharāñcam suvāmasi //3//

Hymn: 128

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śakadhūmam nakṣatrāṇi yad rājānam akurvata /

Halfverse: c bhadrāham asmai prāyachan idam rāṣṭram asād iti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a bhadrāham no madhyamdine bhadrāham sāyam astu naḥ /

Halfverse: c bhadrāham no ahnām prātā rātrī bhadrāham astu naḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ahorātrābhyām nakṣatrebhyaḥ suryācandramasābhyām /

Halfverse: c bhadrāham asmabhyam rājan chakadhūma tvam kṛdhi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yo no bhadrāham akaraḥ sāyam naktam atho divā /

Halfverse: c tasmai te nakṣatrarāja śakadhūma sadā namaḥ //4//

Hymn: 129

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhagena mā śāmśapena sākam indreṇa medinā /

Halfverse: c kṛṇomi bhaginam māpa drāntv arātayaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yena vṛkṣāṁ abhyabhavo bhagena varcasā saha /

Halfverse: c tena mā bhaginam kṛṇv apa drāntv arātayaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo andho yaḥ punaḥsaro bhago vṛkṣeṣv āhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c tena mā bhaginam kṛṇv apa drāntv arātayaḥ //3//

Hymn: 130

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a rathajitām rāthajiteyīnām apsarasām ayam smaraḥ /

Halfverse: c devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asau me smaratād iti priyo me smaratād iti /

Halfverse: c devāh pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā mama smarād asau nāmuṣyāham kadā cana /

Halfverse: c devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a un mādayata maruta ud antarikṣa mādaya /

Halfverse: c agna un mādayā tvam asau mām anu śocatu //4//

Hymn: 131

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ni śīrṣato ni pattata ādhyo3 ni tirāmi te /

Halfverse: c devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anumate ‘nv idam manyasvākute sam idam namaḥ /

Halfverse: c devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad dhāvasi triyojanam pañcayojanam āśvinam /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam punar āyasi putrāṇām no asaḥ pitā //3//

Hymn: 132

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yam devāḥ smaram asiñcann apsv antaḥ śośucānam sahādhyā /

Halfverse: c tam te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yam viśve devāḥ smaram asiñcann apsv antaḥ śośucānam sahādhyā /

Halfverse: c tam te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yam indrāṇī smaram asiñcad apsv antaḥ śośucānam sahādhyā /

Halfverse: c tam te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yam indrāgnī smaram asiñcatām apsv antaḥ śośucānam sahādhyā /

Halfverse: c tam te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yam mitrāvaruṇau smaram asiñcatām apsv antaḥ śośucānam sahādhyā /

Halfverse: c tam te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā //5//

Hymn: 133

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ya imām devo mekhalām ābabandha yaḥ samnanāha ya u no yuyoja /

Halfverse: c yasya devasya praśiṣā carāmaḥ sa pāram ichāt sa u no vi muñcāt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āhutāsy abhihuta ṛṣīṇām asy āyudham /

Halfverse: c pūrvā vratasya prāśnatī vīraghnī bhava mekhale //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mṛtyor aham brahmacārī yad asmi niryācan bhūtāt puruṣam yamāya /

Halfverse: c tam aham brahmaṇā tapasā śrameṇānayainam mekhalayā sināmi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śraddhāyā duhitā tapaso ‘dhi jātā svasā ṛṣīṇām bhūtakṛtām babhūva /

Halfverse: c sā no mekhale matim ā dhehi medhām atho no dhehi tapa indriyam ca //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yām tvā pūrve bhūtakṛta ṛṣayaḥ paribedhire /

Halfverse: c sā tvam pari ṣvajasva mām dīrghāyutvāya mekhale //5//

Hymn: 134

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam vajras tarpayatām ṛtasyāvāsya ṛāṣṭram apa hantu jīvitam /

Halfverse: c śṛṇātu grīvāḥ pra śṛṇātūṣṇihā vṛtrasyeva śacīpatiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a adharo’dhara uttarebhyo gūḍhaḥ pṛthivyā mot sṛpat /

Halfverse: c vajreṇāvahataḥ śayām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo jināti tam anvicha yo jināti tam ij jahi /

Halfverse: c jinato vajra tvam sīmantam anvañcam anu pātaya //3//

Hymn: 135

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad aśnāmi balam kurva ittham vajram ā dade /

Halfverse: c skandhān amuṣya śātayan vṛtrasyeva śacīpatiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yat pibāmi sam pibāmi samudra iva sampibaḥ /

Halfverse: c prāṇān amuṣya sampāya sam pibāmo amum vayam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad girāmi sam girāmi samudra iva samgiraḥ /

Halfverse: c prāṇān amuṣya samgīrya sam girāmo amum vayam //3//

Hymn: 136

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a devī devyām adhi jātā pṛthivyām asy oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c tām tvā nitatni keśebhyo dṛmhaṇāya khanāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dṛmha pratnān janayājātān jātān u varṣīyasas kṛdhi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yas te keśo ‘vapadyate samūlo yaś ca vṛścate /

Halfverse: c idam tam viśvabheṣajyābhi ṣiñcāmi vīrudhā //3//

Hymn: 137

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yām jamadagnir akhanad duhitre keśavardhanīm /

Halfverse: c tām vītahavya ābharad asitasya gṛhebhyaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a abhīśunā meyā āsan vyāmenānumeyāḥ /

Halfverse: c keśā naḍā iva vardhantām śīrṣṇas te asitāḥ pari //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dṛmha mūlam āgram yacha vi madhyam yāmayauṣadhe /

Halfverse: c keśā naḍā iva vardhantām śīrṣṇas te asitāḥ pari //3//

Hymn: 138

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvam vīrudhām śreṣṭhatamābhiśrutāsy oṣadhe /

Halfverse: c imam me adya puruṣam klībam opaśinam kṛdhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a klībam kṛdhy opaśinam atho kurīriṇam kṛdhi /

Halfverse: c athāsyendro grāvabhyām ubhe bhinattv āṇḍyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a klība klībam tvākaram vadhre vadhrim tvākaram arasārasam tvākaram /

Halfverse: c kurīram asya śīrṣaṇi kumbam cādhinidadhmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ye te nādyaḥ devakṛte yayos tiṣṭhati vṛṣṇyam /

Halfverse: c te te bhinadmi śamyayāmuṣyā adhi muṣkayoḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yathā naḍam kaśipune striyo bhindanty aśmanā /

Halfverse: c evā bhinadmi te śepo ‘muṣyā adhi muṣkayoḥ //5//

Hymn: 139

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nyastikā rurohitha subhagamkaraṇī mama /

Halfverse: c śatam tava pratānās trayastrimśan nitānāḥ //

Halfverse: e tayā sahasraparṇyā hṛdayam śoṣayāmi te //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śuṣyatu mayi te hṛdayam atho śuṣyatv āsyam /

Halfverse: c atho ni śuṣya mām kāmenātho śuṣkāsyā cara //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samvananī samuṣpalā babhru kalyāṇi sam nuda /

Halfverse: c amūm ca mām ca sam nuda samānam hṛdayam kṛdhi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yathodakam apapuṣo ‘paśuṣyaty āsyam /

Halfverse: c evā ni śuṣya mām kāmenātho śuṣkāsyā cara //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yathā nakulo vichidya samdadhāty ahim punaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā kāmasya vichinnam sam dhehi vīryāvati //5//

Hymn: 140

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yau vyāghrāv avarūdhau jighatsataḥ pitaram mātaram ca /

Halfverse: c tau dantam brahmaṇas pate śivau kṛṇu jātavedaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vrīhim attam yavam attam atho māṣam atho tilam /

Halfverse: c eṣa vām bhāgo nihito ratnadheyāya dantau mā himsiṣṭam pitaram mātaram ca //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a upahūtau sayujau syonau dantau sumaṅgalau /

Halfverse: c anyatra vām ghoram tanvaḥ paraitu dantau mā himsiṣṭam pitaram mātaram ca //3//

Hymn: 141

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vāyur enāḥ samākarat tvaṣṭā poṣāya dhriyatām /

Halfverse: c indra ābhyo adhi bravad rudro bhūmne cikitsatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a lohitena svadhitinā mithunam karṇayoḥ kṛdhi /

Halfverse: c akartām aśvinā lakṣma tad astu prajayā bahu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā cakrur devāsurā yathā manuṣyā uta /

Halfverse: c evā sahasrapoṣāya kṛṇutam lakṣmāśvinā //3//

Hymn: 142

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a uc chrayasva bahur bhava svena mahasā yava /

Halfverse: c mṛṇīhi viśvā pātrāṇi mā tvā divyāśanir vadhīt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āśṛṇvantam yavam devam yatra tvāchāvadāmasi /

Halfverse: c tad uc chrayasva dyaur iva samudra ivaidhy akṣitaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a akṣitās ta upasado ‘kṣitāḥ santu rāśayaḥ /

Halfverse: c pṛṇanto akṣitāḥ santv attāraḥ santv akṣitāḥ //3//

## Book: 7

7

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dhītī vā ye anayan vāco agram manasā vā ye ‘vadann ṛtāni /

Halfverse: c tṛtīyena brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānās turīyeṇāmanvata nāma dhenoḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sa veda putraḥ pitaram sa mātaram sa sūnur bhuvat sa bhuvat punarmaghaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa dyām aurṇod antarikṣam svaḥ sa idam viśvam abhavat sa ābharat //2//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a atharvāṇam pitaram devabandhum mātur garbham pitur asum yuvānam /

Halfverse: c ya imam yajñam manasā ciketa pra ṇo vocas tam iheha bravaḥ //1//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayā viṣṭhā janayan karvarāṇi sa hi ghṛṇir urur varāya gātuḥ /

Halfverse: c sa pratyudaid dharuṇam madhvo agram svayā tanvā tanvam airayata //1//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ekayā ca daśabhiś ca suhute dvābhyām iṣṭaye vimśatyā ca /

Halfverse: c tisṛbhiś ca vahase trimśatā ca viyugbhir vāya iha tā vi muñca //1//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yajñena yajñam ayajanta devās tāni dharmāṇi prathamāny āsan /

Halfverse: c te ha nākam mahimānaḥ sacanta yatra pūrve sādhyāḥ santi devāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yajño babhūva sa ā babhūva sa pra jajñe sa u vāvṛdhe punaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa devānām adhipatir babhūva so asmāsu draviṇam ā dadhātu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad devā devān haviṣā ‘yajantāmartyān manasā martyena /

Halfverse: c madema tatra parame vyoman paśyema tad uditau sūryasya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yat puruṣeṇa haviṣā yajñam devā atanvata /

Halfverse: c asti nu tasmād ojīyo yad vihavyenejire //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mugdhā devā uta śunā ‘yajantota gor aṅgaiḥ purudhā ‘yajanta /

Halfverse: c ya imam yajñam manasā ciketa pra ṇo vocas tam iheha bravaḥ //5//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aditir dyaur aditir antarikṣam aditir mātā sa pitā sa putraḥ /

Halfverse: c viśve devā aditir pañca janā aditir jātam aditir janitvam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mahīm ū ṣu mātaram suvratānām ṛtasya patnīm avase havāmahe /

Halfverse: c tuvikṣatrām ajarantīm urūcīm suśarmāṇam aditim supraṇītim //2//

{7}

Verse: 3 {1}

Halfverse: a sutrāmāṇam pṛthivīm dyām anehasam suśarmāṇam aditim supraṇītim /

Halfverse: c daivīm nāvam svaritrām anāgaso asravantīm ā ruhemā svastaye //1//

Verse: 4 {2}

Halfverse: a vājasya nu prasave mātaram mahīm aditim nāma vacasā karāmahe /

Halfverse: c yasyā upastha urv antarikṣam sā naḥ śarma trivarūtham ni yachāt //2//

Hymn: 7 {8}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a diteḥ putrāṇām aditer akāriṣam ava devānām bṛhatām anarmaṇām /

Halfverse: c teṣām hi dhāma gabhiṣak samudriyam nainān namasā paro asti kaścana //1//

Hymn: 8 {9}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhadrād adhi śreyaḥ prehi bṛhaspatiḥ puraetā te astu /

Halfverse: c athemam asyā vara ā pṛthivyā āreśatrum kṛṇuhi sarvavīram //1//

Hymn: 9 {10}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prapathe pathām ajaniṣṭa pūṣā prapathe divaḥ prapathe pṛthivyāḥ /

Halfverse: c ubhe abhi priyatame sadhasthe ā ca parā ca carati prajānan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pūṣemā āśā anu veda sarvāḥ so asmāṁ abhayatamena neṣat /

Halfverse: c svastidā āghṛṇiḥ sarvavīro ‘prayuchan pura etu prajānan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pūṣan tava vrate vayam na riṣyema kadā cana /

Halfverse: c stotāras ta iha smasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pari pūṣā parastād dhastam dadhātu dakṣiṇam /

Halfverse: c punar no naṣṭam ājatu sam naṣṭena gamemahi //4//

Hymn: 10 {11}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yas te stanaḥ śaśayur yo mayobhūr yaḥ sumnayuḥ suhavo yaḥ sudatraḥ /

Halfverse: c yena viśvā puṣyasi vāryāṇi sarasvati tam iha dhātave kaḥ //1//

Hymn: 11 {12}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yas te pṛthu stanayitnur ya ṛṣvo daivaḥ ketur viśvam ābhūṣatīdam /

Halfverse: c mā no vadhīr vidyutā deva sasyam mota vadhī raśmibhiḥ sūryasya //1//

Hymn: 12 {13}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sabhā ca mā samitiś cāvatām prajāpater duhitarau samvidāne /

Halfverse: c yenā samgachā upa mā sa śikṣāc cāru vadāni pitarah samgateṣu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vidma te sabhe nāma nariṣṭā nāma vā asi /

Halfverse: c ye te ke ca sabhāsadas te me santu savācasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a eṣām aham samāsīnānām varco vijñānam ā dade /

Halfverse: c asyāḥ sarvasyāḥ samsado mām indra bhaginam kṛṇu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad vo manaḥ parāgatam yad baddham iha veha vā /

Halfverse: c tad va ā vartayāmasi mayi vo ramatām manaḥ //4//

Hymn: 13 {14}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathā sūryo nakṣatrāṇām udyams tejāmsy ādade /

Halfverse: c evā strīṇām ca pumsām ca dviṣatām varca ā dade //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yāvanto mā sapatnānām āyantam pratipaśyatha /

Halfverse: c udyant sūrya iva suptānām dviṣatām varca ā dade //2//

Hymn: 14 {15}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi tyam devam savitāram oṇyoḥ kavikratum /

Halfverse: c arcāmi satyasavam ratnadhām abhi priyam matim //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a urdhvā yasyāmatir bhā adidyutat savīmani /

Halfverse: c hiraṇyapāṇir amimīta sukratuḥ kṛpāt svaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sāvīr hi deva prathamāya pitre varṣmāṇam asmai varimāṇam asmai /

Halfverse: c athāsmabhyam savitar vāryāṇi divodiva ā suvā bhūri paśvaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a damūnā devaḥ savitā vareṇyo dadhad ratnam pitṛbhya āyūmṣi /

Halfverse: c pibāt somam mamadad enam iṣṭe parijmā cit kramate asya dharmaṇi //4//

Hymn: 15 {16}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tām savitaḥ satyasavām sucitrām āham vṛṇe sumatim viśvavārām /

Halfverse: c yām asya kaṇvo aduhat prapīnām sahasradhārām mahiṣo bhagāya //1//

Hymn: 16 {17}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bṛhaspate savitar vardhayainam jyotayainam mahate saubhagāya /

Halfverse: c samśitam cit samtaram sam śiśādhi viśva enam anu madantu devāḥ //1//

Hymn: 17 {18}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dhātā dadhātu no rayim īśāno jagatas patiḥ /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ pūrṇena yachatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dhātā dadhātu dāśuṣe prācīm jīvātum akṣitām /

Halfverse: c vayam devasya dhīmahi sumatim viśvarādhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dhātā viśvā vāryā dadhātu prajākāmāya dāśuṣe duroṇe /

Halfverse: c tasmai devā amṛtam sam vyayantu viśve devā aditiḥ sajoṣāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a dhātā rātiḥ savitedam juṣantām prajāpatir nidhipatir no agniḥ /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭā viṣṇuḥ prajayā samrarāṇo yajamānāya draviṇam dadhātu //4//

Hymn: 18 {19}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pra nabhasva pṛthivi bhinddhī3dam divyam nabhaḥ /

Halfverse: c udno divyasya no dhātar īśāno vi ṣyā dṛtim //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na ghrams tatāpa na himo jaghāna pra nabhatām pṛthivī jīradānuḥ /

Halfverse: c āpaś cid asmai ghṛtam it kṣaranti yatra somaḥ sadam it tatra bhadram //2//

Hymn: 19 {20}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prajāpatir janayati prajā imā dhātā dadhātu sumanasyamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c samjānānāḥ sammanasaḥ sayonayo mayi puṣṭam puṣṭapatir dadhātu //1//

Hymn: 20 {21}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a anv adya no ‘numatir yajñam deveṣu manyatām /

Halfverse: c agniś ca havyavāhano bhavatām dāśuṣe mama //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anv id anumate tvam mamsase śam ca nas kṛdhi /

Halfverse: c juṣasva havyam āhutam prajām devi rarāsva naḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anu manyatām anumanyamānaḥ prajāvantam rayim akṣīyamāṇam /

Halfverse: c tasya vayam heḍasi māpi bhūma sumṛḍīke asya sumatau syāma //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yat te nāma suhavam supraṇīte ‘numate anumatam sudānu /

Halfverse: c tenā no yajñam pipṛhi viśvavāre rayim no dhehi subhage suvīram //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a emam yajñam anumatir jagāma sukṣetratāyai suvīratāyai sujātam /

Halfverse: c bhadrā hy asyāḥ pramatir babhūva semam yajñam avatu devagopā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a anumatiḥ sarvam idam babhūva yat tiṣṭhati carati yad u ca viśvam ejati /

Halfverse: c tasyās te devi sumatau syāmānumate anu hi mamsase naḥ //6//

Hymn: 21 {22}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sameta viśve vacasā patim diva eko vibhūr atithir janānām /

Halfverse: c sa pūrvyo nūtanam āvivāsat tam vartanir anu vāvṛta ekam it puru //1//

Hymn: 22 {23}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam sahasram ā no dṛśe kavīnām matir jyotir vidharmaṇi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a bradhnaḥ samīcīr uṣasaḥ sam airayan /

Halfverse: c arepasaḥ sacetasaḥ svasare manyumattamāś cite goḥ //2//

Hymn: 23 {24}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dauṣvapnyam daurjīvityam rakṣo abhvam arāyyaḥ /

Halfverse: c durṇāmnīḥ sarvā durvācas tā asman nāśayāmasi //1//

Hymn: 24 {25}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yan na indro akhanad yad agnir viśve devā maruto yat svarkāḥ /

Halfverse: c tad asmabhyam savitā satyadharmā prajāpatir anumatir ni yachāt //1//

Hymn: 25 {26}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yayor ojasā skabhitā rajāmsi yau vīryair vīratamā śaviṣṭhā /

Halfverse: c yau patyete apratītau sahobhir viṣṇum agan varuṇam pūrvahūtiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yasyedam pradiśi yad virocate pra cānati vi ca caṣṭe śacībhiḥ /

Halfverse: c purā devasya dharmaṇā sahobhir viṣṇum agan varuṇam pūrvahūtiḥ //2//

Hymn: 26 {27}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a viṣṇor nu kam prā vocam vīryāṇi yaḥ pārthivāni vimame rajāmsi /

Halfverse: c yo askabhāyad uttaram sadhastham vicakramāṇas tredhorugāyaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pra tad viṣṇu stavate vīryāṇi mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ /

Halfverse: c parāvata ā jagamyāt parasyāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yasyoruṣu triṣu vikramaneṣv adhikṣiyanti bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c uru viṣṇo vi kramasvoru kṣayāya nas kṛdhi /

Halfverse: e ghṛtam ghṛtayone piba prapra yajñapatim tira //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a idam viṣṇur vi cakrame tredhā ni dadhe padā /

Halfverse: c samūḍham asya pamsure //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a trīṇi padā vi cakrame viṣṇur gopā adābhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c ito dharmāṇi dhārayan //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi paśyata yato vratāni paspaśe /

Halfverse: c indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tad viṣṇoḥ paramam padam sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ /

Halfverse: c divīva cakṣur ātatam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a divo viṣṇa uta pṛthivyā maho viṣṇa uror antarikṣāt /

Halfverse: c hastau pṛṇasva bahubhir vasavyair āprayacha dakṣiṇād ota savyāt //8//

Hymn: 27 {28}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a iḍaivāsmāṁ anu vastām vratena yasyāḥ pade punate devayantaḥ /

Halfverse: c ghṛtapadī śakvarī somapṛṣṭhopa yajñam asthita vaiśvadevī //1//

Hymn: 28 {29}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vedaḥ svastir drughaṇaḥ svastiḥ paraśur vediḥ paraśur naḥ svasti /

Halfverse: c haviṣkṛto yajñiyā yajñakāmās te devāso yajñam imam juṣantām //1//

Hymn: 29 {30}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnāviṣṇū mahi tad vām mahitvam pātho ghṛtasya guhyasya nāma /

Halfverse: c damedame sapta ratnā dadhānau prati vām jihvā ghṛtam ā caraṇyāt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agnāviṣṇū mahi dhāma priyam vām vītho ghṛtasya guhyā juṣāṇau /

Halfverse: c damedame suṣṭutyā vāvṛdhānau prati vām jihvā ghṛtam uc caraṇyāt //2//

Hymn: 30 {31}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a svāktam me dyāvāpṛthivī svāktam mitro akar ayam /

Halfverse: c svāktam me brahmaṇas patiḥ svāktam savitā karat //1//

Hymn: 31 {32}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrotibhir bahulābhir no adya yāvacchreṣṭhābhir maghavan chūra jinva /

Halfverse: c yo no dveṣṭy adhara sas padīṣṭa yam u dviṣmas tam u prāṇo jahātu //1//

Hymn: 32 {33}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a upa priyam panipnatam yuvānam āhutīvṛdham /

Halfverse: c aganma bibhrato namo dīrgham āyuḥ kṛṇotu me //1//

Hymn: 33 {34}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam mā siñcantu marutaḥ sam pūṣa sam bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c sam māyam agniḥ siñcatu prajayā ca dhanena ca dīrgham āyuḥ kṛṇotu //1//

Hymn: 34 {35}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agne jātān pra ṇudā me sapatnān praty ajātān jātavedo nudasva /

Halfverse: c adhaspadam kṛṇuṣva ye pṛtanyavo ‘nāgasas te vayam aditaye syāma //1//

Hymn: 35 {36}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prānyānt sapatnānt sahasā sahasva praty ajātān jātavedo nudasva /

Halfverse: c idam rāṣṭram pipṛhi saubhagāya viśva enam anu madantu devāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a imā yās te śatam hirāḥ sahasram dhamanīr uta /

Halfverse: c tāsām te sarvāsām aham aśmanā bilam apy adhām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a param yoner avaram te kṛṇomi mā tvā prajābhi bhūn mota sūtuḥ /

Halfverse: c asva1m tvāprajasam kṛṇomy aśmānam te apidhānam kṛṇomi //3//

Hymn: 36 {37}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a akṣyaḥ nau madhusamkāśe anīkam nau samañjanam /

Halfverse: c antaḥ kṛṣṇuṣva mām hṛdi mana in nau sahāsati //1//

Hymn: 37 {38}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi tvā manujātena dadhāmi mama vāsasā /

Halfverse: c yāthā ‘so mama kevalo nānyāsām kīrtayāś cana //1//

Hymn: 38 {39}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam khanāmi bheṣajam māmpaśyam abhirorudam /

Halfverse: c parāyato nivartanam āyataḥ pratinandanam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yenā nicakra āsurīndram devebhyas pari /

Halfverse: c tenā ni kurve tvām aham yathā te ‘sāni supriyā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pratīcī somam asi pratīcī uta sūryam /

Halfverse: c pratīcī viśvān devān tām tvāchāvadāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a aham vadāmi net tvam sabhāyām aha tvam vada /

Halfverse: c mamed asas tvam kevalo nānyāsām kīrtayāś cana //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yadi vāsi tirojanam yadi vā nadyas tiraḥi /

Halfverse: c iyam ha mahyam tvām oṣadhir baddhveva nyānayat //5//

Hymn: 39 {40}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a divyam suparṇam payasam bṛhantam apām garbham vṛṣabham oṣadhīnām /

Halfverse: c abhīpato vṛṣṭyā tarpayantam ā no goṣṭhe rayiṣṭhām sthāpayāti //1//

Hymn: 40 {41}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yasya vratam paśavo yanti sarve yasya vrata upatiṣṭhanta āpaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya vrate puṣṭapatir niviṣṭas tam sarasvantam avase havāmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā pratyañcam dāśuṣe dāśvamsam sarasvantam puṣṭapatim rayiṣṭhām /

Halfverse: c rāyas poṣam śravasyum vasānā iha sadanam rayīṇām //2//

Hymn: 41 {42}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ati dhanvāny aty apas tatarda śyeno nṛcakṣā avasānadarśaḥ /

Halfverse: c taran viśvāny avarā rajamsīndreṇa sakhyā śiva ā jagamyāt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śyeno nṛcakṣā divyaḥ suparṇaḥ sahasrapāc chatayonir vayodhāḥ /

Halfverse: c sa no ni yachād vasu yat parābhṛtam asmākam astu pitṛṣu svadhāvat //2//

Hymn: 42 {43}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a somārudrā vi vṛhatam viṣūcīm amīvā yā no gayam āviveśa /

Halfverse: c bādhethām dūram nirṛtim parācaiḥ kṛtam cid enaḥ pra mumuktam asmat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a somārudrā yuvam etāny asmad viśvā tanūṣu bheṣajāni dhattam /

Halfverse: c ava syatam muñcatam yan no asat tanūṣu baddham kṛtam eno asmat //2//

Hymn: 43 {44}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śivās ta ekā aśivās ta ekāḥ sarvā bibharṣi sumanasyamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c tisro vāco nihitā antar asmin tāsām ekā vi papātānu ghoṣam //1//

Hymn: 44 {45}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ubhā jigyathur na parā jayethe na parā jigye kataraś canainayoḥ /

Halfverse: c indraś ca viṣṇo yad apaspṛdhethām tredhā sahasram vi tad airayethām //1//

Hymn: 45 {46}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a janād viśvajanīnāt sindhutas pary ābhṛtam /

Halfverse: c dūrāt tvā manya udbhṛtam īrṣyāyā nāma bheṣajam //1//

{47}

Verse: 2 {1}

Halfverse: a agner ivāsya dahato dāvasya dahataḥ pṛthak /

Halfverse: c etām etasyerṣyām udrāgnim iva śamaya //1//

Hymn: 46 {48}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sinīvāli pṛthuṣṭuke yā devānām asi svasā /

Halfverse: c juṣasva havyam āhutam prajām devi didiḍḍhi naḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yā subāhuḥ svaṅguriḥ suṣūmā bahusūvarī /

Halfverse: c tasyai viśpatnyai haviḥ sinīvālyai juhotana //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yā viśpatnīndram asi pratīcī sahasrastukābhiyantī devī /

Halfverse: c viṣṇoḥ patni tubhyam rātā havīmṣi patim devi rādhase codayasva //3//

Hymn: 47 {49}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kuhūm devīm sukṛtam vidmanāpasam asmin yajñe suhavā johavīmi /

Halfverse: c sā no rayim viśvavāram ni yachād dadātu vīram śatadāyam ukthyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kuhūr devānām amṛtasya patnī havyā no asya haviṣo juṣeta /

Halfverse: c śṛnotu yajñam uśatī no adya rāyas poṣam cikituṣī dadhātu //2//

Hymn: 48 {50}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a rākām aham suhavā suṣṭutī huve śṛṇotu naḥ subhagā bodhatu tmanā /

Halfverse: c sīvyatv apaḥ sūcyāchidyamānayā dadātu vīram śatadāyam ukthyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yās te rāke sumatayaḥ supeśaso yābhir dadāsi dāśuṣe vasūni /

Halfverse: c tābhir no adya sumanā upāgahi sahasrāpoṣam subhage rarāṇā //2//

Hymn: 49 {51}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a devānām patnīr uśatīr avantu naḥ prāvantu nas tujaye vājasātaye /

Halfverse: c yāḥ pārthivāso yā apām api vrate tā no devīḥ suhavāḥ śarma yachantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a uta gnā vyantu devapatnīr indrāṇy agnāyy aśvinī rāṭ /

Halfverse: c ā rodasī varunānī śṛṇotu vyantu devīr ya ṛtur janīnām //2//

Hymn: 50 {52}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathā vṛkṣam aśanir viśvāhā hanty aprati /

Halfverse: c evāham adya kitavān akṣair badhyāsam aprati //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a turāṇām aturāṇām viśām avarjuṣīṇām /

Halfverse: c samaitu viśvato bhago antarhastam kṛtam mama //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a īḍe agnim svāvasum namobhir iha prasakto vi cayat kṛtam naḥ /

Halfverse: c rathair iva pra bhare vājayadbhiḥ pradakṣiṇam marutām stomam ṛdhyām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vayam jayema tvayā yujā vṛtam asmākam amśam ud ava bharebhare /

Halfverse: c asmabhyam indra varīyaḥ sugam kṛdhi pra śatrūṇām maghavan vṛṣṇyā ruja //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ajaiṣam tvā samlikhitam ajaiṣam uta samrudham /

Halfverse: c avim vṛko yathā mathad evā mathnāmi te kṛtam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a uta prahām atidīvā jayati kṛtam iva śvaghnī vi cinoti kāle /

Halfverse: c yo devakāmo na dhanam ruṇaddhi sam it tam rāyaḥ sṛjati svadhābhiḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a gobhiṣ ṭaremāmatim durevām yavena vā kṣudham puruhūta viśve /

Halfverse: c vayam rājasu prathamā dhanāny ariṣṭāso vṛjanībhir jayema //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a kṛtam me dakṣiṇe haste jayo me savya āhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c gojid bhūyāsam aśvajid dhanamjayo hiraṇyajit //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a akṣāḥ phalavatīm dyuvam datta gām kṣīriṇīm iva /

Halfverse: c sam mā kṛtasya dhārayā dhanuḥ snāvneva nahyata //9//

Hymn: 51 {53}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir naḥ pari pātu paścād utottarasmād adharād aghayoḥ /

Halfverse: c indraḥ purastād uta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyo varīyaḥ kṛṇotu //1//

Hymn: 52 {54}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samjñānam naḥ svebhiḥ samjñānam araṇebhiḥ /

Halfverse: c samjñānam aśvinā yuvam ihāsmāsu ni yachatam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam jānāmahai manasā sam cikitvā mā yuṣmahi manasā daivyena /

Halfverse: c mā ghoṣā ut sthur bahule vinirhate meṣuḥ paptad indrasyāhany āgate //2//

Hymn: 53 {55}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a amutrabhūyād adhi yad yamasya bṛhaspate abhiśaster amuñcaḥ /

Halfverse: c praty auhatām aśvinā mṛtyum asmad devānām agne bhiṣajā śacībhiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam krāmatam mā jahītam śarīram prāṇāpānau te sayujāv iha stām /

Halfverse: c śatam jīva śarado vardhamāno ‘gniṣ ṭe gopā adhipā vasiṣṭhaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āyur yat te atihitam parācair apānaḥ prāṇaḥ punar ā tāv itām /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭad āhār nirṛter upasthāt tad ātmani punar ā veśayāmi te //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a memam prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno ‘vahāya parā gāt /

Halfverse: c saptarṣibhya enam pari dadāmi te enam svasti jarase vahantu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pra viṣatam prāṇāpānāv anaḍvāhāv iva vrajam /

Halfverse: c ayam jarimnaḥ śevadhir ariṣṭa iha vardhatām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ā te prāṇam suvāmasi parā yakṣmam suvāmi te /

Halfverse: c āyur no viśvato dadhad ayam agnir vareṇyaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ud vayam tamasas pari rohanto nākam uttamam /

Halfverse: c devam devatrā sūryam aganma jyotir uttamam //7//

Hymn: 54 {56}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ṛcam sāma yajāmahe yābhyām karmāṇi kurvate /

Halfverse: c ete sadasi rājato yajñam deveṣu yachataḥ //1//

{57}

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ṛcam sāma yad aprākṣam havir ojo yajur balam /

Halfverse: c eṣa mā tasmān mā himsīd vedaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ śacīpate //1//

Hymn: 55

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye te panthāno ‘va divo yebhir viśvam airayaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhiḥ sumnayā dhehi no vaso //2//

Hymn: 56 {58}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tiraścirājer asitāt pṛdākoḥ pari sambhṛtam /

Halfverse: c tat kaṅkaparvaṇo viṣam iyam vīrud anīnaśat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a iyam vīrun madhujātā madhuścun madhulā madhūḥ /

Halfverse: c sā vihrutasya bheṣajy atho maśakajambhanī //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yato daṣṭam yato dhītam tatas te nir hvayāmasi /

Halfverse: c arbhasya tṛpradamśino maśakasyārasam viṣam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ayam yo vakro viparur vyaṅgo mukhāni vakrā vṛjinā kṛṇoṣi /

Halfverse: c tāni tvam brahmaṇas pate iṣīkām iva sam namaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a arasasya śarkoṭasya nīcīnasyopasarpataḥ /

Halfverse: c viṣam hy asyādiṣy atho enam ajījabham //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a na te bāhvor balam asti na śīrṣe nota madhyataḥ /

Halfverse: c atha kim pāpayā ‘muyā puche bibharṣy arbhakam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a adanti tvā pipīlikā vi vṛścanti mayūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarve bhala bravātha śārkoṭam arasam viṣam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ya ubhābhyām praharasi puchena cāsyṝna ca /

Halfverse: c āsyṝ3 na te viṣam kim u te puchadhāv asat //8//

Hymn: 57 {59}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad āśasā vadato me vicukṣubhe yad yācamānasya carato janāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c yad ātmani tanvo me viriṣṭam sarasvatī tad ā pṛṇad ghṛtena //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sapta kṣaranti siśave marutvate pitre putrāso apy avīvṛtann ṛtāni /

Halfverse: c ubhe id asyobhe asya rājata ubhe yatete ubhe asya puṣyataḥ //2//

Hymn: 58 {60}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrāvaruṇā sutapāv imam sutam somam pibatam madyam dhṛtavratau /

Halfverse: c yuvo ratho adhvaro devavītaye prati svasaram upa yātu pītaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indrāvaruṇā madhumattamasya vṛṣṇaḥ somasya vṛṣaṇā vṛṣethām /

Halfverse: c idam vām andhaḥ pariṣiktam āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayethām //2//

Hymn: 59 {61}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo naḥ śapād aśapataḥ śapato yaś ca naḥ śapāt /

Halfverse: c vṛkṣa iva vidyutā hata ā mūlād anu śuṣyatu //1//

Hymn: 60 {62}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ūrjam bibhrad vasuvanih sumedhā aghoreṇa cakṣuṣā mitriyeṇa /

Halfverse: c gṛhān aimi sumanā vandamāno ramadhvam mā bibhīta mat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ime gṛhā mayobhuva ūrjasvantaḥ payasvantaḥ /

Halfverse: c pūrṇā vāmena tiṣṭhantas te no jānantv āyataḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yeṣām adhyeti pravasan yeṣu saumanaso bahuḥ /

Halfverse: c gṛhān upa hvayāmahe te no jānantv āyataḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a upahūtā bhūridhanāḥ sakhāyaḥ svādusammudaḥ /

Halfverse: c akṣudhyā atṛṣyā sta gṛhā māsmad bibhītana //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a upahūtā iha gāva upahūtā ajāvayaḥ /

Halfverse: c atho annasya kīlāla upahūto gṛheṣu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sūnṛtāvantaḥ subhagā irāvanto hasāmudāḥ /

Halfverse: c atṛṣyā aksudhyā sta gṛhā māsmad bibhītana //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ihaiva sta mānu gāta viśvā rūpāṇi puṣyata /

Halfverse: c aiṣyāmi bhadreṇā saha bhūyāmso bhavatā mayā //7//

Hymn: 61 {63}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad agne tapasā tapa upatapyāmahe tapaḥ /

Halfverse: c priyāḥ śrutasya bhūyāsmāyuṣmantaḥ sumedhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agne tapas tapyāmaha upa tapyāmahe tapaḥ /

Halfverse: c śrutāni śṛṇvantaḥ vayam āyuṣmantaḥ sumedhasaḥ //2//

Hymn: 62 {64}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam agniḥ satpatir vṛddhavṛṣṇo rathīva pattīn ajayat purohitaḥ /

Halfverse: c nābhā pṛthivyām nihito davidyutad adhaspadam kṛṇutām ye pṛtanyavaḥ //1//

Hymn: 63 {65}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pṛtanājitam sahamānam agnim ukthyair havāmahe paramāt sadhasthāt /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ parṣad ati durgāṇi viśvā kṣāmad devo ‘ti duritāny agniḥ //1//

Hymn: 64 {66}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam yat kṛṣṇaḥ śakunir abhiniṣpatann apīpatat /

Halfverse: c āpo mā tasmāt sarvasmād duritāt pāntv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a idam yat kṛṣṇaḥ śakunir avāmṛkṣan nirṛte te mukhena /

Halfverse: c agnir mā tasmād enaso gārhapatyaḥ pra muñcatu //2//

Hymn: 65 {67}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pratīcīnaphalo hi tvam apāmārga rurohitha /

Halfverse: c sarvān mac chapathām adhi varīyo yavayā itaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad duṣkṛtam yac chamalam yad vā cerima pāpayā /

Halfverse: c tvayā tad viśvatomukhāpāmārgāpa mṛjmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śyāvadatā kunakhinā baṇḍena yat sahāsima /

Halfverse: c apāmārga tvayā vayam sarvam tad apa mṛjmahe //3//

Hymn: 66 {68}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yady antarikṣe yadi vāta āsa yadi vṛkṣeṣu yadi volapeṣu /

Halfverse: c yad aśravan paśava udyamānam tad brāhmaṇ punar asmān upaitu //1//

Hymn: 67 {69}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a punar maitv indriyam punar ātmā draviṇam brāhmaṇam ca /

Halfverse: c punar agnayo dhiṣṇyā yathāsthāma kalpayantām ihaiva //1//

Hymn: 68 {70}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sarasvati vrateṣu te divyeṣu devi dhāmasu /

Halfverse: c juṣasva havyam āhutam prajām devi rarāsva naḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a idam te havyam ghṛtavat sarasvatīdam pitṝṇām havir āsya1m yat /

Halfverse: c imāni ta uditā śamtamāni tebhir vayam madhumantaḥ syāma //2//

{71}

Verse: 3 {1}

Halfverse: a śivā naḥ śamtamā bhava sumṛḍīkā sarasvati /

Halfverse: c mā te yuyoma samdṛśaḥ //1//

Hymn: 69 {72}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śam no vāto vātu śam nas tapatu sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c ahāni śam bhavantu naḥ śam rātrī prati dhīyatām /

Halfverse: e śam uṣā no vy ḥchatu //1//

Hymn: 70 {73}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yat kim cāsau manasā yac ca vācā yajñair juhoti haviṣā yajuṣā /

Halfverse: c tan mṛtyunā nirṛtiḥ samvidānā purā satyād āhutim hantv asya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yātudhānā nirṛtir ād u rakṣas te asya ghnantv anṛtena satyam /

Halfverse: c indreṣitā devā ājam asya mathnantu mā tat sam pādi yad asau juhoti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ajirādhirājau śyenau sampātināv iva /

Halfverse: c ājyam pṛtanyato hatām yo naḥ kaścābhyaghāyati //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a apāñcau ta ubhau bāhū api nahyāmy āsyam /

Halfverse: c agner devasya manyunā tena te ‘vadhiṣam haviḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a api nahyāmi te bāhū api nahyāmy āsyam /

Halfverse: c agner ghorasya manyunā tena ‘vadhiṣam haviḥ //5//

Hymn: 71 {74}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pari tvāgne puram vayam vipram sahasya dhīmahi /

Halfverse: c dhṛṣadvarṇam divedive hantāram bhaṅgurāvataḥ //1//

Hymn: 72 {75}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭhatāva paśyatendrasya bhāgam ṛtviyam /

Halfverse: c yadi śrātam juhotana yady aśrātam mamattana //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śrātam havir o ṣv ṅndra pra yāhi jagāma sūro adhvano vi madhyam /

Halfverse: c pari tvāsate nidhibhiḥ sakhāyaḥ kulapā na vrājapatim carantam //2//

{76}

Verse: 3 {1}

Halfverse: a śrātam manya ūdhani śrātam agnau suśṛtam manye tad ṛtam navīyaḥ /

Halfverse: c mādhyandinasya savanasya dadhnaḥ pibendra vajrin purukṛj juṣāṇaḥ //1//

Hymn: 73 {77}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samiddho agnir vṛṣaṇā rathī divas tapto gharmo duhyate vām iṣe madhu /

Halfverse: c vayam hi vām purudamāso aśvinā havāmahe sadhamādeṣu kāravaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a samiddho agnir aśvinā tapto vām gharma ā gatam /

Halfverse: c duhyante nūnam vṛṣaṇeha dhenavo dasrā madanti vedhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ivāhākṛtaḥ śucir deveṣu yajño yo aśvinoś camaso devapānaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam u viśve amṛtāso juṣāṇā gandharvasya praty āsnā rihanti //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad usriyāsv āhutam ghṛtam payo ‘yam sa vām aśvinā bhāga ā gatam /

Halfverse: c mādhvī dhartārā vidathasya satpatī taptam gharmam pibatam divaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tapto vām gharmo nakṣatu svahotā pra vām adhvaryuś caratu payasvān /

Halfverse: c madhor dugdhasyāśvinā tanāyā vītam pātam payasa usriyāyāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a upa drava payasā godhug oṣam ā gharme siñca paya usriyāyāḥ /

Halfverse: c vi nākam akhyat savitā vareṇyo ‘nuprayāṇam uṣaso vi rājati //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a upa hvaye sudughām dhenum etām suhasto godhug uta dohad enām /

Halfverse: c śreṣṭham savam savitā sāviṣan no ‘bhīddho gharmas tad u ṣu pra vocat //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a hiṅkṛṇvatī vasupatnī vasūnām vatsam ichantī manasā nyāgan /

Halfverse: c duhām aśvibhyām payo aghnyeyam sā vardhatām mahate saubhagāya //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a juṣṭo damūnā atithir duroṇa imam no yajñam upa yāhi vidvān /

Halfverse: c viśvā agne abhiyujo vihatya śatrūyatām ā bharā bhojanāni //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a agne śardha mahate saubhagāya tava dyumnāny uttamāni santu /

Halfverse: c sam jāspatyam suyamam ā kṛṇuṣva śatrūyatām abhi tiṣṭhā mahāmsi //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a sūyavasād bhagavatī hi bhūyā adhā vayam bhagavantaḥ syāma /

Halfverse: c addhi tṛṇam aghnye viśvadānīm piba śuddham udakam ācarantī //11//

Hymn: 74 {78}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apacitām lohinīnām kṛṣṇā māteti śuśruma /

Halfverse: c muner devasya mūlena sarvā vidhyāmi tā aham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vidhyāmy āsām prathamām vidhyāmi uta madhyamām /

Halfverse: c idam jaghanyām āsām ā chinadmi stukām iva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvāṣṭreṇāham vacasā vi ta īrṣyām amīmadam /

Halfverse: c atho yo manyuṣ ṭe pate tam u te śamayāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vratena tvam vratapate samakto viśvāhā sumanā dīdihīha /

Halfverse: c tam tvā vayam jātavedaḥ samiddham prajāvanta upa sadema sarve //4//

Hymn: 75 {79}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prajāvatīḥ sūyavase ruśantīḥ śuddhā apaḥ suprapāṇe pibantīḥ /

Halfverse: c mā va stena īśata māghaśamsaḥ pari vo rudrasya hetir vṛṇaktu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a padajñā stha ramatayaḥ samhitā viśvanāmnīḥ /

Halfverse: c upa mā devīr devebhir eta /

Halfverse: e imam goṣṭham idam sado ghṛtenāsmānt sam ukṣata //2//

Hymn: 76 {80}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā susrasaḥ susraso asatībhyo asattarāḥ /

Halfverse: c sehor arasatarā havaṇād vikledīyasīḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yā graivyā apacito ‘tho yā upapakṣyāḥ /

Halfverse: c vijāmni yā apacitaḥ svayamsrasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaḥ kīkasāḥ praśṛṇāti talīdyam avatiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: c nir hās tam sarvam jāyānyam yaḥ kaś ca kakudi śritaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pakṣī jāyānyah patati sa ā viśati pūruṣam /

Halfverse: c tad akṣitasya bheṣajam ubhayoḥ sukṣatasya ca //4//

{81}

Verse: 5 {1}

Halfverse: a vidma vai te jāyānya jānam yato jāyānya jāyase /

Halfverse: c katham ha tatra tvam hano yasya kṛṇmo havir gṛhe //1//

Verse: 6 {2}

Halfverse: a dhṛṣat piba kalaśe somam indra vṛtrahā śūra samare vasūnām /

Halfverse: c mādhyandine savana ā vṛṣasva rayiṣṭhāno rayim asmāsu dhehi //2//

Hymn: 77 {82}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sāmtapanā idam havir marutas taj jujuṣṭana /

Halfverse: c asmākotī riśādasah //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo no marto maruto durhṛṇāyus tiraś cittāni vasavo jighāmsati /

Halfverse: c druhaḥ pāśān prati muñcatām sas tapiṣṭhena tapasā hantanā tam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samvatsarīṇā marutaḥ svarkā urukṣayāḥ sagaṇā mānuṣāsaḥ /

Halfverse: c te asmat pāśān pra muñcantv enasas sāmtapanā matsarā mādayiṣṇavaḥ //3//

Hymn: 78 {83}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vi te muñcāmi raśanām vi yoktram vi niyojanam /

Halfverse: c ihaiva tvam ajasra edhy agne //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asmai kṣatrāṇi dhārayantam agne yunajmi tvā brahmaṇā daivyena /

Halfverse: c dīdihy asmabhyam draviṇeha bhadram premam voco havirdām devatāsu //2//

Hymn: 79 {84}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yat te devā akṛṇvan bhāgadheyam amāvāsye samvasanto mahitvā /

Halfverse: c tenā no yajñam pipṛhi viśvavāre rayim no dhehi subhage suvīram //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aham evāsmy amāvāsyā3 mām ā vasanti sukṛto mayīme /

Halfverse: c mayi devā ubhaye sādyāś cendrajyeṣṭhāḥ sam agachanta sarve //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āgan rātrī saṅgamanī vasūnām ūrjam puṣṭam vasv āveśayantī /

Halfverse: c amāvāsyāyai haviṣa vidhemorjam duhānā payasā na āgan //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a amāvāsye na tvad etāny anyo viśvā rūpāṇi paribhūr jajāna /

Halfverse: c yatkāmās te juhumas tan no astu vayam syāma patayo rayiṇām //4//

Hymn: 80 {85}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a paurṇamāsī jigāya /

Halfverse: c tasyām devaiḥ samvasanto mahitvā nākasya pṛṣṭhe sam iṣā madema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vṛṣabham vājinam vayam paurṇamāsam yajāmahe /

Halfverse: c sa no dadātv akṣitām rayim anupadasvatīm //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a prajāpate na tvad etāny anyo viśvā rūpāṇi paribhūr jajāna /

Halfverse: c yatkāmās te juhumas tan no astu vayam syāma patayo rayīṇām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a paurṇamāsī prathamā yajñiyāsīd ahnām rātrīṇām atiśarvareṣu /

Halfverse: c ye tvām yajñair yajñiye ardhayanty amī te nāke sukṛtaḥ praviṣṭāḥ //4//

Hymn: 81 {86}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pūrvāparam carato mayayaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto ‘rṇavam /

Halfverse: c viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭa ṛtūṁr anyo vidadhaj jāyase navaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a navonavo bhavasi jāyamāno ‘hnām ketur uṣasām eṣy agram /

Halfverse: c bhāgam devebhyo vi dadhāsy āyan pra candramas tirase dhīrgham āyuḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a somasyāmśo yudhām pate ‘nūno nāma vā asi /

Halfverse: c anūnam darśa mā kṛdhi prajayā ca dhanena ca //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a darśo ‘si darśato ‘si samagro ‘si samantaḥ /

Halfverse: c samagraḥ samanto bhūyāsam gobhir aśvaiḥ prajayā paśubhir gṛhair dhanena //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmas tasya tvam prāṇenā pyāyasva /

Halfverse: c ā vayam pyāsiṣīmahi gobhir aśvaiḥ prajayā paśubhir gṛhair dhanena //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yam devā amśum āpyāyayanti yam akṣitam akṣitā bhakṣayanti /

Halfverse: c tenāsmān indro varuṇo bṛhaspatir ā pyāyayantu bhuvanasya gopāḥ //6//

Hymn: 82 {87}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhy arcata suṣṭutim gavyam ājim asmāsu bhadrā draviṇāni dhatta /

Halfverse: c imam yajñam nayata devatā no ghṛtasya dhārā madhumat pavantām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mayy agre agnim gṛhṇāmi saha kṣatreṇa varcasā balena /

Halfverse: c mayi prajām mayy āyur dadhāmi svāhā mayy agnim //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ihaivāgne adhy dhārayā rayim mā tvā ni kran pūrvacittā nikāriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c kṣatreṇāgne suyamam astu tubhyam upasattā vardhatām te aniṣṭṛtaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anv agnir uṣasām agram akhyad anv ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c anu sūrya uṣaso anu raśmīn anu dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a praty agnir uṣasām agram akhyat prati ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c prati sūryasya purudhā ca raśmīn prati dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatāna //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ghṛtam te agne divye sadhasthe ghṛtena tvām manur adyā sam indhe /

Halfverse: c ghṛtam te devīr naptya1 ā vahantu ghṛtam tubhyam duhratām gāvo agne //6//

Hymn: 83 {88}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apsu te rājan varuṇa gṛho hiraṇyayo mithaḥ /

Halfverse: c tato dhṛtavrato rājā sarvā dhāmāni muñcatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dāmnodāmno rājann ito varuṇa muñca naḥ /

Halfverse: c yad āpo aghnyā iti varuṇeti yad ūcima tato varuṇa muñca naḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ud uttamam varuṇa pāśam asmad avādhamam vi madhyamam śrathāya /

Halfverse: c adhā vayam āditya vrate tavānāgaso aditaye syāma //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prāsmat pāśān varuṇa muñca sarvān ya uttamā adhamā vāruṇā ye /

Halfverse: c duṣvapnyam duritam ni ṣvāsmad atha gachema sukṛtasya lokam //4//

Hymn: 84 {89}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a anādhṛṣyo jātavedā amartyo virāḍ agne kṣatrabhṛd dīdihīha /

Halfverse: c viśvā amīvāḥ pramuñcan mānuṣībhiḥ śivābhir adya pari pāhi no gayam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indra kṣatram abhi vāmam ojo ‘jāyathā vṛṣabha carṣaṇīnām /

Halfverse: c apānudo janam amitrayantam urum devebhyo akṛṇor u lokam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ parāvata ā jagamyāt parasyāḥ /

Halfverse: c sṛkam samśāya pavim indra tigmam vi śatrūn tāḍhi vi mṛgho nudasva //3//

Hymn: 85 {90}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tyam ū ṣu vājinam devajūtam sahovānam tarutāram rathānām /

Halfverse: c ariṣṭanemim pṛtanājim āśum svastaye tārkṣyam ihā huvema //1//

Hymn: 86 {91}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a trātāram indram avitāram indram havehave suhavam śūram indram /

Halfverse: c huve nu śakram puruhūtam indram svasti na indro maghavān kṛṇotu //1//

Hymn: 87 {92}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo agnau rudro yo apsv antar ya oṣadhīr vīrudha āviveśa /

Halfverse: c ya imāviśvā bhuvanāni cākḷpe tasmai rudrāya namo astv agnaye //1//

Hymn: 88 {93}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apehy arir asy arir vā asi viṣe viṣam apṛkthā viṣam id vā apṛkthāḥ /

Halfverse: c ahim evābhyapehi tam jahi //1//

Hymn: 89 {94}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apo divyā acāyiṣam rasena sam apṛkṣmahi /

Halfverse: c payasvān agna āgamam tam mā sam sṛja varcasā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam māgne varcasā sṛja sam prajayā sam āyuṣā /

Halfverse: c vidyur me asya devā indro vidyāt saha ṛṣibhiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a idam āpaḥ pra vahatāvadyam ca malam ca yat /

Halfverse: c yac cābhidudrohānṛtam yac ca śepe abhīruṇam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a edho ‘sy edhiṣīya samid asi sam edhiṣīya /

Halfverse: c tejo ‘si tejo mayi dhehi //4//

Hymn: 90 {95}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a api vṛśca purāṇavad vratater iva guṣpitam /

Halfverse: c ojo dāsasya dambhaya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vayam tad asya sambhṛtam vasv indrena vi bhajāmahai /

Halfverse: c mlāpayāmi bhrajaḥ śibhram varuṇasya vratena te //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yathā śepo apāyātai strīṣu cāsad anāvayāḥ /

Halfverse: c avasthasya knadīvataḥ śāṅkurasya nitodinaḥ /

Halfverse: e yad ātatam ava tat tanu yad uttatam ni tat tanu //3//

Hymn: 91 {96}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indraḥ sutrāmā svavāṁ avobhiḥ sumṛḍīko bhavatu viśvavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c bādhatām dveṣo abhayam naḥ kṛṇotu suvīryasya patayaḥ syāma //1//

Hymn: 92 {97}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sa sutrāmā svavāṁ indro asmad ārāc cid dveṣaḥ sanutar yuyotu /

Halfverse: c tasya vayam sumatau yajñiyasyāpi bhadre saumanase syāma //1//

Hymn: 93 {98}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indreṇa manyunā vayam abhi ṣyāma pṛtanyataḥ /

Halfverse: c ghnanto vṛtrāṇy aprati //1//

Hymn: 94 {99}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dhruvam dhruveṇa haviṣāva somam nayāmasi /

Halfverse: c yathā na indraḥ kevalīr viśaḥ sammanasas karat //1//

Hymn: 95 {100}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud asya śyāvau vithurau gṛdhrau dyām iva petatuḥ /

Halfverse: c ucchocanapraśocanav asyocchocanau hṛdaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aham enāv ud atiṣṭhipam gāvau śrāntasadāv iva /

Halfverse: c kurkurāv iva kūjantāv udavantau vṛkāv iva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ātodinau nitodināv atho samtodināv uta /

Halfverse: c api nahyāmy asya meḍhram ya itaḥ strī pumān jabhāra //3//

Hymn: 96 {101}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asadan gāvaḥ sadane ‘paptad vasatim vayaḥ /

Halfverse: c āsthāne parvatā asthuḥ sthāmni vṛkkāv atiṣṭhipam //1//

Hymn: 97 {102}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad adya tvā prayati yajñe asmin hotaś cikitvann avṛṇīmahīha /

Halfverse: c dhruvam ayo dhruvam utā śaviṣṭhaipravidvān yajñam upa yāhi somam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam indra no manasā neṣa gobhiḥ sam sūribhir harivant sam svastyā /

Halfverse: c sam brahmaṇā devahitam yad asti sam devānām sumatau yajñiyānām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yān āvaha uśato deva devāms tān preraya sve agne sadhasthe /

Halfverse: c jakṣivāmsaḥ papivāmso madhūny asmai dhatta vasavo vasūni //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sugā vo devāḥ sadanā akarma ya ājagma savane mā juṣāṇāḥ /

Halfverse: c vahamānā bharamāṇāḥ svā vasūni vasum gharmam divam ā rohatānu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yajña yajñam gacha yajñapatim gacha /

Halfverse: c svām yonim gacha svāhā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a eṣa te yajño yajñapate sahasūktavākaḥ /

Halfverse: c suvīryaḥ svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a vaṣaḍ dhutebhyo vaṣaḍ ahutebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c devā gātuvido gātum vittvā gātum ita //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a manasas pata imam no divi deveṣu yajñam /

Halfverse: c svāhā divi svāhā pṛthivyām svāhāntarikṣe svāhā vāte dhām svāhā //8//

Hymn: 98 {103}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam barhir aktam haviṣā ghṛtena sam indreṇa vasunā sam marudbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sam devair viśvadevebhir aktam indram gachatu haviḥ svāhā //1//

Hymn: 99 {104}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pari stṛṇīhi pari dhehi vedim mā jāmim moṣīr amuyā śayānām /

Halfverse: c hotṛṣadanam haritam hiraṇyayam niṣkā ete yajamānasya loke //1//

Hymn: 100 {105}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a paryāvarte duṣvapnyāt pāpāt svapnyād abhūtyāḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmāham antaram kṛṇve parā svapnamukhāḥ śucaḥ //1//

Hymn: 101 {106}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yat svapne annam aśnāmi na prātar adhigamyate /

Halfverse: c sarvam tad astu me śivam nahi tad dṛṣyate divā //1//

Hymn: 102 {107}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a namaskṛtya dyāvāpṛthivībhyām antarikṣāya mṛtyave /

Halfverse: c mekṣāmy ūrdhvas tiṣṭhan mā mā himsiṣur īśvarāḥ //1//

Hymn: 103 {108}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ko asyā no druho ‘vadyavatyā un neṣyati kṣatriyo vasya ichan /

Halfverse: c ko yajñakāmaḥ ka u pūrtikāmaḥ ko deveṣu vanute dīrgham āyuḥ //1//

Hymn: 104 {109}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kaḥ pṛśnim dhenum varuṇena dattām atharvane sudughām nityavatsām /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatinā sakhyam juṣaṇo yathāvaśam tanvaḥ kalpayāti //1//

Hymn: 105 {110}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apakrāman pauruṣeyād vṛṇāno daivyam vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c praṇītīr abhyāvartasva viśvebhiḥ sakhibhiḥ saha //1//

Hymn: 106 {111}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad asmṛti cakṛma kim cid agna upārima caraṇe jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c tataḥ pāhi tvam naḥ pracetaḥ śubhe sakhibhyo amṛtatvam astu naḥ //1//

Hymn: 107 {112}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ava divas tārayanti sapta sūryasya raśmayaḥ /

Halfverse: c āpaḥ samudriyā dhārās tās śalyam asisrasan //1//

Hymn: 108 {113}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo na stāyad dipsati yo na āviḥ svo vidvān araṇo vā no agne /

Halfverse: c pratīcy etv araṇī datvatī tān maiṣām agne vāstu bhūn mo apatyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo naḥ suptān jāgrato vābhidāsāt tiṣṭhato vā carato jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānareṇa sayujā sajoṣās tān pratīco nir daha jātavedaḥ //2//

Hymn: 109 {114}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam ugrāya babhrave namo yo akṣeṣu tanūvaśī /

Halfverse: c ghṛtena kalim śikṣāmi sa no mṛḍātīdṛśe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ghṛtam apsarābhyo vaha tvam agne pāmsūn akṣebhyaḥ sikatā apaś ca /

Halfverse: c yathābhagam havyadātim juṣāṇā madanti devā ubhayāni havyā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apsarasaḥ sadhamādam madanti havirdhānam antarā sūryam ca /

Halfverse: c tā me hastau sam sṛjantu ghṛtena sapatnam me kitavam randhayantu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ādinavam pratidīvne ghṛtenāsmāṁ abhi kṣara /

Halfverse: c vṛkṣam ivāśanyā jahi yo asmān pratidīvyati //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yo no dyuve dhanam idam cakāra yo akṣāṇām glahanam śeṣaṇam ca /

Halfverse: c sa no devo havir idam juṣāṇo gandharvebhiḥ sadhamādam madema //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a samvasava iti vo nāmadheyam ugrampaśyā rāṣṭrabhṛto hy akṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo va indavo haviṣā vidhema vayam syāma patayo rayīṇām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a devān yan nāthito huve brahmacaryam yad ūṣima /

Halfverse: c akṣān yad babhrūn ālabhe te no mṛḍantv īdṛśe //7//

Hymn: 110 {115}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agna indraś ca dāśuṣe hato vṛtrāṇy aprati /

Halfverse: c ubhā hi vṛtrahantamā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yābhyām ajayant sva1r agra eva yāv ātasthatur bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c pra carṣaṇīvṛṣaṇā vajrabāhū agnim indram vṛtrahaṇā huve ‘ham //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a upa tvā devo agramīc camasena bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c indra gīrbhir na ā viśa yajamānāya sunvate //3//

Hymn: 111 {116}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrasya kukṣir asi somadhāna ātmā devānām uta mānuṣāṇām /

Halfverse: c iha prajā janaya yās ta āsu yā anyatreha tās te ramantām //1//

Hymn: 112 {117}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śumbhanī dyāvāpṛthivī antisumne mahivrate /

Halfverse: c āpaḥ sapta susruvur devīs tā no muñcantv amhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a muñcantu mā śapathyā3d atho varuṇyād uta /

Halfverse: c atho yamasya paḍvīśād viśvasmād devakilbiṣāt //2//

Hymn: 113 {118}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tṛṣṭike tṛṣṭavandana ud amūm chindhi tṛṣṭike /

Halfverse: c yathā kṛtadviṣṭāso ‘muṣmai śepyāvate //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tṛṣṭāsi tṛṣṭikā viṣā viṣātaky asi /

Halfverse: c parivṛktā yathāsasy ṛṣabhasya vaśeva //2//

Hymn: 114 {119}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā te dade vakṣaṇābhya ā te ‘ham hṛdayād dade /

Halfverse: c ā te mukhasya saṅkāśāt sarvam te varca ā dade //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a preto yantu vyādhyaḥ prānudhyāḥ pro aśastayaḥ /

Halfverse: c agnī rakṣasvinīr hantu somo hantu durasyatīḥ //2//

Hymn: 115 {120}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pra patetaḥ pāpi lakṣmi naśyetaḥ prāmutah pata /

Halfverse: c ayasmayenāṅkena dviṣate tvā sajāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yā mā lakṣmīḥ patayālūr ajuṣṭābhicaskanda vandaneva vṛkṣam /

Halfverse: c anyatrāsmat savitas tām ito dhā hiraṇyahasto vasu no rarāṇaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ekaśatam lakṣmyo3 martyasya sākam tanvā januṣo ‘dhi jātāḥ /

Halfverse: c tāsām pāpiṣṭhā nir itaḥ pra hiṇmaḥ śivā asmabhyam jātavedo niyacha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a etā enā vyākaram khile gā viṣṭhitā iva /

Halfverse: c ramantām puṇyā lakṣmīr yāḥ pāpīs tā anīnaśam //4//

Hymn: 116 {121}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a namo rūrāya cyavanāya nodanāya dhṛṣṇave /

Halfverse: c namaḥ śītāya pūrvakāmakṛtvane //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo anyedyur ubhayadyur abhyetīmam maṇḍūkam /

Halfverse: c abhy ṝtv avrataḥ //2//

Hymn: 117 {122}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā mandrair indra haribhir yāhi mayūraromabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c mā tvā ke cid vi yaman vim na pāśino ‘ti dhanveva tāṁ ihi //1//

Hymn: 118 {123}

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a marmāṇi te varmaṇā chādayāmi somas tvā rājāmṛtenānu vastām /

Halfverse: c uror varīyo varuṇas te kṛṇotu jayantam tvānu devā madantu //1//

## Book: 8

8

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a antakāya mṛtyave namaḥ prānā apānā iha te ramantām /

Halfverse: c ihāyam astu puruṣaḥ sahāsunā sūryasya bhāge amṛtasya loke //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ud enam bhago agrabhīd ud enam somo amśumān /

Halfverse: c ud enam maruto devā ud indrāgnī svastaye //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a iha te ‘sur iha prāṇa ihāyur iha te manaḥ /

Halfverse: c ut tvā nirṛtyāḥ pāśebhyo daivyā vacā bharāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ut krāmātaḥ puruṣa māva patthā mṛtyoḥ paḍvīṣam avamuñcamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c mā chitthā asmāl lokād agneḥ sūryasya samdṛśaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tubhyam vātaḥ pavatām mātariśvā tubhyam varṣantv amṛtāny āpaḥ /

Halfverse: c sūryas te tanvṝ3 śam tapāti tvām mṛtyur dayatām mā pra meṣṭhāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a udyānam te puruṣa nāvayānam jīvātum te dakṣatātim kṛnomi /

Halfverse: c ā hi rohemam amṛtam sukham ratham atha jirvir vidatham ā vadāsi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a mā te manas tatra gān mā tiro bhūn mā jīvebhyaḥ pra mado mānu gāḥ pitṝn /

Halfverse: c viśve devā abhi rakṣantu tveha //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mā gatānām ā dīdhīthā ye nayanti parāvatam /

Halfverse: c ā roha tamaso jyotir ehy ā te hastau rabhāmahe //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a śyāmaś ca tvā mā śabalaś ca preṣitau yamasya yau pathirakṣī śvānau /

Halfverse: c arvāṅ ehi mā vi dīdhyo mātra tiṣṭhaḥ parāṅmanāḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a maitam panthām anu gā bhīma eṣa yena pūrvam neyatha tam bravīmi /

Halfverse: c tama etat puruṣa mā pra patthā bhayam parastād abhayam te arvāk //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a rakṣantu tvāgnayo ye apsv a1ntā rakṣatu tvā manuṣyā3 yam indhate /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānaro rakṣatu jātavedā divyas tvā mā pra dhāg vidyutā saha //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a mā tvā kravyād abhi mamstārāt samkasukāc cara rakṣatu tvā dyau rakṣatu /

Halfverse: c pṛthivī sūryaś ca tvā rakṣatām candramāś ca /

Halfverse: e antarikṣam rakṣatu devahetyāḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a bodhaś ca tvā pratibodhaś ca rakṣatām asvapnaś ca tvānavadrāṇaś ca rakṣatām /

Halfverse: c gopāyamś ca tvā jāgṛviś ca rakṣatām //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a te tvā rakṣantu te tvā gopāyantu tebhyo namas tebhyaḥ svāhā //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a jīvebhyas tvā samude vāyur indro dhātā dadhātu savitā trāyamāṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c mā tvā prāṇo balam hāsīd asum te ‘nu hvayāmasi //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a mā tvā jambhaḥ samhanur mā tamo vidan mā jihvā barhis pramayuḥ kathā syāḥ /

Halfverse: c ut tvādityā vasavo bharantūd indrāgnī svastaye //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ut tvā dyaur ut pṛthivy ut prajāpatir agrabhīt /

Halfverse: c ut tvā mṛtyor oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīr apīparan //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a ayam devā ihaivāstv ayam māmutra gād itaḥ /

Halfverse: c imam sahasravīryeṇa mṛtyor ut pārayāmasi //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ut tvā mṛtyor apīparam sam dhamantu vayodhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c mā tvā vyastakeśyo3 mā tvāgharudo rudan //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a āhārṣam avidam tvā punar āgāḥ punarṇavaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvāṅga sarvam te cakṣuḥ sarvam āyuś ca te ‘vidam //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a vy avāt te jyotir abhūd apa tvat tamo akramīt /

Halfverse: c apa tvan mṛtyum nirṛtim apa yakṣmam ni dadhmasi //21// {2}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā rabhasvemām amṛtasya śnuṣṭim achidyamānā jaradaṣṭir astu te /

Halfverse: c asum ta āyuḥ punar ā bharāmi rajas tamo mopa gā mā pra meṣṭhāh //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jīvatām jyotir abhyehy arvāṅ ā tvā harāmi śataśāradāya /

Halfverse: c avamuñcan mṛtyupāśān aśastim drāghīya āyuḥ prataram te dadhāmi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vātāt te prānam avidam sūryāc cakṣur aham tava /

Halfverse: c yat te manas tvayi tad dhārayāmi sam vitsvāṅgair vada jihvayālapan //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prāṇena tvā dvipadām catuṣpadām agnim iva jātam abhi sam dhamāmi /

Halfverse: c namas te mṛtyo cakṣuṣe namaḥ prāṇāya te ‘karam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ayam jīvatu mā mṛtemam sam īrayāmasi /

Halfverse: c kṛṇomy asmai bheṣajam mṛtyo mā puruṣam vadhīḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a jīvalām naghāriṣām jīvantīm oṣadhīm aham /

Halfverse: c trāyamāṇām sahamānām sahasvatīm iha huve ‘smā ariṣṭatātaye //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a adhi brūhi mā rabhathāh sṛjemam tavaiva sant sarvahāyāḥ ihāstu /

Halfverse: c bhavāśarvau mṛḍatam śarma yachatam apasidhya duritam dhattam āyuḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a asmai mṛtyo adhi brūhīmam dayasvod ito ‘yam etu /

Halfverse: c ariṣṭaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ suśruj jarasā śatahāyana ātmanā bhujam aśnutām //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a devānām hetih pari tvā vṛṇaktu pārayāmi tvā rajasa ut tvā mṛtyor apīparam /

Halfverse: c ārād agnim kravyādam nirūham jīvātave te paridhim dadhāmi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yat te niyānam rajasam mṛtyo anavadharṣyam /

Halfverse: c patha imam tasmād rakṣanto brahmāsmai varma kṛṇmasi //10// {3}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a kṛṇomi te prāṇāpānau jarām mṛtyum dīrgham āyuḥ svasti /

Halfverse: c vaivasvatena prahitān yamadūtāmś carato ‘pa sedhāmi sarvān //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ārād arātim nirṛtim paro grāhim kravyādaḥ piśācān /

Halfverse: c rakṣo yat sarvam durbhūtam tat tama ivāpa hanmasi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a agneṣ ṭa prānam amṛtād āyuṣmato vanve jātavedasaḥ /

Halfverse: c yathā na riṣyā amṛtaḥ sajūr asas tat te kṛṇomi tad u te sam ṛdhyatām //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a śive te stām dyāvāpṛthivī asamtāpe abhiśriyau /

Halfverse: c śam te sūrya ā tapatu śam vāto vātu te hṛde /

Halfverse: e śivā abhi kṣarantu tvāpo divyāḥ payasvatīḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a śivās te santv oṣadhaya ut tvāhārṣam adharasyā uttarām pṛthivīm abhi /

Halfverse: c tatra tvādityau rakṣatām sūryācandramasāv ubhā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yat te vāsaḥ paridhānam yām nīvim kṛṇuṣe tvam /

Halfverse: c śivam te tanvṝ3 tat kṛṇmaḥ samsparśe ‘drūkṣṇam astu te //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yat kṣureṇa marcayatā sutejasā vaptā vapasi keśaśmaśru /

Halfverse: c śubham mukham mā na āyuḥ pra moṣīḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a śivau te stām vrīhiyavāv abalāsāv adomadhau /

Halfverse: c etau yakṣmam vi bādhete etau muñcato amhasaḥ //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yad aśnāsi yat pibasi dhānyam kṛṣyāḥ payaḥ /

Halfverse: c yad ādya1m yad anādyam sarvam te annam aviṣam kṛṇomi //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ahne ca tvā rātraye cobhābhyām pari dadmasi /

Halfverse: c arāyebhyo jighatsubhya imam me pari rakṣata //20// {4}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a śatam te ‘yutam hāyanān dve yuge trīṇi catvāri kṛṇmaḥ /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī viśve devās te ‘nu manyantām ahṛṇīyamānāḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a śarade tvā hemantāya vasantāya grīṣmāya pari dadmasi /

Halfverse: c varṣāṇi tubhyam syonāni yeṣu vardhanta oṣadhīḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a mṛtyur īśe dvipadām mṛtyur īśe catuṣpadām /

Halfverse: c tasmāt tvām mṛtyor gopater ud bharāmi sa mā bibheḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a so ‘riṣṭa na mariṣyasi na mariṣyasi mā bibheḥ /

Halfverse: c na vai tatra mriyante no yanti adhamam tamaḥ //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a sarvo vai tatra jīvati gaur aśvaḥ puruṣaḥ paśuḥ /

Halfverse: c yatredam brahma kriyate paridhir jīvanāya kam //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a pari tvā pātu samānebhyo ‘bhicārāt sabandhubhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c amamrir bhavāmṛto ‘tijīvo mā te hāsiṣur asavaḥ śarīram //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a ye mṛtyava ekaśatam yā nāṣṭrā atitāryāḥ /

Halfverse: c muñcantu tasmāt tvām devā agner vaiśvānarād adhi //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a agneḥ śarīram asi pārayiṣṇu rakṣohāsi sapatnahā /

Halfverse: c atho amīvacātanaḥ pūtudrur nāma bheṣajam //28// {5}

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a rakṣohaṇam vājinam ā jigharmi mitram prathiṣṭham upa yāmi śarma /

Halfverse: c śiśāno agniḥ kratubhiḥ samiddhaḥ sa no divā sa riṣaḥ pātu naktam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayodamṣṭro arciṣā yātudhānān upa spṛśa jātavedaḥ samiddhaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā jihvayā mūradevān rabhasva kravyādo vṛṣṭvāpi dhatsvāsan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ubhobhayāvinn upa dhehi damṣṭrau himsraḥ śiśāno ‘varam param ca /

Halfverse: c utāntarikṣe pari yāhy agne jambhaiḥ sam dhehy abhi yātudhānān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a agne tvacam yātudhānasya bhindhi himsrāśanir harasā hantv enam /

Halfverse: c pra parvāṇi jātavedaḥ śṛṇīhi kravyāt kraviṣṇur vi cinotv enam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yatredānīm paśyasi jātavedas tiṣṭhantam agna uta vā carantam /

Halfverse: c utāntarikṣe patantam yātudhānam tam astā vidhya śarvā śiśānaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yajñair iṣūḥ samnamamāno agne ivācā śalyāṁ aśanibhir dihānaḥ /

Halfverse: c tābhir vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān pratīco bāhūn prati bhaṅgdhy eṣām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a utārabdhānt spṛnuhi jātaveda utārebhāṇāṁ ṛṣṭibhir yātudhānān /

Halfverse: c agne pūrvo ni jahi śośucāna āmādaḥ kṣviṅkās tam adantv enīḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a iha pra brūhi yatamaḥ so agne yātudhāno ya idam kṛṇoti /

Halfverse: c tam ā rabhasva samidhā yaviṣṭha nṛcakṣasaś cakṣuṣe randhayainam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tīkṣṇenāgne cakṣuṣā rakṣa yajñam prāñcam vasubhyaḥ pra ṇaya pracetaḥ /

Halfverse: c himsram rakṣāmsy abhi śośucānam mā tvā dabhan yātudhānā nṛcakṣaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a nṛcakṣā rakṣaḥ pari paśya vikṣu tasya trīṇi prati śṛṇīhy agrā /

Halfverse: c tasyāgne pṛṣṭīr harasā śṛṇīhi tredhā mūlam yātudhānasya vṛśca //10// {6}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a trir yātudhānaḥ prasitim ta etv ṛtam yo agne anṛtena hanti /

Halfverse: c tam arciṣā sphūrjayan jātavedaḥ samakṣam enam gṛṇate ni yuṅgdhi //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yad agne adya mithunā śapāto yad vācas tṛṣṭam janayanta rebhāḥ /

Halfverse: c manyor manasaḥ śaravyā3 jāyate yā tayā vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a parā śṛṇīhi tapasā yātudhānān parāgne rakṣo harasā śṛṇīhi /

Halfverse: c parārciṣā mūradevān chṛṇīhi parāsutṛpaḥ śośucataḥ śṛṇīhi //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a parādya devā vṛjinam śṛṇantu pratyag enam śapathā yantu sṛṣṭāḥ /

Halfverse: c vācāstenam śarava ṛchantu marman viśvasyaitu prasitim yātudhānaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yaḥ pauruṣeyeṇa kraviṣā samaṅkte yo aśvyena paśunā yātudhānaḥ /

Halfverse: c yo aghnyāyā bharati kṣīram agne teṣām śīrṣāṇi harasāpi vṛśca //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a viṣam gavām yātudhānā bharantām ā vṛścantām aditaye durevāḥ /

Halfverse: c paraiṇān devaḥ savitā dadātu parā bhāgam oṣadhīnām jayantām //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a samvatsarīṇam paya usriyāyās tasya māśīd yātudhāno nṛcakṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c pīyūṣam agne yatamas titṛpsāt tam pratyañcam arciṣā vidhya marmaṇi //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a sanād agne mṛṇasi yātudhānān na tvā rakṣāmsi pṛtanāsu jigyuḥ /

Halfverse: c sahamūrān anu daha kravyādo mā te hetyā mukṣata daivyāyāḥ //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a tvam no agne adharād udaktas tvam paścād uta rakṣā purastāt /

Halfverse: c prati tye te ajarāsas tapiṣṭhā aghaśamsam śośucato dahantu //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a paścāt purastād adharād utottarāt kaviḥ kāvyena pari pāhy agne /

Halfverse: c sakhā sakhāyam ajaro jarimne agne martāṁ amartyas tvam naḥ //20// {7}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a tad agne cakṣuḥ prati dhehi rebhe śaphārujo yena paśyasi yātudhānān /

Halfverse: c atharvavaj jyotiṣā daivyena satyam dhūrvantam acitam nyoṣa //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a pari tvāgne puram vayam vipram sahasya dhīmahi /

Halfverse: c dhṛṣadvarṇam divedive hantāram bhaṅgurāvataḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a viṣeṇa bhaṅgurāvataḥ prati sma rakṣaso jahi /

Halfverse: c agne tigmena śociṣā tapuragrābhir arcibhiḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a vi jyotiṣā bṛhatā bhāty agnir āvir viśvāni kṛṇute mahitvā /

Halfverse: c prādevīr māyāḥ sahate durevāḥ śiśīte śṛṅge rakṣobhyo vinikṣe //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a ye te śṛṅge ajare jātavedas tigmahetī brahmasamśite /

Halfverse: c tābhyām durhārdam abhidāsantam kimīdinam /

Halfverse: e pratyañcam arciṣā jātavedo vi nikṣva //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a agnī rakṣāmsi sedhati śukraśocir amartyaḥ /

Halfverse: c śuciḥ pāvaka īḍyaḥ //26// {8}

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrāsomā tapatam rakṣa ubjatam ny arpayatam vṛṣaṇā tamovṛdhaḥ /

Halfverse: c parā śṛṇītam acito ny oṣatam hatam nudethām ni śiśītam attriṇaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indrāsomā sam aghaśamsam abhy agham tapur yayastu carur agnimāṁ iva /

Halfverse: c brahmadviṣe kravyāde ghoracakṣase dveṣo dhattam anavāyam kimīdine //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indrāsomā duṣkṛto vavre antar anārambhaṇe tamasi pra vidhyatam /

Halfverse: c yato naiṣām punar ekaś canodayat tad vām astu sahase manyumac chavaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indrāsomā vartayatam divo vadham sam pṛthivyā aghaśamsāya tarhaṇam /

Halfverse: c ut takṣatam svaryam1 parvatebhyo yena rakṣo vāvṛdhānam nijūrvathaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indrāsomā vartayatam divas pary agnitaptebhir yuvam aśmahanmabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c tapurvadhebhir ajarebhir attriṇo ni parśāne vidhyatam yantu nisvaram //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indrāsomā pari vām bhūtu viśvata iyam matiḥ kakṣyāśveva vājinā /

Halfverse: c yām vām hotrām parihinomi medhayemā brahmāṇi nṛpatī iva jinvatam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a prati smarethām tujayadbhir evair hatam druho rakṣaso bhaṅgurāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c indrāsomā duṣkṛte mā sugam bhūd yo mā kadā cid abhidāsati druhuḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yo mā pākena manasā carantam abhicaṣṭe anṛtebhir vacobhiḥ /

Halfverse: c āpa iva kāśinā samgṛbhītā asann astv asataḥ indra vaktā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ye pākaśamsam viharanta evair ye vā bhadram dūṣayanti svadhābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c ahaye vā tān pradadātu soma ā vā dadhātu nirṛter upaṣṭhe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yo no rasam dipsati pitvo agne aśvānām gavām yas tanūnām /

Halfverse: c ripu stena steyakṛd dabhram etu ni ṣa hīyatām tanvā3 tanā ca //10// {9}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a paraḥ so astu tanvā3 tanā ca tisraḥ pṛthivīr adho astu viśvāḥ /

Halfverse: c prati śuṣyatu yaśo asya devā yo mā divā dipsati yaś ca naktam //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a suvijñānam cikituṣe janāya sac cāsac ca vacasī paspṛdhāte /

Halfverse: c tasyor yat satyam yatarad ṛjīyas tad it somo ‘vati hanty asat //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a na vā u somo vṛjinam hinoti na kṣatriyam mithuyā dhārayantam /

Halfverse: c hanti rakṣo hanty āsad vadantam ubhāv indrasya prasitau śayāte //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yadi vāham anṛtadevo asmi mogham vā devāṁ apyūhe agne /

Halfverse: c kim asmabhyam jātavedo hṛṇīṣe droghavācas te nirṛtham sacantām //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a adyā murīya yadi yātudhāno asmi yadi vāyus tatapa puruṣasya /

Halfverse: c adhā sa vīrair daśabhir vi yūyā yo mā mogham yātudhānety āha //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yo māyātum yātudhānety āha yo vā rakṣāḥ śicir asmīty āha /

Halfverse: c indras tam hantu mahatā vadhena viśvasya jantor adhamas padīṣṭa //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a pra yā jigāti khargaleva naktam apa druhus tanvam1 gūhamānā /

Halfverse: c vavram anantam ava sā padīṣṭia grāvāṇo ghnantu rakṣasa upabdaiḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a vi tiṣṭhadhvam maruto vikṣv ṅ3chata gṛbhāyata rakṣasaḥ sam pinaṣṭan /

Halfverse: c vayo ye bhūtvā patayanti naktabhir ye vā ripo dadhire deve adhvare //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a pra vartaya divo ‘śmānam indra somaśitam maghavant sam śiśādhi /

Halfverse: c prākto apākto adharād udakto ‘bhi jahi rakṣasaḥ parvatena //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a eta u tye patayanti śvayātava indram dipsanti dipsavo ‘dābhyam /

Halfverse: c śiśīte śakraḥ piśunebhyo vadham nunam sṛjad aśanim yātumadbhyaḥ //20// {10}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a indro yātūnām abhavat parāśaro havirmathīnām abhy ā3vivāsatām /

Halfverse: c abhīd u śakraḥ paraśur yathā vanam pātreva bhindant sata etu rakṣasaḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a ulūkayātum śuśulūkayātum jahi śvayātum uta kokayātum /

Halfverse: c suparṇayātum uta gṛdhrayātum dṛṣadeva pra mṛṇa rakṣa indra //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a mā no rakṣo abhi naḍ yātumāvad apochantu mithunā ye kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c pṛthivī naḥ pārthivāt pātv amhaso ‘ntarikṣam divyāt pātv asmān //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a indra jahi pumāmsam yātudhānam uta striyam māyayā śāśadānām /

Halfverse: c vigrīvāso mūradevā ṛdantu mā te dṛśant sūryam uccarantam //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a prati cakṣva vi cakṣvendraś ca soma jāgṛtam /

Halfverse: c rakṣobhyo vadham asyatam aśanim yātumadbhyaḥ //25// {11}

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam pratisaro maṇir vīro vīrāya badhyate /

Halfverse: c vīryavānt sapatnahā śūravīraḥ paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayam maṇiḥ sapatnahā suvīraḥ sahasvān vājī sahamāna ugraḥ /

Halfverse: c pratyak kṛtyā dūṣayann eti vīraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anenendro maṇinā vṛtram ahann anenāsurān parābhāvayan manīṣī /

Halfverse: c anenājayad dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime anenājayat pradiśaś catasraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ayam srāktyo maṇiḥ pratīvartaḥ pratisaraḥ /

Halfverse: c ojasvān vimṛdho vaśī so asmān pātu sarvataḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tad agnir āha tad u soma āha bṛhaspatiḥ savitā tad indraḥ /

Halfverse: c te me devāḥ purohitāḥ pratīcīḥ kṛtyāḥ pratisarair ajantu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a antar dadhe dyāvāpṛthivī utāhar uta sūryam /

Halfverse: c te me devāḥ purohitāḥ pratīcīḥ kṛtyāḥ pratisarair ajantu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ye srāktyam maṇim janā varmāṇi kṛṇvate /
