Halfverse: c sūrya iva divam āruhya vi kṛtyā bādhate vaśī //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a srāktyena maṇinā ṛṣiṇeva manīṣiṇā /

Halfverse: c ajaiṣam sarvāḥ pṛtanā vi mṛdho hanmi rakṣasaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yāḥ kṛtyā āṅgirasīr yāḥ kṛtyā āsurīr yāḥ /

Halfverse: c kṛtyāḥ svayamkṛtā yā u cānyebhir ābhṛtāḥ /

Halfverse: e ubhayīs tāḥ parā yantu parāvato navatim nāvyā3 ati //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a asmai maṇim varma badhnantu devā indro viṣṇuḥ savitā rudro agniḥ /

Halfverse: c prajāpatiḥ parameṣṭhī virāḍ vaiśvānara ṛṣayaś ca sarve //10// {12}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a uttamo asy oṣadhīnām anaḍvān jagatām iva vyāghraḥ śvapadām iva /

Halfverse: c yam aichāmāvidāma tam pratispāśanam antitam //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sa id vyāghro bhavaty atho simho atho vṛṣā /

Halfverse: c atho sapatnakarśano yo bibhartīmam maṇim //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a nainam ghnanty apsaraso na gandharvā na martyāḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvā diśo vi rājati yo bibhartīmam maṇim //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a kaśyapas tvām asṛjata kaśyapas tvā sam airayat /

Halfverse: c abibhas tvendro mānuṣe bibhrat samśreṣiṇe ‘jayat /

Halfverse: e maṇim sahasravīryam varma devā akṛṇvata //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yas tvā kṛtyābhir yas tvā dīkṣābhir yajñair yas tvā jighāmsati /

Halfverse: c pratyak tvam indra tam jahi vajreṇa śataparvaṇā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ayam id vai pratīvarta ojasvān samjayo maṇiḥ /

Halfverse: c prajām dhanam ca rakṣatu paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a asapatnam no adharād asapatnam na uttarāt /

Halfverse: c indrāsapatnam naḥ paścāj jyotiḥ śūra puras kṛdhi //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a varma me dyāvāpṛthivī varmāhar varma sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c varma ma indraś cāgniś ca varma dhātā dadhātu me //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a aindrāgnam varma bahulam yad ugram viśve devā nātividhyanti sarve /

Halfverse: c tan me tanvam trāyatām sarvato bṛhad āyuṣmām jaradaṣṭir yathāsāni //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ā mārukṣad devamaṇir mahyā ariṣṭatātaye /

Halfverse: c imam methim abhisamviśadhvam tanūpānam trivarūtham ojase //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a asminn indro ni dadhātu nṛmṇam imam devāso abhisamviśadhvam /

Halfverse: c dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāyāyuṣmān jaradaṣṭir yathāsat //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a svastidā viśām patir vṛtrahā vimṛdho vaśī /

Halfverse: c indro badhnātu te maṇim jigīvāṁ aparājitaḥ /

Halfverse: e somapā abhayaṅkaro vṛṣā /

Halfverse: g sa tvā rakṣatu sarvato divā naktam ca viśvataḥ //22// {13}

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yau te mātonmamārja jātāyāḥ pativedanau /

Halfverse: c durṇāmā tatra mā gṛdhad alimśa uta vatsapaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a palālānupalālau śarkum kokam malimlucam palījakam /

Halfverse: c āśreṣam vavrivāsasam ṛkṣagrīvam pramīlinam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mā sam vṛto mopa sṛpa ūrū māva sṛpo ‘ntarā /

Halfverse: c kṛṇomy asyai bheṣajam bajam durṇāmacātanam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a durṇāmā ca sunāmā cobhā samvṛtam ichataḥ /

Halfverse: c arāyān apa hanmaḥ sunāmā straiṇam ichatām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ keśy asura stambaja uta tuṇḍikaḥ /

Halfverse: c arāyān asyā muṣkābhyām bhamsaso ‘pa hanmasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a anujighram pramṛśantam kravyādam uta reriham /

Halfverse: c arāyām chvakiṣkiṇo bajaḥ piṅgo anīnaśat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yas tvā svapne nipadyate bhrātā bhūtvā piteva ca /

Halfverse: c bajas tānt sahatām itaḥ klībarūpāms tirītinaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yas tvā svapantīm tsarati yas tvā dipsati jāgratīm /

Halfverse: c chāyām iva pra tānt sūryaḥ parikrāmann anīnaśat //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yaḥ kṛṇoti mṛtavatsām avatokām imām striyam /

Halfverse: c tam oṣadhe tvam nāśayāsyāḥ kamalam añjivam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ye śālāḥ parinṛtyanti sāyam gardabhanādinaḥ /

Halfverse: c kusūlā ye ca kukṣilāḥ kakubhāḥ karumāḥ srimāḥ /

Halfverse: e tān oṣadhe tvam gandhena viṣūcīnān vi nāśaya //10// {14}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ye kukundhāḥ kukirabhāḥ kṛttīr dūrśāni bibhrati /

Halfverse: c klībā iva pranṛtyanto vane ye kurvate ghoṣam tān ito nāśayāmasi //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ye sūryam na titikṣanta ātapantam amum divaḥ /

Halfverse: c arāyān bastavāsino durgandhīml lohitāsyān makakān nāśayāmasi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ya ātmānam atimātram amsa ādhāya bibhrati /

Halfverse: c strīṇām śroṇipratodina indra rakṣāmsi nāśaya //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ye pūrve badhvo3 yanti haste śṛṅgāni bibhrataḥ /

Halfverse: c āpākesthāḥ prahāsina stambe ye kurvate jyotis tān ito nāśayāmasi //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yeṣām paścāt prapadāni puraḥ pārṣṇīḥ puro mukhā /

Halfverse: c khalajāḥ śakadhūmajā uruṇḍā ye ca maṭmaṭāḥ kumbhamuṣkā ayāśavaḥ /

Halfverse: e tān asyā brahmaṇas pate pratībodhena nāśaya //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a paryastākṣā apracaṅkaśā astraiṇāḥ santu paṇḍagāḥ /

Halfverse: c ava bheṣaja pādaya ya imām samvivṛtsaty apatiḥ svapatim striyam //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a uddharṣiṇam munikeśam jambhayantam marīmṛśam /

Halfverse: c upeṣantam udumbalam tuṇḍelam uta śāluḍam /

Halfverse: e padā pra vidhya pārṣṇyā sthālīm gaur iva spandanā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yas te garbham pratimṛśāj jātam vā mārayāti te /

Halfverse: c piṅgas tam ugradhanvā kṛṇotu hṛdayāvidham //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ye amno jatān mārayanti sūtikā anuśerate /

Halfverse: c strībhāgān piṅgo gandharvān vāto abhram ivājatu //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a parisṛṣṭam dharayatu yad dhitam māva pādi tat /

Halfverse: c garbham ta ugrau rakṣatām bheṣajau nīvibhāryaḥ //20// {15}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a pavīnasāt taṅgalvā3c chāyakād uta nagnakāt /

Halfverse: c prajāyai patye tvā piṅgaḥ pari pātu kimīdinaḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a dvyāsyāc caturakṣāt pañcapadād anaṅgureḥ /

Halfverse: c vṛntād abhi prasarpataḥ pari pāhi varīvṛtāt //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ya āmam māmsam adanti pauruṣeyam ca ye kraviḥ /

Halfverse: c garbhān khādanti keśavās tān ito nāśayāmasi //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ye sūryāt parisarpanti snuṣeva śvaśurād adhi /

Halfverse: c bajaś ca teṣām piṅgaś ca hṛdaye ‘dhi ni vidhyatām //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a piṅga rakṣa jāyamānam mā pumāmsam striyam kran /

Halfverse: c āṇḍādo garbhān mā dabhan bādhasvetaḥ kimīdinaḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a aprajāstvam mārtavatsam ād rodam agham āvayam /

Halfverse: c vṛkṣād iva srajam kṛtvāpriye prati muñca tat //26// {16}

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yā babhravo yāś ca śukrā rohiṇīr uta pṛśnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c asiknīḥ kṛṣṇā oṣadhīḥ sarvā achāvadāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a trāyantām imam purusam yakṣmād deveṣitād adhi /

Halfverse: c yāsām dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā samudro mūlam vīrudhām babhūva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āpo agram divyā oṣadhayaḥ /

Halfverse: c tās te yakṣmam enasya1m aṅgādaṅgād anīnaśan //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prastṛṇatī stambinīr ekaśuṅgāḥ pratanvatīr oṣadhīr ā vadāmi /

Halfverse: c amśumatīḥ kaṇḍinīr yā viśākhā hvayāmi te vīrudho vaiśvadevīr ugrāḥ puruṣajīvanīḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yad vaḥ sahaḥ sahamānā vīryam1 yac ca vo balam /

Halfverse: c tenemam asmād yakṣmāt puruṣam muñcatauṣadhīr atho kṛṇomi bheṣajam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a jīvalām naghāriṣām jīvantīm oṣadhīm aham /

Halfverse: c arundhatīm unnayantīm puṣpām madhumatīm iha huve ‘smā ariṣṭatātaye //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ihā yantu pracetaso medinīr vacaso mama /

Halfverse: c yathemam pārayāmasi puruṣam duritād adhi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a agner ghāso apām garbho yā rohanti punarṇavāḥ /

Halfverse: c dhruvāḥ sahasranāmnīr bheṣajīḥ santv ābhṛtāḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a avakolbā udakātmāna oṣadhayaḥ /

Halfverse: c vyṛṣantu duritam tīkṣṇaśṛṅgyaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a unmuñcantīr vivaruṇā ugrā yā viṣadūṣanīḥ /

Halfverse: c atho balāsanāśanīḥ kṛtyādūṣaṇīś ca yās tā ihā yantv oṣadhīh //10// {17}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a apakrītāḥ sahīyasīr vīrudho yā abhiṣṭutāḥ /

Halfverse: c trāyantām asmin grāme gām aśvam puruṣam paśum //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a madhuman mūlam madhumad agram āsām madhuman madhyam vīrudhām babhūva /

Halfverse: c madhumat parṇam madhumat puṣpam āsām madhoḥ sambhaktā amṛtasya bhakṣo ghṛtam annam duhratām gopurogavam //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yāvatīḥ kiyatīś cemāḥ pṛthivyām adhy oṣadhīḥ /

Halfverse: c tā mā sahasraparṇyo mṛtyor muñcantv amhasaḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a vaiyāghro maṇir vīrudhām trāyamāno ‘bhiśastipāh /

Halfverse: c amīvāḥ sarvā rakṣāmsy apa hantv adhi dūram asmat //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a simhasyeva stanathoḥ sam vijante ‘gner iva vijante ābhṛtābhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c gavām yakṣmaḥ puruṣāṇām vīrudbhir atinutto nāvyā etu srotyāḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a mumucānā oṣadhayo ‘gner vaiśvānarād adhi /

Halfverse: c bhūmim samtavatīr ita yāsām rājā vanaspatiḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yā rohanty āṅgirasīḥ parvateṣu sameṣu ca /

Halfverse: c tā naḥ payasvatīḥ śivā oṣadhīḥ santu śam hṛde //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yāś cāham veda vīrudho yāś ca paśyāmi cakṣuṣā /

Halfverse: c ajñātā jānīmaś ca yā yāsu vidma ca sambhṛtam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a sarvāḥ samagrā oṣadhīr bodhantu vacaso mama /

Halfverse: c yathemam pārayāmasi puruṣam duritād adhi //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a aśvattho darbho vīrudhām somo rājāmṛtam haviḥ /

Halfverse: c vrīhir yavaś ca bheṣajau divasi putrāv amartyau //20// {18}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a uj jihīdhve stanayaty abhikrandaty oṣadhīḥ /

Halfverse: c yadā vaḥ pṛśnimātaraḥ parjanyo retasāvati //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a tasyāmṛtasyemam balam puruṣam payayāmasi /

Halfverse: c atho kṛṇomi bheṣajam yathāsac chatahāyanaḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a varāho veda vīrudham nakulo veda bheṣajīm /

Halfverse: c sarpā gandharvā yā vidus tā asmā avase huve //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yāḥ suparṇā āṅgirasīr divyā yā raghato viduḥ /

Halfverse: c vayāmsi hamsā yā vidur yās ca sarve patatriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: e mṛgā yā vidur oṣadhīs tā asmā avase huve //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yāvatīnām oṣadhīnām gāvaḥ prāśnanty aghnyā yavatīnām ajāvayaḥ /

Halfverse: c tāvatīs tubhyam oṣadhīḥ śarma yachantv ābhṛtāḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a yāvatīṣu manuṣyā bheṣajam bhiṣajo viduḥ /

Halfverse: c tāvatīr viśvabheṣajīr ā bharāmi tvām abhi //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a puṣpavatīh prasūmatīḥ phalinīr aphalā uta /

Halfverse: c sammātara iva duhrām asmā ariṣṭatātaye //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a ut tvāhārṣam pañcaśalād atho daśaśalād uta /

Halfverse: c atho yamasya paḍvīśād viśvasmād devakilbiṣāt //28// {19}

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indro manthatu manthitā śakraḥ śūraḥ puramdaraḥ /

Halfverse: c yathā hanāma senā amitrāṇām sahasraśaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pūtirajjur upadhmānī pūtim senām kṛṇotv amūm /

Halfverse: c dhūmam agnim parādṛśyā ‘mitrā hṛtsv ā dadhatām bhayam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a amūn aśvattha niḥ śṛṇīhi khādāmūn khadirājiram /

Halfverse: c tājadbhaṅga iva bhajantām hantv enān vadhako vadhaiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a paruṣān amūn paruṣāhvaḥ kṛṇotu hantv enān vadhako vadhaiḥ /

Halfverse: c kṣipram śara iva bhajantām bṛhajjālena samditāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a antarikṣam jālam āsīj jāladaṇḍā diśo mahīḥ /

Halfverse: c tenābhidhāya dasyūnām śakraḥ senām apāvapat //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a bṛhad dhi jālam bṛhataḥ śakrasya vājinīvataḥ /

Halfverse: c tena śatrūn abhi sarvān ny ḥbja yathā na mucyātai katamaś canaiṣām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a bṛhat te jālam bṛhata indra śūra sahasrārghasya śatavīryasya /

Halfverse: c tena śatam sahasram ayutam nyarbudam jaghāna śakro dasyūnām abhidhāya senayā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ayam loko jālam āsīc chakrasya mahato mahān /

Halfverse: c tenāham indrajālenāmūms tamasābhi dadhāmi sarvān //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a sedir ugrā vyṛddhir ārtiś cānapavācanā /

Halfverse: c śramas tandrīś ca mohaś ca tair amūn abhi dadhāmi sarvān //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a mṛtyave ‘mūn pra yachāmi mṛtyupāśair amī sitāḥ /

Halfverse: c mṛtyor ye aghalā dūtās tebhya enān prati nayāmi baddhvā //10// {20}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a nayatāmūn mṛtyudūtā yamadūtā apombhata /

Halfverse: c paraḥsahasrā hanyantām tṛṇedv enān matyam bhavasya //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sādhyā ekam jāladaṇḍam udyatya yanty ojasā /

Halfverse: c rudrā ekam vasava ekam ādityair eka udyataḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a viśve devāḥ upariṣṭād ubjanto yantv ojasā /

Halfverse: c madhyena ghnanto yantu senām aṅgiraso mahīm //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a vanaspatīn vānaspatyān oṣadhīr uta vīrudhaḥ /

Halfverse: c dvipāc catuṣpād iṣṇāmi yathā senām amūm hanan //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a gandharvāpsarasaḥ sarpān devān puṇyajanān pitṝn /

Halfverse: c dṛṣṭān adṛṣṭān iṣṇāmi yathā senām amūm hanan //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ima uptā mṛtyupāśā yān ākramya na mucyase /

Halfverse: c amuṣyā hantu senāyā idam kūtam sahasraśaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a gharmaḥ samiddho agnināyam homaḥ sahasrahaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhavaś ca pṛśnibāhuś ca śarva senām amūm hatam //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a mṛtyor āṣam ā padyantām kṣudham sedim vadham bhayam /

Halfverse: c indraś cākṣujālābhyām śarva senām amūm hatam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a parājitāḥ pra trasatāmitrā nuttā dhāvata brahmaṇā /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatipranuttānām māmīṣām moci kaś cana //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ava padyantām eṣām āyudhāni mā śakan pratidhām iṣum /

Halfverse: c athaiṣām bahu bibhyatām iṣavaḥ ghnantu marmaṇi //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a sam krośatām enān dyāvāpṛthivī sam antarikṣam saha devatābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c mā jñātāram mā pratiṣṭhām vidanta mitho vighnānā upa yantu mṛtyum //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a diśaś catasro ‘śvataryo devarathasya purodāśāḥ śaphā antarikṣam uddhiḥ /

Halfverse: c dyāvāpṛthivī pakṣasī ṛtavo ‘bhīśavo ‘ntardeśāḥ kimkarā vāk parirathyam //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a samvatsaro rathaḥ parivatsaro rathopastho virāḍ īṣāgnī rathamukham /

Halfverse: c indraḥ savyaṣṭhāś candramāḥ sārathiḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ito jayeto vi jaya sam jaya jaya svāhā /

Halfverse: c ime jayantu parāmī jayantām svāhaibhyo durāhāmībhyaḥ /

Halfverse: e nīlalohitenāmūn abhyavatanomi //24// {21}

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kutas tau jātau katamaḥ so ardhaḥ kasmāl lokāt katamasyāḥ pṛthivyāḥ /

Halfverse: c vatsau virājaḥ salilād ud aitām tau tvā pṛchāmi katareṇa dugdhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo akrandayat salilam mahitvā yonim kṛtvā tribhujam śayānaḥ /

Halfverse: c vatsaḥ kāmadugho virājaḥ sa guhā cakre tanvaḥ parācaiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yāni trīṇi bṛhanti yeṣām caturtham viyunakti vācam /

Halfverse: c brahmainad vidyāt tapasā vipaścid yasminn ekam yujyate yasminn ekam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a bṛhataḥ pari sāmāni ṣaṣṭhāt pañcādhi nirmitā /

Halfverse: c bṛhad bṛhatyā nirmitam kuto ‘dhi bṛhatī mitā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bṛhatī pari mātrāyā mātur mātrādhi nirmitā /

Halfverse: c māyā ha jajñe māyāyā māyāyā mātalī pari //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a vaiśvānarasya pratimopari dyaur yāvad rodasī vibabādhe agniḥ /

Halfverse: c tataḥ ṣaṣṭhād āmuto yanti stomā ud ito yanty abhi ṣaṣṭham ahnaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ṣaṭ tvā pṛchāma ṛṣayaḥ kaśyapeme tvam hi yuktam yuyukṣe yogyam ca /

Halfverse: c virājam āhur brahmaṇaḥ pitaram tām no vi dhehi yatidhā sakhibhyaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yām pracyutām anu yajñāh pracyavanta upatiṣṭhanta upatiṣṭhamānām /

Halfverse: c yasyā vrate prasave yakṣam ejati sā virāṭ ṛṣayaḥ parame vyoman //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a aprāṇaiti prāṇena prāṇatīnām virāṭ svarājam abhy ṝti paścāt /

Halfverse: c viśvam mṛśantīm abhirūpām virājam paśyanti tve na tve paśyanty enām //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ko virājo mithunatvam pra veda ka ṛtūn ka u kalpam asyāḥ /

Halfverse: c kramān ko asyāḥ katidhā vidugdhān ko asyā dhāma katidhā vyḥṣṭīḥ //10// {22}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a iyam eva sā yā prathamā vyauchad āsv itarāsu carati praviṣṭā /

Halfverse: c mahānto asyām mahimāno antar vadhūr jigāya navagaj janitrī //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a chandaḥpakṣe uṣasā pepiśāne samānam yonim anu sam careme /

Halfverse: c sūryapatnī sam carataḥ prajānatī ketumatī ajare bhūriretasā //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ṛtasya panthām anu tisra āgus trayo gharmā anu reta āguḥ /

Halfverse: c prajām ekā jinvaty ūrjam ekā rāṣṭram ekā rakṣati devayūnām //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a agnīṣomāv adadhur yā turīyāsīd yajñasya pakṣāv ṛṣayaḥ kalpayantaḥ /

Halfverse: c gāyatrīm triṣṭubham jagatīm anuṣṭubham bṛhadarkīm yajamānāya svar ābharantīm //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a pañca vyḥṣṭīr anu pañca dohā gām pañcanāmnīm ṛtavo ‘nu pañca /

Halfverse: c pañca diśaḥ pañcadaśena kḷptās tā ekamūrdhnīr abhi lokam ekam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ṣaṭ jātā bhūtā prathamajā ṛtasya ṣaṭ u sāmāni ṣaṭaham vahanti /

Halfverse: c ṣaṭyogam sīram anu sāmasāma ṣaṭ āhur dyāvāpṛthivīḥ ṣaṭ urvīḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ṣaḍ āhuḥ śītān ṣaḍ u māsa uṣṇān ṛtum no brūta yatamo ‘tiriktaḥ /

Halfverse: c sapta suparṇāḥ kavayo ni ṣeduḥ sapta chandāmsy anu sapta dīkṣāḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a sapta homāḥ samidho ha sapta madhūni sapta ṛtavo ha sapta /

Halfverse: c saptājyāni pari bhūtam āyan tāḥ saptagṛdhrā iti śuśrumā vayam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a sapta chandāmsi caturuttarāṇy anyo anyasminn adhy ārpitāni /

Halfverse: c katham stomāḥ prati tiṣṭhanti teṣu tāni stomeṣu katham ārpitāni //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a katham gāyatrī trivṛtam vy āpa katham triṣṭup pañcadaśena kalpate /

Halfverse: c trayastrimśena jagatī katham anuṣṭup katham ekavimśaḥ //20// {23}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a aṣṭa jātā bhūtā prathamajā ṛtasyāṣṭendra ṛtvijo daivyā ye /

Halfverse: c aṣṭayonir aditir aṣṭaputrāstamīm rātrim abhi havyam eti //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a ittham śreyo manyamānedam āgamam yuṣmākam sakhye aham asmi śevā /

Halfverse: c samānajanmā kratur asti vaḥ śivaḥ sa vaḥ sarvāḥ sam carati prajānan //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a aṣṭendrasya ṣaḍ yamasya ṛṣīṇām sapta saptadhā /

Halfverse: c apo manuṣyā3n oṣadhīs tāṁ u pañcānu secire //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a kevalīndrāya duduhe hi gṛṣṭir vaśam pīyūṣam prathamam duhānā /

Halfverse: c athātarpayac caturaś caturdhā devān manuṣyā3ṁ asurān uta ṛṣīn //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a ko nu gauḥ ka ekaṛṣiḥ kim u dhāma kā āśiṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c yakṣam pṛthivyām ekavṛd ekartuḥ katamo nu saḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a eko gaur eka ekaṛṣir ekam dhāmaikadhāśiṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c yakṣam pṛthivyām ekavṛd ekartur nāti ricyate //26// {24}

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1 {1}

Halfverse: a virāḍ vā idam agra āsīt tasyā jātāyāḥ sarvam abibhed iyam evedam bhaviṣyatīti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā gārhapatye ny akrāmat /

Halfverse: c gṛhamedhī gṛhapatir bhavati ya evam veda //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sāhavanīye ny akrāmat /

Halfverse: c yanty asya devā devahūtim priyo devānām bhavati ya evam veda //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā dakṣiṇāgnau ny akrāmat /

Halfverse: c yajñarto dakṣiṇīyo vāsateyo bhavati ya evam veda //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā sabhāyām ny akrāmat /

Halfverse: c yanty asya sabhām sabhyo bhavati ya evam veda //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā samitau ny akrāmat /

Halfverse: c yanty asya samitim sāmityo bhavati ya evam veda //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sāmantraṇe ny akrāmat /

Halfverse: c yanty asyāmantraṇam āmantraṇīyo bhavati ya evam veda //7// {25}

Verse: 8 {2}

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sāntarikṣe caturdhā vikrāntātiṣṭhat //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tām devamanuṣyā abruvann iyam eva tad veda yad ubhaya upajīvememām upa hvayāmahā iti //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tām upāhvayanta //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ūrja ehi svadha ehi sūnṛta ehīrāvaty ehīti //10//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a tasyā indro vatsa āsīd gāyatry abhidhāny abhram ūdhaḥ //11//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a bṛhac ca rathamtaram ca dvau stanāv āstām yajñāyajñiyam ca vāmadevyam ca dvau //12//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a oṣadhīr eva rathamtareṇa devā aduhran vyaco bṛhat //13//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a apo vāmadevyena yajñam yajñāyajñiyena //14//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a oṣadhīr evāsmai rathamtaram duhe vyaco bṛhat //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a apo vāmadevyam yajñam yajñāyajñiyam ya veda //17// {26}

Verse: 18 {3}

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā vanaspatīn āgachat tām vanaspatayo ‘ghnata sā samvatsare sam abhavat /

Halfverse: c tasmād vanaspatīnām samvatsare vṛkṇam api rohati vṛścate ‘syāpriyo bhrātṛvyo ya evam veda //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā pitṝn āgachat tām pitaro ‘ghnata sā māsi sam abhavat /

Halfverse: c tasmāt pitṛbhyo māsy upamāsyam dadati pra pitṛyāṇam panthām jānāti ya evam veda //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā devān āgachat tām devā aghnata sārdhamāse sam abhavat /

Halfverse: c tasmād devebhyo ‘rdhamāse vaṣaṭ kurvanti pra devayānam panthām jānāti ya evam veda //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā manuṣyā3n āgachat tām manuṣyā aghnata sā sadyaḥ sam abhavat /

Halfverse: c tasmān manuṣyṝbhya ubhayadyur upa haranty upāsya gṛhe haranti ya evam veda //21// {27}

Verse: 22 {4}

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sāsurān āgachat tām asurā upāhvayanta māya ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyā virocanaḥ prāhrādir vatsa āsīd ayaspātram pātram /

Halfverse: e tām dvimūrdhārtvyo ‘dhok tām māyām evādhok /

Halfverse: g tām māyām asurā upa jīvanty upajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā pitṝn āgachat tām pitara upāhvayanta svadha ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyā yamo rājā vatsa āsīd rajatapātram pātram /

Halfverse: e tām antako mārtyavo ‘dhok tām svadhām evādhok /

Halfverse: g tām svadhām pitara upa jīvanty upajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā manuṣyā3n āgachat tām manuṣyā3 upāhvayanterāvaty ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyā manur vaivasvato vatsa āsīt pṛthivī pātram /

Halfverse: e tām pṛthī vainyo ‘dhok tām kṛṣim ca sasyam cādhok /

Halfverse: g te kṛṣim ca sasyam ca manuṣyā3 upa jīvanti kṛṣṭarādhir upajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā saptaṛṣīn āgachat tām saptaṛṣaya upāhvayanta brahmaṇvaty ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyāḥ somo rājā vatsa āsīc chandaḥ pātram /

Halfverse: e tām bṛhaspatir āṅgiraso ‘dhok tām brahma ca tapaś cādhok /

Halfverse: g tad brahma ca tapaś ca saptaṛṣaya upa jīvanti brahmavarcasy ḥpajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //25// {28}

Verse: 26 {5}

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā devān āgachat tām devā upāhvayantorja ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyā indro vatsa āsīc camasaḥ pātram /

Halfverse: e tām devaḥ savitādhok tām ūrjām evādhok /

Halfverse: g tām ūrjām devā upa jīvanty upajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā gandharvāpsarasa āgachat tām gandharvāpsarasa upāhvayanta puṇyagandha ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyāś citrarathaḥ sauryavarcaso vatsa āsīt puṣkaraparṇam pātram /

Halfverse: e tām vasuruciḥ sauryavarcaso ‘dhok tām puṇyam eva gandham adhok /

Halfverse: g tām puṇyam gandham gandharvāpsarasa upa jīvanti puṇyagandhir upajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat setarajanān āgachat tām itarajanā upāhvayanta tirodha ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyāḥ kubero vaiśravaṇo vatsa āsīd āmapātram pātram /

Halfverse: e tām rajatanābhiḥ kaberako ‘dhok tām tirodhām evādhok /

Halfverse: g tām tirodhām itarajanā upa jīvanti tiro dhatte sarvam pāpmānam upajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a sod akrāmat sā sarpān āgachat tām sarpā upāhvayanta viṣavaty ehīti /

Halfverse: c tasyās takṣako vaiśaleyo vatsa āsīd alābupātram pātram /

Halfverse: e tām dhṛtarāṣṭra airāvato ‘dhok tām viṣam evādhok /

Halfverse: g tad viṣam sarpā upa jīvanty upajīvanīyo bhavati ya evam veda //29// {29}

Verse: 30 {6}

Halfverse: a tad yasmā evam viduṣe ‘lābunābhiṣiñcet pratyāhanyāt //30//

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a na ca pratyāhanyān manasā tvā pratyāhanmīti pratyāhanyāt //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a yat pratyāhanti viṣam eva tat pratyāhanti //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a viṣam evāsyāpriyam bhrātṛvyam anuviṣicyate ya evam veda //33// {30}

## Book: 9

9

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a divas pṛthivyā antarikṣāt samudrād agner vātān madhukaśā hi jajñe /

Halfverse: c tām cāyitvāmṛtam vasānām hṛdbhiḥ prajāḥ prati nandanti sarvāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mahat payo viśvarūpam asyāḥ samudrasya tvota reta āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c yata aiti madhukaśā rarāṇā tat prāṇas tad amṛtam niviṣṭam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a paśyanty asyāś caritam pṛthivyām pṛthaṅ naro bahudhā mīmāmsamānāḥ /

Halfverse: c agner vātān madhukaśā hi jajñe marutām ugrā naptiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a mātādityānām duhitā vasūnām prāṇaḥ prajānām amṛtasya nābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c hiraṇyavarṇā madhukaśā ghṛtācī mahān bhargaś carati martyeṣu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a madhoḥ kaśām ajanayanta devās tasyā garbho abhavad viśvarūpaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam jātam taruṇam piparti mātā sa jāto viśvā bhuvanā vi caṣṭe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kas tam pra veda ka u tam ciketa yo asyā hṛdaḥ kalaśaḥ somadhāno akṣitaḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmā sumedhāḥ so asmin madeta //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sa tau pra veda sa u tau ciketa yāv asyāḥ stanau sahasradhārāv akṣitau /

Halfverse: c ūrjam duhāte anapasphurantau //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a hiṅkarikratī bṛhatī vayodhā uccairghoṣābhyeti yā vratam /

Halfverse: c trīn gharmān abhi vāvaśānā mimāti māyum payate payobhiḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yām āpīnām upasīdanty āpaḥ śākvarā vṛṣabhā ye svarājaḥ /

Halfverse: c te varṣanti te varṣayanti tadvide kāmam ūrjam āpaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a stanayitnus te vāk prajāpate vṛṣā śuṣmam kṣipasi bhūmyām adhi /

Halfverse: c agner vātān madhukaśā hi jajñe marutām ugrā naptiḥ //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yathā somaḥ prātaḥsavane aśvinor bhavati priyaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me aśvinā varca ātmani dhriyatām //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yathā somo dvitīye savana indrāgnyor bhavati priyaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā ma indrāgnī varca ātmani dhriyatām //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yathā somas tṛtīye savana ṛbhūṇām bhavati priyaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā ma ṛbhavo varca ātmani dhriyatām //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a madhu janiṣīya madhu vamsiṣīya /

Halfverse: c payasvān agna āgamam tam mā sam sṛja varcasā //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a sam māgne varcasā sṛja sam prajayā sam āyuṣā /

Halfverse: c vidyur me asya devā indro vidyāt saha ṛṣibhiḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yathā madhu madhukṛtaḥ sambharanti madhāv adhi /

Halfverse: c evā me aśvinā varca ātmani dhriyatām //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yathā makṣāḥ idam madhu nyañjanti madhāv adhi /

Halfverse: c evā me aśvinā varcas tejo balam ojaś ca dhriyatām //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yad giriṣu parvateṣu goṣv aśveṣu yan madhu /

Halfverse: c surāyām sicyamānāyām yat tatra madhu tan mayi //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a aśvinā sāragheṇa mā madhunāṅktam śubhas patī /

Halfverse: c yathā varcasvatīm vācam āvadāni janāṁ anu //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a stanayitnus te vāk prajāpate vṛṣā śuṣmam kṣipasi bhūmyām divi /

Halfverse: c tām paśava upa jīvanti sarve teno seṣam ūrjam piparti //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a pṛthivī daṇḍo ‘ntarikṣam garbho dyauḥ kaśā vidyut prakaśo hiraṇyayo binduḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yo vai kaśāyāḥ sapta madhūni veda madhumān bhavati /

Halfverse: c brāhmaṇaś ca rājā ca dhenuś cānaḍvāmś ca vrīhiś ca yavaś ca madhu saptamam //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a madhumān bhavati madhumad asyāhāryam bhavati /

Halfverse: c madhumato lokān jayati ya evam veda //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yad vīdhre stanayati prajāpatir eva tat prajābhyaḥ prādur bhavati /

Halfverse: c tasmāt prācīnopavītas tiṣṭhe prajāpate ‘nu mā budhyasveti /

Halfverse: e anv enam prajā anu prajāpatir budhyate ya evam veda //24// {2}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sapatnahanam ṛṣabham ghṛtena kāmam śikṣāmi haviṣājyena /

Halfverse: c nīcaiḥ sapatnān mama padaya tvam abhiṣṭuto mahatā vīryṝṇa //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yan me manaso na priyam cakṣuṣo yan me babhasti nābhinandati /

Halfverse: c tad duṣvapnyam prati muñcāmi sapatne kāmam stutvod aham bhideyam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a duṣvapnyam kāma duritam ca kamāprajastām asvagatām avartim /

Halfverse: c ugra īśānaḥ prati muñca tasmin yo asmabhyam amhūraṇā cikitsāt //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a nudasva kāma pra ṇudasva kāmāvartim yantu mama ye sapatnāḥ /

Halfverse: c teṣām nuttānām adhamā tamāmsy agne vāstūni nir daha tvam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sā te kāma duhitā dhenur ucyate yām āhur vācam kavayo virājam /

Halfverse: c tayā sapatnān pari vṛṅgdhi ye mama pary enān prāṇaḥ paśavo jīvanam vṛṇaktu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kāmasyendrasya varuṇasya rājño viṣṇor balena savituḥ savena /

Halfverse: c agner hotreṇa pra ṇude sapatnām chambīva nāvam udakeṣu dhīraḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a adhyakṣo vājī mama kāma ugraḥ kṛṇotu mahyam asapatnam eva /

Halfverse: c viśve devā mama nātham bhavantu sarve devā havam ā yantu ma imam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a idam ājyam ghṛtavaj juṣāṇāḥ kāmajyeṣṭhā iha mādayadhvam /

Halfverse: c kṛṇvanto mahyam asapatnam eva //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a indrāgnī kāma saratham hi bhūtvā nīcaiḥ sapatnān mama pādayāthaḥ /

Halfverse: c teṣām pannānām adhamā tamāmsy agne vāstūny anunirdaha tvam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a jahi tvam kāma mama ye sapatnā andhā tamāmsy ava pādayainān /

Halfverse: c nirindriyā arasāḥ santu sarve mā te jīviṣuḥ katamac canāhaḥ //10// {3}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a avadhīt kāmo mama ye sapatnā urum lokam akaran mahyam edhatum /

Halfverse: c mahyam namantām pradiśaś catasro mahyam ṣaḍ urvīr ghṛtam ā vahantu //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a te ‘dharāñcaḥ pra plavantām chinnā naur iva bandhanāt /

Halfverse: c na sāyakapraṇuttānām punar asti nivartanam //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a agnir yava indro yavaḥ somo yavaḥ /

Halfverse: c yavayāvāno devā yavayantv enam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a asarvavīraś caratu praṇutto dveṣyo mitrānām parivargya1ḥ svānām /

Halfverse: c uta pṛthivyām ava syanti vidyuta ugro vo devaḥ pra mṛṇat sapatnān //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a cyutā ceyam bṛhaty acyutā ca vidyud bibharti stanayitnūmś ca sarvān /

Halfverse: c udyann ādityo draviṇena tejasā nīcaiḥ sapatnān nudatām me sahasvān //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yat te kāma śarma trivarūtham udbhu brahma varma vitatam anativyādhyam kṛtam /

Halfverse: c tena sapatnān pari vṛṅgdhi ye mama pary enān prāṇaḥ paśāvo jīvanam vṛṇaktu //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yena devā asurān prāṇudanta yenendro dasyūn adhamam tamo nināya /

Halfverse: c tena tvam kāma mama ye sapatnās tān asmāl lokāt pra ṇudasva dūram //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yathā devā asurān prāṇudanta yathendro dasyūn adhamam tamo babādhe /

Halfverse: c tathā tvam kāma mama ye sapatnās tān asmāl lokāt pra ṇudasva dūram //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a kāmo jajñe prathamo nainam devā āpuḥ pitaro na martyāḥ /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāms tasmai te kāma nama it kṛnomi //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yāvatī dyāvāpṛthivī varimṇā yāvad āpaḥ siṣyadur yāvad agniḥ /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāms tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi //20// {4}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yāvatīr diśaḥ pradiśo viṣūcīr yāvatīr āśā abhicakṣaṇā divaḥ /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāms tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yāvatīr bhṛṅgā jatvaḥ kurūravo yāvatīr vaghā vṛkṣasarpyo babhūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāms tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a jyāyān nimiṣato ‘si tiṣṭhato jyāyānt samudrād asi kāma manyo /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāms tasmai te kāma nama it kṛnomi //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a na vai vātaś cana kāmam āpnoti nāgniḥ sūryo nota candramāḥ /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāms tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yās te śivās tanvaḥ kāma bhadrā yābhiḥ satyam bhavati yad vṛṇiṣe /

Halfverse: c tābhiṣ ṭvam asmāṁ abhisamviśasvānyatra pāpīr apa veśayā dhiyaḥ //25// {5}

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a upamitām pratimitām atho parimitām uta /

Halfverse: c śālāyā viśvavārāyā naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yat te naddham viśvavāre pāśo granthiś ca yaḥ kṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir ivāham balam vācā vi sramsayāmi tat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ā yayāma sam babarha granthīmś cakāra te dṛḍhān /

Halfverse: c parūmṣi vidvām chastevendreṇa vi cṛtāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vamśānām te nahanānām prāṇāhasya tṛṇasya ca /

Halfverse: c pakṣāṇām viśvavāre te naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a samdamśānām paladānām pariṣvañjalyasya ca /

Halfverse: c idam mānasya patnyā naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yāni te ‘ntaḥ śikyāny ābedhū raṇyāya kam /

Halfverse: c pra te tāni cṛtāmasi śivā mānasya patni na uddhitā tanvṝ bhava //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a havirdhānam agniśālam patnīnām sadanam sadaḥ /

Halfverse: c sado devānām asi devi śāle //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a akṣum opaśam vitatam sahasrākṣam viṣūvati /

Halfverse: c avanaddham abhihitam brahmaṇā vi cṛtāmasi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yas tvā śāle pratigṛhṇāti yena cāsi mitā tvam /

Halfverse: c ubhau mānasya patni tau jīvatām jaradaṣṭī //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a amutrainam ā gachatād dṛḍhā naddhā pariṣkṛtā /

Halfverse: c yasyās te vicṛtāmasy aṅgamaṅgam paruṣparuḥ //10// {6}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yas tvā śāle nimimāya samjabhāra vanaspatīn /

Halfverse: c prajāyai cakre tvā śāle parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a namas tasmai namo dātre śālāpataye ca kṛṇmaḥ /

Halfverse: c namo ‘gnaye pracarate puruṣāya ca te namaḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a gobhyo aśvebhyo namo yac chālāyām vijāyate /

Halfverse: c vijāvati prajāvati vi te pāśāmś cṛtāmasi //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a agnim antaś chādayasi puruṣān paśubhiḥ saha /

Halfverse: c vijāvati prajāvati vi te pāśāmś cṛtāmasi //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a antarā dyām ca pṛthivīm ca yad vyacas tena śālām prati gṛhṇāmi ta imām /

Halfverse: c yad antarikṣam rajaso vimānam tat kṛṇve ‘ham udaram śevadhibhyaḥ /

Halfverse: e tena śālām prati gṛhṇāmi tasmai //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ūrjasvatī payasvatī pṛthivyām nimitā mitā /

Halfverse: c viśvānnam bibhratī śāle mā himsīḥ pratigṛhṇataḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a tṛṇair āvṛtā paladān vasānā rātrīva śālā jagato niveśanī /

Halfverse: c mitā pṛthivyām tiṣṭhasi hastinīva padvatī //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a itasya te vi cṛtāmy apinaddham aporṇuvan /

Halfverse: c varuṇena samubjitām mitraḥ prātar vy ḥbjatu //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a brahmaṇā śālām nimitām kavibhir nimitām mitām /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī rakṣatām śālām amṛtau somyam sadah //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a kulāye ‘dhi kulāyam kośe kośaḥ samubjitaḥ /

Halfverse: c tatra marto vi jāyate yasmād viśvam prajāyate //20// {7}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yā dvipakṣā catuṣpakṣā ṣaṭpakṣā yā nimīyate /

Halfverse: c aṣṭāpakṣām daśapakṣām śālām mānasya patnīm agnir garbha ivā śaye //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a pratīcīm tvā pratīcīnaḥ śāle praimy ahimsatīm /

Halfverse: c agnir hy antar āpaś ca ṛtasya prathamā dvāḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a imā āpaḥ pra bharāmy ayakṣmā yakṣmanāśanīḥ /

Halfverse: c gṛhān upa pra sīdāmy amṛtena sahāgninā //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a mā naḥ pāśam prati muco gurur bhāro laghur bhava /

Halfverse: c vadhūm iva tvā śāle yatrakāmam bharāmasi //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a prācyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyṝbhyaḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyṝbhyaḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a pratīcyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyṝbhyaḥ //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a udīcyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyṝbhyaḥ //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a dhruvāyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyṝbhyaḥ //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a ūrdhvāyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyṝbhyaḥ //30//

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a diśodiśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyṝbhyaḥ //31// {8}

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sāhasras tveṣa ṛṣabhaḥ payasvān viśvā rūpāṇi vakṣaṇāsu bibhrat /

Halfverse: c bhadram dātre yajamānāya śīkṣan bārhaspatya usriyas tantum ātān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a apām yo agne pratimā babhūva prabhūḥ sarvasmai pṛthivīva devī /

Halfverse: c pitā vatsānām patir aghnyānām sāhasre poṣe api naḥ kṛṇotu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pumān antarvānt sthaviraḥ payasvān vasoḥ kabandham ṛṣabho bibharti /

Halfverse: c tam indrāya pathibhir devayānair hutam agnir vahatu jātavedāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pitā vatsānām patir aghnyānām atho pitā mahatām gargarāṇām /

Halfverse: c vatso jarāyu pratidhuk pīyūṣa āmikṣā ghṛtam tad v asya retaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a devānām bhāga upanāha eṣo ‘pām rasa oṣadhīnām ghṛtasya /

Halfverse: c somasya bhakṣam avṛṇīta śakro bṛhann adrir abhavad yac charīram //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a somena pūrṇam kalaśam bibharṣi tvastā rupāṇām janitā paśūnām /

Halfverse: c śivās te santu prajanva iha yā imā ny asmabhyam svadhite yacha yā amūḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ājam bibharti ghṛtam asya retaḥ sāhasraḥ poṣas tam u yajñam āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c indrasya rūpam ṛṣabho vasānaḥ so asmān devāḥ śiva aitu dattaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a indrasyaujo varuṇasya bāhū aśvinor amsau marutām iyam kakut /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatim sambhṛtam etam āhur ye dhīrāsaḥ kavayo ye manīṣiṇaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a daivīr viśaḥ payasvān ā tanoṣi tvām indram tvām sarasvantam āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c sahasram sa ekamukhā dadāti yo brāhmaṇa ṛṣabham ājuhoti //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatiḥ savitā te vayo dadhau tvaṣṭur vāyoḥ pary ātmā ta ābhṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c antarikṣe manasā tvā juhomi barhiṣ ṭe dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām //10// {9}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ya indra iva deveṣu goṣv eti vivāvadat /

Halfverse: c tasya ṛṣabhasyāṅgāni brahmā sam stautu bhadrayā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a pārśve āstām anumatyā bhagasyāstām anūvṛjau /

Halfverse: c aṣṭhīvantāv abravīn mitro mamaitau kevalāv iti //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a bhasad āsīd ādityānām śroṇī āstām bṛhaspateḥ /

Halfverse: c pucham vātasya devasya tena dhūnoty oṣadhīḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a gudā āsant sinīvālyāḥ sūryāyās tvacam abruvan /

Halfverse: c utthātur abruvan pada ṛṣabham yad akalpayan //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a kroḍa āsīj jāmiśamsasya somasya klaśo dhṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c devāḥ samgatya yat sarva ṛṣabham vyakalpayan //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a te kuṣṭhikāḥ saramāyai kurmebhyo adadhuḥ śaphān /

Halfverse: c ūbadhyam asya kītebhyaḥ śvavartebhyo adhārayan //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a śṛṅgābhyām rakṣa ṛṣaty avartim hanti cakṣuṣā /

Halfverse: c śṛṇoti bhadram karṇābhyām gavām yaḥ patir aghnyaḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a śatayājam sa yajate nainam dunvanty agnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c jinvanti viśve tam devā yo brāhmaṇa ṛṣabham ājuhoti //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a brāhmaṇebhya ṛṣabham dattvā varīyaḥ kṛṇute manaḥ /

Halfverse: c puṣṭim so aghnyānām sve goṣṭhe ‘va paśyate //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a gāvaḥ santu prajāḥ santv atho astu tanūbalam /

Halfverse: c tat sarvam anu manyantām devā ṛṣabhadāyine //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a ayam pipāna indra id rayim dadhātu cetanīm /

Halfverse: c ayam dhenum sudughām nityavatsām vaśam duhām vipaścitam paro divaḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a piśaṅgarūpo nabhaso vayodhā aindraḥ śuṣmo viśvarūpo na āgan /

Halfverse: c āyur asmabhyam dadhat prajām ca rāyaś ca poṣair abhi naḥ sacatām //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a upehopaparcanāsmin goṣṭha upa pṛñca naḥ /

Halfverse: c upa ṛṣabhasya yad reta upendra tava vīryam //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a etam vo yuvānam prati dadhmo atra tena krīḍantīś carata vaśāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c mā no hāsiṣṭa januṣā subhāgā rāyaś ca poṣair abhi naḥ sacadhvam //24// {10}

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā nayaitam ā rabhasva sukṛtām lokam api gachatu prajānan /

Halfverse: c tīrtvā tamāmsi bahudhā mahānty ajo nākam ā kramatām tṛtīyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indrāya bhāgam pari tvā nayāmy asmin yajñe yajamānāya sūrim /

Halfverse: c ye no dviṣanty anu tān rabhasvānāgaso yajamānasya vīrāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pra pado ‘va nenigdhi duścaritam yac cacāra śuddhaiḥ śaphair ā kramatām prajānan /

Halfverse: c tīrtvā tamāmsi bahudhā vipaśyann ajo nākam ā kramatām tṛtīyam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anuchya śyāmena tvacam etām viśastar yathāparv a1sinā mābhi mamsthāḥ /

Halfverse: c mābhi druhaḥ paruśaḥ kalpayainam tṛtīye nāke adhi vi śrayainam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ṛcā kumbhīm adhy agnau śrayāmy ā siñcodakam ava dhehy enam /

Halfverse: c paryādhattāgninā śamitāraḥ sṛto gachatu sukṛtām yatra lokaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ut kāmātaḥ pari ced ataptas taptāc caror adhi nākam tṛtīyam /

Halfverse: c agner agnir adhi sam babhūvitha jyotiṣmantam abhi lokam jayaitam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ajo agnir ajam u jyotir āhur ajam jīvatā brahmaṇe deyam āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c ajas tamāmsy apa hanti dūram asmiml loke śraddadhānena dattaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a pañcaudanaḥ pañcadhā vi kramatām ākramsyamānas trīṇi jyotīmṣi /

Halfverse: c ījānānām sukṛtām prehi madhyam tṛtīye nāke adhi vi śrayasva //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ajā roha sukṛtām yatra lokaḥ śarabho na catto ‘ti durgāny eṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c pañcaudano brahmaṇe dīyamānaḥ sa dātāram tṛptyā tarpayāti //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ajas trināke tridive tripṛṣṭhe nākasya pṛṣṭhe dadivāmsam dadhāti /

Halfverse: c pañcaudano brahmaṇe dīyamāno viśvarūpā dhenuḥ kāmadughāsy ekā //10// {11}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a etad vo jyotiḥ pitaras tṛtīyam pañcaudanam brahmaṇe ‘jam dadāti /

Halfverse: c ajas tamāmsy apa hanti dūram asmiml loke śraddadhānena dattaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ījānānām sukṛtām lokam īpsan pañcaudanam brahmaṇe ‘jam dadāti /

Halfverse: c sa vyāptim abhi lokam jayaitam śivo3 ‘smabhyam pratigṛhīto astu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ajo hy a1gner ajaniṣṭa śokād vipro viprasya sahaso vipaścit /

Halfverse: c iṣṭam pūrtam abhipūrtam vaṣaṭkṛtam tad devā ṛtuśaḥ kalpayantu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a amotam vāso dadyād dhiraṇyam api dakṣiṇām /

Halfverse: c tathā lokānt sam āpnoti ye divyā ye ca pārthivāḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a etās tvājopa yantu dhārāḥ somyā devīr ghṛtapṛṣṭhā madhuścutaḥ /

Halfverse: c stabhān pṛthivīm uta dyām nākasya pṛṣṭhe ‘dhi saptaraśmau //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ajo ‘sy aja svargo ‘si tvayā lokam aṅgirasaḥ prājānan /

Halfverse: c tam lokam puṇyam pra jñeṣam //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yenā sahasram vahasi yenāgne sarvavedasam /

Halfverse: c tenemam yajñam no vaha svar deveṣu gantave //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a ajaḥ pakvaḥ svarge loke dadhāti pañcaudano nirṛtim bādhamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c tena lokānt sūryavato jayema //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yam brāhmaṇe nidadhe yam ca vikṣu yā vipruṣa odanānām ajasya /

Halfverse: c sarvam tad agne sukṛtasya loke jānītān naḥ samgamane pathīnām //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ajo vā idam agne vy akramata tasyora iyam abhavad dyauḥ pṛṣṭiham /

Halfverse: c antarikṣam madhyam diśaḥ pārśve samudrau kukṣī //20// {12}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a satyam ca rtam ca cakṣuṣī viśvam satyam śraddhā prāṇo virāṭ śiraḥ /

Halfverse: c eṣa vā aparimito yajño yad ajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a aparimitam eva yajñam āpnoty aparimitam lokam ava runddhe /

Halfverse: c yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a nāsyāsthīni bhindyān na majjño nir dhayet /

Halfverse: c sarvam enam samādāyedamidam pra veśayet //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a idamidam evāsya rūpam bhavati tenainam sam gamayati /

Halfverse: c iṣam maha ūrjam asmai duhe yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a pañca rukmā pañca navāni vastrā pañcāsmai dhenavaḥ kāmadughā bhavanti /

Halfverse: c yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a pañca rukmā jyotir asmai bhavanti varma vāsāmsi tanvṝ bhavanti /

Halfverse: c svargam lokam aśnute yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a yā pūrvam patim vittvā ‘thānyam vindate ‘param /

Halfverse: c pañcaudanam ca tāv ajam dadāto na vi yoṣataḥ //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a samānaloko bhavati punarbhuvāparaḥ patiḥ /

Halfverse: c yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a anupūrvavatsām dhenum anaḍvāham upabarhaṇam /

Halfverse: c vāso hiraṇyam dattvā te yanti divam uttamām //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a ātmānam pitaram putram pautram pitāmaham /

Halfverse: c jāyām janitrīm mātaram ye priyās tān upa hvaye //30// {13}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a yo vai naidāgham nāma rtum veda /

Halfverse: c eṣa vai naidāgho nāma rtur yad ajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ /

Halfverse: e nir evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam dahati bhavaty ātmanā /

Halfverse: g yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a yo vai kurvantam nāma rtum veda /

Halfverse: c kurvatīmkurvatīm evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam ā datte /

Halfverse: e eṣa vai kurvan nāma rtur yad ajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ /

Halfverse: g nir evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam dahati bhavaty ātmanā yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣinājyotiṣam dadāti //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a yo vai samyantam nāma rtum veda /

Halfverse: c samyatīmsamyatīm evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam ā datte /

Halfverse: e eṣa vai samyan nāma rtur yad ajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ /

Halfverse: g nir evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam dahati bhavaty ātmanā yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a yo vai pinvantam nāma rtum veda /

Halfverse: c pinvatīmpinvatīm evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam ā datte /

Halfverse: e eṣa vai pinvan nāma rtur yad ajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ /

Halfverse: g nir evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam dahati bhavaty ātmanā yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣinājyotiṣam dadāti //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a yo vā udyantam nāma rtum veda /

Halfverse: c udyatīmudyatīm evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam ā datte /

Halfverse: e eṣa vā udyann nāma rtur yad ajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ /

Halfverse: g nir evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam dahati bhavaty ātmanā yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam daksiṇājyotiṣam dadāti //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a yo vā abhibhuvam nāma rtum veda /

Halfverse: c abhibhavantīmabhibhavantīm evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam ā datte /

Halfverse: e eṣa vā abhibhūr nāma rtur yad ajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ /

Halfverse: g nir evāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyam dahati bhavaty ātmanā /

Halfverse: i yo3 ‘jam pañcaudanam dakṣinājyotiṣam dadāti //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a ajam ca pacata pañca caudanān /

Halfverse: c sarvā diśaḥ sammanasaḥ sadhrīcīḥ sāntardeśāḥ prati gṛhnantu ta etam //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a tās te rakṣantu tava tubhyam etam tābhya ājyam havir idam juhomi //38// {14}

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1 {1}

Halfverse: a yo vidyād brahma pratyakṣam parūmṣi yasya sambhārā ṛco yasyānūkyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sāmāni yasya lomāni yajur hṛdayam ucyate paristaraṇam id dhaviḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad vā atithipatir atithīn pratipaśyati devayajanam prekṣate //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad abhivadati dīkṣām upaiti yad udakam yācaty apaḥ pra ṇayati //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yā eva yajña āpaḥ praṇīyante tā eva tāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yat tarpaṇam āharanti ya evāgnīṣomīyaḥ paśur badhyate sa eva saḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yad āvasathān kalpayanti sadohavirdhānāny eva tat kalpayanti //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yad upastṛṇanti barhir eva tat //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yad upariśayanam āharanti svargam eva tena lokam ava runddhe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yat kaśipūpabarhaṇam āharanti paridhaya eva te //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yad āñjanābhyañjanam āharanty ājyam eva tat //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yat purā pariveṣāt svādam āharanti purodāśāv eva tau //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yad aśanakṛtam hvayanti haviṣkṛtam eva tad dhvayanti //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ye vrīhayo yavā nirupyante ‘mśava eva te //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yāny ulūkhalamusalāni grāvāṇa eva te //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a śūrpam pavitram tuṣā ṛjīṣābhiṣavaṇīr āpaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a srug darvir nekṣaṇam āyavanam droṇakalaśāḥ kumbhyo vāyavyāni pātrāṇīyam eva kṛṣṇājinam //17// {15}

Verse: 18 {2}

Halfverse: a yajamānabrāhmaṇam vā etad atithipatiḥ kurute yad āhāryāṇi prekṣata idam bhūyā3 idā3m iti //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yad āha bhūya ud dhareti prāṇam eva tena varṣīyāmsam kurute //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a upa harati havīmṣy ā sādayati //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a teṣām āsannānām atithir ātman juhoti //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a srucā hastena prāṇe yūpe srukkāreṇa vaṣaṭkāreṇa //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ete vai priyāś cāpriyāś ca rtvijaḥ svargam lokam gamayanti yad atithayaḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a sa ya evam vidvān na dviṣann aśnīyān na dviṣato ‘nnam aśnīyān na mīmāmsitasya na mīmāmsamānasya //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a sarvo vā eṣa jagdhapāpmā yasyānnam aśnanti //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a sarvo vā eso ‘jagdhapāpmā yasyānnam nāśnanti //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a sarvadā vā eṣa yuktagrāvārdrapavitro vitatādhvara āhṛtayajñakratur ya upaharati //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a prājāpatyo vā etasya yajño vitato ya upaharati //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a prajāpater vā eṣa vikramān anuvikramate ya upaharati //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a yo ‘tithīnām sa āhavanīyo yo veśmani sa gārhapatyo yasmin pacanti sa dakṣiṇāgnih //30// {16}

Verse: 31 {3}

Halfverse: a iṣṭam ca vā eṣa pūrtam ca gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a payaś ca vā eṣa rasam ca gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a ūrjām ca vā eṣa sphātim ca gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a prajām vā eṣa paśūmś ca gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a kīrtim vā eṣa yaśaś ca gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a śriyam vā eṣa samvidam ca gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a eṣa vā atithir yac chrotriyas tasmāt pūrvo nāśnīyāt //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a aśitāvaty atithāv aśnīyād yajñasya sātmatvāya yajñasyāvichedāya tad vratam //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a etad vā u svādīyo yad adhigavam kṣīram vā māmsam vā tad eva nāśnīyāt //39// {17}

Verse: 40 {4}

Halfverse: a sa ya evam vidvān kṣīram upasicyopaharati /

Halfverse: c yāvad agniṣṭomeneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe //40//

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a sa ya evam vidvānt sarpir upasicyopaharati /

Halfverse: c yāvad atirātreṇeṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a sa ya evam vidvān madhūpasicyopaharati /

Halfverse: c yāvad sattrasadyeneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a sa ya evam vidvān māmsam upasicyopaharati /

Halfverse: c yāvad dvādaśāheneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a sa ya evam vidvān udakam upasicyopaharati /

Halfverse: c prajānām prajananāya gachati pratiṣṭhām priyah prajānām bhavati ya evam vidvān upasicyopaharati //44// {18}

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a {5} tasmā uṣā hiṅ kṛṇoti savitā pra stauti /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir ūrjayod gāyati tvaṣṭā puṣṭyā prati harati viśve devā nidhanam //2//

Halfverse: e nidhanam bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnām bhavati ya evam veda //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a tasmā udyant sūryo hiṅ kṛṇoti samgavaḥ pra stauti /

Halfverse: c madhyandina ud gāyaty aparāhṇaḥ prati haraty astamyan nidhanam /

Halfverse: e nidhanam bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnām bhavati ya evam veda //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a tasmā abhro bhavan hiṅ kṛṇoti stanayan pra stauti /

Halfverse: c vidyotamānaḥ prati harati varṣann ud gāyaty udgṛhṇan nidhanam /

Halfverse: e nidhanam bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnām bhavati ya evam veda //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a atithīn prati paśyati hiṅ kṛṇoty abhi vadati pra stauty udakam yācaty ud gāyati /

Halfverse: c upa harati prati haraty ucchiṣṭam nidhanam /

Halfverse: e nidhanam bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnām bhavati ya evam veda //48// {19}

Verse: 49 {6}

Halfverse: a yat kṣattāram hvayaty ā śrāvayaty eva tat //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a yat pratiśṛṇoti pratyāśrāvayaty eva tat //50//

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a yat pariveṣṭāraḥ pātrahastāḥ pūrve cāpare ca prapadyante camasādhvaryava eva te //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a teṣām na kaś canāhotā //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a yad vā atithipatir atithīn pariviṣya gṛhān upodaity avabhṛtham eva tad upāvaiti //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a yat sabhāgayati dakṣiṇāḥ sabhāgayati yad anutiṣṭhata udavasyaty eva tat //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a sa upahūtaḥ pṛthivyām bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yat pṛthivyām viśvarūpam //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a sa upahūto ‘ntarikṣe bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a sa upahūto divi bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a sa upahūto deveṣu bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a sa upahūto lokeṣu bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a sa upahūta upahūtaḥ //60//

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a āpnotīmam lokam āpnoty amum //61//

Verse: 62

Halfverse: a jyotiṣmato lokān jayati ya evam veda //62// {20}

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prajāpatiś ca parameṣṭhī ca śṛṅge indraḥ śiro agnir lalāṭam yamaḥ kṛkāṭam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a somo rājā mastiṣko dyaur uttarahanuḥ pṛthivy adharahanuḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vidyuj jihvā maruto dantā revatir grīvāḥ kṛttikā skandhā gharmo vahaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a viśvam vāyuḥ svargo lokaḥ kṛṣṇadram vidharaṇī niveṣyaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śyenaḥ kroto ‘ntarikṣam pājasya1m bṛhaspatiḥ kakud bṛhatīḥ kīkasāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a devānām patnīḥ pṛṣṭaya upasadaḥ parśavaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a mitraś ca varuṇaś cāmsau tvaṣṭā cāryamā ca doṣaṇī mahādevo bāhū //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a indrāṇī bhasad vāyuḥ pucham pavamāno bālāḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a brahma ca kṣatram ca śroṇī balam ūrū //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a dhātā ca savitā cāṣṭhīvantau jaṅghā gandharvā apsarasaḥ kuṣṭhikā aditiḥ śaphāḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ceto hṛdayam yakṛn medhā vratam purītat //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a kṣut kukṣir irā vaniṣṭhuḥ parvatāḥ plāśayaḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a krodho vṛkkau manyur āṇḍau prajā śepaḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a nadī sūtrī varṣasya pataya stanā stanayitnur ūdhaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a viśvavyacās carmauṣadhayo lomāni nakṣatrāṇi rūpam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a devajanā gudā manuṣyā āntrāṇy atrā udaram //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a rakṣāmsi lohitam itarajanā ūbadhyam //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a abhram pībo majjā nidhanam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a agnir āsīna utthito ‘śvinā //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a indraḥ prāṅ tiṣṭhan dakṣiṇā tiṣṭhan yamaḥ //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a pratyaṅ tiṣṭhan dhātodaṅ tiṣṭhant savitā //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a tṛṇāmi prāptaḥ somo rājā //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a mitra īkṣamāṇa āvṛtta ānandaḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yujyamāno vaiśvadevo yuktaḥ prajāpatir vimuktaḥ sarvam //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a etad vai viśvarūpam sarvarūpam gorūpam //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a upainam viśvarūpāḥ sarvarūpāḥ paśavas tiṣṭhanti ya evam veda //26// {21}

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śīrṣaktim śīrṣāmayam karṇaśūlam vilohitam /

Halfverse: c sarvam śīrṣanyam te rogam bahir nir mantrayāmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a karṇābhyām te kaṅkūṣebhyaḥ karṇaśūlam visalpakam /

Halfverse: c sarvam śīrṣanyam te rogam bahir nir mantrayāmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yasya hetoḥ pracyavate yakṣmaḥ karṇato āsyataḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvam śīrṣanyam te rogam bahir nir mantrayāmahe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yaḥ kṛṇoti pramotam andham kṛṇoti pūruṣam /

Halfverse: c sarvam śīrṣanyam te rogam bahir nir mantrayāmahe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a aṅgabhedam aṅgajvaram viśvāṅgyam visalpakam /

Halfverse: c sarvam śīrṣanyam te rogam bahir nir mantrayāmahe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yasya bhīmaḥ pratīkāśa udvepayati pūruṣam /

Halfverse: c takmānam viśvaśāradam bahir nir mantrayāmahe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ya ūrū anusarpaty atho eti gavīnike /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam te antar aṅgebhyo bahir nir mantrayāmahe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yadi kāmād apakāmād dhṛdayāj jāyate pari /

Halfverse: c hṛdo balāsam aṅgebhyo bahir nir mantrayāmahe //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a harimāṇam te aṅgebhyo ‘pvām antarodarāt /

Halfverse: c yakṣmodhām antar ātmano bahir nir mantrayāmahe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a āso balāso bhavatu mūtram bhavatv āmayat /

Halfverse: c yakṣmāṇām sarveṣām viṣam nir avocam aham tvat //10// {22}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a bahir bilam nir dravatu kāhābāham tavodarāt /

Halfverse: c yakṣmāṇām sarveṣām viṣam nir avocam aham tvat //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a udarāt te klomno nābhyā hṛdayād adhi /

Halfverse: c yakṣmāṇām sarveṣām viṣam nir avocam aham tvat //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yāḥ sīmānam virujanti mūrdhānam praty arṣanīḥ /

Halfverse: c ahimsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yā hṛdayam uparṣanty anutanvanti kīkasāḥ /

Halfverse: c ahimsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yāḥ pārśve uparṣanty anunikṣanti pṛṣṭīḥ /

Halfverse: c ahimsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yās tiraścīḥ uparṣanty arṣaṇīr vakṣaṇāsu te /

Halfverse: c ahimsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yā gudā anusarpanty āntrāṇi mohayanti ca /

Halfverse: c ahimsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yā majjño nirdhayanti parūmṣi virujanti ca /

Halfverse: c ahimsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ye aṅgāni madayanti yakṣmāso ropaṇās tava /

Halfverse: c yakṣmāṇām sarveṣām viṣam nir avocam aham tvat //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a visalpasya vidradhasya vātīkārasya vālajeḥ /

Halfverse: c yakṣmāṇām sarveṣām viṣam nir avocam aham tvat //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a pādābhyām te jānubhyām śroṇibhyām pari bhamsasaḥ /

Halfverse: c anūkād arṣaṇīr uṣṇihābhyaḥ śīrṣṇo rogam anīnaśam //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a sam te śīrṣṇaḥ kapālāni hṛdayasya ca yo vidhuḥ /

Halfverse: c udyann āditya raśmibhiḥ śīrṣṇo rogam anīnaśo ‘ṅgabhedam aśīśamaḥ //22// {23}

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asya vāmasya palitasya hotus tasya bhrātā madhyamo asty aśnaḥ /

Halfverse: c tṛtīyo bhrātā ghṛtapṛṣṭho asyātrāpaśyam viśpatim saptaputram //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sapta yuñjanti ratham ekacakram eko aśvo vahati saptanāmā /

Halfverse: c trinābhi cakram ajaram anarvam yatremā viśvā bhuvanādhi tasthuḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a imam ratham adhi ye sapta tasthuḥ saptacakram sapta vahanty aśvāḥ /

Halfverse: c sapta svasāro abhi sam navanta yatra gavām nihitā sapta nāma //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ko dadarśa prathamam jāyamānam asthanvantam yad anasthā bibharti /

Halfverse: c bhūmyā asur asṛg ātmā kva svit ko vidvāmsam upa gāt praṣṭum etat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a iha bravītu ya īm aṅga vedāsya vāmasya nihitam padam veḥ /

Halfverse: c śīrṣṇaḥ kṣīram duhrate gāvo asya vavrim vasānā udakam padā ‘puḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pākaḥ pṛchāmi manasā ‘vijānan devānām enā nihitā padāni /

Halfverse: c vatse baṣkaye ‘dhi sapta tantūn vi tatnire kavaya otavā u //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a acikitvāms cikituṣaś cid atra kavīn pṛchāmi vidvano na vidvān /

Halfverse: c vi yas tastambha ṣaṭ imā rajāmsy ajasya rūpe kim api svid ekam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mātā pitaram ṛta ā babhāja ‘dhīty agre manasā sam hi jagme /

Halfverse: c sā bībhatsur garbharasā nividdhā namasvanta id upavākam īyuḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yuktā mātāsid dhuri dakṣiṇāyā atiṣṭhad garbho vṛjanīṣv antaḥ /

Halfverse: c amīmed vatso anu gām apaśyad viśvarūpyam triṣu yoganeṣu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tisro matṝs trīn pitṝn bibhrad eka urdhvas tasthau nem ava glāpayanta /

Halfverse: c mantrayante divo amuṣya pṛṣṭhe viśvavido vācam aviśvavinnām //10// {24}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a pañcāre cakre parivartamāne yasminn ātasthur bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c tasya nākṣas tapyate bhūribhāraḥ sanād eva na chidyate sanābhiḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a pañcapādam pitaram dvādaśākṛtim diva āhuḥ pare ardhe purīṣiṇam /

Halfverse: c atheme anya upare vicakṣaṇe saptacakre ṣaḍara āhur arpitam //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a dvādaśāram nahi taj jarāya varvarti cakram pari dyām ṛtasya /

Halfverse: c ā putrā agne mithunāso atra sapta śatāni vimśatiś ca tasthuḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a sanemi cakram ajaram vi vavṛta uttānāyām daśa yuktā vahanti /

Halfverse: c sūryasya cakṣū rajasaity āvṛtam yasminn ātasthur bhuvanāni viśvā //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a striyaḥ satīs tām u me pumsaḥ āhuḥ paśyad akṣaṇvānn vi cetad andhaḥ /

Halfverse: c kavir yaḥ putraḥ sa īm ā ciketa yas tā vijānāt sa pituṣ pitāsat //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a sākamjānām saptatham āhur ekajam ṣaḍ id yamā ṛṣayo devajā iti /

Halfverse: c teṣām iṣṭāni vihitāni dhāmaśa sthātre rejante vikṛtāni rūpaśaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a avaḥ pareṇa para enā avareṇa padā vatsam bibhratī gaur ud asthāt /

Halfverse: c sā kadrīcī kam svid ardham parāgāt kva svit sūte nahi yūthe asmin //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a avaḥ pareṇa pitaram yo asya vedāvaḥ pareṇa para enāvareṇa /

Halfverse: c kavīyamānaḥ ka iha pra vocad devam manaḥ kuto adhi prajātam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ye arvāñcas tām u parāca āhur ye parāñcas tāṁ u arvāca āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c indraś ca yā cakrathuḥ soma tāni dhurā na yuktā rajaso vahanti //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānam vṛkṣam pari ṣasvajāte /

Halfverse: c tayor anyaḥ pippalam svādv atty anaśnann anyo abhi cākaśīti //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yasmin vṛkṣe madhvadaḥ suparṇā niviśante suvate cādhi viśve /

Halfverse: c tasya yad āhuḥ pippalam svādv agre tan non naśad yaḥ pitaram na veda //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yatrā suparṇā amṛtasya bhakṣam animeṣam vidathābhisvaranti /

Halfverse: c enā viśvasya bhuvanasya gopāḥ sa mā dhīraḥ pākam atrā viveśa //22// {25}

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad gāyatre adhi gāyatram āhitam traiṣṭubham vā traiṣṭubhān niratakṣata /

Halfverse: c yad vā jagaj jagaty āhitam padam ya it tad vidus te amṛtatvam ānuśuḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a gāyatreṇa prati mimīte arkam arkeṇa sāma traiṣṭubhena vākam /

Halfverse: c vākena vākam dvipadā catuṣpadākṣareṇa mimate sapta vāṇīḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a jagatā sindhum divy askabhāyad rathamtare sūryam pary apaśyat /

Halfverse: c gāyatrasya samidhas tisra āhus tato mahnā pra ririce mahitvā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a upa hvaye sudughām dhenum etām suhasto godhug uta dohad enām /

Halfverse: c śreṣṭham savam savitā sāviṣan no ‘bhīddho gharmas tad u ṣu pra vocat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a hiṅkṛṇvatī vasupatnī vasūnām vatsam ichantī manasābhyāgāt /

Halfverse: c duhām aśvibhyām payo aghnyeyam sā vardhatām mahate saubhagāya //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a gaur amīmed abhi vatsam miṣantam mūrdhānam hiṅṅ akṛṇon mātavā u /

Halfverse: c sṛkvāṇam gharmam abhi vāvaśānā mimāti māyum payate payobhiḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ayam sa śiṅkte yena gaur abhivṛtā mimāti mayum dhvasanāv adhi śritā /

Halfverse: c sā cittibhir ni hi cakāra martyān vidyud bhavantī prati vavrim auhata //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a anac chaye turagātu jīvam ejad dhruvam madhya ā pastyānām /

Halfverse: c jīvo mṛtasya carati svadhābhir amartyo martyenā sayoniḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a vidhum dadrāṇam salilasya pṛṣṭhe yuvānam santam palito jagāra /

Halfverse: c devasya paśya kāvyam mahitvādya mamāra sa hyaḥ sam āna //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ya īm cakāra na so asya veda ya īm dadarśa hirug in nu tasmāt /

Halfverse: c sa mātur yonā parivīto antar bahuprajā nirṛtir ā viveśa //10// {26}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a apaśyam gopām anipadyamānam ā ca parā ca pathibhiś carantam /

Halfverse: c sa sadhrīcīḥ sa viṣūcīr vasāna ā varīvarti bhuvaneṣv antaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a dyaur naḥ pitā janitā nābhir atra bandhur no mātā pṛthivī mahīyam /

Halfverse: c uttānayoś camvo3r yonir antar atrā pitā duhitur garbham ādhāt //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a pṛchāmi tvā param antam pṛthivyāḥ pṛchāmi vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ /

Halfverse: c pṛchāmi viśvasya bhuvanasya nābhim pṛchāmi vācaḥ paramam vyoma //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a iyam vediḥ paro antaḥ pṛthivyā ayam somo vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ /

Halfverse: c ayam yajño viśvasya bhuvanasya nābhir brahmāyam vācaḥ paramam vyoma //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a na vi jānāmi yad ivedam asmi niṇyaḥ samnaddho manasā carāmi /

Halfverse: c yadā māgan prathamajā ṛtasyād id vāco aśnuve bhāgam asyāḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a apāṅ prāṅ eti svadhayā gṛbhīto ‘martyo martyenā sayoniḥ /

Halfverse: c tā śaśvantā viṣūcīnā viyantā ny anyam cikyur na ni cikyur anyam //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a saptārdhagarbhā bhuvanasya reto viṣṇos tiṣṭhanti pradiśā vidharmaṇi /

Halfverse: c te dhītibhir manasā te vipaścitaḥ paribhuvaḥ pari bhavanti viśvataḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a ṛco akṣare parame vyoman yasmin devā adhi viśve niṣeduḥ /

Halfverse: c yas tan na veda kim ṛcā kariṣyati ya it tad vidus te amī sam āsate //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ṛcaḥ padam mātrayā kalpayanto ‘rdharcena cakḷpur viśvam ejat /

Halfverse: c tripād brahma pururūpam vi taṣṭhe tena jīvanti pradiśaś catasraḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a sūyavasād bhagavatī hi bhūyā adhā vayam bhagavantaḥ syāma /

Halfverse: c addhi tṛṇam aghnye viśvadānīm piba śuddham udakam ācarantī //20// {27}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a gaur in mimāya salilāni takṣatī ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī /

Halfverse: c aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī sahasrākṣarā bhuvanasya paṅktis tasyāḥ samudrā adhi vi kṣaranti //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a kṛṣṇam niyānam harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divam ut patanti /

Halfverse: c tam āvavṛtrant sadanād ṛtasyād id ghṛtena pṛthivīm vy ūduḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a apād eti prathamā padvatīnām kas tad vām mitrāvaruṇā ciketa /

Halfverse: c garbho bhāram bharaty ā cid asyā ṛtam piparti anṛtam ni pāti //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a virāḍ vāg virāṭ pṛthivī virāḍ antarikṣam virāṭ prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c virāṇ mṛtyuḥ sādhyānām adhirājo babhūva tasya bhūtam bhavyam vaśe sa me bhūtam bhavyam vaśe kṛṇotu //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a śakamayam dhūmam ārād apaśyam viṣūvatā para enāvareṇa /

Halfverse: c ukṣāṇam pṛśnim apacanta vīrās tāni dharmāṇi prathamāny āsan //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a trayaḥ keśina ṛtuthā vi cakṣate samvatsare vapata eka eṣām /

Halfverse: c viśvam anyo abhicaṣṭe śacībhir dhrājir ekasya dadṛśe na rūpam //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a catvāri vāk parimitā padāni tāni vidur brāhmaṇā ye manīṣiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c guhā trīṇi nihitā neṅgayanti turīyam vāco manuṣyā vadanti //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a indram mitram varuṇam agnim āhur atho divyaḥ sa suparṇo garutmān /

Halfverse: c ekam sad viprā bahudhā vadanty agnim yamam mātariśvānam āhuḥ //28// {28}

## Book: 10

10

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yām kalpayanti vahatau vadhūm iva viśvarūpām hastakṛtām cikitsavaḥ /

Halfverse: c sārād etv apa nudāma enām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śīrṣaṇvatī nasvatī karṇiṇī kṛtyākṛtā sambhṛtā viśvarūpā /

Halfverse: c sārād etv apa nudāma enām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śūdrakṛtā rājakṛtā strīkṛtā brahmabhiḥ kṛtā /

Halfverse: c jāyā patyā nutteva kartāram bandhv ṛchatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anayāham oṣadhyā sarvāḥ kṛtyā adūduṣam /

Halfverse: c yām kṣetre cakrur yām goṣu yām vā te puruṣeṣu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a agham astv aghakṛte śapathaḥ śapathīyate /

Halfverse: c pratyak pratiprahiṇmo yathā kṛtyākṛtam hanat //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pratīcīna āṅgiraso ‘dhyakṣo naḥ purohitaḥ /

Halfverse: c pratīcīḥ kṛtyā ākṛtyāmūn kṛtyākṛto jahi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yas tvovāca parehīti pratikūlam udāyyam /

Halfverse: c tam kṛtye ‘bhinivartasva māsmān icho anāgasaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yas te parūmṣi samdadhau rathasyeva rbhur dhiyā /

Halfverse: c tam gacha tatra te ‘yanam ajñātas te ‘yam janaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ye tvā kṛtvālebhire vidvalā abhicāriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c śambhv ṅ3dam kṛtyādūṣaṇam prativartma punaḥsaram tena tvā snapayāmasi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yad durbhagām prasnapitām mṛtavatsām upeyima /

Halfverse: c apaitu sarvam mat pāpam draviṇam mopa tiṣṭhatu //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yat te pitṛbhyo dadato yajñe vā nāma jagṛhuḥ /

Halfverse: c samdeśyā3t sarvasmāt pāpād imā muñcantu tvauṣadhīḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a devainasāt pitryān nāmagrāhāt samdeśyād abhiniṣkṛtāt /

Halfverse: c muñcantu tvā vīrudho vīryeṇa brahmaṇā ṛgbhiḥ payasā ṛṣīṇām //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yathā vātaś cyāvayati bhūmyā reṇum antarikṣāc cābhram /

Halfverse: c evā mat sarvam durbhūtam brahmanuttam apāyati //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a apa krāma nānadatī vinaddhā gardabhīva /

Halfverse: c kartṝn nakṣasveto nuttā brahmaṇā vīryāvatā //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ayam panthāḥ kṛtyeti tvā nayāmo ‘bhiprahitām prati tvā pra hiṇmaḥ /

Halfverse: c tenābhi yāhi bhañjaty anasvatīva vāhinī viśvarūpā kurūtinī //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a parāk te jyotir apatham te arvāg anyatrāsmad ayanā kṛṇuṣva /

Halfverse: c pareṇehi navatim nāvyā3 ati durgāḥ srotyā mā kṣaṇiṣṭhāḥ parehi //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a vāta iva vṛkṣān ni mṛṇīhi pādaya mā gām aśvam puruṣam uc chiṣa eṣām /

Halfverse: c kartṝn nivṛtyetaḥ kṛtye ‘prajāstvāya bodhaya //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yām te barhiṣi yām śmaśāne kṣetre kṛtyām valagam vā nicakhnuḥ /

Halfverse: c agnau vā tvā gārhapatye ‘bhiceruḥ pākam santam dhīratarā anāgasam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a upāhṛtam anubuddham nikhātam vairam tsāry anv avidāma kartram /

Halfverse: c tad etu yata ābhṛtam tatrāśva iva vi vartatām hantu kṛtyākṛtaḥ prajām //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a svāyasā asayaḥ santi no gṛhe vidmā te kṛtye yatidhā parūmṣi /

Halfverse: c ut tiṣṭhaiva parehīto ‘jñāte kim ihechasi //20// {2}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a grīvās te kṛtye pādau cāpi kartsyāmi nir drava /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī asmān rakṣatām yau prajānām prajāvatī //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a somo rājādhipā mṛḍitā ca bhūtasya naḥ patayo mṛḍayantu //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a bhavāśarvāv asyatām pāpakṛte kṛtyākṛte /

Halfverse: c duṣkṛte vidyutam devahetim //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yady eyatha dvipadī catuṣpadī kṛtyākṛtā sambhṛtā viśvarūpā /

Halfverse: c seto ‘ṣṭāpadī bhūtvā punaḥ parehi duchune //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a abhyaktāktā svaramkṛtā sarvam bharantī duritam parehi /

Halfverse: c jānīhi kṛtye kartāram duhiteva pitaram svam //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a parehi kṛtye mā tiṣṭho viddhasyeva padam naya /

Halfverse: c mṛgaḥ sa mṛgayus tvam na tvā nikartum arhati //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a uta hanti pūrvāsinam pratyādāyāpara iṣvā /

Halfverse: c uta pūrvasya nighnato ni hanty aparaḥ prati //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a etad dhi śṛṇu me vaco ‘thehi yata eyatha /

Halfverse: c yas tvā cakāra tam prati //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a anāgohatyā vai bhīmā kṛtye mā no gām aśvam puruṣam vadhīḥ /

Halfverse: c yatrayatrāsi nihitā tatas tvot thāpayāmasi parṇāl laghīyasī bhava //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a yadi stha tamasāvṛtā jālenabhihitā iva /

Halfverse: c sarvāḥ samlupyetaḥ kṛtyāḥ punaḥ kartre pra hiṇmasi //30//

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a kṛtyākṛto valagino ‘bhiniṣkāriṇaḥ prajām /

Halfverse: c mṛṇīhi kṛtye moc chiṣo ‘mūn kṛtyākṛto jahi //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a yathā sūryo mucyate tamasas pari rātrim jahāty uṣasaśca ketūn /

Halfverse: c evāham sarvam durbhūtam kartram kṛtyākṛtā kṛtam hastīva rajo duritam jahāmi //32// {3}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kena pārṣṇī ābhṛte pūruṣasya kena māmsam sambhṛtam kena gulphau /

Halfverse: c kenāṅgulīḥ peśanīḥ kena khāni kenocchlaṅkhau madhyataḥ kaḥ pratiṣṭhām //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kasmān nu gulphāv adharāv akṛṇvann aṣṭhīvantāv uttarau puruṣasya /

Halfverse: c jaṅghe nirṛtya ny adadhuḥ kva svij jānunoḥ samdhī ka u tac ciketa //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a catuṣṭayam yujate samhitāntam jānubhyām ūrdhvam śithiram kabandham /

Halfverse: c śroṇī yad ūrū ka u taj jajāna yābhyām kusindham sudṛḍham babhūva //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kati devāḥ katame ta āsan ya uro grīvāś cikyuḥ puruṣasya /

Halfverse: c kati stanau vy adadhuḥ kaḥ kaphodau kati skandhān kati pṛṣṭīr acinvan //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ko asya bāhū sam abharad vīryām karavād iti /

Halfverse: c amsau ko asya tad devaḥ kusindhe adhy ā dadhau //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kaḥ sapta khāni vi tatarda śīrṣaṇi karṇāv imau nāsike cakṣaṇī mukham /

Halfverse: c yeṣām purutrā vijayasya mahnani catuṣpādo dvipado yanti yāmam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a hanvor hi jihvām adadhāt purūcīm adhā mahīm adhi śiśrāya vācam /

Halfverse: c sa ā varīvarti bhuvaneṣv antar apo vasānaḥ ka u tac ciketa //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mastiṣkam asya yatamo lalātam kakāṭikām prathamo yaḥ kapālam /

Halfverse: c citvā cityam hanvoḥ pūruṣasya divam ruroha katamaḥ sa devaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a priyāpriyāṇi bahulā svapnam sambādhatandyaḥ /

Halfverse: c ānandān ugro nandāmś ca kasmād vahati pūruṣaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ārtir avartir nirṛtiḥ kuto nu puruṣe ‘matiḥ /

Halfverse: c rāddhiḥ samṛddhir avyṛddhir matir uditayaḥ kutaḥ //10// {4}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ko asminn āpo vy adadhāt viṣūvṛtaḥ purūvṛtaḥ sindhusṛtyāya jātāḥ /

Halfverse: c tīvrā aruṇā lohinīs tāmradhūmrā ūrdhvā avācīḥ puruṣe tiraścīḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ko asmin rūpam adadhāt ko mahmānam ca nāma ca /

Halfverse: c gātum ko asmin kaḥ ketum kaś caritrāni puruṣe //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ko asmin prāṇam avayat ko apānam vyānam u /

Halfverse: c samānam asmin ko devo ‘dhi śiśrāya puruṣe //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ko asmin yajñam adadhād eko devo ‘dhi puruṣe /

Halfverse: c ko asmint satyam ko ‘nṛtam kuto mṛtyuḥ kuto ‘mṛtam //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ko asmai vāsaḥ pary adadhāt ko asyāyur akalpayat /

Halfverse: c balam ko asmai prāyachat ko asyākalpayaj javam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a kenāpo anv atanuta kenāhar akarod ruce /

Halfverse: c uṣasam kenānv ainddha kena sāyambhavam dade //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ko asmin reto ny adadhāt tantur ā tāyatām iti /

Halfverse: c medhām ko asminn adhy auhat ko bāṇam ko nṛto dadhau //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a kenemām bhūmim aurṇot kena pary abhavad divam /

Halfverse: c kenābhi mahnā parvatān kena karmāṇi puruṣaḥ //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a kena parjanyam anv eti kena somam vicakṣaṇam /

Halfverse: c kena yajñam ca śraddhām ca kenāsmin nihitam manaḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a kena śrotriyam āpnoti kenemam parameṣṭhinam /

Halfverse: c kenemam agnim pūruṣaḥ kena samvatsaram mame //20// {5}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a brahma śrotriyam āpnoti brahmemam parameṣṭhinam /

Halfverse: c brahmemam agnim pūruṣo brahma samvatsaram mame //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a kena devāṁ anu kṣiyati kena daivajanīr viśaḥ /

Halfverse: c kenedam anyan nakṣatram kena sat kṣatram ucyate //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a brahma devāṁ anu kṣiyati brahma daivajanīr viśaḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmedam anyan nakṣatram brahma sat kṣatram ucyate //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a keneyam bhūmir vihitā kena dyaur uttarā hitā /

Halfverse: c kenedam ūrdhvam tiryak cāntarikṣam vyaco hitam //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a brahmaṇā bhūmir vihitā brahma dyaur uttarā hitā /

Halfverse: c brahmedam ūrdhvam tiryak cāntarikṣam vyaco hitam //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a mūrdhānam asya samsīvyātharvā hṛdayam ca yat /

Halfverse: c mastiṣkād ūrdhvaḥ prairayat pavamāno ‘dhi śīrṣataḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a tad vā atharvaṇaḥ śiro devakośaḥ samubjitaḥ /

Halfverse: c tat prāṇo abhi rakṣati śiro annam atho manaḥ //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a ūrdhvo nu sṛṣṭā3s tiryaṅ nu sṛṣṭā3s sarvā diśaḥ puruṣa ā babhūvā3ṁ /

Halfverse: c puram yo brahmaṇo veda yasyāḥ puruṣa ucyate //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a yo vai tām brahmaṇo vedāmṛtenāvṛtām puram /

Halfverse: c tasmai brahma ca brāhmāś ca cakṣuḥ prāṇam prajām daduḥ //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a na vai tam cakṣur jahāti na prāṇo jarasaḥ purā /

Halfverse: c puram yo brahmaṇo veda yasyāḥ puruṣa ucyate //30//

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a aṣṭācakrā navadvārā devānām pūr ayodhyā /

Halfverse: c tasyām hiraṇyayaḥ kośaḥ svargo jyotiṣāvṛtaḥ //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a tasmin hiraṇyaye kośe tryare tripratiṣṭhite /

Halfverse: c tasmin yad yakṣam ātmanvat tad vai brahmavido viduḥ //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a prabhrājamānām hariṇīm yaśasā samparīvṛtām /

Halfverse: c puram hiraṇyayīm brahmā viveśāparājitām //33// {6}

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam me varaṇo maṇiḥ sapatnakṣayaṇo vṛṣā /

Halfverse: c tenā rabhasva tvam śatrūn pra mṛṇīhi durasyataḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a praiṇān chṛṇīhi pra mṛṇā rabhasva maṇis te astu puraetā purastāt /

Halfverse: c avārayanta varaṇena devā abhyācāram asurāṇām śvaḥśvaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ayam maṇir varaṇo viśvabheṣajaḥ sahasrākṣo harito hiraṇyayaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa te śatrūn adharān pādayāti pūrvas tān dabhnuhi ye tvā dviṣanti //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ayam te kṛtyām vitatām pauruṣeyād ayam bhayāt /

Halfverse: c ayam tvā sarvasmāt pāpād varaṇo vārayiṣyate //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a varaṇo vārayātā ayam devo vanaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c yakṣmo yo asminn āviṣṭas tam u devā avīvaran //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a svapnam suptvā yadi paśyāsi pāpam mṛgaḥ sṛtim yati dhāvād ajuṣṭām /

Halfverse: c parikṣavāc chakuneḥ pāpavādād ayam maṇir varaṇo vārayiṣyate //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a arātyās tvā nirṛtyā abhicārād atho bhayāt /

Halfverse: c mṛtyor ojīyaso vadhād varaṇo vārayiṣyate //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yan me mātā yan me pitā bhrātaro yac ca me svā yad enaś cakṛmā vayam /

Halfverse: c tato no vārayiṣyate ‘yam devo vanaspatiḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a varaṇena pravyathitā bhrātṛvyā me sabandhavaḥ /

Halfverse: c asūrtam rajo apy agus te yantv adhamam tamaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ariṣṭo ‘ham ariṣṭagur āyuṣmānt sarvapūruṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam māyam varaṇo maṇiḥ pari pātu diśodiśaḥ //10// {7}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ayam me varaṇa urasi rājā devo vanaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c sa me śatrūn vi bādhatām indro dasyūn ivāsurān //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a imam bibharmi varaṇam āyuṣmān chataśāradaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa me rāṣṭram ca kṣatram ca paśūn ojaś ca me dadhat //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yathā vāto vanaspatīn vṛkṣān bhanakty ojasā /

Halfverse: c evā sapatnān me bhaṅgdhi pūrvān jātāṁ utāparān varaṇas tvābhi rakṣatu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yathā vātaś cāgniś ca vṛkṣān psāto vanaspatīn /

Halfverse: c evā sapatnān me psāhi pūrvān jātāṁ utāparān varaṇas tvābhi rakṣatu //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yathā vātena prakṣīṇā vṛkṣāḥ śere nyarpitāḥ /

Halfverse: c evā sapatnāms tvam mama pra kṣiṇīhi ny arpaya /

Halfverse: e pūrvān jātāṁ utāparān varaṇas tvābhi rakṣatu //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a tāms tvam pra chinddhi varaṇa purā diṣṭāt purāyuṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c ya enam paśuṣu dipsanti ye cāsya rāṣṭradipsavaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yathā sūryo atibhāti yathāsmin teja āhitam /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yathā yaśaś candramasy āditye ca nṛcakṣasi /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yathā yaśaḥ pṛthivyām yathāsmin jātavedasi /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yathā yaśaḥ kanyāyām yathāsmint sambhṛte rathe /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //20// {8}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yathā yaśaḥ somapīthe madhuparke yathā yaśaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yathā yaśo ‘gnihotre vaṣaṭkāre yathā yaśaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //22//

Verse: 23
