Halfverse: a yathā yaśo yajamāne yathāsmin yajña āhitam /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yathā yaśaḥ prajāpatau yathāsmin parameṣṭhini /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yathā deveṣv amṛtam yathaiṣu satyam āhitam /

Halfverse: c evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtim bhūtim ni yachatu /

Halfverse: e tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā //25// {9}

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrasya prathamo ratho devānām aparo ratho varuṇasya tṛtīya it /

Halfverse: c ahīnām apamā ratha sthānum ārad athārṣat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a darbhaḥ śocis tarūṇakam aśvasya vāraḥ paruṣasya vāraḥ /

Halfverse: c rathasya bandhuram //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ava śveta padā jahi pūrveṇa cāpareṇa ca /

Halfverse: c udaplutam iva dārv ahīnām arasam viṣam vār ugram //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a aramghuṣo nimajyonmaja punar abravīt /

Halfverse: c udaplutam iva dārv ahīnām arasam viṣam vār ugram //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a paidvo hanti kasarṇīlam paidvaḥ śvitram utāsitam /

Halfverse: c paidvo ratharvyāḥ śiraḥ sam bibheda pṛdākvāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a paidva prehi prathamo ‘nu tvā vayam emasi /

Halfverse: c ahīn vy asyatāt patho yena smā vayam emasi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a idam paidvo ajāyatedam asya parāyaṇam /

Halfverse: c imāny arvataḥ padāhighnyo vājinīvataḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a samyatam na vi ṣparad vyāttam na sam yamat /

Halfverse: c asmin kṣetre dvāv ahī strī ca pumāmś ca tāv ubhāv arasā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a arasāsa ihāhayo ye anti ye ca dūrake /

Halfverse: c ghanena hanmi vṛścikam ahim daṇḍenāgatam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a aghāśvasyedam bheṣajam ubhayo svajasya ca /

Halfverse: c indro me ‘him aghāyantam ahim paidvo arandhayat //10// {10}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a paidvasya manmahe vayam sthirasya sthiradhāmnaḥ /

Halfverse: c ime paścā pṛdākavaḥ pradīdhyata āsate //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a naṣṭāsavo naṣṭaviṣā hatā indreṇa vajriṇā /

Halfverse: c jaghānendro jaghnimā vayam //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a hatās tiraścirājayo nipiṣṭāsaḥ pṛdākavaḥ /

Halfverse: c darvim karikratam śvitram darbheṣv asitam jahi //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a kairātikā kumārikā sakā khanati bheṣajam /

Halfverse: c hiraṇyayībhir abhribhir girīnām upa sānuṣu //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a āyam agan yuvā bhiṣak pṛśnihāparājitaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa vai svajasya jambhana ubhayor vṛścikasya ca //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a indro me ‘him arandhayan mitraś ca varuṇaś ca /

Halfverse: c vātāparjanyobhā //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a indro me ‘him arandhayat pṛdākum ca pṛdākvam /

Halfverse: c svajam tiraścirājim kasarṇīlam daśonasim //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a indro jaghāna prathamam janitāram ahe tava /

Halfverse: c teṣām u tṛhyamāṇānām kaḥ svit teṣām asad rasaḥ //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a sam hi śīrṣāṇy agrabham pauñjiṣṭha iva karvaram /

Halfverse: c sindhor madhyam paretya vy anijam aher viṣam //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ahīnām sarveṣām viṣam parā vahantu sindhavaḥ /

Halfverse: c hatās tiraścirājayo nipiṣṭāsaḥ pṛdākavaḥ //20// {11}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a oṣadhīnām aham vṛṇa urvarīr iva sādhuyā /

Halfverse: c nayāmy arvatīr ivāhe niraitu viṣam //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yad agnau sūrye viṣam pṛthivyām oṣadhīṣu yat /

Halfverse: c kāndāviṣam kanaknakam niraitv aitu te viṣam //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ye agnijā oṣadhijā ahīnām ye apsujā vidyuta ābabhūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c yeṣām jātāni bahudhā mahānti tebhyaḥ sarpebhyo namasā vidhema //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a taudī nāmāsi kanyā ghṛtācī nāma vā asi /

Halfverse: c adhaspadena te padam ā dade viṣadūṣaṇam //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a aṅgādaṅgāt pra cyāvaya hṛdayam pari varjaya /

Halfverse: c adhā viṣasya yat tejo ‘vācīnam tad etu te //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a āre abhūd viṣam araud viṣe viṣam aprāg api /

Halfverse: c agnir viṣam aher nir adhāt somo nir aṇayīt /

Halfverse: e damṣṭāram anv agād viṣam ahir amṛta //26// {12}

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balam sthendrasya vīrya1m sthendrasya nṛmṇam stha /

Halfverse: c jiṣṇave yogāya brahmayogair vo yunajmi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balam sthendrasya vīrya1m sthendrasya nṛmṇam stha /

Halfverse: c jiṣṇave yogāya kṣatrayogair vo yunajmi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balam sthendrasya vīrya1m sthendrasya nṛmṇam stha /

Halfverse: c jiṣṇave yogāyendrayogair vo yunajmi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balam sthendrasya vīrya1m sthendrasya nṛmṇam stha /

Halfverse: c jiṣṇave yogāya somayogair vo yunajmi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balam sthendrasya vīrya1m sthendrasya nṛmṇam stha /

Halfverse: c jiṣṇave yogāyāpsuyogair vo yunajmi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balam sthendrasya vīrya1m sthendrasya nṛmṇam stha /

Halfverse: c jiṣṇave yogāya viśvāni mā bhūtāny upa tiṣṭhantu yuktā ma āpa stha //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a agner bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a indrasya bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a somasya bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a varuṇasya bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //10// {13}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a mitrāvaruṇayor bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yamasya bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a pitṝṇām bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a devasya savitur bhāga stha apām śukram āpo devīr varco asmāsu dhatta /

Halfverse: c prajāpater vo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘pām bhāgo ‘psv antar yajuṣyo devayajanaḥ /

Halfverse: c idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi /

Halfverse: e tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: g tam vadheyam tam stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘pām ūrmir apsv antar yajuṣyo devayajanaḥ /

Halfverse: c idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi /

Halfverse: e tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: g tam vadheyam tam stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘pām vatso ‘psv antar yajuṣyo devayajanaḥ /

Halfverse: c idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi /

Halfverse: e tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: g tam vadheyam tam stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘pām vṛṣabho ‘psv antar yajuṣyo devayajanah //

Halfverse: c idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi /

Halfverse: e tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //

Halfverse: g tam vadheyam tam stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘pām hiraṇyagarbho ‘psv antar yajuṣyo devayajanah /

Halfverse: c idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi /

Halfverse: e tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: g tam vadheyam tam stṛsīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘pām aśmā pṛśnir divyo ‘psv antar yajuṣyo devayajanaḥ /

Halfverse: c idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi /

Halfverse: e tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: g tam vadheyam tam stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā //20// {14}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘pām agnayo ‘psv antar yajuṣyo devayajanaḥ /

Halfverse: c idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi /

Halfverse: e tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: g tam vadheyam tam stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yad arvācīnam traihāyaṇād anṛtam kim codima /

Halfverse: c āpo mā tasmāt sarvasmād duritāt pāntv amhasaḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a samudram vaḥ pra hiṇomi svām yonim apītana /

Halfverse: c ariṣṭāḥ sarvahāyaso mā ca naḥ kim canāmamat //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ariprā āpo apa ripram asmat /

Halfverse: c prāsmad eno duritam supratīkāḥ pra duṣvapnyam pra malam vahantu //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahā pṛthivīsamśito ‘gnitejāh /

Halfverse: c pṛthivīm anu vi krame ‘ham pṛthivyās tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahāntarikṣasamśito vāyutejāḥ /

Halfverse: c antarikṣam anu vi krame ‘ham antarikṣāt tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahā dyausamśitaḥ sūryatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c divam anu vi krame ‘ham divas tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jivīt tam prāno jahātu //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahā diksamśito manastejāḥ /

Halfverse: c diśo anu vi krame ‘ham digbhyas tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahāśāsamśito vātatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c āśā anu vi krame ‘ham āśābhyas tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahā ṛksamśito sāmatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛco ‘nu vi krame ‘ham ṛgbhyas tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //30// {15}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahā yajñasamśito brahmatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c yajñam anu vi krame ‘ham yajñāt tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahauṣadhīsamśito somatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c oṣadhīr anu vi krame ‘ham oṣadhībhyas tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahāpsusamśito varuṇatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c apo ‘nu vi krame ‘ham adbhyas tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahā kṛṣisamśito ‘nnatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c kṛṣim anu vi krame ‘ham kṛṣyās tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a visṇoḥ kramo ‘si sapatnahā prāṇasamśitaḥ puruṣatejāḥ /

Halfverse: c prāṇam anu vi krame ‘ham prāṇāt tam nir bhajāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa mā jīvīt tam prāno jahātu //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a jitam asmākam udbhinnam asmākam abhy aṣṭhām viśvāḥ pṛtanā arātīḥ /

Halfverse: c idam aham āmuṣyāyaṇasyāmuṣyāḥ putrasya varcas tejaḥ prāṇam āyur ni veṣṭayāmīdam enam adharāñcam pādayāmi //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a sūryasyāvṛtam anvāvarte dakṣiṇām anv āvṛtam /

Halfverse: c sā me draviṇam yachatu sā me brāhmaṇavarcasam //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a diśo jyotiṣmatīr abhyāvarte /

Halfverse: c tā me draviṇam yachantu tā me brāhmaṇavarcasam //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a saptaṛṣīn abhyāvarte /

Halfverse: c te me draviṇam yachantu te me brāhmaṇavarcasam //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a brahmābhyāvarte /

Halfverse: c tan me draviṇam yachantu tan me brāhmaṇavarcasam //40// {16}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a brāhmaṇāṁ abhyāvarte /

Halfverse: c te me draviṇam yachantu te me brāhmaṇavarcasam //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a yam vayam mṛgayāmahe tam vadhai stṛṇavāmahai /

Halfverse: c vyātte parameṣṭhino brahmaṇāpīpadāma tam //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a vaiśvānarasya damṣṭrābhyām hetis tam sam adhāt abhi /

Halfverse: c iyam tam psātv āhutiḥ samid devī sahīyasī //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a rājño varuṇasya bandho ‘si /

Halfverse: c so ‘mum āmuṣyāyaṇam amuṣyāḥ putram anne prāṇe badhāna //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a yat te annam bhuvas pata ākṣiyati pṛthivīm anu /

Halfverse: c tasya nas tvam bhuvas pate samprayacha prajāpate //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a apo divyā acāyiṣam rasena sam apṛkṣmahi /

Halfverse: c payasvān agna āgamam tam mā sam sṛja varcasā //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a sam māgne varcasā sṛja sam prajayā sam āyuṣā /

Halfverse: c vidyur me asya devā indro vidyāt saha ṛṣibhiḥ //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a yad agne adya mithunā śapato yad vācas tṛṣṭam janayanta rebhāḥ /

Halfverse: c manyor manasaḥ śaravyā3 jāyate yā tayā vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a parā śṛṇīhi tapasā yātudhānān parāgne rakṣo harasā śṛṇīhi /

Halfverse: c parārciṣā mūradevām chṛṇīhi parāsutṛpaḥ śośucataḥ śṛṇīhi //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a apām asmai vajram pra harāmi caturbhṛṣṭim śīrṣabhidyāya vidvān /

Halfverse: c so asyāṅgāni pra śṛṇātu sarvā tan me devā anu jānantu viśve //50// {17}

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a arātīyor bhrātṛvyasya durhārdo dviṣataḥ śiraḥ /

Halfverse: c api vṛścāmy ojasā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a varma mahyam ayam maṇiḥ phālāj jātaḥ kariṣyati /

Halfverse: c pūrṇo manthena māgamad rasena saha varcasā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat tvā śikvaḥ parāvadhīt takṣā hastena vāsyā /

Halfverse: c āpas tvā tasmaj jīvalāḥ punantu śucayaḥ śucim //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a hiraṇyasrag ayam maṇiḥ śraddhām yajñam maho dadhat /

Halfverse: c gṛhe vasatu no ‘tithiḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tasmai ghṛtam suram madhv annamannam kṣadāmahe /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ piteva putrebhyaḥ śreyaḥśreyaś cikitsatu bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvo devebhyo maṇir etya //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇim phālam ghṛtaścutam ugram kadhiram ojase /

Halfverse: c tam agniḥ praty amuñcata so asmai duha ājyam bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇim phālam ghṛtaścutam ugram kadhiram ojase /

Halfverse: c tam indraḥ praty amuñcataujase vīryāya kam /

Halfverse: e so asmai balam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇim phālam ghṛtaścutam ugram kadhiram ojase /

Halfverse: c tam somaḥ praty amuñcata mahe śrotrāya cakṣase /

Halfverse: e so asmai varca id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇim phālam ghṛtaścutam ugram khadiram ojase /

Halfverse: c tam sūryaḥ praty amuñcata tenemā ajayad diśaḥ /

Halfverse: e so asmai bhūtim id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇim phālam ghṛtaścutam ugram khadiram ojase /

Halfverse: c tam bibhrac candramā maṇim asurāṇām puro ‘jayad dānavānām hiraṇyayīḥ /

Halfverse: e so asmai śriyam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //10// {18}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave /

Halfverse: c so asmai vājinam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave /

Halfverse: c tenemām maṇinā kṛṣim aśvināv abhi rakṣataḥ /

Halfverse: e sa bhiṣagbhyām maho duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave /

Halfverse: c tam bibhrat savitā maṇim tenedam ajayat svaḥ /

Halfverse: e so asmai sūnṛtām duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave /

Halfverse: c tam āpo bibhratīr maṇim sadā dhāvanty akṣitāḥ /

Halfverse: e sa ābhyo ‘mṛtam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave /

Halfverse: c tam rājā varuṇo maṇim praty amuñcata śambhuvam /

Halfverse: e so asmai satyam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave /

Halfverse: c tam devā bibhrato maṇim sarvāml lokān yudhājayan /

Halfverse: e sa ebhyo jitim id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave /

Halfverse: c tam imam devatā maṇim praty amuñcanta śambhuvam /

Halfverse: e sa ābhyo viśvam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a ṛtavas tam abadhnatārtavās tam abadhnata /

Halfverse: c samvatsaras tam baddhvā sarvam bhūtam vi rakṣati //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a antardeśā abadhnata pradiśas tam abadhnata /

Halfverse: c prajāpatisṛṣṭo maṇir dviṣato me ‘dharāṁ akaḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a atharvāṇo abadhnatātharvaṇā abadhnata /

Halfverse: c tair medino aṅgiraso dasyūnām bibhiduḥ puras tena tvam dviṣato jahi //20// {19}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a tam dhātā praty amuñcata sa bhūtam vy akalpayat /

Halfverse: c tena tvam dviṣato jahi //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim /

Halfverse: c sa māyam maṇir āgamad rasena saha varcasā //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim /

Halfverse: c sa māyam maṇir āgamat saha gobhir ajāvibhir annena prajayā saha //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim /

Halfverse: c sa māyam maṇir āgamat saha vrīhiyavābhyām mahasā bhūtyā saha //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim /

Halfverse: c sa māyam maṇir āgaman madhor ghṛtasya dhārayā kīlālena maṇiḥ saha //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim /

Halfverse: c sa māyam maṇir āgamad ūrjayā payasā saha draviṇena śriyā saha //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim /

Halfverse: c sa māyam maṇir āgamat tejasā tviṣyā saha yaśasā kīrtyā saha //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim /

Halfverse: c sa māyam maṇir āgamat sarvābhir bhūtibhiḥ saha //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a tam imam devatā maṇim mahyam dadatu puṣṭaye /

Halfverse: c abhibhum kṣatravardhanam sapatnadambhanam maṇim //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a brahmaṇā tejasā saha prati muñcāmi me śivam /

Halfverse: c asapatnaḥ sapatnahā sapatnān me ‘dharāṁ akaḥ //30// {20}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a uttaram dviṣato mām ayam maṇiḥ kṛṇotu devajāḥ /

Halfverse: c yasya lokā ime trayaḥ payo dugdham upāsate /

Halfverse: e sa māyam adhi rohatu maṇiḥ śraiṣṭhyāya mūrdhataḥ //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a yam devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā upajīvanti sarvadā /

Halfverse: c sa māyam adhi rohatu maṇiḥ śraiṣṭhyāya mūrdhataḥ //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a yathā bījam urvarāyām kṛṣṭe phālena rohati /

Halfverse: c evā mayi prajā paśavo ‘nnamannam vi rohatu //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a yasmai tvā yajñavardhana maṇe pratyamucam śivam /

Halfverse: c tam tvam śatadakṣiṇa maṇe śraiṣṭhyāya jinvatāt //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a etam idhmam samāhitam juṣaṇo agne prati harya homaiḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmin vidhema sumatim svasti prajām cakṣuḥ paśūnt samiddhe jātavedasi brahmaṇā //35// {21}

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kasminn aṅge tapo asyādhi tiṣṭhati kasminn aṅga ṛtam asyādhy āhitam /

Halfverse: c kva vratam kva śraddhāsya tiṣṭhati kasminn aṅge satyam asya pratiṣṭhitam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kasmād aṅgād dīpyate agnir asya kasmād aṅgāt pavate mātariśva /

Halfverse: c kasmād aṅgād vi mimīte ‘dhi candramā maha skambhasya mimāno aṅgam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhati bhūmir asya kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhaty antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhaty āhitā dyauḥ kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhaty uttaram divaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kva1 prepsan dīpyata ūrdhvo agniḥ kva1 prepsan pavate mātariśvā /

Halfverse: c yatra prepsantīr abhiyanty āvṛtaḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kvārdhamāsāḥ kva yanti māsāḥ samvatsareṇa saha samvidānāḥ /

Halfverse: c yatra yanty ṛtavo yatrārtavāḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kva1 prepsantī yuvatī virūpe ahorātre dravataḥ samvidāne /

Halfverse: c yatra prepsantīr abhiyanty āpaḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yasmint stabdhvā prajāpatir lokānt sarvāṁ adhārayat /

Halfverse: c skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yat paramam avamam yac ca madhyamam prajāpatiḥ sasṛje viśvarūpam /

Halfverse: c kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa tatra yan na prāviśat kiyat tad babhūva //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa bhūtam kiyad bhaviṣyad anvāśaye ‘sya /

Halfverse: c ekam yad aṅgam akṛṇot sahasradhā kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa tatra //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yatra lokāmś ca kośāmś cāpo brahma janā viduḥ /

Halfverse: c asac ca yatra sac cānta skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //10// {22}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yatra tapaḥ parākramya vratam dhārayaty uttaram /

Halfverse: c ṛtam ca yatra śraddhā cāpo brahma samāhitāḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yasmin bhūmir antarikṣam dyaur yasminn adhy āhitā /

Halfverse: c yatrāgniś candramāḥ sūryo vātas tiṣṭhanty ārpitāḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yasya trayastrimśad devā aṅge sarve samāhitāḥ /

Halfverse: c skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yatra ṛṣayaḥ prathamajā ṛcaḥ sāma yajur mahī /

Halfverse: c ekarṣir yasminn ārpitaḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yatrāmṛtam ca mṛtyuś ca puruṣe ‘dhi samāhite /

Halfverse: c samudro yasya nāḍya1ḥ puruṣe ‘dhi samāhitāḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yasya catasraḥ pradiśo nāḍya1s tiṣṭhanti prathamāḥ /

Halfverse: c yajño yatra parākrāntaḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ye puruṣe brahma vidus te viduḥ parameṣṭhinam /

Halfverse: c yo veda parameṣṭhinam yaś ca veda prajāpatim /

Halfverse: e jyeṣṭham ye brāhmaṇam vidus te skambham anusamviduḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yasya śiro vaiśvānaraś cakṣur aṅgiraso ‘bhavan /

Halfverse: c aṅgāni yasya yātavaḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yasya brahma mukham āhur jihvām madhukaśām uta /

Halfverse: c virājam ūdho yasyāhuḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yasmād ṛco apātakṣan yajur yasmād apākaṣan /

Halfverse: c sāmāni yasya lomāny atharvāṅgiraso mukham skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //20// {23}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a asaccākhām pratiṣṭhantīm paramam iva janā viduḥ /

Halfverse: c uto san manyante ‘vare ye te śākhām upāsate //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yatrādityāś ca rudrāś ca vasavaś ca samāhitāḥ /

Halfverse: c bhūtam ca yatra bhavyam ca sarve lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yasya trayastrimśad devā nidhim rakṣanti sarvadā /

Halfverse: c nidhim tam adya ko veda yam devā abhirakṣatha //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yatra devā brahmavido brahma jyeṣṭham upāsate /

Halfverse: c yo vai tān vidyāt pratyakṣam sa brahmā veditā syāt //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a bṛhanto nāma te devā ye ‘sataḥ pari jajñire /

Halfverse: c ekam tad aṅgam skambhasyāsad āhuḥ paro janāḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a yatra skambhaḥ prajanayan purāṇam vyavartayat /

Halfverse: c ekam tad aṅgam skambhasya purāṇam anusamviduḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a yasya trayastrimśad devā aṅge gātrā vibhejire /

Halfverse: c tān vai trayastrimśad devān eke brahamvido viduḥ //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a hiraṇyagarbham paramam anatyudyam janā viduḥ /

Halfverse: c skambhas tad agre prāsiñcad dhiraṇyam loke antarā //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a skambhe lokāḥ skambhe tapaḥ skambhe ‘dhy ṛtam āhitam /

Halfverse: c skambha tvā veda pratyakṣam indre sarvam samāhitam //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a indre lokā indre tapa indre ‘dhy ṛtam āhitam /

Halfverse: c indram tvā veda pratyakṣam skambhe sarvam pratiṣṭhitam //30// {24}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a nāma nāmnā johavīti purā sūryāt puroṣasaḥ /

Halfverse: c yad ajaḥ prathamam sambabhūva sa ha tat svarājyam iyāya yasmān nānyat param asti bhūtam //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a yasya bhūmiḥ pramāntarikṣam utodaram /

Halfverse: c divam yaś cakre mūrdhānam tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a yasya sūryaś cakṣuś candramāś ca punarṇavaḥ /

Halfverse: c agnim yaś cakra āsya1m tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a yasya vātaḥ prāṇāpānau cakṣur aṅgiraso ‘bhavan /

Halfverse: c diśo yaś cakre prajñānīs tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a skambho dādhāra dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime skambho dādhārorv antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c skambho dādhāra pradiśaḥ ṣaḍ urvīḥ skambha idam viśvam bhuvanam ā viveśa //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a yaḥ śramāt tapaso jāto lokānt sarvānt samānaśe /

Halfverse: c somam yaś cakre kevalam tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a katham vāto nelayati katham na ramate manaḥ /

Halfverse: c kim āpaḥ satyam prepsantīr nelayanti kadā cana //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a mahad yakṣam bhuvanasya madhye tapasi krāntam salilasya pṛṣṭhe /

Halfverse: c tasmin chrayante ya u ke ca devā vṛkṣasya skandhaḥ parita iva śākhāḥ //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a yasmai hastābhyām pādābhyām vācā śrotreṇa cakṣuṣā /

Halfverse: c yasmai devāḥ sadā balim prayachanti vimite ‘mitam skambham tam brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a apa tasya hatam tamo vyāvṛttaḥ sa pāpmanā /

Halfverse: c sarvāṇi tasmin jyotīmṣi yāni trīṇi prajāpatau //40//

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a yo vetasam hiraṇyayam tiṣṭhantam salile veda /

Halfverse: c sa vai guhyaḥ prajāpatiḥ //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a tantram eke yuvatī virūpe abhyākrāmam vayataḥ ṣaṇmayūkham /

Halfverse: c prānyā tantūms tirate dhatte anyā nāpa vṛñjāte na gamāto antam //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a tayor aham parinṛtyantyor iva na vi jānāmi yatarā parastāt /

Halfverse: c pumān enad vayaty ud gṛṇanti pumān enad vi jabhārādhi nāke //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a ime mayūkhā upa tastabhur divam sāmāni cakrus tasarāṇi vātave //44// {25}

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo bhūtam ca bhavyam ca sarvam yaś cādhitiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: c sv a1ryasya ca kevalam tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a skambheneme viṣṭabhite dyauś ca bhūmiś ca tiṣṭhataḥ /

Halfverse: c skambha idam sarvam ātmanvad yat prāṇan nimiṣac ca yat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tisro ha prajā atyāyam āyan ny anyā arkam abhito ‘viśanta /

Halfverse: c bṛhan ha tasthau rajaso vimāno harito hariṇīr ā viveśa //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a dvādaśa pradhayaś cakram ekam trīṇi nabhyāni ka u tac ciketa /

Halfverse: c tatrāhatās trīṇi śatāni śaṅkavaḥ ṣaṣṭiś ca khīlā avicācalā ye //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a idam savitar vi jānīhi ṣaḍ yamā eka ekajaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmin hāpitvam ichante ya eṣām eka ekajaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a āviḥ san nihitam guhā jaran nāma mahat padam /

Halfverse: c tatredam sarvam ārpitam ejat prāṇat pratiṣṭhitam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ekacakram vartata ekanemi sahasrākṣaram pra puro ni paścā /

Halfverse: c ardhena viśvam bhuvanam jajāna yad asyārdham kva1 tad babhūva //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a pañcavāhī vahatyagram eṣām praṣṭayo yuktā anusamvahanti /

Halfverse: c ayātam asya dadṛśe na yātam param nedīyo ‘varam davīyaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tiryagbilaś camasa ūrdhvabudhnas tasmin yaśo nihitam viśvarūpam /

Halfverse: c tad āsata ṛṣayaḥ sapta sākam ye asya gopā mahato babhūvuḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yā purastād yujyate yā ca paścād yā viśvato yujyate yā ca sarvataḥ /

Halfverse: c yayā yajñaḥ prāṅ tāyate tām tvā pṛchāmi katamā sā ṛcām //10// {26}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yad ejati patati yac ca tiṣṭhati prāṇad aprāṇan nimiṣac ca yad bhuvat /

Halfverse: c tad dādhāra pṛthivīm viśvarūpam tat sambhūya bhavaty ekam eva //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a anantam vitatam purutrānantam antavac cā samante /

Halfverse: c te nākapālaś carati vicinvan vidvān bhūtam uta bhavyam asya //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a prajāpatiś carati garbhe antar adṛśyamāno bahudhā vi jāyate /

Halfverse: c ardhena viśvam bhuvanam jajāna yad asyārdham katamaḥ sa ketuḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ūrdhvam bharantam udakam kumbhenevodahāryam /

Halfverse: c paśyanti sarve cakṣuṣā na sarve manasā viduḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a dūre pūrṇena vasati dūra ūnena hīyate /

Halfverse: c mahad yakṣam bhuvanasya madhye tasmai balim rāṣṭrabhṛto bharanti //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yataḥ sūryaḥ udety astam yatra ca gachati /

Halfverse: c tad eva manye ‘ham jyeṣṭham tad u nāty eti kim cana //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ye arvāṅ madhya uta vā purāṇam vedam vidvāmsam abhito vadanti /

Halfverse: c ādityam eva te pari vadanti sarve agnim dvitīyam trivṛtam ca hamsam //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a sahasrāhṇyam viyatāv asya pakṣau harer hamsasya patataḥ svargam /

Halfverse: c sa devānt sarvān urasy upadadya sampaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a satyenordhvas tapati brahmaṇārvāṅ vi paśyati /

Halfverse: c prāṇena tiryaṅ prāṇati yasmin jyeṣṭham adhi śritam //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yo vai te vidyād araṇī yābhyām nirmathyate vasu /

Halfverse: c sa vidvān jyeṣṭham manyeta sa vidyād brāhmaṇam mahat //20// {27}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a apād agre sam abhavat so agre sva1r ābharat /

Halfverse: c catuṣpād bhūtvā bhogyaḥ sarvam ādatta bhojanam //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a bhogyo bhavad atho annam adad bahu /

Halfverse: c yo devam uttarāvantam upāsātai sanātanam //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a sanātanam enam āhur utādya syāt punarṇavaḥ /

Halfverse: c ahorātre pra jāyete anyo anyasya rūpayoḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a śatam sahasram ayutam nyarbudam asamkhyeyam svam asmin niviṣṭam /

Halfverse: c tad asya ghnanty abhipaśyata eva tasmād devo rocat eṣa etat //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a bālād ekam aṇīyaskam utaikam neva dṛśyate /

Halfverse: c tataḥ pariṣvajīyasī devatā sā mama priyā //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a iyam kalyāṇy a1jarā martyasyāmṛtā gṛhe /

Halfverse: c yasmai kṛtā śaye sa yaś cakāra jajāra saḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a tvam strī tvam pumān asi tvam kumāra uta vā kumārī//

Halfverse: b tvam jīrṇo daṇḍena vañcasi tvam jāto bhavasi viśvatomukhaḥ //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a utaiṣām pitota vā putra eṣām utaiṣām jyeṣṭha uta vā kaniṣṭhah /

Halfverse: c eko ha devo manasi praviṣṭaḥ prathamo jātaḥ sa u garbhe antah //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a pūrṇāt pūrṇam ud acati pūrṇam pūrṇena sicyate /

Halfverse: c uto tad adya vidyāma yatas tat pariṣicyate //29//

Verse: 0

Halfverse: a eṣā sanatnī sanam eva jātaiṣā purāṇī pari sarvam babhūva /

Halfverse: c mahī devy ḥ1ṣaso vibhātī saikenaikena miṣatā vi caṣṭe //30// {28}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a avir vai nāma devatartenāste parīvṛtā /

Halfverse: c tasyā rūpeṇeme vṛkṣā haritā haritasrajaḥ //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a anti santam na jahāty anti santam na paśyati /

Halfverse: c devasya paśya kāvyam na mamāra na jīryati //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a apūrveṇeṣitā vācas tā vadanti yathāyatham /

Halfverse: c vadantīr yatra gachanti tad āhur brāhmaṇam mahat //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a yatra devāś ca manuṣyāś cārā nābhāv iva śritāḥ /

Halfverse: c apām tvā puṣpam pṛchāmi yatra tan māyayā hitam //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a yebhir vāta iṣitaḥ pravāti ye dadante pañca diśaḥ sadhrīcīḥ /

Halfverse: c ya āhutim atyamanyanta devā apām netāraḥ katame ta āsan //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a imām eṣām pṛthivīm vasta eko ‘ntarikṣam pary eko babhūva /

Halfverse: c divam eṣām dadate yo vidhartā viśvā āśāḥ prati rakṣanty eke //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a yo vidyāt sūtram vitatam yasminn otāḥ prajā imāḥ /

Halfverse: c sūtram sūtrasya yo vidyād sa vidyād brāhmaṇam mahat //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a vedāham sūtram vitatam yasminn otāḥ prajā imāḥ /

Halfverse: c sūtram sūtrasyāham vedātho yad brāhmaṇam mahad //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a yad antarā dyāvāpṛthivī agnir ait pradahan viśvadāvyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yatrātiṣṭhann ekapatnīḥ parastāt kvṝvāsīn mātariśvā tadānīm //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a apsv āsīn mātariśvā praviṣṭaḥ praviṣṭā devāḥ salilāny āsan//

Halfverse: b bṛhan ha tasthau rajaso vimānaḥ pavamāno harita ā viveśa //40//

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a uttareṇeva gayatrīm amṛte ‘dhi vi cakrame /

Halfverse: c sāmnā ye sāma samvidur ajas tad dadṛśe kva //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a niveśanaḥ samgamano vasūnām deva iva savitā satyadharmā /

Halfverse: c indro na tasthau samare dhanānām //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a puṇḍarīkam navadvāram tribhir guṇebhir āvṛtam /

Halfverse: c tasmin yad yakṣam ātmanvat tad vai brahmavido viduh //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a akāmo dhīro amṛtaḥ svayambhū rasena tṛpto na kutaś canonaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam eva vidvān na bibhāya mṛtyor ātmānam dhīram ajaram yuvānam //44// {29}

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aghāyatām api nahyā mukhāni sapatneṣu vajram arpayaitam /

Halfverse: c indreṇa dattā prathamā śataudanā bhrātṛvyaghnī yajamānasya gātuḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vediṣ ṭe carma bhavatu barhir lomāni yāni te /

Halfverse: c eṣā tvā raśanāgrabhīd grāvā tvaiṣo ‘dhi nṛtyatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bālās te prokṣaṇīḥ santu jīhvā sam mārṣṭu aghnye /

Halfverse: c śuddhā tvam yajñiyā bhūtvā divam prehi śataudane //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yaḥ śataudanām pacati kāmapreṇa sa kalpate /

Halfverse: c prītā hy asya ṛtvijaḥ sarve yanti yathāyatham //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sa svargam ā rohati yatrādas tridivam divaḥ /

Halfverse: c apūpanābhim kṛtvā yo dadāti śataudanām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sa tāml lokānt sam āpnoti ye divyā ye ca pārthivāḥ /

Halfverse: c hiraṇyajyotiṣam kṛtvā yo dadāti śataudanām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ye te devi śamitāraḥ paktāro ye ca te janāḥ /

Halfverse: c te tvā sarve gopsyanti maibhyo bhaiṣīḥ śataudane //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a vasavas tvā dakṣiṇata uttarān marutas tvā /

Halfverse: c ādityāḥ paścād gopsyanti sāgniṣṭomam ati drava //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā gandharvāpsarasaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c te tvā sarve gopsyanti sātirātram ati drava //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a antarikṣam divam bhūmim ādityān maruto diśaḥ /

Halfverse: c lokānt sa sarvān āpnoti yo dadāti śataudanām //10// {30}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ghṛtam prokṣantī subhagā devī devān gamiṣyati /

Halfverse: c paktāram aghnye mā himsīr divam prehi śataudane //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ye devā diviṣado antarikṣasadaś ca ye ye ceme bhūmyām adhi /

Halfverse: c tebhyas tvam dhukṣva sarvadā kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yat te śiro yat te mukham yau karṇau ye ca te hanū /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yau ta oṣṭhau ye nāsike ye śṛṅge ye ca te ‘kṣiṇī /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yat te klomā yad dhṛdayam purītat sahakaṇṭhikā /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yat te yakṛd ye matasne yad āntram yāś ca te gudāḥ /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yas te plāśir yo vaniṣṭhur yau kukṣī yac ca carma te /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre ksīram sarpir atho madhu //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yat te majjā yad asthi yan mamsam yac ca lohitam /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre ksīram sarpir atho madhu //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yau te bāhū ye doṣaṇī yāv amsau yā ca te kakut /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre ksīram sarpir atho madhu //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yās te grīvā ye skandhā yāḥ pṛṣṭīr yāś ca parśavaḥ /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //20// {31}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yau ta urū aṣṭhīvantau ye śroṇī yā ca te bhasat /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yat te pucham ye te bālā yad ūdho ye ca te stanāḥ /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yās te jaṅghāḥ yāḥ kuṣṭhikā ṛcharā ye ca te śaphāḥ /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yat te carma śataudane yāni lomāny aghnye /

Halfverse: c āmikṣām duhratām dātre kṣīram sarpir atho madhu //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a kroḍau te stām purodāśāv ājyenābhighāritau /

Halfverse: c tau pakṣau devi kṛtvā sā paktāram divam vaha //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a ulūkhale musale yaś ca carmaṇi yo vā śūrpe taṇḍulaḥ kaṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c yam vā vāto mātariśvā pavamāno mamāthāgniṣ ṭad dhotā suhutam kṛṇotu //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a apo devīr madhumatīr ghṛtaścuto brahmaṇām hasteṣu prapṛthak sādayāmi /

Halfverse: c yatkāma idam abhiṣiñcāmi vo ‘ham tan me sarvam sam padyatām vayam syāma patayo rayīṇām //27// {32}

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a namas te jāyamānāyai jātāyā uta te namaḥ /

Halfverse: c bālebhyaḥ śaphebhyo rūpāyāghnye te namaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo vidyāt sapta pravataḥ sapta vidyāt parāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c śiro yajñasya yo vidyāt sa vaśām prati gṛhṇīyāt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vedāham sapta pravataḥ sapta veda parāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c śiro yajñasyāham veda somam cāsyām vicakṣaṇam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yayā dyaur yayā pṛthivī yayāpo gupitā imāḥ /

Halfverse: c vaśām sahasradhārām brahmaṇāchāvadāmasi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śatam kamsāḥ śatam dogdhāraḥ śatam goptāro adhi pṛṣṭhe asyāḥ /

Halfverse: c ye devās tasyām prāṇanti te vaśām vidur ekadhā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yajñapadīrākṣīrā svadhāprāṇā mahīlukā /

Halfverse: c vaśā parjanyapatnī devāṁ apy eti brahmaṇā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a anu tvāgniḥ prāviśad anu somo vaśe tvā /

Halfverse: c ūdhas te bhadre parjanyo vidyutas te stanā vaśe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a apas tvam dhukṣe prathamā urvarā aparā vaśe /

Halfverse: c tṛtīyam rāṣṭram dhukṣe ‘nnam kṣīram vaśe tvam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yad ādityair hūyamānopātiṣṭha ṛtavari /

Halfverse: c indraḥ sahasram pātrānt somam tvāpāyayad vaśe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yad anūcīndram air āt tvā ṛṣabho ‘hvayat /

Halfverse: c tasmāt te vṛtrahā payaḥ kṣīram kruddho ‘harad vaśe //10// {33}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yat te kruddho dhanapatir ā kṣīram aharad vaśe /

Halfverse: c idam tad adya nākas triṣu pātreṣu rakṣati //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a triṣu pātreṣu tam somam ā devy aharad vaśā /

Halfverse: c atharvā yatra dīkṣito barhiṣy āsta hiraṇyaye //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sam hi somenāgata sam u sarveṇa padvatā /

Halfverse: c vaśā samudram adhy aṣṭhad gandharvaiḥ kalibhiḥ saha //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a sam hi vātenāgata sam u sarvaiḥ patatribhiḥ /

Halfverse: c vaśā samudre prānṛtyad ṛcaḥ sāmāni bibhratī //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a sam hi sūryeṇāgata sam u sarveṇa cakṣuṣā /

Halfverse: c vaśā samudram aty akhyad bhadrā jyotīmṣi bibhratī //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a abhīvṛtā hiraṇyena yad atiṣṭha ṛtāvari /

Halfverse: c aśvaḥ samudro bhūtvādhy askandad vaśe tvā //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a tad bhadrāḥ sam agachanta vaśā deṣṭry atho svadhā /

Halfverse: c atharvā yatra dīkṣito barhiṣy āsta hiraṇyaye //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a vaśā mātā rājanyasya vaśā mātā svadhe tava /

Halfverse: c vaśāyā yajña āyudham tataś cittam ajāyata //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ūrdhvo bindur ud acarad brahmaṇaḥ kakudād adhi /

Halfverse: c tatas tvam jajñiṣe vaśe tato hotājāyata //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a āsnas te gāthā abhavann uṣṇihābhyo balam vaśe /

Halfverse: c pājasyāj jajñe yajña stanebhyo raśmayas tava //20// {34}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a īrmābhyām ayanam jātam sakthibhyām ca vaśe tava /

Halfverse: c āntrebhyo jajñire atrā udarād adhi vīrudhaḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yad udaram varuṇasyānuprāviśathā vaśe /

Halfverse: c tatas tvā brahmod ahvayat sa hi netram avet tava //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a sarve garbhād avepanta jāyamānād asūsvaḥ /

Halfverse: c sasūva hi tām āhu vaśeti brahmabhiḥ kḷptaḥ sa hy asyā bandhuḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yudha ekaḥ sam sṛjati yo asyā eka id vaśī /

Halfverse: c tarāmsi yajñā abhavan tarasām cakṣur abhavad vaśā //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a vaśā yajñam praty agṛhṇād vaśā sūryam adhārayat /

Halfverse: c vaśāyām antar aviśad odano brahmaṇā saha //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a vaśām evāmṛtam āhur vaśām mṛtyum upāsate /

Halfverse: c vaśedam sarvam abhavad devā manuṣyā3 asurāḥ pitara ṛṣayaḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a ya evam vidyāt sa vaśām prati gṛhṇīyāt /

Halfverse: c tathā hi yajñaḥ sarvapād duhe dātre ‘napasphuran //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a tisro jihvā varuṇasyāntar dīdyaty āsani /

Halfverse: c tāsām yā madhye rājati sā vaśā duṣpratigrahā //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a caturdhā reto abhavad vaśāyāḥ /

Halfverse: c āpas turīyam amṛtam turīyam yajñas turīyam paśavas turīyam //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a vaśā dyaur vaśā pṛthivī vaśā viṣṇuḥ prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c vaśāyā dugdham apibant sādhyā vasavaś ca ye //30//

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a vaśāyā dugdham pītvā sādhyā vasavaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c te vai bradhnasya viṣṭapi payo asyā upāsate //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a somam enām eke duhre ghṛtam eka upāsate /

Halfverse: c ya evam viduṣe vaśām dadus te gatās tridivam divaḥ //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a brāhmaṇebhyo vaśām dattvā sarvāml lokānt sam aśnute /

Halfverse: c ṛtam hy asyām ārpitam api brahmātho tapaḥ //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a vaśām devā upa jīvanti vaśām manuṣyā uta /

Halfverse: c vaśedam sarvam abhavad yāvat sūryo vipaśyati //34// {35}

## Book: 11

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agne jāyasvāditir nāthiteyam brahmaudanam pacati putrakāmā /

Halfverse: c saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛtas te tvā manthantu prajayā saheha //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kṛṇuta dhūmam vṛṣaṇaḥ sakhāyo ‘droghāvitā vācam acha /

Halfverse: c ayam agniḥ pṛtanāṣāt suvīro yena devā asahanta dasyūn //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a agne ‘janiṣṭhā mahate vīryāya brahmaudanāya paktave jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛtas te tvājījanann asyai rayim sarvavīram ni yacha //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a samiddho agne samidhā sam idhyasva vidvān devān yajñiyāṁ eha vakṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo haviḥ śrapayam jātaveda uttamam nākam adhi rohayemam //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tredhā bhāgo nihito yaḥ purā vo devānām pitṝṇām martyānām /

Halfverse: c amśān jānīdhvam vi bhajāmi tān vo yo devānām sa imām pārayāti //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a agne sahasvān abhibhūr abhīd asi nīco ny ḥbja dviṣataḥ sapatnān /

Halfverse: c iyam mātrā mīyamānā mitā ca sajātāms te balihṛtaḥ kṛṇotu //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sākam sajātaiḥ payasā sahaidhy ud ubjainām mahate vīryāya /

Halfverse: c ūrdhvo nākasyādhi roha viṣṭapam svargo loka iti yam vadanti //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a iyam mahī prati gṛhṇātu carma pṛthivī devī sumanasyamānā /

Halfverse: c atha gachema sukṛtasya lokam //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a etau grāvāṇau sayujā yuṅgdhi carmaṇi nirbindhy amśūn yajamānāya sādhu /

Halfverse: c nir avaghnatī ni jahi ya imām pṛtanyava ūrdhvam prajām udbharanty ud ūha //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a gṛhāṇa grāvāṇau sakṛtau vīra hasta ā te devā yajñiyā yajñam aguḥ /

Halfverse: c trayo varā yatamāms tvam vṛṇīṣe tās te samṛddhīr iha rādhayāmi // {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a iyam te dhītir idam u te janitram gṛhṇātu tvām aditiḥ śūraputrā /

Halfverse: c parā punīhi ya imām pṛtanyavo ‘syai rayim sarvavīram ni yacha //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a upaśvase druvaye sīdatā yūyam vi vicyadhvam yajñiyāsas tuṣaiḥ /

Halfverse: c śriyā samānān ati sarvānt syāmādhaspadam dviṣatas pādayāmi //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a parehi nāri punar ehi kṣipram apām tvā goṣṭho ‘dhy arukṣad bharāya /

Halfverse: c tāsām gṛhṇītād yatamā yajñiyā asan vibhājya dhīrītarā jahītāt //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a emā agur yoṣitaḥ śumbhamānā ut tiṣṭha nāri tavasam rabhasva /

Halfverse: c supatnī patyā prajayā prajāvaty ā tvāgan yajñaḥ prati kumbham gṛbhāya //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ūrjo bhāgo nihito yaḥ purā va ṛṣipraśiṣṭāpa ā bharaitāḥ /

Halfverse: c ayam yajo gātuvin nāthavit prajāvid ugraḥ paśuvid vīravid vo astu //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a agne carur yajñiyas tvādhy arukṣac chucis tapiṣṭhas tapasā tapainam /

Halfverse: c ārṣeyā daivā abhisamgatya bhāgam emam tapiṣṭhā ṛtubhis tapantu //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a śuddhāḥ putā yoṣito yajñiyā imā āpaś carum ava sarpantu śubhrāḥ /

Halfverse: c aduḥ prajām bahulān paśūn naḥ paktaudanasya sukṛtām etu lokam //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a brahmaṇā śuddhā uta pūtā ghṛtena somasyāmśavas taṇḍulā yajñiyā ime /

Halfverse: c apaḥ pra viśata prati gṛhṇātu vaś carur imam paktvā sukṛtām eta lokam //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a uruḥ prathasva mahatā mahimnā sahasrapṛṣṭhaḥ sukṛtasya loke /

Halfverse: c pitāmahāḥ pitaraḥ prajopajāham paktā pañcadaśas te asmi //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a sahasrapṛṣṭhaḥ śatadhāro akṣito brahmaudano devayānaḥ svargaḥ /

Halfverse: c amūms ta ā dadhāmi prajayā reṣayainān balihārāya mṛḍatān mahyam eva // {2}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a udehi vedim prajayā vardhayainām nudasva rakṣaḥ prataram dhehy enām /

Halfverse: c śriyā samānān ati sarvānt syāmādhaspadam dviṣatas pādayāmi //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a abhyāvartasva paśubhiḥ sahainām pratyaṅ enām devatābhiḥ sahaidhi /

Halfverse: c mā tvā prāpac chapatho mābhicāraḥ sve kṣetre anamīvā vi rāja //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ṛtena taṣṭā manasā hitaiṣā brahmaudanasya vihitā vedir agre /

Halfverse: c amsadrīm śuddhām upa dhehi nāri tatraudanam sādaya daivānām //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a aditer hastām srucam etām dvitīyām saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛto yām akṛṇvan /

Halfverse: c sā gātrāṇi viduṣy odanasya darvir vedyām adhy enam cinotu //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a śṛtam tvā havyam upa sīdantu daivā niḥsṛpyāgneḥ punar enān pra sīda /

Halfverse: c somena pūto jathare sīda brahmaṇām ārṣeyās te mā riṣan prāśitāraḥ //

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a soma rājant samjñānam ā vapaibhyaḥ subrāhmaṇā yatame tvopasīdān /

Halfverse: c ṛṣīn ārṣeyāms tapaso ‘dhi jātān brahmaudane suhavā johavīmi //

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a śuddhāḥ pūtā yosito yajñiyā imā brahmaṇām hasteṣu prapṛthaḥ sādayāmi /

Halfverse: c yatkāma idam abhiṣiñcāmi vo ‘ham indro marutvānt sa dadāt idam me //

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a idam me jyotir amṛtam hiraṇyam pakvam kṣetrāt kāmadughā ma eṣā /

Halfverse: c idam dhanam ni dadhe brāhmaṇeṣu kṛṇve panthām pitṛṣu yaḥ svargaḥ //

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a agnau tuṣān ā vapa jātavedasi paraḥ kambūkāṁ apa mṛḍḍhi dūram /

Halfverse: c etam śuśruma gṛharājasya bhāgam atho vidma nirṛter bhāgadheyam //

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a śrāmyataḥ pacato viddhi sunvataḥ panthām svargam adhi rohayainam /

Halfverse: c yena rohāt param āpadya yad vaya uttamam nākam paramam vyoma // {3}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a babhrer adhvaryo mukham etad vi mṛḍḍhy ājyāya lokam kṛṇuhi pravidvān /

Halfverse: c ghṛtena gātrānu sarvā vi mṛḍḍhi kṛṇve panthām pitṛṣu yaḥ svargaḥ //

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a babhre rakṣaḥ samadam ā vapaibhyo ‘brāhmaṇā yatame tvopasīdān /

Halfverse: c purīṣiṇaḥ prathamānāḥ purastād ārṣeyās te mā riṣan prāśitāraḥ //

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a ārṣeyeṣu ni dadha odana tvā nānārṣeyāṇām apy asty atra /

Halfverse: c agnir me goptā marutaś ca sarve viśve devā abhi rakṣantu pakvam //

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a yajñam duhānam sadam it prapīnam pumāmsam dhenum sadanam rayīṇām /

Halfverse: c prajāmṛtatvam uta dīrgham āyu rāyaś ca poṣair upa tvā sadema //

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a vṛṣabho ‘si svarga ṛṣīn ārṣeyān gacha /

Halfverse: c sukṛtām loke sīda tatra nau samskṛtam //

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a samācinuṣvānusamprayāhy agne pathaḥ kalpaya devayānān /

Halfverse: c etaiḥ sukṛtair anu gachema yajñam nāke tiṣṭhantam adhi saptaraśmau //

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a yena devā jyotiṣā dyām udāyan brahmaudanam paktvā sukṛtasya lokam /

Halfverse: c tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokam svar ārohanto abhi nākam uttamam // {4}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhavāśarvau mṛḍatam mābhi yātam bhūtapatī paśupatī namo vām /

Halfverse: c pratihitām āyatām mā vi srāṣṭam mā no himsiṣṭam dvipado mā catuṣpadaḥ //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śune kroṣṭre mā śarīrāṇi kartam aliklavebhyo gṛdhrebhyo ye ca kṛṣṇā aviṣyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c makṣikās te paśupate vayāmsi te vighase mā vidanta //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a krandāya te prāṇāya yāś ca te bhava ropayaḥ /

Halfverse: c namas te rudra kṛṇmaḥ sahasrākṣāyāmartya //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a purastāt te namaḥ kṛṇma uttarād adharād uta /

Halfverse: c abhīvargād divas pary antarikṣāya te namaḥ //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mukhāya te paśupate yāni cakṣūmṣi te bhava /

Halfverse: c tvace rūpāya samdṛśe pratīcīnāya te namaḥ //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a aṅgebhyas ta udarāya jihvāyā āsyāya te /

Halfverse: c dadbhyo gandhāya te namaḥ //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a astrā nīlaśikhaṇḍena sahasrākṣeṇa vājinā /

Halfverse: c rudreṇārdhakaghātinā tena mā sam arāmahi //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a sa no bhavaḥ pari vṛṇaktu viśvata āpa ivāgniḥ pari vṛṇaktu no bhavaḥ /

Halfverse: c mā no ‘bhi māmsta namo astv asmai //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a catur namo aṣṭakṛtvo bhavāya daśa kṛtvaḥ paśupate namas te /

Halfverse: c taveme pañca paśavo vibhaktā gāvo aśvāḥ puruṣā ajāvayaḥ //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tava catasraḥ pradiśas tava dyaus tava pṛthivī tavedam ugrorv antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c tavedam sarvam ātmanvad yat prāṇat pṛthivīm anu // {5}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a uruḥ kośo vasudhānas tavāyam yasminn imā viśvā bhuvanāny antaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa no mṛḍa paśupate namas te paraḥ kroṣṭāro abhibhāḥ śvānaḥ paro yantv agharudo vikeśyaḥ //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a dhanur bibharṣi haritam hiraṇyayam sahasraghni śatavadham śikhaṇḍin /

Halfverse: c rudrasyeṣuś carati devahetis tasyai namo yatamasyām diśītaḥ //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yo ‘bhiyāto nilayate tvām rudra nicikīrṣati /

Halfverse: c paścād anuprayuṅkṣe tam viddhasya padanīr iva //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a bhavārudrau sayujā samvidānāv ubhāv ugrau carato vīryāya /

Halfverse: c tabhyām namo yatamasyām diśītaḥ //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a namas te ‘stv āyate namo astu parāyate /

Halfverse: c namas te rudra tiṣṭhata āsīnāyota te namaḥ //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a namaḥ sāyam namaḥ prātar namo rātryā namo divā /

Halfverse: c bhavāya ca śarvāya cobhābhyām akaram namaḥ //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a sahasrākṣam atipaśyam purastād rudram asyantam bahudhā vipaścitam /

Halfverse: c mopārāma jihvayeyamānam //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a śyāvāśvam kṛṣṇam asitam mṛṇantam bhīmam ratham keśinaḥ pādayantam /

Halfverse: c pūrve pratīmo namo astv asmai //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a mā no ‘bhi srā matyam devahetim mā naḥ krudhaḥ paśupate namas te /

Halfverse: c anyatrāsmad divyām śākhām vi dhūnu //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a mā no himsīr adhi no brūhi pari ṇo vṛṅgdhi mā krudhaḥ /

Halfverse: c mā tvayā sam arāmahi // {6}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a mā no goṣu puruṣeṣu mā gṛdho no ajāviṣu /

Halfverse: c anyatrogra vi vartaya piyārūṇām prajām jahi //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yasya takmā kāsikā hetir ekam aśvasyeva vṛṣaṇaḥ kranda eti /

Halfverse: c abhipūrvam nirṇayate namo astv asmai //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yo ‘ntarikṣe tiṣṭhati viṣṭabhito ‘yajvanaḥ pramṛṇan devapīyūn /

Halfverse: c tasmai namo daśabhiḥ śakvarībhiḥ //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a tubhyam āraṇyāḥ paśavo mṛgā vane hitā hamsāḥ suparṇāḥ śakunā vayāmsi /

Halfverse: c tava yakṣam paśupate apsv antas tubhyam kṣaranti divyā āpo vṛdhe //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a śiśumārā ajagarāḥ purīkayā jaṣā matsyā rajasā yebhyo asyasi /

Halfverse: c na te dūram na pariṣṭhāsti te bhava sadyaḥ sarvān pari paśyasi bhūmim pūrvasmād dhamsy uttarasmin samudre //

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a mā no rudra takmanā mā viseṇa mā naḥ sam srā divyenāgninā /

Halfverse: c anyatrāsmad vidyutam pātayaitām //

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a bhavo divo bhava īśe pṛthivyā bhava ā papra urv antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c tasmai namo yatamasyām diśītaḥ //

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a bhava rajan yajamānāya mṛḍa paśūnām hi paśupatir babhūtha /

Halfverse: c yaḥ śraddadhāti santi devā iti catuṣpade dvipade ‘sya mṛḍa //

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a mā no mahāntam uta mā no arbhakam mā no vahantam uta mā no vakṣyataḥ /

Halfverse: c mā no himsīḥ pitaram mātaram ca svām tanvam rudra mā rīriso naḥ //

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a rudrasyailabakārebhyo ‘samsūktagilebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c idam mahāsyebhyaḥ śvabhyo akaram namaḥ //

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a namas te ghoṣiṇībhyo namas te keśinībhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c namo namaskṛtābhyo namaḥ sambhuñjatībhyaḥ /

Halfverse: e namas te deva senābhyaḥ svasti no abhayam ca naḥ // {7}

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tasyaudanasya bṛhaspatiḥ śiro brahma mukham /

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dyāvāpṛthivī śrotre sūryācandramasāv akṣiṇī saptaṛṣayaḥ prāṇāpānāḥ /

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a cakṣur musalam kāma ulūkhalam /

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ditiḥ śūrpam aditiḥ śūrpagrāhī vāto ‘pāvinak /

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a aśvāḥ kaṇā gāvas taṇḍulā maśakās tuṣāḥ /

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kabru phalīkaraṇāḥ śaro ‘bhram /

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śyāmam ayo ‘sya māmsāni lohitam asya lohitam /

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a trapu bhasma haritam varṇaḥ puṣkaram asya gandhaḥ /

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a khalaḥ pātram sphyāv amsāv īṣe anūkyṝ /

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a āntrāṇi jatravo gudā varatrāḥ /

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a iyam eva pṛthivī kumbhī bhavati rādhyamānasyaudanasya dyaur apidhānam /

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sītāḥ parśavaḥ sikatā ūbadhyam /

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ṛtam hastāvanejanam kulyopasecanam /

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ṛcā kumbhy adhihitārtvijyena preṣitā /

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a brahmaṇā parigṛhītā sāmnā paryūḍhā /

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a bṛhad āyavanam rathantaram darviḥ /

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ṛtavaḥ paktāra ārtavāḥ sam indhate /

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a carum pañcabilam ukham gharmo ‘bhīndhe /

\[note CORRIGENDA ed. ŚPP\]

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a odanena yajñavacaḥ sarve lokāḥ samāpyāḥ /

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yasmint samudro dyaur bhūmis trayo ‘varaparam śritāḥ /

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yasya devā akalpantocchiṣṭe ṣaḍ aśītayaḥ /

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a tam tvaudanasya pṛchāmi yo asya mahimā mahān /

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a tam tvaudanasya mahimānam vidyāt /

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a nālpa iti brūyān nānupasecana iti nedam ca kim ceti /

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yāvad dātābhimanasyeta tan nāti vadet /

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a brahmavādino vadanti parāñcam odanam prāśī3ḥ pratyañcā3m iti /

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a tvam odanam prāśī3s tvām odanā3 iti /

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a parāñcam cainam prāśīḥ prāṇās tvā hāsyantīty enam āha /

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a pratyañcam cainam prāśīr apānās tvā hāsyantīty enam āha /

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a naivāham odanam na mām odanaḥ /

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a odana evaudanam prāśīt / {8}

Verse: 32̮\[4̮1\]

Halfverse: a tatas cainam anyena śīrṣṇā prāśīr yena caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b jyeṣṭhatas te prajā mariṣyatīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d bṛhaspatinā śīrṣṇā /

Halfverse: e tenainam prāśiṣam tenainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[1\]

Verse: 33̮\[4̮2\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyābhyām śrotrābhyam prāśīr yābhyām caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b badhiro bhaviṣyasīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d dyāvāpṛthivībhyām śrotrābhyām /

Halfverse: e tābhyām enam prāśiṣam tābhyām enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[2\]

Verse: 34̮\[4̮3\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyābhyām akṣībhyām prāśīr yābhyām caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b andho bhaviṣyasīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d sūryācandramasābhyām akṣībhyām /

Halfverse: e tābhyām enam prāśiṣam tābhyām enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[3\]

Verse: 35̮\[4̮4\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyena mukhena prāśīr yena caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b mukhatas te prajā mariṣyatīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d brahmaṇā mukhena /

Halfverse: e tenainam prāśiṣam tenainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[4\]

Verse: 36̮\[4̮5\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyayā jihvayā prāśīr yayā caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b jihvā te mariṣyatīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d agner jihvayā /

Halfverse: e tayainam prāśiṣam tayainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[5\]

Verse: 37̮\[4̮6\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyair dantaiḥ prāśīr yaiś caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b dantās te śatsyantīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d ṛtubhir dantaiḥ /

Halfverse: e tair enam prāśiṣam tair enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[6\]

Verse: 38̮\[4̮7\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyaiḥ prāṇāpānaiḥ prāśīr yaiś caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b prāṇāpānās tvā hāsyantīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d saptaṛṣibhiḥ prāṇāpānaiḥ /

Halfverse: e tair enam prāśiṣam tair enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[7\]

Verse: 39̮\[4̮8\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyena vyacasā prāśīr yena caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b rājayakṣmas tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d antarikṣeṇa vyacasā /

Halfverse: e tenainam prāśiṣam tenainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[8\]

Verse: 40̮\[4̮9\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyena pṛṣṭhena prāśīr yena caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b vidyut tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d divā pṛṣṭhena /

Halfverse: e tenainam prāśiṣam tenainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[9\]

Verse: 41̮\[4̮10\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyenorasā prāśīr yena caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b kṛṣyā na rātsyasīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d pṛthivyorasā /

Halfverse: e tenainam prāśiṣam tenainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[10\]

Verse: 42̮\[4̮11\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyenodareṇa prāśīr yena caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b udaradāras tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d satyenodareṇa /

Halfverse: e tenainam prāśiṣam tenainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[11\]

Verse: 43̮\[4̮12\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyena vastinā prāśīr yena caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b apsu mariṣyasīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d samudreṇa vastinā /

Halfverse: e tenainam prāśiṣam tenainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[12\]

Verse: 44̮\[4̮13\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyābhyām ūrubhyām prāśīr yābhyām caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b ūrū te mariṣyata ity enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d mitrāvaruṇayor urubhyām /

Halfverse: e tābhyām enam prāśiṣam tābhyām enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[13\]

Verse: 45̮\[4̮14\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyābhyām aṣṭhīvadbhyām prāśīr yābhyām caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b srāmo bhaviṣyasīti enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d tvaṣṭur aṣṭhīvadbhyām /

Halfverse: e tābhyām enam prāśiṣam tābhyām enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[14\]

Verse: 46̮\[4̮15\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyābhyām pādābhyām prāśīr yābhyām caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b bahucārī bhaviṣyasīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d aśvinoḥ pādābhyām /

Halfverse: e tābhyām enam prāśiṣam tābhyām enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[15\]

Verse: 47̮\[4̮16\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyābhyām prapadābhyām prāśīr yābhyām caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b sarpas tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d savituḥ prapadābhyām /

Halfverse: e tābhyām enam prāśiṣam tābhyām enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[16\]

Verse: 48̮\[4̮17\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyābhyām hastābhyām prāśīr yābhyām caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b brāhmaṇam haniṣyasīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d savituḥ prapadābhyām /

Halfverse: e tābhyām enam prāśiṣam tābhyām enam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[17\]

Verse: 49̮\[4̮18\]

Halfverse: a tataś cainam anyayā pratiṣṭhayā prāśīr yayā caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan /

Halfverse: b apratiṣṭhāno ‘nāyatano mariṣyasīty enam āha /

Halfverse: c tam vā aham nārvāñcam na parāñcam na pratyañcam /

Halfverse: d satye pratiṣṭhāya /

Halfverse: e tayainam prāśiṣam tayainam ajīgamam /

Halfverse: f eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ /

Halfverse: g sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ sam bhavati ya evam veda / \[18\] {9}

Verse: 50̮\[5̮1\]

Halfverse: a etad vai bradhnasya viṣṭapam yad odanaḥ / \[1\]

Verse: 51̮\[5̮2\]

Halfverse: a bradhnaloko bhavati bradhnasya viṣṭapi śrayate ya evam veda / \[2\]

Verse: 52̮\[5̮3\]

Halfverse: a etasmād vā odanāt trayastrimśatam lokān nir amimīta prajāpatiḥ / \[3\]

Verse: 53̮\[5̮4\]

Halfverse: a teṣām prajñānāya yajñam asṛjata / \[4\]

Verse: 54̮\[5̮5\]

Halfverse: a sa ya evam viduṣa upadraṣṭā bhavati prāṇam ruṇaddhi / \[5\]

Verse: 55̮\[5̮6\]

Halfverse: a na ca prānam ruṇaddhi sarvajyānim jīyate / \[6\]

Verse: 56̮\[5̮7\]

Halfverse: a na ca sarvajyānim jīyate purainam jarasaḥ prāṇo jahāti / \[7\] {10}

Hymn: 4̮\[6\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prāṇāya namo yasya sarvam idam vaśe /

Halfverse: c yo bhūtaḥ sarvasyeśvaro yasmint sarvam pratiṣṭhitam //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a namas te prāṇa krandāya namas te stanayitnave /

Halfverse: c namas te prāṇa vidyute namas te prāṇa varṣate //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat prāṇa stanayitnunābhikrandaty oṣadhīḥ /

Halfverse: c pra vīyante garbhān dadhate ‘tho bahvīr vi jāyante //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yat prāṇa ṛtāv āgate ‘bhikrandaty oṣadhīḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvam tadā pra modate yat kim ca bhūmyām adhi //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yadā prāṇo abhyavarṣīd varṣeṇa pṛthivīm mahīm /

Halfverse: c paśavas tat pra modante maho vai no bhaviṣyati //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a abhivṛṣṭā oṣadhayaḥ prāṇena sam avādiran /

Halfverse: c āyur vai naḥ prātītaraḥ sarvā naḥ surabhīr akaḥ //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a namas te astv āyate namo astu parāyate /

Halfverse: c namas te prāṇa tiṣṭhata āsīnāyota te namaḥ //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a namas te prāṇa prāṇate namo astv apānate /

Halfverse: c parācīnāya te namaḥ pratīcīnāya te namaḥ sarvasmai ta idam namaḥ //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yā te prāṇa priyā tanūr yo te prāṇa preyasī /

Halfverse: c atho yad bheṣajam tava tasya no dhehi jīvase //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a prāṇaḥ prajā anu vaste pitā putram iva priyam /

Halfverse: c prāṇo ha sarvasyeśvaro yac ca prāṇati yac ca na // {11}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a prāṇo mṛtyuḥ prāṇas takmā prāṇam devā upāsate /

Halfverse: c prāṇo ha satyavādinam uttame loka ā dadhat //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a prāṇo virāṭ prāṇo deṣṭrī prānam sarva upāsate /

Halfverse: c prāṇo ha sūryaś candramāḥ prāṇam āhuḥ prajāpatim //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a prāṇāpānau vrīhiyavāv anaḍvān prāṇa ucyate /

Halfverse: c yave ha prāṇa āhito ‘pāno vrīhir ucyate //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a apānati prāṇati puruṣo garbhe antarā /

Halfverse: c yadā tvam prāṇa jinvasy atha sa jāyate punaḥ //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a prāṇam āhur mātariśvānam vāto ha prāṇa ucyate /

Halfverse: c prāṇe ha bhūtam bhavyam ca prāṇe sarvam pratiṣṭhitam //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ātharvaṇīr āṅgirasīr daivīr manuṣyajā uta /

Halfverse: c oṣadhayaḥ pra jāyante yadā tvam prāṇa jinvasi //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yadā prāṇo abhyavarṣīd varṣeṇa pṛthivīm mahīm /

Halfverse: c oṣadhayaḥ pra jāyante ‘tho yāḥ kāś ca vīrudhaḥ //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yas te prāṇedam veda yasmimś cāsi pratiṣṭhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarve tasmai balim harān amuṣmiml loka uttame //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yathā prāṇa balihṛtas tubhyam sarvāḥ prajā imāḥ /

Halfverse: c evā tasmai balim harān yas tvā śṛṇavat suśravaḥ //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a antar garbhaś carati devatāsv ābhūto bhūtaḥ sa u jāyate punaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa bhūto bhavyam bhaviṣyat pitā putram pra viveśā śacībhiḥ // {12}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a ekam pādam not khidati salilād dhamsa uccaran /

Halfverse: c yad aṅga sa tam utkhiden naivādya na śvaḥ syāt /

Halfverse: e na rātrī nāhaḥ syān na vy ḥchet kadā cana //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a aṣṭācakram vartata ekanemi sahasrākṣaram pra puro ni paścā /

Halfverse: c ardhena viśvam bhuvanam jajāna yad asyārdham katamaḥ sa ketuḥ //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yo asya viśvajanmana īśe viśvasya ceṣṭataḥ /

Halfverse: c anyeṣu kṣipradhanvane tasmai prāṇa namo ‘stu te //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yo asya sarvajanmana īśe sarvasya ceṣṭataḥ /

Halfverse: c atandro brahmaṇā dhīraḥ prāṇo mānu tiṣṭhātu //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a ūrdhvaḥ supteṣu jāgāra nanu tiryaṅ ni padyate /

Halfverse: c na suptam asya supteṣv anu śuśrāva kaś cana //

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a prāṇa mā mat paryāvṛto na mad anyo bhaviṣyasi /

Halfverse: c apām garbham iva jīvase prāṇa badhnāmi tvā mayi // {13}

Hymn: 5̮\[7\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a brahmacārīṣṇamś carati rodasī ubhe tasmin devāḥ sammanaso bhavanti /

Halfverse: c sa dādhāra pṛthivīm divam ca sa ācāryam tapasā piparti //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a brahmacāriṇam pitaro devajanāḥ pṛthag devā anusamyanti sarve /

Halfverse: c gandharvā enam anv āyan trayastrimśat triśatāh ṣaṭsahasrāḥ sarvānt sa devāms tapasā piparti //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ācārya upanayamāno brahmacāriṇam kṛṇute garbham antaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam rātrīs tisra udare bibharti tam jātam draṣṭum abhisamyanti devāḥ //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a iyam samit pṛthivī dyaur dvitīyotāntarikṣam samidhā pṛṇāti /

Halfverse: c brahmacārī samidhā mekhalayā śrameṇa lokāms tapasā piparti //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pūrvo jāto brahmaṇo brahmacārī gharmam vasānas tapasod atiṣṭhat /

Halfverse: c tasmāj jātam brāhmaṇam brahma jyeṣṭham devāś ca sarve amṛtena sākam //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a brahmacāry ṝti samidhā samiddhaḥ kārṣṇam vasāno dīkṣito dīrghaśmaśruḥ /

Halfverse: c sa sadya eti pūrvasmād uttaram samudram lokānt samgṛbhya muhur ācarikrat //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a brahmacārī janayan brahmāpo lokam prajāpatim parameṣṭhinam virājam /

Halfverse: c garbho bhūtvāmṛtasya yonāv indro ha bhūtvāsurāms tatarha //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ācāryas tatakṣa nabhasī ubhe ime urvī gambhīre pṛthivīm divam ca /

Halfverse: c te rakṣati tapasā brahmacārī tasmin devāḥ sammanaso bhavanti //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a imām bhūmim pṛthivīm brahmacārī bhikṣām ā jabhāra prathamo divam ca /

Halfverse: c te kṛtvā samidhāv upāste tayor ārpitā bhuvanāni viśvā //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a arvāg anyaḥ paro anyo divas pṛṣṭhād guhā nidhī nihitau brāhmaṇasya /

Halfverse: c tau rakṣati tapasā brahmacārī tat kevalam kṛṇute brahma vidvān // {14}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a arvāg anya ito anyaḥ pṛthivyā agnī sameto nabhasī antareme /

Halfverse: c tayoḥ śrayante raśmayo ‘dhi dṛḍhās tān ā tiṣṭhati tapasā brahmacārī //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a abhikrandan stanayann aruṇaḥ śitiṅgo bṛhac chepo ‘nu bhūmau jabhāra /

Halfverse: c brahmacārī siñcati sānau retaḥ pṛthivyām tena jīvanti pradiśaś catasraḥ //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a agnau sūrye candramasi mātariśvan brahmacāry apsu samidham ā dadhāti /

Halfverse: c tāsām arcīṣi pṛthag abhre caranti tāsām ājyam puruṣo varṣam āpaḥ //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ācāryo mṛtyur varuṇaḥ somo oṣadhayaḥ payaḥ /

Halfverse: c jībhūtā āsant satvānas tair idam svar ābhṛtam //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a amā ghṛtam kṛṇute kevalam ācāryo bhūtvā varuṇo yadyad aichat prajāpatau /

Halfverse: c tad brahmacārī prāyachat svān mitro adhy ātmanaḥ //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ācāryo brahmacārī brahmacarī prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c prajāpatir vi rājati virāḍ indro ‘bhavad vaśī //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a brahmacaryeṇa tapasā rājā rāṣṭram vi rakṣati /

Halfverse: c ācāryo brahmacaryeṇa brahmacāriṇam ichate //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a brahmacaryeṇa kanyā yuvānam vindate patim /

Halfverse: c anaḍvān brahmacaryeṇāśvo ghāsam jigīrṣati //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a brahmacaryeṇa tapasā devā mṛtyum apāghnata /

Halfverse: c indro ha brahmacaryeṇa devebhyaḥ svar ābharat //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a oṣadhayo bhūtabhavyam ahorātre vanaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c samvatsaraḥ saha rtubhis te jātā brahmacāriṇaḥ // {15}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a pārthivā divyāḥ paśava āraṇyā grāmyāś ca ye /

Halfverse: c apakṣāḥ pakṣiṇaś ca ye te jātā brahmacāriṇaḥ //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a pṛthak sarve prājāpatyāḥ prāṇān ātmasu bibhrati /

Halfverse: c tānt sarvān brahma rakṣati brahmacāriṇy ābhṛtam //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a devānām etat pariṣūtam anabhyārūḍham carati rocamānam /

Halfverse: c tasmāj jātam brāhmaṇam brahma jyeṣṭham devāś ca sarve amṛtena sākam //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a brahmacārī brahma bhrājad bibharti tasmin devā adhi viśve samotāḥ /

Halfverse: c prāṇāpānau janayann ād vyānam vācam mano hṛdayam brahma medhām //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a cakṣuḥ śrotram yaśo asmāsu dhehy annam reto lohitam udaram /

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a tāni kalpan brahmacārī salilasya pṛṣṭhe tapo ‘tiṣṭhat tapyamānaḥ samudre /

Halfverse: c sa snāto babhruḥ piṅgalaḥ pṛthivyām bahu rocate // {16}

Hymn: 6̮\[8\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnim brūmo vanaspatīn oṣadhīr uta vīrudhaḥ /

Halfverse: c indram bṛhaspatim sūryam te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a brūmo rājānam varuṇam mitram viṣṇum atho bhagam /

Halfverse: c amśam vivasvantam brūmas te no muñcantv amhasah //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a brūmo devam savitāram dhātāram uta pūṣaṇam /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭāram agriyam brūmas te no muñcantv amhasah //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a gandharvāpsaraso brūmo aśvinā brahmaṇas patim /

Halfverse: c aryamā nāma yo devas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ahorātre idam brūmaḥ sūryācandramasāv ubhā /

Halfverse: c viśvān ādityān brūmas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a vātam brūmaḥ parjanyam antarikṣam atho diśaḥ /

Halfverse: c āśāś ca sarvā brūmas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a muñcantu mā śapathyād ahorātre atho uṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c somo mā devo muñcatu yam āhuś candramā iti //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a pārthivā divyaḥ paśava āraṇyā uta ye mṛgāḥ /

Halfverse: c śakuntān pakṣino brūmas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a bhavāśarvāv idam brūmo rudram paśupatiś ca yaḥ /

Halfverse: c iṣūr yā eṣām samvidma tā naḥ santu sadā śivāḥ //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a divam brūmo nakṣatrāṇi bhūmim yakṣāṇi parvatān /

Halfverse: c samudrā nadyo veśantās te no muñcantv amhasaḥ // {17}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a saptarṣīn vā idam brūmo ‘po devīḥ prajāpatim /

Halfverse: c pitṝn yamaśreṣṭhān brūmas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ye devā diviṣado antarikṣasadaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c pṛthivyām śakrā ye śritās te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ādityā rudrā vasavo divi devā atharvānaḥ /

Halfverse: c aṅgiraso manīṣiṇas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yajñam brūmo yajamānam ṛcaḥ sāmāni bheṣajā /

Halfverse: c yajūmṣi hotrā brūmas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a pañca rājyāni vīrudhām somaśreṣṭhāni brūmaḥ /

Halfverse: c darbho bhaṅgo yavaḥ sahas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a arāyān brūmo rakṣāmsi sarpān puṇyajanān pitṝn /

Halfverse: c mṛtyūn ekaśatam brūmas te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ṛtūn brūma ṛtupatīn ārtavān uta hāyanān /

Halfverse: c samāḥ samvatsarān māsāms te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a eta devā dakṣiṇataḥ paścāt prāñca udeta /

Halfverse: c purastād uttarāc chakrā viśve devāḥ sametya te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a viśvān devān idam brūmaḥ satyasamdhān ṛtavṛdhaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvābhiḥ patnībhiḥ saha te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a sarvān devān idam brūmaḥ satyasamdhān ṛtāvṛdhaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvābhiḥ patnībhiḥ saha te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a bhūtam brūmo bhūtapatim bhūtānām uta yo vaśī /

Halfverse: c bhūtāni sarvā samgatya te no muñcantv amhasaḥ //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yā devīḥ pañca pradiśo ye devā dvādaśa rtavah /

Halfverse: c samvatsarasya ye damṣṭrās te naḥ santu sadā śivāḥ //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yan mātalī rathakrītam amṛtam veda bheṣajam /

Halfverse: c tad indro apsu prāveśayat tad āpo datta bheṣajam // {18}

Hymn: 7̮\[9\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ucchiṣṭe nāma rūpam cocchiṣṭe loka āhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭa indraś cāgniś ca viśvam antaḥ samāhitam //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ucchiṣṭe dyāvāpṛthivī viśvam bhūtam samāhitam /

Halfverse: c āpaḥ samudra ucchiṣṭe candramā vāta āhitaḥ //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sann ucchiṣṭe asamś ca ubhau mṛtyur vājaḥ prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c laikyā ucchiṣṭa āyattā vraś ca draś cāpi śrīr mayi //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a dṛḍho dṛmhasthiro nyo brahma viśvasṛjaḥ daśa /

Halfverse: c nābhim iva sarvataḥ cakram ucchiṣṭe devatās śritāḥ //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ṛk sāma yajur ucchiṣṭa udgīthaḥ prastutam stutam /

Halfverse: c hiṅkāra ucchiṣṭe svaraḥ sāmno mediś ca tan mayi //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a aindrāgnam pāvamānam mahānāmnīr mahāvratam /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭe yajñasyāṅgāny antar garbha iva mātari //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a rājasūyam vājapeyam agniṣṭomaḥ tad adhvaraḥ /

Halfverse: c arkāśvamedhāv ucchiṣṭe jīvabarhir madintamaḥ //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a agnyādheyam atho dīkṣā kāmapraś chandasā saha /

Halfverse: c utsannā yajñāḥ satrāṇy ucchiṣṭe ‘dhi samāhitāḥ //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a agnihotram ca śraddhā ca vaṣaṭkāro vratam tapaḥ /

Halfverse: c dakṣiṇeṣṭam pūrtam cocchiṣṭe ‘dhi samāhitāḥ //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ekarātro dvirātraḥ sadyaḥkrīḥ prakrīr ukthyaḥ /

Halfverse: c otam nihitam ucchiṣṭe yajñasyāṇūni vidyayā // {19}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a catūrātraḥ pañcarātraḥ ṣaḍrātraś cobhayaḥ saha /

Halfverse: c ṣoḍaśī saptarātraś cocchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve ye yajñā amṛte hitāḥ //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a pratīhāro nidhanam viśvajic cābhijic ca yaḥ /

Halfverse: c sāhnātirātrāv ucchiṣṭe dvādaśāho ‘pi tan mayi //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sūnṛtā samnatiḥ kṣemaḥ svadhorjāmṛtam sahaḥ /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭe sarve pratyañcaḥ kāmāḥ kāmena tatṛpuh //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a nava bhūmīḥ samudrā ucchisṭe adhi śritā divaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā sūryo bhāty ucchiṣṭe ‘horātre api tan mayi //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a upahavyam viṣūvantam ye ca yajñā guhā hitāḥ /

Halfverse: c bibharti bhartā viśvasyocchiṣṭo janituḥ pitā //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a pitā janitur ucchisṭo ‘soḥ pautraḥ pitāmahaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa kṣiyati viśvasyeśāno vṛṣā bhūmyām atighnyaḥ //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ṛtam satyam tapo rāsṭram śramo dharmaś ca karma ca /

Halfverse: c bhūtam bhaviṣyad ucchiṣṭe vīryam lakṣmīr balam bale //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a samṛddhir oja ākutiḥ kṣatram rāṣṭram ṣaḍ urvyaḥ /

Halfverse: c samvatsaro ‘dhy ucchista iḍā praiṣā grahā haviḥ //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a caturhotāra āpriyaś cāturmāsyāni nīvidaḥ /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭe yajñāḥ hotrāḥ paśubandhās tad iṣṭayaḥ //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ardhamāsāś ca māsāś cārtavā ṛtubhiḥ saha /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭe ghoṣiṇīr āpaḥ stanayitnuḥ śrutir mahī // {20}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a śarkarāḥ sikatā aśmāna oṣadhayo vīrudhas tṛṇā /

Halfverse: c abhrāṇi vidyuto varṣam ucchiṣṭe samśritā śritā //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a rāddhiḥ prāptiḥ samāptir vyāptir maha edhatuḥ /

Halfverse: c atyāptir ucchiṣṭe bhūtiś cāhitā nihitā hitā //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yac ca prāṇati prāṇena yac ca paśyati cakṣuṣā /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ṛcaḥ sāmāni chandāmsi purāṇam yajuṣā saha /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotram akṣitiś ca kṣitiś ca yā /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ //

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a ānandā modāḥ pramudo ‘bhimodamudaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ //

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā gandharvāpsarasaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c ucchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ // {21}

Hymn: 8̮\[10\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yan manyur jāyām āvahat samkalpasya gṛhād adhi /

Halfverse: c ka āsam janyāḥ ke varāḥ ka u jyeṣṭhavaro ‘bhavat //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tapaś caivāstām karma cāntar mahaty arṇave /

Halfverse: c ta āsam janyās te varā brahma jyeṣṭhavaro ‘bhavat //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a daśa sākam ajāyanta devā devebhyaḥ purā /

Halfverse: c yo vai tān vidyāt pratyakṣam sa vā adya mahad vadet //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotram akṣitiś ca kṣitiś ca yā /

Halfverse: c vyānodānau vāṅ manas te vā ākūtim āvahan //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ajātā āsann ṛtavo ‘tho dhātā bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī aśvinā tarhi kam te jyeṣṭham upāsata //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tapaś caivāstām karma cāntar mahaty arṇave /

Halfverse: c tapo ha jajñe karmaṇas tat te jyeṣṭham upāsata //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yeta āsīd bhūmiḥ pūrvā yām addhātaya id viduḥ /

Halfverse: c yaḥ vai tām vidyān nāmathā sa manyeta purāṇavit //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a kuta indraḥ kutaḥ somaḥ kuto agnir ajāyata /

Halfverse: c kutas tvaṣṭā sam abhavat kuto dhātājāyata //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a indrād indraḥ somāt somo agner agnir ajāyata /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭā ha jajñe tvaṣṭur dhātur dhātājāyata //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ye ta āsan daśa jātā devā devebhyaḥ purā /

Halfverse: c putrebhyo lokam dattvā kasmims te loka āsate // {22}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yadā keśān asthi snāva māmsam majjānam ābharat /

Halfverse: c śarīram kṛtvā pādavat kam lokam anu prāviśat //

Verse: 12
