Halfverse: a kutaḥ keśān kutaḥ snāva kuto asthīny ābharat /

Halfverse: c aṅgā parvāṇi majjānam ko māmsam kuta ābharat //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a samsico nāma te devā ye sambhārānt samabharan /

Halfverse: c sarvam samsicya martyam devāḥ puruṣam āviśan //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ūrū pādāv aṣṭhīvantau śiro hastāv atho mukham /

Halfverse: c pṛṣṭīr barjahye pārśve kas tat sam adadhād ṛṣiḥ //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a śiro hastāv atho mukham jihvām grīvāś ca kīkasāḥ /

Halfverse: c tvacā prāvṛtya sarvam tat samdhā sam adadhān mahī //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yat tac charīram aśayat samdhayā samhitam mahat /

Halfverse: c yenedam adya rocate ko asmin varṇam ābharat //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a sarve devā upāśikṣan tad ajānād vadhūḥ satī /

Halfverse: c īśā vaśasya yā jāyā sāsmin varṇam ābharat //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yadā tvaṣṭā vyatṛṇat pitā tvaṣṭur ya uttaraḥ /

Halfverse: c gṛham kṛtvā martyam devāḥ puruṣam āviśan //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a svapno vai tandrīr nirṛtiḥ pāpmāno nāma devatāḥ /

Halfverse: c jarā khālatyam pālityam śarīram anu prāviśan //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a steyam duṣkṛtam vṛjinam satyam yajño yaśo bṛhat /

Halfverse: c balam ca kṣatram ojaś ca śarīram anu prāviśan // {23}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a bhūtiś ca vā abhūtiś ca rātayo ‘rātayaś ca yāḥ /

Halfverse: c kṣudhaś ca sarvās tṛṣṇāś ca śarīram anu prāviśan //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a nindāś ca vā anindāś ca yac ca hanteti neti ca /

Halfverse: c śarīram śraddhā dakṣiṇāśraddhā cānu prāviśan //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a vidyāś ca vā avidyāś ca yac cānyad upadeśyam /

Halfverse: c śarīram brahma prāviśad ṛcaḥ sāmātho yajuḥ //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ānandā modāḥ pramudo ‘bhimodamudaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c haso nariṣṭā nṛttāni śarīram anu prāviśan //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a ālāpāś ca pralāpāś cābhilāpalapaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c śarīram sarvam prāviśann āyujaḥ prayujo yujaḥ //

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotram akṣitiś ca kṣitiś ca yā /

Halfverse: c vyānodānau vān manaḥ śarīreṇa ta īyante //

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a āśiṣaś ca praśiṣaś ca samśiṣo viśiṣaś ca yāḥ /

Halfverse: c cittāni sarve samkalpāḥ śarīram anu prāviśan //

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a āsteyīś ca vāsteyīś ca tvaraṇāḥ kṛpaṇāś ca yāḥ /

Halfverse: c guhāḥ śukrā sthūlā apas tā bībhatsāv asādayan //

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a asthi kṛtvā ṣamidham tad aṣṭa āpo asādayan /

Halfverse: c retaḥ kṛtivājyam devāḥ puruṣam āviśan //

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a yā āpo yāś ca devatā yā virāṭ brahmaṇā saha /

Halfverse: c śarīram brahma prāviśac charīre ‘dhi prajāpatiḥ //

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a sūryaś cakṣur vātaḥ prāṇam puruṣasya vi bhejire /

Halfverse: c athāsyetaram ātmānam devāḥ prāyachann agnaye //

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a tasmād vai vidvān puruṣam idam brahmeti manyate /

Halfverse: c sarvā hy asmin devatā gāvo goṣṭha ivāsate //

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a prathamena pramāreṇa tredhā viṣvaṅ vi gachati /

Halfverse: c ada ekena gachaty ada ekena gachaty ihaikena ni ṣevate //

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a apsu stīmāsu vṛddhāsu śarīram antarā hitam /

Halfverse: c tasmim chavo ‘dhi antarā tasmāc chavo ‘dhy ucyate // {24}

Hymn: 9̮\[11\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ye bāhavo yā iṣavo dhanvanām vīryāṇi ca /

Halfverse: c asīn paraśūn āyudham cittākūtam ca yad dhṛdi /

Halfverse: e sarvam tad arbude tvam amitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāmś ca pra darśaya //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭhata sam nahyadhvam mitrā devajanā yūyam /

Halfverse: c samdṛṣṭā guptā vaḥ santu yā no mitrāṇy arbude //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭhatam ā rabhetām ādānasamdānābhyām /

Halfverse: c amitrāṇām senā abhi dhattam arbude //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a arbudir nāma yo deva īśānaś ca nyarbudiḥ /

Halfverse: c yābhyām antarikṣam āvṛtam iyam ca pṛthivī mahī /

Halfverse: e tābhyām indramedibhyām aham jitam anv emi senayā //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭha tvam devajanārbude senayā saha /

Halfverse: c bhañjann amitrāṇām senām bhogebhiḥ pari vāraya //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sapta jātān nyarbuda udārāṇām samīkṣayan /

Halfverse: c tebhiṣ ṭvam ājye hute sarvair ut tiṣṭha senayā //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a pratighnānāśrumukhī kṛdhukarṇī ca krośatu /

Halfverse: c vikeśī puruṣe hate radite arbude tava //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a samkarṣantī karūkaram manasā putram ichantī /

Halfverse: c patim bhrātaram āt svān radite arbude tava //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a aliklavā jāṣkamadā gṛdhrāḥ śyenāḥ patatriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c dhvāṅkṣāḥ śakunayas tṛpyantv amitreṣu samīkṣayan radite arbude tava //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a atho sarvam śvāpadam makṣikā tṛpyatu krimiḥ /

Halfverse: c pauruṣeye ‘dhi kuṇape radite arbude tava // {25}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ā gṛhṇītam sam bṛhatam prānāpānān nyarbude /

Halfverse: c nivāśā ghoṣāḥ sam yantv amitreṣu samīkṣayan radite arbude tava //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ud vepaya sam vijantām bhiyāmitrānt sam sṛja /

Halfverse: c urugrāhair bāhvaṅkair vidhyāmitrān nyarbude //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a muhyantv eṣām bāhavaś cittākūtam ca yad dhṛdi /

Halfverse: c maiṣām uc cheṣi kim cana radite arbude tava //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a pratighnānāḥ sam dhāvantūraḥ paṭūrāv āghnānāḥ /

Halfverse: c aghāriṇīr vikeśyo rudatyaḥ puruṣe hate radite arbude tava //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a śvanvatīr apsaraso rūpakā utārbude /

Halfverse: c antaḥpātre rerihatīm riśām durṇihitaiṣiṇīm /

Halfverse: e sarvās tā arbude tvam amitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāmś ca pra darśaya //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a khadūre ‘dhicaṅkramām kharvikām kharvavāsinīm /

Halfverse: c ya udārā antarhitā gandharvāpsarasaś ca ye sarpā itarajanā rakṣāmsi //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a caturdamṣṭrām chyāvadataḥ kumbhamuṣkāṁ asṛṅmukhān /

Halfverse: c svabhyasā ye codbhyasāḥ //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a ud vepaya tvam arbude ‘mitrāṇām amūḥ sicaḥ /

Halfverse: c jayāmś ca jiṣṇuś cāmitrāṁ jayatām indramedinau //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a prablīno mṛditaḥ śayām hato ‘mitro nyarbude /

Halfverse: c agnijihvā dhūmaśikhā jayantīr yantu senayā //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a tayārbude praṇuttānām indro hantu varamvaram /

Halfverse: c amitrāṇām śacīpatir māmīṣām moci kaś cana // {26}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a ut kasantu hṛdayāny ūrdhvaḥ prāṇa ud īṣatu /

Halfverse: c śauṣkāsyam anu vartatām amitrān mota mitriṇaḥ //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a ye ca dhīrā ye cādhīrāḥ parāñco badhirāś ca ye /

Halfverse: c tamasā ye ca tūparā atho bastābhivāsinaḥ /

Halfverse: e sarvāms tām arbude tvam amitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāmś ca pra darśaya //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a arbudiś ca triṣandhiś cāmitrān no vi vidhyatām /

Halfverse: c yathaiṣām indra vṛtrahan hanāma śacīpate ‘mitrāṇām sahasraśaḥ //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a vanaspatīn vānaspatyān oṣadhīr uta vīrudhaḥ /

Halfverse: c gandharvāpsarasaḥ sarpān devān puṇyajanān pitṝn /

Halfverse: e sarvāms tāṁ arbude tvam amitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāmś ca pra darśaya //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a īśām vo maruto deva ādityo brahmaṇas patiḥ /

Halfverse: c īśām va indraś cāgniś ca dhātā mitraḥ prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: e īśām va ṛṣayaś cakrur amitreṣu samīkṣayan radite arbude tava //

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a teṣām sarveṣām īśānā ut tiṣṭhata sam nahyadhvam mitrā devajanā yūyam /

Halfverse: c imam samgrāmam samjitya yathālokam vi tiṣṭhadhvam // {27}

Hymn: 10̮\[12\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭhata sam nahyadhvam udārāḥ ketubhiḥ saha /

Halfverse: c sarpā itarajanā rakṣāmsy amitrān anu dhāvata //

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a īśām vo veda rājyam triṣandhe aruṇaiḥ ketubhiḥ saha /

Halfverse: c ye antarikṣe ye divi pṛthivyām ye ca manavāḥ /

Halfverse: e triṣandhes te cetasi durṇāmāna upāsatām //

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ayomukhāḥ sūcīmukhā atho vikaṅkatīmukhāḥ /

Halfverse: c kravyādo vātaramhasaḥ ā sajantv amitrān vajreṇa triṣandhinā //

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a antar dhehi jātaveda āditya kuṇapam bahu /

Halfverse: c trisandher iyam senā suhitāstu me vaśe //

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭha tvam devajanārbude senayā saha /

Halfverse: c ayam balir va āhutas triṣandher āhutiḥ priyā //

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śitipadī sam dyatu śaravyṝyam catuṣpadī /

Halfverse: c kṛtye ‘mitrebhyo bhava triṣandheḥ saha senayā //

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a dhūmākṣī sam patatu kṛdhukarnī ca krośatu /

Halfverse: c triṣandheḥ senayā jite aruṇāḥ santu ketavaḥ //

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a avāyantām pakṣiṇo ye vayāmsy antarikṣe divi ye caranti /

Halfverse: c śvāpado makṣikāḥ sam rabhantām āmādo gṛdhrāḥ kuṇape radantām //

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yām indreṇa samdhām samadhatthā brahmaṇā ca bṛhaspate /

Halfverse: c tayāham indrasamdhayā sarvān devān iha huva ito jayata māmutaḥ //

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir āṅgirasa ṛṣayo brahmasamśitāḥ /

Halfverse: c asurakṣayaṇam vadham triṣandhim divy āsrayan // {28}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yenāsau gupta āditya ubhāv indraś ca tiṣṭhataḥ /

Halfverse: c triṣandhim devā abhajantaujase ca balāya ca //

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sarvāml lokānt sam ajayan devā āhutyānayā /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir āṅgiraso vajram yam asiñcatāsuraksayaṇam vadham //

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir āṅgiraso vajram yam asiñcatāsurakṣayaṇam vadham /

Halfverse: c tenāham amūm senām ni limpāmi bṛhaspate ‘mitrān hanmy ojasā //

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a sarve devā atyāyanti ye aśnanti vaṣaṭkṛtam /

Halfverse: c imām juṣadhvam āhutim ito jayata māmutaḥ //

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a sarve devā atyāyantu triṣandher āhutiḥ priyā /

Halfverse: c samdhām mahatīm rakṣata yayāgre asurā jitāh //

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a vāyur amitrāṇām iṣvagrāṇy āñcatu /

Halfverse: c indra eṣām bahūn prati bhanaktu mā śakan pratidhām iṣum /

Halfverse: e āditya eṣām astram vi nāśayatu candramā yutām agatasya panthām //

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yadi preyur devapurā brahma varmāṇi cakrire /

Halfverse: c tanūpānam paripāṇam kṛṇvānā yad upocire sarvam tad arasam kṛdhi //

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a kravyādānuvartayan mṛtyunā ca purohitam /

Halfverse: c triṣandhe prehi senayā jayāmitrān pra padyasva //

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a triṣandhe tamasā tvam amitrān pari vāraya /

Halfverse: c pṛṣadājyapraṇuttānām māmīṣām moci kaś cana //

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a śitipadī sam patatv amitrānām amūḥ śicaḥ /

Halfverse: c muhyantv adyāmūḥ senāḥ amitrānām nyarbude // {29}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a mūḍhā amitrā nyarbude jahy ṝṣām varamvaram /

Halfverse: c anayā jahi senayā //

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yaś ca kavacī yaś cākavaco ‘mitro yaś cājmani /

Halfverse: c jyāpāśaiḥ kavacapāśair ajmanābhihataḥ śayām //

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ye varmiṇo ye ‘varmāṇo amitrā ye ca varmiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvāms tāṁ arbude hatām chvāno ‘dantu bhūmyām //

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ye rathino ye arathā asādā ye ca sādinaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvān adantu tān hatān gṛdhrāḥ śyenāḥ patatriṇaḥ //

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a sahasrakuṇapā śetām āmitrī senā samare vadhānām /

Halfverse: c vividdhā kakajākṛtā //

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a marmāvidham roruvatam suparṇair adantu duścitam mṛditam śayānam /

Halfverse: c ya imām pratīcīm āhutim amitro no yuyutsati //

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a yām devā anutisṭhanti yasyā nāsti virādhanam /

Halfverse: c tayendro hantu vṛtrahā vajreṇa triṣamdhinā //

## Book: 12

12

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a satyam bṛhad ṛtam ugram dīkṣā tapo brahma yajñaḥ pṛthivīm dhārayanti /

Halfverse: c sā no bhūtasya bhavyasya patny urum lokam pṛthivī naḥ kṛṇotu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asambādham madhyato mānavānām yasyā udvataḥ pravataḥ samam bahu /

Halfverse: c nānāvīryā oṣadhīr yā bibharti pṛthivī naḥ prathatām rādhyatām naḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yasyām samudra uta sindhur āpo yasyām annam kṛṣṭayaḥ sambabhūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c yasyām idam jinvati prāṇad ejat sā no bhūmiḥ pūrvapeye dadhātu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yasyāś catasraḥ pradiśaḥ pṛthivyā yasyām annam kṛṣṭayaḥ sambabhūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c yā bibharti bahudhā prāṇad ejat sā no bhūmir goṣv apy anne dadhātu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasyām pūrve pūrvajanā vicakrire yasyām devā asurān abhyavartayan /

Halfverse: c gavām aśvānām vayasaś ca viṣṭhā bhagam varcaḥ pṛthivī no dadhātu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a viśvambharā vasudhānī pratiṣṭhā hiraṇyavakṣā jagato niveśanī /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānaram bibhratī bhūmir agnim indraṛṣabhā draviṇe no dadhātu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yām rakṣanty asvapnā viśvadānīm devā bhūmim pṛthivīm apramādam /

Halfverse: c sā no madhu priyam duhām atho ukṣatu varcasā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yārṇave ‘dhi salilam agra āsīt yām māyābhir anvacaran manīṣiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasyā hṛdayam parame vyomant satyenāvṛtam amṛtam pṛthivyāḥ /

Halfverse: e sā no bhūmis tviṣim balam rāṣṭre dadhātūttame //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yasyām āpaḥ paricarāḥ samānīr ahorātre apramādam kṣaranti /

Halfverse: c sā no bhūmir bhūridhārā payo duhām atho ukṣatu varcasā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yām aśvināv amimātām viṣṇur yasyām vicakrame /

Halfverse: c indro yām cakra ātmane ‘namitrām śacīpatiḥ /

Halfverse: e sā no bhūmir vi sṛjatām mātā putrāya me payaḥ //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a girayas te parvatā himavanto ‘raṇyam te pṛthivi syonam astu /

Halfverse: c babhrum kṛṣṇām rohiṇīm viśvarūpām dhruvām bhūmim pṛthivīm indraguptām /

Halfverse: e ajīto ‘hato akṣato ‘dhy aṣṭhām pṛthivīm aham //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yat te madhyam pṛthivi yac ca nabhyam yās ta ūrjas tanvaḥ sambabhūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c tāsu no dhehy abhi naḥ pavasva mātā bhūmiḥ putro aham pṛthivyāḥ parjanyaḥ pitā sa u naḥ pipartu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yasyām vedim parigṛhṇanti bhūmyām yasyām yajñam tanvate viśvakarmāṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasyām mīyante svaravaḥ pṛthivyām ūrdhvāḥ śukrā āhutyāḥ purastāt /

Halfverse: e sā no bhūmir vardhayad vardhamānā //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yo no dveṣat pṛthivi yaḥ pṛtanyād yo ‘bhidāsān manasā yo vadhena /

Halfverse: c tam no bhūme randhaya pūrvakṛtvari //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a tvaj jātās tvayi caranti martyās tvam bibharṣi dvipadas tvam catuṣpadaḥ /

Halfverse: c taveme pṛthivi pañca mānavā yebhyo jyotir amṛtam martyebhya udyant sūryo raśmibhir ātanoti //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a tā naḥ prajāḥ sam duhratām samagrā vāco madhu pṛthivi dhehi mahyam //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a viśvasvam mātaram oṣadhīnām dhruvām bhūmim pṛthivīm dharmaṇā dhṛtām /

Halfverse: c śivām syonām anu carema viśvahā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a mahat sadhastham mahatī babhūvitha mahān vega ejathur vepathuṣ ṭe /

Halfverse: c mahāms tvendro rakṣaty apramādam /

Halfverse: e sā no bhūme pra rocaya hiraṇyasyeva samdṛśi mā no dvikṣata kaś cana //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a agnir bhūmyām oṣadhīṣv agnim āpo bibhraty agnir aśmasu /

Halfverse: c agnir antaḥ puruṣeṣu goṣv aśveṣv agnayaḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a agnir diva ā tapaty agner devasyorv a1ntarikṣam /

Halfverse: c agnim martāsa indhate havyavāham ghṛtapriyam //20// {2}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a agnivāsāḥ pṛthivy asitajñūs tviṣīmantam samśitam mā kṛṇotu //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a bhūmyām devebhyo dadati yajñam havyam aramkṛtam /

Halfverse: c bhūmyām manuṣyā jīvanti svadhayānnena martyāḥ /

Halfverse: e sā no bhūmiḥ prāṇam āyur dadhātu jaradaṣṭim mā pṛthivī kṛṇotu //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a yas te gandhaḥ pṛthivi sambabhūva yam bibhraty oṣadhayo yam āpaḥ /

Halfverse: c yam gandharvā apsarasaś ca bhejire tena mā surabhim kṛṇu mā no dvikṣata kaś cana //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yas te gandhaḥ puṣkaram āviveśa yam samjabhruḥ sūryāyā vivāhe /

Halfverse: c amartyāḥ pṛthivi gandham agre tena mā surabhim kṛṇu mā no dvikṣata kaś cana //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yas te gandhaḥ puruṣeṣu strīṣu pumsu bhago ruciḥ /

Halfverse: c yo aśveṣu vīreṣu yo mṛgeṣūta hastiṣu /

Halfverse: e kanyāyām varco yad bhūme tenāsmāṁ api sam sṛja mā no dvikṣata kaś cana //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a śilā bhūmir aśmā pāmsuḥ sā bhūmiḥ samdhṛtā dhṛtā /

Halfverse: c tasyai hiraṇyavakṣase pṛthivyā akaram namaḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a yasyām vṛkṣā vānaspatyā dhruvās tiṣṭhanti viśvahā /

Halfverse: c pṛthivīm viśvadhāyasam dhṛtām achāvadāmasi //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a udīrāṇā utāsīnās tiṣṭhantaḥ prakrāmantaḥ /

Halfverse: c padbhyām dakṣiṇasavyābhyām mā vyathiṣmahi bhūmyām //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a vimṛgvarīm pṛthivīm ā vadāmi kṣamām bhūmim brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānām /

Halfverse: c ūrjam puṣṭam bibhratīm annabhāgam ghṛtam tvābhi ni ṣīdema bhūme //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a śuddhā na āpas tanvṝ kṣarantu yo naḥ sedur apriye tam ni dadhmaḥ /

Halfverse: c pavitreṇa pṛthivi mot punāmi //30// {3}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a yās te prācīḥ pradiśo yā udīcīr yās te bhūme adharād yāś ca paścāt /

Halfverse: c syonās tā mahyam carate bhavantu mā ni paptam bhuvane śiśriyāṇaḥ //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a mā naḥ paścān mā purastān nudiṣṭhā mottarād adharād uta /

Halfverse: c svasti bhūme no bhava mā vidan paripanthino varīyo yāvayā vadham //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a yāvat te ‘bhi vipaśyāmi bhūme sūryeṇa medinā /

Halfverse: c tāvan me cakṣur mā meṣṭottarāmuttarām samām //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a yac chayānaḥ paryāvarte dakṣiṇam sakhyam abhi bhūme pārśvam uttānās tvā pratīcīm yat pṛṣṭībhir adhiśemahe /

Halfverse: c mā himsīs tatra no bhūme sarvasya pratiśīvari //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a yat te bhūme vikhanāmi kṣipram tad api rohatu /

Halfverse: c mā te marma vimṛgvari mā te hṛdayam arpipam //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a grīṣmas te bhūme varṣāṇi śarad dhemantaḥ śiśiro vasantaḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛtavas te vihitā hāyanīr ahorātre pṛthivi no duhātām //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a yāpa sarpam vijamānā vimṛgvarī yasyām āsann agnayo ye apsv antaḥ /

Halfverse: c parā dasyūn dadatī devapīyūn indram vṛṇānā pṛthivī na vṛtram śakrāya dadhre vṛṣabhāya vṛṣṇe //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a yasyām sadohavirdhāne yūpo yasyām nimīyate /

Halfverse: c brahmāṇo yasyām arcanty ṛgbhiḥ sāmnā yajurvidaḥ yujyante yasyām ṛtvijaḥ somam indrāya pātave //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a yasyām pūrve bhūtakṛta ṛṣayo gā udānṛcuḥ /

Halfverse: c sapta satreṇa vedhaso yajñena tapasā saha //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a sā no bhūmir ā diśatu yad dhanam kāmayāmahe /

Halfverse: c bhago anuprayuṅktām indra etu purogavaḥ //40// {4}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a yasyām gāyanti nṛtyanti bhūmyām martyā vyaṅlabāḥ /

Halfverse: c yudhyante yasyām ākrando yasyām vadati dundubhiḥ /

Halfverse: e sā no bhūmiḥ pra ṇudatām sapatnān asapatnam mā pṛthivī kṛṇotu //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a yasyām annam vrīhiyavau yasyā imāḥ pañca kṛṣṭayaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhūmyai parjanyapatnyai namo ‘stu varṣamedase //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a yasyāḥ puro devakṛtāḥ kṣetre yasyā vikurvate /

Halfverse: c prajāpatiḥ pṛthivīm viśvagarbhām āśāmāśām raṇyām naḥ kṛṇotu //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a nidhim bibhratī bahudhā guhā vasu maṇim hiraṇyam pṛthivī dadātu me /

Halfverse: c vasūni no vasudā rāsamānā devī dadhātu sumanasyamānā //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a janam bibhratī bahudhā vivācasam nānādharmāṇam pṛthivī yathaukasam /

Halfverse: c sahasram dhārā draviṇasya me duhām dhruveva dhenur anapasphurantī //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a yas te sarpo vṛścikas tṛṣṭadamśmā hemantajabdho bhṛmalo guhā śaye /

Halfverse: c krimir jinvat pṛthivi yadyad ejati prāvṛṣi tan naḥ sarpan mopa sṛpad yac chivam tena no mṛḍa //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a ye te panthāno bahavo janāyanā rathasya vartmānasaś ca yātave /

Halfverse: c yaiḥ samcaranty ubhaye bhadrapāpās tam panthānam jayemānamitram ataskaram yac chivam tena no mṛḍa //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a malvam bibhratī gurubhṛd bhadrapāpasya nidhanam titikṣuḥ /

Halfverse: c varāheṇa pṛthivī samvidānā sūkarāya vi jihīte mṛgāya //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a ye ta āraṇyāḥ paśavo mṛgā vane hitāḥ simhā vyāghrāḥ puruṣādaś caranti /

Halfverse: c ulam vṛkam pṛthivi duchunām ita ṛkṣīkām rakṣo apa bādhayāsmat //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a ye gandharvā apsaraso ye cārāyāḥ kimīdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c piśācānt sarvā rakṣāmsi tān asmad bhūme yāvaya //50// {5}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a yām dvipādaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ sampatanti hamsāḥ suparṇāḥ śakunā vayāmsi /

Halfverse: c yasyām vāto mātariśveyate rajāmsi kṛṇvamś cyāvayamś ca vṛkṣān /

Halfverse: e vātasya pravām upavām anu vāty arciḥ //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a yasyām kṛṣṇam aruṇam ca samhite ahorātre vihite bhūmyām adhi /

Halfverse: c varṣeṇa bhūmiḥ pṛthivī vṛtāvṛtā sā no dadhātu bhadrayā priye dhāmanidhāmani //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a dyauś ca ma idam pṛthivī cāntarikṣam ca me vyacaḥ /

Halfverse: c agniḥ sūrya āpo medhām viśve devāś ca sam daduḥ //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a aham asmi sahamāna uttaro nāma bhūmyām /

Halfverse: c abhīṣāḍ asmi viśvāṣāḍ āśāmāśām viṣāsahiḥ //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a ado yad devi prathamānā purastād devair uktā vyasarpo mahitvam /

Halfverse: c ā tvā subhūtam aviśat tadānīm akalpayathāḥ pradiśaś catasraḥ //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a ye grāmā yad araṇyam yāḥ sabhā adhi bhūmyām /

Halfverse: c ye samgrāmāḥ samitayas teṣu cāru vadema te //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a aśva iva rajo dudhuve vi tān janān ya ākṣiyan pṛthivīm yād ajāyata /

Halfverse: c mandrāgretvarī bhuvanasya gopā vanaspatīnām gṛbhir oṣadhīnām //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a yad vadāmi madhumat tad vadāmi yad īkṣe tad vananti mā /

Halfverse: c tviṣīmān asmi jūtimān avānyān hanmi dodhataḥ //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a śantivā surabhiḥ syonā kīlālodhnī payasvatī /

Halfverse: c bhūmir adhi bravītu me pṛthivī payasā saha //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a yām anvaichad dhaviṣā viśvakarmāntar arṇave rajasi praviṣṭām /

Halfverse: c bhujiṣya1m pātram nihitam guhā yad āvir bhoge abhavan mātṛmadbhyaḥ //60//

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a tvam asy āvapanī janānām aditiḥ kāmadughā paprathānā /

Halfverse: c yat ta ūnam tat ta ā pūrayāti prajāpatiḥ prathamajā ṛtasya //61//

Verse: 62

Halfverse: a upasthās te anamīvā ayakṣmā asmabhyam santu pṛthivi prasūtāḥ /

Halfverse: c dīrgham na āyuḥ pratibudhyamānā vayam tubhyam balihṛtaḥ syāma //62//

Verse: 63

Halfverse: a bhūme mātar ni dhehi mā bhadrayā supratiṣṭhitam /

Halfverse: c samvidānā divā kave śriyām mā dhehi bhūtyām //63// {6}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a naḍam ā roha na te atra loka idam sīsam bhāgadheyam ta ehi /

Halfverse: c yo goṣu yakṣmaḥ puruṣeṣu yakṣmas tena tvam sākam adharāṅ parehi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aghaśamsaduḥśamsābhyām kareṇānukareṇa ca /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam ca sarvam teneto mṛtyum ca nir ajāmasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nir ito mṛtyum nirṛtim nir arātim ajāmasi /

Halfverse: c yo no dveṣṭi tam addhy agne akravyād yam u dviṣmas tam u te pra suvāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yady agniḥ kravyād yadi vā vyāghra imam goṣṭham praviveśānyokāḥ /

Halfverse: c tam māṣājyam kṛtvā pra hiṇomi dūram sa gachatv apsuṣado ‘py agnīn //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yat tvā kruddhāḥ pracakrur manyunā puruṣe mṛte /

Halfverse: c sukalpam agne tat tvayā punas tvod dīpayāmasi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a punas tvādityā rudrā vasavaḥ punar brahmā vasunītir agne /

Halfverse: c punas tvā brahmaṇas patir ādhād dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yo agniḥ kravyāt praviveśa no gṛham imam paśyann itaram jātavedasam /

Halfverse: c tam harāmi pitṛyajñāya dūram sa gharmam indhām parame sadhasthe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a kravyādam agnim pra hiṇomi dūram yamarājño gachatu ripravāhaḥ /

Halfverse: c ihāyam itaro jātavedā devo devebhyo havyam vahatu prajānan //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a kravyādam agnim iṣito harāmi janān dṛmhantam vajreṇa mṛtyum /

Halfverse: c ni tam śāsmi gārhapatyena vidvān pitṝṇām loke ‘pi bhāgo astu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a kravyādam agnim śaśamānam ukthya1m pra hiṇomi pathibhiḥ pitṛyāṇaiḥ /

Halfverse: c mā devayānaiḥ punar ā gā atraivaidhi pitṛṣu jāgṛhi tvam //10// {7}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a sam indhate samkasukam svastaye śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ /

Halfverse: c jahāti ripram aty ena eti samiddho agniḥ supunā punāti //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a devo agniḥ samkasuko divas pṛṣṭhāny āruhat /

Halfverse: c mucyamāno nir eṇaso ‘mog asmāṁ aśastyāḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a asmin vayam samkasuke agnau riprāṇi mṛjmahe /

Halfverse: c abhūma yajñiyāḥ śuddhāḥ pra ṇa āyūmṣi tāriṣat //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a samkasuko vikasuko nirṛtho yaś ca nisvaraḥ /

Halfverse: c te te yakṣmam savedaso dūrād dūram anīnaśan //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yo no aśveṣu vīreṣu yo no goṣv ajāviṣu /

Halfverse: c kravyādam nir nudāmasi yo agnir janayopanaḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a anyebhyas tvā puruṣebhyo gobhyo aśvebhyas tvā /

Halfverse: c niḥ kravyādam nudāmasi yo agnir jīvitayopanaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yasmin devā amṛjata yasmin manuṣyā uta /

Halfverse: c tasmin ghṛtastāvo mṛṣṭvā tvam agne divam ruha //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a samiddho agna āhuta sa no mābhyapakramīḥ /

Halfverse: c atraiva dīdihi dyavi jyok ca sūryam dṛśe //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a sīse mṛḍḍhvam nade mṛḍḍhvam agnau samkasuke ca yat /

Halfverse: c atho avyām rāmāyām śīrṣaktim upabarhaṇe //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a sīse malam sādayitvā śīrṣaktim upabarhaṇe /

Halfverse: c avyām asiknyām mṛṣṭvā śuddhā bhavata yajñiyāḥ //20// {8}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a param mṛtyo anu parehi panthām yas ta eṣa itaro devayānāt /

Halfverse: c cakṣuṣmate śṛṇvate te bravīmīheme vīrā bahavo bhavantu //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a ime jīvā vi mṛtair āvavṛtrann abhūd bhadrā devahutir no adya /

Halfverse: c prāñco agāma nṛtaye hasāya suvīrāso vidatham ā vadema //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a imam jīvebhyaḥ paridhim dadhāmi maiṣām nu gād aparo artham etam /

Halfverse: c śatam jīvantaḥ śaradaḥ purūcīs tiro mṛtyum dadhatām parvatena //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ā rohatāyur jarasam vṛṇānā anupūrvam yatamānā yati stha /

Halfverse: c tān vas tvaṣṭā sujanimā sajoṣāḥ sarvam āyur nayatu jīvanāya //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yathāhāny anupūrvam bhavanti yatha rtava ṛtubhir yanti sākam /

Halfverse: c yathā na pūrvam aparo jahāty evā dhātar āyūmṣi kalpayaiṣām //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a aśmanvatī rīyate sam rabhadhvam vīrayadhvam pra taratā sakhāyaḥ /

Halfverse: c atrā jahīta ye asan durevā anamīvān ut taremābhi vājān //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭhatā pra taratā sakhāyo ‘śmanvatī nadī syandata iyam /

Halfverse: c atrā jahīta ye asann aśivāḥ śivānt syonān ut taremābhi vājān //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a vaiśvadevīm varcasā ā rabhadhvam śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ /

Halfverse: c atikrāmanto duritā padāni śatam himāḥ sarvavīrā madema //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a udīcīnaiḥ pathibhir vāyumadbhir atikrāmanto ‘varān parebhiḥ /

Halfverse: c triḥ sapta kṛtva ṛṣayaḥ paretā mṛtyum praty auhan padayopanena //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a mṛtyoḥ padam yopayanta eta drāghīya āyuḥ prataram dadhānāḥ /

Halfverse: c āsīnā mṛtyum nudatā sadhasthe ‘tha jīvāso vidatham ā vadema //30// {9}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a imā nārīr avidhavāḥ supatnīr āñjanena sarpiṣā sam spṛśantām /

Halfverse: c anaśravo anamīvāḥ suratnā ā rohantu janayo yonim agre //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a vyākaromi haviṣāham etau tau brahmaṇā vy aham kalpayāmi /

Halfverse: c svadhām pitṛbhyo ajarām kṛṇomi dīrgheṇāyuṣā sam imānt sṛjāmi //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a yo no agniḥ pitaro hṛtsv antar āviveśāmṛto martyeṣu /

Halfverse: c mayy aham tam pari gṛhṇāmi devam mā so asmān dvikṣata mā vayam tam //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a apāvṛtya gārhapatyāt kravyādā preta dakṣiṇā /

Halfverse: c priyam pitṛbhya ātmane brahmabhyaḥ kṛṇutā priyam //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a dvibhāgadhanam ādāya pra kṣiṇāty avartyā /

Halfverse: c agniḥ putrasya jyeṣṭhasya yaḥ kravyād anirāhitah //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a yat kṛṣate yad vanute yac ca vasnena vindate /

Halfverse: c sarvam martyasya tan nāsti kravyāc ced anirāhitaḥ //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a ayajñiyo hatavarcā bhavati nainena havir attave /

Halfverse: c chinatti kṛṣyā gor dhanād yam kravyād anuvartate //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a muhur gṛdhyaiḥ pra vadaty ārtim martyo nītya /

Halfverse: c kravyād yān agnir antikād anuvidvān vitāvati //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a grāhyā gṛhāḥ sam sṛjyante striyā yan mriyate patiḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmaiva vidvān eṣyo3 yaḥ kravyādam nirādadhat //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a yad ripram śamalam cakṛma yac ca duṣkṛtam /

Halfverse: c āpo mā tasmāc chumbhantv agneḥ samkasukāc ca yat //40// {10}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a tā adharād udīcīr āvavṛtran prajānaitīḥ pathibhir devayānaiḥ /

Halfverse: c parvatasya vṛṣabhasyādhi pṛṣṭhe navāś caranti saritaḥ purāṇīḥ //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a agne akravyān niḥ kravyādam nudā devayajanam vaha //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a imam kravyād ā viveśāyam kravyādam anv agāt /

Halfverse: c vyāgrau kṛtvā nānānam tam harāmi śivāparam //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a antardhir devānām paridhir manuṣyāṇām agnir gārhpatya ubhayān antarā śritaḥ //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a jīvānām āyuḥ pra tira tvam agne pitṝṇām lokam api gachantu ye mṛtāḥ /

Halfverse: c sugārhapatyo vitapann arātim uṣāmuṣām śreyasīm dhehy asmai //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a sarvān agne sahamānaḥ sapatnān aiṣām ūrjam rayim asmāsu dhehi //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a imam indram vahnim paprim anvārabhadhvam sa vo nir vakṣad duritād avadyāt /

Halfverse: c tenāpa hata śarum āpatantam tena rudrasya pari pātāstām //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a anaḍvāham plavam anvārabhadhvam sa vo nir vakṣad duritād avadyāt /

Halfverse: c ā rohata savitur nāvam etām ṣaḍbhir urvībhir amatim tarema //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a ahorātre anv eṣi bibhrat kṣemyas tiṣṭhan prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ /

Halfverse: c anāturānt sumanasas talpa bibhraj jyog eva naḥ puruṣagandhir edhi //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a te devebhya ā vṛścante pāpam jīvanti sarvadā /

Halfverse: c kravyād yān agnir antikād aśva ivānuvapate naḍam //50// {11}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a ye ‘śraddhā dhanakāmyā kravyādā samāsate /

Halfverse: c te vā anyeṣām kumbhīm paryādadhati sarvadā //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a preva pipatiṣati manasā muhur ā vartate punaḥ /

Halfverse: c kravyād yān agnir antikād anuvidvān vitāvati //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a aviḥ kṛṣṇā bhāgadheyam paśūnām sīsam kravyād api candram ta āhuḥ /

Halfverse: c māṣāḥ piṣṭā bhāgadheyam te havyam araṇyānyā gahvaram sacasva //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a iṣīkām jaratīm iṣṭvā tilpiñjam daṇḍanam naḍam /

Halfverse: c tam indra idhmam kṛtvā yamasyāgnim nirādadhau //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a pratyañcam arkam pratyarpayitvā pravidvān panthām vi hy āviveśa /

Halfverse: c parāmīṣām asūn dideśa dīrgheṇāyuṣā sam imānt sṛjāmi //55// {12}

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pumān pumso ‘dhi tiṣṭha carmehi tatra hvayasva yatamā priyā te /

Halfverse: c yāvantāv agre prathamam sameyathus tad vām vayo yamarājye samānam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tāvad vām cakṣus tati vīryāṇi tāvat tejas tatidhā vājināni /

Halfverse: c agniḥ śarīram sacate yadaidho ‘dhā pakvān mithunā sam bhavāthaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sam asmiml loke sam u devayāne sam smā sametam yamarājyeṣu /

Halfverse: c pūtau pavitrair upa tad dhvayethām yadyad reto adhi vām sambabhūva //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a āpas putrāso abhi sam viśadhvam imam jīvam jīvadhanyāḥ sametya /

Halfverse: c tāsām bhajadhvam amṛtam yam āhur odanam pacati vām janitrī //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yam vām pitā pacati yam ca mātā riprān nirmuktyai śamalāc ca vācaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa odanaḥ śatadhāraḥ svarga ubhe vy āpa nabhasī mahitvā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ubhe nabhasī ubhayāmś ca lokān ye yajvanām abhijitāḥ svargāḥ /

Halfverse: c teṣām jyotiṣmān madhumān yo agre tasmin putrair jarasi sam śrayethām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a prācīmprācīm pradiśam ā rabhethām etam lokam śraddadhānāḥ sacante /

Halfverse: c yad vām pakvam pariviṣṭam agnau tasya guptaye dampatī sam śrayethām //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a dakṣiṇām diśam abhi nakṣamāṇau paryāvartethām abhi pātram etat /

Halfverse: c tasmin vām yamaḥ pitṛbhiḥ samvidānaḥ pakvāya śarma bahulam ni yachāt //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a pratīcī diśām iyam id varam yasyām somo adhipā mṛḍitā ca /

Halfverse: c tasyām śrayethām sukṛtaḥ sacethām adhā pakvān mithunā sam bhavāthaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a uttaram rāṣṭram prajayottarāvad diśām udīcī kṛṇavan no agram /

Halfverse: c pāṅktam chandaḥ puruṣo babhūva viśvair viśvāṅgaiḥ saha sam bhavema //10// {13}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a dhruveyam virāṇ namo astv asyai śivā putrebhya uta mahyam astu /

Halfverse: c sā no devy adite viśvavāra irya iva gopā abhi rakṣa pakvam //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a piteva putrān abhi sam svajasva naḥ śivā no vātā iha vāntu bhūmau /

Halfverse: c yam odanam pacato devate iha tam nas tapa uta satyam ca vettu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yadyad kṛṣṇaḥ śakuna eha gatvā tsaran viṣaktam bila āsasāda /

Halfverse: c yad vā dāsy ārdrahastā samaṅkta ulūkhalam musalam śumbhatāpaḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ayam grāvā pṛthubudhno vayodhāḥ pūtaḥ pavitrair apa hantu rakṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā roha carma mahi śarma yacha mā dampatī pautram agham ni gātām //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a vanaspatiḥ saha devair na āgan rakṣaḥ piśācāṁ apabādhamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa uc chrayātai pra vadāti vācam tena lokāṁ abhi sarvān jayema //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a sapta medhān paśavaḥ pary agṛhṇan ya eṣām jyotiṣmāṁ uta yaś cakarśa /

Halfverse: c trayastrimśad devatās tān sacante sa naḥ svargam abhi neṣa lokam //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a svargam lokam abhi no nayāsi sam jāyayā saha putraiḥ syāma /

Halfverse: c gṛhṇāmi hastam anu maitv atra mā nas tārīn nirṛtir mo arātiḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a grāhim pāpmānam ati tāṁ ayāma tamo vy asya pra vadāsi valgu /

Halfverse: c vānaspatya udyato mā jihimsīr mā taṇḍulam vi śarīr devayantam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a viśvavyacā ghṛtapṛṣṭho bhaviṣyant sayonir lokam upa yāhy etam /

Halfverse: c varṣavṛddham upa yacha śūrpam tuṣam palāvān apa tad vinaktu //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a trayo lokāḥ sammitā brāhmaṇena dyaur evāsau pṛthivy a1ntarikṣam /

Halfverse: c amśūn gṛbhītvānvārabhethām ā pyāyantām punar ā yantu śūrpam //20// {14}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a pṛthag rūpāṇi bahudhā paśūnām ekarūpo bhavasi sam samṛddhyā /

Halfverse: c etām tvacam lohinīm tām nudasva grāvā śumbhāti malaga iva vastrā //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a pṛthivīm tvā pṛthivyām ā veśayāmi tanūḥ samānī vikṛtā ta eṣā /

Halfverse: c yadyad dyuttam likhitam arpaṇena tena mā susror brahmaṇāpi tad vapāmi //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a janitrīva prati haryāsi sūnum sam tvā dadhāmi pṛthivīm pṛthivyā /

Halfverse: c ukhā kumbhī vedyām mā vyathiṣṭhā yajñāyudhair ājyenātiṣaktā //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a agniḥ pacan rakṣatu tvā purastād indro rakṣatu dakṣiṇato marutvān /

Halfverse: c varuṇas tvā dṛmhād dharuṇe pratīcyā uttarāt tvā somaḥ sam dadātai //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a pūtāḥ pavitraiḥ pavante abhrād divam ca yanti pṛthivīm ca lokān /

Halfverse: c tā jīvalā jīvadhanyāḥ pratiṣṭhāḥ pātra āsiktāḥ pary agnir indhām //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a ā yanti divaḥ pṛthivīm sacante bhūmyāḥ sacante adhy antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c śuddhāḥ satīs tā u śumbhanta eva tā naḥ svargam abhi lokam nayantu //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a uteva prabhvīr uta sammitāsa uta śukrāḥ śucayaś cāmṛtāsaḥ /

Halfverse: c tā odanam dampatibhyām praśiṣṭā āpaḥ śikṣantīḥ pacatā sunāthāḥ //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a samkhyātā stokāḥ pṛthivīm sacante prāṇāpānaiḥ sammitā oṣadhībhiḥ /

Halfverse: c asamkhyātā opyamānāḥ suvarṇāḥ sarvam vyāpuḥ śucayaḥ śucitvam //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a ud yodhanty abhi valganti taptāḥ phenam asyanti bahulāmś ca bindūn /

Halfverse: c yoṣeva dṛṣṭvā patim ṛtviyāyaitais taṇḍulair bhavatā sam āpaḥ //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a ut thāpaya sīdato budhna enān adbhir ātmānam abhi sam spṛśantām /

Halfverse: c amāsi pātrair udakam yad etan mitās taṇḍulāḥ pradiśo yadīmāḥ //30// {15}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a pra yacha parśum tvarayā harausam ahimsanta oṣadhīr dāntu parvan /

Halfverse: c yāsām somaḥ pari rājyam babhūvāmanyutā no vīrudho bhavantu //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a navam barhir odanāya stṛṇīta priyam hṛdaś cakṣuṣo valgv astu /

Halfverse: c tasmin devāḥ saha daivīr viśantv imam prāśnantv ṛtubhir niṣadya //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a vanaspate stīrṇam ā sīda barhir agniṣṭobhaiḥ sammito devatābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭreva rūpam sukṛtam svadhityainā ehāḥ pari pātre dadṛśrām //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a ṣaṣṭyām śaratsu nidhipā abhīchāt svaḥ pakvenābhy aśnavātai /

Halfverse: c upainam jīvān pitaraś ca putrā etam svargam gamayāntam agneḥ //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a dhartā dhriyasva dharuṇe pṛthivyā acyutam tvā devatāś cyāvayantu /

Halfverse: c tam tvā dampatī jīvantau jīvaputrāv ud vāsayātaḥ pary agnidhānāt //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a sarvānt samāgā abhijitya lokān yāvantaḥ kāmāḥ sam atītṛpas tān /

Halfverse: c vi gāhethām āyavanam ca darvir ekasmin pātre adhy ud dharainam //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a upa stṛṇīhi prathaya purastād ghṛtena pātram abhi ghārayaitat /

Halfverse: c vāśrevosrā taruṇam stanasyum imam devāso abhihiṅkṛṇota //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a upāstarīr akaro lokam etam uruḥ prathatām asamaḥ svargaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmim chrayātai mahiṣaḥ suparṇo devā enam devatābhyaḥ pra yachān //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a yadyaj jāyā pacati tvat paraḥparaḥ patir vā jāye tvat tiraḥ /

Halfverse: c sam tat sṛjethām saha vām tad astu sampādayantau saha lokam ekam //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a yāvanto asyāḥ pṛthivīm sacante asmat putrāḥ pari ye sambabhūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvāms tāṁ upa pātre hvayethām nābhim jānānāḥ śiśavaḥ samāyān //40// {16}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a vasor yā dhārā madhunā prapīnā ghṛtena miśrā amṛtasya nābhayaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvās tā ava rundhe svargaḥ ṣaṣṭyām śaratsu nidhipā abhīchāt //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a nidhim nidhipā abhy ṝnam ichād anīśvarā abhitaḥ santu ye ‘nye /

Halfverse: c asmābhir datto nihitaḥ svargas tribhiḥ kāṇḍais trīnt svargān arukṣat //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a agnī rakṣas tapatu yad videvam kravyād piśāca iha mā pra pāsta /

Halfverse: c nudāma enam apa rudhmo asmad ādityā enam aṅgirasaḥ sacantām //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a ādityebhyo aṅgirobhyo madhv idam ghṛtena miśram prati vedayāmi /

Halfverse: c śuddhahastau brāhmaṇasyānihatyaitam svargam sukṛtāv apītam //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a idam prāpam uttamam kāṇḍam asya yasmāl lokāt parameṣṭhī samāpa /

Halfverse: c ā siñca sarpir ghṛtavat sam aṅgdhy eṣa bhāgo aṅgiraso no atra //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a satyāya ca tapase devatābhyo nidhim śevadhim pari dadma etam /

Halfverse: c mā no dyūte ‘va gān mā samityām mā smānyasmā ut sṛjatā purā mat //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a aham pacāmy aham dadāmi mamed u karman karuṇe ‘dhi jāyā /

Halfverse: c kaumāro loko ajaniṣṭa putro3 ‘nvārabhethām vaya uttarāvat //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a na kilbiṣam atra nādhāro asti na yan mitraiḥ samamamāna eti /

Halfverse: c anūnam pātram nihitam na etat paktāram pakvaḥ punar ā viśāti //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a priyam priyāṇām kṛṇavāma tamas te yantu yatame dviṣanti /

Halfverse: c dhenur anaḍvān vayovaya āyad eva pauruṣeyam apa mṛtyum nudantu //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a sam agnayaḥ vidur anyo anyam ya oṣadhīḥ sacate yaś ca sindhūn /

Halfverse: c yāvanto devā divy ā3tapanti hiraṇyam jyotiḥ pacato babhūva //50// {17}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a eṣā tvacām puruṣe sam babhūvānagnāḥ sarve paśavo ye anye /

Halfverse: c kṣatreṇātmānam pari dhāpayātho ‘motam vāso mukham odanasya //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a yad akṣeṣu vadā yat samityām yad vā vadā anṛtam vittakāmyā /

Halfverse: c samānam tantum abhi samvasānau tasmint sarvam śamalam sādayāthaḥ //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a varṣam vanuṣvāpi gacha devāms tvaco dhūmam pary ut pātayāsi /

Halfverse: c viśvavyacā ghṛtapṛṣṭho bhaviṣyant sayonir lokam upa yāhy etam //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a tanvam svargo bahudhā vi cakre yathā vida ātmann anyavarṇām /

Halfverse: c apājait kṛṣṇām ruśatīm punāno yā lohinī tām te agnau juhomi //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a prācyai tvā diśṝ3 ‘gnaye ‘dhipataye ‘sitāya rakṣitra ādityāyeṣumate /

Halfverse: c etam pari dadmas tam no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ /

Halfverse: e diṣṭam no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha sam bhavema //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a dakṣiṇāyai tvā diśa indrāyādhipataye tiraścirājaye rakṣitre yamāyeṣumate /

Halfverse: c etam pari dadmas tam no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ /

Halfverse: e diṣṭam no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha sam bhavema //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a pratīcyai tvā diśe varuṇāyādhipataye pṛdākave rakṣitre ‘nnāyeṣumate /

Halfverse: c etam pari dadmas tam no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ /

Halfverse: e diṣṭam no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha sam bhavema //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a udīcyai tvā diśe somāyādhipataye svajāya rakṣitre ‘śanyā iṣumatyai /

Halfverse: c etam pari dadmas tam no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ /

Halfverse: e diṣṭam no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha sam bhavema //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a dhruvāyai tvā diśe viṣṇave ‘dhipataye kalmāṣagrīvāya rakṣitra oṣadhībhya iṣumatībhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c etam pari dadmas tam no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ /

Halfverse: e diṣṭam no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha sam bhavema //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a ūrdhvāyai tvā diśe bṛhaspataye ‘dhipataye śvitrāya rakṣitre varṣāyeṣumate /

Halfverse: c etam pari dadmas tam no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ /

Halfverse: e diṣṭam no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha sam bhavema //60// {18}

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a dadāmīty eva brūyād anu cainām abhutsata /

Halfverse: c vaśām brahmabhyo yācadbhyas tat prajāvad apatyavat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a prajayā sa vi krīṇīte paśubhiś copa dasyati /

Halfverse: c ya ārṣeyebhyo yācadbhyo devānām gām na ditsati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kūṭayāsya sam śīryante śloṇayā kātam ardati /

Halfverse: c baṇḍayā dahyante gṛhāḥ kāṇayā dīyate svam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vilohito adhiṣṭhānāc chakno vindati gopatim /

Halfverse: c tathā vaśāyāḥ samvidyam duradabhnā hy ḥcyase //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pador asyā adhiṣṭhānād viklindur nāma vindati /

Halfverse: c anāmanāt sam śīryante yā mukhenopajighrati //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yo asyāḥ karṇāv āskunoty ā sa deveṣu vṛścate /

Halfverse: c lakṣma kurva iti manyate kanīyaḥ kṛṇute svam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yad asyāḥ kasmai cid bhogāya bālān kaś cit prakṛntati /

Halfverse: c tataḥ kiśorā mriyante vatsāmś ca ghātuko vṛkaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yad asyā gopatau satyā loma dhvāṅkṣo ajīhiḍat /

Halfverse: c tataḥ kumārā mriyante yakṣmo vindaty anāmanāt //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yad asyāḥ palpūlanam śakṛd dāsī samasyati /

Halfverse: c tato ‘parūpam jāyate tasmād avyeṣyad enasaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a jāyamānābhi jāyate devānt sabrāhmaṇān vaśā /

Halfverse: c tasmād brahmabhyo deyaiṣā tad āhuḥ svasya gopanam //10// {19}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ya enām vanim āyanti teṣām devakṛtā vaśā /

Halfverse: c brahmajyeyam tad abruvan ya enām nipriyāyate //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ya ārṣeyebhyo yācadbhyo devānām gām na ditsati /

Halfverse: c ā sa deveṣu vṛścate brāhmaṇānām ca manyave //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yo asya syād vaśābhogo anyām icheta tarhi saḥ /

Halfverse: c himste adattā puruṣam yācitām ca na ditsati //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yathā śevadhir nihito brāhmaṇānām tathā vaśā /

Halfverse: c tām etad achāyanti yasmin kasmimś ca jāyate //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a svam etad achāyanti yad vaśām brāhmaṇā abhi /

Halfverse: c yathainān anyasmin jinīyād evāsyā nirodhanam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a cared evā traihāyaṇād avijñātagadā satī /

Halfverse: c vaśām ca vidyān nārada brāhmaṇās tarhy eṣyāḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ya enām avaśām āha devānām nihitam nidhim /

Halfverse: c ubhau tasmai bhavāśarvau parikramyeṣum asyatah //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yo asyā ūdho na vedātho asyā stanān uta /

Halfverse: c ubhayenaivāsmai duhe dātum ced aśakad vaśām //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a duradabhnainam ā śaye yācitām ca na ditsati /

Halfverse: c nāsmai kāmāḥ sam ṛdhyante yām adattvā cikīrṣati //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a devā vaśām ayācan mukham kṛtvā brāhmaṇam /

Halfverse: c teṣām sarveṣām adadad dheḍam ny ṝti mānuṣaḥ //20// {20}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a heḍam paśūnām ny ṝti brāhmaṇebhyo ‘dadad vaśām /

Halfverse: c devānām nihitam bhāgam martyaś cen nipriyāyate //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yad anye śatam yāceyur brāhmaṇā gopatim vaśām /

Halfverse: c athainām devā abruvann evam ha viduṣo vaśā //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ya evam viduṣe ‘dattvāthānyebhyo dadad vaśām /

Halfverse: c durgā tasmā adhiṣṭhāne pṛthivī sahadevatā //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a devā vaśām ayācan yasminn agre ajāyata /

Halfverse: c tām etām vidyān nāradaḥ saha devair ud ājata //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a anapatyam alpapaśum vaśā kṛṇoti pūruṣam /

Halfverse: c brāhmaṇaiś ca yācitām athainām nipriyāyate //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a agnīṣomābhyām kāmāya mitrāya varuṇāya ca /

Halfverse: c tebhyo yācanti brāhmaṇās teṣv ā vṛścate ‘dadat //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a yāvad asyā gopatir nopaśṛṇuyād ṛcaḥ svayam /

Halfverse: c cared asya tāvad goṣu nāsya śrutvā gṛhe vaset //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a yo asyā ṛca upaśrutyātha goṣv acīcarat /

Halfverse: c āyuś ca tasya bhūtim ca devā vṛścanti hīḍitāḥ //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a vaśā carantī bahudhā devānām nihito nidhiḥ /

Halfverse: c āviṣ kṛṇuṣva rūpāṇi yadā sthāma jighāmsati //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a āvir ātmānam kṛṇute yadā sthāma jighāmsati /

Halfverse: c atho ha brahmabhyo vaśā yāñcyāya kṛṇute manaḥ //30// {21}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a manasā sam kalpayati tad devāṁ api gachati /

Halfverse: c tato ha brahmāṇo vaśām upaprayanti yācitum //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a svadhākāreṇa pitṛbhyo yajñena devatābhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c dānena rājanyo vaśāyā mātur heḍam na gachati //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a vaśā mātā rājanyasya tathā sambhūtam agraśaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasyā āhur anarpaṇam yad brahmabhyaḥ pradīyate //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a yathājyam pragṛhītam ālumpet sruco agnaye /

Halfverse: c evā ha brahmabhyo vaśām agnaya ā vṛścate ‘dadat //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a puroḍāśavatsā sudughā loke ‘smā upa tiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: c sāsmai sarvān kāmān vaśā pradaduṣe duhe //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a sarvān kāmān yamarājye vaśā pradaduṣe duhe /

Halfverse: c athāhur nārakam lokam nirundhānasya yācitām //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a pravīyamānā carati kruddhā gopataye vaśā /

Halfverse: c vehatam mā manyamāno mṛtyoḥ pāśeṣu badhyatām //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a yo vehatam manyamāno ‘mā ca pacate vaśām /

Halfverse: c apy asya putrān pautrāmś ca yācayate bṛhaspatiḥ //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a mahad eṣāva tapati carantī goṣu gaur api /

Halfverse: c atho ha gopataye vaśādaduṣe viṣam duhe //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a priyam paśūnām bhavati yad brahmabhyaḥ pradīyate /

Halfverse: c atho vaśāyās tat priyam yad devatrā haviḥ syāt //40// {22}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a yā vaśā udakalpayan devā yajñād udetya /

Halfverse: c tāsām viliptyam bhīmām udākuruta nāradaḥ //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a tām devā amīmāmsanta vaśeyā3m avaśeti /

Halfverse: c tām abravīn nārada eṣā vaśānām vaśatameti //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a kati nu vaśā nārada yās tvam vettha manuṣyajāḥ /

Halfverse: c tās tvā pṛchāmi vidvāmsam kasyā nāśnīyād abrāhmaṇaḥ //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a viliptyā bṛhaspate yā ca sūtavaśā vaśā /

Halfverse: c tasyā nāśnīyād abrāhmaṇo ya āśamseta bhūtyām //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a namas te astu nāradānuṣṭhu viduṣe vaśā /

Halfverse: c katamāsām bhīmatamā yām adattvā parābhavet //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a viliptī yā bṛhaspate ‘tho sūtavaśā vaśā /

Halfverse: c tasyā nāśnīyād abrāhmaṇo ya āśamseta bhūtyām //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a trīṇi vai vaśājātāni viliptī sūtavaśā vaśā /

Halfverse: c tāḥ pra yached brahmabhyaḥ so ‘nāvraskaḥ prajāpatau //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a etad vo brāhmaṇā havir iti manvīta yācitaḥ /

Halfverse: c vaśām ced enam yāceyur yā bhīmādaduṣo gṛhe //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a devā vaśām pary avadan na no ‘dād iti hīḍitāḥ /

Halfverse: c etābhir ṛgbhir bhedam tasmād vai sa parābhavat //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a utainām bhedo nādadād vaśām indreṇa yācitaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmāt tam devā āgaso ‘vṛścann ahamuttare //50//

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a ye vaśāyā adānāya vadanti parirāpiṇah /

Halfverse: c indrasya manyave jālmā ā vṛścante acittyā //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a ye gopatim parāṇīyāthāhur mā dadā iti /

Halfverse: c rudrasyāstām te hetīm pari yanty acittyā //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a yadi hutam yady ahutām amā ca pacate vaśām /

Halfverse: c devānt sabrāhmaṇān ṛtvā jihmo lokān nir ṛchati //53// {23}

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1 {1}

Halfverse: a śrameṇa tapasā sṛṣṭā brahmaṇā vittā rte śritā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a satyenāvṛtā śriyā prāvṛtā yaśasā parīvṛtā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a svadhayā parihitā śraddhayā paryūḍhā dīkṣayā guptā yajñe pratiṣṭhitā loko nidhanam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a brahma padavāyam brāhmaṇo ‘dhipatiḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tām ādadānasya brahmagavīm jinato brāhmaṇam kṣatriyasya //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a apa krāmati sūnṛtā vīrya1m punyā lakṣmīḥ //6// {24}

Verse: 7 {2}

Halfverse: a ojaś ca tejaś ca sahaś ca balam ca vāk cendriyam ca śrīś ca dharmaś ca //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a brahma ca kṣatram ca rāṣṭram ca viśaś ca tviṣiś ca yaśaś ca varcaś ca draviṇam ca //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a āyuś ca rūpam ca nāma ca kīrtiś ca prāṇaś cāpānaś ca cakṣuś ca śrotram ca //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a payaś ca rasaś cānnam cānnādyam ca rtam ca satyam ceṣṭam ca pūrtam ca prajā ca paśavaś ca //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tāni sarvāṇy apa krāmanti brahmagavīm ādadānasya jinato brāhmaṇam kṣatriyasya //11// {25}

Verse: 12 {3}

Halfverse: a saiṣā bhīmā brahmagavy a1ghaviṣā sākṣāt kṛtyā kūlbajam āvṛtā //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sarvāṇy asyām ghorāṇi sarve ca mṛtyavaḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a sarvāṇy asyām krūrāṇi sarve puruṣavadhāḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a sā brahmajyam devapīyum brahmagavy ādīyamānā mṛtyoḥ padvīṣa ā dyati //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a meniḥ śatavadhā hi sā brahmajyasya kṣitir hi sā //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a tasmād vai brāhmaṇānām gaur durādharṣā vijānatā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a vajro dhāvantī vaiśvānara udvītā //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a hetiḥ śaphān utkhidantī mahādevo3 ‘pekṣamāṇā //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a kṣurapavir īkṣamāṇā vāśyamānābhi sphūrjati //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a mṛtyur hiṅkṛṇvaty ḥ1gro devaḥ pucham paryasyantī //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a sarvajyāniḥ karṇau varīvarjayantī rājayakṣmo mehantī //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a menir duhyamānā śīrṣaktir dugdhā //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a sedir upatiṣṭhantī mithoyodhaḥ parāmṛṣṭā //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a śaravyā3 mukhe ‘pinahyamāna ṛtir hanyamānā //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a aghaviṣā nipatantī tamo nipatitā //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a anugachantī prāṇān upa dāsayati brahmagavī brahmajyasya //27// {26}

Verse: 28 {4}

Halfverse: a vairam vikṛtyamānā pautrādyam vibhājyamānā //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a devahetir hriyamāṇā vyṛddhir hṛtā //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a pāpmādhidhīyamānā pāruṣyam avadhīyamānā //30//

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a viṣam prayasyantī takmā prayastā //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a agham pracyamānā duṣvapnyam pakvā //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a mūlabarhaṇī paryākriyamāṇā kṣitiḥ paryākṛtā //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a asamjñā gandhena śug uddhriyamāṇāśīviṣa uddhṛtā //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a abhūtir upahriyamāṇā parābhūtir upahṛtā //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a śarvaḥ kruddhaḥ piśyamānā śimidā piśitā //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a avartir aśyamānā nirṛtir aśitā //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a aśitā lokāc chinatti brahmagavī brahmajyam asmāc cāmuṣmāc ca //38// {27}

Verse: 39 {5}

Halfverse: a tasyā āhananam kṛtyā menir āśasanam valaga ūbadhyam //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a asvagatā parihṇutā //40//

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a agniḥ kravyād bhūtvā brahmagavī brahmajyam praviśyātti //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a sarvāsyāṅgā parvā mūlāni vṛścati //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a chinatty asya pitṛbandhu parā bhāvayati mātṛbandhu //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a vivāhām jñātīnt sarvān api kṣāpayati brahmagavī brahmajyasya kṣatriyeṇāpunardīyamānā //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a avāstum enam asvagam aprajasam karoty aparāparaṇo bhavati kṣīyate //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a ya evam viduṣo brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyo gām ādatte //46// {28}

Verse: 47 {6}

Halfverse: a kṣipram vai tasyāhanane gṛdhrāḥ kurvata ailabam //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a kṣipram vai tasyādahanam pari nṛtyanti keśinīr āghnānāḥ pāṇinorasi kurvāṇāḥ pāpam ailabam //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a kṣipram vai tasya vāstuṣu vṛkāḥ kurvata ailabam //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a kṣipram vai tasya pṛchanti yat tad āsī3d idam nu tā3d iti //50//

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a chindhy ā chindhi pra chindhy api kṣāpaya kṣāpaya //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a ādadānam āṅgirasi brahmajyam upa dāsaya //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a vaiśvadevī hy ḥ1cyase kṛtyā kūlbajam āvṛtā //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a oṣantī samoṣantī brahmaṇo vajraḥ //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a kṣurapavir mṛtyur bhūtvā vi dhāva tvam //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a ā datse jinatām varca iṣṭam pūrtam cāśiṣaḥ //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a ādāya jītam jītāya lokṝ3 ‘muṣmin pra yachasi //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a aghnye padavīr bhava brāhmaṇasyābhiśastyā //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a meniḥ śaravyā bhavāghād aghaviṣā bhava //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a aghnye pra śiro jahi brahmajyasya kṛtāgaso devapīyor arādhasaḥ //60//

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a tvayā pramūrṇam mṛditam agnir dahatu duścitam //61// {29}

Verse: 62 {7}

Halfverse: a vṛśca pra vṛśca sam vṛśca daha pra daha sam daha //62//

Verse: 63

Halfverse: a brahmajyam devy aghnya ā mūlād anusamdaha //63//

Verse: 64

Halfverse: a yathāyād yamasādanāt pāpalokān parāvataḥ //64//

Verse: 65

Halfverse: a evā tvam devy aghnye brahmajyasya kṛtāgaso devapīyor arādhasaḥ //65//

Verse: 66

Halfverse: a vajreṇa śataparvaṇā tīkṣṇena kṣurabhṛṣṭinā //66//

Verse: 67

Halfverse: a pra skandhān pra śiro jahi //67//

Verse: 68

Halfverse: a lomāny asya sam chindhi tvacam asya vi veṣṭaya //68//

Verse: 69

Halfverse: a māmsāny asya śātaya snāvāny asya sam vṛha //69//

Verse: 70

Halfverse: a asthīny asya pīḍaya majjānam asya nir jahi //70//

Verse: 71

Halfverse: a sarvāsyāṅgā parvāṇi vi śrathaya //71//

Verse: 72

Halfverse: a agnir enam kravyāt pṛthivyā nudatām ud oṣatu vāyur antarikṣān mahato varimṇaḥ //72//

Verse: 73

Halfverse: a sūrya enam divaḥ pra ṇudatām ny oṣatu //73// {30}

## Book: 13

13

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a udehi vājin yo apsv a1ntar idam rāṣṭram pra viśa sūnṛtāvat /

Halfverse: c yo rohito viśvam idam jajāna sa tvā rāṣṭrāya subhṛtam bibhartu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ud vāja ā gan yo apsv a1ntar viśa ā roha tvadyonayo yāḥ /

Halfverse: c somam dadhāno ‘pa oṣadhīr gāś catuṣpado dvipada ā veśayeha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yūyam ugrā marutaḥ pṛśnimātara indreṇa yujā pra mṛṇīta śatrūn /

Halfverse: c ā vo rohitaḥ śṛṇavat sudānavas triṣaptāso marutaḥ svādusammudaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ruho ruroha rohita ā ruroha garbho janīnām januṣām upastham /

Halfverse: c tābhiḥ samrabdham anv avindan ṣaḍ urvīr gātum prapaśyann iha rāṣṭram āhāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā te rāṣṭram iha rohito ‘hārṣīd vy āsthan mṛdho abhayam te abhūt /

Halfverse: c tasmai te dyāvāpṛthivī revatībhiḥ kāmam duhātām iha śakvarībhiḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a rohito dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna tatra tantum parameṣṭhī tatāna /

Halfverse: c tatra śiśriye ‘ja ekapādo ‘dṛmhad dyāvāpṛthivī balena //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a rohito dyāvāpṛthivī adṛmhat tena sva stabhitam tena nākaḥ /

Halfverse: c tenāntarikṣam vimitā rajāmsi tena devā amṛtam anv avindan //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a vi rohito amṛśad viśvarūpam samākurvāṇaḥ praruho ruhaś ca /

Halfverse: c divam rūḍhvā mahatā mahimnā sam te rāṣṭram anaktu payasā ghṛtena //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yās te ruhaḥ praruho yās ta āruho yābhir āpṛṇāsi divam antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c tāsām brahmaṇā payasā vavṛdhāno viśi rāṣṭre jāgṛhi rohitasya //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yas te viśas tapasaḥ sambabhūvur vatsam gāyatrīm anu tā ihāguḥ /

Halfverse: c tās tvā viśantu manasā śivena sammātā vatso abhy ṝtu rohitaḥ //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ūrdhvo rohito adhi nāke asthād viśvā rūpāṇi janayan yuvā kaviḥ /

Halfverse: c tigmenāgnir jyotiṣā vi bhāti tṛtīye cakre rajasi priyāṇi //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho jātavedā ghṛtāhutaḥ somapṛṣṭhaḥ suvīraḥ /

Halfverse: c mā mā hāsīn nāthito net tvā jahāni gopoṣam ca me vīrapoṣam ca dhehi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a rohito yajñasya janitā mukham ca rohitāya vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi /

Halfverse: c rohitam devā yanti sumanasyamānāḥ sa mā rohaiḥ sāmityai rohayatu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a rohito yajñam vy adadhād viśvakarmaṇe tasmāt tejāmsy upa memāny āguḥ /

Halfverse: c voceyam te nābhim bhuvanasyādhi majmani //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ā tvā ruroha bṛhaty ḥta paṅktir ā kakub varcasā jātavedaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā tvā rurohoṣṇihākṣaro vaṣaṭkāra ā tvā ruroha rohito retasā saha //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ayam vaste garbham pṛthivyā divam vaste ‘yam antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c ayam bradhnasya viṣṭapi svar lokān vy ānaśe //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a vācas pate pṛthivī naḥ syonā syonā yonis talpā naḥ suśevā /

Halfverse: c ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu tam tvā parameṣṭhin pary agnir āyuṣā varcasā dadhātu //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a vācas pata ṛtavaḥ pañca ye nau vaiśvakarmaṇāḥ pari ye sambabhūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu tam tvā parameṣṭhin pari rohita āyuṣā varcasā dadhātu //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a vācas pate saumanasam manaś ca goṣṭhe no gā janaya yoniṣu prajāḥ /

Halfverse: c ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu tam tvā parameṣṭhin pary aham āyuṣā varcasā dadhātu //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a pari tvā dhāt savitā devo agnir varcasā mitrāvaruṇāv abhi tvā /

Halfverse: c sarvā arātīr avakrāmann ehīdam rāṣṭram akaraḥ sunṛtāvat //20// {2}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yam tvā pṛṣatī rathe praṣṭir vahati rohita /

Halfverse: c śubhā yāsi riṇann apaḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a anuvratā rohiṇī rohitasya sūriḥ suvarṇā bṛhatī suvarcāḥ /

Halfverse: c tayā vājān viśvarūpām jayema tayā viśvāḥ pṛtanā abhi ṣyāma //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a idam sado rohiṇī rohitasyāsau panthāḥ pṛṣatī yena yāti /

Halfverse: c tām gandharvāḥ kaśyapā un nayanti tām rakṣanti kavayo ‘pramādam //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a sūryasyāśvā harayaḥ ketumantaḥ sadā vahanty amṛtāḥ sukham ratham /

Halfverse: c ghṛtapāvā rohito bhrājamāno divam devaḥ pṛṣatīm ā viveśa //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a yo rohito vṛṣabhas tigmaśṛṅgaḥ pary agnim pari sūryam babhūva /

Halfverse: c yo viṣṭabhnāti pṛthivīm divam ca tasmād devā adhi sṛṣṭīḥ sṛjante //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a rohito divam āruhan mahataḥ pary arṇavāt /

Halfverse: c sarvo ruroha rohito ruhaḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a vi mimīṣva payasvatīm ghṛtācīm devānām dhenur anapaspṛg eṣā /

Halfverse: c indraḥ somam pibatu kṣemo astv agniḥ pra stautu vi mṛdho nudasva //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a samiddho agniḥ samidhāno ghṛtavṛddho ghṛtāhutaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhīṣāṭ viśvāṣāḍ agniḥ sapatnān hantu ye mama //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a hantv enān pra dahatv arir yo naḥ pṛtanyati /

Halfverse: c kravyādāgninā vayam sapatnān pra dahāmasi //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a avācīnān ava jahīndra vajreṇa bāhumān /

Halfverse: c adhā sapatnān māmakān agnes tejobhir ādiṣi //30// {3}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a agne sapatnān adharān pādayāsmad vyathayā sajātam utpipānam bṛhaspate /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī mitrāvaruṇāv adhare padyantām apratimanyūyamānāḥ //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a udyams tvam deva sūrya sapatnān ava me jahi /

Halfverse: c avainān aśmanā jahi te yantv adhamam tamaḥ //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a vatso virājo vṛṣabho matīnām ā ruroha śukrapṛṣṭho ‘ntarikṣam /

Halfverse: c ghṛtenārkam abhy arcanti vatsam brahma santam brahmaṇā vardhayanti //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a divam ca roha pṛthivīm ca roha rāṣṭram ca roha draviṇam ca roha /

Halfverse: c prajām ca rohāmṛtam ca roha rohitena tanva1m sam spṛṣasva //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a ye devā rāṣṭrabhṛto ‘bhito yanti sūryam /

Halfverse: c taiṣ ṭe rohitaḥ samvidāno rāṣṭram dadhātu sumanasyamānaḥ //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a ut tvā yajñā brahmapūtā vahanty adhvagato harayas tvā vahanti /

Halfverse: c tiraḥ samudram ati rocase ‘rṇavam //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a rohite dyāvāpṛthivī adhi śrite vasujiti gojiti samdhanājiti /

Halfverse: c sahasram yasya janimāni sapta ca voceyam te nābhim bhuvanasyādhi majmani //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a yaśā yāsi pradiśo diśaś ca yaśāḥ paśūnām uta carṣaṇīnām /

Halfverse: c yaśāḥ pṛthivyā adityā upasthe ‘ham bhūyāsam saviteva cāruḥ //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a amutra sann iha vetthetaḥ sams tāni paśyasi /

Halfverse: c itaḥ paśyanti rocanam divi sūryam vipaścitam //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a devo devān marcayasy antaś carasy arṇave /

Halfverse: c samānam agnim indhate tam viduḥ kavayaḥ pare //40// {4}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a avaḥ pareṇa para enāvareṇa padā vatsam bibratī gaur ud asthāt /

Halfverse: c sā kadrīcī kam svid ardham parāgāt kva svit sūte nahi yūthe asmin //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpady aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī /

Halfverse: c sahasrākṣarā bhuvanasya paṅktis tasyāḥ samudrā adhi vi kṣaranti //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a ārohan dyām amṛtaḥ prāva me vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c ut tvā yajñā brahmapūtā vahanty adhvagato harayas tvā vahanti //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a veda tat te amartya yat ta ākramaṇam divi /

Halfverse: c yat te sadhastham parame vyoman //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a sūryo dyām sūryaḥ pṛṭhivīm sūrya āpo’ti paśyati /

Halfverse: c sūryo bhūtasyaikam cakṣur ā ruroha divam mahīm //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a urvīr āsan paridhayo vedir bhūmir akalpata /

Halfverse: c tatraitāv agnī ādhatta himam ghramsam ca rohitaḥ //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a himam ghramsam cādhāya yūpān kṛtvā parvatān /

Halfverse: c varṣājyāv agnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a svarvido rohitasya brahmaṇāgniḥ sam idhyate /

Halfverse: c tasmād ghramsas tasmād dhimas tasmād yajño ‘jāyata //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a brahmaṇāgnī vāvṛdhānau brahmavṛddhau brahmāhutau /

Halfverse: c brahmeddhāv agnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a satye anyaḥ samāhito ‘psv a1nyaḥ sam idhyate /

Halfverse: c brahmeddhāv agnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ //50// {5}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a yam vātaḥ pariśumbhati yam vendro brahmaṇaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmeddhāvagnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a vedim bhūmim kalpayitvā divam kṛtvā dakṣiṇām /

Halfverse: c ghramsam tad agnim kṛtvā cakāra viśvam ātmanvad varṣeṇājyena rohitaḥ //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a varṣam ājam ghramso agnir vedir bhūmir akalpata /

Halfverse: c tatraitān parvatān agnir gīrbhir ūrdhvāṁ akalpayat //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a gīrbhir ūrdhvān kalpayitvā rohito bhūmim abravīt /

Halfverse: c tvayīdam sarvam jāyatām yad bhūtam yac ca bhāvyam //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a sa yajñaḥ prathamo bhūto bhavyo ajāyata /

Halfverse: c tasmād dha jajña idam sarvam yat kim cedam virocate rohitena ṛṣiṇābhṛtam //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a yaś ca gām padā sphurati pratyaṅ sūryam ca mehati /

Halfverse: c tasya vṛścāmi te mūlam na chāyām karavo ‘param //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a yo mābhichāyam atyeṣi mām cāgnim cāntarā /

Halfverse: c tasya vṛścāmi te mūlam na chāyām karavo ‘param //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a yo adya deva sūrya tvām ca mām cāntarāyati /

Halfverse: c duṣvapnyam tasmim chamalam duritāni ca mṛjmahe //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a mā pra gāma patho vayam mā yajñād indra sominaḥ /

Halfverse: c mānta sthur no arātayaḥ //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a yo yajñasya prasādhanas tantur deveṣv ātataḥ /

Halfverse: c tam āhutam aśīmahi //60// {6}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud asya ketavo divi śukrā bhrājanta īrate /

Halfverse: c ādityasya nṛcakṣaso mahivratasya mīḍhuṣaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a diśām prajñānām svarayantam arciṣā supakṣam āśum patayantam arṇave /

Halfverse: c stavāma sūryam bhuvanasya gopām yo raśmibhir diśa ābhāti sarvāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat prāṅ pratyaṅ svadhayā yāsi śībham nānārūpe ahanī karṣi māyayā /

Halfverse: c tad āditya mahi tat te mahi śravo yad eko viśvam pari bhūma jāyase //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vipaścitam taraṇim bhrājamānam vahanti yam haritaḥ sapta bahvīḥ /

Halfverse: c srutād yam attrir divam unnināya tam tvā paśyanti pariyāntam ājim //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mā tvā dabhan pariyāntam ājim svasti durgāṁ ati yāhi śībham /

Halfverse: c divam ca sūrya pṛthivīm ca devīm ahorātre vimimāno yad eṣi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a svasti te sūrya carase rathāya yenobhāv antau pariyāsi sadyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yam te vahanti harito vahiṣṭhāḥ śatam aśvā yadi vā sapta bahvīḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sukham sūrya ratham amśumantam syonam suvahnim adhi tiṣṭha vājinam /

Halfverse: c yam te vahanti harito vahiṣṭhāḥ śatam aśvā yadi vā sapta bahvīḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a sapta sūryo harito yātave rathe hiraṇyatvacaso bṛhatīr ayukta /

Halfverse: c amoci śukro rajasaḥ parastād vidhūya devas tamo divam āruhat //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ut ketunā bṛhatā deva āgann apāvṛk tamo ‘bhi jyotir aśrait /

Halfverse: c divyaḥ suparṇaḥ sa vīro vy akhyad aditeḥ putro bhuvanāni viśvā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a udyan raśmīn ā tanuṣe viśvā rupāṇi puṣyasi /

Halfverse: c ubhā samudrau kratunā vi bhāsi sarvāml lokān paribhūr bhrājamānaḥ //10// {7}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a pūrvāparam carato māyayaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto ‘rṇavam /

Halfverse: c viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭe hairaṇyair anyam harito vahanti //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a divi tvāttrir adhārayat sūryā māsāya kartave /

Halfverse: c sa eṣi sudhṛtas tapan viśvā bhūtāvacākaśat //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ubhāv antau sam arṣasi vatsaḥ sammātarāv iva /

Halfverse: c nanv ṝ3tad itaḥ purā brahma devā amī viduḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yat samudram anu śritam tat siṣāsati sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c adhvāsya vitato mahān pūrvaś cāparaś ca yaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a tam sam āpnoti jūtibhis tato nāpa cikitsati /

Halfverse: c tenāmṛtasya bhakṣam devānām nāva rundhate //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ud u tyam jātavedasam devam vahanti ketavaḥ /

Halfverse: c dṛśe viśvāya sūryam //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a apa tye tāyavo yathā nakṣatrā yanty aktubhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sūrāya viśvacakṣase //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a adṛśrann asya ketavo vi raśmayo janāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c bhrājanto agnayo yathā //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a taraṇir viśvadarśato jyotiṣkṛd asi sūrya /

Halfverse: c viśvam ā bhāsi rocana //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a pratyaṅ devānām viśaḥ pratyaṅṅ ud eṣi mānuṣīḥ /

Halfverse: c pratyaṅ viśvam svar dṛśe //20// {8}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a yenā pāvaka cakṣasā bhuraṇyantam janāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c tvam varuṇa paśyasi //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a vi dyām eṣi rajas pṛthv ahar mimāno aktubhiḥ /

Halfverse: c paśyan janmāni sūrya //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a sapta tvā harito rathe vahanti deva sūrya /

Halfverse: c śociṣkeśam vicakṣaṇam //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ayukta sapta śundhyuvaḥ sūro rathasya naptyaḥ /

Halfverse: c tābhir yāti svayuktibhiḥ //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a rohito divam āruhat tapasā tapasvī /

Halfverse: c sa yonim aiti sa u jāyate punaḥ sa devānām adhipatir babhūva //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a yo viśvacarṣaṇir uta viśvatomukho yo viśvataspāṇir uta viśvataspṛthaḥ /

Halfverse: c sam bāhubhyām bharati sam patatrair dyāvāpṛthivī janayan deva ekaḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a ekapād dvipado bhūyo vi cakrame dvipāt tripādam abhy ṝti paścāt /

Halfverse: c dvipād dha ṣaṭpado bhūyo vi cakrame ta ekapadas tanva1m sam āsate //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a atandro yāsyan harito yad āsthād dve rūpe kṛṇute rocamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c ketumān udyant sahamāno rajāmsi viśvā āditya pravato vi bhāsi //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a baṇ mahāṁ asi sūrya baḍ āditya mahāṁ asi /

Halfverse: c mahāms te mahato mahimā tvam āditya mahāṁ asi //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a rocase divi rocase antarikṣe pataṅga pṛthivyām rocase rocase apsv a1ntaḥ /

Halfverse: c ubhā samudrau rucyā vy āpitha devo devāsi mahiṣaḥ svarjit //30// {9}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a arvāṅ parastāt prayato vyadhva āśur vipaścit patayan pataṅgaḥ /

Halfverse: c viṣṇur vicittaḥ śavasādhitiṣṭhan pra ketunā sahate viśvam ejat //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a citrāś cikitvān mahiṣaḥ suparṇa ārocayan rodasī antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c ahorātre pari sūryam vasāne prāsya viśvā tirato vīryāṇi //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a tigmo vibhrājan tanva1m śiśāno ‘ramgamāsaḥ pravato rarāṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c jyotiṣmān pakṣī mahiṣo vayodhā viśvā āsthāt pradiśaḥ kalpamānaḥ //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a citram devānām ketur anīkam jyotiṣmān pradiśaḥ sūrya udyan /

Halfverse: c divākaro ‘ti dyumnais tamāmsi viśvātārīd duritāni śukraḥ //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a citram devānām ud agād anīkam cakṣur mitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ /

Halfverse: c āprād dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam sūrya ātmā jagatas tasthuṣaś ca //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a uccā patantam aruṇam suparṇam madhye divas taraṇim bhrājamānam /

Halfverse: c paśyama tvā savitāram yam āhur ajasram jyotir yad avindad attriḥ //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a divas pṛṣṭhe dhāvamānam suparṇam adityāḥ putram nāthakāma upa yāmi bhītaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ sūrya pra tira dīrgham āyur mā riṣāma sumatau te syāma //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a sahasrāhṇyam viyatāv asya pakṣau harer hamsasya patataḥ svargam /

Halfverse: c sa devānt sarvān urasy upadadya sampaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a rohitaḥ kālo abhavad rohito ‘gre prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c rohito yajñānām mukham rohitaḥ sva1r ābharat //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a rohito loko abhavad rohito ‘ty atapad divam /

Halfverse: c rohito raśmibhir bhūmim samudram anu sam carat //40// {10}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a sarvā diśaḥ sam acarad rohito ‘dhipatir divaḥ /

Halfverse: c divam samudram ād bhūmim sarvam bhūtam vi rakṣati //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a ārohan chukro bṛhatīr atandro dve rūpe kṛṇute rocamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c citraś cikitvān mahiṣo vātamāyā yāvato lokān abhi yad vibhāti //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a abhy a1nyad eti pary anyad asyate ‘horātrābhyām mahiṣaḥ kalpamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c sūryam vayam rajasi kṣiyantam gātuvidam havāmahe nādhamānāḥ //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a pṛthivīpro mahiṣo nādhamānasya gātur adabdhacakṣuḥ pari viśvam babhūva /

Halfverse: c viśvam sampaśyant suvidatro yajatra idam śṛṇotu yad aham bravīmi //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a pary asya mahimā pṛthivīm samudram jyotiṣā vibhrājan pari dyām antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c sarvam sampaśyant suvidatro yajatra idam śṛṇotu yad aham bravīmi //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a abodhy agniḥ samidhā janānām prati dhenum ivāyatīm uṣasam /

Halfverse: c yahvā iva pra vayām ujjihānāḥ pra bhānavaḥ sisrate nākam acha //46// {11}

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna yo drāpim kṛtvā bhuvanāni vaste /

Halfverse: c yasmin kṣiyanti pradiśaḥ ṣaḍ urvīr yāḥ pataṅgo anu vicākaśīti /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yasmād vātā ṛtuthā pavante yasmāt samudrā adhi vikṣaranti /

Halfverse: c tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: e ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo mārayati prāṇayati yasmāt prāṇanti bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: e ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yaḥ prāṇena dyāvāpṛthivī tarpayaty apānena samudrasya jaṭharam yaḥ piparti /

Halfverse: c tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: e ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasmin virāṭ parameṣṭhī prajāpatir agnir vaiśvānaraḥ saha paṅktyā śritaḥ /

Halfverse: c yaḥ parasya prāṇam paramasya teja ādade /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yasmin ṣaḍ urvīḥ pañca diśo adhiśritāś catasra āpo yajñasya trayo ‘kṣarāḥ /

Halfverse: c yo antarā rodasī kruddhaś cakṣuṣaikṣata /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yo annādo annapatir babhūva brahmaṇas patir uta yaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhūto bhaviṣyat bhuvanasya yas patiḥ /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ahorātrair vimitam trimśadaṅgam trayodaśam māsam yo nirmimīte /

Halfverse: c tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: e ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a kṛsṇam niyānam harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divam ut patanti /

Halfverse: c ta āvavṛtrant sadanād ṛtasya /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yat te candram kaśyapa rocanāvad yat samhitam puṣkalam citrabhānu yasmint sūryā ārpitāḥ sapta sākam /

Halfverse: c tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: e ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //10// {12}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a bṛhad enam anu vaste purastād rathamtaram prati gṛhṇāti paścāt /

Halfverse: c jyotir vasāne sadam apramādam /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a bṛhad anyataḥ pakṣa āsīd rathamtaram anyataḥ sabale sadhrīcī /

Halfverse: c yad rohitam ajanayanta devāḥ /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sa varuṇaḥ sāyam agnir bhavati sa mitro bhavati prātar udyan /

Halfverse: c sa savitā bhūtvāntarikṣeṇa yāti sa indro bhūtvā tapati madhyato divam /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a sahasrāhṇyam viyatāv asya pakṣau harer hamsasya patataḥ svargam /

Halfverse: c sa devānt sarvān urasy upadadya sampaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ayam sa devo apsv a1ntaḥ sahasramūlaḥ paruśāko attriḥ /

Halfverse: c ya idam viśvam bhuvanam jajāna /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a śukram vahanti harayo raghuṣyado devam divi varcasā bhrājamānam /

Halfverse: c yasyordhvā divam tanva1s tapanty arvāṅ suvarṇaiḥ paṭarair vi bhāti /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yenādityān haritaḥ samvahanti yena yajñena bahavo yanti prajānantaḥ /

Halfverse: c yad ekam jyotir bahudhā vibhāti /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a sapta yuñjanti ratham ekacakram eko aśvo vahati saptanāmā /

Halfverse: c trinābhi cakram ajaram anarvam yatremā viśvā bhuvanādhi tasthuḥ /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a aṣṭadhā yukto vahati vahnir ugraḥ pitā devānām janitā matīnām /

Halfverse: c ṛtasya tantum manasā mimānaḥ sarvā diśaḥ pavate mātariśvā /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a samyañcam tantum pradiśo ‘nu sarvā antar gāyatryām amṛtasya garbhe /

Halfverse: c tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: e ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //20// {13}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a nimrucas tisro vyuṣo ha tisras trīṇi rajāmsi divo aṅga tisrah /

Halfverse: c vidmā te agne tredhā janitram tredhā devānām janimāni vidma /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a vi ya aurṇot pṛthivīm jāyamāna ā samudram adadhāt antarikṣe /

Halfverse: c tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: e ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a tvam agne kratubhiḥ ketubhir hito3 ‘rkaḥ samiddha ud arocathā divi /

Halfverse: c kim abhy ārcan marutaḥ pṛśnimātaro yad rohitam ajanayanta devāḥ /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ya ātmadā baladā yasya viśva upāsate praśiṣam yasya devāḥ /

Halfverse: c yo3 ‘syeśe dvipado yaś catuṣpadaḥ /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a ekapād dvipado bhūyo vi cakrame dvipāt tripādam abhy ṝti paścāt /

Halfverse: c catuṣpāc cakre dvipadām abhisvare sampaśyan paṅktim upatiṣṭhamānaḥ /

Halfverse: e tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evam vidvāmsam brāhmaṇam jināti /

Halfverse: g ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a kṛṣṇāyaḥ putro arjuno rātryā vatso ‘jāyata /

Halfverse: c sa ha dyām adhi rohati ruho ruroha rohitaḥ //26// {14}

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1 {1}

Halfverse: a sa eti savitā svar divas pṛṣṭhe ‘vacākaśat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a raśmibhir nabha ābhṛtam mahendra ety āvṛtaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sa dhātā sa vidhartā sa vāyur nabha ucchritam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a so ‘ryamā sa varuṇaḥ sa rudraḥ sa mahādevaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a so agniḥ sa u sūryaḥ sa u eva mahāyamaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tam vatsā upa tiṣṭhanty ekaśīrṣāṇo yutā daśa //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a paścāt prāñca ā tanvanti yad udeti vi bhāsati //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tasyaiṣa māruto gaṇaḥ sa eti śikyākṛtaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a raśmibhir nabha ābhṛtam mahendra ety āvṛtaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tasyeme nava kośā viṣṭambhā navadhā hitāḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a sa prajābhyo vi paśyati yac ca prāṇati yac ca na //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a tam idam nigatam sahaḥ sa eṣa eka ekavṛd eka eva //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ete asmin devā ekavṛto bhavanti //13// {15}

Verse: 14 {2}

Halfverse: a kīrtiś ca yaśaś cāmbhaś ca nabhaś ca brāhmaṇavarcasam cānnam cānnādyam ca //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ya etam devam ekavṛtam veda //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a na dvitīyo na tṛtīyaś caturtho nāpy ucyate //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a na pañcamo na ṣaṣṭhaḥ saptamo nāpy ucyate //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a nāṣṭamo na navamo daśamo nāpy ucyate //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a sa sarvasmai vi paśyati yac ca prāṇati yac ca na //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a tam idam nigatam sahaḥ sa eṣa eka ekavṛd eka eva //20//
