Verse: 21

Halfverse: a sarve asmin devā ekavṛto bhavanti //21// {16}

Verse: 22 {3}

Halfverse: a brahma ca tapaś ca kīrtiś ca yaśaś cāmbhaś ca nabhaś ca brāhmaṇavarcasam cānnam cānnādyam ca //2//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a bhūtam ca bhavyam ca śraddhā ca ruciś ca svargaś ca svadhā ca //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ya etam devam ekavṛtam veda //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a sa eva mṛtyuḥ so3 ‘mṛtam so3 ‘bhva1m sa rakṣaḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a sa rudro vasuvanir vasudeye namovāke vaṣaṭkāro ‘nu samhitaḥ //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a tasyeme sarve yātava upa praśiṣam āsate //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a tasyāmū sarvā nakṣatrā vaśe candramasā saha //28// {17}

Verse: 29 {4}

Halfverse: a sa vā ahno ‘jāyata tasmād ahar ajāyata //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a sa vai rātryā ajāyata tasmād rātrir ajāyata //30//

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a sa vā antarikṣād ajāyata tasmād antarikṣam ajāyata //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a sa vai vāyor ajāyata tasmād vāyur ajāyata //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a sa vai divo ‘jāyata tasmād dyaur adhi ajāyata //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a sa vai digbhyo ‘jāyata tasmād diśo ‘jāyanta //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a sa vai bhūmer ajāyata tasmād bhūmir ajāyata //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a sa vā agner ajāyata tasmād agnir ajāyata //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a sa vā adbhyo ‘jāyata tasmād āpo ‘jāyanta //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a sa vā ṛgbhyo ‘jāyata tasmād ṛco ‘jāyanta //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a sa vai yajñād ajāyata tasmād yajño ‘jāyata //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a sa yajñas tasya yajñaḥ sa yajñasya śiras kṛtam //40//

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a sa stanayati sa vi dyotate sa u aśmānam asyati //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a pāpāya vā bhadrāya vā puruṣāyāsurāya vā //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a yad vā kṛṇoṣy oṣadhīr yad vā varṣasi bhadrayā yad vā janyam avīvṛdhaḥ //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a tāvāms te maghavan mahimopo te tanvaḥ śatam //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a upo te badhve baddhāni yadi vāsi nyarbudam //45// {18}

Verse: 46 {5}

Halfverse: a bhūyān indro namurād bhūyān indrāsi mṛtyubhyaḥ //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a bhūyān arātyāḥ śacyāḥ patis tvam indrāsi vibhūḥ prabhūr iti tvopāsmahe vayam //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a namas te astu paśyata paśya mā paśyata //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a annādyena yaśasā tejasā brāhmaṇavarcasena //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a ambho amo mahaḥ saha iti tvopāsmahe vayam //50//

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a ambho aruṇam rajatam rajaḥ saha iti tvopāsmahe vayam //51// {19}

Verse: 52 {6}

Halfverse: a uruḥ pṛthuḥ subhūr bhuva iti tvopāsmahe vayam //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a pratho varo vyaco loka iti tvopāsmahe vayam //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a bhavadvasur idadvasuḥ samyadvasur āyadvasur iti tvopāsmahe vayam //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a namas te astu paśyata paśya mā paśyata //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a annādyena yaśasā tejasā brāhmaṇavarcasena //56// {20}

## Book: 14

14

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a satyenottabhitā bhūmiḥ sūryeṇottabhitā dyauḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛtenādityās tiṣṭhanti divi somo adhi śritaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a somenādityā balinaḥ somena pṛthivī mahī /

Halfverse: c atho nakṣatrāṇām eṣām upasthe soma āhitaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a somam manyate papivān yat sampimṣanty oṣadhim /

Halfverse: c somam yam brahmāṇo vidur na tasyāśnāti pārthivaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yat tvā soma prapibanti tata ā pyāyase punaḥ /

Halfverse: c vāyuḥ somasya rakṣitā samānām māsa ākṛtiḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a āchadvidhānair gupito bārhataiḥ soma rakṣitaḥ /

Halfverse: c grāvṇām ic chṛṇvan tiṣṭhasi na te aśnāti pārthivaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a cittir ā upabarhaṇam cakṣur ā abhyañjanam /

Halfverse: c dyaur bhūmiḥ kośa āsīd yad ayāt sūryā patim //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a raibhy āsīd anudeyī nārāśamsī nyocanī /

Halfverse: c suryāyā bhadram id vāso gāthayati pariṣkṛtā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a stomā āsan pratidhayaḥ kurīram chanda opaśaḥ /

Halfverse: c sūryāyā aśvinā varāgnir āsīt purogavaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a somo vadhūyur abhavad aśvināstām ubhā varā /

Halfverse: c sūryām yat patye śamsantīm manasā savitādadāt //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a mano asyā ana āsīd dyaur āsīd uta chadiḥ /

Halfverse: c śukrāv anaḍvāhāv āstām yad ayāt sūryā patim //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ṛksāmābhyām abhihitau gāvau te sāmanāv aitām /

Halfverse: c śrotre te cakre āstām divi panthāś carācaraḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a śucī te cakre yātyā vyāno akṣa āhataḥ /

Halfverse: c ano manasmayam sūryārohat prayatī patim //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sūryāyā vahatuḥ prāgāt savitā yam avāsṛjat /

Halfverse: c maghāsu hanyante gāvaḥ phalgunīṣu vy ḥhyate //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yad aśvinā pṛchamānāv ayātam tricakreṇa vahatum sūryāyāḥ /

Halfverse: c kvaikam cakram vām āsīt kva deṣṭrāya tasthathuḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yad ayātam śubhas patī vareyam sūryām upa /

Halfverse: c viśve devā anu tad vām ajānan putraḥ pitaram avṛṇīta pūṣā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a dve te cakre sūrye brahmāṇa ṛtuthā viduḥ /

Halfverse: c athaikam cakram yad guhā tad addhātaya id viduḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a aryamaṇam yajāmahe subandhum pativedanam /

Halfverse: c urvārukam iva bandhanāt preto muñcāmi nāmutaḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a preto muñcāmi nāmutaḥ subaddhām amutas karam /

Halfverse: c yatheyam indra mīḍhvaḥ suputrā subhagāsati //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a pra tvā muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśād yena tvābadhnāt savitā suśevāḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛtasya yonau sukṛtasya loke syonam te astu sahasambhalāyai //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a bhagas tveto nayatu hastagṛhyāśvinā tvā pra vahatām rathena /

Halfverse: c gṛhān gacha gṛhapatnī yathāso vaśinī tvam vidatham ā vadāsi //20// {2}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a iha priyam prajāyai te sam ṛdhyatām asmin gṛhe gārhapatyāya jāgṛhi /

Halfverse: c enā patyā tanva1m sam spṛśasvātha jirvir vidatham ā vadāsi //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a ihaiva stam mā vi yauṣṭam viśvam āyur vy aśnutam /

Halfverse: c krīḍantau putrair naptṛbhir modamānau svastakau //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a pūrvāparam carato māyaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto ‘rṇavam /

Halfverse: c viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭa ṛtūmr anyo vidadhaj jāyase navaḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a navonavo bhavasi jāyamāno ‘hnām ketur uṣasām eṣy agram /

Halfverse: c bhāgam devebhyo vi dadhāsy āyan pra candramas tirase dīrgham āyuḥ //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a parā dehi śāmulyam brahmabhyo vi bhajā vasu /

Halfverse: c kṛtyaiṣā padvatī bhūtvā jāyā viśate patim //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a nīlalohitam bhavati kṛtyāsaktir vy ajyate /

Halfverse: c edhante asyā jñātayaḥ patir bandheṣu badhyate //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a aślīlā tanūr bhavati ruśatī pāpayāmuyā /

Halfverse: c patir yad vadhvo3 vāsasaḥ svam aṅgam abhyūrṇute //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a āśasanam viśasanam atho adhivikartanam /

Halfverse: c sūryāyāḥ paśya rūpāṇi tāni brahmota śumbhati //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a tṛṣṭam etat kaṭukam apāṣṭhavad viṣavan naitad attave /

Halfverse: c sūryām yo brahmā veda sa id vādhūyam arhati //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a sa it tat syonam harati brahmā vāsaḥ sumaṅgalam /

Halfverse: c prāyaścittim yo adhyeti yena jāyā na riṣyati //30// {3}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a yuvam bhagam sam bharatam samṛddham ṛtam vadantāv ṛtodyeṣu /

Halfverse: c brahmaṇas pate patim asyai rocaya cāru sambhalo vadatu vācam etām //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a ihed asātha na paro gamāthemam gāvaḥ prajayā vardhayātha /

Halfverse: c śubham yatīr usriyāḥ somavarcaso viśve devāḥ krann iha vo manāmsi //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a imam gāvaḥ prajayā sam viśāthāyam devānām na mināti bhāgam /

Halfverse: c asmai vaḥ pūṣā marutaś ca sarve asmai vo dhātā savitā suvāti //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a anṛkṣarā ṛjavaḥ santu panthano yebhiḥ sakhāyo yanti no vareyam /

Halfverse: c sam bhagena sam aryamṇā sam dhātā sṛjatu varcasā //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a yac ca varco akṣeṣu surāyām ca yad āhitam /

Halfverse: c yad goṣv aśvinā varcas tenemām varcasāvatam //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a yena mahānaghnyā jaghanam aśvinā yena vā surā /

Halfverse: c yenākṣā abhyaṣicyanta tenemām varcasāvatam //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a yo anidhmo dīdayad apsv a1ntar yam viprāsa īḍate adhvareṣu /

Halfverse: c apām napān madhumatīr apo dā yābhir indro vāvṛdhe vīryāvān //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a idam aham ruśantam grābham tanūdūṣim apohāmi /

Halfverse: c yo bhadro rocanas tam ud acāmi //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a āsyai brāhmaṇāḥ snapanīr harantv avīraghnīr ud ajantv āpaḥ /

Halfverse: c aryamṇo agnim pary etu pūṣan pratīkṣante śvaśuro devaraś ca //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a śam te hiraṇyam śam u santv āpaḥ śam methir bhavatu śam yugasya tardma /

Halfverse: c śam ta āpaḥ śatapavitrā bhavantu śam u patyā tanva1m sam spṛśasva //40// {4}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a khe rathasya khe ‘nasaḥ khe yugasya śatakrato /

Halfverse: c apālām indra triṣ pūtvākṛṇoḥ sūryatvacam //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a āśāsānā saumanasam prajām saubhāgyam rayim /

Halfverse: c patyur anuvratā bhūtvā sam nahyasvāmṛtāya kam //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a yathā sindhur nadīnām sāmrājyam suṣuve vṛṣā /

Halfverse: c evā tvam samrājñy edhi patyur astam paretya //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a samrājñy edhi śvaśureṣu samrājñy uta devṛṣu /

Halfverse: c nanānduḥ samrājñy edhi samrājñy uta śvaśrvāḥ //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a yā akṛntann avayan yāś ca tatnire yā devīr antāṁ abhito ‘dadanta /

Halfverse: c tās tvā jarase sam vyayantv āyuṣmatīdam pari dhatsva vāsaḥ //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a jīvam rudanti vi nayanty adhvaram dīrghām anu prasitim dīdhyur naraḥ /

Halfverse: c vāmam pitṛbhyo ya idam samīrire mayaḥ patibhyo janaye pariṣvaje //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a syonam dhruvam prajāyai dhārayāmi te ‘śmānam devyāḥ pṛthivyā upasthe /

Halfverse: c tam ā tiṣṭhānumādyā suvarcā dīrgham ta āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a yenāgnir asyā bhūmyā hastam jagrāha dakṣiṇam /

Halfverse: c tena gṛhṇāmi te hastam mā vyathiṣṭhā mayā saha prajayā ca dhanena ca //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a devas te savitā hastam gṛhṇātu somo rājā suprajasam kṛṇotu /

Halfverse: c agniḥ subhagām jatavedāḥ patye patnīm jaradaṣṭim kṛṇotu //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a gṛhṇāmi te saubhagatvāya hastam mayā patyā jaradaṣṭir yathāsaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhago aryamā savitā puramdhir mahyam tvādur gārhapatyāya devāḥ //50// {5}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a bhagas te hastam agrahīt savitā hastam agrahīt /

Halfverse: c patnī tvam asi dharmaṇāham gṛhapatis tava //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a mameyam astu poṣyā mahyam tvādād bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c mayā patyā prajāvati sam jīva śaradaḥ śatam //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a tvaṣṭā vāso vy adadhāc chubhe kam bṛhaspateḥ praśiṣā kavīnām /

Halfverse: c tenemām nārīm savitā bhagaś ca sūryām iva pari dhattām prajayā //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a indrāgnī dyāvāpṛthivī mātariśvā mitrāvaruṇā bhago aśvinobhā /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir maruto brahma soma imām nārim prajayā vardhayantu //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatiḥ prathamaḥ sūryāyāḥ śīrṣe keśāṁ akalpayat /

Halfverse: c tenemām aśvinā nārīm patye sam śobhayāmasi //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a idam tad rūpam yad avasta yoṣā jāyām jijñāse manasā carantīm /

Halfverse: c tām anv artiṣye sakhibhir navagvaiḥ ka imān vidvān vi cacarta pāśān //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a aham vi ṣyāmi mayi rūpam asyā vedad it paśyan manasaḥ kulāyam /

Halfverse: c na steyam admi manasod amucye svayam śrathnāno varuṇasya pāśān //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a pra tvā muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśād yena tvābadhnāt savitā suśevāḥ /

Halfverse: c urum lokam sugam atra panthām kṛṇomi tubhyam sahapatnyai vadhu //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a ud yachadhvam apa rakṣo hanāthemam nārīm sukṛte dadhāta /

Halfverse: c dhātā vipaścit patim asyai viveda bhago rājā pura etu prajānan //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a bhagas tatakṣa caturaḥ pādān bhagas tatakṣa catvāry uṣpalāni /

Halfverse: c tvaṣṭā pipeśa madhyato ‘nu vardhrānt sā no astu sumaṅgalī //60//

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a sukimśukam vahatum viśvarūpam hiraṇyavarṇam suvṛtam sucakram /

Halfverse: c ā roha sūrye amṛtasya lokam syonam patibhyo vahatum kṛṇu tvam //61//

Verse: 62

Halfverse: a abhrātṛghnīm varuṇāpaśughnīm bṛhaspate /

Halfverse: c indrāpatighnīm putriṇīm āsmabhyam savitar vaha //62//

Verse: 63

Halfverse: a mā himsiṣṭam kumārya1m sthūṇe devakṛte pathi /

Halfverse: c śālāyā devyā dvāram syonam kṛṇmo vadhūpatham //63//

Verse: 64

Halfverse: a brahmāparam yujyatām brahma pūrvam brahmāntato madhyato brahma sarvataḥ /

Halfverse: c anāvyādhām devapurām prapadya śivā syonā patiloke vi rāja //64// {6}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tubhyam agre pary avahant sūryām vahatunā saha /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ patibhyo jāyām dā agne prajayā saha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a punaḥ patnīm agnir adād āyuṣā saha varcasā /

Halfverse: c dīrghāyur asyā yaḥ patir jīvāti śaradaḥ śatam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a somasya jāyā prathamam gandharvas te ‘paraḥ patiḥ /

Halfverse: c tṛtīyo agniṣ ṭe patis turīyas te manuṣyajāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a somo dadad gandharvāya gandharvo dadad agnaye /

Halfverse: c rayim ca putrāms cādād agnir mahyam atho imām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā vām agant sumatir vājinīvasū ny aśvinā hṛtsu kāmā aramsata /

Halfverse: c abhūtam gopā mithunā śubhas patī priyā aryamṇo duryāṁ aśīmahi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sā mandasānā manasā śivena rayim dhehi sarvavīram vacasyam /

Halfverse: c sugam tīrtham suprapāṇam śubhas patī sthāṇum pathiṣṭhām apa durmatim hatam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yā oṣadhayo yā nadyo3 yāni kṣetrāṇi yā vanā /

Halfverse: c tās tvā vadhu prajāvatīm patye rakṣantu rakṣasaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a emam panthām arukṣāma sugam svastivāhanam /

Halfverse: c yasmin vīro na riṣyaty anyeṣām vindate vasu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a idam su me naraḥ śṛṇuta yayāśiṣā dampatī vāmam aśnutaḥ /

Halfverse: c ye gandharvā apsarasaś ca devīr eṣu vānaspatyeṣu ye ‘dhi tasthuḥ /

Halfverse: e syonās te asyai vadhvaṅ bhavantu mā himsiṣur vahatum uhyamānam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ye vadhvaś candram vahatum yakṣmā yanti janāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c punas tān yajñiyā devā nayantu yata āgatāḥ //10// {7}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a mā vidan paripanthino ya āsīdanti dampatī /

Halfverse: c sugena durgam atītām apa drāntv arātayaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sam kāśayāmi vahatum brahmaṇā gṛhair aghoreṇa cakṣuṣā mitriyeṇa /

Halfverse: c paryāṇaddham viśvarūpam yad asti syonam patibhyaḥ savitā tat kṛṇotu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a śivā nārīyam astam āgann imam dhātā lokam asyai dideśa /

Halfverse: c tām aryamā bhago aśvinobhā prajāpatiḥ prajayā vardhayantu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ātmanvaty urvarā nārīyam āgan tasyām naro vapata bījam asyām /

Halfverse: c sā vaḥ prajām janayad vakṣaṇābhyo bibhratī dugdham ṛṣabhasya retaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a prati tiṣṭha virāḍ asi viṣṇur iveha sarasvati /

Halfverse: c sinīvāli pra jāyatām bhagasya sumatāv asat //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ud va ūrmiḥ śamyā hantv āpo yoktrāṇi muñcata /

Halfverse: c māduṣkṛtau vyṝnasāv aghnyāv aśunam āratām //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a aghoracakṣur apatighnī syonā śagmā suśevā suyamā gṛhebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c vīrasūr devṛkāmā sam tvayaidhiṣīmahi sumasyamānā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a adevṛghny apatighnīhaidhi śivā paśubhyaḥ suyamā suvarcāh /

Halfverse: c prajāvatī vīrasūr devṛkāmā syonemam agnim gārhapatyam saparya //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭhetaḥ kim ichantīdam āgā aham tveḍe abhibhūḥ svād gṛhāt /

Halfverse: c śūnyaiṣī nirṛte yājaganthot tiṣṭhārāte pra pata meha ramsthāḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yadā gārhapatyam asaparyait pūrvam agnim vadhūr iyam /

Halfverse: c adhā sarasvatyai nāri pitṛbhyaś ca namas kuru //20// {8}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a śarma varmaitad ā harāsyai nāryā upastire /

Halfverse: c sinīvāli pra jāyatām bhagasya sumatāv asat //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yam balbajam nyasyatha carma copastṛṇīthana /

Halfverse: c tad ā rohatu suprajā yā kanyā vindate patim //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a upa stṛṇīhi balbajam adhi carmaṇi rohite /

Halfverse: c tatropaviśya suprajā imam agnim saparyatu //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ā roha carmopa sīdāgnim eṣa devo hanti rakṣāmsi sarvā /

Halfverse: c iha prajām janaya patye asmai sujyaiṣṭhyo bhavat putras ta eṣaḥ //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a vi tiṣṭhantām mātur asyā upasthān nānārūpāḥ paśavo jāyamānāḥ /

Halfverse: c sumaṅgaly upa sīdemam agnim sampatnī prati bhūṣeha devān //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a sumaṅgalī prataraṇī gṛhāṇām suśevā patye śvaśurāya śambhūḥ /

Halfverse: c syonā śvaśrvai pra gṛhān viśemān //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a syonā bhava śvaśurebhyaḥ syonā patye gṛhebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c syonāsyai sarvasyai viśe syonā puṣṭāyaiṣām bhava //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a sumaṅgalīr iyam vadhūr imām sameta paśyata /

Halfverse: c saubhāgyam asyai dattvā daurbhāgyair viparetana //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a yā durhārdo yuvatayo yāś ceha jaratīr api /

Halfverse: c varco nv asyai sam dattāthāstam viparetana //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a rukmaprastaraṇam vahyam viśvā rūpāṇi bibhratam /

Halfverse: c ārohat sūryā sāvitrī bṛhate saubhagāya kam //30// {9}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a ā roha talpam sumanasyamāneha prajām janaya patye asmai /

Halfverse: c indrāṇīva subudhā budhyamānā jyotiragrā uṣasaḥ prati jāgarāsi //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a devā agre ny apadyanta patnīḥ sam aspṛśanta tanvas tanūbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sūryeva nāri viśvarūpā mahitvā prajāvatī patyā sam bhaveha //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭheto viśvāvaso namaseḍāmahe tvā /

Halfverse: c jāmim icha pitṛṣadam nyaktām sa te bhāgo januṣā tasya viddhi //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a apsarasaḥ sadhamādam madanti havirdhānam antarā sūryam ca /

Halfverse: c tās te janitram abhi tāḥ parehi namas te gandharvartunā kṛṇomi //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a namo gandharvasya namase namo bhāmāya cakṣuṣe ca kṛṇmaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvāvaso brahmaṇā te namo ‘bhi jāyā apsarasaḥ parehi //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a rāyā vayam sumanasaḥ syāmod ito gandharvam āvīvṛtāma /

Halfverse: c agant sa devaḥ paramam sadhastham aganma yatra pratiranta āyuḥ //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a sam pitarāv ṛtviye sṛjethām mātā pitā ca retaso bhavāthaḥ /

Halfverse: c marya iva yoṣām adhi rohayainām prajām kṛṇvāthām iha puṣyatam rayim //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a tām pūṣam chivatamām erayasva yasyām bījam manuṣyā3 vapanti /

Halfverse: c yā na ūrū uśatī viśrayāti yasyām uśantaḥ praharema śepaḥ //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a ā rohorum upa dhatsva hastam pari ṣvajasva jāyām sumanasyamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c prajām kṛṇvāthām iha modamānau dīrgham vām āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a ā vām prajām janayatu prajāpatir ahorātrābhyām sam anaktv aryamā /

Halfverse: c adurmaṅgalī patilokam ā viśemam śam no bhava dvipade śam catuṣpade //40// {10}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a devair dattam manunā sākam etad vādhūyam vāso vadhvaś ca vastram /

Halfverse: c yo brahmaṇe cikituṣe dadāti sa id rakṣāmsi talpāni hanti //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a yam me datto brahmabhāgam vadhūyor vādhūyam vāso vadhvaś ca vastram /

Halfverse: c yuvam brahmaṇe ‘numanyamānau bṛhaspate sākam indraś ca dattam //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a syonād yoner adhi badhyamānau hasāmudau mahasā modamānau /

Halfverse: c sugū suputrau sugṛhau tarātho jīvāv uṣaso vibhātīḥ //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a navam vasānaḥ surabhiḥ suvāsā udāgām jīva uṣaso vibhātīḥ /

Halfverse: c āṇḍāt patatrīvāmukṣi viśvasmād enasas pari //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a śumbhanī dyāvāpṛthivī antisumne mahivrate /

Halfverse: c āpaḥ sapta susruvur devīs tā no muñcantv amhasaḥ //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a sūryāyai devebhyo mitrāya varuṇāya ca /

Halfverse: c ye bhūtasya pracetasas tebhya idam akaram namaḥ //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a ya ṛte cid abhiśriṣaḥ purā jatrubhya ātṛdaḥ /

Halfverse: c samdhātā samdhim maghavā purūvasur niṣkartā vihrutam punaḥ //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a apāsmat tama uchatu nīlam piśaṅgam uta lohitam yat /

Halfverse: c nirdahanī yā pṛṣātaky asmin tām sthāṇāv adhy ā sajāmi //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a yāvatīḥ kṛtyā upavāsane yāvanto rājño varuṇasya pāśāḥ /

Halfverse: c vyṛddhayo yā asamṛddhayo yā asmin tā sthāṇāv adhi sādayāmi //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a yā me priyatamā tanūḥ sā me bibhāya vāsasaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasyāgre tvam vanaspate nīvim kṛṇuṣva mā vayam riṣāma //50// {11}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a ye antā yāvatīḥ sico ya otavo ye ca tantavaḥ /

Halfverse: c vāso yat patnībhir utam tan na syonam upa spṛśāt //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a uśatīḥ kanyalā imāḥ pitṛlokāt patim yatīḥ /

Halfverse: c ava dīkṣām asṛkṣata svāhā //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭām viśve devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c varco goṣu praviṣṭam yat tenemām sam sṛjāmasi //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭām viśve devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c tejo goṣu praviṣṭam yat tenemām sam sṛjāmasi //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭām viśve devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c bhajo goṣu praviṣṭo yas tenemām sam sṛjāmasi //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭām viśve devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c yaśo goṣu praviṣṭam yat tenemām sam sṛjāmasi //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭām viśve devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c payo goṣu praviṣṭam yat tenemām sam sṛjāmasi //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭām viśve devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c raso goṣu praviṣṭo yas tenemām sam sṛjāmasi //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a yadīme keśino janā gṛhe te samanartiṣū rodena kṛṇvanto ‘gham /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a yadīyam duhitā tava vikeśy arudad gṛhe rodena kṛṇvaty agham /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām //60// {12}

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a yaj jāmayo yad yuvatayo gṛhe te samanartiṣū rodena kṛṇvatīr agham /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām //61//

Verse: 62

Halfverse: a yat te prajāyām paśuṣu yad vā gṛheṣu niṣṭhitam aghakṛdbhir agham kṛtam /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām //62//

Verse: 63

Halfverse: a iyam nāry upa brūte pūlyāny āvapantikā /

Halfverse: c dīrghāyur astu me patir jīvāti śaradaḥ śatam //63//

Verse: 64

Halfverse: a ihemāv indra sam nuda cakravākeva dampatī /

Halfverse: c prajayainau svastakau viśvam āyur vy aśnutām //64//

Verse: 65

Halfverse: a yad āsandyām upadhāne yad vopavāsane kṛtam /

Halfverse: c vivāhe kṛtyām yām cakrur āsnāne tām ni dadhmasi //65//

Verse: 66

Halfverse: a yad duṣkṛtam yac chamalam vivāhe vahatau ca yat /

Halfverse: c tat sambhalasya kambale mṛjmahe duritam vayam //66//

Verse: 67

Halfverse: a sambhale malam sādayitvā kambale duritam vayam /

Halfverse: c abhūma yajñiyāḥ śuddhāḥ pra ṇa āyūmṣi tāriṣat //67//

Verse: 68

Halfverse: a kṛtrimaḥ kaṇṭakaḥ śatadan ya eṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c apāsyāḥ keśyam malam apa śīrṣaṇyam likhāt //68//

Verse: 69

Halfverse: a aṅgādaṅgād vayam asyā apa yakṣmam ni dadhmasi /

Halfverse: c tan mā prāpat pṛthivīm mota devān divam mā prāpad urv antarikṣam /

Halfverse: e apo mā prāpan malam etad agne yamam mā prāpat pitṝmś ca sarvān //69//

Verse: 70

Halfverse: a sam tvā nahyāmi payasā pṛthivyāḥ sam tvā nahyāmi payasauṣadhīnām /

Halfverse: c sam tvā nahyāmi prajayā dhanena sā samnaddhā sanuhi vājam emam //70// {13}

Verse: 71

Halfverse: a amo ‘ham asmi sā tvam sāmāham asmy ṛk tvam dyaur aham pṛthivī tvam /

Halfverse: c tāv iha sam bhavāva prajām ā janayāvahai //71//

Verse: 72

Halfverse: a janiyanti nāv agravaḥ putriyanti sudānavaḥ /

Halfverse: c ariṣṭāsū sacevahi bṛhate vājasātaye //72//

Verse: 73

Halfverse: a ye pitaro vadhūdarśā imam vahatum āgaman /

Halfverse: c te asyai vadhvaṅ sampatnyai prajāvac charma yachantu //73//

Verse: 74

Halfverse: a yedam pūrvāgan raśanāyamānā prajām asyai draviṇam ceha dattvā /

Halfverse: c tām vahantv agatasyānu panthām virāḍ iyam suprajā aty ajaiṣīt //74//

Verse: 75

Halfverse: a pra budhyasva subudhā budhyamānā dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya /

Halfverse: c gṛhān gacha gṛhapatnī yathāso dīrgham ta āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu //75// {14}

## Book: 15

15

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vrātya āsīd īyamāna eva sa prajāpatim sam airayat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sa prajāpatiḥ suvarṇam ātmann apaśyat tat prājanayat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tad ekam abhavat tal lalāmam abhavat tan mahad abhavat taj jyeṣṭham abhavat tad /

Halfverse: c brahmābhavat tat tapo ‘bhavat tat satyam abhavat tena prājāyata //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a so ‘vardhata sa mahān abhavat sa mahādevo ‘bhavat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sa devānām īśām pary ait sa īśāno ‘bhavat //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sa ekavrātyo bhavat sa dhanur ādatta tad evendradhanuḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a nīlam asyodaram lohitam pṛṣṭham //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a nīlenaivāpriyam bhrātṛvyam prorṇoti lohitena dviṣantam vidhyatīti brahmavādino vadanti //8//

Hymn: 2:1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sa ud atiṣṭhat sa prācīm diśam anu vy acalat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tam bṛhac ca rathamtaram cādityāś ca viśve ca devā anuvyacalan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bṛhate ca vai sa rathamtarāya cādityebhyaś ca viśvebhyaś ca devebhya ā vṛścate ya evam vidvāmsam vrātyam upavadati //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a bṛhataś ca vai sa rathamtarasya cādityānām ca viśveṣām ca devānām priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tasya prācyām diśi śraddhā pumścalī mitro māgadho vijñānam vaso ‘har uṣṇīṣam rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a bhūtam ca bhaviṣyac ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ainam kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evam veda //8// \[1\]

Hymn: 2:2

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a sa ud atiṣṭhat sa dakṣiṇām diśam anu vy acalat //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tam yajñāyajñiyam ca vai sa vāmadevyam ca yajñaś ca yajamānaś ca paśavaś cānuvyacalan //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yajñāyajñiyāya ca vai sa vāmadevyāya ca yajñāya ca yajamānāya ca paśubhyaś cā vṛścate ya evam vidvāmsam vrātyam upavadati //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yajñāyajñiyasya ca vai sa vāmadevyasya ca yajñasya ca yajamānasya ca paśūnām ca priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a tasya dakṣiṇāyām diśy ḥṣāḥ pumścalī mantro māgadho vijñānam vāso ‘har uṣṇīṣam rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a amāvāsyā ca paurṇamāsī ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham /

Halfverse: c mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau /

Halfverse: e ainam kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evam veda //14// \[2\]

Hymn: 2:3

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a sa ud atiṣṭhat sa pratīcīm diśam anu vy acalat //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a tam vairūpam ca vairājam cāpaś ca varuṇaś ca rājānuvyacalan //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a vairūpāya ca vai sa vairājāya cādbhyaś ca varuṇāya ca rājña ā vṛścate ya evam vidvāmsam vrātyam upavadati //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a vairūpasya ca vai sa vairājasya cāpām ca varuṇasya ca rājñaḥ priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a tasya pratīcyām diśīrā pumścalī haso māgadho vijñānam vāso ‘har uṣṇīṣam rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ahaś ca rātrī ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham /

Halfverse: c mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau /

Halfverse: e ainam kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evam veda //20// \[3\]

Hymn: 2:4

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a sa ud atiṣṭhat sa udīcīm diśam anu vy acalat //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a tam śyaitam ca naudhasam ca saptarṣayaś ca somaś ca rājānuvyacalan //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a śyaitāya ca vai sa naudhasāya ca saptarṣibhyaś ca somāya ca rājña ā vṛścate ya evam vidvāmsam vrātyam upavadati //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a śyaitasya ca vai sa naudhasasya ca saptarṣīṇām ca somasya ca rājñaḥ priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a tasyodīcyām diśi vidyut pumścalī stanayitnur māgadho vijñānam vāso ‘har uṣṇīṣam rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a śrutam ca viśrutam ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ /

Halfverse: c kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a ainam kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evam veda //28// \[4\]

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sa samvatsaram ūrdhvo ‘tiṣṭhat tam devā abruvan vrātya kim nu tiṣṭhasīti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a so ‘bravīd āsandīm me sam bharantv iti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tasmai vrātyāyāsandīm sam abharan //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tasyā grīṣmaś ca vasantaś ca dvau pādāv āstām śarac ca varṣāś ca dvau //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bṛhac ca rathamtaram cānūcyṝ3 āstām yajñāyajñiyam ca vāmadevyam ca tiraścyṝ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ṛcaḥ prāñcas tantavo yajūmṣi tiryañcaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a veda āstaraṇam brahmopabarhaṇam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a sāmāsāda udgītho ‘paśrayaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tām āsandīm vrātya ārohat //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tasya devajanāḥ pariṣkandā āsant samkalpāḥ prahāyyā3 viśvāni bhūtāny upasadaḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Hymn: 4:1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tasmai prācyā diśaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vāsantau māsau goptārāv akurvan bṛhac ca rathamtaram cānuṣṭhātārau //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vāsantāv enam māsau prācyā diśo gopāyato bṛhac ca rathamtaram cānu tiṣṭhato ya evam veda //3// \[1\]

Hymn: 4:2

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tasmai dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a graiṣmau māsau goptārāv akurvan yajñāyajñiyam ca vāmadevyam cānuṣṭhātārau //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a graiṣmāv enam māsau dakṣiṇāyā diśo gopāyato yajñāyajñiyam ca vāmadevyam cānu tiṣṭhato ya evam veda //6// \[2\]

Hymn: 4:3

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tasmai pratīcyā diśaḥ //7//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tasmā udīcyā diśaḥ //10//

Halfverse: a śāradāv enam māsāv udīcyā diśo gopāyataḥ śyaitam ca naudhasam cānu tiṣṭhato ya evam veda //12// \[4\]

Halfverse: a tasmai dhruvāyā diśaḥ //13//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a haimanāv enam māsau dhruvāyā diśo gopāyato bhūmiś cāgniś cānu tiṣṭhato ya evam veda //15// \[5\]

Hymn: 5:1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tasmai prācyā diśo antardeśād bhavam iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan //1// \[1\]

Hymn: 5:1

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a bhava enam iṣvāsaḥ prācyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nainam śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evam veda //3// \[1\]

Hymn: 5:2

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tasmai dakṣiṇāyā diśo antardeśāc charvam iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śarva enam iśvāso dakṣiṇāyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainam śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evam veda //5// \[2\]

Hymn: 5:3

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a paśupatir enam iṣvāsaḥ pratīcyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainam śarvo na bhavo neśano nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evam veda //7// \[3\]

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a nainam śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evam veda //16// \[7\]

Hymn: 6:1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sa dhruvām diśam anu vy acalat //1//

Verse: 2

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bhūmeś ca vai so3 ‘gneś cauṣadhīnām ca vanaspatīnām ca vānaspatyānām /

Halfverse: c ca vīrudhām ca priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //3// \[1\]

Hymn: 6:2

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sa ūrdhvām diśam anu vy acalat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tam ṛtam ca satyam ca sūryaś ca candraś ca nakṣatrāṇi cānuvyacalan //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ṛtasya ca vai sa satyasya ca sūryasya ca candrasya ca nakṣatrāṇām ca /

Halfverse: c priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //6// \[2\]

Hymn: 6:3

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sa uttamām diśam anu vy acalat //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tam ṛcaś ca sāmāni ca yajūmṣi ca brahma cānuvyacalan //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ṛcām ca vai sa sāmnām ca yajuṣām ca brahmaṇaś ca priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //9// \[3\]

Hymn: 6:4

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a sa bṛhatīm diśam anu vy acalat //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tam itihāsaś ca purāṇam ca gāthāś ca nārāśamsīś cānuvyacalan //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a itihāsasya ca vai sa purāṇasya ca gāthānām ca nārāśamsīnām ca priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //12// \[4\]

Hymn: 6:5

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sa paramām diśam anu vy acalat //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a tam āhavanīyaś ca gārhapatyaś ca dakṣiṇāgniś ca yajñaś ca yajamānaś ca paśavaś cānuvyacalan //14//

Halfverse: a āhavanīyasya ca vai sa gārhapatyasya ca dakṣiṇāgneś ca yajñasya ca yajamānasya ca paśūnām ca priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //15// \[5\]

Hymn: 6:6

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a so ‘nādiṣṭām diśam anu vy acalat //16//

Halfverse: a tam ṛtavaś cārtavāś ca lokāś ca laukyāś ca māsāś cārdhamāsāś cāhorātre cānuvyacalan //17//

Halfverse: a so ‘nāvṛttām diśam anu vy acalat tato nāvartsyann amanyata //19//

Verse: 20

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a diteś ca vai so ‘diteś ceḍāyāś cendrāṇyāś ca priyam dhāma bhavati ya evam veda //21// \[7\]

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tad yasyaivam vidvān vrātyo rājño ‘tithir gṛhān āgachet //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ato vai brahma ca kṣatram cod atiṣṭhatām te abrūtām kam pra viśāveti //3//

Halfverse: a iyam vā u pṛthivī bṛhaspatir dyaur evendraḥ //6//

Halfverse: a yaḥ pṛthivīm bṛhaspatim agnim brahma veda //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ainam indriyam gachatīndriyavān bhavati //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ya ādityam kṣatram divam indram veda //11//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tad yasyaivam vidvān vrātyo ‘tithir gṛhān āgachet //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a svayam enam abhyudetya brūyād vrātya kvāvātsīr vrātyodakam vrātya tarpayantu vrātya yathā te priyam tathāstu vrātya yathā te vaśas tathāstu vrātya yathā te nikāmas tathāstv iti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad enam āha vrātya kvāvātsīr iti patha eva tena devayānān ava runddhe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad enam āha vrātyodakam ity apa eva tenāva runddhe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yad enam āha vrātya tarpayantv iti prāṇam eva tena varṣīyāmsam kurute //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yad enam āha vrātya yathā te priyam tathāstv iti priyam eva tenāva runddhe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ainam priyam gachati priyaḥ priyasya bhavati ya evam veda //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yad enam āha vrātya yathā te vaśas tathāstv iti vaśam eva tenāva runddhe //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ainam vaśo gachati vaśī vaśinām bhavati ya evam veda //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yad enam āha vrātya yathā te nikāmas tathāstv iti nikāmam eva tenāva runddhe //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ainam nikāmo gachati nikāme nikāmasya bhavati ya evam veda //11//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tad yasyaivam vidvān vrātya uddhṛteṣv agniṣu adhiśrite ‘gnihotre ‘tithir gṛhān āgachet //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a svayam enam abhyudetya brūyād vrātyāti sṛja hoṣyāmīti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sa cātisṛjej juhuyān na cātisṛjen na juhuyāt //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sa ya evam viduṣā vrātyenātisṛsṭo juhoti //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pra pitṛyāṇam panthām jānāti pra devayānam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a na deveṣv ā vṛścate hutam asya bhavati //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a pary asyāsmiml loka āyatanam śiṣyate ya evam viduṣā vrātyenātisṛṣṭo juhoti //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a atha ya evam viduṣā vrātyenānatisṛṣṭo juhoti //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a na pitṛyāṇam panthām jānāti na devayānam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ā deveṣu vṛścate ahutam asya bhavati //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a nāsyāsmiml loka āyatanam śiṣyate ya evam viduṣā vrātyenānatisṛṣṭo juhoti //11//

Halfverse: a tad yasyaivam vidvān vrātya ekām rātrim atithir gṛhe vasati //1//

Halfverse: a ye ‘ntarikṣe punyā lokās tān eva tenāva runddhe //4// \[2\]

Halfverse: a tad yasyaivam vidvān vrātyas tṛtīyām rātrim atithir gṛhe vasati //5//

Halfverse: a ye divi punyā lokās tān eva tenāva runddhe //6// \[3\]

Hymn: 13:4

Verse: 7

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a manasānnādenānnam atti ya evam veda //2// \[1\]

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a balenānnādenānnam atti ya evam veda //4// \[2\]

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a adbhir annādībhir annam atti ya evam veda //6// \[3\]

Hymn: 15

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya //1//

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya ṣaṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ priyo nāma ta ime paśavaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya saptamaḥ prāṇo ‘parimito nāma tā prajāḥ //9//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya prathamo ‘pānaḥ sā paurṇamāsī //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya dvitīyo ‘pānaḥ sāṣṭakā /

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya tṛtīyo ‘pānaḥ sāmāvāsyā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya caturtho ‘pānaḥ sā śraddhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya pañcamo ‘pānaḥ sā dīkṣā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya ṣaṣṭo ‘pānaḥ sa yajñaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya saptamo ‘pānas tā imā dakṣiṇāḥ //7//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya prathamo vyānaḥ seyam bhūmiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya dvitīyo vyānas tad antarikṣam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya tṛtīyo vyānaḥ sā dyauḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya caturtho vyānas tāni nakṣatrāṇi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya pañcamo vyānas ta ṛtavaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya ṣaṣṭho vyānas ta ārtavāḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yo ‘sya saptamo vyānaḥ sa samvatsaraḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c samānam artham pari yanti devāḥ samvatsaram vā etad ṛtavo ‘nupariyanti vrātyam ca //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c yad ādityam abhisamviśanty amāvāsyām caiva paurṇamāsīm ca //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya /

Halfverse: c ekam tad eṣām amṛtatvam ity āhutir eva //10//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tasya vrātyasya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad asya dakṣiṇam akṣy asau sa ādityo yad asya savyam akṣy asau sa candramāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo ‘sya dakṣiṇaḥ karṇo ‘yam so agnir yo ‘sya savyaḥ karṇo ‘yam sa pavamānaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ahorātre nāsike ditiś cāditiś ca śīrṣakapāle samvatsaraḥ śiraḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ahnā pratyaṅ vrātyo rātryā prāṅ namo vrātyāya //5//

## Book: 16

16

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a atisṛṣṭo apām vṛṣabho ‘tisṛṣṭā agnayo divyāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a rujan parirujan mṛṇan pramṛṇan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mroko manohā khano nirdāha ātmadūṣis tanūdūṣiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a idam tam ati sṛjāmi tam mābhyavanikṣi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi yam vayam dviṣmaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a apām agram asi samudram vo ‘bhyavasṛjāmi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yo3 ‘psv a1gnir ati tam sṛjāmi mrokam khanim tanūdūṣim //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yo va āpo ‘gnir āviveśa sa eṣa yad vo ghoram tad etat //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a indrasya va indriyeṇābhi ṣiñcet //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ariprā āpo apa ripram asmat //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a prāsmad eno vahantu pra duṣvapnyam vahantu //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a śivena mā cakṣuṣā paśyatāpaḥ śivayā tanvopa spṛśata tvacam me //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a śivān agnīn apsuṣado havāmahe mayi kṣatram varca ā dhatta devīḥ //13//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nir durarmaṇya ūrjā madhumatī vāk //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a madhumatī stha madhumatīm vācam udeyam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a upahūto me gopāḥ upahūto gopīthaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a suśrutau karṇau bhadraśrutau karṇau bhadram ślokam śrūyāsam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a suśrutiś ca mopaśrutiś ca mā hāsiṣṭām sauparṇam cakṣur ajasram jyotiḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ṛṣīṇām prastaro ‘si namo ‘stu daivāya prastarāya //6//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mūrdhāham rayīṇām mūrdhā samānānām bhūyāsam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a rujaś ca mā venaś ca mā hāsiṣṭām mūrdhā ca mā vidharmā ca mā hāsiṣṭām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a urvaś ca mā camasaś ca mā hāsiṣṭām dhartā ca mā dharuṇaś ca mā hāsiṣṭām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vimokaś ca mārdrapaviś ca mā hāsiṣṭām ārdradānuś ca mā mātariśvā ca mā hāsiṣṭām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir ma ātmā nṛmaṇā nāma hṛdyaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a asamtāpam me hṛdayam urvī gavyūtiḥ samudro asmi vidharmaṇā //6//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nābhir aham rayīṇām nābhiḥ samānānām bhūyāsam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a svāsad asi sūṣā amṛto martyeśv ā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mā mām prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno ‘vahāya parā gāt //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sūryo māhnaḥ pātv agniḥ pṛthivyā vāyur antarikṣād yamo manuṣyṝbhyaḥ sarasvatī pārthivebhyaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a prāṇāpanau mā mā hāsiṣṭam mā jane pra meṣi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a svasty adyoṣaso doṣasaś ca sarva āpaḥ sarvagaṇo aśīya //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śakvarī stha paśavo mopa stheṣur mitrāvaruṇau me prāṇāpānāv agnir me dakṣam dadhātu //7//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1 {1}

Halfverse: a vidma te svapna janitram grāhyāḥ putro ‘si yamasya karaṇaḥ //1//

Halfverse: c {2} antako ‘si mṛtyur asi //2//

Halfverse: d {3} tam tvā svapna tathā sam vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyāt pāhi //3//

Verse: 2 {4}

Halfverse: a vidma te svapna janitram nirṛtyāḥ putro ‘si yamasya karaṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c antako ‘si mṛtyur asi /

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ajaiṣmādyāsanām adyāmūm anāgaso vayam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a uṣo yasmād duṣvapnyād abhaiṣmāpa tad uchatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dviṣate tat parā vaha śapate tat parā vaha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yam dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tasmā enad gamayāmaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a uṣā devī vācā samvidānā vāg devy ḥṣasā samvidānā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a uṣas patir vācas patinā samvidāno vācas patinā samvidānaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tṝ3 ‘muṣmai parā vahantv arāyān durṇāmnaḥ sadānvāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a kumbhīkāḥ dūṣīkāḥ pīyakān //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a jāgradduṣvapnyam svapneduṣvapnyam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a anāgamiṣyato varān avitteḥ samkalpān amucyā druhaḥ pāśān //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tad amuṣmā agne devāḥ parā vahantu vaghrir yathāsad vithuro na sādhuḥ //11//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tenainam vidhyāmy abhūtyainam vidhyāmi nirbhūtyainam vidhyāmi parābhūtyainam vidhyāmi grāhyainam vidhyāmi tamasainam vidhyāmi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a devānām enam ghoraiḥ krūraiḥ praiṣair abhipreṣyāmi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vaiśvānarasyainam damṣṭrayor api dadhāmi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a evānevāva sā garat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yo3 ‘smān dveṣṭi tam ātmā dveṣṭu yam vayam dviṣmaḥ sa ātmānam dveṣṭu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a nir dviṣantam divo niḥ pṛthivyā nir antarikṣād bhajāma //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a suyāmamś cākṣuṣa //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a idam aham āmuṣyāyaṇṝ3 ‘muṣyāḥ putre duṣvapnyam mṛje //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yad adoado abhyagacham yad doṣā yat pūrvām rātrim //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yaj jāgrad yat supto yad divā yan naktam //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yad aharahar abhigachāmi tasmād enam ava daye //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a tam jahi tena mandasva tasya pṛṣṭīr api śṛṇīhi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sa mā jīvīt tam prāṇo jahātu //13//
