## Book: 17

17

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a viṣāsahim sahamānam sāsahānam sahīyāmsam /

Halfverse: c sahamānam sahojitam svarjitam gojitam samdhanājitam /

Halfverse: e īḍyam nāma hva indram āyuṣmān bhūyāsam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a viṣāsahim sahamānam sāsahānam sahīyāmsam /

Halfverse: c sahamānam sahojitam svarjitam gojitam samdhanājitam /

Halfverse: e īḍyam nāma hva indram priyo devānām bhūyāsam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a viṣāsahim sahamānam sāsahānam sahīyāmsam /

Halfverse: c sahamānam sahojitam svarjitam gojitam samdhanājitam /

Halfverse: e īḍyam nāma hva indram priyaḥ prajānām bhūyāsam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a viṣāsahim sahamānam sāsahānam sahīyāmsam /

Halfverse: c sahamānam sahojitam svarjitam gojitam samdhanājitam /

Halfverse: e īḍyam nāma hva indram priyah paśūnām bhūyāsam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a viṣāsahim sahamānam sāsahānam sahīyāmsam /

Halfverse: c sahamānam sahojitam svarjitam gojitam samdhanājitam /

Halfverse: e īḍyam nāma hva indram priyaḥ samānānām bhūyāsam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ud ihy ud ihi sūrya varcasā mābhyudihi /

Halfverse: c dviṣamś ca mahyam radhyatu mā cāham dviṣate radham taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ud ihy ud ihi sūrya varcasā mābhyudihi /

Halfverse: c āmś ca paśyāmi yāmś ca na teṣu mā sumatim kṛdhi taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mā tvā dabhant salile apsv a1ntar ye pāśina upatiṣṭhanty atra /

Halfverse: c hitvāśastim divam ārukṣa etām sa no mṛḍa sumatau te syāma taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tvam na indra mahate saubhagāyādabdhebhiḥ pari pāhy aktubhis taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni /

Halfverse: c tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tvam na indrotibhiḥ śivābhiḥ śamtamo bhava /

Halfverse: c ārohams tridivam divo gṛṇānaḥ somapītaye priyadhāmā svastaye taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tvam indrāsi viśvajit sarvavit puruhūtas tvam indra /

Halfverse: c tvam indremam suhavam stomam erayasva sa no mṛḍa sumatau te syāma taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a adabdho divi pṛthivyām utāsi na ta āpur mahimānam antarikṣe /

Halfverse: c adabdhena brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānaḥ sa tvam na indra divi sam charma yacha taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yā ta indra tanūr apsu yā pṛthivyām yāntar agnau yā te indra pavamāne svarvidi /

Halfverse: c yayendra tanvā3 ‘ntarikṣam vyāpitha tayā na indra tanvā3 śarma yacha taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a tvām indra brahmaṇā vardhayantaḥ sattram ni ṣedur ṛṣayo nādhamānās taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: c tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a tvam tṛtam tvam pary eṣy utsam sahasradhāram vidatham svarvidam taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: c tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a tvam rakṣase pradiśaś catasras tvam śociṣā nabhasī vi bhāsi /

Halfverse: c tvam imā viśvā bhuvanānu tiṣṭhasa ṛtasya panthām anv eṣi vidvāms taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a pañcabhiḥ parāṅ tapasy ekayārvāṅ aśastim eṣi sudine bādhamānas taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: c tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a tvam indras tvam mahendras tvam lokas tvam prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c tubhyam yajño vi tāyate tubhyam juhvati juhvatas taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a asati sat pratiṣṭhitam sati bhūtam pratiṣṭhitam /

Halfverse: c bhūtam ha bhavya āhitam bhavyam bhūte pratiṣṭhitam taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a śukro ‘si bhrājo ‘si /

Halfverse: c sa yathā tvam bhrājatā bhrājo ‘sy evāham bhrājatā bhrājyāsam //20// {2}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a rucir asi roco ‘si /

Halfverse: c sa yathā tvam rucyā roco ‘sy evāham paśubhiś ca brāhmaṇavarcasena ca ruciṣīya //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a udyate nama udāyate nama uditāya namaḥ /

Halfverse: c virāje namaḥ svarāje namaḥ samrāje namaḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a astamyate namo ‘stameṣyate namo ‘stamitāya namaḥ /

Halfverse: c virāje namaḥ svarāje namah samrāje namaḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a ud agād ayam ādityo viśvena tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c sapatnān mahyam randhayan mā cāham dviṣate radham taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi /

Halfverse: e tvam naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyām mā dhehi parame vyoman //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a āditya nāvam ārukṣaḥ śatāritrām svastaye /

Halfverse: c ahar māty apīparo rātrim satrāti pāraya //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a sūrya nāvam ārukṣaḥ śatāritrām svastaye /

Halfverse: c rātrim māty apīparo ‘haḥ satrāti pāraya //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a prajāpater āvṛto brahmaṇā varmaṇāham kaśyapasya jyotiṣā varcasā ca /

Halfverse: c jaradaṣṭiḥ kṛtavīryo vihāyāḥ sahasrāyuḥ sukṛtaś careyam //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a parivṛto brahmaṇā varmaṇāham kaśyapasya jyotiṣā varcasā ca /

Halfverse: c mā mā prāpann iṣavo daivyā yā mā mānuṣīr avasṛṣṭāḥ vadhāya //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a ṛtena gupta ṛtubhiś ca sarvair bhūtena gupto bhavyena cāham /

Halfverse: c mā mā prāpat pāpmā mota mṛtyur antar dadhe ‘ham salilena vācaḥ //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a agnir mā goptā pari pātu viśvataḥ udyant sūryo nudatām mṛtyupāśān /

Halfverse: c vyuchantīr uṣasaḥ parvatā dhruvāḥ sahasram prāṇā mayy ā yatantām //30// {3}

## Book: 18

18

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a o cit sakhāyam sakhyā vavṛtyām tiraḥ puru cid arṇavam jaganvān /

Halfverse: c pitur napātam ā dadhīta vedhā adhi kṣami prataram dīdhyānaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na te sakhā sakhyam vaṣṭy etat salakṣmā yad viṣurūpā bhavati /

Halfverse: c mahas putrāso asurasya vīrā divo dhartāra urviyā pari khyan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a uśanti ghā te amṛtāsa etad ekasya cit tyajasam martyasya /

Halfverse: c ni te mano manasi dhāyy asme janyuḥ patis tanva1m ā viviṣyāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a na yat purā cakṛmā kad dha nūnam ṛtam vadanto anṛtam rapema /

Halfverse: c gandharvo apsv apyā ca yoṣā sā nau nābhiḥ paramam jāmi tan nau //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a garbhe nu nau janitā dampatī kar devas tvaṣṭā savitā viśvarūpaḥ /

Halfverse: c nakir asya pra minanti vratāni veda nāv asya pṛthivī uta dyauḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ko adya yuṅkte dhuri gā ṛtasya śimīvato bhāmino durhṛṇāyūn /

Halfverse: c āsanniṣūn hṛtsvaso mayobhūn ya eṣām bhṛtyām ṛṇadhat sa jīvāt //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ko asya veda prathamasyāhnaḥ ka īm dadarśa ka iha pra vocat /

Halfverse: c bṛhan mitrasya varuṇasya dhāma kad u brava āhano vīcyā nṝn //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yamasya mā yamya1m kāma āgant samāne yonau sahaśeyyāya /

Halfverse: c jāyeva patye tanvam riricyām vi cid vṛheva rathyeva cakrā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a na tiṣṭhanti na ni miṣanty ete devānām spaśa iha ye caranti /

Halfverse: c anyena mad āhano yāhi tūyam tena vi vṛha rathyeva cakrā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a rātrībhir asmā ahabhir daśasyet sūryasya cakṣur muhur un mimīyāt /

Halfverse: c divā pṛthivyā mithunā sabandhū yamīr yamasya vivṛhād ajāmi //10// {1}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ā ghā tā gachān uttarā yugāni yatra jāmayaḥ kṛṇavann ajāmi /

Halfverse: c upa barbṛhi vṛṣabhāya bāhum anyam ichasva subhage patim mat //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a kim bhrātāsad yad anātham bhavāti kim u svasā yan nirṛtir nigachāt /

Halfverse: c kāmamūtā bahv ṝ3tad rapāmi tanvā me tanva1m sam pipṛgdhi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a na te nātham yamy atrāham asmi na te tanūm tanvā3 sam papṛcyām /

Halfverse: c anyena mat pramudaḥ kalpayasva na te bhrātā subhage vaṣṭy etat //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a na vā u te tanūm tanvā3 sam pipṛcyām pāpam āhur yaḥ svasāram nigachāt /

Halfverse: c asamyad etan manaso hṛdo me bhrātā svasuḥ śayane yac chayīya //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a bato batāsi yama naiva te mano hṛdayam cāvidām ā /

Halfverse: c anyā kila tvām kakṣyṝva yuktam pari ṣvajātau libujeva vṛkṣam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a anyam ū ṣu yamy anya u tvām pari ṣvajātau libujeva vṛkṣam /

Halfverse: c tasya vā tvam mana ichā sa vā tavādhā kṛṇuṣva samvidam subhadrām //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a trīṇi chandāmsi kavayo vi yetire pururūpam darśatam viśvacakṣaṇam /

Halfverse: c āpo vātā oṣadhayas tāny ekasmin bhuvana ārpitāni //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a vṛṣā vṛṣṇe duduhe dohasā divaḥ payāmsi yahvo aditer adābhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvam sa veda varuṇo yathā dhiyā sa yajñiyo yajati yajñiyāṁ ṛtūn //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a rapad gandharvīr apyā ca yoṣaṇā nadasya nāde pari pātu no manaḥ /

Halfverse: c iṣṭasya madhye aditir ni dhātu no bhrātā no jyeṣṭhaḥ prathamo vi vocati //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a so cit nu bhadrā kṣumatī yaśasvaty uṣā uvāsa manave svarvatī /

Halfverse: c yad īm uśantam uśatām anu kratum agnim hotāram vidathāya jījanan //20// {2}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a adha tyam drapsam vibhvam vicakṣanam vir ābharad iṣiraḥ śyeno adhvare /

Halfverse: c yadī viśo vṛṇate dasmam āryā agnim hotāram adha dhīr ajāyata //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a sadāsi raṇvo yavaseva puṣyate hotrābhir agne manuṣaḥ svadhvaraḥ /

Halfverse: c viprasya vā yac chaśamāna ukthyo3 vājam sasavāṁ upayāsi bhūribhiḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ud īraya pitarā jāra ā bhagam iyakṣati haryato hṛtta iṣyati /

Halfverse: c vivakti vahniḥ svapasyate makhas taviṣyate asuro vepate matī //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a yas te agne sumatim marto akhyat sahasaḥ sūno ati sa pra śṛṇve /

Halfverse: c iṣam dadhāno vahamāno aśvair ā sa dyumāṁ amavān bhūṣati dyūn //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a śrudhī no agne sadane sadhasthe yukṣvā ratham amṛtasya dravitnum /

Halfverse: c ā no vaha rodasī devaputre mākir devānām apa bhūr iha syāḥ //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a yad agna eṣā samitir bhavāti devī deveṣu yajatā yajatra /

Halfverse: c ratnā ca yad vibhajāsi svadhāvo bhāgam no atra vasumantam vītāt //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a anv agnir uṣasām agram akhyad anv ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c anu sūrya uṣaso anu raśmīn dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a praty agnir uṣasām agram akhyat praty ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c prati sūryasya purudhā ca raśmīn prati dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatāna //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a dyāvā ha kṣāmā prathame ṛtenābhiśrāve bhavataḥ satyavācā /

Halfverse: c devo yan martān yajathāya kṛṇvant sīdad dhotā pratyaṅ svam asum yan //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a devo devān paribhūr ṛtena vahā no havyam prathamaś cikitvān /

Halfverse: c dhūmaketuḥ samidhā bhāṛjīko mandro hotā nityo vācā yajīyān //30// {3}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a arcāmi vām vardhāyāpo ghṛtasnū dyāvābhūmī śṛṇutam rodasī me /

Halfverse: c ahā yad devā asunītim āyan madhvā no atra pitarā śiśītām //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a svāvṛg devasyāmṛtam yadī gor ato jātāso dhārayanta urvī /

Halfverse: c viśve devā anu tat te yajur gur duhe yad enī divyam ghṛtam vāḥ //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a kim svin no rājā jagṛhe kad asyāti vratam cakṛmā ko vi veda /

Halfverse: c mitras cid dhi ṣmā juhurāṇo devām chloko na yātām api vājo asti //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a durmantv atrāmṛtasya nāma salakṣmā yad viṣurūpā bhavāti /

Halfverse: c yamasya yo manavate sumantv agne tam ṛṣva pāhy aprayuchan //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a yasmin devā vidathe mādayante vivasvataḥ sadane dhārayante /

Halfverse: c sūrye jyotir adadhur māsy a1ktūn pari dyotanim carato ajasrā //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a yasmin devā manmani samcaranty apīcyṝ3 na vayam asya vidma /

Halfverse: c mitro no atrāditir anāgānt savitā devo varuṇāya vocat //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a sakhāya ā śiṣāmahe brahmendrāya vajriṇe /

Halfverse: c stuṣa ū ṣu nṛtamāya dhṛṣṇave //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a śavasā hy asi śruto vṛtrahatyena vṛtrahā /

Halfverse: c maghair maghono ati śūra dāśasi //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a stego na ksām aty eṣi pṛthivīm mahī no vātā iha vāntu bhūmau /

Halfverse: c mitro no atra varuṇo yujamāno agnir vane na vy asṛṣṭa śokam //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a stuhi śrutam gartasadam janānām rājānam bhīmam upahatnum ugram /

Halfverse: c mṛḍā jaritre rudra stavāno anyam asmat te ni vapantu senyam //40// {4}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a sarasvatīm devayanto havante sarasvatīm adhvare tāyamāne /

Halfverse: c sarasvatīm sukṛto havante sarasvatī dāśuṣe vāryam dāt //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a sarasvatīm pitaro havante dakṣinā yajñam abhinakṣamāṇāḥ /

Halfverse: c āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam anamīvā iṣa ā dhehy asme //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a sarasvati yā saratham yayāthokthaiḥ svadhābhir devi pitṛbhir madantī /

Halfverse: c sahasrārgham iḍo atra bhāgam rāyas poṣam yajamānāya dhehi //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a ud īrathām avara ut parāsa un madhyamāḥ pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ /

Halfverse: c asum ya īyur avṛkā ṛtajñās te no ‘vantu pitaro haveṣu //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a āham pitṝnt suvidatrāṁ avitsi napātam ca vikramaṇam ca viṣṇoḥ /

Halfverse: c barhiṣado ye svadhayā sutasya bhajanta pitvas ta ihāgamiṣṭhāḥ //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a idam pitṛbhyo namo astv adya ye pūrvāso ye aparāsa īyuḥ /

Halfverse: c ye pārthive rajasy ā niṣaktā ye vā nūnam suvṛjanāsu dikṣu //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a mātalī kavyair yamo aṅgirobhir bṛhaspatir ṛkvabhir vāvṛdhānaḥ /

Halfverse: c yāmś ca devā vāvṛdhur ye ca devāms te no ‘vantu pitaro haveṣu //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a svāduṣ kilāyam madhumāṁ utāyam tīvraḥ kilāyam rasavāṁ utāyam /

Halfverse: c uto nv asya papivāmsam indram na kaś cana sahata āhaveṣu //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a pareyivāmsam pravato mahīr iti bahubhyaḥ panthām anupaspaśānam /

Halfverse: c vaivasvatam samgamanam janānām yamam rājānam haviṣā saparyata //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a yamo no gātum prathamo viveda naiṣā gavyūtir apabhartavā u /

Halfverse: c yatrā naḥ pūrve pitaraḥ paretā enā jajñānāḥ pathyā3 anu svāḥ //50// {5}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a barhiṣadaḥ pitara ūty arvāg imā vo havyā cakṛmā juṣadhvam /

Halfverse: c ta ā gatāvasā śamtamenādhā naḥ śam yor arapo dadhāta //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a ācyā jānu dakṣiṇato niṣadyedam no havir abhi gṛṇantu viśve /

Halfverse: c mā himsiṣṭa pitaraḥ kena cin no yad va āgaḥ puruṣatā karāma //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a tvaṣṭā duhitre vahatum kṛṇoti tenedam viśvam bhuvanam sam eti /

Halfverse: c yamasya mātā paryuhyamānā maho jāyā vivasvato nanāśa //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a prehi prehi pathibhiḥ pūryāṇair yenā te pūrve pitaraḥ paretāḥ /

Halfverse: c ubhā rājānau svadhayā madantau yamam paśyāsi varuṇam ca devam //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a apeta vīta vi ca sarpatāto ‘smā etam pitaro lokam akran /

Halfverse: c ahobhir adbhir aktubhir vyaktam yamo dadāty avasānam asmai //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a uśantas tvedhīmahy uśantaḥ sam idhīmahi /

Halfverse: c uśann uśata ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a dyumantas tvedhīmahi dyumantaḥ sam idhīmahi /

Halfverse: c dyumān dyumata ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a aṅgiraso naḥ pitaro navagvā atharvāṇo bhṛgavaḥ somyāsaḥ /

Halfverse: c teṣām vayam sumatau yajñiyānām api bhadre saumanase syāma //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a aṅgirobhir yajñiyair ā gahīha yama vairūpair iha mādayasva /

Halfverse: c vivasvantam huve yaḥ pitā te ‘smin barhiṣy ā niṣadya //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a imam yama prastaram ā hi rohāṅgirobhiḥ pitṛbhiḥ samvidānaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā tvā mantrāḥ kaviśastā vahantv enā rājan haviṣo mādayasva //60//

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a ita eta udāruhan divas pṛṣṭhānv āruhan /

Halfverse: c pra bhūrjayo yathā pathā dyām aṅgiraso yayuḥ //61// {6}

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yamāya somaḥ pavate yamāya kriyate haviḥ /

Halfverse: c yamam ha yajño gachaty agnidūto aramkṛtaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yamāya madhumattamam juhotā pra ca tiṣṭhata /

Halfverse: c idam nama ṛṣibhyaḥ pūrvajebhyaḥ pūrvebhyaḥ pathikṛdbhyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yamāya ghṛtavat payo rājñe havir juhotana /

Halfverse: c sa no jīveṣv ā yamed dīrgham āyuḥ pra jīvase //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a mainam agne vi daho mābhi śūśuco māsya tvacam cikṣipo mā śarīram /

Halfverse: c śṛtam yadā karasi jātavedo ‘them enam pra hiṇutāt pitṝṁr upa //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yadā śṛtam kṛṇavo jātavedo ‘themam enam pari dattāt pitṛbhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yado gachāty asunītim etām atha devānām vaśanīr bhavāti //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a trikadrukebhiḥ pavate ṣaḍ urvīr ekam id bṛhat /

Halfverse: c triṣṭub gāyatrī chandāmsi sarvā tā yama ārpitā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sūryam cakṣuṣā gacha vātam ātmanā divam ca gacha pṛthivīm ca dharmabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c apo vā gacha yadi tatra te hitam oṣadhīṣu prati tiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ajo bhāgas tapasas tam tapasva tam te śocis tapatu tam te arciḥ /

Halfverse: c yās te śivās tanvo jātavedas tābhir vahainam sukṛtām u lokam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yās te śocayo ramhayo jātavedo yābhir āpṛṇāsi divam antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c ajam yantam anu tāḥ sam ṛṇvatām athetarābhiḥ śivatamābhiḥ śṛtam kṛdhi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ava sṛja punar agne pitṛbhyo yas ta āhutaś carati svadhāvān /

Halfverse: c āyur vasāna upa yātu śeṣaḥ sam gachatām tanvā suvarcāḥ //10// {7}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ati drava śvānau sārameyau caturakṣau śabalau sādhunā pathā /

Halfverse: c adhā pitṝnt suvidatrāṁ apīhi yamena ye sadhamādam madanti //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yau te śvānau yama rakṣitārau caturakṣau pathiṣadī nṛcakṣasā /

Halfverse: c tābhyām rājan pari dhehy enam svasty asmā anamīvam ca dhehi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a urūṇasāv asutṛpāv udumbalau yamasya dūtau carato janāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c tāv asmabhyam dṛśaye sūryāya punar dātām asum adyeha bhadram //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a soma ekebhyaḥ pavate ghṛtam eka upāsate /

Halfverse: c yebhyo madhu pradhāvati tāmś cid evāpi gachatāt //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ye cit pūrva ṛtasātā ṛtajātā ṛtāvṛdhaḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛṣīn tapasvato yama tapojāṁ api gachatāt //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a tapasā ye anādhṛṣyās tapasā ye svar yayuḥ /

Halfverse: c tapo ye cakrire mahas tāmś cid evāpi gachatāt //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ye yudhyante pradhaneṣu śūrāso ye svar tanūtyajaḥ /

Halfverse: c ye vā sahasradakṣiṇās tām cid evāpi gachatāt //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a sahasraṇīthāḥ kavayo ye gopāyanti sūryam /

Halfverse: c ṛṣīn tapasvato yama tapojāṁ api gachatāt //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a syonāsmai bhava pṛthivy anṛkṣarā niveśanī /

Halfverse: c yachāsmai śarma saprathāḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a asambādhe pṛthivyā urau loke ni dhīyasva /

Halfverse: c svadhā yāś cakṛṣe jīvan tās te santu madhuścutaḥ //20// {8}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a hvayāmi te manasā mana ihemān gṛhām upa jujuṣāṇa ehi /

Halfverse: c sam gachasva pitṛbhiḥ sam yamena syonās tvā vātā upa vāntu śagmāḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a ut tvā vahantu maruta udavāhā udaprutaḥ /

Halfverse: c ajena kṛṇvantaḥ śītam varṣeṇokṣantu bāl iti //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ud ahvam āyur āyuṣe kratve dakṣāya jīvase /

Halfverse: c svān gachatu te mano adhā pitṝṁr upa drava //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a mā te mano māsor māṅgānām mā rasasya te /

Halfverse: c mā te hāsta tanva1ḥ kim caneha //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a mā tvā vṛkṣaḥ sam bādhiṣṭa mā devī pṛthivī mahī /

Halfverse: c lokam pitṛṣu vittvaidhasva yamarājasu //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a yat te aṅgam atihitam parācair apānaḥ prāṇo ya u vā te paretaḥ /

Halfverse: c tat te samgatya pitaraḥ sanīḍā ghāsād ghāsam punar ā veśayantu //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a apemam jīvā arudhan gṛhebhyas tam nir vahata pari grāmād itaḥ /

Halfverse: c mṛtyur yamasyāsīd dūtaḥ pracetā asūn pitṛbhyo gamayām cakāra //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a ye dasyavaḥ pitṛṣu praviṣṭā jñātimukhā ahutādaś caranti /

Halfverse: c parāpuro nipuro ye bharanty agniṣ ṭān asmāt pra dhamāti yajñāt //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a sam viśantv iha pitaraḥ svā naḥ syonam kṛṇvantah pratiranta āyuḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyaḥ śakema haviṣā nakṣamāṇā jyog jīvantaḥ śaradaḥ purūcīḥ //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a yām te dhenum nipṛṇāmi yam u kṣīra odanam /

Halfverse: c tenā janasyāso bhartā yo ‘trāsad ajīvanaḥ //30// {9}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a aśvāvatīm pra tara yā suśevā rkṣākam vā prataram navīyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yas tvā jaghāna vadhyaḥ so astu mā so anyad vidata bhāgadheyam //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a yamaḥ paro ‘varo vivasvān tataḥ param nāti paśyāmi kim cana /

Halfverse: c yame adhvaro adhi me niviṣṭo bhuvo vivasvān anvātatāna //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a apāgūhann amṛtām martyebhyaḥ kṛtvā savarṇām adadhur vivasvate /

Halfverse: c utāśvināv abharad yat tad āsīd ajahād u dvā mithunā saraṇyūḥ //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a ye nikhātā ye paroptā ye dagdhā ye coddhitāḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvāms tān agna ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a ye agnidagdhā ye anagnidagdhā madhye divaḥ svadhayā mādayante /

Halfverse: c tvam tān vettha yadi te jātavedaḥ svadhayā yajñam svadhitim juṣantām //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a śam tapa māti tapo agne mā tanva1m tapaḥ /

Halfverse: c vaṇeṣu śuṣmo astu te pṛthivyām astu yad dharaḥ //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a dadāmy asmā avasānam etad ya eṣa āgan mama ced abhūd iha /

Halfverse: c yamaś cikitvān praty etad āha mamaiṣa rāya upa tiṣṭhatām iha //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a imām mātrām mimīmahe yathāparam na māsātai /

Halfverse: c śate śaratsu no purā //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a premām mātrām mimīmahe yathāparam na māsātai /

Halfverse: c śate śaratsu no purā //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a apemām mātrām mimīmahe yathāparam na māsātai /

Halfverse: c śate śaratsu no purā //40// {10}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a vīmām mātrām mimīmahe yathāparam na māsātai /

Halfverse: c śate śaratsu no purā //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a nir imām mātrām mimīmahe yathāparam na māsātai /

Halfverse: c śate śaratsu no purā //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a ud imām mātrām mimīmahe yathāparam na māsātai /

Halfverse: c śate śaratsu no purā //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a sam imām mātrām mimīmahe yathāparam na māsātai /

Halfverse: c śate śaratsu no purā //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a amāsi mātrām svar agām āyuṣmān bhūyāsam /

Halfverse: c yathāparam na māsātai śate śaratsu no purā //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a prāṇo apāno vyāna āyuś cakṣur dṛśaye sūryāya /

Halfverse: c aparipareṇa pathā yamarājñaḥ pitṝn gacha //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a ye agravaḥ śaśamānāh pareyur hitvā dveṣāmsy anapatyavantaḥ /

Halfverse: c te dyām udityāvidanta lokam nākasya pṛṣṭhe adhi dīdhyānāḥ //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a udanvatī dyaur avamā pīlumatīti madhyamā /

Halfverse: c tṛtīyā ha pradyaur iti yasyām pitara āsate //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a ye na pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā ya āviviśur urv antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c ya ākṣiyanti pṛthivīm uta dyām tebhyaḥ pitṛbhyo namasā vidhema //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a idam id vā u nāparam divi paśyasi sūryam /

Halfverse: c mātā putram yathā sicābhy ṝnam bhūma ūrṇuhi //50// {11}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a idam id vā u nāparam jarasy anyad ito ‘param /

Halfverse: c jāyā patim iva vāsasābhy ṝnam bhūma ūrṇuhi //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a abhi tvorṇomi pṛthivyā mātur vastreṇa bhadrayā /

Halfverse: c jīveṣu bhadram tan mayi svadhā pitṛṣu sā tvayi //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a agnīṣomā pathikṛtā syonam devebhyo ratnam dadhathur vi lokam /

Halfverse: c upa preṣyantam pūṣaṇam yo vahāty añjoyānaiḥ pathibhis tatra gachatam //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a pūṣā tvetaś cyāvayatu pra vidvān anaṣṭapaśur bhuvanasya gopāḥ /

Halfverse: c sa tvaitebhyaḥ pari dadat pitṛbhyo ‘gnir devebhyaḥ suvidatriyebhyaḥ //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a āyur viśvāyuḥ pari pātu tvā pūṣā tvā pātu prapathe purastāt /

Halfverse: c yatrāsate sukṛto yatra ta īyus tatra tvā devaḥ savitā dadhātu //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a imau yunajmi te vahnī asunītāya voḍhave /

Halfverse: c tābhyām yamasya sādanam samitiś cāva gachatāt //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a etat tvā vāsaḥ prathamam nv āgann apaitad ūha yad ihābibhaḥ purā /

Halfverse: c iṣṭāpūrtam anusamkrāma vidvān yatra te dattam bahudhā vibandhuṣu //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a agner varma pari gobhir vyayasva sam prorṇuṣva medasā pīvasā ca /

Halfverse: c net tvā dhṛṣṇur harasā jarhṛṣāṇo dadhṛg vidhakṣan parīṅkhayātai //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a daṇḍam hastād ādadāno gatāsoḥ saha śrotreṇa varcasā balena /

Halfverse: c atraiva tvam iha vayam suvīrā viśvā mṛdho abhimātīr jayema //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a dhanur hastād ādadāno mṛtasya saha kṣatreṇa varcasā balena /

Halfverse: c samāgṛbhāya vasu bhūri puṣṭam arvāṅ tvam ehy upa jīvalokam //60// {12}

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a iyam nārī patilokam vṛṇānā ni padyata upa tvā martya pretam /

Halfverse: c dharmam purāṇam anupālayantī tasyai prajām draviṇam ceha dhehi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ud īrṣva nāry abhi jīvalokam gatāsum etam upa śeṣa ehi /

Halfverse: c hastagrābhasya dadhiṣos tavedam patyur janitvam abhi sam babhūtha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apaśyam yuvatim nīyamānām jīvām mṛtebhyaḥ pariṇīyamānām /

Halfverse: c andhena yat tamasā prāvṛtāsīt prākto apācīm anayam tad enām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prajānaty aghnye jīvalokam devānām panthām anusamcarantī /

Halfverse: c ayam te gopatis tam juṣasva svargam lokam adhi rohayainam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a upa dyām upa vetasam avattaro nadīnām /

Halfverse: c agne pittam apām asi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yam tvam agne samadahas tam u nir vāpaya punaḥ /

Halfverse: c kyāmbūr atra rohatu śāṇḍadūrvā vyalkaśā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a idam ta ekam pura ū ta ekam tṛtīyena jyotiṣā sam viśasva /

Halfverse: c samveśane tanvā3 cārur edhi priyo devānām parame sadhasthe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭha prehi pra dravaukaḥ kṛṇuṣva salile sadhasthe /

Halfverse: c tatra tvam pitṛbhiḥ samvidānaḥ sam somena madasva sam svadhābhiḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a pra cyavasva tanva1m sam bharasva mā te gātrā vi hāyi mo śarīram /

Halfverse: c mano niviṣṭam anusamviśasva yatra bhūmer juṣase tatra gacha //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a varcasā mām pitaraḥ somyāso añjantu devā madhunā ghṛtena /

Halfverse: c cakṣuṣe mā prataram tārayanto jarase mā jaradaṣṭim vardhantu //10// {13}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a varcasā mām sam anaktv agnir medhām me viṣṇur ny anaktv āsan /

Halfverse: c rayim me viśve ni yachantu devāḥ syonā māpaḥ pavanaiḥ punantu //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a mitrāvaruṇā pari mām adhātām ādityā mā svaravo vardhayantu /

Halfverse: c varco ma indro ny anaktu hastayor jaradaṣṭim mā savitā kṛṇotu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yo mamāra prathamo martyānām yaḥ preyāya prathamo lokam etam /

Halfverse: c vaivasvatam samgamanam janānām yamam rājānam haviṣā saparyata //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a parā yāta pitara ā ca yātāyam vo yajño madhunā samaktaḥ /

Halfverse: c datto asmabhyam draviṇeha bhadram rayim ca naḥ sarvavīram dadhāta //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a kaṇvaḥ kakṣīvān purumīḍho agastyaḥ śyāvāśvaḥ sobhary arcanānāḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvāmitro ‘yam jamadagnir atrir avantu naḥ kaśyapo vāmadevaḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a viśvāmitra jamadagne vasiṣṭha bharadvāja gotama vāmadeva /

Halfverse: c śardir no atrir agrabhīn namobhiḥ susamśāsaḥ pitaro mṛḍatā naḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a kasye mṛjānā ati yanti ripram āyur dadhānāḥ prataram navīyaḥ /

Halfverse: c āpyāyamānāḥ prajayā dhanenādha syāma surabhayo gṛheṣu //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a añjate vy añjate sam añjate kratum rihanti madhunābhy añjate /

Halfverse: c sindhor ucchvāse patayantam ukṣaṇam hiraṇyapāvāḥ paśum āsu gṛhnate //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a yad vo mudram pitaraḥ somyam ca teno sacadhvam svayaśaso hi bhūta /

Halfverse: c te arvāṇaḥ kavaya ā śṛṇota suvidatrā vidathe huyamānāḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a ye atrayo aṅgiraso navagvā iṣṭāvanto rātiṣāco dadhānāḥ /

Halfverse: c dakṣiṇāvantaḥ sukṛto ya u sthāsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam //20// {14}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a adhā yathā naḥ pitaraḥ parāsaḥ pratnāso agna ṛtam āśaśānāḥ /

Halfverse: c śucīd ayan dīdhyata ukthaśasaḥ kṣāmā bhindanto aruṇīr apa vran //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a sukarmānaḥ suruco devayanto ayo na devā janimā dhamantaḥ /

Halfverse: c śucanto agnim vāvṛdhanta indram urvīm gavyām pariṣadam no akran //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a ā yūtheva kṣumati paśvo akhyad devānām janimānty ugraḥ /

Halfverse: c martāsaś cid urvaśīr akṛpran vṛdhe cid arya uparasyāyoḥ //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a akarma te svapaso abhūma ṛtam avasrann uṣaso vibhātīḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvam tad bhadram yad avanti devā bṛhad vadema vidathe suvīrāḥ //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a indro mā marutvān prācyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a dhātā mā nirṛtyā dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a aditir mādityaiḥ pratīcyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a somo mā viśvair devair udīcyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a dhartā ha tvā dharuṇo dhārayātā ūrdhvam bhānum savitā dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a prācyām tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyām ā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //30// {15}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a dakṣiṇāyām tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyām ā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a pratīcyām tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyām ā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a udīcyām tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyām ā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a dhruvāyām tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyām ā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a ūrdhvāyām tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyām ā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a dhartāsi dharuno ‘si vamsago ‘si //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a udapūr asi madhupūr asi vātapūr asi //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a itaś ca māmutaś cāvatām yame iva yatamāne yad aitam /

Halfverse: c pra vām bharan mānuṣā devayanto ā sīdatām svam u lokam vidāne //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a svāsasthe bhavatam indave no yuje vām brahma pūrvyam namobhiḥ /

Halfverse: c vi śloka eti pathyṝva sūriḥ śṛṇvantu viśve amṛtāsa etat //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a trīṇi padāni rupo anv arohac catuṣpadīm anv etad vratena /

Halfverse: c akṣareṇa prati mimīte arkam ṛtasya nābhāv abhi sam punāti //40// {16}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a devebhyaḥ kam avṛṇīta mṛtyum prajāyai kim amṛtam nāvṛṇīta /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir yajñam atanuta ṛṣiḥ priyām yamas tanva1m ā rireca //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a tvam agna īḍito jātavedo ‘vāḍ ḍhavyāni surabhīṇi kṛtvā /

Halfverse: c prādāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhayā te akṣann addhi tvam deva prayatā havīmṣi //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a āsīnāso aruṇīnām upasthe rayim dhatta dāśuṣe martyāya /

Halfverse: c putrebhyaḥ pitaras tasya vasvaḥ pra yachata ta ihorjam dadhāta //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a agniṣvāttāḥ pitara eha gachata sadaḥsadaḥ sadata supraṇītayaḥ /

Halfverse: c atto havīmṣi prayatāni barhiṣi rayim ca naḥ sarvavīram dadhāta //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a upahūtā naḥ pitaraḥ somyāso barhiṣyṝṣu nidhiṣu priyeṣu /

Halfverse: c ta ā gamantu ta iha śruvantv adhi bruvantu te ‘vantv asmān //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a ye naḥ pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā anūjahire somapītham vasiṣṭhāḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhir yamaḥ samrarāṇo havīmṣy uśann uśadbhiḥ pratikāmam attu //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a ye tātṛṣur devatrā jehamānā hotrāvidaḥ stomataṣṭāso arkaiḥ /

Halfverse: c āgne yāhi sahasram devavandaiḥ satyaiḥ kavibhir ṛṣibhir gharmasadbhiḥ //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a ye satyāso havirado haviṣpā indreṇa devaiḥ saratham tureṇa /

Halfverse: c āgne yāhi suvidatrebhir arvāṅ paraiḥ pūrvair ṛṣibhir gharmasadbhiḥ //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a upa sarpa mātaram bhūmim etām uruvyacasam pṛthivīm suśevām /

Halfverse: c ūrṇamradāḥ pṛthivī dakṣiṇāvata eṣā tvā pātu prapathe purastāt //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a uc chvañcasva pṛthivi mā ni bādhathāḥ sūpāyanāsmai bhava sūpasarpaṇā /

Halfverse: c mātā putram yathā sicābhy ṝnam bhūma ūrṇuhi //50// {17}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a ucchvañcamānā pṛthivī su tiṣṭhatu sahasram mita upa hi śrayantām /

Halfverse: c te gṛhāso ghṛtaścutaḥ syonā viśvāhāsmai śaraṇāḥ santv atra //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a ut te stabhnāmi pṛthivīm tvat parīmam logam nidadhan mo aham riṣam /

Halfverse: c etām sthūṇām pitaro dhārayanti te tatra yamaḥ sādanā te kṛṇotu //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a imam agne camasam mā vi jihvaraḥ priyo devānām uta somyānām /

Halfverse: c ayam yaś camaso devapānas tasmin devā amṛtā mādayantām //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a atharvā pūrṇam camasam yam indrāyābibhar vājinīvate /

Halfverse: c tasmin kṛṇoti sukṛtasya bhakṣam tasmin induḥ pavate viśvadānim //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a yat te kṛṣṇaḥ śakuna ātutoda pipīlaḥ sarpa uta vā śvāpadaḥ /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭad viśvād agadam kṛṇotu somaś ca yo brāhmaṇāṁ āviveśa //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a payasvatīr oṣadhayaḥ payasvan māmakam payaḥ /

Halfverse: c apām payaso yat payas tena mā saha śumbhatu //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a imā nārīr avidhavāḥ supatnīr āñjanena sarpiṣā sam spṛśantām /

Halfverse: c anaśravo anamīvāḥ suratnā ā rohantu janayo yonim agre //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a sam gachasva pitṛbhiḥ sam yameneṣṭāpūrtena parame vyoman /

Halfverse: c hitvāvadyam punar astam ehi sam gachatām tanvā suvarcāḥ //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a ye naḥ pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā ya āviviśur urv a1ntarikṣam /

Halfverse: c tebhyaḥ svarād asunītir no adya vathāvaśam tanvaḥ kalpayāti //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a śam te nīhāro bhavatu śam te pruṣvāva śīyatām /

Halfverse: c śītike śītikāvati hlādike hlādikāvati /

Halfverse: e maṇḍūky a1psu śam bhuva imam sv agnim śamaya //60// {18}

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a vivasvān no abhayam kṛṇotu yaḥ sutrāmā jīradānuḥ sudānuḥ /

Halfverse: c iheme vīrā bahavo bhavantu gomad aśvavan mayy astu puṣṭam //61//

Verse: 62

Halfverse: a vivasvān no amṛtatve dadhātu paraitu mṛtyur amṛtam na aitu /

Halfverse: c imān rakṣatu puruṣān ā jarimṇo mo sv ṝṣām asavo yamam guḥ //62//

Verse: 63

Halfverse: a yo dadhre antarikṣe na mahnā pitṝṇām kaviḥ pramatir matīnām /

Halfverse: c tam arcata viśvamitrā havirbhiḥ sa no yamaḥ prataram jīvase dhāt //63//

Verse: 64

Halfverse: a ā rohata divam uttamām ṛṣayo mā bibhītana /

Halfverse: c somapāḥ somapāyina idam vaḥ kriyate havir aganma jvotir uttamam //64//

Verse: 65

Halfverse: a pra ketunā bṛhatā bhāty agnir ā rodasī vṛṣabho roravīti /

Halfverse: c divaś cid antād upamām ud ānaḍ apām upasthe mahiṣo vavardha //65//

Verse: 66

Halfverse: a nāke suparṇam upa yat patantam hrdā venanto abhyacakṣata tvā /

Halfverse: c hiraṇyapakṣam varuṇasya dūtam yamasya yonau śakunam bhuranyum //66//

Verse: 67

Halfverse: a indra kratum na ā bhara pitā putrebhyo yathā /

Halfverse: c śikṣā ṇo asmin puruhūta yāmani jīvā jyotir aśīmahi //67//

Verse: 68

Halfverse: a apūpāpihitān kumbhān yāms te devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutaḥ //68//

Verse: 69

Halfverse: a yās te dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrā svadhāvatīḥ /

Halfverse: c tās te santu vibhvīḥ prabhvīs tās te yamo rājānu manyatām //69//

Verse: 70

Halfverse: a punar dehi vanaspate ya eṣa nihitas tvayi /

Halfverse: c yathā yamasya sādana āsātau vidathā vadan //70//

Verse: 71

Halfverse: a ā rabhasva jātavedas tejasvad dharo astu te /

Halfverse: c śarīram asya sam dahāthainam dehi sukṛtām u loke //71//

Verse: 72

Halfverse: a ye te pūrve parāgatā apare pitaraś ca ye /

Halfverse: c tebhyo ghṛtasya kulyaitu śatadhārā vyundatī //72//

Verse: 73

Halfverse: a etad ā roha vaya unmṛjānaḥ svā iha bṛhad u dīdayante /

Halfverse: c abhi prehi madhyato māpa hāsthāḥ pitṝnām lokam prathamo yo atra //73// {19}

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā rohata janitrīm jātavedasaḥ pitṛyānaiḥ sam va ā rohayāmi /

Halfverse: c avāḍ ḍhavyeṣito havyavāha ījānam yuktāḥ sukṛtām dhatta loke //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a devā yajñam ṛtavaḥ kalpayanti haviḥ puroḍāśam sruco yajñāyudhāni /

Halfverse: c tebhir yāhi pathibhir devayānair yair ījānāḥ svargam yanti lokam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ṛtasya panthām anu paśya sādhv aṅgirasaḥ sukṛto yena yanti /

Halfverse: c tebhir yāhi pathibhiḥ svargam yatrādityā madhu bhakṣayanti tṛtīye nāke adhi vi śrayasva //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a trayaḥ suparṇā uparasya māyū nākasya pṛṣṭhe adhi viṣṭapi śritāḥ /

Halfverse: c svargā lokā amṛtena viṣṭhā iṣam ūrjam yajamānāya duhrām //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a juhūr dādhāra dyām upabhṛd antarikṣam dhruvā dādhāra pṛthivīm pratiṣṭhām /

Halfverse: c pratīmām lokā ghṛtapṛṣṭhāḥ svargāḥ kāmamkāmam yajamānāya duhrām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a dhruva ā roha pṛthivīm viśvabhojasam antarikṣam upabhṛd ā kramasva /

Halfverse: c juhu dyām gacha yajamānena sākam sruveṇa vatsena diśaḥ prapīnāḥ sarvā dhukṣvāhṛṇyamānaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tīrthais taranti pravato mahīr iti yajñakṛtaḥ sukṛto yena yanti /

Halfverse: c atrādadhur yajamānāya lokam diśo bhūtāni yad akalpayanta //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a aṅgirasām ayanam pūrvo agnir ādityānām ayanam gārhapatyo dakṣiṇānām ayanam dakṣiṇāgniḥ /

Halfverse: c mahimānam agner vihitasya brahmaṇā samaṅgaḥ sarva upa yāhi śagmaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a pūrvo agniṣ ṭvā tapatu śam purastāc cham paścāt tapatu gārhapatyaḥ /

Halfverse: c dakṣiṇāgniṣ ṭe tapatu śarma varmottarato madhyato antarikṣād diśodiśo agne pari pāhi ghorāt //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yūyam agne śamtamābhis tanūbhir ījānam abhi lokam svargam /

Halfverse: c aśvā bhūtvā pṛṣṭivāho vahātha yatra devaiḥ sadhamādam madanti //10// {20}

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a śam agne paścāt tapa śam purastāc cham uttarāc cham adharāt tapainam /

Halfverse: c ekas tredhā vihito jātavedaḥ samyag enam dhehi sukṛtām u loke //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a śam agnayaḥ samiddhā ā rabhantām prājāpatyam medhyam jātavedasaḥ /

Halfverse: c śṛtam kṛṇvanta iha māva cikṣipan //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yajña eti vitataḥ kalpamāna ījānam abhi lokam svargam /

Halfverse: c tam agnayaḥ sarvahutam juṣantām prājāpatyam medhyam jātavedasaḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ījānaś citam ārukṣad agnim nākasya pṛṣṭhād divam utpatiṣyan /

Halfverse: c tasmai pra bhāti nabhaso jyotiṣīmānt svargaḥ panthāḥ sukṛte devayānaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a agnir hotādhvaryuṣ ṭe bṛhaspatir indro brahmā dakṣiṇatas te astu /

Halfverse: c huto ‘yam samsthito yajña eti yatra pūrvam ayanam hutānām //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a apūpavān kṣīravāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a apūpavān dadhivāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a apūpavān drapsavāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a apūpavān ghṛtavāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a apūpavān māmsavāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //20// {21}

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a apūpavān annavāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a apūpavān madhumāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a apūpavān rasavāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a apūpavān apavāmś carur eha sīdatu /

Halfverse: c lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānām hutabhāgā iha stha //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a apūpāpihitān kumbhān yāms te devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutah //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a yās te dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrāḥ svadhāvatīḥ /

Halfverse: c tās te santūdbhvīḥ prabhvīs tās te yamo rājānu manyatām //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a akṣitim bhūyasīm //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a drapsaś caskanda pṛthivīm anu dyām imam ca yonim anu yaś ca pūrvaḥ /

Halfverse: c samānam yonim anu samcarantam drapsam juhomy anu sapta hotrāḥ //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a śatadhāram vāyum arkam svarvidam nṛcakṣasas te abhi cakṣate rayim /

Halfverse: c ye pṛnanti pra ca yachanti sarvadā te duhrate daksiṇām saptamātaram //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a kośam duhanti kalaśam caturbilam iḍām dhenum madhumatīm svastaye /

Halfverse: c ūrjam madantīm aditim janeṣv agne mā himsīḥ parame vyoman //30// {22}

Verse: 31

Halfverse: a etat te devaḥ savitā vāso dadāti bhartave /

Halfverse: c tat tvam yamasya rājye vasānas tārpyam cara //31//

Verse: 32

Halfverse: a dhānā dhenur abhavad vatso asyās tilo ‘bhavat /

Halfverse: c tām vai yamasya rājye akṣitām upa jīvati //32//

Verse: 33

Halfverse: a etās te asau dhenavaḥ kāmadughā bhavantu /

Halfverse: c enīḥ śyenīḥ sarūpā virūpās tilavatsā upa tiṣṭhantu tvātra //33//

Verse: 34

Halfverse: a enīr dhānā hariṇīḥ śyenīr asya kṛṣṇā dhānā rohiṇīr dhenavas te /

Halfverse: c tilavatsā ūrjam asmai duhānā viśvāhā santv anapasphurantīḥ //34//

Verse: 35

Halfverse: a vaiśvānare havir idam juhomi sāhasram śatadhāram utsam /

Halfverse: c sa bibharti pitaram pitāmahān prapitāmahān bibharti pinvamānaḥ //35//

Verse: 36

Halfverse: a sahasradhāram śatadhāram utsam akṣitam vyacyamānam salilasya pṛṣṭhe /

Halfverse: c ūrjam duhānam anapasphurantam upāsate pitaraḥ svadhābhih //36//

Verse: 37

Halfverse: a idam kasāmbu cayanena citam tat sajātā ava paśyateta /

Halfverse: c martyo ‘yam amṛtatvam eti tasmai gṛhān kṛṇuta yāvatsabandhu //37//

Verse: 38

Halfverse: a ihaivaidhi dhanasanir ihacitta ihakratuḥ /

Halfverse: c ihaidhi vīryavattaro vayodhā aparāhataḥ //38//

Verse: 39

Halfverse: a putram pautram abhitarpayantīr āpo madhumatīr imāḥ /

Halfverse: c svadhām pitṛbhyo amṛtam duhānā āpo devīr ubhayāms tarpayantu //39//

Verse: 40

Halfverse: a āpo agnim pra hiṇuta pitṝṁr upemam yajñam pitaro me juṣantām /

Halfverse: c āsīnām ūrjam upa ye sacante te no rayim sarvavīram ni yachān //40// {23}

Verse: 41

Halfverse: a sam indhate amartyam havyavāham ghṛtapriyam /

Halfverse: c sa veda nihitān nidhīn pitṝn parāvato gatān //41//

Verse: 42

Halfverse: a yam te mantham yam odananam yan māmsam nipṛṇāmi te /

Halfverse: c te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutaḥ //42//

Verse: 43

Halfverse: a yās te dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrāḥ svadhāvatīḥ /

Halfverse: c tās te santūdbhvīḥ prabhvīs tās te yamo rājānu manyatām //43//

Verse: 44

Halfverse: a idam pūrvam aparam niyānam yenā te pūrve pitaraḥ paretāḥ /

Halfverse: c purogavā ye abhisāco asya te tvā vahanti sukṛtām u lokam //44//

Verse: 45

Halfverse: a sarasvatīm devayanto havante sarasvatīm adhvare tāyamāne /

Halfverse: c sarasvatīm sukṛto havante sarasvatī dāśuṣe vāryam dāt //45//

Verse: 46

Halfverse: a sarasvatīm pitaro havante dakṣiṇā yajñam abhinakṣamāṇāḥ /

Halfverse: c āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam anamīvā iṣa ā dhehy asme //46//

Verse: 47

Halfverse: a sarasvati yā saratham yayāthokthaiḥ svadhābhir devi pitṛbhir madantī /

Halfverse: c sahasrārgham iḍo atra bhāgam rāyas poṣam yajamānāya dhehi //47//

Verse: 48

Halfverse: a pṛthivīm tvā pṛthivyām ā veśayāmi devo no dhātā pra tirāty āyuḥ /

Halfverse: c parāparaitā vasuvid vo astv adhā mṛtāḥ pitṛṣu sam bhavantu //48//

Verse: 49

Halfverse: a ā pra cyavethām apa tan mṛjethām yad vām abhibhā atrocuḥ /

Halfverse: c asmād etam aghnyau tad vaśīyo dātuḥ pitṛṣv ihabhojanau mama //49//

Verse: 50

Halfverse: a eyam agan dakṣiṇā bhadrato nā anena dattā sudughā vayodhāḥ /

Halfverse: c yauvane jīvān upapṛñcatī jarā pitṛbhya upasamparāṇayād imān //50// {24}

Verse: 51

Halfverse: a idam pitṛbhyaḥ pra bharāmi barhir jīvam devebhya uttaram stṛṇāmi /

Halfverse: c tad ā roha puruṣa medhyo bhavan prati tvā jānantu pitarah paretam //51//

Verse: 52

Halfverse: a edam barhir asado medhyo ‘bhūḥ prati tvā jānantu pitaraḥ paretam /

Halfverse: c yathāparu tanva1m sam bharasva gātrāṇi te brahmaṇā kalpayāmi //52//

Verse: 53

Halfverse: a parṇo rājāpidhānam carūṇām ūrjo balam saha ojo na āgan /

Halfverse: c āyur jīvebhyo vidadhad dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya //53//

Verse: 54

Halfverse: a ūrjo bhāgo ya imam jajānāśmānnānām ādhipatyam jagāma /

Halfverse: c tam arcata viśvamitrā havirbhiḥ sa no yamaḥ prataram jīvase dhāt //54//

Verse: 55

Halfverse: a yathā yamāya harmyam avapan pañca mānavāḥ /

Halfverse: c evā vapāmi harmyam yathā me bhūrayo ‘sata //55//

Verse: 56

Halfverse: a idam hiraṇyam bibhṛhi yat te pitābibhaḥ purā /

Halfverse: c svargam yataḥ pitur hastam nir mṛḍḍhi dakṣiṇam //56//

Verse: 57

Halfverse: a ye ca jīvā ye ca mṛtā ye jātā ye ca yajñiyāḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhyo ghṛtasya kulyaitu madhudhārā vyundatī //57//

Verse: 58

Halfverse: a vṛṣā matīnām pavate vicakṣaṇaḥ sūro ahnām pratarītoṣasām divaḥ /

Halfverse: c prāṇaḥ sindhūnām kalaśāṁ acikradad indrasya hārdim āviśan manīṣayā //58//

Verse: 59

Halfverse: a tveṣas te dhūma ūrṇotu divi ṣam chukra ātataḥ /

Halfverse: c sūro na hi dyutā tvam kṛpā pāvaka rocase //59//

Verse: 60

Halfverse: a pra vā etīndur indrasya niṣkṛtim sakhā sakhyur na pra mināti samgiraḥ /

Halfverse: c marya iva yoṣāḥ sam arṣase somaḥ kalaśe śatayāmanā pathā //60// {25}

Verse: 61

Halfverse: a akṣann amīmadanta hy ava priyāṁ adhūṣata /

Halfverse: c astoṣata svabhānavo viprā yaviṣṭhā īmahe //61//

Verse: 62

Halfverse: a ā yāta pitaraḥ somyāso gambhīraiḥ pathibhiḥ pitṛyāṇaiḥ /

Halfverse: c āyur asmabhyam dadhataḥ prajām ca rāyaś ca poṣair abhi naḥ sacadhvam //62//

Verse: 63

Halfverse: a parā yāta pitaraḥ somyāso gambhīraiḥ pathibhiḥ pūryāṇaiḥ /

Halfverse: c adhā māsi punar ā yāta no gṛhān havir attum suprajasaḥ suvīrāḥ //63//

Verse: 64

Halfverse: a yad vo agnir ajahād ekam aṅgam pitṛlokam gamayam jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c tad va etat punar ā pyāyayāmi sāṅgāḥ svarge pitaro mādayadhvam //64//

Verse: 65

Halfverse: a abhūd dūtaḥ prahito jātavedāḥ sāyam nyahna upavandyo nṛbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c prādāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhayā te akṣann addhi tvam deva prayatā havīmṣi //65//

Verse: 66

Halfverse: a asau hā iha te manaḥ kakutsalam iva jāmayaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhy ṝnam bhūma ūrṇuhi //66//

Verse: 67

Halfverse: a śumbhantām lokāḥ pitṛṣadanāḥ pitṛṣadane tvā loka ā sādayāmi //67//

Verse: 68

Halfverse: a ye asmākam pitaras teṣām barhir asi //68//

Verse: 69

Halfverse: a ud uttamam varuṇa pāśam asmad avādhamam śrathāya /

Halfverse: c adhā vayam āditya vrate tavānāgaso aditaye syāma //69//

Verse: 70

Halfverse: a prāsmat pāśān varuṇa muñca sarvān yaih samāme badhyate yair vyāme /

Halfverse: c adhā jīvema śaradam śatāni tvayā rājan gupitā rakṣamāṇāḥ //70// {26}

Verse: 71

Halfverse: a agnaye kavyavāhanāya svadhā namaḥ //71//

Verse: 72

Halfverse: a somāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ //72//

Verse: 73

Halfverse: a pitṛbhyaḥ somavadbhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ //73//

Verse: 74

Halfverse: a yamāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ //74//

Verse: 75

Halfverse: a etat te pratatāmaha svadhā ye ca tvām anu //75//

Verse: 76

Halfverse: a etat te tatāmaha svadhā ye ca tvām anu //76//

Verse: 77

Halfverse: a etat te tata svadhā //77//

Verse: 78

Halfverse: a svadhā pitṛbhyaḥ pṛthiviṣadbhyaḥ //78//

Verse: 79

Halfverse: a svadhā pitṛbhyo antarikṣasadbhyaḥ //79//

Verse: 80

Halfverse: a svadhā pitṛbhyo diviṣadbhyaḥ //80// {27}

Verse: 81

Halfverse: a namo vaḥ pitara ūrje namo vaḥ pitaro rasāya //81//

Verse: 82

Halfverse: a namo vaḥ pitaro bhāmāya namo vaḥ pitaro manyave //82//

Verse: 83

Halfverse: a namo vaḥ pitaro yad ghoram tasmai namo vaḥ pitaro yat krūram tasmai //83//

Verse: 84

Halfverse: a namo vaḥ pitaro yac chivam tasmai namo vaḥ pitaro yat syonam tasmai //84//

Verse: 85

Halfverse: a namo vaḥ pitaraḥ svadhā vaḥ pitaraḥ //85//

Verse: 86

Halfverse: a ye ‘tra pitaraḥ pitaro ye ‘tra yūyam stha yuṣmāms te ‘nu yūyam teṣām śreṣṭhā bhūyāstha //86//

Verse: 87

Halfverse: a ya iha pitaro jīvā iha vayam smaḥ /

Halfverse: c asmāṁs te ‘nu vayam teṣām śreṣṭhā bhūyāsma //87//

Verse: 88

Halfverse: a ā tvāgna idhīmahi dyumantam devājaram /

Halfverse: c yad gha sā te panīyasī samid dīdayati dyavi /

Halfverse: e iṣam stotṛbhya ā bhara //88//

Verse: 89

Halfverse: a candramā apsv a1ntar ā suparṇo dhāvate divi /

Halfverse: c na vo hiraṇyanemayaḥ padam vindanti vidyuto vittam me asya rodasī //89// {28}

## Book: 19

19

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a samsam sravantu nadya1ḥ sam vātāḥ sam patatriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c yajñam imam vardhayatā giraḥ samsrāvyṝṇa haviṣā juhomi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a imam homā yajñam avatemam samsrāvaṇā uta yajñam imam vardhayatā giraḥ samsrāvyṝṇa haviṣā juhomi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a rūpamrūpam vayovayaḥ samrabhyainam pari ṣvaje /

Halfverse: c yajñam imam catasraḥ pradiśo vardhayantu samsrāvyṝṇa haviṣā juhomi //3//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śam ta āpo haimavatīḥ śam u te santūtsyāḥ /

Halfverse: c śam te saniṣyadā āpaḥ śam u te santu varṣyāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śam ta āpo dhanvanyā3ḥ śam te santv anūpyāḥ /

Halfverse: c śam te khanitrimā āpaḥ śam yāḥ kumbhebhir ābhṛtāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anabhrayaḥ khanamānā viprā gambhīre apasaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhiṣagbhyo bhiṣaktarā āpo achā vadāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a apām aha divyānām apām srotasyānām /

Halfverse: c apām aha praṇejane ‘śvā bhavatha vājinaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tā apaḥ śivā apo ‘yakṣmamkaraṇīr apaḥ /

Halfverse: c yathaiva tṛpyate mayas tās ta ā datta bhesajīḥ //5//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a divas pṛthivyāḥ pary antarikṣād vanaspatibhyo adhy oṣadhībhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yatrayatra vibhṛto jātavedās tata stuto juṣamāṇo na ehi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yas te apsu mahimā yo vaneṣu ya oṣadhīṣu paśuṣv apsv antaḥ /

Halfverse: c agne sarvās tanva1ḥ sam rabhasva tābhir na ehi draviṇodā ajasraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yas te deveṣu mahimā svargo yā te tanuḥ pitṛṣv āviveśa /

Halfverse: c puṣṭir yā te manuṣyṝṣu paprathe ‘gne tayā rayim asmāsu dhehi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śrutkarṇāya kavaye vedyāya vacobhir vākair upa yāmi rātim /

Halfverse: c yato bhayam abhayam tan no astv ava devānām yaja heḍo agne //4//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yām āhutim prathamām atharvā yā jātā yā havyam akṛṇoj jātavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ta etām prathamo johavīmi tābhiṣ ṭupto vahatu havyam agnir agnaye svāha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ākūtim devīm subhagām puro dadhe cittasya mātā suhavā no astu /

Halfverse: c yām āśām emi kevalī sā me astu videyam enām manasi praviṣṭām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ākūtyā no bṛhaspata ākūtyā na upā gahi /

Halfverse: c atho bhagasya no dhehy atho naḥ suhavo bhava //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir ma ākūtim āṅgirasaḥ prati jānātu vācam etām /

Halfverse: c yasya devā devatāḥ sambabhūvuḥ sa supraṇītāḥ kāmo anv etv asmān //4//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indro rājā jagataś carṣaṇīnām adhi kṣami viṣurūpam yad asti /

Halfverse: c tato dadāti dāśuṣe vasūni codad rādha upastutaś cid arvāk //1//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sahasrabāhuḥ puruṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt /

Halfverse: c sa bhūmim viśvato vṛtvāty atiṣṭhad daśāṅgulam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tribhiḥ padbhir dyām arohat pād asyehābhavat punaḥ /

Halfverse: c tathā vy akrāmad viṣvaṅ aśanānaśane anu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tāvanto asya mahimānas tato jyāyāmś ca pūruṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c pādo ‘sya viśvā bhūtāni tripād asyāmṛtam divi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a puruṣa evedam sarvam yad bhūtam yac ca bhāvyam /

Halfverse: c utāmṛtatvasyeśvaro yad anyenābhavat saha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yat puruṣam vy adadhuḥ katidhā vy akalpayan /

Halfverse: c mukham kim asya kim bāhū kim ūrū pādā ucyate //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a brāhmaṇo ‘sya mukham āsīd bāhū rājanyo ‘bhavat /

Halfverse: c madhyam tad asya yad vaiśyaḥ padbhyām śūdro ajāyata //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a candramā manaso jātaś cakṣoḥ sūryo ajāyata /

Halfverse: c mukhād indraś cāgniś ca prāṇād vāyur ajāyata //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a nābhyā āsīd antarikṣam śīrṣṇo dyauḥ sam avartata /

Halfverse: c padbhyām bhūmir diśaḥ śrotrāt tathā lokāṁ akalpayan //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a virāḍ agre sam abhavad virājo adhi pūruṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa jāto aty aricyata paścād bhūmim atho puraḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yat puruṣeṇa haviṣā devā yajñam atanvata /

Halfverse: c vasanto asyāsīd ājyam grīṣma idhmaḥ śarad dhaviḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tam yajñam prāvṛṣā praukṣan puruṣam jātam agraśaḥ /

Halfverse: c tena devā ayajanta sādhyā vasavaś ca ye //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a tasmād aśvā ajāyanta ye ca ke cobhayādataḥ /

Halfverse: c gāvo ha jajñire tasmāt tasmāj jātā ajāvayaḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a tasmād yajñāt sarvahuta ṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire /

Halfverse: c chando ha jajñire tasmād yajus tasmād ajāyata //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a tasmād yajñāt sarvahutaḥ sambhṛtam pṛṣadājyam /

Halfverse: c paśūms tāmś cakre vāyavyān āraṇyā grāmyāś ca ye //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a saptāsyāsan paridhayas triḥ sapta samidhaḥ kṛtāḥ /

Halfverse: c devā yad yajñam tanvānā abadhnan puruṣam paśum //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a mūrdhno devasya bṛhato amśavaḥ sapta saptatīḥ /

Halfverse: c rājñaḥ somasyājāyanta jātasya puruṣād adhi //16//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a citrāṇi sākam divi rocanāni sarīsṛpāṇi bhuvane javāni /

Halfverse: c turmiśam sumatim ichamāno ahāni gīrbhiḥ saparyāmi nākam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a suhavam agne kṛttikā rohiṇī cāstu bhadram mṛgaśiraḥ śam ārdrā /

Halfverse: c punarvasū sūnṛtā cāru puṣyo bhānur āśleṣā ayanam maghā me //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a puṇyam pūrvā phalgunyau cātra hastaś citrā śivā svāti sukho me astu /

Halfverse: c rādhe viśākhe suhavānurādhā jyeṣṭhā sunakṣatram ariṣṭa mūlam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a annam pūrvā rāsatām me aṣāḍhā ūrjam devy uttarā ā vahantu /

Halfverse: c abhijin me rāsatām puṇyam eva śravaṇaḥ śraviṣṭhāḥ kurvatām supuṣṭim //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā me mahac chatabhiṣag varīya ā me dvayā proṣṭhapadā suśarma /

Halfverse: c ā revatī cāśvayujau bhagam ma ā me rayim bharaṇya ā vahantu //5//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yāni nakṣatrāṇi divy a1ntarikṣe apsu bhūmau yāni nageṣu dikṣu /

Halfverse: c prakalpayamś candramā yāny eti sarvāṇi mamaitāni śivāni santu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aṣṭāvimśāni śivāni śagmāni saha yogam bhajantu me /

Halfverse: c yogam pra padye kṣemam ca kṣemam pra padye yogam ca namo ‘horātrābhyām astu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a svastitam me suprātaḥ susāyam sudivam sumṛgam suśakunam me astu /

Halfverse: c suhavam agne svasty a1martyam gatvā punar āyābhinandan //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anuhavam parihavam parivādam parikṣavam /

Halfverse: c sarvair me riktakumbhān parā tānt savitaḥ suva //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a apapāpam parikṣavam puṇyam bhakṣīmahi kṣavam /

Halfverse: c śivā te pāpa nāsikām puṇyagaś cābhi mehatām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a imā yā brahmaṇas pate viṣucīr vāta īrate /

Halfverse: c sadhrīcīr indra tāḥ kṛtvā mahyam śivatamās kṛdhi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a svasti no astv abhayam no astu namo ‘horatrābhyām astu //7//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntam idam urv a1ntarikṣam /

Halfverse: c śāntā udanvatīr āpaḥ śāntā naḥ santv oṣadhīḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śāntāni pūrvarūpāṇi śāntam no astu kṛtākṛtam /

Halfverse: c śāntam bhūtam ca bhavyam ca sarvam eva śam astu naḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a iyam yā parameṣṭhinī vāg devī brahmasamśitā /

Halfverse: c yayaiva sasṛje ghoram tayaiva śāntir astu naḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a idam yat parameṣṭhinam mano vām brahmasamśitam /

Halfverse: c yenaiva sasṛje ghoram tenaiva śāntir astu naḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a imāni yāni pañcendriyāni manaḥṣaṣṭhāni me hṛdi brahmaṇā samśitāni /

Halfverse: c yair eva sasṛje ghoram tair eva śāntir astu naḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śam no mitraḥ śam varuṇaḥ śam viṣṇuḥ śam prajāpatiḥ /

Halfverse: c śam na indro bṛhaspatiḥ śam no bhavatv aryamā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śam no mitraḥ śam varuṇaḥ śam vivasvām cham antakaḥ /

Halfverse: c utpātāḥ pārthivāntarikṣāḥ śam no divicarā grahāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a śam no bhūmir vepyamānā śam ulkā nirhatam ca yat /

Halfverse: c śam gāvo lohitakṣīrāḥ śam bhūmir ava tīryatīḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a nakṣatram ulkābhihatam śam astu naḥ śam no ‘bhicārāḥ śam u santu kṛtyāḥ /

Halfverse: c śam no nikhātā valgāḥ śam ulkā deśopasargāḥ śam u no bhavantu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a śam no grahāś cāndramasāḥ śam ādityaś ca rāhuṇā /

Halfverse: c śam no mṛtyur dhūmaketuḥ śam rudrās tigmatejasaḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a śam rudrāḥ śam vasavaḥ śam ādityāḥ śam agnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c śam no maharṣayo devāḥ śam devāḥ śam bṛhaspatiḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a brahma prajāpatir dhātā lokā vedāḥ saptaṛṣayo ‘gnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c tair me kṛtam svastyayanam indro me śarma yachatu brahmā me śarma yachatu /

Halfverse: e viśve me devāḥ śarma yachantu sarve me devāḥ śarma yachantu //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yāni kāni cic chāntāni loke saptaṛṣayo viduḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvāṇi śam bhavantu me śam me astv abhayam me astu //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a pṛthivī śāntir antarikṣam śāntir dyauḥ śāntir āpaḥ śāntir oṣadhayaḥ śāntir vanaspatayaḥ śāntir viśve me devāḥ śāntiḥ sarve me devāḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c yad iha ghoram yad iha krūram yad iha pāpam tac chāntam tac chivam sarvam eva śam astu naḥ //14//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śam na indrāgnī bhavatām avobhiḥ śam na indrāvaruṇā rātahavyā /

Halfverse: c śam indrāsomā suvitāya śam yoḥ śam na indrāpūṣaṇā vājasātau //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śam no bhagaḥ śam u naḥ śamso astu śam naḥ puramdhiḥ śam u santu rāyaḥ /

Halfverse: c śam naḥ satyasya suyamasya śamsaḥ śam no aryamā purujāto astu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śam no dhātā śam u dhartā no astu śam na urūcī bhavatu svadhābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c śam rodasī bṛhatī śam no adriḥ śam no devānām suhavāni santu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śam no agnir jyotiranīko astu śam no mitrāvaruṇāv aśvinā śam /

Halfverse: c śam naḥ sukṛtām sukṛtāni santu śam na iṣiro abhi vātu vātaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śam no dyāvāpṛthivī pūrvahūtau śam antarikṣam dṛśaye no astu /

Halfverse: c śam na oṣadhīr vanino bhavantu śam no rajasas patir astu jiṣṇuḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śam na indro vasubhir devo astu śam ādityebhir varuṇaḥ suśamsaḥ /

Halfverse: c śam no rudro rudrebhir jalāṣaḥ śam nas tvaṣṭā gnābhir iha śṛṇotu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śam naḥ somo bhavatu brahma śam naḥ śam no grāvāṇaḥ śam u santu yajñāḥ /

Halfverse: c śam naḥ svarūnām mitayo bhavantu śam naḥ prasva1ḥ śam v astu vediḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a śam naḥ sūrya urucakṣā ud etu śam no bhavantu pradiśaś catasraḥ /

Halfverse: c śam naḥ parvatā dhruvayo bhavantu śam naḥ sindhavaḥ śam u santv āpah //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a śam no aditir bhavatu vratebhiḥ śam no bhavantu marutaḥ svarkāḥ /

Halfverse: c śam no viṣṇuḥ śam u pūṣā no astu śam no bhavitram śam v astu vāyuḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a śam no devaḥ savitā trāyamāṇaḥ śam no bhavantūṣaso vibhātīḥ /

Halfverse: c śam naḥ parjanyo bhavatu prajābhyaḥ śam naḥ kṣetrasya patir astu śambhuḥ //10//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śam naḥ satyasya patayo bhavantu śam no arvantaḥ śam u santu gāvaḥ /

Halfverse: c śam na ṛbhavaḥ sukṛtaḥ suhastāḥ śam no bhavatu pitaro haveṣu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śam no devā viśvadevā bhavantu śam sarasvatī saha dhībhir astu /

Halfverse: c śam abhiṣācaḥ śam u rātiṣācaḥ śam no divyāḥ pārthivāḥ śam no apyāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śam no aja ekapād devo astu śam ahir budhnya1ḥ śam samudraḥ /

Halfverse: c śam no apām napāt perur astu śam naḥ pṛṣṇir bhavatu devagopā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ādityā rudrā vasavo juṣantām idam brahma kriyamāṇam navīyaḥ /

Halfverse: c sṛṇvantu no divyāḥ pārthivāso gojātā uta ye yajñiyāsaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye devānām ṛtvijo yajñiyāso manor yajatrā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ /

Halfverse: c te no rāsantām urugāyam adya yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tad astu mitrāvaruṇā tad agne śam yor asmabhyam idam astu śastam /

Halfverse: c aśīmahi gādham uta pratiṣṭhām namo dive bṛhate sādanāya //6//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a uṣā apa svasus tamaḥ sam vartayati vartanim sujātatā /

Halfverse: c ayā vājam devahitam sanema madema śatahimāḥ suvīrāḥ //1//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrasya bāhū sthavirau vṛṣāṇau citrā imā vṛṣabhau pārayiṣṇū /

Halfverse: c tau yokṣe prathamo yoga āgate yābhyām jitam asurāṇām sva1r yat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āśuḥ śiśāno vṛṣabho na bhīmo ghanāghanaḥ kṣobhaṇaś carṣanīnām /

Halfverse: c samkrandano ‘nimiṣa ekavīraḥ śatam senā ajayat sākam indraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samkrandanenānimiṣeṇa jiṣṇunāyodhyena duścyavanena dhṛsnunā /

Halfverse: c tad indreṇa jayata tat sahadhvam yudho nara iṣuhastena vṛṣṇā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sa iṣuhastaiḥ sa niṣaṅgibhir vaśī samsraṣṭā sa yudha indro gaṇena /

Halfverse: c samsṛṣṭajit somapā bāhuśardhy ḥgradhanvā pratihitābhir astā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a balavijñāyaḥ sthaviraḥ pravīraḥ sahasvān vājī sahamāna ugraḥ /

Halfverse: c abhivīro abhiṣatvā sahojij jaitram indra ratham ā tiṣṭha govidam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a imam vīram anu harṣadhvam ugram indram sakhāyo anu sam rabhadhvam /

Halfverse: c grāmajitam gojitam vajrabāhum jayantam ajma pramṛṇantam ojasā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a abhi gotrāṇi sahasā gāhamāno ‘dāya ugraḥ śatamanyur indraḥ /

Halfverse: c duścyavanaḥ pṛtanāṣāḍ ayodhyo3 ‘smākam senā avatu pra yutsu //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bṛhaspate pari dīyā rathena rakṣohāmitrām apabādhamānaḥ /

Halfverse: c prabhañjam chatrūn pramṛṇann amitrān asmākam edhy avitā tanūnām //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a indra eṣām netā bṛhaspatir dakṣiṇā yajñaḥ pura etu somaḥ /

Halfverse: c devasenānām abhibhañjatīnām jayantīnām maruto yantu madhye //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a indrasya vṛṣṇo varuṇasya rājño ādityānām marutām śardha ugram /

Halfverse: c mahāmanasām bhuvanacyavānām ghoṣo devānām jayatām ud asthāt //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a asmākam indraḥ samṛteṣu dhvajeṣv asmākaṣ yā iṣavas tā jayantu /

Halfverse: c asmākam vīrā uttare bhavantv asmān devāso ‘vatā haveṣu //11//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam ucchreyo ‘vasānam āgām śive me dyāvāpṛthivī abhūtām /

Halfverse: c asapatnāḥ pradiśo me bhavantu na vai tvā dviṣmo abhayam no astu //1//

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yata indra bhayāmahe tato no abhayam kṛdhi /

Halfverse: c maghavam chagdhi tava tvam na ūtibhir vi dviṣo vi mṛdho jahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indram vayam anūrādham havāmahe ‘nu rādhyāsma dvipadā catuṣpadā /

Halfverse: c mā naḥ senā araruṣīr upa gur viṣūcir indra druho vi nāśaya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indras trātota vṛtrahā parasphāno vareṇyaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa rakṣitā caramataḥ sa madhyataḥ sa paścāt sa purastān no astu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a urum no lokam anu neṣi vidvānt sva1r yaj jyotir abhayam svasti /

Halfverse: c ugrā ta indra sthavirasya bāhū upa kṣayema śaraṇā bṛhantā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a abhayam naḥ karaty antarikṣam abhayam dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime /

Halfverse: c abhayam paścād abhayam purastād uttarād adharād abhayam no astu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a abhayam mitrād abhayam amitrād abhayam jñātād abhayam puro yaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhayam naktam abhayam divā naḥ sarvā āśā mama mitram bhavantu //6//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asapatnam purastāt paścān no abhayam kṛtam /

Halfverse: c savitā mā dakṣiṇata uttarān mā śacīpatiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a divo mādityā rakṣatu bhūmyā rakṣantv agnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī rakṣatām mā purastād aśvināv abhitaḥ śarma yachatām /

Halfverse: e tiraścīn aghnyā rakṣatu jātavedā bhūtakṛto me sarvataḥ santu varma //2//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnir mā pātu vasubhiḥ purastāt tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vāyur māntarikṣenaitasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a somo mā rudrair dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a varuṇo mādityair etasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sūryo mā dyāvāpṛthivībhyām pratīcyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a āpo mauṣadhīmatīr etasyā diśaḥ pāntu tāsu krame tāsu śraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c tā mā rakṣantu tā mā gopāyantu tābhya ātmānam pari dade svāha //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a viśvakarmā mā saptaṛṣibhir udīcyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a indro mā marutvān etasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a prajāpatir mā prajananavānt saha pratiṣṭhayā dhruvāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir mā viśvair devair ūrdhvāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmim chraye tām puram praimi /

Halfverse: c sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha //10//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnim te vasuvantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye māghāyavaḥ prācyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vāyum tṝ3 ‘ntarikṣavantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye māghāyavaḥ etasyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a somam te rudravantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye mā ‘ghāyavo dakṣiṇāyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a varuṇam ta ādityavantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye māghāyava etasyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sūryam te dyāvāpṛthivīvantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye māghāyava pratīcyāḥ diśo ‘bhidāsān //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a apas ta oṣadhīmatīr ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye māghāyava etasyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a viśvakarmāṇam te saptaṛṣivantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye mā ‘ghāyava udīcyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a indram te marutvantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye mā ‘ghāyava etasyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a prajāpatim te prajananavantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye māghāyavo dhruvāyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatim te viśvadevavantam ṛchantu /

Halfverse: c ye māghāyava ūrdhvāyā diśo ‘bhidāsān //10//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mitraḥ pṛthivyod akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vāyur antarikṣeṇod akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sūryo divod akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a candramā nakṣatrair ud akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a soma oṣadhībhir ud akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yajño dakṣiṇābhir ud akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a samudro nadībhir ud akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a brahma brahmacāribhir ud akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a indro vīryṝ3ṇod akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a devā amṛtenod akrāmams tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a prajāpatiḥ prajābhir ud akrāmat tām puram pra ṇayāmi vaḥ /

Halfverse: c tām ā viśata tām pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu //11//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apa nyadhuḥ pauruṣeyam vadham yam indrāgnī dhātā savitā bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c somo rājā varuṇo aśvinā yamaḥ pūṣāsmān pari pātu mṛtyoḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yāni cakāra bhuvanasya yas patiḥ prajāpatir mātariśvā prajābhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c pradiśo yāni vasate diśaś ca tāni me varmāṇi bahulāni santu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat te tanūṣv anahyanta devā dyurājayo dehinaḥ /

Halfverse: c indro yac cakre varma tad asmān pātu viśvataḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a varma me dyāvāpṛthivī varmāhar varma sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c varma me viśve devāḥ kran mā mā prāpat pratīcikā //4//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a gāyatry ḥ1ṣṇig anuṣṭub bṛhatī paṅktis triṣṭub jagatyai //1//

Hymn: 22

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āṅgirasānām ādyaiḥ pañcānuvākaiḥ svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ṣaṣṭhāya svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a saptamāṣṭamābhyām svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a nīlanakhebhyaḥ svāhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a haritebhyaḥ svāhā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kṣudrebhyaḥ svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a paryāyikebhyaḥ svāhā //7//

Halfverse: a prathamebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a dvitīyebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tṛtīyebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a upottamebhyaḥ svāhā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a uttamebhyaḥ svāhā //12//

Halfverse: a uttarebhyaḥ svāhā //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ṛṣibhyaḥ svāhā //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a śikhibhyaḥ svāhā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a gaṇebhyaḥ svāhā //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a mahāgaṇebhyaḥ svāhā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a sarvebhyo ‘ṅgirobhyo vidagaṇebhyaḥ svāhā //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a pṛthakṣahasrābhyām svāhā //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a brahmaṇe svāhā //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a brahmajyeṣṭhā sambhṛtā viryāṇi brahmāgre jyeṣṭham divam ā tatāna /

Halfverse: c bhūtānām brahmā prathamota jajñe tenārhati brahmaṇā spardhitum kaḥ //21//

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ātharvaṇānam caturṛcebhyaḥ svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ṣaḷṛcebhyaḥ svāhā //3//

Halfverse: a kṣudrebhyaḥ svāhā //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a ekānṛcebhyaḥ svāhā //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a rohitebhyaḥ svāhā //23//

Verse: 24

Halfverse: a sūryābhyām svāhā //24//

Verse: 25

Halfverse: a vrātyābhyām svāhā //25//

Verse: 26

Halfverse: a prājāpatyābhyām svāhā //26//

Verse: 27

Halfverse: a viṣāsahyai svāhā //27//

Verse: 28

Halfverse: a maṅgalikebhyaḥ svāhā //28//

Verse: 29

Halfverse: a brahmaṇe svāhā //29//

Verse: 30

Halfverse: a brahmajyeṣṭhā sambhṛtā vīryāṇi brahmāgre jyeṣṭham divam ā tatāna /

Halfverse: c bhūtānām brahmā prathamota jajñe tenārhati brahmaṇā spardhitum kaḥ //30//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yena devam savitāram pari devā adhārayan /

Halfverse: c tenemam brahmaṇas pate pari rāṣṭrāya dhattana //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a parīmam indram āyuṣe mahe kṣatrāya dhattana /

Halfverse: c yathainam jarase nayāj jyok kṣatre ‘dhi jāgarat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a parīmam indram āyuṣe mahe śrotrāya dhattana /

Halfverse: c yathainam jarase nayāj jyok śrotre ‘dhi jāgarat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pari dhatta dhatta no varcasemam jarāmṛtyum kṛṇuta dīrgham āyuḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatiḥ prāyachad vāsa etat somāya rājñe paridhātavā u //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a jarām su gacha pari dhatsva vāso bhavā gṛṣṭīnām abhiśastipā u /

Halfverse: c śatam ca jīva śaradaḥ purūcī rāyaś ca poṣam upasamvyayasva //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a parīdam vāso adhithāḥ svastaye ‘bhūr vāpīnām abhiśastipā u /

Halfverse: c śatam ca jīva śaradaḥ purūcīr vasūni cārur vi bhajāsi jīvan //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yogeyoge tavastaram vājevāje havāmahe /

Halfverse: c sakhāya indram ūtaye //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a hiraṇyavarṇo ajaraḥ suvīro jarāmṛtyuḥ prajayā sam viśasva /

Halfverse: c tad agnir āha tad u soma āha bṛhaspatiḥ savitā tad indraḥ //8//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aśrāntasya tvā manasā yunajmi prathamasya ca /

Halfverse: c utkūlam udvaho bhavoduhya prati dhāvatāt //1//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agneḥ prajātam pari yad dhiraṇyam amṛtam dadhre adhi martyeṣu /

Halfverse: c ya enad veda sa id enam arhati jarāmṛtyur bhavati yo bibharti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad dhiraṇyam sūryeṇa suvarṇam prajāvanto manavaḥ pūrva īṣire /

Halfverse: c tat tvā candram varcasā sam sṛjaty āyuṣmān bhavati yo bibharti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āyuṣe tvā varcase tvaujase ca balāya ca /

Halfverse: c yathā hiraṇyatejasā vibhāsāsi janāṁ anu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad veda rājā varuṇo veda devo bṛhaspatiḥ /

Halfverse: c indro yad vṛtrahā veda tat ta āyuṣyam bhuvat tat te varcasyam bhuvat //4//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a gobhiṣ ṭvā pātv ṛṣabho vṛṣā tvā pātu vājibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c vāyuṣ tvā brahmaṇā pātv indras tvā pātv indriyaiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a somas tvā pātv oṣadhībhir nakṣatraiḥ pātu sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c mādbhyas tvā candro vṛtrahā vātaḥ prāṇena rakṣatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tisro divas tisraḥ pṛthivīs trīṇy antarikṣāṇi caturaḥ samudrān /

Halfverse: c trivṛtam stomam trivṛta āpa āhus tās tvā rakṣantu trivṛtā trivṛdbhiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a trīn nākāms trīn samudrāms trīn bradhnāms trīn vaiṣṭapān /

Halfverse: c trīn mātariśvanas trīnt sūryān goptṝn kalpayāmi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ghṛtena tvā sam ukṣāmy agne ājyena vardhayan /

Halfverse: c agneś candrasya sūryasya mā prāṇam māyino dabhan //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a mā vaḥ prāṇam mā vo ‘pānam mā haro māyino dabhan /

Halfverse: c bhrājanto viśvavedaso devā daivyena dhāvata //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a prānenāgnim sam sṛjati vātaḥ prāṇena samhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c prāṇena viśvatomukham suryam devā ajanayan //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a āyuṣāyuḥkṛtām jīvāyuṣmān jīva mā mṛthāḥ /

Halfverse: c prāṇenātmanvatām jīva mā mṛtyor ud agā vaśam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a devānām nihitam nidhim yam indro ‘nvavindat pathibhir devayānaiḥ /

Halfverse: c āpo hiraṇyam jugupus trivṛdbhis tās tvā rakṣantu trivṛtā trivṛdbhiḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a trayastrimśad devatās trīṇi ca vīryāṇi priyāyamānā jugupur apsv antaḥ /

Halfverse: c asmimś candre adhi yad dhiraṇyam tenāyam kṛṇavad vīryāṇi //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ye devā divy ekādaśa stha te devāso havir idam juṣadhvam //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ye devā antarikṣa ekādaśa stha te devāso havir idam juṣadhvam //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ye devā pṛthivyām ekādaśa stha te devāso havir idam juṣadhvam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a asapatnam purastāt paścān no abhayam kṛtam /

Halfverse: c savitā mā dakṣiṇata uttarān mā śacīpatiḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a divo mādityā rakṣantu bhūmyā rakṣantv agnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c indrāgnī rakṣatām mā purastād aśvināv abhitaḥ śarma yachatām /

Halfverse: e tiraścīn aghnyā rakṣatu jātavedā bhūtakṛto me sarvataḥ santu varma //15//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imam badhnāmi te maṇim dīrghāyutvāya tejase /

Halfverse: c darbham sapatnadambhanam dviṣatas tapanam hṛdaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dviṣatas tāpayan hṛdaḥ śatrūṇām tāpayan manaḥ /

Halfverse: c durhārdaḥ sarvāms tvam darbha gharma ivābhīnt samtāpayan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a gharma ivābhitapan darbha dviṣato nitapan maṇe /

Halfverse: c hṛdaḥ sapatnānām bhinddhīndra iva virujam balam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a bhinddhi darbha sapatnānām hṛdayaḥ dviṣatām maṇe /

Halfverse: c udyan tvacam iva bhūmyāḥ śira eṣām vi pātaya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bhinddhi darbha sapatnān me bhinddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c bhinddhi me sarvān durhārdo bhinddhi me dviṣato maṇe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a chinddhi darbha sapatnān me chinddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c chinddhi me sarvān durhārdo chinddhi me dviṣato maṇe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a vṛśca darbha sapatnān me vṛśca me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c vṛśca me sarvān durhārdo vṛśca me dviṣato maṇe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a kṛnta darbha sapatnān me kṛnta me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c kṛnta me sarvān durhārdo kṛnta me dviṣato maṇe //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a pimśa darbha sapatnān me pimśa me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c pimśa me sarvān durhārdo pimśa me dviṣato maṇe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a vidhya darbha sapatnān me vidhya me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c vidhya me sarvān durhārdo vidhya me dviṣato maṇe //10//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a nikṣa darbha sapatnān me nikṣa me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c nikṣa me sarvān durhārdo nikṣa me dviṣato maṇe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tṛnddhi darbha sapatnān me tṛnddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c tṛnddhi me sarvān durhārdo tṛnddhi me dviṣato maṇe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a runddhi darbha sapatnān me runddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c runddhi me sarvān durhārdo runddhi me dviṣato maṇe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a mṛṇa darbha sapatnān me mṛṇa me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c mṛṇa me sarvān durhārdo mṛṇa me dviṣato maṇe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mantha darbha sapatnān me mantha me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c mantha me sarvān durhārdo mantha me dviṣato maṇe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a piṇḍḍhi darbha sapatnān me piṇḍḍhi me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c piṇḍḍhi me sarvān durhārdo piṇḍḍhi me dviṣato maṇe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a oṣa darbha sapatnān me oṣa me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c oṣa me sarvān durhārdo oṣa me dviṣato maṇe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a daha darbha sapatnān me daha me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c daha me sarvān durhārdo daha me dviṣato maṇe //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a jahi darbha sapatnān me jahi me pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c jahi me sarvān durhārdo jahi me dviṣato maṇe //9//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yat te darbha jarāmṛtyu śatam varmasu varma te /

Halfverse: c tenemam varmiṇam kṛtvā sapatnām jahi vīryaṅḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śatam te darbha varmāṇi sahasram vīryāṇi te /
