Halfverse: c tam asmai viśve tvām devā jarase bhartavā aduḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvām āhur devavarma tvām darbha brahmaṇas patim /

Halfverse: c tvām indrasyāhur varma tvam rāṣṭrāṇi rakṣasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sapatnakṣayaṇam darbha dviṣatas tapanam hṛdah /

Halfverse: c maṇim kṣatrasya vardhanam tanūpānam kṛṇomi te //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yat samudro abhyakrandat parjanyo vidyutā saha /

Halfverse: c tato hiranyayo bindus tato darbho ajāyata //5//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a audumbareṇa maṇinā puṣṭikāmāya vedhasā /

Halfverse: c paśūṇām sarveṣām sphātim goṣṭhe me savitā karat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo no agnir gārhapatyaḥ paśūnām adhipā asat /

Halfverse: c audumbaro vṛso maṇiḥ sam mā sṛjatu puṣṭyā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a karīṣiṇīm phalavatīm svadhām irām ca no gṛhe /

Halfverse: c audumbarasya tejasā dhātā puṣṭim dadhātu me //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad dvipāc ca catuṣpāc ca yāny annāni ye rasāḥ /

Halfverse: c gṛhṇṝ3 ‘ham tv ṝṣām bhūmānam bibhrad audumbaram maṇim //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a puṣṭim paśūnām pari jagrabhāham catuṣpadām dvipadām yac ca dhānyam /

Halfverse: c payaḥ paśūnām rasam oṣadhīnām bṛhaspatiḥ savitā me ni yachāt //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a aham paśūnām adhipā asāni mayi puṣṭam puṣṭapatir dadhātu /

Halfverse: c mahyam audumbaro maṇir draviṇāni ni yachatu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a upa maudumbaro maṇiḥ prajayā ca dhanena ca /

Halfverse: c indreṇa jinvito maṇir ā māgant saha varcasā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a devo maṇiḥ sapatnahā dhanasā dhanasātaye /

Halfverse: c paśor annasya bhūmānam gavām sphātim ni yachatu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yathāgre tvam vanaspate puṣṭhyā saha jajñiṣe /

Halfverse: c evā dhanasya me sphātim ā dadhātu sarasvatī //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ā me dhanam sarasvatī payasphātim ca dhānyam /

Halfverse: c sinīvāly upā vahād ayam caudumbaro maṇiḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tvam maṇīṇām adhipā vṛṣāsi tvayi puṣṭam puṣṭapatir jajāna /

Halfverse: c tvayīme vājā draviṇāni sarvaudumbaraḥ sa tvam asmat sahasvārād ārād arātim amatim kṣudham ca //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a grāmaṇīr asi grāmaṇīr utthāya abhiṣikto ‘bhi mā siñca varcasā /

Halfverse: c tejo ‘si tejo mayi dhārayādhi rayir asi rayim me dhehi //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a puṣṭir asi puṣṭyā mā sam aṅgdhi gṛhamedhī gṛhapatim mā kṛṇu /

Halfverse: c audumbaraḥ sa tvam asmāsu dhehi rayim ca naḥ sarvavīram ni yacha rāyas poṣāya prati muñce aham tvām //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ayam audumbaro maṇir vīro vīrāya badhyate /

Halfverse: c sa naḥ sanim madhumatīm kṛṇotu rayim ca naḥ sarvavīram ni yachāt //14//

Hymn: 32

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śatakāṇḍo duścyavanaḥ sahasraparṇa uttiraḥ /

Halfverse: c darbho ya ugra oṣadhis tam te badhnāmy āyuṣe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nāsya keśān pra vapanti norasi tāḍam ā ghnate /

Halfverse: c yasmā achinnaparṇena darbhena śarma yachati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a divi te tūlam oṣadhe pṛthivyām asi niṣṭhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvayā sahasrakāṇḍenāyuḥ pra vardhayāmahe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tisro divo aty atṛṇat tisra imāḥ pṛthivīr uta /

Halfverse: c tvayāham durhārdo jihvām ni tṛṇadmi vacāmsi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tvam asi sahamāno ‘ham asmi sahasvān /

Halfverse: c ubhau sahasvantau bhūtvā sapatnān sahiṣīvahi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sahasva no abhimātim sahasva pṛtanāyataḥ /

Halfverse: c sahasva sarvān durhārdaḥ suhārdo me bahūn kṛdhi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a darbheṇa devajātena divi ṣṭambhena śaśvad it /

Halfverse: c tenāham śaśvato janāṁ asanam sanavāni ca //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a priyam mā darbha kṛṇu brahmarājanyābhyām śūdrāya cāryāya ca /

Halfverse: c yasmai ca kāmayāmahe sarvasmai ca vipaśyate //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yo jāyamānaḥ pṛthivīm adṛmhad yo astabhnād antarikṣam divam ca /

Halfverse: c yam bibhratam nanu pāpmā viveda sa no ‘yam darbho varuṇo divā kaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a sapatnahā śatakāṇḍaḥ sahasvān oṣadhīnām prathamaḥ sam babhūva /

Halfverse: c sa no ‘yam darbhaḥ pari pātu viśvatas tena sākṣīya pṛtanāḥ pṛtanyataḥ //10//

Hymn: 33

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sahasrārghaḥ śatakāṇḍaḥ payasvān apām agnir vīrudhām rājasūyam /

Halfverse: c sa no ‘yam darbhaḥ pari pātu viśvato devo maṇir āyuṣā sam sṛjāti naḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ghṛtād ullupto madhumān payasvān bhūmidṛmho ‘cyutaś cyāvayiṣṇuḥ /

Halfverse: c nudant sapatnān adharāmś ca kṛṇvan darbhā roha mahatām indriyeṇa //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvam bhūmim aty eṣy ojasā tvam vedyām sīdasi cārur adhvare /

Halfverse: c tvām pavitram ṛṣayo ‘bharanta tvam punīhi duritāny asmat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tīkṣṇo rājā viṣāsahī rakṣohā viśvacarṣaṇiḥ /

Halfverse: c ojo devānām balam ugram etat tam te badhnāmi jarase svastaye //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a darbheṇa tvam kṛṇavad vīryāṇi darbham bibhrad ātmanā mā vyathiṣṭhāḥ /

Halfverse: c atiṣṭhāya varcasādhānyānt sūrya ivā bhāhi pradiśaś catasraḥ //5//

Hymn: 34

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a jāṅgiḍo ‘si jaṅgiḍo rakṣitāsi jaṅgidaḥ /

Halfverse: c dvipāc catuṣpād asmākam sarvam rakṣatu jaṅgidaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yā gṛtsyas tripañcāśīḥ śatam kṛtyākṛtaś ca ye /

Halfverse: c sarvān vinaktu tejaso ‘rasām jaṅgidas karat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a arasam kṛtrimam nādam arasāḥ sapta visrasaḥ /

Halfverse: c apeto jaṅgiḍāmatim iṣum asteva śātaya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kṛtyādūṣaṇa evāyam atho arātidūṣaṇah /

Halfverse: c atho sahasvāñ jaṅgiḍaḥ pra na āyumṣi tāriṣat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sa jaṅgiḍasya mahimā pari ṇaḥ pātu viśvataḥ /

Halfverse: c viṣkandham yena sāsaha samskandham oja ojasā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a triṣ ṭvā devā ajanayan niṣṭhitam bhūmyām adhi /

Halfverse: c tam u tvāṅgirā iti brāhmaṇāḥ pūrvyā viduḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a na tvā pūrvā oṣadhayo na tvā taranti yā navāḥ /

Halfverse: c vibādha ugro jaṅgiḍaḥ paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a athopadāna bhagavo jāṅgiḍāmitavīrya /

Halfverse: c purā ta ugrā grasata upendro vīryam dadau //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ugra it te vanaspata indra ojmānam ā dadhau /

Halfverse: c amīvāḥ sarvāś cātayam jahi rakṣāmsy oṣadhe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a āśarīkam viśarīkam balāsam pṛṣṭyāmayam /

Halfverse: c takmānam viśvaśāradam arasām jaṅgiḍas karat //10//

Hymn: 35

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrasya nāma gṛhṇanta ṛsayo jaṅgidam daduḥ /

Halfverse: c devā yam cakrur bheṣajam agre viṣkandhadūṣaṇam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sa no rakṣatu jaṅgiḍo dhanapālo dhaneva /

Halfverse: c devā yam cakrur brāhmaṇāḥ paripāṇam arātiham //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a durhārdaḥ samghoram cakṣuḥ pāpakṛtvānam āgamam /

Halfverse: c tāms tvam sahasracakṣo pratībodhena nāśaya paripāṇo ‘si jaṅgiḍaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a pari mā divaḥ pari mā pṛthivyāḥ pary antarikṣāt pari mā vīrudbhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c pari mā bhūtāt pari mota bhavyād diśodiśo jaṅgiḍaḥ pātv asmān //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ya ṛṣṇavo devakṛtā ya uto vavṛte ‘nyaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvām stān viśvabheṣajo ‘rasām jaṅgiḍas karat //5//

Hymn: 36

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śatavāro anīnaśad yakṣmān rakṣāmsi tejasā /

Halfverse: c ārohan varcasā saha maṇir durṇāmacātanaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śṛṅgābhyām rakṣo nudate mūlena yātudhānyaḥ /

Halfverse: c madhyena yakṣmam bādhate nainam pāpmāti tatrati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye yakṣmāso arbhakā mahānto ye ca śabdinaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvām durṇāmahā maṇiḥ śatavāro anīnaśat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śatam vīrān ajanayac chatam yakṣmān apāvapat /

Halfverse: c durṇāmnaḥ sarvān hatvāva rakṣāmsi dhūnute //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a hiraṇyaśṛṅga ṛṣabhaḥ śātavāro ayam maṇiḥ /

Halfverse: c durṇāmnaḥ sarvāms tṛdhvāva rakṣāmsy akramīt //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śatam aham durṇāmnīnām gandharvāpsarasām śatam /

Halfverse: c śatam śaśvanvatīnām śatavāreṇa vāraye //6//

Hymn: 37

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam varco agninā dattam āgan bhargo yaśaḥ saha ojo vayo balam /

Halfverse: c trayastrimśad yāni ca vīryāṇi tāny agniḥ pra dadātu me //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a varca ā dhehi me tanvā3m saha ojo vayo balam /

Halfverse: c indriyāya tvā karmaṇe vīryāya prati gṛhṇāmi śataśāradāya //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ūrje tvā balāya tvaujase sahase tvā /

Halfverse: c abhibhūyāya tvā rāṣṭrabhṛtyāya pary ūhāmi śataśāradāya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ṛtubhyaṣ ṭvārtavebhyo mādbhyaḥ samvatsarebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c dhātre vidhātre samṛdhe bhūtasya pataye yaje //4//

Hymn: 38

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a na tam yakṣmā arundhate nainam śapatho aśnute /

Halfverse: c yam bheṣajasya gulguloḥ surabhir gandho aśnute //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a viṣvañcas tasmād yakṣmā mṛgā aśvā iverate /

Halfverse: c yad gulgulu saindhavam yad vāpy asi samudriyam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ubhayor agrabham nāmāsmā ariṣṭatātaye //3//

Hymn: 39

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aitu devas trāyamāṇaḥ kuṣṭho himavatas pari /

Halfverse: c takmānam sarvam nāśaya sarvāś ca yātudhānyah //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a trīṇi te kuṣṭha nāmāni nadyamāro nadyāriṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c nadyāyam puruso riṣat /

Halfverse: e yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyamprātar atho divā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a jīvalā nāma te mātā jīvanto nāma te pitā /

Halfverse: c nadyāyam puruṣo riṣat /

Halfverse: e yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyamprātar atho divā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a uttamo asy oṣadhīnām anaḍvān jagatām iva vyāghraḥ śvapadām iva /

Halfverse: c nadyāyam puruṣo riṣat /

Halfverse: e yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyamprātar atho divā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a triḥ śāmbubhyo aṅgirebhyas trir ādityebhyas pari /

Halfverse: c trir jato viśvadevebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: e sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākam somena tiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: g takmānam sarvam nāśaya sarvāś ca yātudhānyaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a aśvattho devasadanas tṛtīyasyām ito divi /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇam tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata /

Halfverse: e sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākam somena tiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: g takmānam sarvam nāśaya sarvāś ca yātudhānyaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a hiraṇyayī naur acarad dhiraṇyabandhanā divi /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇam tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata /

Halfverse: e sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajah sākam somena tiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: g takmānam sarvam nāśaya sarvāś ca yātudhānyaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yatra nāvaprabhramśanam yatra himavataḥ śiraḥ /

Halfverse: c tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇam tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata /

Halfverse: e sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākam somena tiṣṭhati /

Halfverse: g takmānam sarvam nāśaya sarvāś ca yātudhānyah //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yam tvā veda pūrva ikṣvāko yam vā tvā kuṣṭha kāmyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yam vā vaso yam ātsyas tenāsi viśvabheṣajaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a śīrṣaśokam tṛtīyakam sadamdir yaś ca hāyanaḥ /

Halfverse: c takmānam viśvadhāvīryādharāñcam parā suva //10//

Hymn: 40

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yan me chidram manaso yac ca vācaḥ sarasvatī manyumantam jagāma /

Halfverse: c viśvais tad devaiḥ saha samvidānaḥ sam dadhātu bṛhaspatih //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mā na āpo medhām mā brahma pra mathiṣṭana /

Halfverse: c suṣyadā yūyam syandadhvam upahūto ‘ham sumedhā varcasvī //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mā no medhām mā no dīkṣām mā no himsiṣṭam yat tapaḥ /

Halfverse: c śivā naḥ śam santv āyuṣe śivā bhavantu mātaraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yā naḥ pīparad aśvinā jyotiṣmatī tamas tiraḥ /

Halfverse: c tām asme rāsatām iṣam //4//

Hymn: 41

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhadram ichanta ṛṣayaḥ svarvidas tapo dīkṣām upaniṣedur agre /

Halfverse: c tato rāṣṭram balam ojaś ca jātam tad asmai devā upasamnamantu //1//

Hymn: 42

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a brahma hotā brahma yajñā brahmaṇā svaravo mitāḥ /

Halfverse: c adhvaryur brahmaṇo jāto brahmaṇo ‘ntarhitam haviḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a brahma sruco ghṛtavatīr brahmaṇā vedir uddhitā /

Halfverse: c brahma yajñasya tattvam ca ṛtvijo ye haviṣkṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: e śamitāya svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a amhomuce pra bhare manīṣām ā sutrāvṇe sumatim āvṛṇānaḥ /

Halfverse: c imam indra prati havyam gṛbhāya satyāḥ santu yajamānasya kāmāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a amhomucam vrṣabham yajñiyānām virājantam prathamam adhvarāṇam /

Halfverse: c apām napātam aśvinā huve dhiya indriyeṇa ta indriyam dattam ojaḥ //4//

Hymn: 43

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c agnir mā tatra nayatv agnir medhā dadhātu me /

Halfverse: e agnaye svāhā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c vāyur mā tatra nayatu vāyuḥ praṇān dadhātu me vāyave svāhā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c sūryo mā tatra nayatu cakṣuḥ sūryo dadhātu me /

Halfverse: e sūryāya svāhā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c candro mā tatra nayatu manaś candro dadhātu me /

Halfverse: e candrāya svāhā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c somo mā tatra nayatu payaḥ somo dadhātu me /

Halfverse: e somāya svāhā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c indro mā tatra nayatu balam indro dadhātu me /

Halfverse: e indrāya svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c āpo mā tatra nayatv amṛtam mopa tiṣṭhatu /

Halfverse: e adbhyaḥ svāhā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha /

Halfverse: c brahmā mā tatra nayatu brahmā brahma dadhātu me /

Halfverse: e brahmaṇe svāhā //8//

Hymn: 44

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āyuṣo ‘si prataraṇam vipram bheṣajam ucyase /

Halfverse: c tad āñjana tvam śamtāte śam āpo abhayam kṛtam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo harimā jāyānyo ‘ṅgabhedo viṣalpakaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvam te yakṣmam aṅgebhyo bahir nir hantv āñjanam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āñjanam pṛthivyām jātam bhadram puruṣajīvanam /

Halfverse: c kṛṇotv apramāyukam rathajūtim anāgasam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prāṇa prāṇam trāyasvāso asave mṛḍa /

Halfverse: c nirṛte nirṛtyā naḥ pāśebhyo muñca //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sindhor garbho ‘si vidyutam puṣpam /

Halfverse: c vātaḥ prāṇaḥ sūryaś cakṣur divas payaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a devāñjana traikakuda pari mā pāhi viśvataḥ /

Halfverse: c na tvā taranty oṣadhayo bāhyāḥ parvatīyā uta //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a vīdam madhyam avāsṛpad rakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ /

Halfverse: c amīvāḥ sarvāś cātayan nāśayad abhibhā itaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bahv ṅ3dam rājan varuṇānṛtam āha pūruṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmāt sahasravīrya muñca naḥ pary amhasaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yad āpo aghnyā iti varuṇeti yad ūcima /

Halfverse: c tasmāt sahasravīrya muñca naḥ pary amhasaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a mitraś ca tvā varuṇaś cānupreyatur āñjana /

Halfverse: c tau tvānugatya dūram bhogāya punar ohatuḥ //10//

Hymn: 45

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ṛṇād ṛṇam iva sam naya kṛtyām kṛtyākṛto gṛham /

Halfverse: c cakṣurmantrasya durhārdaḥ pṛṣṭīr api śṛṇāñjana //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad asmāsu duṣvapnyam yad goṣu yac ca no gṛhe /

Halfverse: c anāmagas tam ca durhārdaḥ priyaḥ prati muñcatām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apām ūrja ojaso vāvṛdhānam agner jātam adhi jātavedasaḥ /

Halfverse: c caturvīram parvatīyam yad āñjanam diśaḥ pradiśaḥ karad ic chivās te //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a caturvīram badhyata āñjanam te sarvā diśo abhayās te bhavantu /

Halfverse: c dhruvas tiṣṭhāsi saviteva cārya imā viśo abhi harantu te balim //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ākṣvaikam maṇim ekam krṇuṣva snāhy ekenā pibaikam eṣām /

Halfverse: c caturvīram nairṛtebhyaś caturbhyo grāhyā bandhebhyaḥ pari pātv asmān //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a agnir māgnināvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase /

Halfverse: c tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a indro mendriyeṇāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase /

Halfverse: c tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a somo mā saumyenāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase /

Halfverse: c tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a bhago ma bhagenāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase /

Halfverse: c tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a maruto mā gaṇair avantu prāṇāyāpānāyuṣe varcasa ojase tejase /

Halfverse: c svastaye subhūtaye svāhā //10//

Hymn: 46

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a prajāpatiṣ ṭvā badhnāt prathamam astṛtam vīryāya kam /

Halfverse: c tat te badhnāmy āyuṣe varcasa ojase ca balāya cāstṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ūrdhvas tiṣṭhatu rakṣann apramādam astṛtemam mā tvā dabhan paṇayo yātudhānāḥ /

Halfverse: c indra iva dasyūn ava dhūnuṣva pṛtanyataḥ sarvām chatrūn vi ṣahasvāstṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śatam ca na praharanto nighnanto na tastire /

Halfverse: c tasminn indraḥ pary adatta cakṣuḥ prāṇam atho balam astṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indrasya tvā varmaṇā pari dhāpayāmo yo devānām adhirājo babhūva /

Halfverse: c punas tvā devāḥ pra ṇayantu sarve ‘stṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a asmin maṇāv ekaśatam vīryāṇi sahasram prāṇā asminn astṛte /

Halfverse: c vyāghraḥ śatrūn abhi tiṣṭha sarvān yas tvā pṛtanyād adharaḥ so astv astṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ghṛtād ullupto madhumān payasvānt sahasraprāṇaḥ śatayonir vayodhāḥ /

Halfverse: c śambhūś ca mayobhūś corjasvāmś ca payasvāmś cāstṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yathā tvam uttaro ‘so asapatnaḥ sapatnahā /

Halfverse: c sajātānām asad vaśī tathā tvā savitā karad astṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu //7//

Hymn: 47

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā rātri pārthivam rajaḥ pitur aprāyi dhāmabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c divaḥ sadāmsi bṛhatī vi tiṣṭhasa ā tveṣam vartate tamaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na yasyāḥ pāram dadṛśe na yoyuvad viśvam asyām ni viśate yad ejāti /

Halfverse: c ariṣṭāsas ta urvi tamasvati rātri pāram aśīmahi bhadre pāram aśīmahi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye te rātri nṛcakṣaso draṣṭāro navatīr nava /

Halfverse: c aśītiḥ santy aṣṭā uto te sapta saptatiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ṣaṣṭiś ca ṣaṭ ca revati pañcāśat pañca sumnayi /

Halfverse: c catvāraś catvārimśac ca trayas trimśac ca vājini //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dvau ca te vimśatiś ca te rātry ekādaśāvamāḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhir no adya pāyubhir nu pāhi duhitar divaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a rakṣā mākir no adhaśamsa īśata mā no duḥśamsa īśata /

Halfverse: c mā no adya gavām steno māvīnām vṛka īśata //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a māśvānām bhadre taskaro mā nṛṇām yātudhānyaḥ /

Halfverse: c paramebhiḥ pathibhiḥ steno dhāvatu taskaraḥ /

Halfverse: e pareṇa datvatī rajjuḥ pareṇāghayur arṣatu //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a adha rātri tṛṣṭadhūmam aśīrṣāṇam ahim kṛṇu /

Halfverse: c hanū vṛkasya jambhayā stenam drupade jahi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tvayi rātri vasāmasi svapiṣyāmasi jāgṛhi /

Halfverse: c gobhyo naḥ śarma yachāśvebhyaḥ puruṣebhyaḥ //9//

Hymn: 48

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a atho yāni ca yasmā ha yāni cāntaḥ parīṇahi /

Halfverse: c tāni te pari dadmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a rātri mātar uṣase naḥ pari dehi /

Halfverse: c uṣo no ahne pari dadātv ahas tubhyam vibhāvari //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yat kim cedam patayati yat kim cedam sarīsṛpam /

Halfverse: c yat kim ca parvatāyāsatvam tasmāt tvam rātri pāhi naḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sā paścāt pāhi sā puraḥ sottarād adharād uta /

Halfverse: c gopāya no vibhāvari stotāras ta iha smasi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye rātrim anutiṣṭhanti ye ca bhūteṣu jāgrati /

Halfverse: c paśūn ye sarvān rakṣanti te na ātmasu jāgrati te naḥ paśuṣu jāgrati //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a veda vai rātri te nāma ghṛtācī nāma vā asi /

Halfverse: c tām tvām bharadvājo veda sā no vitte ‘dhi jāgrati //6//

Hymn: 49

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a iṣirā yoṣā yuvatir damūnā rātrī devasya savitur bhagasya /

Halfverse: c aśvakṣabhā suhavā sambhṛtaśrīr ā paprau dyāvāpṛthivī mahitvā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ati viśvāny aruhad gambhiro varṣiṣṭham aruhanta śraviṣṭhāḥ /

Halfverse: c uśatī rātry anu sā bhadrābhi tiṣṭhate mitra iva svadhābhiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a varye vande subhage sujāta ājagan rātri sumanā iha syām /

Halfverse: c asmāms trāyasva naryāṇi jatā atho yāni gavyāni puṣṭhyā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a simhasya rātry uśatī pīmṣasya vyāghrasya dvīpino varca ā dade /

Halfverse: c aśvasya bradhnam puruṣasya māyum puru rūpāṇi kṛṇuṣe vibhātī //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śivām rātrim anusūryam ca himasya mātā suhavā no astu /

Halfverse: c asya stomasya subhage ni bodha yena tvā vande viśvāsu dikṣu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a stomasya no vibhāvari rātri rājeva joṣase /

Halfverse: c asāma sarvavīrā bhavāma sarvavedaso vyuchantīr anūṣasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śamyā ha nāma dadhiṣe mama dipsanti ye dhanā /

Halfverse: c rātrīhi tān asutapā ya steno na vidyate yat punar na vidyate //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bhadrāsi rātri camaso na viṣṭo viṣvaṅ gorūpam yuvatir bibharṣi /

Halfverse: c cakṣuṣmatī me uśatī vapūmṣi prati tvam divyā na kṣām amukthāḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yo adya stena āyaty aghāyur martyo ripuḥ /

Halfverse: c rātrī tasya pratītya pra grīvāḥ pra śiro hanat //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a pra pādau na yathāyati pra hastau na yathāśiṣat /

Halfverse: c yo malimlur upāyati sa sampiṣṭo apāyati /

Halfverse: e apāyati svapāyati śuṣke sthāṇāv apāyati //10//

Hymn: 50

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a adha rātri tṛṣṭadhūmam aśīrṣāṇam ahim kṛṇu /

Halfverse: c akṣau vṛkasya nir jahyās tena tam drupade jahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ye te rātry anaḍvāhas tīkṣṇaśṛṅgāḥ svāśavaḥ /

Halfverse: c tebhir no adya pārayāti durgāṇi viśvahā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a rātrimrātrim ariṣyantas tarema tanvā vayam /

Halfverse: c gambhīram aplavā iva na tareyur arātayaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yathā śāmyākaḥ prapatann apavān nānuvidyate /

Halfverse: c evā rātri pra pātaya yo asmāṁ abhyaghāyati //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a apa stenam vāsayo goajam uta taskaram /

Halfverse: c atho yo arvataḥ śiro ‘bhidhāya ninīṣati //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yad adya rātri subhage vibhajanty ayo vasu /

Halfverse: c yad etad asmān bhojaya yathed anyān upāyasi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a uṣase naḥ pari dehi sarvān rātry anāgasaḥ /

Halfverse: c uṣā no ahne ā bhajād ahas tubhyam vibhāvari //7//

Hymn: 51

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayuto ‘ham ayuto ma ātmāyutam me cakṣur ayutam me śrotram /

Halfverse: c ayuto me prāṇo ‘yuto me ‘pāno ‘yuto me vyāno ‘yuto ‘ham sarvaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a devasya tvā savituḥ prasave ‘śvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām prasūta ā rabhe //2//

Hymn: 52

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kāmas tad agre sam avartata manaso retaḥ prathamam yad āsīt /

Halfverse: c sa kāma kāmena bṛhatā sayonī rāyas poṣam yajamānāya dhehi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvam kāma sahasāsi pratiṣṭhito vibhur vibhāvā sakha ā sakhīyate /

Halfverse: c tvam ugraḥ pṛtanāsu sasahiḥ saha ojo yajamānāya dhehi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dūrāc cakamānāya pratipāṇāyākṣaye /

Halfverse: c āsmā aśṛṇvann āśāḥ kāmenājanayant svaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kāmena mā kāma āgan hṛdayād dhṛdayam pari /

Halfverse: c yad amīṣām ado manas tad aitūpa mām iha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yat kāma kāmayamānā idam kṛṇmasi te haviḥ /

Halfverse: c tan naḥ sarvam sam ṛdhyatām athaitasya haviṣo vīhi svāhā //5//

Hymn: 53

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kālo aśvo vahati saptaraśmiḥ sahasrākṣo ajaro bhūriretāḥ /

Halfverse: c tam ā rohanti kavayo vipaścitas tasya cakrā bhuvanāni viśvā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sapta cakrān vahati kāla eṣa saptāsya nābhīr amṛtam nv akṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa imā viśvā bhuvanāny añjat kālaḥ sa īyate prathamo nu devaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pūrṇaḥ kumbho ‘dhi kāla āhitas tam vai paśyāmo bahudhā nu santam /

Halfverse: c sa imā viśvā bhuvanāni pratyaṅ kālam tam āhuḥ parame vyoman //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sa eva sam bhuvanāny ābharat sa eva sam bhuvanāni pary ait /

Halfverse: c pitā sann abhavat putra eṣām tasmād vai nānyat param asti tejaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kālo ‘mūm divam ajanayat kāla imāḥ pṛthivīr uta /

Halfverse: c kāle ha bhūtam bhavyam ceṣitam ha vi tiṣṭhate //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kālo bhūtim asṛjata kāle tapati sūryaḥ /

Halfverse: c kāle ha viśvā bhūtāni kāle cakṣur vi paśyati //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a kāle manaḥ kāle prāṇaḥ kāle nāma samāhitam /

Halfverse: c kālena sarvā nandanty āgatena prajā imāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a kāle tapaḥ kāle jyeṣṭham kāle brahma samāhitam /

Halfverse: c kālo ha sarvasyeśvaro yaḥ pitāsīt prajāpateḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a teneṣitam tena jātam tad u tasmin pratiṣṭhitam /

Halfverse: c kālo ha brahma bhūtvā bibharti parameṣṭhinam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a kālah prajā asṛjata kālo agre prajāpatim /

Halfverse: c svayambhūḥ kaśyapaḥ kālāt tapaḥ kālād ajāyata //10//

Hymn: 54

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kālād āpaḥ sam abhavan kālād brahma tapo diśaḥ /

Halfverse: c kālenod eti sūryaḥ kāle ni viśate punaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kālena vātaḥ pavate kālena pṛthivī mahī /

Halfverse: c dyaur mahī kāla āhitā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kālo ha bhūtam bhavyam ca putro ajanayat purā /

Halfverse: c kālād ṛcaḥ sam abhavan yajuḥ kālād ajāyata //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kālo yajñam sam airayad devebhyo bhāgam akṣitam /

Halfverse: c kāle gandharvāpsarasaḥ kāle lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kāle ‘yam aṅgirā devo ‘tharvā cādhi tiṣṭhataḥ /

Halfverse: c imam ca lokam paramam ca lokam puṇyāmś ca lokān vidhṛtīś ca puṇyāḥ /

Halfverse: e sarvāml lokān abhijitya brahmaṇā kālaḥ sa īyate paramo nu devaḥ //5//

Hymn: 55

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a rātrimrātrim aprayātam bharanto ‘śvāyeva tiṣṭhate ghāsam asmai /

Halfverse: c rāyas poṣeṇa sam iṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yā te vasor vāta iṣuḥ sā ta eṣā tayā no mṛḍa /

Halfverse: c rāyas poṣeṇa sam iṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sāyamsāyam gṛhapatir no agniḥ prātaḥprātaḥ saumanasasya dātā /

Halfverse: c vasorvasor vasudāna edhi vayam tvendhānās tanvam puṣema //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a prātaḥprātar gṛhapatir no agniḥ sāyamsāyam saumanasasya dātā /

Halfverse: c vasorvasor vasudāna edhīndhānās tvā śatamhimā ṛdhema //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a apaścā dagdhānnasya bhūyāsam /

Halfverse: c annādāyānnapataye rudrāya namo agnaye /

Halfverse: e sabhyaḥ sabhām me pāhi ye ca sabhyāḥ sabhāsadaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tvām indrā puruhūta viśvam āyur vy aśnavan /

Halfverse: c aharahar balim it te haranto ‘śvāyeva tiṣṭhate ghāsam agne //6//

Hymn: 56

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yamasya lokād adhy ā babhūvitha pramadā martyān pra yunakṣi dhīraḥ /

Halfverse: c ekākinā saratham yāsi vidvānt svapnam mimāno asurasya yonau //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a bandhas tvāgre viśvacayā apaśyat purā rātryā janitor eke ahni /

Halfverse: c tataḥ svapnedam adhy ā babhūvitha bhiṣagbhyo rūpam apagūhamānaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bṛhadgāvāsurebhyo ‘dhi devān upāvartata mahimānam ichan /

Halfverse: c tasmai svapnāya dadhur ādhipatyam trayastrimśāsaḥ svar ānaśānāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a naitām viduḥ pitaro nota devā yeṣām jalpiś caraty antaredam /

Halfverse: c trite svapnam adadhur āptye nara ādityāso varuṇenānuśiṣṭāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasya krūram abhajanta duṣkṛto ‘svapnena sukṛtaḥ puṇyam āyuḥ /

Halfverse: c svar madasi parameṇa bandhunā tapyamānasya manaso ‘dhi jajñiṣe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a vidma te sarvāḥ parijāḥ purastād vidma svapna yo adhipā ihā te /

Halfverse: c yaśaśvino no yaśaseha pāhy ārād dviṣebhir apa yāhi dūram //6//

Hymn: 57

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yathā kalām yathā śapham yathā rṇam samnayanti /

Halfverse: c evā duṣvapnyam sarvam apriye sam nayāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam rājāno aguḥ sam ṛṇāmy aguḥ sam kuṣṭhā aguḥ sam kalā aguḥ /

Halfverse: c sam asmāsu yad duṣvapnyam nir dviṣate duṣvapnyam suvāma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a devānām patnīnām garbha yamasya kara yo bhadraḥ svapna /

Halfverse: c sa mama yaḥ pāpas tad dviṣate pra hiṇmaḥ /

Halfverse: e mā tṛṣṭānām asi kṛṣṇaśakuner mukham //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tam tvā svapna tathā sam vidma sa tvam svapnāśva iva kāyam aśva iva nīnāham /

Halfverse: c anāsmākam devapīyum piyārum vapa yad asmāsu duṣvapnyam yad goṣu yac ca no gṛhe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a anāsmākas tad devapīyuḥ piyārur niṣkam iva prati muñcatām /

Halfverse: c navāratnīn apamayā asmākam tataḥ pari /

Halfverse: e duṣvapnyam sarvam dviṣate nir dayāmasi //5//

Hymn: 58

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ghṛtasya jūtiḥ samanā sadevā samvatsaram haviṣā vardhayantī /

Halfverse: c śrotram cakṣuḥ prāṇo ‘chinno no astv achinnā vayam āyuṣo varcasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a upāsmān prāṇo hvayatām upa prāṇam havāmahe /

Halfverse: c varco jagrāha pṛthivy a1ntarikṣam varcaḥ somo bṛhaspatir vidhattā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a varcaso dyāvāpṛthivī samgrahaṇī babhūvathur varco gṛhītvā pṛthivīm anu sam carema /

Halfverse: c yaśasam gāvo gopatim upa tiṣṭhanty āyatīr yaśo gṛhītvā pṛthivīm anu sam carema //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vrajam kṛṇudhvam sa hi vo nṛpāṇo varmā sīvyadhvam bahulā pṛthūni /

Halfverse: c puraḥ kṛṇudhvam āyasīr adhṛṣṭā mā vaḥ susroc camaso dṛmhata tam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yajñasya cakṣuḥ prabhṛtir mukham ca vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi /

Halfverse: c imam yajñam vitatam viśvakarmaṇā devā yantu sumanasyamānāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ye devānām ṛtvijo ye ca yajñiyā yebhyo havyam kriyate bhāgadheyam /

Halfverse: c imam yajñam saha patnībhir etya yāvanto devās taviṣā mādayantām //6//

Hymn: 59

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvam agne vratapā asi deva ā martyeṣv ā /

Halfverse: c tvam yajñeṣv īḍyaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad vo vayam pramināma vratāni viduṣām devā aviduṣṭarāsaḥ /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭad viśvād ā pṛṇātu vidvānt somasya yo brāhmaṇāṁ āviveśa //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ā devānām api panthām aganma yac chaknavāma tad anupravodum /

Halfverse: c agnir vidvānt sa yajāt sa id dhotā so ‘dhvarānt sa ṛtūn kalpayāti //3//

Hymn: 60

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vāṅ ma āsan nasoḥ prāṇaś cakṣur akṣṇoḥ śrotram karṇayoḥ /

Halfverse: c apalitāḥ keśā aśoṇā dantā bahu bāhvor balam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ūrvor ojo jaṅghayor javaḥ pādayoḥ /

Halfverse: c pratiṣṭhā ariṣṭāni me sarvātmānibhṛṣṭaḥ //2//

Hymn: 61

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tanūs tanvā me sahe dataḥ sarvam āyur aśīya /

Halfverse: c syonam me sīda puruḥ pṛṇasva pavamānaḥ svarge //1//

Hymn: 62

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a priyam mā kṛṇu deveṣu priyam rājasu mā kṛṇu /

Halfverse: c priyam sarvasya paśyata uta śūdra utārye //1//

Hymn: 63

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ut tiṣṭha brahmaṇas pate devān yajñena bodhaya /

Halfverse: c āyuḥ prāṇam prajām paśūn kīrtim yajamānam ca vardhaya //1//

Hymn: 64

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agne samidham āhārṣam bṛhate jātavedase /

Halfverse: c sa me śraddhām ca medhām ca jātavedāḥ pra yachatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a idhmena tvā jātavedaḥ samidhā vardhayāmasi /

Halfverse: c tathā tvam asmān vardhaya prajayā ca dhanena ca //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad agne yāni kāni cid ā te dārūṇi dadhmasi /

Halfverse: c sarvam tad astu me śivam taj juṣasva yaviṣṭhya //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a etās te agne samidhas tvam iddhaḥ samid bhava /

Halfverse: c āyur asmāsu dhehy amṛtatvam ācāryāya //4//

Hymn: 65

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a hariḥ suparṇo divam āruho ‘rciṣā ye tvā dipsanti divam utpatantam /

Halfverse: c ava tām jahi harasā jātavedo ‘bibhyad ugro ‘rciṣā divam ā roha sūrya //1//

Hymn: 66

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayojālā asurā māyino ‘yasmayaiḥ pāśair aṅkino ye caranti /

Halfverse: c tāms te randhayāmi harasā jātavedaḥ sahasraṛṣṭiḥ sapatnān pramṛṇan pāhi vajraḥ //1//

Hymn: 67

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a paśyema śaradaḥ śatam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a jīvema śaradaḥ śatam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a budhyema śaradaḥ śatam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a rohema śaradaḥ śatam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pūṣema śaradaḥ śatam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a bhavema śaradaḥ śatam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a bhūṣema śaradaḥ śatam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a bhūyasīḥ śaradaḥ śatam //8//

Hymn: 68

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a avyasaś ca vyacasaś ca bilam vi ṣyāmi māyayā /

Halfverse: c tābhyām uddhṛtya vedam atha karmāṇi kṛṇmahe //1//

Hymn: 69

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a jīvā stha jīvyāsam sarvam āyur jīvyāsam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a upajīvā sthopa jīvyāsam sarvam āyur jīvyāsam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a samjīvā stha sam jīvyāsam sarvam āyur jīvyāsam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a jīvalā stha jīvyāsam sarvam āyur jīvyāsam //4//

Hymn: 70

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indra jīva sūrya jīva devā jīvā jīvyāsam aham /

Halfverse: c sarvam āyur jīvyāsam //1//

Hymn: 71

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a stutā mayā varadā vedamātā pra codayantām pāvamānī dvijānām /

Halfverse: c āyuḥ prāṇam prajām paśum kīrtim draviṇam brahmavarcasam /

Halfverse: e mahyam dattvā vrajata brahmalokam //1//

Hymn: 72

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yasmāt kośād udabharāma vedam tasminn antar ava dadhma enam /

Halfverse: c kṛtam iṣṭam brahmaṇo vīryṝṇa tena mā devās tapasāvateha //1//

## Book: 20

20

Hymn: 1

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indra tvā vṛṣabham vayam sute some havāmahe /

Halfverse: c sa pāhi madhvo andhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a maruto yasya hi kṣaye pāthā divo vimahasaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa sugopātamo janaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ukṣānnāya vaśānnāya somapṛṣṭhāya vedhase /

Halfverse: c stomair vidhemāgnaye //3//

Hymn: 2

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a marutaḥ potrāt suṣṭubhaḥ svarkād ṛtunā somam pibantu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agnir āgnīdhrāt suṣṭubhaḥ svarkād ṛtunā somam pibatu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indro brahmā brāhmaṇāt suṣṭubhaḥ svarkād ṛtunā somam pibatu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a devo draviṇodāḥ potrāt suṣṭubhaḥ svarkād ṛtunā somam pibatu //4//

Hymn: 3

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā yāhi suṣumā hi ta indra somam pibā imam /

Halfverse: c edam barhiḥ sado mama //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā tvā brahmayujā harī vahatām indra keśinā /

Halfverse: c upa brahmāṇi naḥ śṛṇu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a brahmāṇas tvā vayam yujā somapām indra sominaḥ /

Halfverse: c sutāvanto havāmahe //3//

Hymn: 4

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā no yāhi sutāvato ‘smākam suṣṭutīr upa /

Halfverse: c pibā su śiprinn andhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā te siñcāmi kukṣyor anu gātrā vi dhāvatu /

Halfverse: c gṛbhāya jihvayā madhu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a svāduṣ ṭe astu samsude madhumān tanvṝ3 tava /

Halfverse: c somaḥ śam astu te hṛde //3//

Hymn: 5

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam u tvā vicarṣaṇe janīr ivābhi samvṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c pra soma indra sarpatu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tuvigrīvo vapodaraḥ subāhur andhaso sade /

Halfverse: c indro vṛtrāṇi jighnate //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indra prehi puras tvam viśvasyeśāna ojasā /

Halfverse: c vṛtrāṇi vṛtraham jahi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a dīrghas te astv aṅkuśo yenā vasu prayachasi /

Halfverse: c yajamānāya sunvate //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ayam ta indra somo nipūto adhi barhiṣi /

Halfverse: c ehīm asya dravā piba //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a śācigo śācipūjanāyam raṇāya te sutaḥ /

Halfverse: c ākhaṇḍala pra hūyase //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yas te śṛṅgavṛṣo napāt praṇapāt kuṇḍapāyyaḥ /

Halfverse: c ny asmin dadhra ā manaḥ //7//

Hymn: 6

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indra tvā vṛṣabham vayam sute some havāmahe /

Halfverse: c sa pāhi madhvo andhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indra kratuvidam sutam somam harya puruṣṭuta /

Halfverse: c pibā vṛṣasva tātṛpim //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indra pra ṇo dhitāvānam yajñam viśvebhir devebhir /

Halfverse: c tira stavāna viśpate //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indra somāḥ sutā ime tava pra yanti satpate /

Halfverse: c kṣayam candrāsa indavaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dadhiṣvā jaṭhare sutam somam indra vareṇyam /

Halfverse: c tava dyukṣāsa indavaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a girvaṇaḥ pāhi naḥ sutam madhor dhārābhir ajyase /

Halfverse: c indra tvādātam id yaśaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a abhi dyumnāni vanina indram sacante akṣitā /

Halfverse: c pītvī somasya vāvṛdhe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a arvāvato na ā gahi parāvataś ca vṛtrahan /

Halfverse: c imā juṣasva no giraḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yad antarā parāvatam arvāvatam ca hūyase /

Halfverse: c indreha tata ā gahi //9//

Hymn: 7

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud ghed abhi śrutāmagham vṛṣabham naryāpasam /

Halfverse: c astāram eṣi sūrya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nava yo navatim puro bibheda bāhvojasā /

Halfverse: c ahim ca vṛtrahāvadhīt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sa na indraḥ śivaḥ sakhāśvāvad gomad yavamat /

Halfverse: c urudhāreva dohate //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indra kratuvidam sutam somam harya puruṣṭuta /

Halfverse: c pibā vṛṣasva tātṛpim //4//

Hymn: 8

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a evā pāhi pratnathā mandatu tvā śrudhi brahma vāvṛdhasvota gīrbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c āviḥ sūryam kṛṇuhi pīpihīṣo jahi śatrūṁr abhi gā indra tṛndhi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a arvāṅ ehi somakāmam tvāhur ayam sutas tasya pibā madāya /

Halfverse: c uruvyacā jaṭhara ā vṛṣasva piteva naḥ śṛṇuhi hūyamānaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a āpūrṇo asya kalaśaḥ svāhā sekteva kośam siṣice pibadhyai /

Halfverse: c sam u priyā āvavṛtran madāya pradakṣiṇid abhi somāsa indram //3//

Hymn: 9

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tam vo dasmam ṛtīṣaham vasor mandānam andhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhi vatsam na svasareṣu dhenava indram gīrbhir navāmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dyukṣam sudānum taviṣībhir āvṛtam girim na purubhojasam /

Halfverse: c kṣumantam vājam śatinam sahasriṇam makṣū gomantam īmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tat tvā yāmi suvīryam tad brahma pūrvacittaye /

Halfverse: c yenā yatibhyo bhṛgave dhane hite yena praskaṇvam āvitha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yenā samudram asṛjo mahīr apas tad indra vṛṣṇi te śavaḥ /

Halfverse: c sadyaḥ so asya mahimā na samnaśe yam kṣoṇīr anucakrade //4//

Hymn: 10

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud u te madhumattamā gira stomāsa īrate /

Halfverse: c satrājito dhanasā akṣitotayo vājayanto rathā iva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kaṇvā iva bhṛgavaḥ sūryā iva viśvam id dhītam ānaśuḥ /

Halfverse: c indram stomebhir mahayanta āyavaḥ priyamedhāso asvaran //2//

Hymn: 11

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indraḥ pūrbhid ātirad dāsam arkair vidadvasur dayamāno vi śatrūn /

Halfverse: c brahmajūtas tanvā vāvṛdhāno bhūridātra āpṛṇad rodasī ubhe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a makhasya te taviṣasya pra jūtim iyarmi vācam amṛtāya bhūṣan /

Halfverse: c indra kṣitīnām asi mānuṣīṇām viśām daivīnām uta pūrvayāvā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indro vṛtram avṛṇoc chardhanītiḥ pra māyinām aminād varpaṇītiḥ /

Halfverse: c ahan vyamsam uśadhag vaneṣv āvir dhenā akṛṇod rāmyāṇām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indraḥ svarṣā janayann ahāni jigāyośigbhiḥ pṛtanā abhiṣṭiḥ /

Halfverse: c prārocayan manave ketum ahnām avindaj jyotir bṛhate raṇāya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indras tujo barhaṇā ā viveśa nṛvad dadhāno naryā purūṇi /

Halfverse: c acetayad dhiya imā jaritre premam varṇam atirac chukram āsām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a maho mahāni panayanty asyendrasya karma sukṛtā purūṇi /

Halfverse: c vṛjanena vṛjinānt sam pipeṣa māyābhir dasyūṁr abhibhūtyojāḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yudhendro mahnā varivaś cakāra devebhyaḥ satpatiś carṣaṇiprāḥ /

Halfverse: c vivasvataḥ sadane asya tāni viprā ukthebhiḥ kavayo gṛṇanti //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a satrāsāham vareṇyam sahodām sasavāmsam svar apaś ca devīḥ /

Halfverse: c sasāna yaḥ pṛthivīm dyām utemām indram madanty anu dhīraṇāsaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a sasānātyāṁ uta sūryam sasānendraḥ sasāna purubhojasam gām /

Halfverse: c hiraṇyayam uta bhogam sasāna hatvī dasyūn prāryam varṇam āvat //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a indra oṣadhīr asanod ahāni vanaspatīṁr asanod antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c bibheda balam nunude vivāco ‘thābhavad damitābhikratūnām //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a śunam huvema maghavānam indram asmin bhare nṛtamam vājasātau /

Halfverse: c śṛṇvantam ugram ūtaye samatsu ghnantam vṛtrāṇi samjitam dhanānām //11//

Hymn: 12

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud u brahmāṇy airata śravasyendram samarye mahayā vasiṣṭha /

Halfverse: c ā yo viśvāni śavasā tatānopaśrotā ma īvato vacāmsi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayāmi ghoṣa indra devajāmir irajyanta yac churudho vivāci /

Halfverse: c nahi svam āyuś cikite janeṣu tānīd amhāmsy ati parṣy asmān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yuje ratham gaveṣaṇam haribhyām upa brahmāṇi jujuṣāṇam asthuḥ /

Halfverse: c vi bādhiṣṭa sya rodasī mahitvendro vṛtrāṇy apratī jaghanvān //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a āpaś cit pipyu staryo3 na gāvo nakṣann ṛtam jaritāras ta indra /

Halfverse: c yāhi vāyur na niyuto no achā tvam hi dhībhir dayase vi vājān //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a te tvā madā indra mādayantu śuṣmiṇam tuvirādhasam jaritre /

Halfverse: c eko devatrā dayase hi martān asmin chūra savane mādayasva //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a eved indram vṛṣaṇam vajrabāhum vasiṣṭhāso abhy arcanty arkaiḥ /

Halfverse: c sa na stuto vīravad dhātu gomad yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ṛjīṣī vajrī vṛṣabhas turāṣāṭ chuṣmī rājā vṛtrahā somapāvā /

Halfverse: c yuktvā haribhyām upa yāsad arvāṅ mādhyamdine savane matsad indraḥ //7//

Hymn: 13

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indraś ca somam pibatam bṛhaspate ‘smin yajñe mandasānā vṛṣaṇvasū /

Halfverse: c ā vām viśantv indavaḥ svābhuvo ‘sme rayim sarvavīram ni yachatam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā vo vahantu saptayo raghuṣyado raghupatvānaḥ pra jigāta bāhubhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sīdatā barhir uru vaḥ sadas kṛtam mādayadhvam maruto madhvo andhasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a imam stomam arhate jātavedase ratham iva sam mahemā manīṣayā /

Halfverse: c bhadrā hi naḥ pramatir asya samsady agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayam tava //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a aibhir agne saratham yāhy arvāṅ nānāratham vā vibhavo hy aśvāḥ /

Halfverse: c patnīvatas trimśatam trīmś ca devān anuṣvadham ā vaha mādayasva //4//

Hymn: 14

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vayam u tvām apūrvya sthūram na kac cid bharanto ‘vasyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c vāje citram havāmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a upa tvā karmann ūtaye sa no yuvograś cakrāma yo dhṛṣat /

Halfverse: c tvām id dhy avitāram vavṛmahe sakhāya indra sānasim //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo na idamidam purā pra vasya ānināya tam u va stuṣe /

Halfverse: c sakhāya indram ūtaye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a haryaśvam satpatim carṣaṇīsaham sa hi ṣmā yo amandata /

Halfverse: c ā tu naḥ sa vayati gavyam aśvyam stotṛbhyo maghavā śatam //4//

Hymn: 15

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pra mamhiṣṭhāya bṛhate bṛhadraye satyaśuṣmāya tavase matim bhare /

Halfverse: c apām iva pravaṇe yasya durdharam rādho viśvāyu śavase apāvṛtam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a adha te viśvam anu hāsad iṣṭaya āpo nimneva savanā haviṣmataḥ /

Halfverse: c yat parvate na samaśīta haryata indrasya vajraḥ śnathitā hiraṇyayaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asmai bhīmāya namasā sam adhvara uṣo na śubhra ā bharā panīyase /

Halfverse: c yasya dhāma śravase nāmendriyam jyotir akāri harito nāyase //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ime ta indra te vayam puruṣṭuta ye tvārabhya carāmasi prabhūvaso /

Halfverse: c nahi tvad anyo girvaṇo giraḥ sadhat kṣoṇīr iva prati no harya tad vacaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a bhūri ta indra vīrya1m tava smasy asya stotur maghavan kāmam ā pṛṇa /

Halfverse: c anu te dyaur bṛhatī vīryam mama iyam ca te pṛthivī nema ojase //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tvam tam indra parvatam mahām urum vajreṇa vajrin parvaśaś cakartitha /

Halfverse: c avāsṛjo nivṛtāḥ sartavā apaḥ satrā viśvam dadhiṣe kevalam sahaḥ //6//

Hymn: 16

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a udapruto na vayo rakṣamāṇā vāvadato abhriyasyeva ghoṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c giribhrajo normayo madanto bṛhaspatim abhy arkā anāvan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam gobhir aṅgiraso nakṣamāṇo bhaga ived aryamaṇam nināya /

Halfverse: c jane mitro na dampatī anakti bṛhaspate vājayāśūṁr ivājau //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sādhvaryā atithinīr iṣirā spārhāḥ suvarṇā anavadyarūpāḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatiḥ parvatebhyo vitūryā nir gā ūpe yavam iva sthivibhyah //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a āpruṣāyan madhunā ṛtasya yonim avakṣipann arka ulkām iva dyoḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir uddharann aśmano gā bhūmyā udneva vi tvacam bibheda //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a apa jyotiṣā tamo antarikṣad udnaḥ śīpālam iva vāta ājat /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir anumṛśyā valasyābhram iva vāta ā cakra ā gāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yadā valasya pīyato jasum bhed bṛhaspatir agnitapobhir arkaiḥ /

Halfverse: c dadbhir na jihvā pariviṣṭam ādad āvir nidhīṁr akṛṇod usriyāṇām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir amata hi tyad āsām nāma svarīṇām sadane guhā yat /

Halfverse: c āṇḍeva bhitvā śakunasya garbham ud usriyāḥ parvatasya tmanājat //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a aśnāpinaddham madhu pary apaśyan matsyam na dīna udani kṣiyantam /

Halfverse: c niṣ ṭaj jabhāra camasam na vṛkṣād bṛhaspatir viraveṇā vikṛtya //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a soṣām avindat sa sva1ḥ so agnim so arkeṇa vi babādhe tamāmsi /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir govapuṣo valasya nir majjānam na parvaṇo jabhāra //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a himeva parṇā muṣitā vanāni bṛhaspatinākṛpayad valo gāḥ /

Halfverse: c anānukṛtyam apunaś cakāra yāt sūryāmāsā mitha uccarātaḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a abhi śyāvam na kṛśanebhir aśvam nakṣatrebhiḥ pitaro dyām apimśan /

Halfverse: c rātryām tamo adadhur jyotir ahan bṛhaspatir bhinad adrim vidad gāḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a idam akarma namo abhriyāya yaḥ pūrvīr anv ānonavīti /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatiḥ sa hi gobhiḥ so aśvaiḥ sa vīrebhiḥ sa nṛbhir no vayo dhāt //12//

Hymn: 17

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a achā ma indram matayaḥ svarvidaḥ sadhrīcīr viśvā uśatīr anūṣata /

Halfverse: c pari ṣvajante janayo yathā patim maryam na śundhyum maghavānam ūtaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na ghā tvadrig apa veti me manas tve it kāmam puruhūta śiśraya /

Halfverse: c rājeva dasma ni ṣado ‘dhi barhiṣy asmint su some ‘vapānam astu te //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a viṣūvṛd indro amuter uta kṣudhaḥ sa id rāyo maghavā vasva īśate /

Halfverse: c tasyed ime pravaṇe sapta sindhavo vayo vardhanti vṛṣabhasya śuṣmiṇaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vayo na vṛkṣam supalāśam āsadant somāsa indram mandinaś camūṣadaḥ /

Halfverse: c praiṣām anīkam śavasā davidyutad vidat sva1r manave jyotir āryam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kṛtam na śvaghnī vi cinoti devane samvargam yan maghavā sūryam jayat /

Halfverse: c na tat te anyo anu vīryam śakan na purāṇo maghavan nota nūtanaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a viśamviśam maghavā pary aśāyata janānām dhenā avacākaśad vṛṣā /

Halfverse: c yasyāha śakraḥ savaneṣu raṇyati sa tīvraiḥ somaiḥ sahate pṛtanyataḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a āpo na sindhum abhi yat samakṣarant somāsa indram kulyā iva hradam /

Halfverse: c vardhanti viprā maho asya sādane yavam na vṛṣṭir divyena dānunā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a vṛṣā na kruddhaḥ patayad rajaḥsv ā yo aryapatnīr akṛṇod imā apaḥ /

Halfverse: c sa sunvate maghavā jīradānave ‘vindaj jyotir manave haviṣmate //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a uj jāyatām paraśu jyotiṣā saha bhūyā ṛtasya sudughā purāṇavat /

Halfverse: c vi rocatām aruṣo bhānunā śuciḥ sva1r na śukram śuśucīta satpatiḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a gobhiṣ ṭaremāmatim durevām yavena kṣudham puruhūta viśvām /

Halfverse: c vayam rājabhiḥ prathamā dhanāny asmākena vṛjanenā jayema //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir naḥ pari pātu paścād utottarasmād adharād aghāyoḥ /

Halfverse: c indraḥ purastād uta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyaḥ varivaḥ kṛṇotu //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a bṛhaspate yuvam indraś ca vasvo divyasyeśāthe uta pārthivasya /

Halfverse: c dhattam rayim stuvate kīraye cid yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ //12//

Hymn: 18

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vayam u tvā taditarthā indra tvāyantaḥ sakhāyaḥ /

Halfverse: c kaṇvā ukthebhir jarante //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na ghem anyad ā papana vajrinn apaso naviṣṭau /

Halfverse: c taved u stomam ciketa //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ichanti devāḥ sunvantam na svapnāya spṛhayanti /

Halfverse: c yanti pramādam atandrāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vayam indra tvāyavo ‘bhi pra ṇonumo vṛṣan /

Halfverse: c viddhi tv asya no vaso //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mā no nide ca vaktave ‘ryo randhīr arāvne /

Halfverse: c tve api kratur mama //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tvam varmāsi saprathaḥ puroyodhaś ca vṛtrahan /

Halfverse: c tvayā prati bruve yujā //6//

Hymn: 19

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vārtrahatyāya śavase pṛtanāṣāhyāya ca /

Halfverse: c indra tvā vartayāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a arvācīnam su te mana uta cakṣuḥ śatakrato /

Halfverse: c indra kṛṇvantu vāghataḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nāmāni te śatakrato viśvābhir gīrbhir īmahe /

Halfverse: c indrābhimātiṣāhye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a puruṣṭutasya dhāmabhiḥ śatena mahayāmasi /

Halfverse: c indrasya carṣaṇīdhṛtaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indram vṛtrāya hantave puruhūtam upa bruve /

Halfverse: c bhareṣu vājasātaye //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a vājeṣu sāsahir bhava tvām īmahe śatakrato /

Halfverse: c indra vṛtrāya hantave //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a dyumneṣu pṛtanājye pṛtsutūrṣu śravaḥsu ca /

Halfverse: c indra sākṣvābhimātiṣu //7//

Hymn: 20

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śuṣmintamam na ūtaye dyumninam pāhi jāgṛvim /

Halfverse: c indra somam śatakrato //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indriyāṇi śatakrato yā te janeṣu pañcasu /

Halfverse: c indra tāni ta ā vṛṇe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a agann indra śravo bṛhad dyumnam dadhiṣva duṣṭaram /

Halfverse: c ut te śuṣmam tirāmasi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a arvāvato na ā gahy atho śakra parāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c u loko yas te adriva indreha tata ā gahi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indro aṅgam mahad bhayam abhi ṣad apa cucyavat /

Halfverse: c sa hi sthiro vicarṣaṇiḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indraś ca mṛlayāti no na naḥ paścād agham naśat /

Halfverse: c bhadram bhavāti naḥ puraḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a indra āśābhyas pari sarvābhyo abhayam karat /

Halfverse: c jetā śatrūn vicarṣaṇiḥ //7//

Hymn: 21

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ny ū ṣu vācam pra mahe bharāmahe gira indrāya sadane vivasvataḥ /

Halfverse: c nū cid dhi ratnam sasatām ivāvidan na duṣṭutir draviṇodeṣu śasyate //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a duro aśvasya dura indra gor asi duro yavasya vasuna inas patiḥ /

Halfverse: c śikṣānaraḥ pradivo akāmakarśanaḥ sakhā sakhibhyas tam idam gṛṇīmasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śacīva indra purukṛd dyumattama taved idam abhitaś cekite vasu /

Halfverse: c ataḥ samgṛbhyābhibhūta ā bhara mā tvāyato jarituḥ kāmam ūnayīḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ebhir dyubhir sumanā ebhir indubhir nirundhāno amatim gobhir aśvinā /

Halfverse: c indreṇa dasyum darayanta indubhir yutadveṣasaḥ sam iṣā rabhemahi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sam indra rāyā sam iṣā rabhemahi sam vājebhiḥ puruścandrair abhidyubhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sam devyā pramatyā vīraśuṣmayā goagrayāśvāvatyā rabhemahi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a te tvā madā amadan tāni vṛṣṇyā te somāso vṛtrahatyeṣu satpate /

Halfverse: c yat kārave daśa vṛtrāṇy aprati barhiṣmate ni sahasrāṇi barhayaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yudhā yudham upa ghed eṣi dhṛṣṇuyā purā puram sam idam hamsy ojasā /

Halfverse: c namyā yad indra sakhyā parāvati nibarhayo namucim nāma māyinam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tvam karañjam uta parṇayam vadhīs tejiṣṭhayātithigvasya vartanī /

Halfverse: c tvam śatā vaṅgṛdasyābhinat puro ‘nānudaḥ pariṣūtā ṛjiśvanā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tvam etām janarājño dvir daśābandhunā suśravasopajagmuṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c ṣaṣṭim sahasrā navatim nava śruto ni cakreṇa rathyā duṣpadāvṛṇak //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tvam āvitha suśravasam tavotibhis tava trāmabhir indra tūrvayāṇam /

Halfverse: c tva asmai kutsam atithigvam āyum mahe rājñe yūne arandhanāyaḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ya udṛcīndra devagopāḥ sakhāyas te śivatamā asāma /

Halfverse: c tvām stoṣāma tvayā suvīrā drāghīya āyuḥ prataram dadhānāḥ //11//

Hymn: 22

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi tvā vṛṣabhā sute sutam sṛjāmi pītaye /

Halfverse: c tṛmpā vy aśnuhī madam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mā tvā mūrā aviṣyavo mopahasvāna ā dabhan /

Halfverse: c mākīm brahmadviṣo vanaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a iha tvā goparīṇasā mahe mandantu rādhase /

Halfverse: c saro gauro yathā piba //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a abhi pra gopatim girendram arca yathā vide /

Halfverse: c sūnum satyasya satpatim //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā harayaḥ sasṛjrire ‘ruṣīr adhi barhiṣi /

Halfverse: c yatrābhi samnavāmahe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indrāya gāva āśiram duduhre vajriṇe madhu /

Halfverse: c yat sīm upahvare vidat //6//

Hymn: 23

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā tū na indra madryag ghuvānaḥ somapītaye /

Halfverse: c haribhyām yāhy adrivaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a satto hotā na ṛtviyas tistire barhir ānuṣak /

Halfverse: c ayujran prātar adrayaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a imā brahma brahmavāhaḥ kriyanta ā barhiḥ sīda /

Halfverse: c vīhi śūra purolāśam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a rārandhi savaneṣu ṇa eṣu stomeṣu vṛtrahan /

Halfverse: c uktheṣv indra girvaṇaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a matayaḥ somapām urum rihanti śavasas patim /

Halfverse: c indram vatsam na mātaraḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sa mandasvā hy andhaso rādhase tanvā mahe /

Halfverse: c na stotāram nide karaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a vayam indra tvāyavo haviṣmanto jarāmahe /

Halfverse: c uta tvam asmayur vaso //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a māre asmad vi mumuco haripriyārvāṅ yāhi /

Halfverse: c indra svadhāvo matsveha //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a arvāñcam tvā sukhe rathe vahatām indra keśinā /

Halfverse: c ghṛtasnū barhir āsade //9//

Hymn: 24

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a upa naḥ sutam ā gahi somam indra gavāśiram /

Halfverse: c haribhyām yas te asmayuḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tam indra madam ā gahi barhiṣṭhām grāvabhiḥ sutam /

Halfverse: c kuvin nv asya tṛpṇavaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indram itthā giro mamāchāgur iṣitā itaḥ /

Halfverse: c āvṛte somapītaye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indram somasya pītaye stomair iha havāmahe /

Halfverse: c ukthebhiḥ kuvid āgamat //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indra somāḥ sutā ime tān dadhiṣva śatakrato /

Halfverse: c jathare vājinīvaso //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a vidmā hi tvā dhanamjayam vājeṣu dadhṛṣam kave /

Halfverse: c adhā te sumnam īmahe //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a imam indra gavāśiram yavāśiram ca naḥ piba /

Halfverse: c āgatyā vṛṣabhiḥ sutam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tubhyed indra sva okyṝ3 somam codāmi pītaye /

Halfverse: c eṣa rārantu te hṛdi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tvām sutasya pītaye pratnam indra havāmahe /

Halfverse: c kuśikāso avasyavaḥ //9//

Hymn: 25

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aśvāvati prathamo goṣu gachati suprāvīr indra martyas tavotibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c tam it pṛṇakṣi vasunā bhavīyasā sindhum āpo yathābhito vicetasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a āpo na devīr upa yanti hotriyam avah paśyanti vitatam yathā rajaḥ /

Halfverse: c prācair devāsaḥ pra ṇayanti devayum brahmapriyam joṣayante varā iva //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a adhi dvayor adadhā ukthya1m vaco yatasrucā mithunā yā saparyataḥ /

Halfverse: c asamyatto vrate te kṣeti puṣyati bhadrā śaktir yajamānāya sunvate //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ād aṅgirāḥ prathamam dadhire vaya iddhāgnayaḥ śamyā ye sukṛtyayā /

Halfverse: c sarvam paṇeḥ sam avindanta bhojanam aśvāvantam gomantam ā paśum naraḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yajñair atharvā prathamaḥ pathas tate tataḥ sūryo vratapā vena ājani /

Halfverse: c ā gā ājad uśanā kāvyaḥ sacā yamasya jātam amṛtam yajāmahe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a barhir vā yat svapatyāya vṛjyate ‘rko vā ślokam āghoṣate divi /

Halfverse: c grāvā yatra vadati karur ukthya1s tasyed indro abhipitveṣu raṇyati //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a progrām pītim vṛṣṇa iyarmi satyām prayai sutasya haryaśva tubhyam /

Halfverse: c indra dhenābhir iha mādayasva dhībhir viśvābhiḥ śacyā gṛṇānaḥ //7//

Hymn: 26

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yogeyoge tavastaram vājevāje havāmahe /

Halfverse: c sakhāya indram ūtaye //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā ghā gamad yadi śravat sahasriṇībhir ūtibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c vājebhir upa no havam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a anu pratnasyaukaso huve tuvipratim naram /

Halfverse: c yam te pūrvam pitā huve //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yuñjanti bradhnam aruṣam carantam pari tasthuṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c rocante rocanā divi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yuñjanti asya kāmyā harī vipakṣasā rathe /

Halfverse: c śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛvāhasā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ketum kṛṇvann aketave peśo maryā apeśase /

Halfverse: c sam uṣadbhir ajāyathāḥ //6//

Hymn: 27

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad indrāham yathā tvam īśīya vasva eka it /

Halfverse: c stotā me goṣakhā syāt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śikṣeyam asmai ditseyam śacīpate manīṣiṇe /

Halfverse: c yad aham gopatiḥ syām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dhenuṣ ṭa indra sūnṛtā yajamānāya sunvate /

Halfverse: c gām aśvam pipyuṣī duhe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a na te vartāsti rādhasa indra devo na martyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yad ditsasi stuto magham //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yajña indram avardhayad yad bhūmim vy avartayat /

Halfverse: c cakrāṇa opaśam divi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a vāvṛdhānasya te vayam viśvā dhanāni jigyuṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c ūtim indrā vṛṇīmahe //6//

Hymn: 28

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vy a1ntarikṣam atiran made somasya rocanā /

Halfverse: c indro yad abhinad valam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ud gā ājad aṅgirobhya āviṣ krṇvan guhā satīḥ /

Halfverse: c arvāñcam nunude valam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indreṇa rocanā divo dṛlhāni dṛmhitāni ca /

Halfverse: c sthirāṇi na parāṇude //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a apām ūrmir madann iva stoma indrājirāyate /

Halfverse: c vi te madā arājiṣuḥ //4//

Hymn: 29

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvam hi stomavardhana indrāsy ukthavardhanaḥ /

Halfverse: c stotṝṇām uta bhadrakṛt //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indram it keśinā harī somapeyāya vakṣataḥ /

Halfverse: c upa yajñam surādhasam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apām phenena namuceḥ śira indrod avartayaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvā yad ajaya spṛdhaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a māyābhir utsisṛpsata indra dyām ārurukṣataḥ /

Halfverse: c ava dasyūṁr adhūnuthāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a asunvām indra samsadam viṣūcīm vy anāśayaḥ /

Halfverse: c somapā uttaro bhavan //5//

Hymn: 30

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pra te mahe vidathe śamsiṣam harī pra te vanve vanuṣo haryatam madam /

Halfverse: c ghṛtam na yo haribhiś cāru secata ā tvā viśantu harivarpasam giraḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a harim hi yonim abhi ye samasvaran hinvanto harī divyam yathā sadaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā yam pṛṇanti haribhir na dhenava indrāya śūśam harivantam arcata //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a so asya vajro harito ya āyaso harir nikāmo harir ā gabhastyoḥ /

Halfverse: c dyumnī suśipro harimanyusāyaka indre ni rūpā haritā mimikṣire //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a divi na ketur adhi dhāyi haryato vivyacad vajro harito na ramhyā /

Halfverse: c tudad ahim hariśipro ya āyasaḥ sahasraśokā abhavad dharimbharaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tvamtvam aharyathā upastutaḥ pūrvebhir indra harikeśa yajvabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c tvam haryasi tava viśvam ukthya1m asāmi rādho harijāta haryatam //5//

Hymn: 31

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tā vajriṇam mandinam stomyam mada indram rathe vahato haryatā harī /

Halfverse: c purūṇy asmai savanāni haryata indrāya somā harayo dadhanvire //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aram kāmāya harayo dadhamire sthirāya hinvan harayo harī turā /

Halfverse: c arvadbhir yo haribhir joṣam īyate so asya kāmam harivantam ānaśe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a hariśmaśārur harikeśa āyasas turaspeye yo haripā avardhata /

Halfverse: c arvadbhir yo haribhir vājinīvasur ati viśvā duritā pāriṣad dharī //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śruveva yasya hariṇī vipetatuḥ śipre vājāya hariṇī davidhvataḥ /

Halfverse: c pra yat kṛte camase marmṛjad dharī pītvā madasya haryatasyāndhasaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a uta sma sadna haryatasya pastyo3r atyo na vājam harivāṁ acikradat /

Halfverse: c mahī cid dhi dhiṣaṇāharyad ojasā bṛhad vayo dadhiṣe haryatas cid ā //5//

Hymn: 32

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā rodasī haryamāṇo mahitvā navyamnavyam haryasi manma nu priyam /

Halfverse: c pra pastyam asura haryatam gor āviṣ kṛdhi haraye sūryāya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā tvā haryantam prayujo janānām rathe vahantu hariśipram indra /

Halfverse: c pibā yathā pratibhṛtasya madhvo haryan yajñam sadhamāde daśoṇim //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a apāḥ pūrveṣām harivaḥ sutānām atho idam savanam kevalam te /

Halfverse: c mamaddhi somam madhumantam indra satrā vṛṣam jathara ā vṛṣasva //3//

Hymn: 33

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apsu dhūtasya harivaḥ pibeha nṛbhiḥ sutasya jaṭharam pṛṇasva /

Halfverse: c mimikṣur yam adraya indra tubhyam tebhir vardhasva madam ukthavāhaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a progrām pītim vṛṣṇa iyarmi satyām prayai sutasya haryaśva tubhyam /

Halfverse: c indra dhenābhir iha mādayasva dhībhir viśvābhiḥ śacyā gṛṇānaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ūtī śacīvas tava vīryṝṇa vayo dadhānā uśija ṛtajñāḥ /

Halfverse: c prajāvad indra mamso duroṇe tasthur gṛṇantaḥ sadhamādyāsaḥ //3//

Hymn: 34

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo jāta eva prathamo manasvān devo devān kratunā paryamūṣat /

Halfverse: c yasya śuṣmād rodasī abhyasetām nṛmṇasya mahnā sa janāsa indraḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yaḥ pṛthivīm vyathamānām adṛmhad yaḥ parvatān prakupitāṁ aramṇāt /

Halfverse: c yo antarikṣam vimame varīyo yo dyām astabhnāt sa janāsa indraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo hatvāhim ariṇāt sapta sindhūn yo gā udājad apadhā valasya /

Halfverse: c yo aśmanor antar agnim jajāna samvṛk samatsu sa janāsa indraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yenemā viśvā cyavanā kṛtāni yo dāsam varṇam adharam guhākaḥ /

Halfverse: c śvaghnīva yo jigīvām lakṣam ādad aryaḥ puṣṭāni sa janāsa indraḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yam smā pṛchanti kuha seti ghoram utem āhur naiṣo astīty enam /

Halfverse: c so aryaḥ puṣṭīr vija ivā mināti śrad asmai dhatta sa janāsa indraḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yo radhrasya coditā yaḥ kṛśasya yo brahmaṇo nādhamānasya kīreḥ /

Halfverse: c yuktagrāvṇo yo ‘vitā suśipraḥ sutasomasya sa janāsa indraḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yasyāśvāsaḥ pradiśi yasya gāvo yasya grāmā yasya viśve rathāsaḥ /

Halfverse: c yaḥ sūryam ya uṣasam jajāna yo apām netā sa janāsa indraḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yam krandasī samyatī vihvayete pare ‘vare ubhayā amitrāḥ /

Halfverse: c samānam cid ratham ātasthivāmsā nānā havete sa janāsa indraḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yasmān na ṛte vijayante janāso yam yudhyamānā avase havante /

Halfverse: c yo viśvasya pratimānam babhūva yo acyutacyut sa janāsa indraḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yaḥ śasvato mahy eno dadhānān amanyamānām charvā jaghāna /

Halfverse: c yah śardhate nānudadāti śṛdhyām yo dasyor hantā sa janāsa indraḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yaḥ śambharam parvateṣu kṣiyantam catvārimśyām śarady anvavindat /

Halfverse: c ojāyamānam yo ahim jaghāna dānum śayānam sa janāsa indraḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yaḥ śambharam paryatarat kasībhir yo ‘cārukāsnāpibat sutasya /

Halfverse: c antar girau yajamānam bahum janam yasminn āmūrchat sa janāsa indraḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yaḥ saptaraśmir vṛṣabhas tuviṣmān avāsṛjat sartave sapta sindhūn /

Halfverse: c yo rauhiṇam asphurad vajrabāhur dyām ārohantam sa janāsa indraḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a dyāvā cid asmai pṛthivī mamete śuṣmāc cid asya parvatā bhayante /

Halfverse: c yaḥ somapā nicito vajrabāhur yo vajrahastaḥ sa janāsa indraḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yaḥ sunvantam avati yaḥ pacantam yaḥ śamsantam yaḥ śaśamānam ūtī /

Halfverse: c yasya brahma vardhanam yasya somo yasyedam rādhaḥ sa janāsa indraḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a jāto vy akhyat pitror upasthe bhuvo na veda janituḥ parasya /

Halfverse: c staviṣyamāṇo no yo asmad vratā devānām sa janāsa indraḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a yaḥ somakāmo haryaśvaḥ sūrir yasmād rejante bhuvanāni viśvā /

Halfverse: c yo jaghāna śambaram yaś ca śuṣṇam ya ekavīraḥ sa janāsa indraḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yaḥ sunvate pacate dudhra ā cid vājam dardarṣi sa kilāsi satyaḥ /

Halfverse: c vayam ta indra viśvaha priyāsaḥ suvīrāso vidatham ā vadema //18//

Hymn: 35

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asmā id u pra tavase turāya prayo na harmi stomam māhināya /

Halfverse: c ṛcīṣamāyādhrigava oham indrāya brahmāṇi rātatamā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asmā id u praya iva pra yamsi bharāmy āngūṣam bādhe suvṛkti /

Halfverse: c indrāya hṛdā manasā manīṣā pratnāya patye dhiyo marjayanta //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a asmā id u tyam upamam svarṣām bharāmy āṅgūṣam āsyṝna /

Halfverse: c mamhiṣṭham achoktibhir matīnām suvṛktibhiḥ sūrim vāvṛdhadhyai //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a asmā id u stomam sam hinomi ratham na taṣṭeva tatsināya /

Halfverse: c giraś ca girvāhase suvṛktīndrāya viśvaminvam medhirāya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a asmā id u saptim iva śravasyendrāyārkam juhvā3 sam añje /

Halfverse: c vīram dānaukasam vandadhyai purām gūrtaśravasam darmāṇam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a asmā id u tvaṣṭā takṣad vajram svapastamam svarya1m raṇāya /

Halfverse: c vṛtrasya cid vidad yena marma tujann īśānas tujatā kiyedhāḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a asyed u mātuḥ savaneṣu sadyo mahaḥ pitum papivām cārv annā /

Halfverse: c muṣāyad viṣṇuḥ pacatam sahīyān vidhyad varāham tiro adrim astā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a asmā id u gnāś cid devapatnīr indrāyārkam ahihatya ūvuḥ /

Halfverse: c pari dyāvāpṛthivī jabhra urvī nāsya te mahimānam pari ṣṭaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a asyed eva pra ririce mahitvam divas pṛthivyāḥ pary antarikṣāt /

Halfverse: c svarāl indro dama ā viśvagūrtaḥ svarir amatro vavakṣe raṇāya //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a asyed eva śavasā śuśantam vi vṛścad vajreṇa vṛtram indraḥ /

Halfverse: c gā na vrāṇā avanīr amuñcad abhi śravo dāvane sacetāḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a asyed u tveṣasā ranta sindhavaḥ pari yad vajreṇa sīm ayachat /

Halfverse: c īśānakṛd dāśuṣe daśasyan turvītaye gādham turvaṇiḥ kaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a asmā id u pra bharā tūtujāno vṛtrāya vajram īśānaḥ kiyedhāḥ /

Halfverse: c gor na parva vi radā tiraśceṣyann arṇāmsy apām caradhyai //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a asyed u pra brūhi pūrvyāṇi turasya karmāṇi navya ukthaiḥ /

Halfverse: c yudhe yad iṣṇāna āyudhāny ṛghāyamāṇo niriṇāti śatrūn //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a asyed u bhiyā girayaś ca dṛlhā dyāvā ca bhūmā januṣas tujete /

Halfverse: c upo venasya joguvāna oṇim sadyo bhuvad vīryāya nodhāḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a asmā id u tyad anu dāyy eṣām eko yad vavne bhūrer īśānaḥ /

Halfverse: c praitaśam sūrye paspṛdhānam sauvaśvye suṣvim āvad indraḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a evā te hāriyojanā suvṛktīndra brahmāṇi gotamāso akran /

Halfverse: c aiṣu viśvapeśasam dhiyam dhāḥ prātar makṣū dhiyāvasur jagamyāt //16//

Hymn: 36

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ya eka id dhavyaś carṣaṇīnām indram tam gīrbhir abhy arca ābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c yaḥ patyate vṛṣabho vṛṣṇyāvānt satyaḥ satvā purumāyaḥ sahasvān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tam u naḥ pūrve pitaro navagvāḥ sapta viprāso abhi vājayantaḥ /

Halfverse: c nakṣaddābham taturim parvateṣṭhām adroghavācam matibhiḥ śaviṣṭham //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tam īmahe indram asya rāyaḥ puruvīrasya nṛvataḥ purukṣoḥ /

Halfverse: c yo aṣkṛdhoyur ajaraḥ svarvān tam ā bhara harivo mādayadhyai //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tan no vi voco yadi te purā cij jaritāra ānaśuḥ sumnam indra /

Halfverse: c kas te bhāgaḥ kim vayo dudhra khiduḥ puruhūta purūvaso ‘suraghnaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tam pṛchantī vajrahastam ratheṣṭhām indram vepī vakvarī yasya nū gīḥ /

Halfverse: c tuvigrābham tuvikūrmim rabhodām gātum iṣe nakṣate tumram acha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ayā ha tyam māyayā vāvṛdhānam manojuvā svatavaḥ parvatena /

Halfverse: c acyutā cid vīlitā svojo rujo vi dṛlhā dhṛṣatā virapśin //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tam vo dhiyā navyasyā śaviṣṭham pratnam pratnavat paritamsayadhyai /

Halfverse: c sa no vakṣad animānaḥ suvahnendro viśvāny ati durgahāṇi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ā janāya druhvaṇe pārthivāni divyāni dīpayo ‘ntarikṣā /

Halfverse: c tapā vṛṣan viśvataḥ śociṣā tān brahmadviṣe śocaya kṣām apaś ca //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a bhuvo janasya divyasya rājā pārthivasya jagatas tveṣasamdṛk /

Halfverse: c dhiṣva vajram dakṣiṇa indra haste viśvā ajurya dayase vi māyāḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ā samyatam indra ṇaḥ svastim śatrutūryāya bṛhatīm amṛdhrām /

Halfverse: c yayā dāsāny āryāṇi vṛtrā karo vajrint sutukā nāhuṣāṇi //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a sa no niyudbhiḥ puruhūta vedho viśvavārābhir ā gahi prayajyo /

Halfverse: c na yā adevo varate na deva ābhir yāhi tūyam ā madryadrik //11//

Hymn: 37

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yas tigmaśṛṅgo vṛṣabho na bhīmaḥ ekaḥ kṛṣṭīś cyavayati pra viśvāḥ /

Halfverse: c yaḥ śaśvato adāśuṣo gayasya prayantāsi suṣvitarāya vedaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvam ha tyad indra kutsam āvaḥ śuśrūṣamāṇas tanvā samarye /

Halfverse: c dāsam yac śuṣṇam kuyavam ny asmā arandhaya ārjuneyāya śikṣan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvam dhṛṣṇo dhṛṣatā vītahavyam prāvo viśvābhir ūtibhiḥ sudāsam /

Halfverse: c pra paurukutsim trasadasyum āvaḥ kṣetrasātā vṛtrahatyeṣu pūrum //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tvam nṛbhir nṛmaṇo devavītau bhūrīṇi vṛtrā haryaśva hamsi /

Halfverse: c tvam ni dasyum cumurim dhunim cāsvāpayo dabhītaye suhantu //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tava cyautnāni vajrahasta tāni nava yat puro navatim ca sadyaḥ /

Halfverse: c niveśane śatatamāviveṣīr aham ca vṛtram namucim utāhan //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sanā tā ta indra bhojanāni rātahavyāya dāśuṣe sudāse /

Halfverse: c vṛṣṇe te harī vṛṣaṇā yunajmi vyantu brahmāṇi puruśāka vājam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a mā te asyām sahasāvan pariṣṭāv aghāya bhūma harivaḥ parādau /

Halfverse: c trāyasva no ‘vṛkebhir varūthais tava priyāsaḥ sūriṣu syāma //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a priyāsa it te maghavann abhiṣṭau naro madema śaraṇe sakhāyaḥ /

Halfverse: c ni turvaśam ni yādvam śiśīhy atithigvāya śamsyam kariṣyan //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a sadyaś cin nu te maghavann abhiṣṭau naraḥ śamsanty ukthaśāsa ukthā /

Halfverse: c ye te havebhir vi paṇīṁr adāśann asmān vṛṇīṣva yujyāya tasmai //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a ete stomā narām nṛtama tubhyam asmadryañco dadato maghāni /

Halfverse: c teṣām indra vṛtrahatye śivo bhūḥ sakhā ca śūro ‘vitā ca nṛṇām //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a nū indra śūra stavamāna ūtī brahmajūtas tanvā vāvṛdhasva /

Halfverse: c upa no vājān mimīhy upa stīn yuyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ //11//

Hymn: 38

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā yāhi suṣumā hi ta indra somam pibā imam /

Halfverse: c edam barhiḥ sado mama //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā tvā brahmayujā harī vahatām indra keśinā /

Halfverse: c upa brahmāṇi naḥ śṛṇu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a brahmāṇas tvā vayam yujā somapām indra sominaḥ /

Halfverse: c sutāvanto havāmahe //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indram id gāthino bṛhad indram arkebhir arkiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c indram vāṇīr anūṣata //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indra id dharyoḥ sacā sammiśla ā vacoyujā /

Halfverse: c indro vajrī hiraṇyayaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indro dīrghāya cakṣasa ā sūryam rohayad divi /

Halfverse: c vi gobhir adrim airayat //6//

Hymn: 39

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indram vo viśvatas pari havāmahe janebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c asmākam astu kevalaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vy a1ntarikṣam atiran made somasya rocanā /

Halfverse: c indro yad abhinad valam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ud gā ājad aṅgirobhya āviṣ kṛṇvan guhā satīḥ /

Halfverse: c arvāñcam nunude valam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indreṇa rocanā divo dṛlhāni dṛmhitāni ca /

Halfverse: c sthirāṇi na parāṇude //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a apām ūrmir madann iva stoma indrājirāyate /

Halfverse: c vi te madā arājiṣuḥ //5//

Hymn: 40

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indreṇa sam hi dṛkṣase samjagmāno abibhyuṣā /

Halfverse: c mandū samānavarcasā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anavadyair abhidyubhir makhaḥ sahasvad arcati /

Halfverse: c gaṇair indrasya kāmyaiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ād aha svadhām anu punar garbhatvam erire /

Halfverse: c dadhānā nāma yajñiyam //3//

Hymn: 41

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indro dadhīco asthabhir vṛtrāṇy apratiṣkutaḥ /

Halfverse: c jaghāna navatīr nava //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ichan aśvasya yac chiraḥ parvateṣv apaśritam /

Halfverse: c tad vidac charyaṇāvati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a atrāha gor amanvata nāma tvaṣṭur apīcyam /

Halfverse: c itthā candramaso gṛhe //3//

Hymn: 42

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vācam aṣṭāpadīm aham navasraktim ṛtaspṛśam /

Halfverse: c indrāt pari tanvam mame //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anu tvā rodasī ubhe krakṣamāṇam akṛpetām /

Halfverse: c indra yad dasyuhābhavaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a uttiṣṭhann ojasā saha pītvī śipre avepayaḥ /

Halfverse: c somam indra camū sutam //3//

Hymn: 43

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhindhi viśvā apa dviṣaḥ bādho jahī mṛdhaḥ /

Halfverse: c vasu spārham tad ā bhara //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad vīlāv indra yat sthire yat parśāne parābhṛtam /

Halfverse: c vasu spārham tad ā bhara //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yasya te viśvamānuṣo bhūrer dattasya vedati /

Halfverse: c vasu spārham tad ā bhara //3//

Hymn: 44

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pra samrājam carṣaṇīnām indram stotā navyam gīrbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c naram nṛṣāham mamhiṣṭham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yasminn ukthāni raṇyanti viśvāni ca śravasya /

Halfverse: c apām avo na samudre //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tam suṣṭutyā vivāse jyeṣṭharājam bhare kṛtnum /

Halfverse: c maho vājinam sanibhyaḥ //3//

Hymn: 45

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ayam u te sam atasi kapota iva garbhadhim /

Halfverse: c vacas tac cin na ohase //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a stotram rādhānām pate girvāho vīra yasya te /

Halfverse: c vibhūtir astu sūnṛtā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ūrdhvas tiṣṭhā na ūtaye ‘smin vāje śatakrato /

Halfverse: c sam anyeṣu bravāvahai //3//

Hymn: 46

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a praṇetāram vasyo achā kartāram jyotiḥ samatsu /

Halfverse: c sāsahvāmsam yudhāmitrān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sa naḥ papriḥ pārayāti svasti nāvā puruhūtaḥ /

Halfverse: c indro viśvā ati dviṣaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sa tvam na indra vājobhir daśasyā ca gātuyā ca /

Halfverse: c achā ca naḥ sumnam neṣi //3//

Hymn: 47

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tam indram vājayāmasi mahe vṛtrāya hantave /

Halfverse: c sa vṛṣā vṛṣabho bhuvat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indraḥ sa dāmane kṛta ojiṣṭhaḥ sa made hitaḥ /

Halfverse: c dyumnī ślokī sa somyaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a girā vajro na sambhṛtaḥ sabalo anapacyutaḥ /

Halfverse: c vavakṣa ṛṣvo astṛtaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indram id gāthino bṛhad indram arkebhir arkiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c indram vāṇīr anūṣata //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indra id dharyoḥ sacā sammiśla ā vacoyujā /

Halfverse: c indro vajrī hiraṇyayaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indro dīrghāya cakṣasa ā sūryam rohayad divi /

Halfverse: c vi gobhir adrim airayat //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ā yāhi suṣumā hi ta indra somam pibā imam /

Halfverse: c edam barhiḥ sado mama //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ā tvā brahmayujā harī vahatām indra keśinā /

Halfverse: c upa brahmāṇi naḥ śṛṇu //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a brahmāṇas tvā vayam yujā somapām indra sominaḥ /

Halfverse: c sutāvanto havāmahe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yuñjanti bradhnam aruṣam carantam pari tasthuṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c rocante rocanā divi //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a yuñjanty asya kāmyā harī vipakṣasā rathe /

Halfverse: c śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛvāhasā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ketum kṛṇvann aketave peśo maryā apeśase /

Halfverse: c sam uṣadbhir ajāyathāḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ud u tyam jātavedasam devam vahanti ketavaḥ /

Halfverse: c dṛśe viśvāya sūryam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a apa tye tāyavo yathā nakṣatrā yanty aktubhiḥ /

Halfverse: c sūrāya viśvacakṣase //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a adṛśrann asya ketavo vi raśmayo janāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c bhrājanto agnayo yathā //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a taraṇir viśvadarśato jyotiṣkṛd asi sūrya /

Halfverse: c viśvam ā bhāsi rocana //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a pratyaṅ devānām viśaḥ pratyaṅṅ ud eṣi mānuṣīḥ /

Halfverse: c pratyaṅ viśvam svar dṛśe //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a yenā pāvaka cakṣasā bhuraṇyantam janāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c tvam varuṇa paśyasi //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a vi dyām eṣi rajas pṛthv ahar mimāno aktubhiḥ /

Halfverse: c paśyam janmāni sūrya //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a sapta tvā harito rathe vahanti deva sūrya /

Halfverse: c śociṣkeśam vicakṣaṇam //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a ayukta sapta śundhyuvaḥ sūro rathasya naptyaḥ /

Halfverse: c tābhir yāti svayuktibhiḥ //21//

Hymn: 48

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi tvā varcasā giraḥ siñcanty ā caraṇyuvaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhi vatsam na dhenavaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tā arṣanti śubhriyaḥ pṛñcatīr varcasā payaḥ /

Halfverse: c jātam janir yathā hṛdā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vajrāpavasādhyaḥ kīrtir mriyamāṇam āvahan /

Halfverse: c mahyam āyur ghṛtam payaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a āyam gauḥ pṛśnir akramīd asadan mātaram puraḥ /

Halfverse: c pitaram ca prayant svaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a antaś carati rocanā asya prāṇād apānataḥ /

Halfverse: c vy akhyan mahiṣaḥ svaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a trimśad dhāmā vi rājati vāk pataṅgo aśiśriyat /

Halfverse: c prati vastor ahar dyubhiḥ //6//

Hymn: 49

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yac chakrā vācam āruhann antarikṣam siṣāsathaḥ /

Halfverse: c sam devā amadan vṛṣā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śakro vācam adhṛṣṭāyor uvāco adhṛṣṇuhi /

Halfverse: c mamhiṣṭha ā madardivi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a śakro vācam adhṛṣṇuhi dhāmadharman vi rājati /

Halfverse: c vimadan barhir āsaran //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tam vo dasmam ṛtīṣaham vasor mandānam andhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c abhi vatsam na svasareṣu dhenava indram gīrbhir navāmahe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dyukṣam sudānum taviṣībhir āvṛtam girim na purubhojasam /

Halfverse: c kṣumantam vājam śatinam sahasriṇam makṣū gomantam īmahe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tat tvā yāmi suvīryam tad brahma pūrvacittaye /

Halfverse: c yenā yatibhyo bhṛgave dhane hite yena praskaṇvam āvitha //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yenā samudram asṛjo mahīr apas tad indra vṛṣṇi te śavaḥ /

Halfverse: c sadyaḥ so asya mahimā na samnaśe yam kṣoṇīr anucakrade //7//

Hymn: 50

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kan navyo atasīnām turo gṛṇīta martyaḥ /

Halfverse: c nahī nv asya mahimānam indriyam svar gṛṇanta ānaśuḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kad u stuvanta ṛtayanta devata ṛṣiḥ ko vipra ohate /

Halfverse: c kadā havam maghavann indra sunvataḥ kad u stuvata ā gamaḥ //2//

Hymn: 51

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi pra vaḥ surādhasam indram arca yathā vide /

Halfverse: c yo jaritṛbhyo maghavā purūvasuḥ sahasreṇeva śikṣati //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śatānīkeva pra jigāti dhṛṣṇuyā hanti vṛtrāṇi dāśuṣe /

Halfverse: c girer iva pra rasā asya pinvire datrāṇi purubhojasaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pra su śrutam surādhasam arcā śakram abhiṣṭaye /

Halfverse: c yaḥ sunvate stuvate kāmyam vasu sahasreṇeva mamhate //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śatānīkā hetayo asya duṣṭarā indrasya samiṣo mahīḥ /

Halfverse: c girir na bhujmā maghatsu pinvate yad īm sutā amandiṣuḥ //4//

Hymn: 52

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vayam gha tvā sutāvanta āpo na vṛktabarhiṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c pavitrasya prasravaṇeṣu vṛtrahan pari stotāra āsate //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a svaranti tvā sute naro vaso nireka ukthinaḥ /

Halfverse: c kadā sutam tṛṣāṇa oka ā gama indra svabdīva vamsagaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kaṇvebhir dhṛṣṇav ā dhṛsad vājam darṣi sahasriṇam /

Halfverse: c piśaṅgarūpam maghavan vicarṣaṇe maksū gomantam īmahe //3//

Hymn: 53

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ka īm veda sute sacā pibantam kad vayo dadhe /

Halfverse: c ayam yaḥ puro vibhinatty ojasā mandānaḥ śipry andhasaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dānā mṛgo na vāraṇaḥ purutrā caratham dadhe /

Halfverse: c nakiṣ ṭvā ni yamad ā sute gamo mahāś carasy ojasā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ya ugraḥ sann aniṣṭṛta sthiro raṇāya samskṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c yadi stotur maghavā śṛṇavad dhavam nendro yoṣaty ā gamat //3//

Hymn: 54

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a viśvāḥ pṛtanā abhibhūtaram naram sajūs tatakṣur indram jajanuś ca rājase /

Halfverse: c kratvā variṣṭham vara āmurim utogram ojiṣṭham tavasam tarasvinam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sam īm rebhāso asvarann indram somasya pītaye /
