Halfverse: c svarpatim yad īm vṛdhe dhṛtavrato hy ojasā sam ūtibhiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nemim namanti cakṣasā meṣam viprā abhisvarā /

Halfverse: c sudītayo vo adruho ‘pi karṇe tarasvinaḥ sam ṛkvabhiḥ //3//

Hymn: 55

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tam indram johavīmi maghavānam ugram satrā dadhānam apratiṣkutam śavāmsi /

Halfverse: c mamhiṣṭho gīrbhir ā ca yajñiyo vavartad rāye no viśvā supathā kṛṇotu vajrī //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yā indra bhuja ābharaḥ svarvāṁ asurebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c stotāram in maghavann asya vardhaya ye ca tve vṛktabarhiṣaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yam indra dadhiṣe tvam aśvam gām bhāgam avyayam /

Halfverse: c yajamāne sunvati dakṣiṇāvati tasmin tam dhehi mā paṇau //3//

Hymn: 56

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indro madāya vāvṛdhe śavase vṛtrahā nṛbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c tam in mahatsv ājiṣūtem arbhe havāmahe sa vājeṣu pra no ‘viṣat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asi hi vīra senyo ‘si bhūri parādadiḥ /

Halfverse: c asi dabhrasya cid vṛdho yajamānāya śikṣasi sunvate bhūri te vasu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad udīrata ājayo dhṛṣṇave dhīyate dhanā /

Halfverse: c yukṣvā madacyutā harī kam hanaḥ kam vasau dadho ‘smāṁ indra vasau dadhaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a mademade hi no dadir yūthā gavām ṛjukratuḥ /

Halfverse: c sam gṛbhāya puru śatobhayāhastyā vasu śiśīhi rāya ā bhara //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mādayasva sute sacā śavase śūra rādhase /

Halfverse: c vidmā hi tvā purūvasum upa kāmānt sasṛjmahe ‘thā no ‘vitā bhava //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ete ta indra jantavo viśvam puṣyanti vāryam /

Halfverse: c antar hi khyo janānām aryo vedo adāśuṣām teṣām no veda ā bhara //6//

Hymn: 57

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a surūpakṛtnum ūtaye sudughām iva goduhe /

Halfverse: c juhūmasi dyavidyavi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a upa naḥ savanā gahi somasya somapāh piba /

Halfverse: c godā id revato madaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a athā te antamānām vidyāma sumatīnām /

Halfverse: c mā no ati khya ā gahi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śuṣmintamam na ūtaye dyumninam pāhi jāgṛvim /

Halfverse: c indra somam śatakrato //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indriyāṇi śatakrato yā te janeṣu pañcasu /

Halfverse: c indra tāni ta ā vṛṇe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a agann indra śravo bṛhad dyumnam dadhiṣva duṣṭaram /

Halfverse: c ut te śuṣmam tirāmasi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a arvāvato na ā gahy atho śakra parāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c u loko yas te adriva indreha tata ā gahi //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a indro aṅga mahad bhayam abhī ṣad apa cucyavat /

Halfverse: c sa hi sthiro vicarṣaniḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a indraś ca mṛlayāti no na naḥ paścād agham naśat /

Halfverse: c bhadram bhavāti naḥ puraḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a indra āśābhyas pari sarvābhyo abhayam karat /

Halfverse: c jetā śatrūn vicarṣaṇiḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ka īm veda sute sacā pibantam kad vayo dadhe /

Halfverse: c ayam yaḥ puro vibhinatty ojasā mandānaḥ śipry andhasaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a dānā mṛgo na vāraṇaḥ purutrā caratham dadhe /

Halfverse: c nakiṣ ṭvā ni yamad ā sute gamo mahāmś carasy ojasā //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ya ugraḥ sann aniṣṭṛta sthiro raṇāya samskṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: c yadi stotur maghavā śṛṇavad dhavam nendro yoṣaty ā gamat //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a vayam gha tvā sutāvanta āpo na vṛktabarhiṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c pavitrasya prasravaṇeṣu vṛtrahan pari stotāra āsate //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a svaranti tvā sute naro vaso nireka ukthinaḥ /

Halfverse: c kadā sutam tṛṣāṇa oka ā gama indra svabdīva vamsagaḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a kaṇvebhir dhṛṣṇav ā dhṛṣad vājam darṣi sahasriṇam /

Halfverse: c piśaṅgarūpam maghavan vicarṣaṇe makṣū gomantam īmahe //16//

Hymn: 58

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śrāyanta iva sūryam viśved indrasya bhakṣata /

Halfverse: c vasūni jāte janamāna ojasā prati bhāgam na dīdhima //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anarśarātim vasudām upa stuhi bhadrā indrasya rātayaḥ /

Halfverse: c so asya kāmam vidhato na roṣati mano dānāya codayan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a baṇ mahāṁ asi sūrya baḍ āditya mahāṁ asi /

Halfverse: c mahas te sato mahimā panasyate ‘ddhā deva mahāṁ asi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a baṭ sūrya śravasā mahāṁ asi satrā deva mahāṁ asi /

Halfverse: c mahnā devānām asuryaḥ purohito vibhu jyotir adābhyam //4//

Hymn: 59

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ud u tye madhu mattamā gira stomāsa īrate /

Halfverse: c satrājito dhanasā akṣitotayo vājayanto rathā iva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kaṇvā iva bhṛgavaḥ sūrya iva viśvam id dhītam ānaśuḥ /

Halfverse: c indram stomebhir mahayanta āyavaḥ priyamedhāso asvaran //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ud in nv asya ricyate ‘mśo dhanam na jigyusaḥ /

Halfverse: c ya indro harivān na dabhanti tam ripo daksam dadhāti somini //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a mantram akharvam sudhitam supeśasam dadhāta yajñiyeṣv ā /

Halfverse: c pūrvīś cana prasitayas taranti tam ya indre karmaṇā bhuvat //4//

Hymn: 60

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a evā hy asi vīrayur evā śūra uta sthiraḥ /

Halfverse: c evā te rādhyam manaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a evā rātis tuvīmagha viśvebhir dhāyi dhātṛbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c aghā cid indra me sacā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a mo ṣu brahmeva tandrayur bhuvo vājānām pate /

Halfverse: c matsvā sutasya gomataḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a evā hy asya sūnṛtā virapśī gomatī mahī /

Halfverse: c pakvā śākhā na dāśuṣe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a evā hi te vibhūtaya ūtaya indra māvate /

Halfverse: c sadyaś cit santi dāśuṣe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a evā hy asya kāmyā stoma uktham ca śamsyā /

Halfverse: c indrāya somapītaye //6//

Hymn: 61

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tam te madam gṛṇīmasi vṛṣaṇam pṛtsu sāsahim /

Halfverse: c u lokakṛtnum adrivo hariśriyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yena jyotīmṣy āyave manave ca viveditha /

Halfverse: c mandāno asya barhiṣo vi rājasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tad adyā cit ta ukthino ‘nu ṣṭuvanti pūrvathā /

Halfverse: c vṛṣapatnīr apo jayā divedive //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tam v abhi pra gāyata puruhūtam puruṣṭutam /

Halfverse: c indram gīrbhis taviṣam ā vivāsata //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yasya dvibarhaso bṛhat saho dādhāra rodasī /

Halfverse: c girīṁr ajrāṁ apaḥ svar vṛṣatvanā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sa rājasi puruṣṭutaṁ eko vṛtrāṇi jighnase /

Halfverse: c indra jaitrā śravasya ca yantave //6//

Hymn: 62

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vayam u tvām apūrvya sthūram na kac cid bharanto ‘vasyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c vāje citram havāmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a upa tvā karmann ūtaye sa no yuvograś cakrāma yo dhṛṣat /

Halfverse: c tvām id dhy avitāram vavṛmahe sakhāya indra sānasim //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yo na idamidam purā pra vasya ānināya tam u va stuṣe /

Halfverse: c sakhāya indram ūtaye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a haryaśvam satpatim carṣaṇīsaham sa hi ṣmā yo amandata /

Halfverse: c ā tu naḥ sa vayati gavyam aśvyam stotṛbhyo maghavā śatam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indrāya sāma gāyata viprāya bṛhate bṛhat /

Halfverse: c dharmakṛte vipaścite panasyave //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tvam indrābhibhūr asi tvam sūryam arocayaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvakarmā viśvadevo mahāṁ asi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a vibhrājam jyotiṣā sva1r agacho rocanam divaḥ /

Halfverse: c devās ta indra sakhyāya yemire //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tam v abhi pra gāyata puruhūtam puruṣṭutam /

Halfverse: c indram gīrbhis taviṣam ā vivāsata //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yasya dvibarhaso bṛhat saho dādhāra rodasī /

Halfverse: c girīṁr ajrāṁ apaḥ svar vṛṣatvanā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a sa rājasi puruṣṭutaṁ eko vṛtrāṇi jighnase /

Halfverse: c indra jaitra śravasya ca yantave //10//

Hymn: 63

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imā nu kam bhuvanā sīṣadhāmendraś ca viśve ca devāḥ /

Halfverse: c yajñam ca nas tanvam ca prajām cādityair indraḥ saha cīkḷpāti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ādityair indraḥ sagaṇo marudbhir asmākam bhūtv avitā tanūnām /

Halfverse: c hatvāya devā asurān yad āyan devā devatvam abhirakṣamāṇāḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pratyañcam arkam anayam chacībhir ād it svadhām iṣirām pary apaśyan /

Halfverse: c ayā vājam devahitam sanema madema śatahimāḥ suvīrāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ya eka id vidayate vasu martāya dāśuṣe /

Halfverse: c īśāno apratiṣkuta indro aṅga //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kadā martam arādhasam padā kṣumpam iva sphurat /

Halfverse: c kadā naḥ śuśravad gira indro aṅga //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yaś cid dhi tvā bahubhya ā sutāvāṁ āvivāsati /

Halfverse: c ugram tat patyate śava indro aṅga //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ya indra somapātamo madaḥ śaviṣṭha cetati /

Halfverse: c yenā hamsi ny a1ttriṇam tam īmahe //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a yenā daśagvam adhrigum vepayantam svarṇaram /

Halfverse: c yenā samudram āvithā tam īmahe //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yena sindhum mahīr apo rathāṁ iva pracodayaḥ /

Halfverse: c panthām ṛtasya yātave tam īmahe //9//

Hymn: 64

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a endra no gadhi priyaḥ satrājid agohyaḥ /

Halfverse: c girir na viśvatas pṛthuḥ patir divaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a abhi hi satya somapā ubhe babhūtha rodasī /

Halfverse: c indrāsi sunvato vṛdhaḥ patir divaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvam hi śaśvatīnām indra dartā purām asi /

Halfverse: c hantā dasyor manor vṛdhaḥ patir divaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ed u madhvo madintaram siñca vādhvaryo andhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c evā hi vīra stavate sadāvṛdhaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indra sthātar harīṇām nakiṣ te pūrvyastutim /

Halfverse: c ud ānamśa śavasā na bhandanā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tam vo vājānām patim ahūmahi śravasyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c aprāyubhir yajñebhir vāvṛdhenyam //6//

Hymn: 65

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a eto nv indram stavāma sakhāya stomyam naram /

Halfverse: c kuṣṭīr yo viśvā abhy asty eka it //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agorudhāya gaviṣe dyukṣāya dasmyam vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c ghṛtāt svādīyo madhunaś ca vocata //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yasyāmitāni vīryā3 na rādhaḥ paryetave /

Halfverse: c jyotir na viśvam abhy asti dakṣiṇā //3//

Hymn: 66

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a stuhīndram vyaśvavad anūrmim vājinam yamam /

Halfverse: c aryo gayam mamhamānam vi dāśuṣe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a evā nūnam upa stuhi vaiyaśva daśamam navam /

Halfverse: c suvidvāmsam carkṛtyam caraṇīnām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vetthā hi nirṛtīnām vajrahasta parivṛjam /

Halfverse: c aharahaḥ śundhyuḥ paripadām iva //3//

Hymn: 67

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vanoti hi sunvan kṣayam parīṇasaḥ sunvāno hi ṣmā yajaty ava dviṣo devānām ava dviṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c sunvāna it siṣāsati sahasrā vājy avṛtaḥ /

Halfverse: e sunvānāyendro dadāty ābhuvam rayim dadāty ābhuvam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mo ṣu vo asmad abhi tāni paumsyā sanā bhūvan dyumnāni mota jāriṣur asmat purota jāriṣuḥ /

Halfverse: c yad vaś citram yugeyuge navyam ghoṣād amartyam /

Halfverse: e asmāsu tan maruto yac ca duṣṭaram didhṛtā yac ca duṣṭaram //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a agnim hotāram manye dāsvantam vasum sūnum sahaso jātavedasam vipram na jātavedasam /

Halfverse: c ya ūrdhvayā svadhvaro devo devācyā kṛpā /

Halfverse: e ghṛtasya vibhrāṣṭim anu vaṣṭi śociṣājuhvānasya sarpiṣaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yajñaiḥ sammiślāḥ pṛṣatībhir ṛṣṭibhir yāmam chubhrāso añjiṣu priyā uta /

Halfverse: c āsadyā barhir bharatasya sūnavaḥ potrād ā somam pibatā divo naraḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā vakṣi devāṁ iha vipra yakṣi cośan hotar ni ṣadā yoniṣu triṣu /

Halfverse: c prati vīhi prasthitam somyam madhu pibāgnīdhrāt tava bhāgasya tṛsṇuhi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a eṣa sya te tanvo nṛmṇavardhanaḥ saha ojaḥ pradivi bāhvor hitaḥ /

Halfverse: c tubhyam suto maghavan tubhyam ābhṛtas tvam asya brāhmaṇād ā tṛpat piba //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yam u pūrvam ahuve tam idam huve sed u havyo dadir yo nāma patyate /

Halfverse: c adhvaryubhiḥ prasthitam somyam madhu potrāt somam draviṇodaḥ piba ṛtubhiḥ //7//

Hymn: 68

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a surūpakṛtnum ūtaye sudughām iva goduhe /

Halfverse: c juhūmasi dyavidyavi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a upa naḥ savanā gahi somasya somapāḥ piba /

Halfverse: c godā id revato madaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a athā te antamānām vidyāma sumatīnām /

Halfverse: c mā no ati khya ā gahi //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a parehi vigram astṛtam indram pṛchā vipaścitam /

Halfverse: c yas te sakhibhya ā varam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a uta bruvantu no nido nir anyataś cid ārata /

Halfverse: c dadhānā indra id duvaḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a uta naḥ subhagāṁ arir voceyur dasma kṛṣṭayaḥ /

Halfverse: c syāmed indrasya śarmaṇi //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a em āśum āśave bhara yajñaśriyam nṛmādanam /

Halfverse: c patayan mandayatsakham //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a asya pītvā śatakrato ghano vṛtrāṇām abhavaḥ /

Halfverse: c prāvo vājeṣu vājinam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tam tvā vājeṣu vājinam vājayāmaḥ śatakrato /

Halfverse: c dhanānām indra sātaye //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yo rāyo3 ‘vanir mahānt supāraḥ sunvataḥ sakhā /

Halfverse: c tasmā indrāya gāyata //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ā tv etā ni ṣīdatendram abhi pra gāyata /

Halfverse: c sakhāya stomavāhasaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a purūtamam purūṇām īśānam vāryāṇām /

Halfverse: c indram some sacā sute //12//

Hymn: 69

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sa ghā no yoga ā bhuvat sa rāye sa puramdhyām /

Halfverse: c gamad vājebhir ā sa naḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yasya samsthe na vṛṇvate harī samatsu śatravaḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmā indrāya gāyata //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a sutapāvne sutā ime śucayo yanti vītaye /

Halfverse: c somāso dadhyāśiraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tvam sutasya pītaye sadyo vṛddho ajāyathāḥ /

Halfverse: c indra jyaiṣṭhyāya sukrato //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā tvā viśantv āśavaḥ somāsa indra girvaṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c śam te santu pracetase //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tvām stomā avīvṛdhan tvām ukthā śatakrato /

Halfverse: c tvām vardhantu no giraḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a akṣitotiḥ saned imam vājam indraḥ sahasriṇam /

Halfverse: c yasmin viśvāni paumsyā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mā no martā abhi druhan tanūnām indra girvaṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c īśāno yavayā vadham //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a yuñjanti bradhnam aruṣam carantam pari tasthuṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c rocante rocanā divi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yuñjanty asya kāmyā harī vipakṣasā rathe /

Halfverse: c śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛvāhasā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ketum kṛṇvann aketave peśo maryā apeśase /

Halfverse: c sam uṣadbhir ajāyathāḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a ād aha svadhām anu punar garbhatvam erire /

Halfverse: c dadhānā nāma yajñiyam //12//

Hymn: 70

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vīlu cid ārujatnubhir guhā cid indra vahnibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c avinda usriyā anu //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a devayanto yathā matim achā vidadvasum giraḥ /

Halfverse: c mahām anūṣata śrutam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indreṇa sam hi dṛkṣase samjagmāno abibhyuṣā /

Halfverse: c mandū samānavarcasā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anavadyair abhidyubhir makhaḥ sahasvad arcati /

Halfverse: c gaṇair indrasya kāmyaiḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ataḥ parijmann ā gahi divo vā rocanād adhi /

Halfverse: c sam asminn ṛñjate giraḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ito vā sātim īmahe divo vā pārthivād adhi /

Halfverse: c indram maho vā rajasaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a indram id gathino bṛhad indram arkebhir arkiṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c indram vāṇīr anūṣata //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a indra id dharyoḥ sacā sammiśla ā vacoyujā /

Halfverse: c indro vajrī hiraṇyayaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a indro dīrghāya cakṣasa ā sūryam rohayad divi /

Halfverse: c vi gobhir indram airayat //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a indra vājeṣu no ‘va sahasrapradhaneṣu ca /

Halfverse: c ugra ugrābhir ūtibhiḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a indram vayam mahādhana indram arbhe havāmahe /

Halfverse: c yujam vṛtreṣu vajriṇam //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sa no vṛṣann amum carum satrādāvann apā vṛdhi /

Halfverse: c asmabhyam apratiṣkutaḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a tuñjetuñje ya uttare stomā indrasya vajriṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c na vindhe asya suṣṭutim //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a vṛṣā yūtheva vamsagaḥ kṛṣṭīr iyarty ojasā /

Halfverse: c īśāno apratiṣkutaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a ya ekaś carṣaṇīnām vasūnām irajyati /

Halfverse: c indraḥ pañca kṣitīnām //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a indram vo viśvatas pari havāmahe janebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c asmākam astu kevalaḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a endra sānasim rayim sajitvānam sadāsaham /

Halfverse: c varṣiṣṭham ūtaye bhara //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a ni yena muṣṭihatyayā ni vṛtrā ruṇadhāmahai /

Halfverse: c tvotāso ny arvatā //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a indra tvotāso ā vayam vajram ghanā dadīmahi /

Halfverse: c jayema sam yudhi spṛdhaḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a vayam śūrebhir astṛbhir indra tvayā yujā vayam /

Halfverse: c sāsahyāma pṛtanyataḥ //20//

Hymn: 71

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mahāṁ indraḥ paraś ca nu mahitvam astu vajriṇe /

Halfverse: c dyaur na prathinā śavaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a samohe vā ya āśata naras tokasya sanitau /

Halfverse: c viprāso vā dhiyāyavaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yaḥ kukṣiḥ somapātamaḥ samudra iva pinvate /

Halfverse: c urvīr āpo na kākudaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a evā hy asya sūnṛtā virapśī gomatī mahī /

Halfverse: c pakvā śākhā na dāśuṣe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a evā hi te vibhūtaya ūtaya indra māvate /

Halfverse: c sadyaś cit santi dāśuṣe //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a evā hy asya kāmyā stoma uktham ca śamsyā /

Halfverse: c indrāya somapītaye //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a indrehi matsy andhaso viśvebhiḥ somaparvabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c mahāṁ abhiṣṭir ojasā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a em enam sṛjatā sute mandim indrāya mandine /

Halfverse: c cakrim viśvāni cakraye //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a matsvā suśipra mandibhi stomebhir viśvacarṣaṇe /

Halfverse: c sacaiṣu savaneṣv ā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a asṛgram indra te giraḥ prati tvām ud ahāsata /

Halfverse: c ajoṣā vṛṣabham patim //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a sam codaya citram arvāg rādha indra vareṇyam /

Halfverse: c asad it te vibhu prabhu //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a asmānt su tatra codayendra rāye rabhasvataḥ /

Halfverse: c tuvidyumna yaśasvataḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a sam gomad indra vājavad asme pṛthu śravo bṛhat /

Halfverse: c viśvāyur dhehy akṣitam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a asme dhehi śravo bṛhad dyumnam sahasrasātamam /

Halfverse: c indra tā rathinīr iṣaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a vasor indram vasupatim gīrbhir gṛṇanta ṛgmiyam /

Halfverse: c homa gantāram ūtaye //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a sutesute nyokase bṛhad bṛhata ed ariḥ /

Halfverse: c indrāya śūṣam arcati //16//

Hymn: 72

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a viśveṣu hi tvā savaneṣu tuñjate samānam ekam vṛṣamaṇyavaḥ pṛthak svaḥ saniṣyavaḥ pṛthak /

Halfverse: c tam tvā nāvam na parṣaṇim śūṣasya dhuri dhīmahi /

Halfverse: e indram na yajñaiś catayanta āyava stomebhir indram āyavaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vi tvā tatasre mithunā avasyavo vrajasya sātā gavyasya niḥsṛjaḥ sakṣanta indra niḥsṛjaḥ /

Halfverse: c yad gavyantā dvā janā sva1r yantā samūhasi /

Halfverse: e āviṣ karikrad vṛṣaṇam sacābhuvam vajram indra sacābhuvam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a uto no asyā uṣaso juṣeta hy a1rkasya bodhi haviṣo havīmabhiḥ svarṣātā havīmabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c yad indra hantave mṛgho vṛṣā vajrim ciketasi /

Halfverse: e ā me asya vedhaso navīyaso manma śrudhi navīyasaḥ //3//

Hymn: 73

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tubhyed imā savanā śūra viśvā tubhyam brahmāṇi vardhanā kṛṇomi /

Halfverse: c tvam nṛbhir havyo viśvadhāsi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nū cin nu te manyamānasya dasmod aśnuvanti mahimānam ugra /

Halfverse: c na vīryam indra te na rādhaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a pra vo mahe mahivṛdhe bharadhvam pracetase pra sumatim kṛṇudhvam /

Halfverse: c viśaḥ pūrvīḥ pra carā carṣaṇiprāḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yadā vajram hiraṇyam id athā ratham harī yamasya vahato vi sūribhiḥ /

Halfverse: c ā tiṣṭhati maghavā sanaśruta indro vājasya dīrghaśravasas patiḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a so cin nu vṛṣṭir yūthyā3 svā sacāṁ indraḥ śmaśrūṇi haritābhi pruṣṇute /

Halfverse: c ava veti sukṣayam sute madhūd id dhūṇoti vāto yathā vanam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yo vācā vivāco mṛdhravācaḥ purū sahasrāśivā jaghāna /

Halfverse: c tattad id asya paumsyam gṛṇīmasi piteva yas taviṣīm vāvṛdhe śavaḥ //6//

Hymn: 74

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yac cid dhi satya somapā anāśastā iva smasi /

Halfverse: c ā tū na indra śamsaya goṣv aśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śiprin vājānām pate śacīvas tava damsanā /

Halfverse: c ā tū na indra śamsaya goṣv aśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ni ṣvāpayā mithūdṛśā sastām abudhyamāne /

Halfverse: c ā tū na indra śamsaya goṣv aśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sasantu tyā arātayo bodhantu śūra rātayaḥ /

Halfverse: c ā tū na indra śamsaya goṣv aśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sam indra gardabham mṛṇa nuvantam papayāmuyā /

Halfverse: c ā tū na indra śamsaya goṣv aśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a patāti kuṇḍṛṇācyā dūram vāto vanād adhi /

Halfverse: c ā tū na indra śamsaya goṣv aśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sarvam parikrośam jahi jambhayā kṛkadāśvam /

Halfverse: c ā tū na indra śamsaya goṣv aśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha //7//

Hymn: 75

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vi tvā tatasre mithunā avasyavo vrajasya sātā gavyasya niḥsṛjaḥ sakṣanta indra niḥsṛjaḥ /

Halfverse: c yad gavyanta dvā janā sva1r yantā samūhasi /

Halfverse: e āviṣ karikrad vṛṣaṇam sacābhuvam vajram indra sacābhuvam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a viduṣ ṭe asya vīryasya pūravaḥ puro yad indra śāradīr avātiraḥ sāsahāno avātiraḥ /

Halfverse: c śāsas tam indra martyam ayajum śavasas pate /

Halfverse: e mahīm amuṣṇāḥ pṛthivīm imā apo mandasāna imā apaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ād it te asya vīryasya carkiran madeṣu vṛṣann uśijo yad āvitha sakhīyato yad āvitha /

Halfverse: c cakartha kāram ebhyaḥ pṛtanāsu pravantave /

Halfverse: e te anyāmanyām nadyam saniṣṇata śravasyantaḥ saniṣṇata //3//

Hymn: 76

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vane na vā yo ny adhāyi cākram chucir vām stomo bhuraṇāv ajīgaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasyed indraḥ purudineṣu hotā nṛṇām narvo nṛtamaḥ kṣapāvān //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pra te asyā uṣasaḥ prāparasyā nṛtau syāma nṛtam asya nṛṇām /

Halfverse: c anu triśokaḥ śatam āvahan nṝn kutsena ratho yo asat sasavān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kas te mada indra rantyo bhūd duro giro abhy ḥ1gro vi dhāva /

Halfverse: c kad vāho arvāg upa mā manīṣā ā tvā śakyām upamam rādho annaiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kad u dyumnam indra tvāvato nṝn kayā dhiyā karase kan na āgan /

Halfverse: c mitro na satya urugāya bhṛtyā anne samasya yad asan manīṣāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a preraya sūro artham na pāram ye asya kāmam janidhā iva gman /

Halfverse: c giraś ca ye te tuvijāta pūrvīr nara indra pratiśikṣanty annaiḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a mātre nu te sumite indra pūrvī dyaur majmanā pṛthivī kāvyena /

Halfverse: c varāya te ghṛtavantaḥ sutāsaḥ svādnan bhavantu pītaye madhūni //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ā madhvo asmā asicann amatram indrāya pūrṇam sa hi satyarādhāḥ /

Halfverse: c sa vāvṛdhe varimann ā pṛthivyā abhi kratvā naryaḥ paumsyaiś ca //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a vy ānaḷ indraḥ pṛtanāḥ svojā āsmai yatante sakhyāya pūrvīḥ /

Halfverse: c ā smā ratham na pṛtanāsu tiṣṭha yam bhadrayā sumatyā codayāse //8//

Hymn: 77

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā satyo yātu maghavāṁ ṛjīṣī dravantv asya haraya upa naḥ /

Halfverse: c tasmā id andhaḥ suṣumā sudakṣam ihābhipitvam karate gṛṇānaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ava sya śūrādhvano nānte ‘smin no adya savane mandadhyai /

Halfverse: c śamsāty uktham uśaneva vedhāś cikituṣe asuryāya manma //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kavir na niṇyam vidathāni sādhan vṛṣā yat sekam vipipāno arcāt /

Halfverse: c diva itthā jījanat sapta kārūn ahnā cic cakrur vayunā gṛṇantaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sva1r yad vedi sudṛśīkam arkair mahi jyotī rurucur yad dha vastoḥ /

Halfverse: c andhā tamāmsi dudhitā vicakṣe nṛbhyaś cakāra nṛtamo abhiṣṭau //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vavakṣa indro amitam ṛjiṣy ḥ1bhe ā paprau rodasī mahitvā /

Halfverse: c ataś cid asya mahimā vi recy abhi yo viśvā bhuvanā babhūva //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a viśvāni śakro naryāṇi vidvān apo rireca sakhibhir nikāmaiḥ /

Halfverse: c aśmānam cid ye bibhidur vacobhir vrajam gomantam uśijo vi vavruḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a apo vṛtram vavrivāmsam parāhan prāvat te vajram pṛthivī sacetāḥ /

Halfverse: c prārṇāmsi samudriyāṇy ainoḥ patir bhavam chavasā śūra dhṛṣṇo //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a apo yad adrim puruhūta dardar āvir bhuvat saramā pūrvyam te /

Halfverse: c sa no netā vājam ā darṣi bhūrim gotrā rujann aṅgirobhir gṛṇānaḥ //8//

Hymn: 78

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tad vo gāya sute sacā puruhūtāya satvane /

Halfverse: c śam yad gave na śākine //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na ghā vasur ni yamate dānam vājasya gomataḥ /

Halfverse: c yat sīm upa śravad giraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kuvitsasya pra hi vrajam gomantam dasyuhā gamat /

Halfverse: c śacībhir apa no varat //3//

Hymn: 79

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indra kratum na ā bhara pitā putrebhyo yathā /

Halfverse: c śikṣā ṇo asmin puruhūta yāmani jīvā jyotir aśīmahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mā no ajñātā vṛjanā durādhyo3 māśivāso ava kramuḥ /

Halfverse: c tvayā vayam pravataḥ śaśvatīr apo ‘ti śūra tarāmasi //2//

Hymn: 80

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indra jyeṣṭham na ā bharaṁ ojiṣṭham papuri śravaḥ /

Halfverse: c yeneme citra vajrahasta rodasī obhe suśipra prāḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvām ugram avase carṣaṇīsaham rājan deveṣu hūmahe /

Halfverse: c viśvā su no vithurā pibdanā vaso ‘mitrān suṣahān kṛdhi //2//

Hymn: 81

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad dyāva indra te śatam śatam bhūmir uta syuḥ /

Halfverse: c na tvā vajrint sahasram sūryā anu na jātam aṣṭa rodasī //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā paprātha mahinā kṛṣṇyā vṛṣan viśvā śaviṣṭha śavasā /

Halfverse: c asmāṁ ava maghavan gomati vraje vajrim citrābhir ūtibhiḥ //2//

Hymn: 82

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad indra yāvatas tvam etāvad aham īśīya /

Halfverse: c stotāram id didhiṣeya radāvaso na pāpatvāya rāsīya //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a śikṣeyam in mahayate divedive rāya ā kuhacidvide /

Halfverse: c nahi tvad anyan maghavan na āpyam vasyo asti pitā cana //2//

Hymn: 83

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indra tridhātu śaraṇam trivarūtham svastimat /

Halfverse: c chardir yacha maghavadbhyaś ca mahyam ca yāvayā didyum ebhyaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ye gavyatā manasā śatrum ādabhur abhipraghnanti dhṛṣṇuyā /

Halfverse: c agha smā no maghavann indra girvaṇas tanūpā antamo bhava //2//

Hymn: 84

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrā yāhi citrabhāno sutā ime tvāyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c aṇvībhis tanā pūtāsaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a indrā yāhi dhiyeṣito viprajutaḥ sutāvataḥ /

Halfverse: c upa brahmāṇi vāghataḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indrā yāhi tūtujāna upa brahmāṇi harivaḥ /

Halfverse: c sute dadhiṣva naś canaḥ //3//

Hymn: 85

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mā cid anyad vi śamsata sakhāyo mā riṣaṇyata /

Halfverse: c indram it stotā vṛṣaṇam sacā sute muhur ukthā ca śamsata //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a avakrakṣiṇam vṛṣabham yathājuram gām na carṣaṇīsaham /

Halfverse: c vidveṣaṇam samvananobhayamkaram mamhiṣṭham ubhayāvinam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yac cid dhi tvā janā ime nānā havanta ūtaye /

Halfverse: c asmākam brahmedam indra bhūtu te ‘hā viśvā ca vardhanam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vi tartūryante maghavan vipaścito ‘ryo vipo janānām /

Halfverse: c upa kramasva pururūpam ā bhara vājam nediṣṭham ūtaye //4//

Hymn: 86

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a brahmaṇā te brahmayujā yunajmi harī sakhāyā sadhamāda āśū /

Halfverse: c sthiram ratham sukham indrādhitiṣṭhan prajānan vidvāṁ upa yāhi somam //1//

Hymn: 87

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a adhvaryavo ‘ruṇam dugdham amśum juhotana vṛṣabhāya kṣitīnām /

Halfverse: c gaurād vedīyāṁ avapānam indro viśvāhed yāti sutasomam ichan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad dadhiṣe pradivi cārv annam divedive pītim id asya vakṣi /

Halfverse: c uta hṛdota manasā juṣāṇa uśann indra prasthitān pāhi somān //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a jajñānaḥ somam sahase papātha pra te mātā mahimānam uvāca /

Halfverse: c endra paprāthorv a1ntarikṣam yudhā devebhyo varivaś cakartha //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad yodhayā mahato manyamānān sākṣāma tān bāhubhiḥ śāśadānān /

Halfverse: c yad vā nṛbhir vṛta indrābhiyudhyās tam tvayājim sauśravasam jayema //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a prendrasya vocam prathamā kṛtāni pra nūtanā maghavā yā cakāra /

Halfverse: c yaded adevīr asahiṣṭa māyā athābhavat kevalaḥ somo asya //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tavedam viśvam abhitaḥ paśavya1m yat paśyasi cakṣasā sūryasya /

Halfverse: c gavām asi gopatir eka indra bhakṣīmahi te prayatasya vasvaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a bṛhaspate yuvam indraś ca vasvo divyasyeśāthe uta pārthivasya /

Halfverse: c dhattam rayim stuvate kīraye cid yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ //7//

Hymn: 88

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yas tastambha sahasā vi jmo antān bṛhaspatis triṣadhastho raveṇa /

Halfverse: c tam pratnāsa ṛṣayo dīdhyānāḥ puro viprā dadhire mandrajihvam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dhunetayaḥ supraketam madanto bṛhaspate abhi ye nas tatasre /

Halfverse: c pṛṣantam sṛpram adabdham ūrvam bṛhaspate rakṣatād asya yonim //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bṛhaspate yā paramā parāvad ata ā te ṛtaspṛśo ni ṣeduḥ /

Halfverse: c tubhyam khātā avatā adridugdhā madhva ścotanty abhito virapśam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatiḥ prathamam jāyamāno maho jyotiṣaḥ parame vyoman /

Halfverse: c saptāsyas tuvijāto raveṇa vi saptaraśmir adhamat tamāmsi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sa suṣṭubhā sa ṛkvatā gaṇena valam ruroja phaligam ravena /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir usriyā havyasūdaḥ kanikradad vāvaśatīr ud ājat //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a evā pitre viśvadevāya vṛṣṇe yajñair vidhema namasā havirbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspate suprajā vīravanto vayam syāma patayo rayīṇām //6//

Hymn: 89

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a asteva su prataram lāyam asyan bhūṣann iva pra bharā stomam asmai /

Halfverse: c vācā viprās tarata vācam aryo ni rāmaya jaritaḥ soma indram //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a dohena gām upa śikṣā sakhāyam pra bodhaya jaritar jāram indram /

Halfverse: c kośam na pūrṇam vasunā nyṛṣṭam ā cyāvaya maghadeyāya śūram //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kim aṅga tvā maghavan bhojam āhuḥ śiśīhi mā śiśayam tvā śṛṇomi /

Halfverse: c apnasvatī mama dhīr astu śakra vasuvidam bhagam indrā bharā naḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tvām janā mamasatyeṣv indra samtasthānā vi hvayante samīke /

Halfverse: c atrā yujam kṛṇute yo haviṣmān nāsunvatā sakhyam vaṣṭi śūraḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a dhanam na spandram bahulam yo asmai tīvrānt somāṁ āsunoti prayasvān /

Halfverse: c tasmai śatrūnt sutukān prātar ahno ni svaṣṭrān yuvati hanti vṛtram //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yasmin vayam dadhimā śamsam indre yaḥ śiśrāya maghavā kāmam asme /

Halfverse: c ārāc cit san bhayatām asya śatrur ny asmai dyumnā janyā namantām //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ārāc chatrum apa bādhasva dūram ugro yaḥ śambaḥ puruhūta tena /

Halfverse: c asme dhehi yavamad gomad indra kṛdhī dhiyam jaritre vājaratnām //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a pra yam antar vṛṣasavāso ajman tīvrāḥ somā bahulāntāsa indram /

Halfverse: c nāha dāmānam maghavā ni yamsan ni sunvate vahati bhūri vāmam //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a uta prahām atidīvā jayati kṛtam iva śvaghnī vi cinoti kāle /

Halfverse: c yo devakāmo na dhanam ruṇaddhi sam it tam rāyaḥ sṛjati svadhābhiḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a gobhiṣ ṭaremāmatim durevām yavena vā kṣudham puruhūta viśve /

Halfverse: c vayam rājasu prathamā dhanāny ariṣṭāso vṛjanībhir jayema //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir naḥ pari pātu paścād utottarasmād adharād aghayoḥ /

Halfverse: c indraḥ purastād uta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyo varīyaḥ kṛṇotu //11//

Hymn: 90

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yo adribhit prathamajā ṛtāvā bṛhaspatir āṅgiraso haviṣmān /

Halfverse: c dvibarhajmā prāgharmasat pitā na ā rodasī vṛṣabho roravīti //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a janāya cid ya īvate u lokam bṛhaspatir devahūtau cakāra /

Halfverse: c ghnan vṛtrāṇi vi puro dardarīti jayam chatrūmr amitrān pṛtsu sāhan //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatiḥ sam ajayad vasūni maho vrajān gomate deva eṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c apaḥ siṣāsant sva1r apratīto bṛhaspatir hanty amitram arkaiḥ //3//

Hymn: 91

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imām dhiyam saptaśīrṣṇīm pitā na ṛtaprajātām bṛhatīm avindat /

Halfverse: c turīyam svij janayad viśvajanyo ‘yāsya uktham indrāya śamsan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ṛtam śamsanta ṛju dīdhyānā divas putrāso asurasya vīrāḥ /

Halfverse: c vipram padam aṅgiraso dadhānā yajñasya dhāma prathamam mananta //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a hamsair iva sakhibhir vāvadadbhir aśmanmayāni nahanā vyasyan /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir abhikanikradad gā uta prāstaud uc ca vidvāṁ agāyat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a avo dvābhyām para ekayā gā guhā tiṣṭhantīr anṛtasya setau /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatis tamasi jyotir ichan ud usrā ākar vi hi tisra āvaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vibhidyā puram śayathem apācīm nis trīṇi sākam udadher akṛntat /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir uṣasam sūryam gām arkam viveda stanayann iva dyauḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indro valam rakṣitāram dughānām kareṇeva vi cakartā raveṇa /

Halfverse: c svedāñjibhir āśiram ichamāno ‘rodayat paṇim ā gā amuṣṇāt //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sa īm satyebhiḥ sakhibhiḥ śucadbhir godhāyasam vi dhanasair adardaḥ /

Halfverse: c brahmaṇas patir vṛṣabhir varāhair gharmasvedebhir draviṇam vy ānaṭ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a te satyena manasā gopatim gā iyānāsa iṣaṇayanta dhībhiḥ /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatir mithoavadyapebhir ud usriyā asṛjata svayugbhiḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a tam vardhayanto matibhiḥ śivābhiḥ simham iva nānadatam sadhasthe /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatim vṛṣaṇam śūrasātau bharebhare anu madema jiṣṇum //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yadā vājam asanad viśvarūpam ā dyām arukṣad uttarāṇi sadma /

Halfverse: c bṛhaspatim vṛṣaṇam vardhayanto nānā santo bibhrato jyotir āsā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a satyam āśiṣam kṛṇutā vayodhai kīrim cid dhy avatha svebhir evaiḥ /

Halfverse: c paścā mṛdho apa bhavantu viśvās tad rodasī śṛṇutam viśvaminve //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a indro mahnā mahato arṇavasya vi mūrdhānam abhinad arbudasya /

Halfverse: c ahann ahim ariṇāt sapta sindhūn devair dyāvāpṛthivī prāvatam naḥ //12//

Hymn: 92

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi pra gopatim girendram arca yathā vide /

Halfverse: c sūtum satyasya satpatim //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā harayaḥ sasṛjrire ‘ruṣīr adhi barhiṣi /

Halfverse: c yatrābhi samnavāmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indrāya gāva āśiram duduhre vajriṇe madhu /

Halfverse: c yat sīm upahvare vidat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ud yad bradhnasya viṣṭapam gṛham indraś ca ganvahi /

Halfverse: c madhvaḥ pītvā sacevahi triḥ sapta sakhyuḥ pade //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a arcata prārcata priyamedhāso arcata /

Halfverse: c arcantu putrakā uta puram na dhṛṣṇv arcata //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ava svarāti gargaro godhā pari saniṣvaṇat /

Halfverse: c piṅgā pari caniṣkadad indrāya brahmodyatam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ā yat patanty enyaḥ sudughā anapasphuraḥ /

Halfverse: c apasphuram gṛbhāyata somam indrāya pātave //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a apād indro apād agnir viśve devā amatsata /

Halfverse: c varuṇa id iha kṣayat tam āpo abhy anūṣata vatsam samśiśvarīr iva //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a sudevo asi varuṇa yasya te sapta sindhavaḥ /

Halfverse: c anukṣaranti kākudam sūryam suṣirām iva //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yo vyatīṁr aphāṇayat suyuktāṁ upa dāśuṣe /

Halfverse: c takvo netā tad id vapur upamā yo amucyata //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a atīd u śakra ohata indro viśvā ati dviṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c bhinat kanīna odanam pacyamānam paro girā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a arbhako na kumārako ‘dhi tiṣṭhann avam ratham /

Halfverse: c sa pakṣan mahiṣam mṛgam pitre mātre vibhukratum //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a ā tū suśipra dampate ratham tiṣṭhā hiraṇyayam /

Halfverse: c adha dyukṣam sacevahi sahasrapādam aruṣam svastigām anehasam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a tam ghem itthā namasvina upa svarājam āsate /

Halfverse: c artham cid asya sudhitam yad etave āvartayanti dāvane //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a anu pratnasyaukasaḥ priyamedhāsa eṣām /

Halfverse: c pūrvām anu prayatim vṛktabarhiṣo hitaprayasa āśata //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yo rājā carṣaṇīnām yātā rathebhir adhriguḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvāsām tarutā pṛtanānām jyeṣṭho yo vṛtrahā gṛṇe //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a indram tam śumbha puruhanmann avase yasya dvitā vidhartari /

Halfverse: c hastāya vajraḥ prati dhāyi darśato maho dive na sūryaḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a nakiṣ ṭam karmaṇā naśad yaś cakāra sadāvṛdham /

Halfverse: c indram na yajñair viśvagūrtam ṛbhvasam adhṛṣṭam dhṛṣṇvojasam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a aṣālham ugram pṛtanāsu sāsahim yasmin mahīr urujrayaḥ /

Halfverse: c sam dhenavo jāyamāne anonavur dyāvaḥ kṣāmo anonavuḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a yad dyāva indra te śatam śatam bhūmīr uta syuḥ /

Halfverse: c na tvā vajrint sahasram sūryā anu na jātam aṣṭa rodasī //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a ā paprātha mahinā vṛṣṇyā vṛṣan viśvā śaviṣṭha śavasā /

Halfverse: c asmāṁ ava maghavan gomati vraje vajrim citrābhir ūtibhiḥ //21//

Hymn: 93

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ut tvā mandantu stomāḥ kṛṇuṣva rādho adrivaḥ /

Halfverse: c ava brahmadviṣo jahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a padā paṇīṁr arādhaso ni bādhasva mahāṁ asi /

Halfverse: c nahi tvā kaś cana prati //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvam īśiṣe sutānām indra tvam asutānām /

Halfverse: c tvam rājā janānām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a īṅkhayantīr apasyuva indram jātam upāsate /

Halfverse: c bhejānāsaḥ suvīryam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a tvam indra balād adhi sahaso jāta ojasaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvam vṛṣan vṛṣed asi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tvam indrāsi vṛtrahā vy a1ntarikṣam atiraḥ /

Halfverse: c ud dyām astabhnā ojasā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tvam indra sajoṣasam arkam bibharṣi bāhvoḥ /

Halfverse: c vajram śiśāna ojasā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tvam indrābhibhur asi viśvā jātāny ojasā /

Halfverse: c sa viśvā bhuva ābhavah //8//

Hymn: 94

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā yātv indraḥ svapatir madāya yo dharmaṇā tūtujānas tuviṣmān /

Halfverse: c pratvakṣāṇo ati viśvā sahāmsy apāreṇa mahatā vṛṣṇyena //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a suṣṭhāmā rathaḥ suyamā harī te mimyakṣa vajro nṛpate gabhastau /

Halfverse: c śībham rājan supathā yāhy arvāṅ vardhāma te papuso vṛṣṇyāni //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a endravāho nṛpatim vajrabāhum ugram ugrāsas taviṣāsa enam /

Halfverse: c pratvakṣasam vṛṣabham satyaśuṣmam em asmatrā sadhamādo vahantu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a evā patim droṇasācam sacetasam ūrja skambham dharuṇa ā vṛṣāyase /

Halfverse: c ojaḥ kṛṣva sam gṛbhāya tve apy aso yathā kenipānām ino vṛdhe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a gamann asme vasūny ā hi śamsiṣam svāśiṣam bharam ā yāhi sominaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvam īśiṣe sāsminn ā satsi barhiṣy anādhṛṣyā tava pātrāṇi dharmaṇā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pṛthak prāyan prathamā devahūtayo ‘kṛṇvata śravasyāni duṣṭarā /

Halfverse: c na ye śekur yajñiyām nāvam āruham irmaiva te ny aviśanta kepayaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a evaivāpāg apare santu dūdhyo ‘śvā yeṣām duryuga āyuyujre /

Halfverse: c itthā ye prāg upare santi dāvane purūṇi yatra vayunāni bhojanā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a girīṁr ajrān rejamānāṁ adhārayad dyauḥ krandad antarikṣāṇi kopayat /

Halfverse: c samīcīne dhiṣaṇe vi ṣkabhāyati vṛṣṇaḥ pītvā mada ukthāni śamsati //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a imam bibharmi sukṛtam te aṅkuśam yenārujāsi maghavam chaphārujaḥ /

Halfverse: c asmint su te savane astv oktyam suta iṣṭau maghavan bodhy ābhagaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a gobhiṣ ṭaremāmatim durevām yavena kṣudham puruhūta viśvām /

Halfverse: c vayam rājabhiḥ prathamā dhanāny asmākena vṛjanenā jayema //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a bṛhaspatir naḥ pari pātu paścād utottarasmād adharād aghayoḥ /

Halfverse: c indraḥ purastād uta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyo varivaḥ kṛṇotu //11//

Hymn: 95

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a trikadrukeṣu mahiṣo yavāśiram tuviśuṣmas tṛpat somam apibad viṣṇunā sutam yathāvaśat /

Halfverse: c sa īm mamāda mahi karma kartave mahām urum sainam saścad devo devam satyam indram satya induḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pro ṣv asmai puroratham indrāya śūṣam arcata /

Halfverse: c abhīke cid u lokakṛt samge samatsu vṛtrahāsmākam bodhi coditā nabhantām anyakeṣām jyākā adhi dhanvasu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvam sindhūṁr avāsṛjo ‘dharāco ahann ahim /

Halfverse: c aśatrur indra jajñiṣe viśvam puṣyasi vāryam tam tvā pari ṣvajāmahe nabhantām anyakeṣām jyākā adhi dhanvasu //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vi ṣu viśvā arātayo ‘ryo naśanta no dhiyaḥ /

Halfverse: c astāsi śatrave vadham yo na indra jighāmsati yā te rātir dadir vasu /

Halfverse: e nabhantām anyakeṣām jyakā adhi dhanvasu //4//

Hymn: 96

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tīvrasyābhivayaso asya pāhi sarvarathā vi harī iha muñca /

Halfverse: c indra mā tvā yajamānāso anye ni rīraman tubhyam ime sutāsaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tubhyam sutās tubhyam u sotvāsas tvām giraḥ śvātryā ā hvayanti /

Halfverse: c indredam adya savanam juṣāṇo viśvasya vidvāṁ iha pāhi somam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ya uśatā manasā somam asmai sarvahṛdā devakāmaḥ sunoti /

Halfverse: c na gā indras tasya parā dadāti praśastam ic cārum asmai kṛṇoti //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a anuspaṣṭo bhavaty eṣo asya yo asmai revān na sunoti somam /

Halfverse: c nir aratnau maghavā tam dadhāti brahmadviṣo hanty anānudiṣṭaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a aśvāyanto gavyanto vājayanto havāmahe tvopagantavā u /

Halfverse: c ābhūṣantas te sumatau navāyām vayam indra tvā śunam huvema //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a muñcāmi tvā haviṣā jīvanāya kam ajñātayakṣmād uta rājayakṣmāt /

Halfverse: c grāhir jagrāha yady etad tasyā indrāgnī pra mumuktam enam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yadi kṣitāyur yadi vā pareto yadi mṛtyor antikam nīta eva /

Halfverse: c tam ā harāmi nirṛter upasthād aspārśam enam śataśāradāya //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a sahasrākṣeṇa śatavīryeṇa śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣam enam /

Halfverse: c indro yathainam śarado nayāty ati viśvasya duritasya pāram //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a śatam jīva śarado vardhamānaḥ śatam hemantān chatam u vasantān /

Halfverse: c śatam ta indro agniḥ savitā bṛhaspatiḥ śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣam enam //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a āhārṣam avidam tvā punar āgāḥ punarṇavaḥ /

Halfverse: c sarvāṅga sarvam te cakṣuḥ sarvam āyuś ca te ‘vidam //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a brahmaṇāgniḥ samvidāno rakṣohā bādhatām itaḥ /

Halfverse: c amīvā yas te garbham durṇāmā yonim āśaye //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yas te garbham amīvā durṇāmā yonim āśaye /

Halfverse: c agniṣ ṭam brahmaṇā saha niṣ kravyādam anīnaśat //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a yas te hanti patayantam niṣatsnum yaḥ sarīsṛpam /

Halfverse: c jātam yas te jighāmsati tam ito nāśayāmasi //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yas ta ūrū viharaty antarā dampatī śaye /

Halfverse: c yonim yo antar ārelhi tam ito nāśayāmasi //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a yas tvā bhrātā patir bhūtvā jāro bhūtvā nipadyate /

Halfverse: c prajām yas te jighāmsati tam ito nāśayāmasi //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yas tvā svapnena tamasā mohayitvā nipadyate /

Halfverse: c prajām yas te jighāmsati tam ito nāśayāmasi //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a akṣībhyām te nāsikābhyām karṇābhyām chubukād adhi /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam śīrṣaṇyam mastiṣkāj jihvāyā vi vṛhāmi te //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a grīvābhyas ta uṣṇihābhyaḥ kīkasābhyo anūkyāt /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam doṣaṇya1m amsābhyām bāhubhyām vi vṛhāmi te //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a hṛdayāt te pari klomno halīkṣṇāt pārśvābhyām /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam matasnābhyām plīhno yaknas te vi vṛhāmasi //19//

Verse: 20 {19}

Halfverse: a āntrebhyas te gudābhyo vaniṣṭhor udarād adhi /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam kukṣibhyām plāśer nābhyā vi vṛhāmi te //20//

Verse: 21 {20}

Halfverse: a ūrubhyām te aṣṭhīvadbhyām pārṣṇibhyām prapadābhyām /

Halfverse: c yakṣmam bhasadya1m śroṇibhyām bhāsadam bhāmsaso vi vṛhāmi te //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a mehanād vanamkaraṇāl lomabhyas te nakhebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c yaksmam sarvasmād ātmanas tam idam vi vṛhāmi te //22//

Halfverse: e {22} asthibhyas te majjabhyaḥ snāvabhyo dhamanibhyaḥ

Halfverse: f yaksmam pāṇibhyām aṅgulibhyo nakhebhyo vi vṛhāmi te //22//

Verse: 23 {22}

Halfverse: a aṅgeaṅge lomnilomni yas te parvaṇiparvaṇi /

Halfverse: c yakṣam tvacasyam te vayam kaśyapasya vībarheṇa viṣvañcam vi vṛhāmasi //23//

Verse: 24 {23}

Halfverse: a apehi manasas pate ‘pa kāma paraś cara /

Halfverse: c paro nirṛtyā ā cakṣva bahudhā jīvato manaḥ //24//

Hymn: 97

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vayam enam idā hyopīpemeha vajriṇam /

Halfverse: c tasmā u adya samanā sutam bharā nūnam bhūṣata śrute //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vṛkaś cid asya vāraṇa urāmathir ā vayuneṣu bhūṣati /

Halfverse: c semam naḥ stomam jujuṣāṇa ā gahīndra pra citrayā dhiyā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kad u nv asyākṛtam indrasyāsti paumsyam /

Halfverse: c keno nu kam śromatena na śuśruve januṣaḥ pari vṛtrahā //3//

Hymn: 98

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvām id dhi havāmahe sātā vājasya kāravaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvām vṛtreṣv indra satpatim naras tvām kāṣṭhāsv arvataḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a sa tvam naś citra vajrahasta dhṛṣṇuyā maha stavāno adrivaḥ /

Halfverse: c gām aśvam rathyam indra sam kira satrā vājam na jigyuṣe //2//

Hymn: 99

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi tvā pūrvapītaya indra stomebhir āyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c samīcīnāsa ṛbhavaḥ sam asvaran rudrā gṛṇanta pūrvyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a asyed indro vāvṛdhe vṛṣṇyam śavo made sutasya viṣṇavi /

Halfverse: c adyā tam asya mahimānam āyavo ‘nu ṣṭuvanti pūrvathā //2//

Hymn: 100

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a adhā hīndra girvaṇa upa tvā kāmān mahaḥ sasṛjmahe /

Halfverse: c udeva yanta udabhiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vār ṇa tvā yavyābhir vardhanti śūra brahmāṇi /

Halfverse: c vāvṛdhvāmsam cid adrivo divedive //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yuñjanti harī iṣirasya gāthayorau ratha uruyuge /

Halfverse: c indravāhā vacoyujā //3//

Hymn: 101

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnim dūtam vṛṇīmahe hotāram viśvavedasam /

Halfverse: c asya yajñasya sukratum //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agnimagnim havīmabhiḥ sadā havanta viśpatim /

Halfverse: c havyavāham purupriyam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a agne devāṁ ihā vaha jajñāno vṛktabarhiṣe /

Halfverse: c asi hotā na īḍyaḥ //3//

Hymn: 102

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a īlenyo namasyas tiras tamāmsi darśataḥ /

Halfverse: c sam agnir idhyate vṛṣā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a vṛṣo agniḥ sam idhyate ‘śvo na devavāhanaḥ /

Halfverse: c tam haviṣmantaḥ īlate //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vṛṣaṇam tvā vayam vṛṣan vṛṣaṇaḥ sam idhīmahi /

Halfverse: c agne dīdyatam bṛhat //3//

Hymn: 103

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a agnim īliṣvāvase gāthābhiḥ śīraśociṣam /

Halfverse: c agnim rāye purumīlha śrutam naro ‘gnim sudītaye chardiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a agna ā yāhy agnibhir hotāram tvā vṛṇīmahe /

Halfverse: c ā tvām anaktu prayatā haviṣmatī yajiṣṭham barhir āsade //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a acha hi tvā sahasaḥ sūno aṅgiraḥ srucaś caranty adhvare /

Halfverse: c ūrjo napātam ghṛtakeśam īmahe ‘gnim yajñeṣu pūrvyam //3//

Hymn: 104

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a imā u tvā purūvaso giro vardhantu yā mama /

Halfverse: c pāvakavarṇāḥ śucayo vipaścito ‘bhi stomair anūṣata //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ayam sahasram ṛṣibhiḥ sahaskṛtaḥ samudra iva paprathe /

Halfverse: c satyaḥ so asya mahimā gṛne śavo yajñeṣu viprarājye //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ā no viśvāsu havya indraḥ samatsu bhūṣatu /

Halfverse: c upa brahmāṇi savanāni vṛtrahā paramajyā ṛcīṣamaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tvam dātā prathamo rāghasām asy asi satya īśānakṛt /

Halfverse: c tuvidyumnasya yujyā vṛṇīmahe putrasya śavaso mahaḥ //4//

Hymn: 105

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvam indra pratūrtiṣv abhi viśvā asi spṛdhaḥ /

Halfverse: c aśastihā janitā viśvatūr asi tvam tūrya taruṣyataḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a anu te śuṣmam turayantam īyatuḥ kṣoṇī śiśum na mātarā /

Halfverse: c viśvās te spṛdhaḥ śnathayanta manyave vṛtram yad indra tūrvasi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ita ūtī vo ajaram prahetāram aprahitam /

Halfverse: c āśum jetāram hetāram rathītamam atūrtam tugryāvṛdham //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yo rājā carṣaṇīnām yātā rathebhir adhriguḥ /

Halfverse: c viśvāsām tarutā pṛtanānām jyeṣṭho yo vṛtrahā gṛṇe //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indram tam śumbha puruhanmann avase yasya dvitā vidhartari /

Halfverse: c hastāya vajraḥ prati dhāyi darśato maho dive na sūryaḥ //5//

Hymn: 106

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tava tyad indriyam bṛhat tava śuṣmam uta kratum /

Halfverse: c vajram śiśāti dhiṣaṇā vareṇyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tava dyaur indra paumsyam pṛthivī vardhati śravaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvām āpaḥ parvatāsaś ca hinvire //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvām viṣṇur bṛhan kṣayo mitro gṛṇāti varuṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c tvām śardho madaty anu mārutam //3//

Hymn: 107

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a sam asya manyave viśo viśvā namanta kuṣṭayaḥ /

Halfverse: c samudrāyeva sindhavaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ojas tad asya titviṣa ubhe yat samavartayat /

Halfverse: c indraś carmeva rodasī //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a vi cid vṛtrasya dodhato vajreṇa śataparvaṇā /

Halfverse: c śiro bibhed vṛṣṇinā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tad id āsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭham yato jajña ugras tveṣanṛmṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c sadyo jajñāno ni riṇāti śatrūn anu yad enam madanti viśva ūmāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a vāvṛdhānaḥ śavasā bhūryojāḥ śatrur dāsāya bhiyasam dadhāti /

Halfverse: c avyanac ca vyanac ca sasni sam te navanta prabhṛtā madeṣu //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a tve kratum api pṛñcanti bhūri dvir yad ete trir bhavanty ūmāḥ /

Halfverse: c svādoḥ svādīyaḥ svādunā sṛjā sam adaḥ su madhu madhunābhi yodhīḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yadi cin nu tvā dhanā jayantam raṇeraṇe anumadanti viprāḥ /

Halfverse: c ojīyaḥ śuṣmint sthiram ā tanuṣva mā tvā dabhan durevāsaḥ kaśokāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a tvayā vayam śāśadmahe raṇeṣu prapaśyanto yudhenyāni bhūri /

Halfverse: c codayāmi ta āyudhā vacobhiḥ sam te śiśāmi brahmaṇā vayāmsi //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ni tad dadhiṣe ‘vare pare ca yasminn āvithāvasā duroṇe /

Halfverse: c ā sthāpayata mātaram jigatnum ata invata karvarāṇi bhūri //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a stuṣva varṣman puruvartmānam sam ṛbhvāṇam inatamam āptam āptyānām /

Halfverse: c ā darśati śavasā bhūryojāḥ pra sakṣati pratimānam pṛthivyāḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a imā brahma bṛhaddivaḥ kṛṇavad indrāya śūṣam agniyaḥ svarṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c maho gotrasya kṣayati svarājā turaś cid viśvam arṇavat tapasvān //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a evā mahān bṛhaddivo atharvāvocat svām tanva1m indram eva /

Halfverse: c svasārau mātaribhvarī aripre hinvanti caine śavasā vardhayanti ca //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a citram devānām ketur anīkam jyotiṣmān pradiśaḥ sūrya udyan /

Halfverse: c divākaro ‘ti dyumnais tamāmsi viśvātārīd duritāni śukraḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a citram devānām ud agād anīkam cakṣur mitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ /

Halfverse: c āprād dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam sūrya ātmā jagatas tasthuṣaś ca //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a sūryo devīm uṣasam rocamānām maryo na yoṣām abhy ṝti paścāt /

Halfverse: c yatrā naro devayanto yugāni vitanvate prati bhadrāya bhadram //15//

Hymn: 108

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tvam na indrā bharaṁ ojo nṛmṇam śatakrato vicarṣaṇe /

Halfverse: c ā vīram pṛtanāṣaham //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tvam hi naḥ pitā vaso tvam mātā śatakrato babhūvitha /

Halfverse: c adhā te sumnam īmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tvām śuṣmin puruhūta vājayantam upa bruve śatakrato /

Halfverse: c sa no rāsva suvīryam //3//

Hymn: 109

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a svādor itthā viṣuvato madhvaḥ pibanti gauryaḥ /

Halfverse: c yā indreṇa sayāvarīr vṛṣṇā madanti śobhase vasvīr anu svarājyam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tā asya pṛśanāyuvaḥ somam śrīṇanti pṛśnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c priyā indrasya dhenavo vajram hinvanti sāyakam vasvīr anu svarājyam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tā asya namasā sahaḥ saparyanti pracetasaḥ /

Halfverse: c vratāny asya saścire purūṇi pūrvacittaye vasvīr anu svarājyam //3//

Hymn: 110

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a indrāya madūne sutam pari ṣṭobhantu no giraḥ /

Halfverse: c arkam arcantu kāravaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yasmin viśvā adhi śriyo raṇanti sapta samsadaḥ /

Halfverse: c indram sute havāmahe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a trikadrukeṣu cetanam devāso yajñam atnata /

Halfverse: c tam id vardhantu no giraḥ //3//

Hymn: 111

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yat somam indra viṣṇavi yad vā gha trita āptye /

Halfverse: c yad vā marutsu mandase sam indubhiḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad vā śakra parāvati samudre adhi mandase /

Halfverse: c asmākam it sute raṇā sam indubhiḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad vāsi sunvato vṛdho yajamānasya satpate /

Halfverse: c ukthe vā yasya raṇyasi sam indubhiḥ //3//

Hymn: 112

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad adya kac ca vṛtrahann udagā abhi sūrya /

Halfverse: c sarvam tad indra te vaśe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad vā pravṛddha satpate na marā iti manyase /

Halfverse: c uto tat satyam it tava //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye somāsaḥ parāvati ye arvāvati sunvire /

Halfverse: c sarvāms tāṁ indra gachasi //3//

Hymn: 113

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ubhayam śṛṇavac ca na indro arvāg idam vacaḥ /

Halfverse: c satrācyā maghavā somapītaye dhiyā śaviṣṭha ā gamat //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tam hi svarājam vṛṣabham tam ojase dhiṣaṇe niṣṭatakṣatuḥ /

Halfverse: c utopamānām prathamo ni ṣīdasi somakāmam hi te manaḥ //2//

Hymn: 114

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhrātṛvyo’anā tvam anāpir indra januṣā sanād asi /

Halfverse: c yudhed āpitvam ichase //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a nakī revantam sakhyāya vindase pīyanti te surāśvaḥ /

Halfverse: c yadā kṛṇoṣi nadanum sam ūhasy ād it piteva hūyase //2//

Hymn: 115

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a aham id dhi pituṣ pari medhām ṛtasya jagrabha /

Halfverse: c aham sūrya ivājani //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a aham pratnena manmanā giraḥ śumbhāmi kaṇvavat /

Halfverse: c yenendraḥ śuṣmam id dadhe //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye tvām indra na tuṣṭuvur ṛṣayo ye ca tuṣṭuvuḥ /

Halfverse: c mamed vardhasva suṣṭutaḥ //3//

Hymn: 116

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mā bhūma niṣṭyā ivendra tvad araṇā iva /

Halfverse: c vanāni ni prajahitāny adrivo duroṣāso amanmahi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a amanmahīd anāśavo ‘nugrāsaś ca vṛtrahan /

Halfverse: c sukṛt su te mahatā śūra rādhasānu stomam mudīmahi //2//

Hymn: 117

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a pibā somam indra mandatu tvā yam te suṣāva haryaśvādriḥ /

Halfverse: c sotur bāhubhyām suyato nārvā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yas te mado yujas cārur asti yena vṛtrāṇi haryaśva hamsi /

Halfverse: c sa tvām indra prabhūvaso mamattu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a bodhā su me maghavan vācam emām yām te vasiṣṭho arcati praśastim /

Halfverse: c imā brahma sadhamāde juṣasva //3//

Hymn: 118

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a śagdhy ū ṣu śacīpata indra viśvābhir ūtibhiḥ /

Halfverse: c bhagam na hi tvā yaśasam vasuvidam anu śūra carāmasi //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pauro aśvasya purukṛd gavām asy utso deva hiraṇyayaḥ /

Halfverse: c nakir hi dānam parimardhiṣat tve yadyad yāmi tad ā bhara //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a indram id devatātaye indram prayaty adhvare /

Halfverse: c indram samīke vanino havāmaha indram dhanasya sātaye //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a indro mahnā rodasī paprathac chava indraḥ sūryam arocayat /

Halfverse: c indre ha viśvā bhuvanāni yemira indre suvānāsa indavaḥ //4//

Hymn: 119

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a astāvi manma pūrvyam brahmendrāya vocata /

Halfverse: c pūrvīr ṛtasya bṛhatīr anūṣata stotur meghā asṛkṣata //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a turaṇyavo madhumantam ghṛtaścutam viprāso arkam ānṛcuḥ /

Halfverse: c asme rayiḥ paprathe vṛṣṇyam śavo ‘sme suvānāsa indavaḥ //2//

Hymn: 120

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad indra prāg apāg udaṅ nyag vā hūyase nṛbhiḥ /

Halfverse: c simā purū nṛṣūto asy ānave ‘si praśardha turvaśe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad vā rume ruśame śyāvake kṛpa indra mādayase sacā /

Halfverse: c kaṇvāsas tvā brahmabhi stomavāhasa indrā yachanty ā gahi //2//

Hymn: 121

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhi tvā śūra nonumo ‘dugdhā iva dhenavaḥ /

Halfverse: c īśānam asya jagataḥ svardṛśam īśānam indra tasthuṣaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a na tvāvāṁ anyo divyo na pārthivo na jāto na janiṣyate /

Halfverse: c aśvāyanto maghavann indra vājino gavyantas tvā havāmahe //2//

Hymn: 122

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a revatīr naḥ sadhamāda indre santu tuvivājāḥ /

Halfverse: c kṣumanto yābhir madema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā gha tvāvān tmanāpta stotṛbhyo dhṛṣṇav iyānaḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛṇor akṣam na cakrayoḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ā yad duvaḥ śatakratav ā kāmam jaritṝṇām /

Halfverse: c ṛṇor akṣam na śacībhiḥ //3//

Hymn: 123

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tat sūryasya devatvam tan mahitvam madhyā kartor vitatam sam jabhāra /

Halfverse: c yaded ayukta haritaḥ sadhasthād ād rātrī vāsas tanute simasmai //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tan mitrasya varuṇasyābhicakṣe sūryo rūpam kṛṇute dyor upasthe /

Halfverse: c anantam anyad ruśad asya prājaḥ kṛṣṇam anyad dharitaḥ sam bharanti //2//

Hymn: 124

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a kayā naś citra ā bhuvad ūtī sadāvṛdhaḥ sakhā /

Halfverse: c kayā śaciṣṭhayā vṛtā //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kas tvā satyo madānām mamhiṣṭho matsad andhasaḥ /

Halfverse: c dṛlhā cid āruje vasu //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a abhī ṣu naḥ sakhīnām avitā jaritṝṇām /

Halfverse: c śatam bhavāsy ūtibhiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a imā nu kam bhuvanā sīṣadhāmendraś ca viśve ca devāḥ /

Halfverse: c yajñam ca nas tanvam ca prajām cādityair indraḥ saha cīkḷpāti //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ādityair indraḥ sagaṇo marudbhir asmākam bhūtv avitā tanūnām /

Halfverse: c hatvāya devā asurān yad āyan devā devatvam abhirakṣamāṇāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pratyañcam arkam anayam chacībhir ād it svadhām iṣirām pary apaśyan /

Halfverse: c ayā vājam devahitam sanema madema śatahimāḥ suvīrāḥ //6//

Hymn: 125

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a apendra prāco maghavann amitrān apāpāco abhibhūte nudasva /

Halfverse: c apodīco apa śūrādharāca urau yathā tava śarman madema //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kuvid aṅga yavamanto yavam cid yathā dānty anupūrvam viyūya /

Halfverse: c ihehaiṣām kṛṇuhi bhojanāni ye barhiṣo namovṛktim na jagmuḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nahi sthūry ṛtuthā yātam asti nota śravo vivide samgameṣu /

Halfverse: c gavyanta indram sakhyāya viprā aśvāyanto vṛṣaṇam vājayantaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yuvam surāmam aśvinā namucāv āsure sacā /

Halfverse: c vipipānā śubhas patī indram karmasv āvatam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a putram iva pitarāv aśvinobhendrāvathuḥ kāvyair damsanābhiḥ /

Halfverse: c yat surāmam vy apibaḥ śacībhiḥ sarasvatī tvā maghavann abhiṣṇak //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a indraḥ sutrāmā svavāṁ avobhiḥ sumṛḍīko bhavatu viśvavedāḥ /

Halfverse: c bādhatām dveṣo abhayam naḥ kṛṇotu suvīryasya patayaḥ syāma //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a sa sutrāmā svavāṁ indro asmad ārāc cid dveṣaḥ sanutar yuyotu /

Halfverse: c tasya vayam sumatau yajñiyasyāpi bhadre saumanase syāma //7//

Hymn: 126

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vi hi sotor asṛkṣata nendram devam amamsata /

Halfverse: c yatrāmadad vṛṣākapir aryaḥ puṣṭeṣu matsakhā viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a parā hīndra dhāvasi vṛṣākaper ati vyathiḥ /

Halfverse: c no aha pra vindasy anyatra somapītaye viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kim ayam tvām vṛṣākapiś cakāra harito mṛgaḥ /

Halfverse: c yasmā irasyasīd u nv a1ryo vā puṣṭimad vasu viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yam imam tvam vṛṣākapim priyam indrābhirakṣasi /

Halfverse: c śvā nv asya jambhiṣad api karṇe varāhayur viśvasmād indra uttarah //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a priyā taṣṭāni me kapir vyaktā vy adūduṣat /

Halfverse: c śiro nv asya rāviṣam na sugam duṣkṛte bhuvam viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a na mat strī subhasattarā na suyāśutarā bhuvat /

Halfverse: c na mat praticyavīyasī na sakthy udyamīyasī viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a uve amba sulābhike yathevāṅgam bhaviṣyati /

Halfverse: c bhasan me amba sakthi me śiro me vīva hṛṣyati viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a kim subāho svaṅgure pṛthuṣṭo pṛthujāghane /

Halfverse: c kim śūrapatni nas tvam abhy amīṣi vṛṣākapim viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a avīrām iva mām ayam śarārur abhi manyate /

Halfverse: c utāham asmi vīriṇīndrapatnī marutsakhā viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a samhotram sma purā nārī samanam vāva gachati /

Halfverse: c vedhā ṛtasya vīriṇīndrapatnī mahīyate viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a indrāṇīm āsu nāriṣu subhagām aham aśravam /

Halfverse: c nahy asyā aparam cana jarasā marate patir viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a nāham indrāṇi rāraṇa sakhyur vṛṣākaper ṛte /

Halfverse: c yasyedam apyam haviḥ priyam deveṣu gachati viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a vṛsākapāyi revati suputra ād u susnuṣe /

Halfverse: c ghasat ta indra ukṣaṇaḥ priyam kācitkaram havir viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a ukṣṇo hi me pañcadaśa sākam pacanti vimsatim /

Halfverse: c utāham admi pīva id ubhā kukṣī pṛṇanti me viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a vṛṣabho na tigmaśṛṅgo ‘ntar yūtheṣu roruvat /

Halfverse: c manthas ta indra śam hṛde yam te sunoti bhāvayur viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a na seśe yasya rambate ‘ntarā sakthyā3 kapṛt /

Halfverse: c sedīśe yasya romaśam niṣeduṣo vijṛmbhate viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a na seśe yasya romaśam niṣeduṣo vijṛmbhate /

Halfverse: c sedīśe yasya rambate ‘ntarā sakthyā3 kapṛt viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a ayam indra vṛṣākapiḥ parasvantam hatam vidat /

Halfverse: c asim sūnām navam carum ād edhasyāna ācitam viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a ayam emi vicākaśad vicinvan dāsam āryam /

Halfverse: c pibāmi pākasutvano ‘bhi dhīram acākaśam viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a dhanva ca yat kṛntatram ca kati svit tā vi yojanā /

Halfverse: c nedīyaso vṛṣākape ‘stam ehi gṛhāṁ upa viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //20//

Verse: 21

Halfverse: a punar ehi vṛṣākape suvitā kalpayāvahai /

Halfverse: c ya eṣa svapnanamśano ‘stam eṣi pathā punar viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //21//

Verse: 22

Halfverse: a yad udañco vṛṣākape gṛham indrājagantana /

Halfverse: c kva1 sya pulvagho mṛgaḥ kam agam janayopano viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //22//

Verse: 23

Halfverse: a parśur ha nāma mānavī sākam sasūva vimśatim /

Halfverse: c bhadram bhala tyasyā abhūd yasyā udaram āmayad viśvasmād indra uttaraḥ //23//

Hymn: 127

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a idam janā upa śruta narāśamsa staviṣyate /

Halfverse: c ṣaṣṭim sahasrā navatim ca kaurama ā ruśameṣu dadmahe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a uṣṭrā yasya pravāhaṇo vadhūmanto dvirdaśa /

Halfverse: c varṣmā rathasya ni jihīḍate diva īṣamāṇā upaspṛśaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a eṣa iṣāya māmahe śatam niṣkān daśa srajaḥ /

Halfverse: c trīṇi śatānyarvatām sahasrā daśa gonām //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vacyasva rebha vacyasva vṛkṣe na pakve śakunaḥ /

Halfverse: c naṣṭe jihvā carcarīti kṣuro na bhurijoriva //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pra rebhāso manīṣā vṛṣā gāva iverate /

Halfverse: c amotaputrakā eṣāmamota gā ivāsate //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a pra rebha dhīm bharasva govidam vasuvidam /

Halfverse: c devatremām vācam srīṇīhīṣurnāvīrastāram //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a rājño viśvajanīnasya yo devomartyāṁ ati /

Halfverse: c vaiśvānarasya suṣṭutimā sunotā parikṣitaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a parichinnaḥ kṣemam akarot tama āsanamācaran /

Halfverse: c kulāyan kṛṇvan kauravyaḥ patir vadati jāyayā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a katarat ta ā harāṇi dadhi manthām pari śrutam /

Halfverse: c jāyāh patim vi pṛchati rāṣṭre rājñaḥ parikṣitaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a abhīvasvaḥ pra jihīte yavaḥ pakvaḥ patho bilam /

Halfverse: c janaḥ sa bhadram edhati rāṣṭre rājñaḥ parikṣitah //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a indraḥ kārum abūbudhad uttiṣṭha vi carā janam /

Halfverse: c mamedugrasya carkṛdhi sarva it te pṛṇādariḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a iha gāvaḥ pra jāyadhvam ihāśvā iha pūruṣāḥ /

Halfverse: c iho sahasradakṣiṇopi pūṣā ni ṣīdati //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a nemā indra gāvo riṣan mo āsām gopa rīriṣat /

Halfverse: c māsām amitrayurjana indra mā stena īśata //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a upa no na ramasi sūktena vacasā vayam bhadreṇa vacasā vayam /

Halfverse: c vanād adhidhvano giro na riṣyema kadā cana //14//

Hymn: 128

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yaḥ sabheyo vidathyaḥ sutvā yajvātha pūruṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c sūryam cāmū riśādasas tad devāḥ prāg akalpayan //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yo jāmyā aprathayas tad yat sakhāyam dudhūrṣati /

Halfverse: c jyeṣṭho yad apracetās tad āhur adharāg iti //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad bhadrasya puruṣasya putro bhavati dādhṛṣiḥ /

Halfverse: c tad vipro abravīd u tad gandharvaḥ kāmyam vacaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yaśca paṇi raghujiṣṭhyo yaśca devāṁ adāśuriḥ /

Halfverse: c dhīrāṇām śaśvatām aham tad apāg iti śuśruma //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ye ca devā ayajantātho ye ca parādadiḥ /

Halfverse: c sūryo divam iva gatvāya maghavā no vi rapśate //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yo’nāktākṣo anabhyakto amaṇivo ahiraṇyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c abrahmā brahmaṇaḥ putrastotā kalpeṣu sammitā //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ya āktākṣaḥ subhyaktaḥ sumaṇiḥ suhiraṇyavaḥ /

Halfverse: c subrahmā brahmaṇaḥ putrastotā kalpeṣu sammitā //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a aprapāṇā ca veśantā revāṁ apratidiśyayaḥ /

Halfverse: c ayabhyā kanyā kalyāṇī totā kalpeṣu sammitā //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a suprapāṇā ca veśantā revānt supratidiśyayaḥ /

Halfverse: c suyabhyā kanyā kalyāṇī totā kalpeṣu sammitā //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a parivṛktā ca mahiṣī svastyā ca yudhim gamaḥ /

Halfverse: c anāśuraścāyāmī totā kalpeṣu sammitā //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a vāvātā ca mahiṣī svastyā ca yudhim gamaḥ /

Halfverse: c śvāśuraś cāyāmī totā kalpeṣu sammitā //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a yad indrādo dāśarājñe mānuṣam vi gāhathāḥ /

Halfverse: c virūpaḥ sarvasmā āsīt saha yakṣāya kalpate //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a tvam vṛṣākṣum maghavann amram maryākaro raviḥ /

Halfverse: c tvam rauhiṇam vyāsyo vi vṛtrasyābhinac chiraḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a yaḥ parvatān vyadadhād yo apo vyagāhathāḥ /

Halfverse: c indro yo vṛtrahānmaham tasmād indra namo ‘stu te //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a pṛṣṭham dhāvantam haryor auccaiḥ śravasam abruvan /

Halfverse: c svastyaśva jaitrāyendram ā vaha susrajam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a ye tvā śvetā ajaiśravaso hāryo yuñjanti dakṣiṇam /

Halfverse: c pūrvā namasya devānām bibhrad indra mahīyate //16//

Hymn: 129

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a etā aśvā ā plavante //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pratīpam prāti sutvanam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a tāsām ekā hariknikā //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a hariknike kim ichāsi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a sādhum putram hiraṇyayam //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kvāhatam parāsyaḥ //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yatrāmūs tisraḥ śimśapāḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a pari trayaḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a pṛdākavaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a śṛṅgam dhamanta āsate //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a ayanmahā te arvāhaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sa ichakam saghāghate //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a saghāghate gomīdyā gogatīr iti //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a pumām kuste nimichasi //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a palpa baddha vayo iti //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a baddha vo aghā iti //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a ajāgāra kevikā //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a aśvasya vāro gośapadyake //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a śyenīpatī sā //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a anāmayopajihvikā //20//

Hymn: 130

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ko arya bahulimā iṣūni //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ko asidyāḥ payaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ko arjunyāḥ payaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a kaḥ kārṣṇyāḥ payaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a etam pṛcha kuham pṛcha //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a kuhākam pakvakam pṛcha //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a yavāno yatiṣvabhiḥ kubhiḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a akupyantaḥ kupāyakuḥ //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a āmaṇako maṇatsakaḥ //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a deva tvapratisūrya //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a enaścipaṅktikā haviḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a pradudrudo maghāprati //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a śṛṅga utpanna //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a mā tvābhi sakhā no vidan //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a vaśāyāḥ putram ā yanti //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a irāvedumayam data //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a atho iyanniyann iti //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a atho iyanniti //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a atho śvā asthiro bhavan //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a uyam yakāmśalokakā //20//

Hymn: 131

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a āminoniti bhadyate //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a tasya anu nibhañjanam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a varuṇo yāti vasvabhiḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a śatam vā bhāratī śavaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a śatamāśvā hiraṇyayāḥ /

Halfverse: c śatam rathyā hiraṇyayāḥ /

Halfverse: e śatam kuthā hiraṇyayāḥ /

Halfverse: g śatam niṣkā hiraṇyayāḥ //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ahula kuśa varttaka //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a śaphena iva ohate //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a āya vanenatī janī //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a vaniṣṭhā nāva gṛhyanti //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a idam mahyam madūriti //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a te vṛkṣāḥ saha tiṣṭhati //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a pāka baliḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a śaka baliḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a aśvattha khadiro dhavaḥ //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a araduparama //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a śayo hata iva //16//

Verse: 17

Halfverse: a vyāpa pūruṣaḥ //17//

Verse: 18

Halfverse: a adūhamityām pūṣakam //18//

Verse: 19

Halfverse: a atyardharca parasvataḥ //19//

Verse: 20

Halfverse: a dauva hastino dṛtī //20//

Hymn: 132

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ādalābukamekakam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a alābukam nikhātakam //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a karkariko nikhātakaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a tad vāta unmathāyati //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a kulāyam kṛṇavāditi //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ugram vaniṣadātatam //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a na vaniṣadanātatam //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a ka eṣām karkarī likhat //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ka eṣām dundubhim hanat //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a yadīyam hanat katham hanat //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a devī hanat kuhanat //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a paryāgāram punaḥpunaḥ //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a trīṇyuṣṭrasya nāmāni //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a hiraṇya ityeke abravīt //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a dvau vā ye śiśavaḥ //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a nīlaśikhaṇḍavāhanaḥ //16//

Hymn: 133

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a vitatau kiraṇau dvau tāvā pinaṣṭi pūruṣaḥ /

Halfverse: c na vai kumāri tat tathā yathā kumāri manyase //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a mātuṣṭe kiraṇau dvau nivṛttaḥ puruṣānṛte /

Halfverse: c na vai kumāri tat tathā yathā kumāri manyase //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a nigṛhya karṇakau dvau nirāyachasi madhyame /

Halfverse: c na vai kumāri tat tathā yathā kumāri manyase //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a uttānāyai śayānāyai tiṣṭhantī vāva gūhasi /

Halfverse: c na vai kumāri tat tathā yathā kumāri manyase //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ślakṣṇāyām ślakṣṇikāyām ślakṣṇamevāva gūhasi /

Halfverse: c na vai kumāri tat tathā yathā kumāri manyase //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a avaślaksṇamiva bhramśad antarlomamati hrade /

Halfverse: c na vai kumāri tat tathā yathā kumāri manyase //6//

Hymn: 134

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāg arālāgudabhartsatha //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāg vatsāḥ puruṣanta āsate //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāk sthālīpāko vi līyate //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāk sa vai pṛthu līyate //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāg āste lāhaṇi līśāthī //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāg akṣlilī puchilīyate //6//

Hymn: 135

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a bhug ity abhigataḥ śalityapakrāntaḥ phalityabhiṣṭhitaḥ /

Halfverse: c dundubhim āhananābhyām jaritarothāmo daiva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kośabile rajani granther dhānam upānahi pādam /

Halfverse: c uttamām janimām janyānuttamām janīn vartmanyāt //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a alābūni pṛṣātakānyaśvatthapalāśam /

Halfverse: c pipīlikāvataśvaso vidyutsvāparṇaśapho gośapho jaritarothāmo daiva //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a vīme devā akramsatādhvaryo kṣipram pracara /

Halfverse: c susatyam id gavām asyasi prakhudasi //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a patnī yadṛśyate patnī yakṣyamāṇā jaritarothāmo daiva /

Halfverse: c hotā viṣṭīmena jaritar othāmo daiva //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a ādityā ha jaritar aṅgirobhyo dakṣiṇām anayan /

Halfverse: c tām ha jaritaḥ pratyāyams tām u ha jaritaḥ pratyāyan //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a tām ha jaritar naḥ pratyagṛbhṇams tām u ha jaritar naḥ pratyagṛbhṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c ahānetarasam na vi cetanāni yajñān etarasam na purogavāmaḥ //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a uta śveta āśupatvā uto padyābhir yaviṣṭhaḥ /

Halfverse: c utem āśu mānam piparti //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a ādityā rudrā vasavas tvenu ta idam rādhah prati gṛbhṇīhy aṅgiraḥ /

Halfverse: c idam rādho vibhu prabhu idam rādho bṛhat pṛthu //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a devā dadatvāsuram tad vo astu sucetanam /

Halfverse: c yuṣmāṁ astu divedive pratyeva gṛbhāyat //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tvam indra śarmariṇā havyam pārāvatebhyaḥ /

Halfverse: c viprāya stuvate vasuvanim duraśravase vaha //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a tvam indra kapotāya chinnapakṣāya vañcate /

Halfverse: c śyāmākam pakvam pīlu ca vārasmā akṛṇor bahuh //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a aramgaro vāvadīti tredhā baddho varatrayā /

Halfverse: c irāmaha praśamsaty anirām apa sedhati //13//

Hymn: 136

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad asyā amhubhedyāḥ kṛdhu sthūlam upātasat /

Halfverse: c muṣkāvidasyā ejato gośaphe śakulāv iva //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yadā sthūlena pasasāṇau muṣkā upāvadhīt /

Halfverse: c viṣvañcā vasyā vardhataḥ sikatāsv eva gardabhau //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad alpikāsvalpikā karkadhūkevaṣadyate /

Halfverse: c vāsantikam iva tejanam yanty avātāya vitpati //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad devāso lalāmagum praviṣṭīminam āviṣuḥ /

Halfverse: c sakulā dediśyate nārī satyasyākṣibhuvo yathā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a mahānagnyatṛpnadvi mokradadasthānāsaran /

Halfverse: c śaktikānanā svacamaśakam saktu padyama //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a mahānagnyḥlūkhalam atikrāmanty abravīt /

Halfverse: c yathā tava vanaspate niraghnanti tathaiveti //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a mahānagnyupa brūte bhraṣṭothāpyabhūbhuvaḥ /

Halfverse: c yathaiva te vanaspate pippati tathaiveti //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a mahānagnyupa brūte bhraṣṭothāpyabhūbhuvaḥ /

Halfverse: c yathā vayo vidāhya svarge namavadahyate //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a mahānagnyupa brūte svasāveśitam pasaḥ /

Halfverse: c ittham phalasya vṛkṣasya śūrpe śūrpam bhajemahi //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a mahānagnī kṛkavākam śamyayā pari dhāvati /

Halfverse: c ayam na vidma yo mṛgaḥ śīrṣṇā harati dhāṇikām //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a mahānagnī mahānagnam dhāvantam anu dhāvati /

Halfverse: c imās tad asya gā rakṣa yabha mām addhyaudanam //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a sudevas tvā mahānagnīr babādhate mahataḥ sādhu khodanam /

Halfverse: c kusam pīvaro navat //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a vaśā dagdhām imāṅgurim prasṛjatogratam pare /

Halfverse: c mahān vai bhadro yabha mām addhyaudanam //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a videvas tvā mahānagnīr vibādhate mahataḥ sādhu khodanam /

Halfverse: c kumārīkā piṅgalikā kārda bhasmā ku dhāvati //14//

Verse: 15

Halfverse: a mahān vai bhadro bilvo mahān bhadra udumbaraḥ /

Halfverse: c mahāṁ abhikta bādhate mahataḥ sādhu khodanam //15//

Verse: 16

Halfverse: a yaḥ kumārī piṅgalikā vasantam pīvarī labhet /

Halfverse: c tailakuṇdamimāṅguṣṭham rodantam śudam uddharet //16//

Hymn: 137

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yad dha prācīr ajagantoro maṇḍūradhāṇikīḥ /

Halfverse: c hatā indrasya śatravaḥ sarve budbudayāśavaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a kapṛn naraḥ kapṛtham ud dadhātana codayata khudata vājasātaye /

Halfverse: c niṣṭigryaḥ putram ā cyāvayotaya indram sabādha iha somapītaye //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣam jiṣṇor aśvasya vājinaḥ /

Halfverse: c surabhi no mukhā karat pra ṇa āyūmṣi tāriṣat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a sutāso madhumattamāḥ somā indrāya mandinaḥ /

Halfverse: c pavitravanto akṣaran devān gachantu vo madāḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a indur indrāya pavata iti devāso abruvan /

Halfverse: c vācas patir makhasyate viśvasyeśāna ojasā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a sahasradhāraḥ pavate samudro vācamīṅkhayaḥ /

Halfverse: c somaḥ patī rayīṇām sakhendrasya divedive //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a ava drapso amśumatīm atiṣṭhad iyānaḥ kṛṣṇo daśabhiḥ sahasraiḥ /

Halfverse: c āvat tam indraḥ śacyā dhamantam apa snehitīr nṛmaṇā adhatta //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a drapsam apaśyam viṣuṇe carantam upahvare nadyo amśumatyāḥ /

Halfverse: c nabho na kṛṣṇam avatasthivāmsam iṣyāmi vo vṛṣaṇo yudhyatājau //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a adha drapso amśumatyā upasthe ‘dhārayat tanvam titviṣāṇaḥ /

Halfverse: c viśo adevīr abhy ācarantīr bṛhaspatinā yujendraḥ sasāhe //9//

Verse: 10

Halfverse: a tvam ha tyat saptabhyo jāyamāno ‘śatrubhyo abhavaḥ śatrur indra /

Halfverse: c gūlhe dyāvāpṛthivī anv avindo vibhumadbhyo bhuvanebhyo raṇam dhāḥ //10//

Verse: 11

Halfverse: a tvam ha tyad apratimānam ojo vajreṇa vajrin dhṛṣito jaghantha /

Halfverse: c tvam śuṣṇasyāvātiro vadhatrais tvam gā indra śacyed avindaḥ //11//

Verse: 12

Halfverse: a tam indram vājayāmasi mahe vṛtrāya hantave /

Halfverse: c sa vṛṣā vṛṣabho bhuvat //12//

Verse: 13

Halfverse: a indraḥ sa dāmane kṛta ojiṣṭhaḥ sa made hitaḥ /

Halfverse: c dyumnī ślokī sa somyaḥ //13//

Verse: 14

Halfverse: a girā vajro na sambhṛtaḥ sabalo anapacyutaḥ /

Halfverse: c vavakṣa ṛṣvo astṛtaḥ //14//

Hymn: 138

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a mahāṁ indro ya ojasā parjanyo vṛṣṭimāṁ iva /

Halfverse: c stomair vatsasya vāvṛdhe //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a prajām ṛtasya piprataḥ pra yad bharanta vahnayaḥ /

Halfverse: c viprā ṛtasya vāhasā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a kaṇvāḥ indram yad akrata stomair yajñasya sādhanam /

Halfverse: c jāmi bruvata āyudham //3//

Hymn: 139

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a ā nūnam aśvinā yuvam vatsasya gantam avase /

Halfverse: c prāsmai yachatam avṛkam pṛthu chardir yuyutam yā arātayaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad antarikṣe yad divi yat pañca mānuṣāṁ anu /

Halfverse: c nṛmnam tad dhattam aśvinā //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ye vām damsāmsy aśvinā viprāsaḥ parimāmṛśuḥ /

Halfverse: c evet kāṇvasya bodhatam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ayam vām gharmo aśvinā stomena pari ṣicyate /

Halfverse: c ayam somo madhumān vājinīvasū yena vṛtram ciketathaḥ //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yad apsu yad vanaspatau yad oṣadhīṣu purudamsasā kṛtam /

Halfverse: c tena māviṣṭam aśvinā //5//

Hymn: 140

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yan nāsatyā bhuraṇyatho yad vā deva bhiṣajyathaḥ /

Halfverse: c ayam vām vatso matibhir na vindhate haviṣmantam hi gachathaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a ā nūnam aśvinor ṛṣi stomam ciketa vāmayā /

Halfverse: c ā somam madhumattamam gharmam siñcād atharvaṇi //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ā nūnam raghuvartanim ratham tiṣṭhātho aśvinā /

Halfverse: c ā vām stomā ime mama nabho na cucyavīrata //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad adya vām nāsatyokthair ācucyuvīmahi /

Halfverse: c yad vā vāṇībhir aśvinevet kaṇvasya bodhatam //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yad vām kakṣīvāṁ uta yad vyaśva ṛṣir yad vām dīrghatamā juhāva /

Halfverse: c pṛthī yad vām vainyaḥ sādaneṣv eved ato aśvinā cetayethām //5//

Hymn: 141

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a yātam chardiṣpā uta paraspā bhūtam jagatpā uta nas tanūpā /

Halfverse: c vartis tokāya tanayāya yātam //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yad indreṇa saratham yātho aśvinā yad vā vāyunā bhavathaḥ samokasā /

Halfverse: c yad ādityebhir ṛbhubhiḥ sajoṣasā yad vā viṣṇor vikramaṇeṣu tiṣṭhathaḥ //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad adyāśvināv aham huveya vājasātaye /

Halfverse: c yat pṛtsu turvaṇe sanas tac chreṣṭham aśvinor avaḥ //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a ā nūnam yātam aśvinemā havyāni vām hitā /

Halfverse: c ime somāso adhi turvaśe yadāv ime kaṇveṣu vām atha //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a yan nāsatyā parāke arvāke asti bheṣajam /

Halfverse: c tena nūnam vimadāya pracetasā chardir vatsāya yachatam //5//

Hymn: 142

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a abhutsy u pra devyā sākam vācāham aśvinoh /

Halfverse: c vy āvar devy ā matim vi rātim martyebhyaḥ //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a pra bodhayoṣo aśvinā pra devi sūnṛte mahi /

Halfverse: c pra yajñahotar ānuṣak pra madāya śravo bṛhat //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a yad uṣo yāsi bhānunā sam sūryeṇa rocase /

Halfverse: c ā hāyam aśvino ratho vartir yāti nṛpāyyam //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a yad āpītāso amśavo gāvo na duhra ūdhabhiḥ /

Halfverse: c yad vā vāṇīr anuṣata pra devayanto aśvinā //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a pra dyumnāya pra śavase pra nṛṣāhyāya śarmaṇe /

Halfverse: c pra dakṣāya pracetasā //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a yan nūnam dhībhir aśvinā pitur yonā niṣīdathaḥ /

Halfverse: c yad vā sumnebhir ukthyā //6//

Hymn: 143

Verse: 1

Halfverse: a tam vām ratham vayam adyā huvema pṛthujrayam aśvinā samgatim goḥ /

Halfverse: c yaḥ sūryām vahati vandhurāyur girvāhasam purutamam vasūyum //1//

Verse: 2

Halfverse: a yuvam śriyam aśvinā devatā tām divo napātā vanathaḥ śacībhiḥ /

Halfverse: c yuvor vapur abhi pṛkṣaḥ sacante vahanti yat kakuhāso rathe vām //2//

Verse: 3

Halfverse: a ko vām adyā karate rātahavya ūtaye vā sutapeyāya vārkaiḥ /

Halfverse: c ṛtasya vā vanuṣe pūrvyāya namo yemāno aśvinā vavartat //3//

Verse: 4

Halfverse: a hiraṇyayena purubhū rathenemam yajñam nāsatyopa yātam /

Halfverse: c pibātha in madhunaḥ somyasya dadhatho ratnam vidhate janāya //4//

Verse: 5

Halfverse: a ā no yātam divo acha pṛthivyā hiraṇyayena suvṛtā rathena /

Halfverse: c mā vām anye ni yaman devayantaḥ sam yad dade nābhiḥ pūrvyā vām //5//

Verse: 6

Halfverse: a nū no rayim puruvīram bṛhantam dasrā mimāthām ubhayeṣv asme /

Halfverse: c naro yad vām aśvinā stomam āvant sadhastutim ājamīlhāso agman //6//

Verse: 7

Halfverse: a iheha yad vām samanā papṛkṣe seyam asme sumatir vājaratnā /

Halfverse: c uruṣyatam jaritāram yuvam ha śritaḥ kāmo nāsatyā yuvadrik //7//

Verse: 8

Halfverse: a madhumatīr oṣadhīr dyāva āpo madhuman no bhavatv antarikṣam /

Halfverse: c kṣetrasya patir madhumān no astv ariṣyanto anv enam carema //8//

Verse: 9

Halfverse: a panāyyam tad aśvinā kṛtam vām vṛṣabho divo rajasaḥ pṛthivyāḥ /

Halfverse: c sahasram śamsā uta ye gaviṣṭau sarvāṁ it tāṁ upa yātā pibadhyai //9//
